Explanatory Remarks:

1. Structural elements are enclosed in <>.
2. Editorial notes or additions are enclosed in ().

Text:

<3.1>
oṃ śrīgaṇeśāya namaḥ ||
|| oṃnamo nārāyaṇāya ||
|| atha śrīviṣṇumahāpurāṇe viṣṇudharmottaratṛtīyakhaṇḍaprārambhaḥ ||
|| vajra uvāca ||
kiṃ kartavyaṃ manuṣyeṇa kiṃ kurvansukhamedhate ||
asmim̐lloke pare caiva kenāpnoti mahatsukham || 1 ||
mārkaṇḍeya uvāca || ||
antarvedi bahirvedi puruṣeṇa vijānatā ||
devatāpūjanaṃ kāryaṃ lokadvayamabhīpsatā || 2 ||
yajñeṣu devayajanamantarvedi prakīrtitam ||
bahirvedi tathaivoktamupavāsavratādikam ||
iṣṭāpūrtena labhyante ye lokāstānbubhūṣatā || 3 ||
devānāmālayaḥ kāryo dvayamapyatra dṛśyate ||
viśeṣeṇa kalau kāle kartavyaṃ devatāgṛham || 4 ||
kṛtatretādvāpareṣu narāḥ paśyanti devatām ||
tiṣyaṃ prāpya na paśyanti pūjāstvarcāgatā yataḥ || 5 ||
kṛtādiṣvapi dṛṣṭvā ca devānmanujasattama ||
arcāgataviśeṣeṇa pūjayanti vidhānataḥ || 6 ||
citrasūtravidhānena devatārcāṃ vinirmitām ||
surūpāṃ pūjayedvidvāṃstatra saṃnihitā bhavet || 7||
svākārāṃ lakṣaṇopetāṃ yastāṃ saṃpūjayennaraḥ ||
sa sarvakāmānāpnoti nātra kāryā vicāraṇā ||
asmim̐lloke pare caiva sukhavānsa bhavetsadā || 8 ||
athārcāṃ lakṣaṇairhīnāṃ yastu pūjayate naraḥ ||
tasyānarthā vivardhante tasmāttāṃ ca vivarjayet || 9 ||
prāsādakaraṇaṃ puṇyaṃ devārcākaraṇaṃ tathā ||
surārcāpūjanaṃ puṇyaṃ tatra puṇyā namaskriyā || 3.1.10 ||
tasmātsarvapratnena surārcāṃ lakṣaṇānvitām ||
kārayetpūjanaṃ vidvānna tvalakṣaṇasaṃyutām ||11 ||
dharmamarthaṃ tathā kāmaṃ mokṣaṃ ca yadunandana ||
prayacchanti mahārāja narāṇāmiha devatāḥ || 12 ||
kāmāniṣṭānprayacchanti svargamatyantameva ca ||
tasmātsarvaprayatnena nṛṇāṃ pūjyā divaukasaḥ || 13 ||
nityaṃ narāṇāṃ surapūjakānāṃ lokā manojñāḥ sulabhā bhavanti ||
loke tathāsmindhanadhānyayuktāḥ samṛddhibhājaśca narā bhavanti || 14 ||
iti śrīviṣṇudharmottare tṛtīyakhaṇḍe mārkaṇḍeyavajrasaṃvāde citrasūtre prathamo'dhyāyaḥ ||1||
<3.2>
vajra uvāca ||
devatārūpanirmāṇaṃ kathayasva mamānagha ||
yasmātsannihitā nityaṃ śāstravatsākṛtirbhavet||1||
mārkaṇḍeya uvāca||
citrasūtraṃ na jānāti yastu samyaṅ narādhipa ||
pratimālakṣaṇaṃ vettuṃ na śakyantena karhicit || 2 ||
vajra uvāca ||
citrasūtraṃ samācakṣva bhṛguvaṃśa vivardhana ||
citrasūtravidevātha vetti vāglakṣaṇaṃ yataḥ ||
mārkaṇḍeya uvāca||
vinā tu nṛtyaśāstreṇa citrasūtraṃ sudurvidam||3||
jagato na kriyā kāryā dvayorapi yato nṛpa ||
vajra uvāca ||
nṛtyaśāstraṃ samācakṣva citrasūtraṃ vadiṣyasi||
nṛtyaśāstravidhānaṃ ca citraṃ vetti yato dvija||4||
mārkaṇḍeya uvāca||
ātodyaṃ yo na jānāti tasya vṛttaṃ hi durvidam ||
ātodyena vinā nṛttaṃ vidyate na kathaṃcana || 5 ||
vajra uvāca ||
ātodyaṃ tvaṃ hi dharmajña nṛtyaśāstraṃ vadiṣyasi ||
tasminsuvidite vetti nṛtyaṃ bhārgavasattama||6||
mārkaṇḍeya uvāca ||
na gītena vinā śakyaṃ jñātumātodyamapyuta ||
gītaśāstravidhānajñaḥ sarvaṃ vetti yathāvidhi || 7 ||
vajra uvāca ||
gītaśāstraṃ samācakṣva sarvadharmabhṛtāṃ vara ||
gītaśāstravidevātha sarvajñaḥ puruṣottamaḥ ||8 ||
mārkaṇḍeya uvāca ||
saṃskṛtaṃ prākṛtaṃ caiva gītaṃ dvividhamucyate ||
apabhraṣṭaṃ tṛtīyaṃ ca tadanantaṃ narādhipa || 9 ||
deśabhāṣāviśeṣeṇa tasyānto neha vidyate ||
gītaṃ pāṭhavaśājjñeyaṃ sa ca pāṭho dvidhā mataḥ||3.2.10||
gadyaṃ padyaṃ ca dharmajña gadyaṃ sakathayā smṛtam ||
padyaṃ chandoviśeṣeṇa cchandaśca bahudhā bhavet ||11 ||
gadyapadyānubhāvena jñeyau lakṣaṇasaṃyutau ||
padyaṃ ca dvividhaṃ tatra suvṛtramatimuktakam|| 12 ||
ekaviṃśatisaṃkhyāḥ syuḥ supaḥ suptiṅparaṃ smṛtam ||
prathamaḥ puruṣastveko dvitīyo madhyamo mataḥ || 13 ||
uttamākhyastṛtīyastu tatkriyāvacanaṃ trikam ||
tiṅaśca navakaṃ tatra parasmaipadasaṃjñakam || || 14 ||
ātmanepadasaṃjñaṃ ca navakaṃ parikīrtitam ||
yuṣmadyupapade tatra madhyamaḥ puruṣo bhavet || 15 ||
asmadyupapade caiva syāttathottamasaṃjakaḥ ||
śeṣe prathamamicchanti puruṣaṃ yadusattama ||16||
arthe prātipadikasya svādau vacanamantraṇe ||
parimāṇe ca kathitā vibhaktiḥ prathamā budhaiḥ || 17 ||
dvitīyā karmaṇi matā tṛtīyā karaṇe smṛtā ||
kārake kamarṇi tathā saiva cānyaiḥ prakīrtitā || 18 ||
caturthī saṃpradāne syādapādāne ca pañcamī ||
saṃbandhe ca tathā ṣaṣṭhī saptamyādhārabhāvayoḥ ||19||
teṣāṃ tu pūrvamevoktaṃ vyākhyānaṃ ripusūdana||
dvitīyaṃ tava dharmajña mayā tatparikīrtitam||3.2.20||
prātipadikamiha svaṃ prakṛtiḥ syāttatprakṛtimapi dhātumicchanti || vāṅmayamakhilamanena nibaddhaṃ gadyamapi yadatha padyasametam ||21 ||
iti śrīviṣṇudharmottare tṛtīyakhaṇḍe mārkaṇḍeyavajrasaṃvāde śabdādhyāyo nāma dvitīyo'dhyāyaḥ || 2 ||
<3.3>
mārkaṇḍeya uvāca ||
ṣaḍakṣareṇa pādena gāyatraṃ chanda ucyate ||
uṣṇik saptākṣaraṃ caiva aṣṭākṣaramanuṣṭubham || 1||
navākṣarā ca bṛhatī paṃktisaṃjñaṃ daśākṣaram ||
ekādaśākṣaraṃ triṣṭubjagatī dvādaśākṣaram||2||
trayodaśākṣaraṃ jñeyamatipūrvaṃ tathaiva hi ||
caturdaśākṣarā caiva śakvarī yadunandana||3||
atiśakvarī ca kathitā tathā pañcadaśākṣarā ||
ṣoḍaśākṣaramicchanti cchando'ṣṭiriti yatsmṛtam||4||
atyaṣṭisaṃjñaṃ chandaśca tathā saptadaśākṣaram||
aṣṭādaśākṣaraṃ caiva vṛttisaṃjñaṃ prakīrtitam || 5 ||
tathātivṛttisaṃjñaṃ ca viṃśati stvekavarjitā||
viṃśatiścākṣarāṇi syuḥ kṛtisaṃjñaṃ yadūttama||6||
viṃśatistvekasaṃyuktā prakṛtiḥ parikīrtitā||
dvāviṃśatiśca kathitaṃ tathaivākṛtisaṃjñakama ||7||
trayoviṃśatiśca vikṛtiḥ sā caikātsaṃkṛtirmatā ||
saṃkṛtiścaikasaṃyuktā kathitā vikṛtirnṛpa ||8||
ṣaḍviṃśatiścātikṛtiśchandaścoktaṃ manīṣibhiḥ ||
yathāyathaṃ caturbhiḥ svaiḥ pādairetāni nirdiśet || 9 ||
mātrakastu laghurvarṇaḥ spaṣṭalekhābhicihnitaḥ ||
saṃsthāpyaḥ syāddvimātrastu gururvakrastathā bhavet || 3.3.10 ||
laghuścāntyaśca yo varṇaḥ sa guruśca bhavennṛpa ||
dīrghaplutau ca kathitau tathaiva gurusaṃjñakau ||11 ||
saṃyogādyāḥ pare yasmātsa laghuśca guruḥ smṛtaḥ ||
laghuguruvibhāgena prastāronena yādava || 12 ||
sarve chandovikalpāśca bhavanti bahuśo nṛpa ||
syādyathānayanaṃ teṣāṃ tathā vakṣyāmi tacchṛṇu || 13 ||
yāvatsaṃkhyākṣaraṃ cchaṃdastāvanto guruvo nṛpa ||
sthāpyā adhaśca pūrvāṇāṃ laghu pūrvasya vinyaset || 14 ||
ato'nyeṣu tvavasthāpyaṃ nṛpamukhya yathāsthitam ||
evameva vidhiṃ kuryādādiśca gurubhiḥ punaḥ || 15 ||
pūrayedakṣaraistāvatkuryādetaṃ nṛpottama ||
yāvadakṣarasaṃkhyā syuḥ sarve hi laghavo nṛpa || 16 ||
etatsyādakṣaraicchando mātrāśchandāstathaiva hi ||
uktavarṇākṣaraṃ chando bhavedekavivarjitaḥ || 17 ||
yadā tadā nivṛtsaṃjño hyadhikena tamattathā ||
varṇadvayena rahitaṃ virāḍiti hi śabditam || 18 ||
adhikaṃ ca surāsaṃjñaṃ kathitaṃ yadusattama ||
tebhyo bhavanti sarvāṇi mātrācchandāṃsi yādava || 19 ||
diṅmātrametatkathitaṃ narendra vistārajijñāsurato manuṣyaḥ ||
saṃsādhayettatsvadhiyā yathāvatsudustaraṃ vistaraśo hi vaktum || 3.3.20 ||
iti śrīviṣṇudharmottare tṛtīyakhaṇḍe mārkaṇḍeyavajrasaṃvāde chandovidhirnāma tṛtīyo'dhyāyaḥ || 3 ||
<3.4>
mārkaṇḍeya uvāca ||
tatrājñāyuktamadvaidhaṃ dīptaṃ gambhīraśabdavat ||
kvacinniruktasaṃyuktaṃ vākyametatsvayaṃbhuvaḥ || 1 ||
yatkiṃcinmiśrasaṃyuktaṃ yuktaṃ nāma vibhaktibhiḥ ||
pratyakṣābhihitaṃ yacca tādṛśīnāṃ vacaḥ smṛtam || 2 ||
naigamairvividhaiḥ śabdairnipātaṃ bahulaṃ ca yat ||
na cāpi sumahadvākyamṛcīkānāṃ vacaḥ smṛtam || 3 ||
avispaṣṭapadaṃ jñeyamṛṣiputravaco nṛpa ||
bhūtabhavyabhavajñānaṃ janmaduḥkhavikutsanam || 4 ||
mitrāṇāṃ tadbhavedvākyaṃ garbheṣu ca pravartanam ||
ājñāyuktaṃ ca vacanaṃ tathā hetuvivarjitam || 5 ||
rājarṣīṇāṃ tu vijñeyaṃ bahvarthaṃ bahuvistaram ||
bahvabhidhānaṃ bahvarthe devatānāṃ prakīrtitam || 6 ||
bahvabhidhānamalpārthaṃ dānavānāṃ prakīrtitam ||
alpābhidhānamalpārthaṃ gandharvāṇāṃ tathā bhavet || 7 ||
durbodhaṃ viṣamaṃ caiva rākṣasānāṃ prakīrtitam ||
gūḍhākṣaraṃ tu yakṣāṇāṃ kinnarairuktavattathā || 8 ||
nāgānāmativispaṣṭaṃ punaruktasamanvitam ||
rāgadveṣasamāyuktaṃ hetumatpauruṣaṃ bhavet ||9||
mantrā navavidhāḥ proktāḥ ṛgyajuḥsāmalakṣaṇāḥ ||
stutirnindā praśaṃsā ca ākrośaḥ preṣya eva ca || 3.4.10 ||
praśno'nujñā tathākhyānamāśāstirviṣayā matāḥ ||
evaṃ te sarvavidyānāṃ vihitaṃ sarvalakṣaṇam ||11||
pauruṣe vidyate tacca nānyatreti viniścayaḥ ||
svabhāvānna cyavantyeva ye proktā naravarjiṃtāḥ || 12 ||
dṛśyate hi pumānkaścitkaścicchreṣṭhatamo dhiyā ||
tasmāttatraiva tasyoktā buddhiḥ śreṣṭhatamā nṛpa || 13 ||
etanmayoktaṃ suparīkṣaṇaṃ te vākyasya bho vākyavidāṃ variṣṭha ||
vicakṣaṇairatra ca rāgatastu jñeyaṃ tathā dveṣasamāgamācca || 14 ||
divyena tulyaṃ yadi vārṣavākyaṃ vākyena tulyaṃ yadi vā pareṇa||
vākyaṃ narāṇāṃ nṛparāgatastu jñeyaṃ yathādveṣasamāṃgamatvāt||15||
iti śrīviṣṇudharmottare tṛtīyakhaṇḍe mārkaṇḍeyavajrasaṃvāde vākyaparīkṣādhyāyo nāma caturtho'dhyāyaḥ || 4 ||
<3.5>
alpākṣaramasaṃdigdhaṃ sāravadviśvatomukhama ||
astobhamanavadyañca sūtraṃ sūtravido viduḥ || 1 ||
utsargeṇāpavādena dvividhaṃ tatprakīrtitam ||
sūtraṃ vyudāsaśca tathā tathodāharaṇaṃ nṛpa ||2||
pratyudāharaṇaṃ caiva caturaṅgaṃ prakīrtitam ||
vākyaṃ caivārthavākyārthaṃ padārthaḥ padameva ca || 3 ||
caturaṅgamidaṃ ceti tathaivānyatprakīrtitama ||
pratijñā hetudṛṣṭāntāvupasaṃhāra eva ca || 4 ||
tathā nigamanaṃ caiva pañcāvayavamiṣyate ||
ārambho'thāpi saṃbandhaḥ sūtrārthastadviśeṣaṇam || 5 ||
codanā parihāraśca vyākhyā sūtrasya ṣaḍvidhā ||
sūtreṣveva hi tatsarvaṃ yaddhṛtaṃ samudāhṛtam||6||
vistaroktaṃ matiṃ hanti samāsoktaṃ na gṛhyate||
samāsavistarau hitvā vaktavyaṃ yadvivakṣitam ||7||
apārthaṃ vyāhataṃ caiva punaruktaṃ tathaiva ca ||
tathā vibhinna saṃsthānaṃ yuktihīnaṃ vivarjayet ||8||
kramabhedo vibhaktaśca gurusūtraṃ tathaiva ca ||
abhidhānasya cānyatvaṃ naitāni syurakāraṇāt ||9||
sarvaṃ kṛtvā padacchedaṃ samāsaṃ tadanantaram ||
samāse tu kṛte paścādarthaṃ brūyādvicakṣaṇaḥ || 3.5.10 ||
sūtrārthaśca padārthaśca hetuśca kramaśastathā ||
niruktamatha vinyāso vyākhyā yogasya ṣaḍvidhā|| 11 ||
pratyakṣamanumānaṃ ca tathāptavacanaṃ bhavet ||
tribhiḥ pramāṇaiḥ saṃyuktaistantrī prāmāṇyamarhati || 12 ||
pratyakṣābhāsamapyuktaṃ tathā vai mṛgatṛṣṇikā ||
ābhāsamanumānasya vāṣpareṇucayau yathā || 13 ||
dhūmaśaṃkāñjanayato na ca vahnyupalabdhaye ||
āptatvaṃ darśayitvā tu yadanāptena bhāṣitam || 14||
tadāptavacanābhāsaṃ vadanti nṛpasattama ||
rāgadveṣavinirmukta āpta ityabhidhīyate || 15 ||
nairuktaṃ dvividhaṃ viddhi siddhamautpātikaṃ tathā ||
nirvaktavyaṃ tu tatsiddhamarthasiddhistu sarvadā||16||
tatra tvautpattikaṃ sarvaṃ gauraśvaḥ puruṣo yathā ||
daśadhā tu gaṇodgītaṃ tadvakṣyāmi yathāgatam ||17||
gaṇo naimittikobhyaktaḥ saṃvādaḥ kārttikastathā ||
upacāraśca sambandhaḥ gauṇiko yaugikastathā||18||
guṇāttu gauṇaṃ paśyāmo mahadityeva mādayaḥ ||
nimittatastu naimittaṃ khaṇḍamuṇḍādikaṃ yathā||19||
bhaktyā bhaktaṃ nṛsiṃhāsyādamātari ca mātṛvat ||
samaṃ vadanti yasmim̐śca saṃvādaścitvarī yathā ||3.5.20 ||
kārtakaḥ syāttu kṛtako devavattādiko yathā ||
upacārastūpacārāttailapāśaṃ tulā yathā||21||
saṃbandhatastu sābandhaśchatrī mīmāṃsako yathā ||
saṃyogādapi saṃyogaḥ kriyāyāḥ krayikaḥ smṛtaḥ||22||
paṭakṛllaguḍacchedyo jñeyaḥ śākaṭikastathā ||
evaṃ pareṣāṃ ca paraṃ syānnaigamanicaṇvakam || || 23 ||
sukhārtho na prasiddhaścetsādhanānāṃ vidhiṃ śṛṇu ||
yadvipannaṃ tu dṛśyeta tatsādhyaṃ sādhanairimaiḥ || 24 ||
ātmendriyamanorthānāṃ saṃyoga upadiśyate ||
jñānadeśādisaṃviṣṭaṃ pratyakṣamiti tadbhavet || 25 ||
buddhyā śarīrabhūtātmā yaśca yukto'numīyate ||
sognirdhūmādyathā vidyādanumānaṃ tadiṣyate || 26 ||
vedavidyāviruddhaṃ yatsā smṛtiḥ śiṣṭasaṃmatā ||
apratyakṣaphalaṃ vidyātsādhanaṃ śāstrasaṃjñitam || 27 ||
dvayoḥ sadṛśayorekaṃ nirdiṣṭaṃ sādhayetparam ||
gavayādiva goḥ siddhirupamānaṃ tadiṣyate || 28 ||
sādhyorthaścedatatkāryo vākye yasminprakalpyate ||
sārthāpattirdivā bhuṅkte devarāta itīti yat || 29 || 
nipātanādyogavibhāgadarśanādgurūpadeśādanuvārtikādapi ||
svatantrasiddheḥ paratantradarśanātprasādhayellakṣaṇataśca ṣaḍvidham ||3.5.30 ||
iti śrīviṣṇudharmottare tṛtīyakhaṇḍe mārkaṇḍeyavajrasaṃvāde tantraguṇadoṣavarṇano nāma pañcamo'dhyāyaḥ || 5 ||
<3.6>
mārkaṇḍeya uvāca ||
atha tantrayuktayo bhavanti adhikaraṇaṃ yogaḥ padārthaḥ hetvartha uddeśo nideśaḥ pradeśotideśaḥ apavargo vākyaviśeṣorthāpattiḥ prasaṃga ekānto naikāntaḥ pūrvapakṣo nirṇayo vidhānaparyāyotikrāntāvekṣaṇaṃ saṃśayo vyākhyānamanumatisvasaṃjñānirvacanaṃ dṛṣṭānto viyogo vikalpaḥ samuccayortha iti |
tadadhikaraṇaṃ yena vākyārtho yujyate saṃyogo yortho vikṛtasūtrapadaṃ sa padārtho yadanyata yuktimadarthasya sādhanaṃ sa hetvarthaḥ |
samāsavacanamuddeśo vistaravacanaṃ nirdeśa evamevetyupadeśonena kāraṇenetyapadeśaḥ || prakṛtasyānāgatena sādhanaṃ pradeśotikrāmaṇena sati deśobhiprāyānukarṣaṇamapavargo yenārthaḥ parisamāpyate |
padenāhāryeṇa sa vākyaśeṣo yadakīrtitamaryādāmāpadyate sārthāpattiḥ | prakaraṇābhihitoktaḥ kenacidupoddhātena punaruddiśyamānaḥ prasaṃgaḥ sarvatra yattathā sa ekāntaḥ kvacittathā kvacidanyathāsāvanekāntaḥ pratiṣedhavacanaṃ nirṇayaḥ prakaraṇānupūrvaṃ vidhānaṃ tasya prātilomyaṃ viparyaya ityuktam||
atikrāntāya kṣaṇaṃ paratra vakṣyāmi ityanāgatāvekṣaṇamubhayato hetudarśanaṃ saṃśayaḥ tatrātiśayavarṇanātivyākhyānaṃ paramatamapratiṣiddhamanumataṃ parairasaṃmataḥ śabdaḥ svasaṃjñā loke pratī tamudāharaṇaṃ nirvacanaṃ tadyuktinidarśanaṃ dṛṣṭānta eveti niyoga idaṃ vedaṃ veti vikalpa idaṃ cedaṃ ceti samuścayotra yadanirdiṣṭaṃ yuktigamyaṃ tadū hyamiti ||
prayojanaṃ saṃśayanirṇayau ca vyākhyāviśeṣo guṇalāghavaśca ||
kṛtavyudāso kṛtaśāsanaṃ ca sāṃvartiko dharmaguṇoṣṭakaśca ||
iti śrīviṣṇudharmottare tṛtīyakhaṇḍe mārkaṇḍeyavajrasaṃvāde tantraśuddhirnāma ṣaṣṭho'dhyāyaḥ || 6 ||
<3.7>
|| mārkaṇḍeya uvāca ||
athātaḥ saṃpravakṣyāmi tava prākṛtalakṣaṇam ||
ṛ ṛṝ lṛ lṝ na saṃtyatra naṣmā na ca maṣāvubhau || 1 ||
sakārahīnā ca tathā nāsikyāśca tathā nṛpa ||
rephaśca śayavā rājan saṃyoge nāsti karhicit || 2 ||
ekāraśca tathaukāraḥ padamadhye mahābalaḥ ||
dṛḍhayoge vakārotra ḍagayoge tathaiva ca ||3||
gayayoge gakāro'tra lopa māyāti nityadā ||
damau yuktau pṛthak kṛtvā dumau kāryau tathaiva ca || 4 ||
ṛtayoge dakārasyāttakārastvabhidhīyate ||
kakāraḥ padamadhyotra vaktavyo halvivarjitaḥ || 5 ||
nakārasya ṇakāraḥ syātkṣakārasya kha iṣyate ||
takāraśca thakāraśca jñakāraśca ṇa eva ca || 6 ||
kvacitkvacitsyā ttadā hakāraśca tathā kvacit ||
kṣakārasya tu vaktavyaṃ vāṃkārasya ha iṣyate || 7||
cakārasya chakāraḥ sthātyukare hal ca lupyate ||
yukte ṣakāre taddhībhe hakāro'pi vidhīyate || 8 ||
caturtho nātra vijñeyā na ca dvivacanaṃ kvacit ||
padādau yo guruḥ sotra laghureva vidhīyate || 9 ||
dvimātrametaduddiṣṭaṃ madhyā prākṛtalakṣaṇam ||
prayogādanukartavyo vistaro hyativistaraḥ ||3.7.10||
deśeṣu deśeṣu pṛthagvibhinnaṃ na śakyate lakṣaṇatastu vaktum ||
lokeṣu yatsyādapabhraṣṭasaṃjñaṃ jñeyaṃ hi taddeśavidodhikāram || 11 ||
iti śrīviṣṇudharmottare tṛtīyakhaṇḍe mārkaṇḍeyavajrasaṃvāde prākṛtabhāṣālakṣaṇaṃ nāma saptamo'dhyāyaḥ || 7 ||
<3.8>
mārkaṇḍeya uvāca ||
devā divaukaso divyā gīrvāṇāstridaśāḥ surāḥ ||
harirjanārdano viṣṇurvāsudevastvadhokṣajaḥ || 1 ||
nārāyaṇo hṛṣīkeśaścakrī garuḍavāhanaḥ ||
balaḥ saṃkarṣaṇo'nantaḥ śeṣastāladhvajo halī || 2 ||
pradyumnaḥ kāmadevaśca cāpī makaraketanaḥ ||
khaṅgāyudho'niruddhaśca tathā vai ṛṣyaketanaḥ ||3||
lakṣmīḥ karīṣiṇī śrīśca tathā devī vibhāvarī ||
cakraḥ sudarśanaḥ proktaḥ śārṅgaṃ cāpastu vaiṣṇavam||4||
māheśvaraṃ pinākākhyaṃ vāruṇaṃ gaṇḍivaṃ bhavet ||
śākramājagavaṃ proktaṃ vajraḥ kuliśa ucyate || 5 ||
vaiṣṇavī tu gadā proktā tathā kaumodakī nṛpa ||
halo lalāma ityuktaḥ sānandaṃ musalaṃ smṛtam || 6 ||
pāñcajanyaḥ smṛtaḥ śaṅkhaḥ yaṃ bibharti janārdanaḥ ||
kare'sya kamalaṃ proktaṃ tathāstrālokyasaṃjñitam || 7 |||
bhūmirvasuṃdharā pṛthvī kṣamā kṣoṇī dharā mahī ||
nabhaḥ khaṃ gaganaṃ caiva tathākāśaṃ hareḥ kramam || 8 ||
tārkṣyaḥ suparṇo garuḍo hariketanavāhanaḥ ||
nāgānaṃto haladharaḥ śeṣaḥ tāladhvajī halī || 9 ||
brahmā pitāmahaḥ śaṃbhuḥ svayaṃbhūrbhūtabhāvanaḥ ||
mahādevo bhavaḥ śaṃbhustryaṃbakaḥ pārvatīpatiḥ || 3.8.10 |||
umā satī parvatajā menakājā tathocyate ||
bhadrakālī smṛtā durgā gaṇāśca pramathāḥ smṛtāḥ || 11 ||
vināyako gaṇapatiḥ kumāraḥ skanda iṣyate |||
indraḥ śakraḥ kauśikaśca puruhūtaḥ purandaraḥ || 12 ||
agnirvaiśvānaro vahnirjātavedo hutāśanaḥ ||
vivasvato yamaścaiva kīnāśa pretanāyakaḥ || 13 ||
virūpākṣo nirṛtiśca tathā rātriṃcarādhipaḥ ||
yādasāmīśvaro devaḥ pracetā varuṇoṃbupaḥ ||14||
toyamaṃbu jalaṃ cāpaḥ pānīyaṃ salilaṃ payaḥ ||
vāyuḥ samīraṇo vātaḥ pāvanaḥ sarvago'nilaḥ || 15 ||
rājarājo dhanādhyakṣastathaivaiḍaviḍaḥ smṛtaḥ ||
īśānaḥ śaṅkaraḥ śarvaḥ tathā paśupatiḥ śivaḥ || 16 ||
nāsatyāvaśvinau proktau vasavo vasusaṃjñitāḥ ||
tathaivāṃgiraso nāgā viśve satrāgrabhojinaḥ || 17 ||
vṛṣabhāvagamāruddha bhṛgavo maruto matāḥ ||
sādhyā dharmasutāḥ proktā ādityā kaśyapātmajāḥ || 18 ||
māruto mātariśvāno grahāstribhuvaneśvarāḥ ||
nakṣatrāṇi tathā tāni ṛkṣāṇi ca uḍūni ca || 19 ||
ādityo bhāskaraḥ sūryaḥ savitā raviraryamā ||
candraḥ śītakaraḥ somaḥ śaśāṃko mṛgalāñchanaḥ || 3.8.20 ||
vakraḥ kṣitisuto bhaumo budho jñaḥ somanandanaḥ ||
bṛhaspatirgururjīvaḥ śukra āsphujireva ca || 21 ||
sāraṇaḥ pañcamaḥ prokto pramantheṣu vicakṣaṇaḥ ||
asurā dānavā daityā gaṃdharvā devanāyakāḥ || 22 ||
yakṣāḥ puṇyajanāḥ proktā yātudhānāśca rākṣasāḥ ||
adṛśyāśca tathā bhūtāḥ piśācāḥ piśitāśanāḥ || 23 ||
kinnarākhyāḥ kiṃpuruṣā nāgāḥ kadrusutā matāḥ ||
siddhā vidyādharā proktā devarāmāstathāpsarāḥ || 24||
maharṣayo bhūtakṛtaḥ tathā brahmarṣayomalāḥ ||
proktāḥ saptarṣayo loke tathā citraśikhaṃḍinaḥ || 25 ||
dhruvaḥ sthāsnustathā proktaḥ kṛkalāsastathaiva ca ||
āśā diśastathā kāṣṭhā bhacakraṃ bhagaṇaṃ smṛtam || 26 ||
kṛtāni śāstrāṇi narendra pūrvaiḥ smṛtistathoktā śrutireva vedaḥ ||
samyagyadācāramiti pradiṣṭaṃ vṛttaṃ hi yacchāstravidāṃ narendra || 27 ||
iti śrīviṣṇudharmottare tṛtīyakhaṇḍe mārkaṇḍeyavajrasaṃvāde devādiśabdaparyāyivarṇanaṃ nāmā 'ṣṭamo'dhyāyaḥ || 8 ||
<3.9>
saṃvatsaro'bdo varṣaṃ ca samā hāyana eva ca ||
divaso vāsaraścaiva dinaṃ cāhastathaiva ca || 1 ||
doṣā vibhāvarī rātriḥ śarvarī yāminī kṣapā ||
saṃdhiḥ proktā tathā sandhyā andhakāraṃ tamaḥ smṛtam || 2 ||
candrikā ca tathā jyotsnā caitro madhuriti smṛtaḥ||
vaiśākho mādhavaḥ proktaḥ śucirjyeṣṭha udāhṛtaḥ || 3 ||
śuklaḥ proktastathāṣāḍho nabhaḥ śrāvaṇa iṣyate ||
prauṣṭhapādo nabhasyaśca iṣaścāśvayujaḥ smṛtaḥ || 4 ||
ūrjākhyaḥ kārtikaḥ prokto mārgaśīrṣaḥ sahastathā ||
sahasyaḥ pauṣa ityukto māghaḥ syāttapa eva ca || 5 ||
phālgunaśca tapasyākhyo māso yadu kulodvaha ||
tulāmeṣagate bhānau viṣuvaddinamucyate || 6 ||
dhanvato mithunāntaśca ayane sosya dakṣiṇe ||
meṣānte ca tulānte ca yugādidivasau smṛtau || 7 ||
tathā viṣṇupadaṃ proktaṃ kulīramakarāntayoḥ ||
kanyā mithunamīnānāṃ praveśe dhanuṣastathā || 8 ||
ṣaḍaśītimukhaṃ nāma jñeyaṃ dina catuṣṭayam ||
raśmirgabhastirbhadrā ca karo dīdhitireva ca || 9 ||
sūryodayāstamayayoḥ sa dīrghaḥ paridhirbhavet ||
tiryaggatāṃ megharājīṃ tadeva parigho bhavet || 3.9.10 ||
tadeva daṇḍasaṃsthāno megho daṇḍobhidhīyate ||
śakracāpaṃ śakradhanuḥ taddīrghaṃ rohitaṃ bhavet ||1 1||
tadeva daṇḍasaṃsthāno megho daṇḍo'bhi dhīyate ||
śakraṃ cāpaṃ śakradhanuḥ taddīrghaṃ rohitaṃ matam||12||
tatpītaḥmathālpaṃ ka dupāsyaṃ pratisūryakam||
candrārkayormegharājitapariveṣastu maṇḍalāt ||13||
svargācchubhaphale kṣīṇe patannulketi kathyate ||
anityaḥ saśikho dṛṣṭaḥ tārakaḥ keturiṣyate ||14||
agnivarṇā tu diṅmṛṣṭā digdāha iti viśrutaḥ ||
nagaraṃ śūnyato dṛṣṭaṃ gandharvanagaraṃ smṛtam || 15||
nirghātaṃ stanitaṃ proktaṃ vinā meghanidarśanāt ||
yatoyato yavaṃ dṛṣṭiḥ kathitā mṛgatṛṣṇikā || 16||
megho ghanaṃ vāridaṃ ca jīmūtaṃ ca balāhakam ||
stanitaṃ garjitaṃ vidyād vṛṣṭiḥ proktāḥ pravarṣaṇe || 17 ||
āsāro vegavadvarṣaṃ pṛṣato maṃda ucyate ||
dṛśyamāne tathā sūrye divyaṃ proktaṃ ca varṣaṇam||18||
dṛśyamāne tathā candre sāmṛtaṃ parikīrtitam ||
vātyā vātasamutthā syānnīhāraṃ tama ucyate ||19||
śīkarākhyamavaśyāyaṃ tuṣārastuhinaṃ himam ||
vidyuttaḍittathā lolā cañcalā ca śatahradā || 3.9.20 ||
samudrāścāmbunidhayaḥ sāgarā lavaṇodakāḥ ||
nadyaḥ kuṭilagāminyo nimnagāḥ saritastathā || 21 ||
gaṅgāviṣṇupadī proktā jāhnavī tripathā tathā ||
kālindī yamunā proktā revā proktā ca narmadā ||
vipāśā ca sanandākhyā śatadruḥ śaurisaṃjñikā || 22||
buddhistathā sūrya iti pradiṣṭo bhūtāni coktāni tathendriyāṇi ||
vāṇī tathoktā ca sarasvatī ca manastathoktaṃ nṛpa candrasaṃjñam || 23 ||
iti śrīviṣṇudharmottare tṛtīyakhaṇḍe mārkaṇḍeyavajrasaṃvāde abhidhānakośo nāma navamo 'dhyāyaḥ || 9 ||
<3.10>
|| mārkaṇḍeya uvāca || ||
manuṣyo manujaścaiva puruṣaḥ puruṣastathā ||
rāmā yoṣittathā strī ca lalanā ca tathā smṛtā || 1 ||
varārohā purandhriśca śreṣṭhā sā mattakāśinī ||
brāhmaṇaśca tathā vipro rājanyaḥ kṣatriyo mataḥ || 2 ||
kīnāśaśca smṛto vaiśyaḥ śūdraścāntyaja ucyate ||
caṇḍālaḥ pukkakaḥ prokto mātaṅgaśca tathocyate || 3 ||
hastī dvipa ibho dantī mātaṅgaḥ kuñjaro gajaḥ ||
aśvaśca turagaḥ saptirhayo vājī haristathā || 4 ||
gaurukṣācchāgalo'jaśca urabhro'vistathocyate || 5 ||
asirviśasanaḥ khaḍgastīkṣṇa dhāro durāsadaḥ ||
śrīgarbho vijayaścaiva dharmādhārastathāṣṭamaḥ || 6 ||
suvarṇaṃ kanakaṃ rukmaṃ hemaṃ kārtasvaraṃ tathā ||
jāmbūnadaṃ taddevānāṃ rūpyaṃ rajatamiṣyate || 7 ||
udumbaraṃ smṛtaṃ tāmramayolohaṃ prakīrtitama ||
ārakūṭaṃ tathā rītī ratnaṃ māṇikyamucyate || 8 ||
ātapatraṃ smṛtaṃ chatraṃ kambuḥ śaṃkha udāhṛtaḥ ||
paṅkajaṃ kamalaṃ padmaṃ puṇḍarīkaṃ prakīrtitam ||9||
tathā tāmarasaṃ caiva śatapatraṃ tu daivatam ||
indīvaraṃ cotpalaṃ syātkahlāraṃ śuklameva tat||3.10.10||
saṃgrāmamājiryuddhaṃ ca janyamāhava eva ca ||
dānaṃ saṃvananaṃ proktaṃ vivāho viniveśanam ||11||
patnī bhāryā tathā jāyā sutaḥ putrastanūdbhavaḥ ||
mātā janitrī jananī janitā janakaḥ pitā || 12 ||
kṣayaṃ ca bhavanaṃ proktaṃ mandiraṃ gṛhameva ca ||
bhāṇḍāgāraḥ smṛtaḥ kośo dhanvanaṃ guptiriṣyate || 13||
vāhanaṃ patrasaṃjñaṃ syādamatraṃ pātramucyate ||
vardhamānaṃ śarāve syāccāpaṃ ca dhanurucyate||14||
sāyakaśca śaraḥ prokta iṣurbāṇaḥ śilīmukhaḥ ||
hastamocyaḥ sa evokto bṛhantaścaiva tovaraḥ||15||
puṣpaprakaramityuktamupakāraṃ narādhipa||
bālavyajanamapyuktaṃ tathā cāramasaṃjñitam||16||
mṛjā śobhā vinirdiṣṭā alaṅkāro vibhūṣaṇam ||
prasādhanaṃ maṇḍanaṃ syādbalaḥ sāra udāhṛtaḥ ||17||
arbhakaśca smṛto bālo bālaścāśvaḥ kiśorakaḥ||
hastī kalabha ityukto vatsaścokṣā prakīrtitaḥ || 18 ||
sarvathā keśarī siṃhaḥ śārdūlo vyāghra ucyate ||
vipinaṃ nirjanaṃ proktamajiraṃ syādgṛhāṅgaṇam || || 19 ||
upanīya dadadvedamācāryaḥ sa udāhṛtaḥ ||
ekadeśamupādhyāyo ṛtvigyajñakṛducyate || 3.10.20||
sāṃvatsaro jyautiṣavid vṛto bhavati bhūbhujā ||
tasyaiva yajñakṛdyastu sa purohita iṣyate || 21||
amātyaśca smṛto mantrī deśo nicaya ucyate ||
rājā narendro nṛpatistathaiva siṃhāsanaṃ tasya tathāsanaṃ syāt ||
kṣattā pratīhāra iti pradiṣṭastaddhastadaṇḍaḥ kathitaṃ tu vetram || 22 ||
iti śrīviṣṇudharmottare tṛtīyakhaṇḍe mārkaṇḍeyavajrasaṃvāde abhidhānakośe caṇḍālādiparyyāyavarṇano nāma daśamo'dhyāyaḥ || 10 ||
<3.11>
mārkaṇḍeya uvāca ||
kīrtirlakṣmīrdhṛtirmedhā matiḥ puṣṭiḥ sarasvatī ||
smṛtiḥ śraddhā ratiścaiva dhṛtirnītiḥ sthitiḥ śrutiḥ || 1 ||
rātriḥ prabhā kṣapā dīptiḥ śālā mālā niśā spṛhā ||
dolā helā tathā līlā yāminī kṣaṇadācarā || 2 ||
yātrā bhastrā dyutirmātrā daṃṣṭrā senā vacā tathā ||
kuṭiḥ khorī dayā jyotsnā sīmā veṇistathaiva vāk ||3 ||
tanurdhūliḥ śalākā ca grīvā bhrūśca śarattathā||
daraḥ prāvṛṭ cācamanī sthūṇā cūḍā sirā vasā ||4||
kakṣyā saptā surā jaṃghā jaṭā ghaṇṭā kaśā tathā ||
vidyutkakṣā saridvallīriṣīkā rajjuriṣṭakā || 5 ||
digbhūrbhūmirmahī kṣoṇī kṣapā caiva vasundharā||
chāyā camūrvālukā ca tathā vidyudvipatsarit ||6||
irā sampadavāpaśca līlā velā talā śilā ||
kalā ca mekhalā jvālā senā dadrurvalī samā ||7||
jyā ca mlānistathā hāniḥ śāṭī svāhā tathaiva gīḥ ||
dhīrvṛddhirdhūḥ kṣamā buddhiḥ kṣutkṣamāśīḥ savālukā||8||
viṃśaternavatiryāvatkroḍāsvaduśirodharā ||
māyā ca śaṣkulī jihvā ghaṭotkā snāyureva ca ||9||
lekhā jarā tathā paṅktistretā prāvṛḍarocakā ||
lākṣā kaṇḍūḥ priyaṃguśca māṇḍikā kaṭirauṣadhiḥ || 3.11.10 ||
ṛksphuliṅgā kaliḥ śayyā koṭiḥ kanyā ca nālikā||
niḥśreṇiḥ pṛtanā dīptirnidrā tandrā guhā ruciḥ || 11 ||
sāmnā dāmnā tathā vīthī nīvirdhārā tathā kṛṣiḥ ||
maṃjūṣā parṣadaścaiva patākā kākinī kṛpā || 12 ||
pelā caiva samaṃjiṣṭhā bherī nemirmanaḥśilā ||
darvī sthālī sabhā vīciḥ sthalī caiva samañjarī ||13||
vallarī mandurā lākṣā sūcī cādhiṣaṇā śamī ||
makṣikā vikṛtiryūkā śikhā śākhā tathāṭavī || 14 ||
strīvṛttayaste kathitā mayaite śabdāḥ samāsastvayamatra rājan ||
idaṃ tamānantyamuśaṃti sarvaṃ strīvācakaṃ śabdamuśanti dhīrāḥ || 15 ||
iti śrīviṣṇudharmottare tṛtīyakhaṇḍe mā0 va0 saṃ0 abhidhānakośe strīliṅganirdeśo nāmaikādaśo'dhyāyaḥ || 11 ||
<3.12>
mārkaṇḍeya uvāca ||
ataḥ paraṃ pravakṣyāmi yaduvaṃśavivardhana ||
tava puṃvācakāñśabdāñśṛṇu tāngadato mama || 1 ||
rājā kṛtākṣamūrdhātmā pāyuśleṣmāśmapūraṇaḥ ||
majjā plīhā ca kathitā nātādyaskāḥ pumarthakāḥ || 2 ||
vṛtraḥ putrastathā mantrastathaivāmantra iṣyate ||
puṃsi rāśirmuhurtaśca māsaḥ pakṣo'bdavatsarau || 3 ||
ṛtuḥ kālo giriḥ śailaḥ śiloccayattugācalāḥ ||
surāsurāṇāṃ saṃjñāśca yāśca proktā mahodadheḥ || 4 ||
saṃvatsaraśca divaso graho vāsara eva ca ||
paryāyaḥ paṅkaśarayorarinistriṃśayorapi || 5 ||
putraparyāyaśabdāśca pitṛparyāyasaṃyutāḥ ||
kaṇṭhabāhvostathā saṃjñāḥ stanakeśaradasya ca ||6||
gulphanāmāni karṇaśca hanuḥ praśno vidhirniṃdhiḥ ||
kapolanāmāni tathā nirghokekṣukakelayaḥ || 7 ||
kalirvighno ghaṭo jūṭaḥ paṭaḥ pāyuḥ kuśoṃ'kuśaḥ ||
binduḥ svaraḥ kuṭhāraśca paṇa śāṇau ca parpaṭaḥ || 8 ||
vaṭo bandhaḥ puṭo mṛtyurmanyuḥ śoṇaḥ kiṇaḥ phaṇaḥ ||
guṇaścūrṇo gaṇaḥ panthā adhvāṅguḥ pūga eva ca ||9 ||
kamaṇḍaluḥ palāṃ ḍuśca gaṇḍaḥ sthūlaśca kaṅkaṭaḥ ||
prasthaḥ śaṅkho rathaścaiva raso vargastuṣo budhaḥ || 3.12.10 ||
yavo māṣastathā śabdaḥ sparśo gandhaḥ śaṭho vaṇik ||
karīṣastaralo meḍhro ratho maṇḍapa eva ca || 11 ||
narako nūpuraścaiva samudgī loka eva ca ||
kāṣārṣayaḥ koṇamusalau kusūlaḥ śūrpadardurau ||
pañca piṇyākaśūrpāśca vātadaṇḍāṃkurāṃkuśāḥ || 12 ||
kuṇayaḥ prothayūthau ca kuntadarpaṇaveṇukāḥ ||
dhvajo vaṃśastathā veṇuḥ krimikalkarṣikardarāḥ || 13 ||
pustakaścādhvaro vrīhirambudoñjana eva ca || 14 ||
staṃbhaḥ paśurgharmanidāghakumbhāḥ kaṭāhayūpastabakāḥ saphenā ||
nitambaṣaṇḍhakṣupasūpayūpāḥ puṃsi smṛtā yādavavaṃśamukhya ||15 ||
iti śrīviṣṇudharmottare tṛtīyakhaṇḍe mārkaṇḍeyavajrasaṃvāde'bhidhānakośe puṃvarganirdeśo nāma dvādaśo'dhyāyaḥ || 12 ||
<3.13>
mārkaṇḍeya uvāca ||
antyaliṅge'tha vartante ye śabdā nṛpasattama ||
tānahaṃ saṃpravakṣyāmi śṛṇuṣva gadato mama || 1 ||
jalanāmāni sarvāṇi vināpo yadusattama||
bhrāntāśca pūrvaṃ yenoktāste napuṃsakavṛttayaḥ || 2 ||
dvyṛco nāntāstathā ye ca noktāste yadusattama ||
asatyaṃ ca dhanaṃ sarvaṃ jaraso'nyatra yādava || 3 ||
napuṃsake ca vartante śabdā locanavācakāḥ || 4 ||
sarvāṇi puṣpanāmāni kathitāni napuṃsake ||
śarīramasthi dadhi ca sakthi vā gudajānunī||5||
śmaśrukūle kulaṃ parṇaṃ lohanāmāni yāni ca ||
aharniśaṃ śakṛtsakthi dhanuḥ śoṇitadāruṇī ||6||
kṣīraṃ viṣāmṛte ṣaṇḍaṃ phalamūlamalāni ca ||
puraṃ madhu khalaṃ nālaṃ cīvaraṃ cīramambaram || 7||
gaganaṃ ghanamākāśaṃ sukhaduḥkhapadāni ca ||
kūpabīje'tha palalaṃ talpasadma śavāni ca||8||
nimittacittapittaughatakraśukrabhayāni ca||
tīrthapañjaracelāni phaladvandvākṣarāṇi ca || 9 || 
dhānyaśmaśānaratnāgratalparatnayugāni ca ||
gopapattanasainyārdhamaṅgalāyudhakhāni ca || 3.13.10 ||
nagaraṃ haritālaṃ ca timiraṃ palitaṃ viṣam ||
śmaśānaṃ mithunaṃ tattvaṃ kilbiṣaṃ vidalaṃ himam ||11||
vṛndaṃ kutūhalaṃ śīdhu śarāvaṃ śikharaṃ palam ||
vṛttaṃ ca mukuṭaṃ paṇyaṃ malamaṃśukameva ca || 12 ||
iṃdriyaṃ yugalaṃ kūṭaṃ kapālaṃ śūlaśekhare ||
pātālaṃ vivaraṃ randhraṃ chidramākāśameva ca || 13 ||
tṛṇaṃ śubhramiraṃ caiva kuṅkumaṃ śuṣkacīvaram ||
mṛṇālamajitaṃ khaḍgaṃ tuhinaṃ dvāramāspadama || 14 ||
rāṣṭraṃ talaṃ kiṭṭakaphe tathāyuḥ sthūlaṃ khalīnaṃ palalaṃ palālam ||
kāṣṭhaṃ raṇaṃ pārśvakukundare ca sandehake ceva napuṃsakaṃ syāt ||15||
iti śrīviṣṇudharmottare tṛtīyakhaṇḍe mārkaṇḍeyavajrasaṃvāde abhidhānakośe napuṃsakavidhau trayodaśo'dhyāyaḥ || 13 ||
<3.14>
mārkaṇḍeya uvāca ||
ekaikasya tu varṇasya vinyāso yaḥ punaḥpunaḥ ||
arthagatyā tu saṃkhyātamanuprāsaṃ purātanaiḥ || 1 ||
atyarthaṃ tatkṛtaṃ rājangrāmyatāmupagacchati ||
śabdāḥ samānānupūrvyā yamakaṃ kīrtitaṃ punaḥ || 2 ||
ādau madhye tathaivānte pādasya tu tadiṣyate ||
saṃdaṣṭakasamudrākhyau tathaiva yamakau matau || 3 ||
samastapādayamakaṃ duṣkaraṃ parikīrtitam ||
upamānena tulyattvamupameyasya rūpakam || 4 ||
rūpakābhyadhikaṃ nāma tadevaikaguṇādhikam ||
guṇānāṃ vyatirekeṇa vyatirekamudāhṛtam || 5 ||
upamānaviruddhaiśca guṇaistadaparaṃ matam ||
dvitryarthaṃ vācakaiḥ śabdaiḥ śleṣa ityabhidhīyate || 6 ||
anyarūpasya cārthasya kalpanā yānyathā bhavet ||
utprekṣākhyo hyalaṅkāraḥ kathitaḥ sa purātanaiḥ||7||
upanyāsastathānyaḥ syātprastutādyaḥ kvacidbhavet||
jñeyaḥ sorthāntaranyāsaḥ pūrvārthānugato yadi || 8 ||
upanyāsena vānyasya yadanyaḥ parikīrtyate ||
upanyāsamalaṅkāraṃ taṃ narendra pracakṣate || 9 ||
hetuṃ vinā vitatatāṃ prāptā sā tu vibhāvanā ||
proktā cātiśayoktistu hyatulairupamāguṇaiḥ || 3.14.10 ||
yathā svarūpakathanaṃ svabhāvoktiḥ prakīrtitā ||
bhūyasāmupadiṣṭānāṃ nirdeśaḥ kramaśastathā || 11 ||
yathāsaṃkhyamiti proktamalaṅkāraḥ purātanaiḥ ||
viśeṣaprāpaṇāduktā viśeṣoktistathā nṛpa || 12 ||
yā kriyā cānyaphaladā virodhastu sa iṣyate ||
stutirūpeṇa yā nindā nindāstutirihocyate || 13 ||
nindāstutistathaivoktā nindārūpeṇa yā stutiḥ ||
vastunā rūpyamāṇena darśanaṃ tannidarśanam || 14 ||
vinā tathā syādupamā tu yatra tenaiva tasyaiva bhavennṛvīra ||
ananvayākhyaṃ kathitaṃ purāṇairetāvaduktaṃ tava leśamātram || 15 ||
iti śrīviṣṇudharmottare tṛtīyakhaṇḍe mārkaṇḍeyavajrasaṃvāde alaṅkārādhyāyo nāma caturdaśo'dhyāyaḥ || 14 ||
<3.15>
mārkaṇḍeya uvāca ||
dharmārthakāmamokṣāṇāṃ śāstraṃ syādupadeśakam ||
pūrvairācaritaṃ sadbhirdharmakāmārthasādhakam || 1 ||
mokṣasya yatropanyāsa itihāsaḥ sa ucyate ||
tadeva kāvyamityuktaṃ copadeśaṃ vinā kṛtam || 2 ||
ekasya caritādeva tathā kāvyaṃ prakīrtitam ||
nibaddho yatra rājendra nāyakapratināyakau ||3||
prayāṇodyatasaṃpreṣayuddhayuktaṃ tadeva tu ||
nāyakābhyudayopetaṃ mahākāvyaṃ tadiṣyate ||4||
varṇayecca mahākāvye deśapattanapārthivān ||
ṛtvadrinimnagāścaiva tathā nārīśca pārthiva ||5||
varṇanīyau tathaivātra nāyakapratināyakau ||
nāyako dharmavijayī satāmpanthānamāśritaḥ||6||
tathā ca lokavijayī vijñeyaḥ pratināyakaḥ ||
pratināyakaghātastu vaktavyo netarasya tu || 7 ||
nāyakasya mahārāja maraṇaṃ naiva varṇayet ||
saśarīrasya yasya syātsaṃprāptistasya varṇayet || 8 ||
chandovirahitaṃ gadyaṃ śabdaśāstravirodhi ca ||
kaṣṭākṣarapadanyāsamaślīlavacanānvitam || 9||
tasmādullakṣyavākyārthamaprasiddhābhidhānavat ||
kāvyabandhaṃ na kartavyaṃ punaruktaṃ ca yadbhavet || 3.15.1 0 ||
vismaye vābhyasūyāyāṃ bhayaśokatvarāsu ca ||
harṣe ca vīpsā kartavyā punaruktaṃ na tadviduḥ ||11||
dūtavākye tathā svapne kāryagatyā tathāgate||
punarvardhananirdeśe punaruktaṃ ca tadviduḥ || 12 ||
sasaṃśayaṃ na vaktavyaṃ pratijñārahitaṃ tathā ||
pūrvāparaviruddhaṃ ca yacca lokavigarhitam || 13 ||
śṛṅgārahāsyakaruṇāraudravīrabhayānakaiḥ ||
bībhatsādbhutaśāntākhyai rasaiḥ kāryaṃ samanvitam || 14 ||
kāvyaṃ kalākauśalasaṃprayuktaṃ dharmeṇa cārthena tathopapannam ||
dharmeṇa yuktasya ca nāyakasya kāryaṃ tathā cābhyudayāntameva || 15 ||
iti śrīviṣṇudharmottare tṛtīyakhaṇḍe mārkaṇḍeyavajrasaṃvāde mahākāvyalakṣaṇaṃ nāma pañcadaśo'dhyāyaḥ || 15 ||
<3.16>
|| mārkaṇḍeya uvāca||
kāvye ye'bhihitā doṣāḥ kaiścittebhyaḥ prahelikā ||
kartavyāśca tathaivānyāstāsāṃ vakṣyāmi lakṣaṇam || 1 ||
ślokenaikena vā dvābhyāṃ kartavyā tu narottama ||
na kartavyāśca tā rājanbahuślokanibandhanāḥ || 2 ||
tasyāṃ mātrāvaśādartho gopyate sā samāgatā ||
paryāyavacanādyasyāṃ vanditā sā prakīrtitā || 3 ||
vṛttāntagopikāntāyā padairvyavahitairbhavet ||
aprasiddhaistu paryāyairduḥkhitā nāma sā smṛtā || 4 ||
kalpanāgopitārthāttu kathitā parihāsikā ||
samānarūpatulyārtho śabdairanyaiḥ prakīrtitā || 5 ||
durbodhākṣarabandhā ca paruṣā nāmataḥ smṛtā||
saṃkhyāmātrasamānatvātsaṃkhyātākhyā tathocyate || 6 ||
arthāntarānkalpanayā kalpitā ca tathocyate ||
anyasya saṃjñāyāmohānnamāntaritasaṃjñitā ||7||
arthavyāmohataḥ proktā nibhṛtā ca mahānṛpa ||
samānaśabdā kathitā tulyaśabdanibandhanā ||8||
arthānulomadurbodhā vyāmūḍheti tathocyate ||
gucchākhyā kathitā vṛtte nānābandhani bandhanā || 9 ||
ekobhayabahucchanne yathārthaparigopite ||
saṃkīrṇā sambhavopetā yā tadarthakarī bhavet || 3.16.10 ||
tadarthakārī yatrārtho jñāyate'nyārthakāmukaḥ ||
rājañśabdavaśātsā ca kathitā vyabhicāriṇī || 11 ||
ukterthe vṛttabandhena mātrābhiḥ sthāpitā tathā ||
naṣṭārthā vā ca sā proktā vṛttabandhanidarśitā || 12 ||
naṣṭākṣarā tu kathitā vinaikena tadārthadā ||
anyārthatā tathaivoktā varṇabhraṣṭā nareśvara || 13 ||
kālasya rūparūpāṇāṃ vyākhyānena tadārthadā ||
bījamātreṇa leśākhyā bhūmipāla prakīrtitā || 14 ||
aślīlametāsvapi naiva kāryaṃ tadvarjyamāhuḥ kavayo narendra ||
udvejanīyaṃ tu satāṃ taduktamaślīlabandhaṃ sukhadaṃ na kāvyam || 15 ||
iti śrīviṣṇudharmottare tṛtīyakhaṇḍe mārkaṇḍeyavajrasaṃvāde prahelikālakṣaṇo nāma ṣoḍaśo'dhyāyaḥ || 16 ||
<3.17>
|| vajra uvāca || ||
mantrāḥ sabrāhmaṇāḥ proktāstadarthaṃ brāhmaṇaṃ smṛtam ||
kalpanā ca tathā kalpaḥ kalpaśca brāhmaṇastathā || 1 ||
kathitāni tathārthāni purāṇaṃ ca tathā dvija ||
prakriyā prathamaḥ pādaḥ kathāvastuparigrahaḥ || 2 ||
upodghātānuṣaṅgau ca tathā saṃhāra eva ca ||
catuṣpādaṃ hi kathitaṃ purāṇaṃ bhṛgunandana || 3 ||
sargaśca pratisargaśca vaṃśo manvantarāṇi ca ||
vaṃśānucaritaṃ caiva purāṇaṃ pañcalakṣaṇam || 4 ||
tatra vā viditaṃ sarvaṃ tatroktaṃ gītalakṣaṇam ||
vṛttasya lakṣaṇaṃ caiva nāṭakākhyānalakṣaṇam || 5 ||
tasmādahaṃ tvāṃ pṛcchāmi dvija nāṭyasya lakṣaṇam ||
sabhedaṃ tanmamācakṣva sarvajño'si yataḥ prabho||6||
mārkaṇḍeya uvāca ||
itihāsānusāreṇa purāṇānāṃ samīkṣitam ||
caritaṃ tridaśānāṃ vā nāṭakaṃ tatra kīrtitam||7||
ekanāyakasaṃyuktaṃ nāyakapratināyakaiḥ|
saṃyuktamathavā kāryaḥ kāvye vai tatra nāyakaḥ ||8||
sarvāstu vṛttayastatra tatra sarve tathā rasāḥ ||
kālaprayogaṃ ca tathā yuktyā saṃdarśayennṛpa ||9||
pañcāvarāṅkaṃ tatproktaṃ tathādaśaparaṃ śubham ||
ekadaivatikaṃ vṛttamaṅke rājanpradarśayeta || 3.17.10 ||
tadante sarvapātrāṇāṃ niṣkramaśca vidhīyate ||
yuktyupanyāsasaṃyuktaṃ tathā niṣkamaṇaṃ matam ||11||
maraṇaṃ rājyavibhraṃśo nagarasyoparodhanam ||
etāni darśayennāṃke tathā yuddhaṃ ca pārthiva || 12 ||
praveśakena kartavyaṃ teṣāmākhyānakaṃ budhaiḥ ||
pātradvayena kartavyaṃ tathā nityaṃ praveśakam || 13 ||
pātre parijane tatra kartavyaṃ nāyakena tu ||
bhinnaṃ pātradvayaṃ yatra tatra viṣkambhakaṃ smṛtam ||14||
tayorante tu kartavyā tathā raṅgasya śūnyatā ||
aṅgānte ca tathā kāryā na kāryā sānyathā bhavet || 15 ||
bahudaivatikaṃ vṛttaṃ kartavyaṃ tu praveśakam ||
saṃkṣapoktiśca kartavyā kartavyo nahi vistaraḥ || 16 ||
prakhyātanāyakavadhaṃ na ca tatra praveśayet||
tasya prakāśanaṃ kāryamaṅkepyeva narādhipa || 17 ||
nāyakābhyudayaḥ kāryo nāṭakānte tathaiva ca ||
bahvyo'pi nāyikā yatra tāsāmapi tathā bhavet || 18 ||
evaṃvidhā saśṛṅgārā caturaṅkāḥ tu nāṭikā ||
kṛtaṃ prakaraṇaṃ tadvatsvayamutpādya vastunā || 19 ||
brāhmaṇo nāyakastatra vaṇikca nṛpasattama ||
evaṃ prakaraṇī kāryā caturaṅkāpi sā bhavet || 3.17.20 ||
itihāsānubandho vā svayamutpādya vā kṛtaḥ ||
bhāratīkaruṇaprāyo nivṛttaṃ samanantaram || 21 ||
catuṣṭayāṅkaḥ kartavyo divyanāyakavartitaḥ ||
itihāsānusāreṇa prāṇaścaikāhikaḥ smṛtaḥ || 22 ||
ekāṃko yuddhabahulasvātmaśaṃsāparastathā ||
ākāśakathanairyuktastvekapātrakṛtakriyaḥ || 23 ||
proktaḥ samavatārākhyastridaśāsuranāyakaḥ ||
triśṛṅgārastrikapaṭastathā dvādaśanāyakaḥ || 24 ||
īhāmṛgo'tha bahvaṅkastathā gandharvanāyakaḥ ||
śṛṅgārabahulaḥ kāryo'tṛpta yonirasāśrayaḥ || 25 ||
aikāhiko dṛptaraso vyāyogaścaikanāyakaḥ ||
vīthī trayodaśāṅkā syāttathā nāyakavarjitā ||26 ||
ḍimākhyaśca tathā prokto rasastu suranāyakaḥ ||
tathā raudrarasaprāyaḥ prakhyastaviṣayānvitaḥ || 27 ||
ekāṅko hāsyabahulastathaivodāttanāyakaḥ ||
kāryaḥ prahasanākhyastu veśyāviṭasamanvitaḥ || 28 ||
sūtradhārasya vākyena kāvyavastunibandhanam ||
sarvaṃ prakāśayedādau sarveṣveteṣu vastu yat || 29 ||
saṃskataṃ nāyakavaco liṅgiśrotriyabhūbhujām ||
dvijanmanāṃ surāṇāṃ ca daityagandharvabhoginām || 3.17.30 ||
nāyikādevarāmāṇāṃ vyājaliṅgavatāṃ tathā ||
vidūṣakāṇāṃ ca bhavetprākṛtaṃ vacanaṃ nṛpa || 31 ||
bālastrīnīcaṣaṇḍānāṃ ye cānye nānukīrtitāḥ ||
teṣāṃ vākyamapabhraṣṭaṃ tacca jñātvā prayojayet || 32 ||
svayamutpāditānāṃ me nānātvaṃ śṛṇu lakṣaṇam ||
śarma varmadhanānyante varṇānāṃ dāsavattathā || 33 ||
rājñāṃ vikramasaṃyuktamamātyānāṃ tathā bhavet ||
strīṇāṃ sukhodyamakrūraṃ nāyikānāṃ manoharam || 34 ||
rājñīparijanastrīṇāṃ kalākauśalasaṃyutam ||
rājñaḥ parijanastrīṇāṃ vijayottaramiṣyate || 35 ||
sāṃvatsarāṇāṃ māṅgalyaṃ śāntiprāyaṃ purodhasām ||
śūraprāyamamātyānāmāyupyaṃ bhiṣajāmapi || 36 ||
viṭānāṃ bhūṣaṇaprāyaṃ dāsīnāṃ kusumādikam ||
ṛṣigotradharau kāryau kañcukīyavidūṣakau || 37 ||
dattā mitrā tathā senā vaiśyā nāma tato bhavet ||
adhikārānurūpāṇi tathā caivādhikāriṇām || 38 ||
ataḥ paraṃ pravakṣyāmi yena vācyastu yo yathā ||
deveti rājā vaktavyo bhṛtyaiḥ prakṛtibhistathā || 39 ||
rājeti viprairvaktavyo ṛṣibhirnāmagotrataḥ ||
apatyapratyayāddevairvayasyeti samo janaḥ || 3.17.40 ||
vidūṣako'pi vaktavyo vayasya iti nāyakaiḥ ||
āyuṣmāniti sūtena rathī vācyastathā bhavet ||41 ||
bhagavanniti vaktavyā ṛṣayo guravaḥ surāḥ ||
putra vatseti śiṣyasya tathā vā nāmagotrataḥ || 42 ||
āryaputreti vaktavyaḥ striyā bhartā narādhipa ||
nāmnā bhāryā tu vaktavyā rājñī devīti nāyikā || 43 ||
pitā tātastathā vācyo mātuścaivāryakaḥ pitā ||
yuvarājaḥ kumāraḥ syānmānyo bhāvaḥ samīritaḥ || 44 ||
mānyamānastathā bhāmo bhartṛdāraḥ kumārakaḥ ||
haleti tu samānā strī vaktavyā samayā tathā || 45 ||
hañjetyāmantrayennārī haṇḍe parijane svake ||
svaseti bhaginī vācyā liṅgasthā vratinī tathā || 46 ||
āryeti brāhmaṇī vācyā śeṣā liṅgārthatastathā ||
antaḥpuracaro yastu strībhogaparivarjitaḥ || 47 ||
sa tu varṣadharo jñeyastadadhyakṣastu kañcukiḥ ||
brāhmaṇaḥ sa bhaved vṛddho mānyaśca vasudhādhipaiḥ || 48 ||
vidūṣako brāhmaṇaḥ syānnāyakasya rahasyavit ||
naṭaḥ pradhānaḥ sa bhavetsūtradhāro narādhipa || 49 ||
jñeyā bījasamutpattirmukhaṃ nānā rasodbhavaḥ ||
mukhotthabījodghaṭanaṃ tathā pratimukhaṃ smṛtam || 3.17.50 ||
prāpyāprātiyuto garbhastasyaivodbhedasaṃjñitaḥ ||
tadevodbhinnabījārtho visargo vyasanānvitaḥ || 51 ||
mukhādīnāṃ nirvahaṇaṃ tathā nirvahataṃ bhavet ||
nāṭake saprakaraṇe kartavyāḥ pātrasandhayaḥ || 52 ||
ḍime samavakāre ca visargaṃ naiva kārayet ||
vyāyogehāmṛgau kāryo tathā garbhavivarjitau || 53 ||
utsṛṣṭāṅka prahasanaṃ vīthī bhāṇakameva ca ||
eteṣāṃ nāvakartavyaṃ tathā pratimukhaṃ nṛpa || 54 ||
audāryasatpathaṃ śauryaṃ nāyakastu na saṃtyajet ||
nāyikāpi tathā rājanyajedvā pratināyakaḥ || 55 ||
ataḥ paraṃ pravakṣyāmi nāyikāṣṭakalakṣaṇam ||
nijāparādhātsvagṛhe vāsasajjā tu nāyikā || 56 ||
virahotkaṇṭhitā saiva yā tvanāgatanāyikā ||
svādhīna bhartṛkā proktā yā syātsvādhīnabhartṛkā || 57 ||
kalahāntaritā proktā kalahāhatanāyakā ||
khaṇḍitā yā ca nakharairyutameti pragopatim || 58 ||
vipralabdhā tu sā jñeyā saṃkete kāntavarjitā ||
pravāsagatakāntā tu tathā proṣitabhartṛkā || 59 ||
etāsāmucitākāralīlābhirbandhanaṃ ghanam ||
nāṭakādiṣu rūpeṣu kartavyaṃ dvādaśasvapi || 3.17.60 ||
śṛṅgārahāsyakaruṇavīraraudrabhayānakāḥ ||
bībhatsādbhutaśāntākhyā nava nāṭyarasāḥ smṛtāḥ ||61||
bandho rasānugaḥ kāryaḥ sarveṣveteṣu yatnataḥ ||
rasapradhānamevaitatsarvanāṭyaṃ narādhipaḥ || 62 ||
ete kalākauśalaśīlayuktāḥ kāryāstathā loka vidhānayuktāḥ || dharmārvakāmādyupadeśagāśca hitāya lokasya narendracandra || 63 ||
iti śrīviṣṇudharmottare tṛtīyakhaṇḍe mārkaṇḍeyavajrasaṃvāde dvādaśarūpakavarṇano nāma saptadaśo'dhyāyaḥ || 17 ||
<3.18>
|| mārkaṇḍeya uvāca || ||
atha gītalakṣaṇaṃ bhavati tasya trīṇi sthānāni uraḥ kaṇṭhaḥ śiraśca, tebhyo mandramadhyatārotpattiḥ |
trayo grāmāḥ | ṣaḍjamadhyamagāndhārāḥ |
saptasvarā | ṣaḍjarṣabha gāndhāramadhyamapañcamaniṣādadhaivatāḥ
ekaviṃśatimūrcchanāḥ sapta sapta pratigrāmāśritāḥ |
tadyathāsāṃ nāmāni sauvīrī hāriṇāśvā kalopanatā śuddhamadhyamā mārgī pauravī ṛṣyakā ceti ||
etāḥ sapta madhyamagrāmiṇyaḥ uttarāsaṃjñāḥ |
prāñcinī uttarāyatā śuddhaṣaḍjamatsarīkṛtā aśvakrāntā udgatā ca etāḥ sapta ṣaḍjagrāmikāḥ |
ālāpī kuntimā śraddhā uttarā ṣaḍjā pañcāyatāḥ .....etāḥ sapta gāndhāragrāmikāḥ | athaikonapañcāśattānāḥ | agniṣṭomikaḥ atyagniṣṭomikaḥ pauṇḍarikaḥ āśvamedhikaḥ rājasūyikaḥ vahusuvarṇikaḥ gosavikaḥ mahāvratikaḥ brahma tānaḥ prājāpatyaḥ nagāśrayaḥ yajñāśrayaḥ godānikaḥ hayakrāntaḥ ajakrāntaḥ viṣṇukrāntaḥ araṇyaḥ mattakokilaḥ ujjīvikaśceti ete viṃśatirmadhyamagrāmikāḥ |
prasvāpanaṃ paiśācaḥ jīvanaḥ sāvitraḥ ardhasāvitraḥ sarvatobhadraḥ suvarṇaḥ viṣṇuḥ jiṣṇurviṣṇuvaraḥ śāradaḥ vijayaḥ haṃsaḥ jyeṣṭha iti ete caturdaśaṣaḍjagrāmikāḥ | tumburupriyaḥ mahālakṣmaṇaḥ gandharvānumataḥ alambusapriyaḥ nāradapriyaḥ bhīmasenapriyaḥ vitānaḥ mātaṅgaḥ bhārgavapriyaḥ abhirāmaḥ saṃśrāvyaḥ kinnarapriyaḥ puṇyaḥ manoharaḥ kalyāṇakaraśceti ete pañcadaśa gāndhāramāśritāḥ
etaccaturvidhaṃ svarapadalayāvadhānayogāt |
mūrcchanāsvādimadhyānte vādisaṃvādyantyavādisaṃjñāstisro vṛttayaḥ||
pūrvoktāśca navarasāḥ tatra hāsyaśṛṅgārayormadhyamapañcamau|
vīraraudrādbhuteṣu ṣaḍjapañcamau | karuṇe niṣādagāndhārau |
bībhatsabhayānakayordhaivatam |
śānte madhyamam |
tathā layāḥ hāsyaśṛṅgārayormadhyamāḥ |
bībhatsabhayānakayorvilambitaḥ |
vīraraudrādbhuteṣu drutaḥ |
cokṣāprayogaḥ svarorādhane rūpakeṣu prakaraṇānugataḥ manyuvyapadeśena te bhavanti||
bhavanti cātra ślokāḥ ||
grahāṃśastāramandre ca nyāsopanyāsa eva ca||
alpatvaṃ ca bahutvaṃ ca ṣāḍavoḍavite tathā||
evameva budhairjñeyā jātayo daśalakṣaṇāḥ||
alaṅkārāśca catvāro bhavanti vasudhādhipa ||
prasannādiḥ prasannāntaḥ prasannādyanta eva ca ||
prasannamadhyaśca tathā krameṇaivaṃ vinirdiśet ||
aparāntakamullopyaṃ mandrakaḥ makarī tathā ||
uveṇakaṃ saro bindumuttamaṃ gītakāni tu||
ṛggāthā pāṇikā dakṣavihitā brahmagītikā||
gītametattadābhyāsaḥ kāraṇāccokṣasaṃjñaḥ ||
saṃkṣepeṇa mayā proktaṃ surārādhana kāraṇāt ||
gītajño yadi gītena nāpnoti paramaṃ padam ||
devasyānucaro bhūtvā tenaiva saha modate ||
iti śrīviṣṇudharmottare tṛtīyakhaṇḍe mā0 va0 saṃ0 gītalakṣaṇo nāmāṣṭādaśo'dhyāyaḥ || 18 ||
<3.19>
mārkaṇḍeya uvāca ||
athātodyavidhānam ||
caturvidhamātodyaṃ tataṃ suṣiraṃ ghanamavanaddhaṃ ca |
tataṃ vīṇādi, śuṣiraṃ vaṃśādi ghanaṃ tālādi avanaddhaṃ murajādi tatra svarāstulyāḥ śārīrā vaiṇavāśca|
tadvadvaṃśā kintu yathā śarīrāṇāmā rohāvarohaṇe tathā vaiṇavānāṃ vaṃśānāṃ viparyayāt tālaḥ kālaprayojyaḥ |
pañcalaghvakṣaroccāraṇakalāmātrāstisro vṛttayaḥ |
citrā vṛttirdakṣiṇā dvidhā |
mātrākalācitre caturmātrāvṛttau aṣṭamātrā dakṣiṇāyā |
aṣṭamātrādakṣiṇe avayā niṣkrayo vikṣepaḥ praveśaḥ śampātālaḥ saṃnipātabhedaḥ | kalāsamūho yuktaḥ |
capuṭasthaḥ ayuk cañcatpuṭalaḥ plutāntaḥ pañcapāṇiḥ kha iti tāputrā yathākṣarasthaḥ | tatra layāḥ drutamadhyavilambitāḥ |
layavattālaḥ |
prakaraṇaṃ dvividham | kulakaṃ cchedyakaṃ ca |
athāvanaddhaṃ tatra pauṣkaravatkvaṇānvitaṃ karuṇānvitam |
tatra ṣoḍaśākṣarāṇi ka kha ga gha ṭa ṭha ḍa ṇa ta tha da dha ya ra la va iti, tatra kaṭarathaugādidakṣiṇamukhe, ghadhamā vāme, gadakārā mṛrdhniḥ ṭaṇalahā āliṅge aāiueo ai ca o yathāyogaṃ khagadhā nāṃ repheṇa yogaḥ |
kahabhadānāṃ ṇalathavaḥ |
samahastanipātādaṅgulīpracalanordhavāmaikayoḥ samahastanipātapradeśinī tadvad ḍallānāṃ yogaḥ samapāṇyardha pāṇiradīrghapāṇirekapāṇiḥ dvihastapradeśinīprahatāni samapāṇiprahatopakāra ghathagaḍarāadhyardhapāṇau kathakārā|
adhyardhapāṇau ṭhaṇalahāsnahādvihastena talapradeśinīprahate ahitavitastāliptagomukhīmārgāḥ śṛṅgārahāsyayoraṃdhitaḥ |
vīraraudrādbhuteṣu vitastaḥ karuṇāśāntayorāliptaḥ |
bībhatsabhayānakayorgomukhadardurapaṇavayormṛdaṅgānugamanaṃ bhavati |
bhavanti cātra nepathyabhavanadvārāsamānaraṅgābhivīkṣaṇam |
murajāḥ pṛṣṭhatasteṣāṃ tadvanmārjanikā bhavet ||
yāmyatastasya vijñeyo darduraḥ paṇavastathā ||
uttarābhimukho gātā tathā dakṣiṇato bhavet ||
vāmato gairikastasya dakṣiṇe vaṃśavādakau ||
gāturevābhimukhyena gāyakānāṃ niveśanam ||
iti śrīviṣṇudharmottare tṛtīyakhaṇḍe mārkaṇḍeyavajrasaṃvāde ātodyavidhirnāmaikonaviṃśo'dhyāyaḥ ||19 ||
<3.20>
parasyānukṛtirnāṭayaṃ nāṭyajñaiḥ kathitaṃ nṛpa ||
tasya saṃskārakaṃ nṛttaṃ bhavecchobhāvivardhanam || 1 ||
nṛtyaṃ tu dvividhaṃ proktaṃ nāṭyalāsyāśrayaṃ sadā ||
dvaividhyaṃ dvividhasyāpi punaruktaṃ narādhipa || 2 ||
abhyantarañca bāhyaṃ ca bāhyaṃ lakṣaṇavarjitam ||
abhyantaraṃ lakṣaṇavanmaṅgalyaṃ tatprayogataḥ || 3 ||
lāsyaṃ svacchandataḥ kāryaṃ maṇḍape yadi vā bahiḥ ||
nāṭyaṃ maṇḍapa eva syānmaṇḍapaṃ dvividhaṃ bhavet || 4 ||
āyataṃ caturasraṃ tu dvātriṃśaddhastasammitam ||
caturasraṃ na kartavyamāyataṃ dviguṇāyatam || 5||
hīnādhikaṃ na kartavyaṃ dṛṣṭādṛṣṭaśubhapradam ||
hīne bhavati sammardo vistīrṇe nāṭyageyayoḥ || 6 ||
vyaktirnaivopapadyeta tasmāttau parivarjayeta ||
ādāveva tu kartavyaṃ tridaśānāṃ tu pūjanam || 7 ||
pūjanaṃ jarjarasyāpi vāstudaivatapūjanam ||
evaṃ kṛtvā tato nāṭyaṃ nāndīpūrvakamiṣyate || 8 ||
tato javanikākṣepaiḥ pratipātrapraveśanam ||
praveśanirgamau kāryau kṛtvā kāryasya sūcanam || 9 ||
puṣpāñjaliḥ pradātavyā nāṭyānte vāstupūjanam ||
pāṭhye gīte samāsaktaḥ pātratulyaḥ parikramaḥ ||3.20.10||
catuṣkalo nāyakānāṃ madhyānāṃ trikalo bhaveta ||
dvikalaścādhamānāṃ syānmadhyame nṛpakarmaṇi || 11 ||
ekādhikonaṃ sarveṣāṃ kartavyaṃ kāryayogataḥ ||
devā vīroddhatā jñeyā uddhatā dānavādayaḥ || 12 ||
nṛpāśca dhīralalitā dhīrāstadanujīvinaḥ ||
praśāntadhīrā ṛṣayaḥ praśāntāstatpadānugāḥ || 13 ||
dhīrodāttāstathā viprā udāttā vaṇijo matāḥ ||
evaṃvidhāśca kathitā eteṣāmeva yogataḥ || 14 ||
āharyaḥ sāttvikaścaiva yācikoṃgika eva ca ||
catuṣprakāro'bhinayaḥ kartavyo nāṭyakarmaṇi || 15 ||
āhāryaḥ pratiśīrṣādirdvikṛto veśa ucyate ||
dhīroddhatānāṃ veśaḥ syānna cātyarthasamulbaṇaḥ || 16 ||
uddhataścoddhatānāṃ syādrājñāṃ lalita iṣyate ||
pratināyakaveśastu kartavyaśca tathoddhataḥ || 17 ||
anyeṣāṃ sadṛśo veśo deśakarmāśrayo bhavet ||
aśvādayaśca kartavyāścarmakāṣṭhādibhiḥ samāḥ || 18 ||
ataḥ paraṃ pravakṣyāmi sāttvikābhinayaṃ tava ||
aśruprapātaromāñcasvedanaṃ spandanaṃ tathā||19||
tathā ca varṇavinyāsaiḥ sāttvikābhinayo bhavet||
vāciko vācayā prokto vakṣyāmyaṃgikamuttare ||3.20.20||
nāṭyavṛttaṃ tu kartavyaṃ harṣasthāne sadā bahu ||
śokasthāne na kartavyaṃ kiñcidvā kārayettataḥ ||21||
caturdhā recakaṃ proktaṃ kaṭipādāṃghrikāśrayam ||
cātakānyā mahācārī cārī ca dvividhā matā || 22 ||
sukumārāṅgaḥ vākceṣṭā cārī bhavati yādava ||
etairabhyarthitairjñeyā mahācārī nareśvara || 23 ||
maṇḍalāni daśaivāsya cārīsaṃyogajāni tu ||
atikrāntaṃ vicitraṃ ca tathā lalitasañcaram|| || 24 ||
sūcīviddhaṃ daṇḍapādaṃ vikṛtālātasaṃjñite ||
vāmabaddhaṃ salalitaṃ krāntaṃ cākāśagāni tu || 25 ||
maṇḍalāni daśānyāni śṛṇu bhaumāni pārthiva ||
bhramarāskandite syātāṃ mārdavaṃ ca tataḥ param || 26 ||
samā saritamityāhureḍakākrīḍitaṃ yathā ||
aḍḍitaṃ śakaṭākhyaṃ ca tathāhāryamiti smṛtam || 27 ||
piṣṭakubjaṃ ca vijñeyaṃ tathā cāpagataṃ punaḥ ||
athāṅgahārānvakṣyāmi nāmatastānnibodha me || 28 ||
sthirahastoṅgahārastu tathaivākṣiptakaḥ smṛtaḥ ||
udghāṭitastṛtīyaḥ syāttathā cānyo'parājitaḥ || 29 ||
mattākrīḍo'tha viṣkambhaḥ tathā svastikarecitaḥ ||
vṛścikāpasṛtaścaiva talamandā ca sarpitaḥ || 3.20.30 ||
matallīskhalitaścaiva bhramaraḥ pārśvasvastikaḥ ||
madādvilasitaścaiva vikṣipto gatimaṇḍalaḥ || 31 ||
vaiśākharecitaścaiva parivṛttakarecitaḥ ||
udvartakaḥ paricchinno recako'tha balāhakaḥ || 32 ||
sambhrānto vāṅgikaścaiva iti krīḍo hyalātakaḥ ||
vidyudbhrāntaḥ parāvṛttaḥ pārśvacchedasamanvitaḥ || 33 ||
ākṣipto recitaścaiva saumyo'tha karitastathā ||
sūcīviddho'paviddhaśca vilāpovanikuṭṭitaḥ || 34 ||
ṣaṭtriṃśadete saṃproktā hyaṅgahārā yadūttama ||
eteṣāṃ saṃpravakṣyāmi kāraṇāni yathākramam ||
hastapāda pracāraśca yathā yojyaḥ prayoktṛbhiḥ || 35 ||
sarveṣāmaṅgahārāṇāṃ niṣpattiḥ kāraṇaṃ bhavet ||
tānyahaṃ saṃpravakṣyāmi nāmabhiḥ karmabhistathā || 36 ||
hastapādasamāyukto nṛttasya karaṇaṃ bhaveta ||
dve nṛtyakaraṇe caiva bhavetāṃ nṛpa mātṛkā || 37 ||
tribhiḥ kalāpakaścaiva caturbhiḥ khaṇḍakaḥ smṛtaḥ ||
pañcaiva karaṇāni syuḥ saṃjñāghāta iti smṛtaḥ || 38 ||
ṣaṅbhirvā saptabhirvāpi aṣṭabhirnavabhistathā ||
karaṇairiha saṃyuktā aṅgahārā iti smṛtāḥ || 39 ||
eteṣāṃ caiva vakṣyāmi nāmānyahamataḥ param ||
tālapuṣpāpaviddhe dve līnasvastikarecitam || 3.20.40 ||
maṇḍalaṃ svastikaṃ caiva ūru vṛttaṃ nikuṇḍakam ||
sūcīviddhaṃ kaṭicchinnamardharecitameva ca || 41 ||
vakṣaḥ svastikamutpannaṃ svastikaṃ pṛṣṭhagaṃ punaḥ ||
diksvantikamalātākhyamardhasūcīkaṭīsamam||42||
ākṣiptarecitaṃ kṣipramardhasvastikasañcitam||
bhujaṅgavāsitaṃ caiva ūrdhvajānu nikuñcitam||43||
matallī tvardhamatallī syādrecakanikuṇṭakam ||
pāraviddhaṃ taḍiddhāntaṃ valitaṃ cūrṇitaṃ tathā ||44||
lalitaṃ daṇḍapakṣaṃ ca bhujaṅgatrastarecitam||
nūpuraṃ recitaṃ caiva bhramaraṃ caturaṃ tathā||45||
bhujaṅgāñcitamanyatsyācchinnaṃ vṛścikarecitam ||
latāvṛścikamanyatsyācchinnaṃ vṛścikarecitam ||46||
vṛścikaṃ vyaṃsitaṃ caiva sūcīviddhakameva ca ||
lalāṭatilakaṃ caiva kuṇḍalaṃ cakramaṃḍalam ||47||
tarormaṇḍalamākṣiptaṃ latālasitamārgalama ||
vikṣiptaṃ bhramitaṃ caiva vilāsaṃ vānaraplutam||48||
parivṛttaṃ nivṛttaṃ ca pārśvākrāntaṃ nikuñcitam ||
atikrāntamavakrāntaṃ dolāpādavivarjitam ||49||
nāgaprakrīḍitaṃ caiva viplutaṃ garuḍaplutam ||
gaṇḍasūcī parikṣiptaṃ pārśvajānukameva ca || 3.20.50 ||
gṛhāvalīnakaṃ līnaṃ viṣṇukrāntamajaplutam ||
ākrāntaṃ maṇḍitaṃ caiva mayūralasitaṃ nasam || 51 ||
sarpitaṃ colapādaṃ ca hariṇaplutameva ca ||
preṅkhālitaṃ nitambaṃ ca skhalitaṃ parihastakam || 52 ||
asarpitatalaṃ caiva siṃhavikrīḍitaṃ tathā ||
vṛṣabha krīḍitaṃ caiva gaṅgāvataraṇaṃ tathā || 53 ||
aṣṭottaraśataṃ caiva karaṇānāṃ prakīrtitam ||
sukumāraṃ tathā viddhaṃ vṛttaṃ dvividhamucyate ||54 |||
uddhṛtaṃ puruṣaprāyaṃ strīprāyaṃ sukumārakam ||
devacihnakṛtākārā piṇḍī bhavati parthiva || 55||
bhāratī sāttvatī caiva kauśikyārabhaṭī tathā ||
catasro vṛttayaḥ proktā vākpradhānā tu bhāratī || 56 ||
tathā vīrarasaprāyā vijñeyā sāttvatī nṛpa ||
tathā raudrapracārā ca bhavatyārabhaṭī sadā || 57 |||
śṛṅgārahāsyabahulā tathā bhavati kauśikī ||
āvantī dākṣiṇātyā ca tathā caivātra māgadhī || 58 ||
pañcālī madhyamā ceti vṛttiḥ sā tu caturvidhā ||
veṣabhāṣānukaraṇaṃ tathācārapravartanam || 59 ||
pravṛttiriti vikhyātā vṛttīnāmāśrayāstu tāḥ||
nāṭyalokāśrayā dharmi dharmāśca dvividhā matā || 3.20.60 ||
siddhiśca dvividhā nāṭye daivikī mānuṣī tathā ||
vyāyāmānmānuṣī tatra daivī devaprasādajā || 61 ||
rasena bhāvena samanvitaṃ ca tālānugaṃ kāvyarasānugaṃ ca ||
gītānugaṃ nṛttamuśanti dhanyaṃ sukhapradaṃ dharmavivardhanaṃ ca || 62 || ||
iti śrīviṣṇudharmottare tṛtīyakhaṇḍe mārkaṇḍeyavajrasaṃvāde sāmānyavarṇano nāma viṃśo'dhyāyaḥ || 20 ||
<3.21>
|| mārkaṇḍeya uvāca ||
samākuñcitakaṃ caiva prasāritavivartite ||
udvāhitaṃ nataṃ ṣoḍhā śayyāsthānāni nirdiśet || 1 ||
uttānitaṃ mukhaṃ caiva sāstamuktakaraṃ tathā ||
samaṃ vāmaprasuptasya sthānakaṃ tu vidhīyate || 2 ||
sarvairākuñcitairaṅgaiḥ śayyāviddhe ca jānunī ||
sthānamākuñcitaṃ nāma gītāntānāṃ prayojayeta ||3||
ekaṃ bhujaṃ samādhāya saṃprasāritajānukam ||
sthānaṃ prasāritaṃ nāma sukhasuptasya kārayet || 4 ||
adhomukhe sthitaṃ caiva vivartitamiti smṛtam ||
śastrakṣate mṛte kṣiptaṃ mattonmatteṣu tadbhavet || 5 ||
aṃsopari śiraḥ kṛtvā kuryurakṣobhameva ca ||
udvāhitaṃ tu vijñeyaṃ līlāyāḥ śaṃsane prabho ||6 ||
tasyā yadetatprasṛtaṃ tu jaṃghe strastau karau taṃ natamuddiśanti |
ālasyakhedaśramamokṣaṇeṣu kāryastu tasyābhinayaprayogaḥ || 7 ||
iti śrīviṣṇudharmottare tṛtīyakhaṇḍe mā0 vajrasaṃvāde śayyāsanasthānavarṇano nāmaikaviṃśatitamo'dhyāyaḥ || 21 ||
<3.22>
|| mārkaṇḍeya uvāca || ||
svasthamandālasakrāntaṃ prasthālasamathāpi ca ||
viṣkambhitaṃ tatkaṭukaṃ muktajānu tathā samam || 1 ||
jānūgataṃ vimuktaṃ ca sthānakānyupaveśane ||
viṣkumbhitāñcitau pādau vakṣaḥ kiñcitsamunnatam || 2 ||
hastau kaṭyūruvinyastau svasthau svasthopaveśane ||
ekaḥ prasāritaḥ pādastvanyaścaivāsanāśritaḥ || 3 ||
pārśvaṃ nataṃ tathā kāryaṃ sthānaṃ mandālasaṃ tu tat ||
cintāyāṃ tu tathautsukye nirvede virahe tathā || 4 ||
viṣādeṣu sadā deyaṃ sthānametatprayoktṛbhiḥ ||
cibukāpāśritau hastau bāhuśīrṣācitaṃ śiraḥ || 5 ||
sarvābhinayanaṃ caiva vijñeyaṃ klāntanāyakam ||
balinā nigrahītasya ripuṇā khaṇḍitasya ca || 6 ||
śokamlānasya ca tathā sthānametadvinirdiśet ||
grahau hastau vimuktaṃ ca śarīramalasaṃ tathā || 7 ||
khedālasaṃ tathā cakṣuryatra svasthālasaṃ tu tat ||
śrame glānau made caiva mūrcchāyā vyathite tathā||8||
mohe prāṇabhaye caiva sthānametatprayojayet ||
viṣkumbhitāñcitau pādau kuru viṣkumbhitau bhujau || 9 ||
nimīlitaṃ tathā cakṣuḥ sthāne viṣkumbhitā sati ||
svavakṣogatayā dṛṣṭyā yoge dhyāne vidhīyate || 3.22.10 ||
svabhāvasaṃsthayā caiva sabhyānāmupaveśane ||
samau pādau samādhāya samaṃ yadupadiśyate || 11 ||
aspaṣṭabhūtalaṃ caiva jñeyaṃ mukuṭakāsanam ||
pitrye samādhau japye ca homādikaraṇeṣu ca || 12 ||
etatsthānaṃ vidhātavyaṃ yathāgamanakarmaṇi ||
ekaṃ jānu yadāsye ca mahīpṛṣṭhe vidhīyate || 13 ||
jñātavyaṃ bhūmipaśreṣṭha muktajānukamāsanam ||
etatkṛtavyalīkānāṃ priyāṇāṃ samprasādane || 14 ||
majjane kuṭilānāṃ ca tathā bhūmyanulepane ||
mahīgatābhyāṃ jānubhyāṃ sthānaṃ jānugataṃ bhaveta || 15 ||
rājñāṃ vijñāpane kāryaṃ surāṇāṃ caiva pūjane ||
bhūmau yadūrdhvaṃ patanaṃ tadvimuktamiti smṛtam || 16 ||
prahārataḥ prayoktavyamāveśe krandane tathā ||
bhadrāsanaṃ tu devānāṃ rājñāṃ siṃhāsanaṃ bhaveta || 17 ||
rūpyāsanaṃ tu dātavyaṃ sāṃvatsarapurodhasoḥ ||
vetrāsanamamātyānāṃ mantriṇāṃ tu tathā bhaveta || 18 ||
mandāsanaṃ tu dātavyaṃ senānīyuvarājayoḥ ||
munīnāṃ ca dvijānāṃ ca tathā sabrahmacāriṇām || 19 ||
bṛsī deyā mahārāja ye cānye niyame sthitāḥ ||
sthānīyāḥ puruṣā ye ca mukhyāḥ paurāśca ye tathā || 3.22.20 ||
vaṇijaśca tathā mukhyāsteṣāṃ bhavati pīṭhikā ||
veśastrīṇāṃ kumārāṇāṃ tathā bhṛtyajanasya ca || 21 ||
masūrakaṃ tu dātavyaṃ śeṣe bhūmyāsanaṃ bhavet ||
siṃhāsanaṃ vinā rājangṛhe svacchandamānasam || 22 ||
naikāsanaṃ syādguruṇā tu rājanrathe gaje vāpi śilātale ca ||
naikāsane syādguruṇā tu doṣo'tha vātidīrghe phalake ca nityam || 23 || ||
iti śrīviṣṇudharmottare tṛtīyakhaṇḍe mārkaṇḍeyavajrasaṃvāde āsanavarṇano nāma dvāviṃśatitamo'dhyāyaḥ || 22 ||
<3.23>
|| mārkaṇḍeya uvāca || ||
vaiṣṇavaṃ samapādaṃ ca vaiśākhaṃ maṇḍalaṃ tathā ||
pratyālīḍhamathālīḍhaṃ ṣaṭ pusāṃ sthānakāni tu || 1 ||
svabhāvasaṃśritaḥ pādastryaṃśaḥ pakṣagato'paraḥ ||
kiñcidañcitajaṃghaśca vaiṣṇavaṃ sthānamucyate || 2 ||
sthāne nānena kartavye saṃlāpastu svabhāvataḥ ||
samapādaṃ tu vijñeyaṃ samaistālāntaraiḥ padaiḥ || 3 ||
svasvabhāsauṣṭhavopetaṃ rudrabrahmādidaivatam ||
anena kāryaṃ sthānena vipramaṇḍalakāraṇam || 4 ||
balānāṃ pakṣiṇāṃ caiva varakautukameva ca ||
svasthānaṃ syandanasthānāṃ vimānasthāyināmapi || 5 ||
liṅgasthānāṃ vratasthānāṃ sthānametatprayojayet ||
dvau tālāvardhatālaṃ ca pādayoraṃtaraṃ bhavet || 6 ||
añcitaṃ yatra jaṃghe tu padau pakṣasthito tathā ||
vaiśākhaṃ nāma tatsthānaṃ skandaścaivātra devatā || 7 ||
sthānenānena kartavyamaśvānāṃ vāhanaṃ budhaiḥ ||
vyāyāmo nirgamaścaiva praveśo dhanuṣastathā || 8 |||
ataḥ paraṃ maṇḍalaṃ syāttrīṃstālāntarameva ca ||
kaṭī jānusamā yatra pādau pakṣasthitau yathā || 9||
dhanurvajrapraharaṇaṃ maṇḍalena prayojayet ||
vāhanaṃ kuñjarāṇāṃ tu nṛtte codvahanakriyā || 3.23.1 0||
ādau ca dakṣiṇaṃ pādaṃ pañcatālaṃ prasārayet ||
ālīḍhaṃ nāma tatsthānaṃ rudrakālyatra daivatam ||11||
anena kāryā sthānena sarvatroddharaṇakriyā ||
raudrapādapracārastu saṃgrāmasya nirūpaṇam || 12 ||
siṃhādyabhinayaṃ caiva śaktasandhānameva ca ||
khaḍgayuddhe niyuddhe ca maṇḍalabhramaṇe tathā || 13 ||
uttarottarasaṃjalpe roṣāmarṣasamudbhave ||
mallānāṃ caiva saṃsphoṭe śatrūṇāṃ ca nirūpaṇe || 14 ||
tathābhidravaṇaṃ caiva śāstrāṇāṃ ca vimokṣaṇam ||
añcitaṃ dakṣiṇaṃ kṛtvā vāmaṃ pādaṃ prasārya ca || 15 ||
ālīḍhaṃ parivartante pratyālīḍhamiti smṛtam |||
pratyālīḍhaṃ tu vijñeyaṃ sthānaṃ ca navadaivatam || 16 ||
ālīḍhenoditaṃ śastaṃ pratyālīḍhaṃ na mocayet ||
ataḥ paraṃ pravakṣyāmi strīṇāṃ sthānatrayaṃ nṛpa || 17 ||
āyataṃ vāvahitthaṃ ca hayakrāntamathāpi ca ||
ekaścānuyuto yatra pādau viracitaḥ samaḥ || 18 ||
tālamātrāntaranyastaḥ tryakṣaḥ pakṣagato'paraḥ ||
prasannamānanamuraḥ samaṃ yatra samunnatam || 19 ||
latānitambagau hastau sthānaṃ jñeyaṃ tadāyatam ||
raṅgāvataraṇe hyetattathā puṣpāñjalau bhavet || 3.23.20 ||
pādo viracitastrya (śra)srastadanyopasṛtaḥ samaḥ || 
pādāstalāntaranyastāstrikamīṣatsamunnatam || 21 ||
avahitthaṃ samākhyātaṃ sthānametannarottama ||
vilāsalīlālāvaṇyaśṛṅgārātmanirūpaṇe || 22 ||
sthānametatprayoktavyaṃ varamārgāvalokane ||
samasthito yatra pādastvanyaḥ pādastalācitaḥ || 23 ||
tālairaniyataireṣa sthānako hi vidhīyate ||
sūcīviddhamaviddhaṃ vā tadaśvakrāntamiṣyate || 24 ||
śākhāvalambastabakoccaye ca vighūrṇanetrāsanasampralāpe ||
svabhāvasaṃlāpavidhau ca kāryaṃ sthānaṃ tvidaṃ yādavavaṃśamukhya || 25 ||
iti śrīviṣṇudharmottare tṛtīyakhaṇḍe mā0 va0 saṃ0 utthitādisthānakavaṇarno nāma trayoviṃśatitamo'dhyāyaḥ || 23 ||
<3.24>
mārkaṇḍeya uvāca ||
śirasaḥ prathamaṃ karma gadato me nibodha tat ||
svabhāvādṛjunorvāmakṣepeṇākasmikaṃ sakṛt || 1 ||
saṃjñopadeśapṛcchāsu svabhāvābhāṣaṇe bhavet ||
bahuśaḥ kathitaṃ yacca tadā kampitamiṣyate || 2 ||
(roṣatarjanatarkeṣu pratijñāsu ca dṛśyate ||
śiraso recanaṃ yattu śanaistadyutamiṣyate || 3 ||)
viṣāde vismaye caiva pratiṣedhe tadiṣyate ||
vidhutaṃ nāmataḥ proktaṃ tathā pārśvavikampanaiḥ || 4 ||
śītagraste jvarārte ca matte ca tvarite bhavet ||
maṇḍalārambhaṇe caiva bhavettu parigarhitam || 5||
sādhane vismaye harṣe līlāyāṃ ca tadiṣyate ||
sakṛdudvāhitaṃ cordhvaṃ tadudvāhitamiṣyate ||6||
ucchrāye darśane garve tattathordhvanirīkṣaṇe |||
ākṣiptaṃ sakṛdeva syādavavratamadhogatam || 7 ||
sandehālāpasaṃjñāsu tattathā vāhanādiṣu ||
kiñcitpārśvanatagrīvaṃ śiro vijñeyamañcitam || 8 ||
mūrcchite vyathite caiva sañcite duḥkhite bhavet ||
(utkṣiptabāhuśikharaṃ tathācitaśirodharam || 9 ||
nikuñcitaṃ ca tadgarve vilāse ca prayojayet ||
parāvṛttaparāvṛttaṃ kāryaṃ pṛṣṭhanirīkṣaṇe || 3.24.10 ||)
utkṣiptaṃ cāpi vijñeyamunmukhāvasthitaṃ śiraḥ ||
āśu divyārthayoge ca kāryaṃ tacca prayoktṛbhiḥ || 11 ||
adhomukhasthitaṃ cāpi budhāḥ prāhuradhogatam ||
lajjāyāṃ ca pramāṇe ca duḥkhe tacca prayojayet || 12 ||
sarvato lolanāccaiva parilolitamucyate ||
vyādhite mūrcchite matte bhavettu parilolitam || 13 ||
etattu śirasaḥ karma kathitaṃ te narādhipa ||
añcitaṃ recitaṃ muktaṃ vivṛtaṃ caturaṃ tathā || 14 ||
prasāritaṃ tathā stabdhaṃ grīvākarmaṇi nirdiśet ||
añcitaṃ kiñcidekāṃśaṃ lolanādrecitaṃ tu tat || 15 ||
recitaṃ bhramaṇānmuktaṃ vivṛtaṃ calitaṃ ca tat ||
madhuraṃ caturaṃ proktamāyataṃ syātprasāritam || 16 ||
stabdhaṃ stabdhaṃ tathā proktaṃ śiraḥkarmānugāmi ca ||
añcitaṃ syādvilāsādau recitaṃ maṇḍalekṣaṇe || 17 ||
muktaṃ mūrcchādiyogeṣu vivṛttaṃ lalitādiṣu ||
saumyabhāve tu caturaṃ dṛṣṭau kāryaṃ prasāritam || 18 ||
stabdhaṃ śīte'tha vā vyādhāvavikāri svabhāvataḥ ||
vibuddhaṃ vinivṛttaṃ ca nirbhugnaṃ bhugnameva ca || 19 ||
vivṛttaṃ ca tathaivarjukarmaṇyasya vinirdiśet ||
vyāvṛttaṃ syādvibuddhākhyaṃ nivṛttaṃ pihitaṃ bhavet ||3.24.20||
avāṅmukhamavābhugnaṃ nibhugnaṃ cordhvagaṃ tu tat ||
bhugnaṃ syādānataṃ kiñcinnivṛttaṃ vivṛtaṃ bhavet|| ||21||
ṛjusvabhāvasaṃsthaṃ ca tayoḥ karmāṇi cakṣate ||
viruddhavāraṇe kāryaṃ maivamityevamādiṣu||22||
vinivṛttamasūyāyāmīrṣyākopakṛteṣu ca ||
vibhugnaṃ cāpi vijñeyaṃ gambhīrālokanādiṣu || 23 ||
bhugnaṃ tu lajjite yojyaṃ yatīnāṃ tu svabhāvataḥ ||
nivṛttaṃ vāpi kartavyaṃ hāsyaśokabhayādiṣu || 24 ||
ṛjusvabhāvasaṃsthāne kartavyaṃ yadunandana ||
ābhugnamatha nirbhugnaṃ tathā caiva prakaṃpitam || 25 ||
udvāhitaṃ samaṃ caiva pañcarodhaḥ prakīrtitam ||
dhāryate yacca nirbhugnaṃ tadā bhugnaṃ prakīrtitam || 26 ||
śastrakṣate vighāte ca hṛdroge ca tadiṣyate ||
stabdhaṃ ca nimnapṛṣṭhaṃ ca nirbhugnaṃ nāma tatsmṛtam || 27 ||
stambhe savismaye māne viṣāde ca tatheṣyate ||
urasastūrdhvavikṣepaḥ prakampitamihocyate || 28 ||
hasite rucite kāryaṃ śrame vyādhibhaye tathā ||
udvāhitaṃ yadūrdhvaṃ tu tadudvāhitamucyate || 29 ||
ūrdhvaṃ nigamite kāryaṃ jṛmbhiteṣu prayogataḥ ||
caturasraṃ samaṃ nāma vijñeyaṃ sauṣṭhaveṣu tat || 3.24.30 ||
samunnataṃ nataṃ caiva prasāritavivartite ||
tathāpasṛtamevaṃ hi pārśvayoḥ karma pañcadhā || 31 ||
kaṭipārśvabhujāṃsāntairunnataiḥ syātsasunnatam ||
kaṭī yatra sunirbhugnā pārśvaṃ taddvinataṃ bhavet || 32 ||
prasāraṇādubhayataḥ pārśvayoḥ syātprasāritam ||
parivartanāttrikasyāpi vivartitamiheṣyate || 33 ||
asya caivopagamanādbhavedapasṛtaṃ punaḥ ||
nataṃ tvapasṛte kāryaṃ connataṃ tūpasarpite || 34 ||
prasārite praharṣādau trāse cāpasṛte punaḥ ||
vivartitaṃ vivarte ca kāraṇaṃ pārśvamucyate || 35 ||
kṣāmaṃ nimnaṃ ca pūrṇaṃ ca saṃproktamudaraṃ tridhā ||
kṣāmaṃ tu hasite kāryaṃ tathā caiva vijṛmbhite || 36 ||
vyādhite tapasi śrānte kṣudhārte nimnameva ca ||
pūrṇaṃ niḥśvasite caiva hyārogye lalitaṃ tathā || 37 ||
prakampitā ca cchinnā ca nivṛttā recitā tathā ||
udvāhitā caiva kaṭirnṛtte pañcavidhā smṛtā || 38 ||
tiryaggatā gatā kṣipraṃ vijñeyā vaprakampitā ||
madhyasthabalanāccaiva cchinnā saṃparikīrtitā || 39 ||
parāṅmukhasyābhimukhī nivṛttā parikīrtitā ||
sarvatobhramaṇāccaiva recitetyabhidhīyate || 3.24.40||.
niścitodvāhanāccaiva tathaivodvāhitā matā ||
nīca vāmanakubjānāṃ gatau kāryā prakampitā || 41 ||
vyāyāme tvatha saṃprāpte vyāvṛttaprekṣiteṣu ca ||
chinnā kāryā kaṭistajjñairnivṛttā vartiteṣu ca || 42 ||
recitā recite kāryā anyā līlāgate tridhā ||
kampanaṃ valanaṃ caiva stambhanodvartane tathā ||43 ||
vivartanaṃ ca pañcaiva ūrvoḥ karmāṇi nirdiśet ||
namanonnamane pārantyormahīpṛṣṭhedbhute yadā || 44 ||
sthitā pādatalāgreṇa kampitaṃ tattadiṣyate ||
gacchedabhyantaraṃ jānu śanairyadvalitaṃ tu tat || 45 ||
stambhanaṃ cāpi vijñeyamapavṛttakriyākulam ||
valitāviddhabhāvaṃ syādudvartanamathāpi ca || 46 ||
pārṣṇirabhyantaraṃ gacchedyatra tatra nivartitam ||
gatiṣvadhamapātrāṇāṃ kampitaṃ saṃprayojayet || 47 ||
valanaṃ caiva kartavyaṃ strīṇāṃ svairaparikrame ||
bhīmordhasaviṣādeṣu stambhanaṃ saṃprayojayet || 48 ||
vyāyāme tāṇḍave caiva kāryamudvartanaṃ tathā ||
vivartanaṃ ca kartavyaṃ saṃkramādiparikrame || 49 ||
āvartitaṃ nataṃ kṣiptaṃ codvāhitamathāpi ca ||
parivṛttaṃ tathā pañca jaṅghākarmāṇi nirdiśet || 3.24.50 ||
āvartitaṃ tu vyatyāsādvāmadakṣiṇajaṅghayoḥ ||
jānvorākuñjanāccaiva nataṃ jñeyaṃ prayoktṛbhiḥ||51||
vikṣepāccaiva jaṃghāyāḥ kṣiptamityabhidhīyate ||
udvāhitaṃ tu vijñeyamūrūdvāhanakarmaṇā || 52 ||
pratīpanayanaṃ yatra parivṛttaṃ tadiṣyate ||
āvartanaṃ prayoktavyaṃ vidūṣakaparikrame || 53 ||
nataṃ cāpi hi kartavyaṃ sthānāsanagatādiṣu ||
kṣiptaṃ vyāyāmayogeṣu tāṇḍaveṣu prayojayet || 54 ||
tathā codvāhitaṃ kāryamāviddhagamanādiṣu ||
tāṇḍavādau prayoktavyaṃ parivṛttaṃ prayoktṛbhiḥ || 55 ||
udghāṭitasamaścaiva........saṃcaraḥ ||
añcitaḥ kuñcitaścaiva pādaḥ pañcavidhaḥ smṛtaḥ || 56 ||
sthitvā pādatalāgreṇa pārṣṇirbhūmau nipātyate ||
yasya pādasya karaṇātsa taddhaṭita ucyate|| || 57 ||
kāryo'nuraktakaraṇe (caiva) pracāre lalite tathā ||
svabhāvaracito bhūmau samasthānaḥ samaḥ smṛtaḥ || 58 ||
eṣa kāryaḥ sadā pādaḥ svabhāvābhinayāśrayaḥ ||
utkṣiptā tu bhavetpārṣṇiḥ pāde'gratalasaṃcare || 59 ||
nikṣiptarecite bhrānte pāde yastāḍito bhavet ||
lalitaścāyameva syātpādo recitasaṃjñitaḥ || 3.24.60 ||
eṣa pradānasaṃcāre lalitodvahitaṃ bhavet ||
ukṣiptāgratalaḥ pādaḥ kathitastu tathāñcitaḥ || 61 ||
nānābhramaṇakāleṣu kāryaḥ pādo'yamañcitaḥ ||
pādaḥ kuñcitamadhyo'yaṃ bhavetkuñcitasaṃjñitaḥ || 62 ||
atikrāntābhigamane pādametatprayojayet ||
ekapādapracāro yaḥ sa cārītyeva saṃjñitaḥ || 63 ||
dvipādakramaṇaṃ yatra karaṇaṃ nāma tadbhavet ||
karaṇānāṃ samāyogaḥ khaṇḍa ityabhidhīyate || 64 ||
dvābhyāṃ tribhirvā yadi vā caturbhiryuktaṃ bhavenmaṇḍalameva nāmnā ||
ityaṅgakarmābhihitaṃ bravīmi nibodha rājaṃstvamupāṅgakarma || 65 || ||
iti śrīviṣṇudharmottare tṛtīyakhaṇḍe mārkaṇḍeyavajrasaṃvāde aṅgavarṇanaṃ nāma caturviṃśatitamo'dhyāyaḥ || 24 ||
<3.25>
|| mārkaṇḍeya uvāca ||
kṣāntā bhayānakā hāsyā karuṇā hyadbhutā tathā ||
raudrā vīrā ca bībhatsā vijñeyā rasadṛṣṭayaḥ || 1||
snigdhā hṛṣṭā ca jihmā ca kruddhā bhītā ca lajjitā ||
tṛptātha vismitā saumyā sthāyibhāveṣu dṛṣṭayaḥ || 2 ||
malinā kekarā śrāntā cābhitaptātha viplutā || ||| 3 ||
viṣaṇṇā śaṅkitā caiva trastā śokā nimīlitā ||
vibhrāntā kuñcitā śūnyā muktalā ca vitarkitā ||
madirā lalitā mlānā ityevaṃ dṛṣṭisaṃgrahaḥ || 4 ||
ṣaḍtriṃśaddṛṣṭayo hyetā nāmato'bhihitā mayā ||
sabhrūkṣepakaṭākṣā tu kāntā jñeyā samañjasī || 5 ||
udavṛttapuṭapakṣmāgrā calattārā bhayānakā ||
kiñcidākuñcitapuṭā hāsyavibhrāntatārakā || 6 ||
patitordhvapuṭā sāsrā karuṇā naṣṭatārakā ||
kiñcitkuñcitapakṣmāgrā kiñcidudvṛttatārakā || 7 ||
samā vikasitā caiva sādbhutā dṛṣṭiriṣyate ||
udbhūtā kathitā raudrā bhrukuṭī kuṭilā tathā || 8 ||
utphullamadhyā vīrā tu kathitā samatārakā ||
bībhatsā kathitā dṛṣṭirapāṅgākṣiptatārakā ||9||
dhyānādanimiṣā śāntā hyadhomārgavicāriṇī ||
vyākośamadhyā madhurā snigdhā prahasitā matā || 3.25.10 ||
utphullamadhyā pracalā hṛṣṭā paryantavīkṣiṇī ||
jihmādhogatatārā ca gūḍhā magnā nirīkṣiṇī || 11 ||
bhrukuṭīkuṭilā kruddhā tathā niṣṭabdhatārakā ||
ākuṃcitā lajjitā syāttathā niścalatārakā || 12 ||
sattvamadbhiratīveṣṭā dṛptā vikasitā bhaveta ||
samā vikasitāpāṅgā vismitā sā nigadyate || 13 ||
samā saumyā vinirdiṣṭā tathā ca samatārakā ||
ākuñcitapuṭā bhītā rūkṣā vyāvṛttatārakā || 14 ||
puṭau prasphuritau yasyā malinā sā nigadyate ||
ākuñcitapuṭā yā tu dṛṣṭirākekarā bhavet || 15 ||
kiñcidunnāmitapuṭā śāntā sā tu vidhīyate ||
abhitaptā tathā mandā mandatārāpuṭā tu yā || 16 ||
viplutāviplutā proktā srastā visphuritā ca yā ||
kiñcinniṣṭabdhatārā tu viṣaṇṇā dṛṣṭirucyate || 17 ||
anavasthitatārā ca śaṅkitā parikīrtitā ||
rūkṣā lalita tārā ca pratākhyā sabhayā bhavet || 18 ||
utphullā tu viśokākhyā tathā ca nimiṣekṣaṇā ||
anavasthitanetrā ca vibhrāntā bhrāntatārakā || 19 ||
kuṃcitā kuṃcitā dṛṣṭiḥ kiñcidañcitatārakā ||
sthitatārāpuṭā śūnyā saṃkṣubdhā samadarśanā || 3.25.20 ||
praspandamānapakṣyāgrā mukulā muktalā bhavet ||
udvṛttapuṭapakṣmā tu vitarkā stabdhatārakā || 21 ||
utphullā madirā nāma tathā valitatārakā ||
phullitāntā salāvaṇyā sasmitā lalitā matā || 22 ||
pralūnapakṣmā sā sāgrā glānākhyā mukulīkṛtā ||
kāntā dṛṣṭiḥ kaṭākṣe tu ghātane tu bhayānakā || 23 ||
hāsyā hāsye ca vijñeyā karuṇā karuṇe rase ||
svadbhute cādbhutā kāryā raudrī raudre vidhīyate || 24 ||
vīrā vīre ca kartavyā bībhatsā ca jugupsite ||
śāntā śānte tu nirdiṣṭā snigdhā snigdhāvalokane || 25 ||
hṛṣṭā hṛṣṭe tu kartavyā jihmā kāryābhyasūyite ||
kruddhā kruddhe tu nirdiṣṭā bhītā bhīte tathā bhavet|| || 26 ||
lajjitā lajjitākhyā syāddṛptotsāhagateṣu ca ||
vismitā vismaye dṛṣṭiḥ saumyā saumyāvalokane || 27 ||
malinā virahe kāryā sambhoge kekarā bhavet ||
śrāntā śrāntasya kartavyā nirvede tvabhitaptakā || 28 ||
viplutākhyā tu dainye syādviṣāde tu viṣaṇṇikā ||
śaṃkite śaṃkitā caiva traste trastā vidhīyate || 29 ||
jñāne viśokā ca tathā sparśe kāryā nimīlitā ||
vibhrāntā vibhrame kāryā kuṃcitā sūkṣmadarśane| || 3.25.30 ||
cintānvitasya śūnyā syānmuktalā ghrāṇakarmaṇi ||
vitarke tu vitarkā syānmade tu madirā bhavet || 31 ||
strīṇāṃ helāsu lalitā glāne glānā vidhīyate ||
utsedhaśca nimeṣaśca prasāritavivartite || 32 ||
ākuñcitaṃ samaṃ caiva sphuritaṃ vihataṃ tataḥ ||
tāḍitaṃ puṭayoḥ karma navadhā parikīrtitam || 33 ||
samāgamaḥ puṭānāṃ yaḥ sa nimeṣa iti smṛtaḥ ||
viśleṣaścaiva yastābhyāṃ sa unmeṣaḥ prakīrtitaḥ || 34 ||
prasāritau puṭau yatra bhavettaddhi vivartitam ||
ākuñcitābhyāṃ tābhyāṃ tu jñeyamākuñcitaṃ budhaiḥ || 35 ||
svabhāvapuṭasaṃsthānaṃ samaṃ taddhi prakīrtitam ||
puṭau prasphuritau yatra sphuritaṃ tadihocyate || 36 ||
puṭāvabhihitau yatra pihitaṃ nāma tadbhavet ||
srastamūlau puṭau yatra taddhi tāḍitamucyate || 37 ||
gandhasparśe tathāniṣṭe jñeyamākuñcitaṃ budhaiḥ ||
vismayārthe tathā vīre tacca kāryaṃ prasādhitam || 38 ||
śṛṅgāre tu samaṃ kāryamabhighāte'vatāḍitam ||
strīṇāṃ praṇayaroṣeṣu sphuritaṃ tadiheṣyate ||39 ||
śīte vātoṣṇavarṣeṣu vihitaṃ kāryameva tu ||
krodhe vivartitaṃ kāryaṃ nimeṣonmeṣayoḥ samam || || 3.25.40 ||
valanaṃ bhramaṇaṃ caiva calanaṃ saṃpraṇāśanam ||
vivartitaṃ pāṭanaṃ tu vijñeyaṃ vismitaṃ tathā || 41 ||
udvartaḥ prākṛtaṃ caiva tārakākarma ceṣyate ||
maṇḍalāvartanaṃ madhye puṭayorbhramaṇaṃ smṛtam || 42 ||
valanaṃ gamanaṃ tiryagvalanāccalitaṃ bhavet ||
ābhyantarata ākarṣaḥ praveśātsaṃpraveśanam || 43 ||
tathā cāpāṅgasaṃcārāduddharṣitamudāhṛtam ||
pātanaṃ srastabhāvaśca niṣkāmānniṣkramaṃ tathā || 44 ||
udvartanātparāvṛttiḥ prākṛtaṃ tu svabhāvataḥ ||
hāsye tu valanaṃ kāryaṃ made bhramaṇameva ca || 45 ||
vīre rase syāccalanaṃ bībhatse saṃpraveśanam ||
vivartitaṃ tu śṛṅgāre pātanaṃ karuṇe rase || 46 ||
bhayānake niṣkramaṇaṃ vīre codvartanaṃ tathā ||
prākṛtaṃ śeṣabhāve tu yathāyogaṃ prayojayet||47||
samaṃ sācīkṛtaṃ ca syādanuvṛttamataḥ param ||
ālokitaṃ cāllokitamavalokitameva ca ||48||
samatāraṃ ca saumyaṃ ca dṛṣṭikarma samaṃ bhaveta ||
pakṣmāntargatatāraṃ ca sācīkṛtamihocyate ||49||
rūpanirvartanāyuktamanuvṛttamiti smṛtam ||
sahasā darśanaṃ yatsyāttadālokitamiṣyate||3.25.50 ||
ūrdhvamullokitaṃ jñeyaṃ sarvato lokitaṃ tathā ||
vilokitaṃ tu pārśvābhyāṃ pṛṣṭhataścāvalokitam ||51||
dṛṣṭīnāmanugaṃ caiva bhruvo karma prayojayet ||
yathā netraṃ prasarpeddhi nānābhāvarasāśrayam ||52||
netrasyānukrameṇaiva bhruvostu calanaṃ bhavet ||
utkṣepaḥ patanaṃ caiva bhrukuṭīcaturaṃ tathā || 53 ||
nikuñcitaṃ kuñcitaṃ ca tathā svābhāvikaṃ bhavet ||
bhruvorudgamamutkṣepaḥ pātanaṃ patanaṃ bhavet || 54 ||
dvayormūlordhavikṣepādbhrukuṭī parikīrtitā ||
caturaḥ kiñciducchvāsānmadhurāyatayā bhruvā || 55 ||
ekasyā ubhayorvāpi syādudbhagnaṃ trikuñcitam ||
ekasyā lalitodyogādrecitaṃ nāma kīrtitam || 56 ||
yathāsthite svabhāvasthe bhruvoḥ karma nibodha me ||
samutkṣepastu līlāyāḥ kāruṇyātpātanaṃ bhavet || 57 ||
krodhe ca bhrukuṭī jñeyā śṛṅgāracaturaṃ tathā ||
kṣāmaṃ kṛśaṃ tu vijñeyaṃ phullaṃ vikasitaṃ bhavet || 58 ||
sampūrṇaṃ pūrṇamityuktaṃ kampitaṃ paridhūnitam ||
śṛṅgabhinnaṃ kuñcitakaṃ saromāñcaṃ tathāvidham || 59 ||
prākkṛtaṃ tu svabhāvasthaṃ mandaṃ proktaṃ manīṣibhiḥ ||
kṣāmaṃ duḥkhe tu kartavyaṃ phullaṃ harṣe prayojayet || 3.25.60 ||
pūrṇaṃ tuṣṭe tu kartavyaṃ tathā roṣe ca kampitam ||
hāsye tvākuñcitaṃ sparśe saromāñcaṃ vidhīyate || 61 ||
prākṛtaṃ tu svabhāvasthaṃ tvarthayogena yojayet ||
natā mandā vikṛṣṭā ca socchvāsā kūṇitānatā || 62 ||
svābhāvike ca kartavyā prayoge nāsikā budhaiḥ ||
natānatā tu vijñeyā mandā natapuṭā bhavet || 63 ||
vikṛṣṭā kuñcitā cordhvaṃ socchvāsā vāyupūritā ||
saṃkūṇanātkūṇitā syātsvabhāvasthā svanāsikā || 64 ||
natā tu rucite kāryā mandā śoke vidhīyate ||
tīvragandhe vikṛṣṭā tu socchvāsā tu tathā priye || 65 ||
vikūṇitā jugupsāyāṃ śeṣā svābhāvikī matā ||
kūṇanaṃ khaṇḍanaṃ chinnaṃ mukitaṃ samatā tathā | 66 ||
jihvādharoṣṭhamadaṃ ca dantakarmaṇi kārayeta ||
kuṭṭanaṃ bhaktavacchedaṃ khaṇḍanaṃ tu vighaṭṭanam || 67 ||
chinnaṃ nikṛtameva syācchukktiṃ viddhameva ca ||
vyādhau tu kuṭṭanaṃ kāryamutsāhe khaṇḍanaṃ bhavet || 68 ||
chinnaṃ krodhe paraṃ hāsye samaṃ śeṣeṣu kārayet ||
vivartanaṃ tathā kampo vimarśo'tha vigūhanam || 69 ||
saṃdṛṣṭaśca samudraśca karmāṇyadharajāni ca ||
sṛkkā suvakrakaraṇaṃ vivartitamiti smṛtam || 3.25.70 ||
vepanaṃ cāpi vijñeyamadharasya tu kampane ||
yo niṣkramo'dharasyātha sa visarga iti smṛtaḥ || 71 ||
antaḥpraveśanaṃ caiva vinigūhitamucyate ||
dantoṣṭhapīḍanāccaiva saṃdaṣṭakamiti smṛtam || 72 ||
piṇḍitābhyudgataścaiva samudgaka iti smṛtaḥ ||
vivartitamasūyāyāṃ roṣe kampitamiṣyate ||
bhayānake visargaśca karṣaṇe'tha nigūhanam || 73 ||
tatonmādavikāreṣu sandaṣṭaṃ vīraraudrayoḥ ||
samudgamanakampāyāṃ śeṣe kāryaṃ svabhāvajam || 74 ||
upāṅgakarmābhihitaṃ tathaitaṃ mayā samāsena narendracandra ||
kāryaḥ prayatno'tra sadā budhena yasmādupāṅgāśrayameva nṛtyam || 75 ||
iti śrīviṣṇudharmottare tṛtīyakhaṇḍe mārkaṇḍeyavajrasaṃvāde upāṅgikavarṇano nāma pañcaviṃśatitamo'dhyāyaḥ || 25 ||
<3.26>
|| mārkaṇḍeya uvāca || ||
patākastripatākaśca tathā vaikartaremukhaḥ ||
ardhacandro hyaṭālaśca gurutuṇḍastathāparaḥ || 1 ||
muṣṭiśca śikhirākhyaśca kapitthaḥ khaṭakāmukhaḥ ||
sūcyardhaḥ padmakośaśca mṛgaśīrṣo mṛgasya ca || 2 ||
lāṅgūlaḥ kālapadmaśca caturo bhramarastathā ||
haṃsāsyo haṃsapakṣaśca saṃdaṃśo mukulastathā || 3 ||
asaṃyutāḥ karā hyete dvāviṃśatirudāhṛtāḥ ||
atha saṃyuktato hastāngadatastānnibodha me || 4 ||
aṃjaliśca kapotaśca karkaṭaḥ svastikastathā ||
khaṭako vardhamānaśca utsaṅgo niṣidhastathā || 5 ||
dolaḥ puṣpapuṭaścaiva tathā makara eva ca ||
gajadanto'vahitthaśca vardhamānastathaiva ca || 6 ||
ete vai saṃyutā hastā mayā proktāstrayodaśa ||
nṛttahastāryupaścaiva bhūyo nāmni nibodhata || 7 ||
caturasrastathā vṛttastathā laghumukhau smṛtau ||
tathā caiva tu vijñeyau hyarālakhaṭakāmukhau || 8 ||
āviddhavakrasaṃvyāsvau recitāvarddharecitau ||
avahitthaḥ pallavito nitambaḥ keśavardhanau || 9 ||
latākhyau karihastau ca pakṣodyotā'rthavardhitau ||
jñeyau garuḍapakṣau ca daṇḍapakṣau tathaiva ca |||3.26.10|||
ūrdhvamaṇḍalajau caiva pārśvamaṃḍalājāvapi ||
pārśvārdhamaṇḍalau caiva uromaṇḍalakau tathā||11||
iṣṭasvastikakāvanyau tathā vai padmakauśikau ||
allipallavasaṃjñau ca tathā colvanasaṃjñitau || 12 ||
lalitau valitau caiva jñeyā nṛtyakarāḥ sadā ||
ataḥ paraṃ pravakṣyāmi karāṇāṃ karmalakṣaṇam || 13 ||
prasāritāgrā sahitā yasyāṅgulyo bhavanti hi ||
kuñcitaśca tathāṅguṣṭhaḥ sa patāka iti smṛtaḥ || 14 ||
eṣa prahāragrahaṇe rodhane syātpratāpane ||
calitāṅgulirapyeṣa vāyuvṛṣṭinirūpaṇe || 15 ||
ūrdhvagaśca tathotsāhe chatrādiṣvapyadhomukhaḥ ||
puṣkaraprahitastāle samaḥ pracalitāṅguliḥ || 16 ||
pakṣmotkṣepe recitaka utkṣiptaścaiva dhāraṇe ||
asyaikānāmikā vakrā tripatākā tathā bhavet || 17 ||
āvāhane prayuñjīta mukuṭābhinaye'pyuta |||
acalāṅgulirapyeṣa laghupakṣinirūpaṇe 118 ||
tayānāsikayā kāryaṃ tathaivāśrupramārjanam ||
maṅgalasparśanaṃ kāryaṃ śirasaśca niveśanam || 19 ||
śrotrasaṃvaraṇaṃ caiva kāryaṃ bhūmimukhena tu ||
bhujaṅgānāmābhinayaṃ bhramarāṇāṃ tathaiva ca || 3.26.20 ||
tripatākā yadā haste bhavetpṛṣṭhāvalokinī ||
tarjanī tu tadā jñeyaḥ kartarīmukhasaṃjñakaḥ||21||
kāryo'yaṃ parivāreṣu bhinnaśca pataneṣu ca||
anāmikā tu sāṅguṣṭhā ardhacandre kare bhavet||22||
bālacandrābhinayane bālasyāttaravastathā||
mekhalāṃ jaghanaṃ caiva kuryāccānena kuṇḍalam||23||
khaṇḍapradeśinī kāryā aṅgulyaḥ kuṃcitāstathā||
śeṣā bhinnordhvavalitā mrarāleṅgulayaḥ kare || 24 || 
gāmbhīryasattvaśauṃḍīryakeśasaṃgrahaṇādiṣu ||
svedasya cāpanayane śeṣe caiṣa karo bhavet || | || 25 ||
mrarālaḥ śukatuṇḍaśca vakritānāmikāṅguliḥ ||
nāhantvamityathaitena nityaṃ cābhinayedbudhaḥ || 26 ||
aṅgulyo yasya hastasya talamadhye'grasaṃsthitāḥ ||
tāsāmuparitāṅguṣṭhaḥ sa muṣṭiriti saṃjñitaḥ || 27 ||
eṣa prahāre vyāyāme nirdayastanapīḍane || |
sandhāraṇe'siyaṣṭyośca grahaṇe kuntadaṇḍayoḥ || 28 ||
ūrdhvāṅguṣṭho'yameva syātkaraḥ śikharasaṃjñitaḥ ||
eṣa raśmigrahe kāryaścāpāṅkuśadhanurgrahe ||29|| |
muṣṭimadhyagatoṅguṣṭhaḥ kapittha iti kīrtitaḥ ||
anenābhinayaḥ kāryo madhye cakre śare tathā || 3.26.30 ||
utkṣiptavakrā tu yadānāmikā sakanīyasī |||
asyaiva tu kapitthasya tataḥ syātkhaṭakāmukhaḥ ||31||
hotre chatragraha caiva karṣaṇe vyajane tathā ||
sragdāmadhāraṇe kāryo hyasaṅkocosya saṃgrahaḥ ||32|||
prasṛtā tarjanī cātra yadā sūcīmukhastadā ||
niṣpādane cābhinayennetraśakramaheśayoḥ || 33 ||
asyāṅgulyastu vipulāḥ sahāṃṅguṣṭhena kuñcitāḥ |||
ūrdhvāhaṃsanakhasyaiva sa bhavetpadmakośakaḥ || 34 ||
śliṣṭoṅguṣṭho nimnamadhyaḥ patākohiśiro bhavet ||
salilasya pradāne tu kāryā tena ca sūcanam || || 35 ||
adhomukhīnāṃ sarvāsāmaṅgulīnāṃ yadā bhavet ||
kaniṣṭhāṅguṣṭhakā cordhvā tadā syānmṛgaśīrṣakaḥ || 36 ||
sūcite cchedite kāryaṃ śakyā svoccālane tathā ||
tretāgnisaṃsthitā madhyā tarjanyaṅguṣṭhayoryadā || 37 ||
kāṅgule nāmikā vakrā tathā cordhvā kanīyasī ||
anena khalu kartavyaṃ phalānāṃ tu nirūpaṇam || 38 ||
āvartyante karatale yasyāṅgulyaḥ karasya vai ||
pārśvāgatavikīrṇāśca kolapadma iti smṛtaḥ || 39 ||
pratiṣedha kṛte yojyo nāsti śūnyeṣu cāpyatha ||
tisraḥ prasāritāṅgulyastathā cordhvā kanīyasī || 3.26.40 ||
tasyā madhyāśritoṅguṣṭhaḥ karaḥ sa caturaḥ smṛtaḥ ||
vacasāṃ viniyoge ca mādhye caiva prayojayet||41||
anenaiva tathā kāryaṃ varṇānāṃ tu nirūpaṇam ||
ūrdhvasthe ca nave cśvetaṃ rakte syādardhamaṇḍalam ||42||
maṇḍalaṃ na bhavetpītaṃ nīlaṃ ca mṛditaṃ bhavet ||
svabhāvasthena kṛtsnaṃ ca śeṣā varṇāstathaiva ca || 43 ||
madhyamāṅguṣṭhasandeśo vakrā caiva pradeśinī ||
ūrdhvamanyāḥ prakīrṇāśca bhramaraśca tadā bhavet || 44 ||
padmādigrahaṇe kāryaṃ karṇapūre tathāpyayam ||
tarjanīmadhyamāṅguṣṭhā tretāgnisthā nirantaram || || 45 ||
bhaveyurhaṃsavaktrasya śeṣā hyanyāḥ prasāritāḥ ||
ślakṣṇalāghavaniḥsāramārdaveṣu prayojayet || 46 ||
tiryak piṇḍīkṛtā yasminunnatā ca kaniṣṭhikā ||
aṅguṣṭhaḥ kuñcitastvevaṃ haṃsapakṣa iti smṛtaḥ || 47 ||
gaṇḍasaṃśrayametena bhojanaḥ saparigrahaḥ ||
sparśe caivānulāpārthe kārye saṃvāhane tathā || 48 ||
tarjanyaṅguṣṭhasaṃdaṃśastvarālasya yadā bhavet ||
nirbhugnatalamadhyaśca sasandaṃśa iti smṛtaḥ || 49 ||
saṃdaṃśastrividho jñeyastvagraho mukhajastathā ||
tathā pārśvakṛtaścaiva tasya karmāṇi me śṛṇu || 3.26.50 ||
sandaṃśagrahaṇe kāryaḥ śalyoddhāre tathāgrajaḥ ||
ālekhārañjane caiva tarjanīpīḍane tathā || 51 ||
yajñopavītī pārśvasthaḥ śiraḥsaṃdarśane tathā ||
samāgatāgrāṅguliko muktalaḥ padmakośakaḥ || 52 ||
devārcākaraṇe kāryo balo padmanirūpaṇe ||
asaṃyutāḥ karā hyete yathāvatkīrtitā mayā || 53 ||
patākābhyāṃ tu hastābhyāṃ sasphuṭoñjalirucyate ||
devatānāṃ gurūṇāṃ ca pitṝṇāṃ ceṣyate punaḥ || 54 ||
anyonyapārśvasaktābhyāṃ karābhyāṃ syātkapotakaḥ ||
śīte bhaye ca kāryo'yaṃ viniyogagame tathā || 55 ||
aṅgulyo yasya hastasya aḍulyantaraniḥsṛtāḥ ||
sa karkaṭa iti jñeyaḥ kartavyastu vijṛmbhite || 56 ||
maṇibandhana vinyastāvarālau vardhamānakaḥ ||
uttānau natapārśvasthau svastikaḥ parikīrtitaḥ || 57 ||
vistīrṇaṃ sarvametena ṛtavo gaganaṃ ghanam ||
samudraścābhineyaḥ syādbhūmiśca manujeśvara || 58 ||
khaṭakaḥ khaṭake nyasya khaṭakā vardhamānakaḥ ||
śṛṅgārārthe prayoktavyaḥ praṇāmakaraṇeṣu ca || 59 ||
arālau tu viparyastāvuttānau vardhamānakau ||
utsaṅga iti vijñeyaḥ sparśasya grahaṇe paraḥ || 3.26.60 ||
muktalaṃ tu yadā hastaṃ kapitthaṃ pariveṣṭayet ||
sa vijñeyastadā hasto niṣadho nāma nāmataḥ || 61||
saṃkṣipte caiva nikṣipte tathā kāryaḥ prapīḍite ||
patākau lambitau hastau doleti parikīrtitāḥ || || 62 ||
dhyānādidāne kartavyo bhakṣyādikaraṇeṣu ca ||
dvitīyapārśvasaṃśliṣṭaḥ pūrvaḥ puṣpapuṭaḥ smṛtaḥ || 63 ||
pūrvāṇi cābhineyāni karaṇāneva pārthiva ||
patākau tu yadā hastau mūrtahastāvadhomukhau || 64 ||
uparyupari vinyastau tadā samakaraḥ karaḥ ||
siṃhavyāghramṛgādyānāṃ kartavyo'bhinayo bhavet ||65||
añcitau kūrparāsā tu yadā syātsarpaśīrṣake ||
gajadantastathā nāmnā kāryaḥ śailanibarhaṇe ||66||
śukatuṇḍau karau kṛtvā vakṣasyabhimukhāñcitau ||
śanairadhomukhau viddhāvavahittha iti smṛtaḥ||67||
daurbalyaśca site kāryo gātrāṇāṃ ca nidarśane||
jñeyo vai vardhamānastu haṃsapakṣau parāṅmukhau || 68 ||
jālavātāyanādīnāṃ prayogastena ceṣyate ||
saṃyutānāṃ karāṇāṃ tu lakṣaṇaṃ kathitaṃ mayā || 69 ||
evaṃ jñeyāḥ karāḥ sarve nānārasasamudbhavāḥ ||
ata ūrdhvaṃ pravakṣyāmi nustuhastamapāśrayam || 3.26.70 ||
vakṣaso'ṣṭāṃgulasthau tu prāṅmukhau khaṭakāmukhau ||
samānakūrparāsau tu caturasro prakīrtitau||71||
haṃsapakṣau smṛtau hastau vyāvṛttau tālavṛntavat ||
udvṛttāviti vijñeyāvatha vā tālavṛntakau || 72 ||
caturasrīkṛtau hastau haṃsapakṣīkṛtau tathā ||
tiryaṅamukhāvūrdhvatalau jñeyau laghumukhāviti || 73 ||
tāveva maṇidhanvāṃtāvarālakacitau karau ||
jñeyāvabhinaye tvetau hyarālakhaṭakāmukhau || 74 ||
bhujau sakūrparāgrau tu kuṭilāvartitau karau ||
adhomukhatalāviddhāvāviddhāviti kīrtitau || 75 ||
sarpaśīrṣau yadā hastau bhavatāṃ svastika sthitau ||
tiryakprasāritāsyau ca jñeyau sūcīmukhāviti || 76 ||
recitau cāpi vijñeyau haṃsapakṣādbhutabhramau ||
prasāritottānatalau racitāveva saṃ jñitau || 77 ||
caturasro bhavedvāmaḥ savyahastaśca recitaḥ ||
vijñeyo nāṭyatattvajñairevaṃ recitasaṃjñitaḥ || 78 ||
añcitau kūrparābhyāṃ tu tri patākau yadā karau ||
kiṃcittiryaggatāvetāvāvahittha prakīrtitaḥ || 79 ||
maṇibandhananirmuktau śithilau pallavau smṛtau ||
bāhu śīviniṣkrāntau nitambau parikīrtitau || 3.26.80 ||
aṃsadeśe pravṛttau tu paripārśvotthitau tathā ||
vijñeyau keśabaṃdhākhyau karāvācāryasammatau || 81 ||
tiryakprasāritāveva pārśvasaṃsthau tathaiva ca ||
latākhyau tu karau jñeyau nṛttābhinayanaṃ prati || 82 ||
