samunnatā latāhastaḥ pārśvātpārśvaviloḍitaḥ ||
dvitīyaḥ khaṭakākhyaśca karihastaḥ prakīrtitaḥ || 83 ||
kaṭiśīrṣaniviṣṭāgrau tripatākau yadā karau ||
pakṣāvañcitakau vāme tadā jñeyau prayoktṛbhiḥ || 84 ||
tāveva tu parāvṛttau pakṣāghātau karāviti ||
adhomukhatalāviddhau jñeyau garuḍapakṣakau || 85 ||
tathā prasāritabhujau daṇḍapakṣāviti smṛtau ||
tau cordhvamaṇḍalau jñeyau gurudeśavivartanāt || 86 ||
tāveva pārśvavinyastau vijñeyau pārśvamaṇḍalau ||
veṣṭitāveṣṭitau hastau tayormaṇḍalakau smṛtau || 87 ||
tāveva maṇibandhāntau svastikākṛtisaṃjñitau ||
svastikāviti vikhyātau vikhyātau ca prakī rṇakau || 88 ||
dvāveva padmakośābhau padmakośāviti smṛtau ||
karāvuddhe jitāgrau tu vijñeyāvalipallavau || 89 ||
ūrdhvaprasāritau viddhau vijñeyāvulvaṇāviti ||
pallavau tu śirodeśe samprāptau lalitāviti || 3.26.90 ||
kūrparasvastikanyastau lalitāvalitau smṛtau ||
saṃyutā viyutāścaiva nṛttahastāḥ prakīrtitāḥ || 91 ||
nānārtharasasaṃyuktaṃ prayogakālasaṃśrayam ||
vijñeyaṃ nṛttahastānāṃ tena nābhihitaṃ mayā || 92||
ādhigraste jagante ca niyamasthe jagardite ||
matte pramatte cintāyāṃ nābhineyaḥ karo bhavet || 93 ||
udveṣṭaṃ samaveṣṭaṃ ca viceṣṭitavivartite ||
karmedaṃ nṛttahastānāṃ vijñeyaṃ ca caturvidham || 94 ||
kāryā lalitasaṃcārā nṛttahastāparā budhaiḥ||
lālityameva vijñeyaṃ vṛttasya pratibodhanam ||95||
deśaṃ kālaṃ prayogaṃ ca hyarthayuktimavekṣya ca ||
hastā ete prayoktavyā strīṇāṃ nṝṇāṃ ca nartake || 96 ||
hastā mayaite kathitā nṛvīra sarvaṃ karāyattamidaṃ hi nṛttam ||
yatnaśca kāryastu kareṣu tasmāccāturyalāsyābhinayopapannam ||97 ||
iti śrīviṣṇudharmottare tṛtīyakhaṇḍe mārkaṇḍeyavajrasaṃvāde nṛttahastavyavasthāvarṇano nāma ṣaḍviṃśatitamo 'dhyāyaḥ || 26 ||
<3.27>
mārkaṇḍeya uvāca ||
vācikaśca tathā hāryastvāṅgikassāttvikastathā ||
catuṣprakāro'bhinayaḥ kīrtito nāṭyakovidaiḥ || || 1 ||
vāciko vacasā proktaḥ śṛṇu hāryaṃ narādhipa ||
caturvidhaṃ tu vijñeyaṃ prastolaṅkāra eva ca || 2 ||
tathāṅgaracanā caiva jñeyā sajjīvameva ca ||
mṛdā vā dāruṇā vāpi vastreṇāpyatha carmaṇā || 3 ||
lohairanukṛtirvāpi prasta ityabhidhīyate ||
devadānavayakṣāṇāṃ gajāśvamṛgapakṣiṇām || 4 ||
kāryāṇi pratiśīrṣāṇi nāṭyajñaiḥ prastakarmaṇā ||
alaṅkārastu vijñeyo mālyābharaṇavāsasām || 5 ||
nānāvidhaḥ samāyoge hyaṅgopāṅgavinirgataḥ ||
tacca kāryo nayo buddhvā niyogaṃ jātimeva ca || 6 ||
ādhipatyaṃ tathā sthānaṃ deśe ca nṛpasattama ||
atoṅgaracanā tubhyaṃ vakṣyāmi nṛpa varṇajām || 7 ||
śveto raktastathā pītaḥ kṛṣṇo haritameva ca ||
mūlavarṇāḥ samākhyātāḥ pañca pārthivasattama|| 8 ||
ekadvitrisamāyogādbhāvakalpanayā tathā ||
saṅkhyaivāṃtaravarṇānāṃ loke kartuṃ na śakyate || 9 ||
atyarthasamayogitvālloke'haṃ nṛpapuṃgava ||
vibhāgaṃ sampravakṣyāmi varṇayoḥ śyāmagaurayoḥ || 3.27.10 ||
dviprakārā chaviryasmātsarvasyeha prakīrtitā ||
gaurī pañcavidhā tatra śyāmā dvādaśadhā bhavet || || 11 ||
rukmagaurī dantagaurī sphuṭacandanagauryapi ||
śaradghanacandrakavadgaurī pañcavidhā smṛtā ||12||
raktaśyāmyā bhavetpūrvaṃ mudgaśyāmā nirantaram||
tato dūrvāṃkuraśyāmā pāṇḍuśyāmā ca pārthiva ||13 ||
tataśca haritaśyāmā pītaśyāmā tato bhavet ||
tataḥ priyaṅgukaśyāmā kapiśyāmā ca pārthiva || ||14|
tato nīlotpalaśyāmā cāṣaśyāmā tataḥ param||
tato raktotpalaśyāmā ghanaśyāmā tathā parā||15||
dravyānurūpavarṇaistu tāsāṃ vyaktirudāhṛtā ||
raṅgavyatikaraiḥ śobhāmadhikṛtya mahīyate ||16||
vakṣyāmi varṇāndevānāṃ matvāpatu viniścayam ||
tatra yeṣāṃ na vakṣyāmi te kāryā gauravarṇakāḥ ||17||
śyāmastu vāsukiḥ kāryo nāgā gaurāstu dvāpare|
daityāśca dānavāścaiva rākṣasā guhyakānugāḥ ||18 ||
piśācā jalasaṅkāśā sammitāni tu varṇataḥ||
ṣaṭsu dvīpeṣu puruṣāḥ kartavyāḥ kanakaprabhāḥ || || 19 ||
jambūdvīpasya varṣeṣu muktvaikaṃ bhārataṃ nṛpa ||
bhārateṣu tathā kāryā nānādeśabhavāstathā || || 3.27.20 ||
pulindā dākṣiṇātyāśca prāyaśo varṇato'sitāḥ ||
śakāśca yavanāścaiva pahlavā vāhlikāśca ye || 21 ||
prāyeṇa gaurāḥ kartavyāḥ uttarāpathasambhavāḥ ||
pāñcālāḥ śūrasenāśca tathā ye cātra māgadhāḥ || 22 ||
aṅgā vaṅgāḥ kaliṅgāśca prāyaśo varṇato'sitāḥ ||
candravarṇā dvijāḥ kāryā rājanyāḥ padmasannibhāḥ || 23 ||
āpāṇḍurāstathā vaiśyāḥ śyāmā śūdrāśca varṇataḥ ||
nānāvarṇāḥ smṛtā bhūtā gandharvāpsarasastathā || 24 |||
rājānaḥ padmavarṇābhā ye cāpi sukhino janāḥ ||
kukarmāṇo grahagrastā vyādhitāstapa āśritāḥ || 25 ||
āyastakarmaṇaścaiva asitāḥ kulajālayaḥ ||
varṇaḥ pratyakṣadṛṣṭasya kartavyaḥ sadṛśastathā || 26 ||
devagandharvasiddhānāṃ kumārāṇāṃ ca pārthiva ||
śmaśru teṣāṃ na kartavyaṃ ye ca dīkṣāṃ samāśritāḥ || 27 ||
divyā ye puruṣāścaiva tathā vidyādharāśca ye ||
śṛṅgāriṇo narendrāśca citraśmaśrudharāstathā || 28 ||
śuddhaṃ tu liṅgināṃ kāryaṃ tathāmātyapurodhasām ||
madhyasthā ye ca puruṣā ye ca dīkṣāṃ samāśritāḥ || 29 ||
anirṇītapratijñānāṃ duḥkhitānāṃ tapasvinām ||
munīnāṃ cirabaddhānāṃ dīrghaṃ śmaśru prakīrtitam || 3.27.30 ||
dharmapravṛttervaṃśaśca bhavecchūdro narādhipa ||
vaṇijāṃ kaṃcukīyānāṃ brāhmaṇānāṃ tathaiva ca || 31 ||
rājñāṃ vicitro veśaḥ syādveśyāstrīṇāṃ ca yādava ||
yathāliṅgaṃ bhavedveśastathānyeṣāṃ tu lokataḥ || 32 ||
trividhā mukuṭā jñeyā devapārthivasaṃśrayāḥ ||
devānāṃ pārthivānāṃ ca kartavyāstu kirīṭakāḥ || 33 ||
devānāṃ saptaśikharāḥ pañcabhirbhūbhṛtāṃ tathā ||
tribhiśca śikharaiḥ kāryā mahiṣīyuvarājayoḥ || 34 ||
senādhyakṣasya kartavyastathaikaśikharo nṛpa ||
daityadānavayakṣāṇāṃ pannagānāṃ sarakṣasām || 35 ||
ekapaṭṭāstu vistīrṇā kartavyā mukuṭāḥ śubhāḥ ||
vidyādharāṇāṃ siddhānāṃ vānarāṇāṃ tathaiva ca ||36||
granthimantastu kartavyā mukuṭāstu prayoktṛbhiḥ |||
devadānavagandharvāḥ kṛṣṇapakṣāḥ prakīrtitāḥ || 37 ||
daityadānavayakṣāśca piśācanāgarākṣasāḥ ||
hariśmaśrukacāḥ kāryāstathā piṅgekṣaṇā nṛpa || 38 ||
kañcukīyāśca kartavyāstathā śuklaśiroruhāḥ||
amātyakañcukīyānāṃ tathā śreṣṭhapurodhasām||39||
veṣṭanāpaṭṭabaddhāni pratiśīrṣāṇi kārayet||
piśāconmattabhūtānāṃ tāpasānāṃ tathaiva ca||3.27.40||
anistīrṇapratijñānāṃ lambakeśaṃ bhavecchiraḥ||
bālānāmapi karttavyaṃ śikhiṇḍakavibhūṣaṇam ||41||
jaṭājūṭayutaṃ kāryaṃ munīnāmapi pārthiva ||
ceṭānāmapi karttavyaṃ triśiraḥ khaṇḍameva ca||42||
vidūṣakasya khalatiḥ syātkākapadameva ca||
śeṣāṇāmuktayogena śeṣajātisamāśrayam||43||
sajjīva iti yatproktaṃ tasya vakṣyāmi lakṣaṇam||
praveśastu tiraścāṃ vai sajjīva iti saṃjñitaḥ||44||
teṣāṃ prastena kartavyā prakṛtiḥ puruṣāṃtarā||
tathaivāyudhahastānāṃ kartavyā hetayaḥ śubhāḥ ||45||
evaṃ lokopacāreṇa svabuddhyā vibhavena ca ||
nāṭyopakaraṇānīha yathāvatparikalpayet || 46 ||
hetipramokṣo na tu raṅgamadhye kāryo bhavetpārthivavaṃśamukhya ||
āhāryametatkathitaṃ samāsādvakṣyāmyataścāṅgikameva tubhyam || 47 ||
iti śrīviṣṇudharmottare tṛtīyakhaṇḍe mārkaṇḍeyavajrasaṃvāde āhāryābhinayo nāma saptaviṃśo'dhyāyaḥ || 27 ||
<3.28>
mārkaṇḍeya uvāca ||
ataḥ paraṃ pravakṣyāmi sāmānyābhinayaṃ tava ||
tasminkāryaḥ prayatnastu nāṭyāntarapratiṣṭhitam || 1 ||
śabdaṃ sparśaśca rūpaṃ ca raso gandhastathaiva ca ||
indriyāṇīṃdriyārthāstu bhāvenābhinayed budhaḥ || 2 ||
kṛtvā sācīkṛtāṃ dṛṣṭiṃ śiraḥ kiṃcinnataṃ bhavet ||
tarjanīkarṇadeśe tu śabdantvabhinayedbudhaḥ || 3 ||
kiñcidākuñcite netre kṛtvotphullā tvanāmikām ||
ekaśvāso bhaveccāpi gandhamevaṃ vinirdiśet ||4||
indriyārthāstu pañcaite trividhāḥ parikīrttitāḥ ||
iṣṭāstathā hyaniṣṭāśca madhyasthā nṛpasattama || 5 ||
gātraprahlādaneneha tathā pulakitena ca ||
nitāntāsrakriyābhiśca sarvamiṣṭaṃ nirūpayet || 6 ||
parāvṛttena śirasā tathā cānena cakṣuṣā ||
netranāsāñcitatvena hyaniṣṭamiti nirdiśet || 7 ||
madhyasthenaiva bhāvena madhyasthakaraṇaṃ bhavet |||
ātmānubhāvī yo'rthaḥ syādātmastha iti saṃjñitaḥ || 8 ||
parārthavarṇanaṃ yacca parastheti ca kīrtitam ||
uttānau tu karau kṛtvā svastikau pārśvasaṃsthitau || 9 ||
udvāhitena śirasā tathā cordhvanirīkṣaṇam ||
prabhātaṃ gaganaṃ rātriḥ pradoṣaṃ divasaṃ tathā || 3.28.10 ||
ṛtūnghanāndharāṃ caiva vistīrṇāśca jalāśayān ||
diśograhānsunakṣatrādyaccānyatsvagataṃ nṛpa || 11 ||
tasya tvabhinayaḥ kāryo nānādṛṣṭisamanvitaḥ ||
ebhireva karairbhūyaḥ stanaiśca śirasā tathā || 12 ||
adhonirīkṣitenātha bhūmiṣṭhānsamprayojayet ||
candraṃ jyotsnāṃ mukhaṃ vāyuṃ sparśenābhinayedbudhaḥ || 13 ||
vastrāvaguṇṭhanātsūryaṃ rajodhūmānalāṃstathā ||
bhūmitāpamathoṣṇaṃ ca kuryācchvāsābhilāpataḥ || 14 ||
ūrdhvakekaradṛṣṭyā tu madhyā hvasthaṃ divākaram ||
udayāstagataṃ caiva vismayārthe pradarśayet || 15 ||
candraṃ vismitayā dṛṣṭyā tathā tārāśca darśayet ||
sukhaspṛśyāni sarvāṇi romāṃcaṃ na tu darśayet || 16 ||
duḥkhāni ca tathodvegaṃ mukhasya tu vikūṇanaiḥ ||
gambhīrodātta saṃyuktānarthānsauṣṭhavasaṃyutān||17||
yajñopavītadeśasthamavālaṃ hastamādiśet ||
hārāvahārayāgeṣu sragdāmasya ca darśane || 18 ||
bhramareṇa pradeśinyā dṛṣṭeḥ parigamena ca ||
alaṃpadmākapīḍāyāḥ sarvārthagrahaṇaṃ bhavet || 19 ||
śravyaṃ śravaṇayogena dṛśyaṃ dṛṣṭivilokanaiḥ ||
ātmasaṃsthaṃ parasthaṃ vā madhyasthaṃ vā vinirdiśet || 3.28.20 ||
vidyudulkāghanaravo visphuliṅgaviṣastathā ||
pratodo'kṣinimeṣaśca tebhineyāḥ prayoktṛbhiḥ || 21 ||
mukhāvaguṇṭhanaṃ kāryaṃ bhramarādinivāraṇe ||
kṛtvā svastikasaṃsthānau padmakośāvadhomukhau || 22 ||
siṃharśavānarādīnāṃ kartavyaṃ tu nirūpaṇam ||
svastikau tripatākau tu gurūṇāṃ pādavandane || 23 ||
khaṭakāsvastikau cāpi pratodagrahaṇe bhavet ||
ekādyānāṃ daśāntānāṃ saṃkhyā tvaṃgulibhirbhavet || 24 ||
daśākhyā ca śatākhyā ca sthānasaṃkhyā tataḥ param ||
patākābhyāṃ tu hastābhyāṃ vācā cābhinayo bhavet || 25 ||
chatradhvajapatākāśca vijñeyā daṇḍadhāraṇāt ||
nānā praharaṇaṃ caiva vijñeyamiha vāraṇaiḥ || 26 ||
ekacitto hyadhodṛṣṭiḥ śiraḥ kiñcinnataṃ bhavet ||
savyahastaśca saṃdaṃśasmite vyāne vitaṅkite || 27 ||
udvāhitaṃ śiraḥ kṛtvā haṃsapakṣaṃ pradakṣiṇam ||
apatyarūpaṇe kāryamuśvāyaṃ ca prayoktṛbhiḥ || 28 ||
arālaṃ ca śiraḥ sthāne samudvāhyā ca vāmataḥ ||
gate nivṛtte dhvaste ca śrāntaṃ vākyaṃ niyojayet || 29 ||
jitendriyatayā yuktaṃ prasannavadanaṃ tathā ||
vicitrakusumālokāṃ śaradaṃ tu nirūpayet || ||| 3.28.30 ||
gātrasya kampanāttajjñaistathā cānyābhilāṣitaḥ ||
hemantastvabhidheyaḥ syātpuruṣairmadhyamādhamaiḥ ||31||
śirodantauṣṭhakampena gātrasaṃkocakena |ca ||
kūjitaiśca sasītkāraiḥ śītasyābhinayo bhavet || 32 ||
prasādajananārambhairupabhogaiḥ sasambhramaiḥ ||
vasantastvabhinetavyo nānāpuṣpapradarśanāt|| ||| 33 ||
svedāpamārjanāccāpi bhūmipātopajīvinaḥ ||
uṣṇasya vāyoḥ sparśaśca grīṣmasyābhinayo bhavet || 34 || 
gambhīranādaśravaṇātprāvṛṣyabhi nayo bhaveta ||
vacasābhinayaḥ kāryo ṛtūnāṃ sukhaduḥkhayoḥ || 35 ||
svabhāvābhinaye sthānaṃ puṃbhiḥ kāryaṃ tu vaiṣṇavam ||
āyutaṃ cāvahityaṃ ca strībhiḥ kāryaṃ svabhāvataḥ || 36 ||
dhairyaśīlāṅgasampannaṃ puruṣāṇāṃ viceṣṭitam ||
mṛtaśīlāṅgahāraistu strīṇāṃ kāryaṃ viceṣṭitam || 37 ||
āliṅgana tu kartavyaṃ smitaromāñcasaṃyutam ||
romāñcena tu harṣaḥ syātkrodhamudvṛttacakṣuṣā || 38 ||
īrṣyā krodhastu kartavyo mālyābharaṇavarjitam ||
śirasā kampanaṃ caiva marmabhaṃgaistathaiva ca || 39 ||
adhomukhaḥ saniḥśvāso duḥkhaṃ sandarśayennaraḥ ||
bhūmihastābhighātena strīṇāṃ syādruditena tat ||| 3.28.40 ||
ānandetisamudbhūtaṃ ruditaṃ dvividhaṃ smṛtam ||
hṛṣṭena cāpyahṛṣṭena taṃ mukhena nirūpayet || 41 ||
sambhramāvegaceṣṭābhiḥ puruṣāṇāṃ bhayaṃ bhavet ||
trāturanveṣaṇādeva uccairākranditena ca || 42 ||
narāliṅganataścaivaṃ bhayaṃ kāryaṃ bhavetstriyaḥ ||
tripatākāṃgulībhyāṃ tu calitābhyāṃ prayojayet || 43 ||
śukaśca śārikā caiva sūkṣmā ye caiva pakṣiṇaḥ ||
śikhisārasahaṃsādyāḥ sthalā ye ca svabhāvataḥ || 44 ||
pakṣāṅgahārairdvividhaistaiṣāmabhinayo bhavet||
bhūtāḥ piśācā yakṣāśca dānavā rākṣasāstathā || 45 ||
aṅgahārairvinirdeśyāḥ pratyakṣā na bhavanti ye ||
pratyakṣā abhineyāḥ syustathodvegaiḥ savismayaiḥ || 46 ||
devānpraṇāmakaraṇaiḥ pratyakṣe tu narādhipa ||
parokṣe vividhaiścihnaistathānukaraṇaiḥ śubhaiḥ || 47 ||
savyotthitena hastena tvarālena śiraḥ spṛśet ||
narebhivādanaṃ hyetatkapolena tathā striyāḥ || 48 ||
mahājanaṃ sakhivaraṃ viṭadhūrtajanaṃ tathā ||
parimaṇḍalasaṃjñena hastenābhinayedbudhaḥ || 49 ||
pravṛttānprāṃśuyogena vṛkṣāṃścaiva samutthitān ||
prasāritābhyāṃ bāhubhyāmutkṣiptābhyāṃ pradarśayet || 3.28.50||
(samūhaṃ sumahatsenāṃ bahuvistīrṇameva ca ||
patākābhyāṃ tu hastābhyāṃ cotkṣiptābhyāṃ pradarśayet ||)
śauryaṃ ca dhairyadarpaṃ ca garvamaudāryamucchrayam ||
lalāṭasthānasaṃsthena tvarālena pradarśayet || 51 ||
prajño dehāmadāviddhau karau tu mṛgaśīrṣakau ||
vistīrṇapradrutotkṣepau yojyau yatsyādupāvṛtam|| || 52 ||
adhomukhottānatalaṃ hastaṃ kiṃcitprasāritam ||
kṛtvā cābhinayo dve ca bilagrāhaṃ gṛhaṃ gṛham || 53 ||
kāmārtāñjvaritāṃścaiva śāpopahatacetasaḥ ||
teṣāmabhinayaḥ kārya ucchvāsaiḥ kampanaistathā ||54||
pratyakṣābhinayaḥ kāryo dolāyāḥ paridolane ||
ākāśaracanānīha vakṣyāmyātmagatāni ca || 55 ||
apavāritakaṃ caiva janāntikamathāpi ca ||
parasambhāṣaṇaṃ yatsyāttadākāśavacaḥ smṛtam || 56||
anyairaśravaṇīyaṃ tu janānti kamihocyate||
apavāritakaṃ karṇe punaruktaṃ pracakṣate||57||
hastamantaritaṃ kṛtvā tripatākaṃ prayoktṛbhiḥ|
janāṃtikaṃ prayoktavyamapavāritakaṃ tathā|||52||
svapnāyate cābhinaye vācā gadgadayā bhavet ||
vṛddhānāṃ yojayetṣāṭhyaṃ gadgadaskhalitākṣaram ||59||
asamāptākṣaraṃ caiva bālānāṃ tu kalasvanam||
dīrghocchvāsayutaṃ glānaṃ vyādhitānāṃ tu yojayet || 3.28.60 ||
ucchvāsena tu dīrgheṇa mṛtyukālaṃ tu nāṭayet||
gātranispaṃdabhāvena mṛtasyābhinayo bhaveta ||
sāttvikābhinayaṃ vakṣye rasabhāveṣu pārthiva ||61||
etāvadevābhinaye tu tubhyaṃ śakyaṃ mayā pārthivamukhya vaktum ||
nāṭyaṃ hi viśvasya yato'nukāraṃ kṛtsnaṃ tato vaktumaśakyamīśa || 62||
iti śrīviṣṇudharmottare tṛtīyakhaṇḍe mārkaṇḍeyavajrasaṃvāde sāmānyābhinayo nāmāṣṭāviṃśatitamo'dhyāyaḥ ||28||
<3.29>
mārkaṇḍeya uvāca ||
ataḥ paraṃ pravakṣyāmi gītānāṃ lakṣaṇaṃ tava ||
svabhāvenottuṅgagatau kāryaṃ jānukaṭīsamam || 1 ||
yuddhacārī pracāreṣu jānustanasamaṃ nyaset ||
jvarānte ca kṣudhānte ca tapaḥśrānte rujānvite || 2 ||
vikṛte cāvahitthe ca tathautsukyasamanvite ||
śṛṅgāre caiva śoke ca svacchanda gamane tathā || 3 ||
eteṣveva tu sarvatra sthirā kāryā gatirbudhaiḥ ||
asvasthe kampite naiva bhaye vitrāsite tathā || 4 ||
āvege caiva harṣe ca kāryaṃ yacca tvarānvitam ||
aniṣṭaśravaṇe caiva kṣepe cādbhutadarśane || 5||
api cātyayike kārye tathā caivārimārgaṇe ||
aparādhānusāreṇa śvāpadānugatau tathā || 6 ||
evaṃvidheṣu kāryeṣu gatiṃ śīghrāṃ prayojayet ||
gatiḥ śṛṅgāriṇī kāryā svasthakāmitasambhave || 7 ||
dūtīdarśitamārgastu hṛdyaveśaparikrame ||
sasauṣṭhavasamāyuktairlayatālavaśonugaiḥ || 8 ||
pādayoranugau cāpi hastau kāryau prayoktṛbhiḥ ||
pracchannakāmite gacchanna cātyarthaṃ vibhūṣitaḥ || 9 ||
kṛṣṇavāsāḥ padairmaṃdaiḥ śabdāśaṅkī muhurmuhuḥ ||
vepamānaśarīraśca śaṅkitaḥ praskhalanmuhuḥ || 3.29.10 ||
aṅgasvabhāvanepathyo raudrastu trividho mataḥ ||
rudhiraklinnadehastu padmāṃtrāṅgadharastathā || 11 ||
bahvāyudhadharo raudranepathyena prakīrtitaḥ ||
rūkṣo nirbhartsanakatho nirghṛṇastu svabhāvataḥ || 12 ||
vividhasyāpi raudrasya catustālāntarāgatiḥ ||
ahṛdyā tu mahī yatra śmaśānaraṇasaṅkulā ||13||
āsannā ca vikṛṣṭā ca tatra bībhatsikā gatiḥ ||
gatirvīre ca karttavyā pādavikṣiptasaṃyutā || 14 ||
vismaye caiva harṣe ca vikṣiptapadavikramā ||
gatiḥ sthirapadā proktā tathaiva karuṇārase || 15 ||
na tatra sauṣṭhavaṃ kāryaṃ na pramāṇaṃ tathāvidham ||
vighūrṇitaśarīrā ca stabdhā ca śithilā gatiḥ || 16 ||
gāḍhaprahārite kāryā gatiḥ piṇḍitavigrahā ||
drutā bhayānake kāryā stabdhā kāryavaśādatha || 17 ||
sattve tu vikṛte dṛṣṭe gatirvisphuritekṣaṇā ||
tāpasānāṃ bhavenmandā yugamātrekṣaṇā gatiḥ ||| 18 ||
hastasaṃsparśanānmandāpyandhakāre gatirbhavet ||
rathasthasyāpi karttavyā gatirmandapadairatha ||19||
samapādaṃ budhasthānaṃ kṛtvā rathagatiṃ vrajet||
dhanurgṛhītvā hastena tathā caikena kūbaram || 3.29.20 ||
sūtaścāsya bhavedeva pratodagrahaṇākṛtiḥ ||
vimānasthasya kartavyā gatirānaṃdinī bhavet || 21 ||
ārohāścāvarohāśca ūrdhvādhovīkṣaṇairgataiḥ ||
maṇḍalāvartanaiḥ kāryamākāśagamanaṃ tathā || 22 ||
ṛjvāgatonnataiḥ pādairbhraśyamānastu khādbhavet ||
ucchritārohaṇaṃ kāryamapakrāntapadairatha || 23 ||
tathāvataraṇaṃ kāryaṃ śaṃkitairiva saṃhitaiḥ ||
utkṣiptapadasañcārā salile ca gatirbhavet || 24 ||
utkṣiptahastasañcārā pādapārohaṇe bhavet ||
sūcīviddhairapakrāntaiḥ padaiḥ pārśvagatairatha || 25 ||
aṅkuśagrahaṇānnāgaṃ khalīnagrahaṇādbhayam ||
pragraha grahaṇādeva anyeṣāṃ kārayedgatim || 26 ||
aśvayāne gatiḥ kāryā vaiśākhasthānakena ha ||
pannagānāṃ gatiḥ kāryā padaiḥ svastikasaṃyutaiḥ || ||| 27 ||
viṭasyāpi tu kartavyā gatirlalitavikramā ||
kaṃcukīyasya kartavyā kampitā skhalitā gatiḥ ||28||
viṣṭambhanagatiprāṇā kṛśasyāpi gatirbhavet ||
vyādhigrastasya ca tathā tapaḥśrāntasya caiva hi || 29 ||
dīrghādhvani gatasyāpi śanairmandaparikramā ||
mattasya tu gatiḥ kāryā skhalitā pārśvayordvayoḥ || 3.29.30 ||
unmattasyāpi kartavyā gatistvabhinayakramā ||
asambaddhapralāpī syānmalino lomaśastathā || 31 ||
vikalānāṃ gatiḥ kāryā yathā kāryānurūpataḥ ||
sthūladehasya kartavyā gatirdehānukarṣiṇī || 32 ||
vidūṣakasya kartavyā gatirhāsyapradā tathā ||
svabhāvajātaṃ vinyasya kuṭilaṃ vāmake kare || 33||
gatau rameta ceṭānāṃ dṛṣṭiścāryavicāriṇī ||
nānādeśasamudbhūtā puruṣāṇāṃ svabhāvataḥ || 34 ||
śvāpadānāṃ paśūnāṃ ca gatiḥ kāryā narādhipa ||
śeṣāṇāmarthayogena sthānakāni prayojayet || 35 ||
dhairyopapannā gatiruttamānāṃ madhyā gatirmadhyamasaṃmitānām ||
drutā gatiśca pracurādhamānāṃ layatrayaṃ sattvavaśena yojyam || 36 ||
raṃge vikṛttā tu nṛpapradhāna gatāgataiḥ pādagatipracāraḥ ||
kāryā vaśādeva gṛhasya nityaṃ samīkṣya raṅgaṃ tu yathānurūpam || 37 ||
strīṇāṃ gatiḥ syāllalitapracārā dhīrā narāṇāṃ ca tathoddhatā syāt ||
gati pracāro'rthavaśena yojyaścaitāvaduktaṃ tava rājasiṃha||38||
vayonurūpaḥ prathamastu veśo veśānurūpaśca gatipracāraḥ ||
gatipracārānugataṃ ca nāṭyaṃ nāṭyānu rūpo'bhinayaśca kāryaḥ || 39 ||
iti śrīviṣṇudharmottare tṛtīyakhaṇḍe mārkaṇḍeyavajrasaṃvāde gatipracāravarṇano nāmaikonatriṃśattamo'dhyāyaḥ || 29 ||
<3.30>
mārkaṇḍeya uvāca ||
hāsyaśṛṃgārakaruṇāraudraghorabhayānakāḥ ||
bībhatsādbhutaśāntākhyā nava nāṭye rasāḥ smṛtāḥ || 1 ||
śānto rasaḥ svatantrotra pṛthageva vyavasthitaḥ ||
śṛṅgārāddhi bhaveddhāsyo raudrācca karuṇodayaḥ ||2||
vīrāccaivādbhutotpattirbībhatsācca bhayānakaḥ ||
eteṣāmaṅgavarṇāśca sāttvikobhi nayaḥ smṛtaḥ || 3 ||
śāntasvabhāvavarṇastu rasaḥ prokto narādhipa ||
śṛṅgārastu bhavecchyāmo rakto raudraḥ prakīrtitaḥ || 4 ||
sito hāsyaśca vijñeyaḥ kṛṣṇaścaiva bhayānakaḥ ||
gauro vīrastu vijñeyaḥ pītaścaivādbhutaḥ smṛtaḥ || 5 ||
kāpotaḥ karuṇaścaiva nīlo bībhatsameva ca ||
hāsyaḥ pramathadevastu śṛṅgāro viṣṇudaivataḥ || 6 ||
raudro raudrādhidevaśca karuṇo yamadaivataḥ ||
bībhatsasya mahākālaḥ kāladevo bhayānakaḥ || 7 ||
vīro mahendradevaḥ syādadbhuto brahmadaivataḥ|| śā
ntasya devo vijñeyaḥ paraḥ puruṣa eva tu||8||
śāntasya tu samutpattirnṛpa vairāgyataḥ smṛtā||
sa cābhineyo bhavati liṅgagrahaṇatastathā||9||
sarvabhūtadayādhyānamokṣamārgaprartanaiḥ||
nāsti yatra sukhaṃ duḥkhaṃ na dveṣo nāpi matsaraḥ||3.30.10||
samaḥ sarveṣu bhūteṣu sa śāntaḥ prathito rasaḥ||
hāsyasya tu samutpattirasambaddhapralāpataḥ || 11 ||
asambaddhāttathā veśādbhavatīti viniścayaḥ ||
ātmasthaśca parasthaśca dvividhaḥ sa ca kīrtitaḥ || 12 ||
ātmasthaśca svayaṃ hāsātparasthaḥ parahāsanāt ||
uttamādhamamukhyānāṃ trividhaḥ sa bhavettadā || 13 ||
īṣadvikampitairgaṇḍaiḥ kaṭākṣaissauṣṭhavānvitaiḥ ||
alakṣitadvijaṃ caiva uttamānāṃ smitaṃ bhavet || 14 ||
tathā darśitadantantu madhyamānāṃ prakīrtitam ||
sāsraṃ tadadhamānāṃ syātsasvanaṃ ca mahībhujām || 15 ||
śṛṅgāro dvividhaḥ proktaḥ sambhogādvirahāttathā ||
vipralambhakṛtasyātra nirvedo'bhinayakriyaḥ || 16 ||
vipralambhe tu nirdiṣṭāḥ kāmāvasthāstathā daśa ||
cakṣuḥprītirvinirdiṣṭā prathamāyāṃ narādhipa || 17 ||
manasaśca tathā saṅgo dvitīyāyāṃ prakīrtitaḥ ||
smaraṇaṃ ca tathā proktaṃ tṛtīyāyāṃ nirantaram || 18 ||
nidrābhedaścaturthyāṃ tu paṃcamyāṃ tanutā tathā ||
vyāvṛttiviṣayebhyastu ṣaṣṭhyāṃ proktā narādhipa || 19 ||
lajjāpraṇāśaḥ saptamyāmunmādastadanantaram ||
navamyāṃ tu tathā mūrcchā daśamyāṃ maraṇaṃ bhavet ||3.30.20||
dvayorujjvalayostatra sambhogābhinayo bhaveta ||
sukhaprāyeṣṭasaṃpanno yatra mālyānulepakaḥ || 21||
puruṣapramadāyugmaṃ śṛṃgāra iti saṃjñitaḥ ||
śokādbhavati rājendra tathaiva karuṇo rasaḥ || 22 || 
trastagātrādviniḥśvāsaparidevitarodanaiḥ ||
mukhavaivarṇya śoṣaiśca tasyehābhinayo bhavet || 23 ||
krodhādbhavati raudrasya rasasya tu samudbhavaḥ ||
raktatvanetrabhrukuṭikrodhāmarṣaiḥ sasāhasaiḥ || 24 ||
pīḍanaiḥ śastrasaṅghātaistasyehābhinayo bhaveta ||
jugupsayā bhavati bībhatsasya samudbhavaḥ || 25 ||
nāsāvighūrṇanāttasya codvegena tathaiva ca ||
āścaryeṇa samutpattiradbhutaṃ tatprakīrtitam || 26 || 
prasphuritākṣiromāñcairaṅgulībhramaṇādibhiḥ ||
svedena cābhineyaḥ syādadbhutastu tathā rasaḥ || 27 ||
nāṭyasya mūlaṃ tu rasaḥ pradiṣṭo rasena hīnaṃ na hi vṛttamasti ||
tasmātprayatnena rasāśrayasya vṛttasya yatnaṃ puruṣeṇa kāryam || 28 ||
iti śrīviṣṇudharmottare tṛtīyakhaṇḍe mārkaṇḍeyavajrasaṃvāde rasavaṇarno nāma triṃśattamo'dhyāyaḥ || 30 ||
<3.31>
mārkaṇḍeya uvāca ||
bhāvādhyāyamato vacmi tanme nigadataḥ śṛṇu ||
hāsādyāḥ kathitā bhāvāḥ pañcāśattvekavarjitāḥ || 1 || 
paraceṣṭānukaraṇādyaddhāsyamupajāyate ||
smitahāsābhihasitairabhineyasya paṇḍitaiḥ || 2 ||
iṣṭārthaviṣayaprāptau ratirityupajāyate ||
saumyatvādabhineyā sā vāṅmādhuryādibhistathā || 3 ||
iṣṭabandhuviyogena dhananāśena pārthiva ||
śoko bhavati tasya syādruditābhinayakriyā || 4 ||
krodhaścatuḥprakāraśca kartavyo nāṭyayoktṛbhiḥ ||
ripujo gurujaścaiva bhṛtyajaḥ praṇayodbhavaḥ || 5 ||
niryantritaḥ sa śatrūṇāṃ gurūṇāṃ svedasaṃyutaḥ ||
bhṛtyānāṃ ca dayāluḥ syātstrīṇāṃ ca praṇayodbhavaḥ || 6 ||
karmātiśayanirvṛttau vismayo harṣasaṃbhavaḥ ||
siddhisthāne tvasau sādhyo romāñcahasanādibhiḥ ||7||
asau mohābhinirvṛttau vyavasāyanayātmakaḥ||
utsāhastvabhineyaḥ syādapramādakriyādibhiḥ||8||
svāparādhasamudbhūtaṃ bhayaṃ nāmeha jāyate ||
vepathutrāsapatanairabhineyasya sambhramaiḥ||9||
bībhatsadarśanādyaistu jugupsā nāma jāyate||3.31.10||
udvejanaissahṛllekhairabhineyo vikūṇanaiḥ||
dāridryeṣṭavināśe ca nirvedo nāma jāyate||11|| 
parasparaviniḥśvāsaistasyābhinayanakriyā||
vānte virakte śrānte ca glāniḥ syāttapasā tathā||12||
mandākramaṇakampādyaiḥ kṣāmatvenaṃ ca darśayet ||13||
cauryādibhirgṛhītasya śaṅkā nāmeha jāyate ||
ākārāvaraṇaṃ tasyāḥ kāryaṃ digavalokanam ||
parāparādhasambhūtā asūyā nāma jāyate || 14 ||
guṇanāśanavidveṣaiḥ sābhineyā tathā budhaiḥ ||
madyopayogena mado jāyate ca tribhistathā || 15 ||
uttamādhamamadhyatvātpuruṣāṇāṃ svabhāvataḥ ||
dṛgbhrameṇottamānāṃ syānmadhyānāṃ vākpralāpataḥ || 16 |||
adhamānāṃ ca patanairasambaddhaparikramaiḥ ||
āyāsenādhamanāñca śramo nāmeha jāyate||17||
gātramardanaviśvāsajṛmbhaṇaistatprayojayet ||
garveṇa khedādrāgeṇa hyālasya syātsvabhāvataḥ || 18 ||
tasya prayogaḥ kartavyaḥ śayanāsanasevanāt ||
daurgatyena manastāpāddainyaṃ nāmeha jāyate || 19 |||
hṛdayasya vitarkeṇa tasya tvabhinayo bhavet ||
asthāne taskarāndṛṣṭvā trāsanairvividhaistathā || 3.31.20 ||
tatpratīkāraśūnyasya mohaḥ samupajāyate |||
vyasanenābhighātena vairānusmaraṇena ca ||21 ||
sarvendriyāṇāṃ mohena sa tu kāryaḥ prayoktṛbhiḥ ||
smaraṇātpūrvavṛttānāṃ smṛtirnāmeha jāyate || 22 ||
bhṛśamutkṣepaṇāttasyāḥ prayogaḥ kāryalolanāt ||
lokaṃ vṛttāntavijñānād vṛttirnāmeha jāyate ||23||
prayojyo labdhabhogena alabdhasya vivarjitam ||
krīḍā nāmeha bhavati cāpalyāddharṣasaṃbhavā || 24 ||
krīḍanena prayojyā sā guruvākyapratikramaiḥ ||
kṛtākāryasya rājendra vrīḍā nāmeha jāyate ||25||
adhomukhena sā kāryā mūle svīkaraṇena ca ||
jñeyā capalatā rājanna vicāryā kriyāvatā || 26 ||
ayameva prayogasya tathaiva samudāhṛtaḥ || |
manorathānāṃ lābhe tu harṣo nāmeha jāyate ||27||
sukhaprasādaromāñcaprasvedairdarśayed budhaḥ ||
sambhramājjāyate rājannāvega iti sarvadā ||28||
āḥ śabdaiḥ sahasānarthā darśanīyā budhaistu te ||
viṣādasya samutpattiḥ kāryanāśena jāyate || 09 ||
nidrāniḥśvasitavyājaiḥ sābhineyā tathā bhavet ||
utkaṇṭhākaraṇaiḥ sarvairautsukyaṃ nāma jāyate || 3.31.30 ||
ālasyagurugātratve dhyānenābhinavaiśca tat ||
rātrijāgaraṇālasyamadairnidreha jāyate || 31 ||
netrābhimardanātkāryā jṛmbhaṇā gātragauravāt ||
apasmārastu bhavati devādīnāṃ prakopataḥ || 32 ||
niḥsaṃjñatvaṃ samūrddhānaṃ tasyehābhinayo bhavet ||
nidrāsamarthaṃ suptatvaṃ svapanaṃ tatra yojayet || 33 ||
nidrocchedāddhi bodhantu jṛmbhaṇaṃ tatra yojayet ||
vidyāśauryadhanādiryairamarṣo nāma jāyate || 34 ||
śirasaḥ kampanaistasya kāryastvabhinayo budhaiḥ ||
dhārṣṭyajaihmayādisambhūto hyavahitthobhayātmakaḥ || 32||
sarvāṃgagopanātkāryo'bhinayo nartanasya ca ||
cauryādijātamarthasya glāniḥ kāryā prayoktṛbhiḥ || 36||
vātaprāyastathonmādo yathā jñeyo viyogajaḥ ||
asacceṣṭāpralāpena tasyābhinayakāraṇam|| || 37 ||
vyādhibhirvā prahārairvā maraṇaṃ nāma jāyate ||
kartavyobhinayastasya mūḍhendriyaviceṣṭanaiḥ || 38 ||
mahābhairavanādādyaistrāsaḥ samupajāyate ||
saṃkṣiptāṅgaprakampādyaistasya tvabhinayo bhavet || 39 ||
vicāraṇārthe saṃdehaḥ kāryo bhūkṣepakampanaiḥ ||
krodho'parādhādbhavati krodhābhinayadarśanaḥ || 3.31.40||
bhayoparādhajo jñeyo dṛgbhayābhinayātmakaḥ ||
manorathāptijo harṣo romāñcena pradarśayet || 41 ||
bhītakrodhaśca ubhaye romāñcasya samudbhavaḥ ||
svarabhedo bhayādraukṣyātsvarabhedena darśayet || 42 ||
śokānandasamudbhūtamaśru syādrodanena tu ||
varṇāvaratvādvijñeyaṃ vaivarṇyaṃ nāṭyayoktṛbhiḥ ||43||
vaivarṇyaṃ nābhinetavyaṃ prayatnāttaddhi duṣkaram ||
glāniḥ śaṅkābhyasūyā ca śramaścapalatā tathā || 44 ||
romāñcaharṣe nidrā ca unmādo mada eva ca||
svedaścaiva vahitthaśca bhāvā hāsyarase smṛtāḥ || 45 ||
ālasyogryajugupsābhirbhāvā hi parivarjitāḥ ||
udbhāvayanti śṛṅgāraṃ rasamāsvādyasaṃjñakam || 46 ||
vedasyaiva ca cintā ca dainyaglānyaśrupātanam ||
jaḍatā maraṇaṃ caiva vyādhiśca karuṇārase || 47 ||
asammohastathonmādo viṣādo mada eva ca ||
mṛtyurvyādhirbhayaṃ caiva bhāvā bībhatsasaṃśrayāḥ || 48 ||
stambhaḥ svedaśca romāñco jugupsā vismayastathā ||
pravego jaḍatā harṣo'sūyā daivādbhute rase || || 49 ||
svedaśca vepathuścaiva romāñco gadgadastathā ||
matiścaiva tathogratvamamarṣo mada eva ca || 3.31.50 ||
romāñcaḥ svarabhedaśca krodho'sūyā dhṛtistathā ||
garvaścaiva vitarkaśca vīre bhāvā bhavanti vai||51||
ye cānye sāttvikā bhāvā nānābhinayayonijāḥ ||
raseṣveteṣu te sarve vijñeyā nāṭyakovidaiḥ ||52||
bahūnāṃ samavetānāṃ rūpaṃ yasya bhavedbahu ||
sa mantavyo rasaḥ sthāyī śeṣāḥ sañcāriṇaḥ smṛtāḥ || 53 ||
śṛṅgāraṃ trividhaṃ vindyādvā nepathyakriyātmakam ||
aṅganepathyabhāvaśca hāsyaraudrau prakīrtitau ||54||
dharmārthabandhughātena karuṇastrividhaḥ smṛtaḥ ||
yuddhavīraṃ dayāvīraṃ dānavīraṃ tathaiva ca || 55 ||
rasaṃ vīramiti prāhustajjñāstrividhameva ca ||
vyājatrāsāparādhaiśca tridhā vidyādbhayānakam || 56 ||
na hyekarasajaṃ kāvyaṃ kiñcidasti prayogajam ||
bhāvo vāpi raso vāpi pravṛttirvṛttireva ca ||57||
ekonapañcāśadime samagrā bhāvavyavasthā gaditā mayā te ||
yuktyā ca ye yatra rase niyuktāḥ proktaṃ tadapyatra narendracandra || 58||
iti śrīviṣṇudharmottare tṛtīyakhaṇḍe mārkaṇḍeyavajrasaṃvāde ekonapañcāśadbhāvanirūpaṇo nāmaikatriṃśattamo'dhyāyaḥ ||31||
<3.32>
śrīmārkaṇḍeya uvāca ||
athāto mudrāhastān vyākhyāsyāmaḥ ||
vāsasya prasāritāṅguṣṭhasyopari yadā kubjatarjanī bhavati tadoṅkāraḥ ||
karamadhya gatāsu sarvāsvaṃgulīṣu kramātkubjīkṛtāsvaṅguṣṭhādyāsu a i u e o prasāritāsu a i u ū ai o aṃ mukulaḥ aḥ mukulaviśeṣaḥ aṅguṣṭhamūlagatayā parahastatarjanyā kramātkavargaḥ|
sarvādhaḥparaṃ rekhāgatayā tavargaḥ |
tadupari bavaśatayaiḥ pavargaḥ|
tarjanyādyastu prathamaparvagatayā pavargaḥ|
agreṣu śavargaḥ|
madhyamā nakhasparśe ra kṣa iti |
a vāsudevaḥ |
ā saṅkarṣaṇaḥ| aṃ pradyumnaḥ |
aḥ aniruddhaḥ |
añjalipuruṣaḥ karayoḥ parasparapṛṣṭhalagnayoḥ |
kanīyasyā kanīyasī ceti, tarjanyā tarjanī aṅguṣṭhenāṅguṣṭha iti tārkṣyamudrā | patākaḥ kuñcitāgrastālaḥ tiryakprasāritāṅguṣṭho makaraḥ|
kuñcitāṅgulirardhacandraḥ |

saṃhatāṅguṣṭhaḥ śaṃkhaḥ |
aḥ kārau saṃhatau spaṣṭāṅgulau padmaḥ ||
kubjāṅguṣṭho lakṣmīḥ |
makaraḥ prasāritāṅguliḥ |
śeṣastadupari śikhare bhogaśamanaḥ |
aḥ kāra eva prasāritamadhyamāṅgulirga ūuḥ |
tatraiva madhyamāṅguliḥ śikharagṛhītā garuḍavāhanam |
madhyamānāmikayoraṅguṣṭhayoścāgrayoge cakraḥ |

sarvāsumukulitāsu prasārite madhyamā tarjanī naranārāyaṇau |
tatraiva prasāritāgranāsikā guṇāḥ brahmaviṣṇumaheśvarāḥ |
vāmasya prasāritāṅgulestadupari tathāvidha eva dakṣiṇaḥ kapilaḥ |
catasropyaṃgulyaḥ prasāritastanmūle kuñcitāṅguṣṭhaścāntarātmā toyaḥ |
prasāritakani ṣṭhiko ṛgvedaḥ |
saivānāmikayā saha prasārito yajurvedaḥ ||

adhomukhaśchandovicitiḥ ||

<3.33>
mārkaṇḍeya uvāca||
uttānaṃ tu karaṃ kṛtvā caturāṃgulikuñcitam ||
aṃguṣṭhapārśvato yojyo bhasmamudrāprakārtitā ||1||
sā tiryagucchritāṃguliliṅgamudrā prakīrtitā ||
aṃgulyastrayaḥ sako'syāgrāṃguṣṭhaparisaṃcitāḥ || 2 ||
tarjanīṃ prasṛtāṃ kṛtvā jaṭāmudrā prakīrtitā ||
vetramudrā yadā tasyāṃ netrānte tarjanī bhaveta || 3 ||
bandhasthāṃ tu prakurvīta cāṃguṣṭhaṃ prasṛtaṃ tathā ||
uttānaṃ mūrdhni saṃyojyaṃ śaśāṅkaṃ tu vinirdiśet ||4 ||
aṃgulyaḥ prasṛtāḥ sarvā vartulāḥ śṛṅgarūpakāḥ ||
mūrdhni kṛtvā tu tāṃ mudrāṃ govṛṣaṃ tu vinirdiśet || 5 ||
tiryakkṛtvā tu taṃ hastaṃ sarvasaṅkucitāṃgulim ||
aṃguṣṭhaṃ madhyamāveṣṭya paryastaṃ tu vinirdiśet || 6 ||
kaniṣṭhikāṃ saṅkucitāmaṃguṣṭhopari vinyaset ||
kaniṣṭhānāmikāmadhye dakṣāṃguṣṭhaṃ prakīrtitam || 7 ||
uttānau tu karau kṛtvā anyonyāntaritāṃgulī ||
kaniṣṭhikānte cāṃguṣṭhamananteti prakīrtitā || 8 ||
uttānaṃ dakṣiṇaṃ hastaṃ tvadho vāmaṃ tathopari ||
karajānyonyasaṃyogāddharmasya parikīrtitā || 9 ||
sā caiva viparītā tu jñānasya parikīrtitam ||
kaniṣṭhike dve cāṃguṣṭhau yugmīkṛtvā prasārayet || 3.33.10 ||
vairāgyasya bhavenmudrā sarvakarmakarī śubhā ||
pṛṣṭhe pṛṣṭhaṃ tu saṃyojyaṃ karayorubhayorapi || 11 ||
kanīnyaṃguṣṭhayogena bhavatyaiśvaryameva ca ||
padmākāro bhaveddhastaḥ kaniṣṭhātarjanīyutaḥ || 12 ||
catasra ucchritāḥ kṛtvā pṛṣṭhenyonyaṃ tu yojayet ||
aṃguṣṭhāvekataḥ kṛtvā praṇālaṃ caiva kārayet || 13 ||
sakalasya bhavenmudrā duṣṭānāṃ śamanī śubhā ||
trayaḥ saṅkucitā yatra kaniṣṭhāṃguṣṭhakekalāḥ || 14 ||
uttānaṃ dakṣiṇāṃguṣṭhaṃ vāmāṃguṣṭhena veṣṭayet ||
dakṣiṇāṃgulibhiścātra vāmāṃguṣṭhaṃ tu veṣṭayet || 15 ||
vāmasyāṃgulibhiścātra tvaṃgulī pariveṣṭayet ||
tarjanyau kuñcitau kṛtvā mudrā niṣkalarūpiṇī ||16||
uttānau tu karau kṛtvā aṃguṣṭhāṃgulikuñcitau ||
karamadhyasthitā hyetā nakhām̐stāsāṃ tu gopayet || 17 ||
pṛṣṭhaṃ pṛṣṭhe tu saṃyojya tvaṃguṣṭhau vinyasetsamam ||
mālākāraṃ tataḥ kṛtvā devyā mudrā prakīrtitā || 18 ||
aṃguṣṭhoṃguṣṭhake yojyaḥ kaniṣṭhāyāṃ kaniṣṭhikā ||
karamadhye tu tāṃ kṛtvā ṣaḍābhyāmekataḥ kuru || 19 ||
sakucya tu ṣaḍetāni śaktyākāraṃ tu kārayet ||
skaṃdasya tu smṛtā mudrā abhedyā tridaśairapi || 3.33.20 ||
adhomukhaṃ karaṃ kṛtvā tisroṃgulyastu kuñcitāḥ ||
tritālā madhyamā tatra kiṃcitsaṅkucitā bhavet || 21 ||
tarjanyāṃ yojayāṃguṣṭhe dantākāraṃ tu darśayeta ||
vighnarājasya mudraiṣā sarvavighnapraṇāśinī || 22 ||
uttānau prasṛtau hastau tarjanyaṃguṣṭhasaṃvṛtau ||
asya vāmau tu saṃyojya śakramudrā prakīrtitā || 23 ||
saṃkocya karajāṃ madhyāṃ hastayorubhayorapi ||
prasṛtāṃguṣṭhamuttānāṃ yojyā mudrā hutāśane || 24 ||
adhomukhaṃ karaṃ kṛtvā yasya syātkuṃcitāṃguliḥ||
virūpākṣasya mudraiṣā bhavenmadhyā prasāritā || 25 ||
uttāne prasṛtāṃgulyaḥ kare bhavati vāruṇī ||
tāmeva tu yadā tiryaktadā bhavati mārutī || 26||
nikuṃcitāśca tāḥ sarvāḥ kauberī tu prakīrtitā ||
madhye prasāritāstisro mudreśānī prakīrtitā || 27 ||
brahmajā padmamudrā syādbhogo'naṃtasya kīrtitā ||
vāmāsaktasya vāmāṃ tu dakṣiṇānāmapakrame || 28 ||
tayoragrāṇi gṛhṇīta tarjanyagradvayena tu ||
saṃkocayetṣaḍetāni svāṃguṣṭhe tarjanīṃ nyaset || 29 ||
vajra eṣaḥ samākhyātaḥ śaktiḥ pūrvamudāhṛtā ||
karau bāhudvayaṃ caiva daṇḍamudrā prasāritā || 3.33.30 ||
kaniṣṭhānāmike dvedve antaryāṃguṣṭhakuṃcitau ||
catvāraḥ prasṛtā yugmaḥ khaḍgamudrā prakīrtitā || 31 ||
vartulāṃ tarjanīṃ kṛtvā aṃguṣṭhopari vinyaset ||
dakṣiṇaṃ tarjanī caiva tasyāsye tu niyojayet || 32 ||
dakṣiṇaṃ kuṃcitaṃ kṛtvā tisro vāmāḥ prasāritāḥ ||
muṣṭiṃ kṛtvā tu vāmena tvaṃguṣṭhaṃ tu samucchrayeta || 33 ||
aṃguṣṭhena tu saṃgṛhya patākā prasṛtā karā ||
tiryakkaraṃ tu kṛtvā vai saṅkucya caturaṃgulīḥ || 34 ||
aṃguṣṭhaṃ prasṛtaṃ kṛtvā gadāmudrā tathā parā ||
anyonyaṃ yojayeddhastau kaniṣṭhāṃguṣṭhayojitau || 35 ||
tadeva yugmaṃ prasṛtā śūlamudrā prakīrtitā ||
nityaṃ prasārayeddhastaṃ svalpākāraṃ tu yojayet || 36 ||
madhyamekanyaseddvedve prasṛtāgre niyojayet ||
tarjanīdvayaṃ saṃkucya madhyamāpṛṣṭhasaṃsthitā || 37 ||
dravyarūpā smṛtā hyeṣā ātmabhāve niyojayet ||
uttānau tu karau kṛtvā ṣaḍeteṃgulayaḥ smṛtāḥ || 38 ||
samānāḥ susamānāśca yojitā vaiṣṇavī bhavet ||
tarjanyau kuṃcite kṛtvā madhyamā pṛṣṭhato nyaset || 39 ||
kaniṣṭhopari cāṃguṣṭhau vṛtamudrā prakīrtitā ||
uttānaṃ vāmahastaṃ ca dakṣiṇaṃ cāpyadhomukham || 3.33.40 ||
madhyamānāmike dve dve saṃkucyāṃguṣṭhasaṃsthite ||
tarjanīkanyakā caiva madhyamābhyāṃ samākramet || 41 ||
dvitīyā dravyarūpī ca gandhamudrā prakīrtitā ||
uttānau tu karau kṛtvā kaniṣṭhānāmikā tathā || 42 ||
saṃkocayitvā catvāro madhyame dve prasārayet ||
aṃguṣṭhage ca tarjanyau puṣpamudrā prakīrtitā || 43 ||
uttānau tu karau kṛtvā ṣaḍaṃgulinikuṃcitau ||
prasṛte tarjanīṃ kṛtvā cāgramagre niyojayet || 44 ||
tayormūle tathāṃguṣṭhau dhūmamudrā prakīrtitā ||
tarjanyā tarjanī yatra kanīyasyā kanīyasī || 45 ||
catasra ucchritāḥ kṛtvā pṛṣṭhaṃ pṛṣṭhe niyojayet ||
aṃguṣṭhāvucchritau kṛtvā dīpamudrā prakīrtitā || 46 ||
ucchritāṃgulayaḥ sarvāḥ kanīyorubhayorapi ||
pṛṣṭhaṃ pṛṣṭhe tu saṃyojya aṃguṣṭhāgre niyojayet || 47 ||
naivedyamudrā kathitā phalakārakarā śubhā ||
aṃgulyaḥ saṃhatāḥ sarvāstathā saṃkucitāśca yāḥ || 48 ||
puṣpamudrā vinirdiṣṭā dvitīyā yadunandana ||
aṃguṣṭhau caikataḥ kṛtvā anāmā ca kanīyasī||49||
madhyame dve ca saṃkocya anāmā pṛṣṭhato nyaset ||
dattvā caiva tu tarjanyau madhyamāḥ parivinyaset || 3.33.50 ||
mahāmudrā bhavatyeṣā sarvadravyaprapūraṇī ||
anāmā madhyame dve tu anyonyāntarite kṛte || 51 ||
saṃyojya madhyame svāgraṃ tarjanīdvayameva ca ||
kaniṣṭhādye tathā dvedve svāgre nāmni niyojayet || 52 ||
ardhasthitau tathāṃguṣṭhau amṛtākhyā prakīrtitā ||
caṇḍīśamudrā kathitā añjaliḥ kubjatarjanī || || 53 ||
astramudrā bhavedrājan padmaḥ syātsaṃhatāñjaliḥ ||
dakṣiṇena tu hastena muṣṭiṃ baddhvā prayatnataḥ || 54 ||
aḍuṣṭhamunnataṃ kṛtvā vāmāṅguṣṭhe tu nikṣipet ||
dakṣiṇāṃ ca tathā muṣṭiṃ vāmāṅgulyā tu veṣṭayet || 55 ||
sadyojātasya mudraiṣā kathitā pāpanāśinī ||
iyameva viparyastā vāmadevasya kīrtitā || 56 ||
karasampuṭakaṃ kṛtvā vāmāṃ nāsāṃ tu kārayet ||
dakṣiṇe madhyamākuṃcya aṅguṣṭhau kuñcayettathā|| 57 ||
śūlākāraṃ tataḥ kṛtvā hadayaṃ parivartayet ||
aghorasya smṛtā mudrā sarvavighnavināśinī || 58 ||
uttānau tu karau kṛtvā anāmādvayakuñcitau ||
tasyāmupari cāṅguṣṭhau tarjanyau dve ca kuñcite || 59 ||
madhyānāme tu te dve tu kaniṣṭhāmekato nyaset ||
mudraiṣā vajrasaṃjñasya sarvaduṣṭanivāraṇī || 3.33.60 ||
kaniṣṭhānāmike dvedve antarāntarayojite ||
ākuñcyāntarite yojye śeṣe caiva prasārite || 61 ||
īśānasya bhavenmudrā sarvakarmakarī śubhā ||
tarjanyau kuñcite kṛtvā tathaiva ca kanīyasī || 62||
adhomukhāṃ spṛṣṭanakhāṃ sthitāṃ madhye karasya tu ||
catasraścocchritāḥ pṛṣṭhe tvaṅguṣṭhāvekataḥ kuru || 63 ||
nālavyavasitau dvau tu mudrā vyomnaḥ prakīrtitā ||
muṣṭiṃ baddhvā tu vāmena tarjanīṃ samprasārayet || 64 ||
grāhyā dakṣiṇamuṣṭyā tu mudrā sarvātmanā bhavet ||
vāmamuṣṭau tathāṅguṣṭhaṃ dakṣiṇaṃ prakṣipedbudhaḥ || 65 ||
karajā pṛṣṭhato yojyā mudraiṣā śivasaṃjñitā||
aṅgulyontarhitāḥ sarvā vartulā garbhasaṃsthitā || 66 ||
prasṛtā madhyamā yuktā śikhāmudrā prakīrtitā ||
adhomukhāstu vai tisraḥ tiryaktāśca susaṃsthitāḥ || 67 ||
kanīyasyupari tvāsāṃ tāśca sarvā adhomukhāḥ ||
tāsāmupari cāṅguṣṭhe dakṣiṇaṃ samprayojayeta || 68 ||
vāme dharmajñamudraiṣā kathitā lāṅgalasya tu ||
bandhe tvanāmikā tvasminmudrā hyastrasya kīrtitā || 69 ||
uttānau tu karau kṛtvā ubhāvekatra yojayeta ||
karajāstu smṛtāḥ sarvā mudrā gāyatrasaṃjñitāḥ || 3.33.70 ||
muṣṭau prasārayedekāmaṃgulīṃ tu kramānnṛpa ||
tarjanyantāṃ kaniṣṭhādyāṃ śaktimudrā prakīrtitā || 71 ||
hṛdi nyasto'ñjalirjñeyo namaskāro narādhipa ||
kapitthayajanīṃ gṛhya śikhareṇa dhvajo bhaveta || 72 ||
aṅguṣṭhe yojya tarjanyau hastottānau smṛtāṅgulī ||
maṇibandhau nipīḍyaiva śaśikarṇī bhavennṛpa || 73 ||
sampuṭīkṛtya hastau dvau kiñcitsaṅkucitāṅguliḥ ||
mukulā tu samākhyātā paṅkajaṃ prasṛtaiva sā ||74 ||
uttāno tu sṛtau hastau kṛtvāṅguṣṭhau tu kuñcitau ||
madhyasaṃsthau karau kṛtvā āvāhanyāstu lakṣaṇam || 75 ||
aṅguṣṭhau kuñcitau dvau tu svakīyāṅguliveṣṭitau ||
ubhau cābhimukhau hastau yojayitvā tu riṣṭrarā || 76 ||
uttānaṃ dakṣiṇāṅguṣṭhaṃ vāmāṅguṣṭhena veṣṭayet ||
bhrāmayedvartulāṅguṣṭhā mudrā proktā visarjane || 77 ||
vitastidvayasaṃyogādbhadramudrā prakīrtitā ||
muṣṭiḥ pramārjitā jñeyā liṅgamudrā tathaiva ca || 78||
sandaṣṭe mārjitāvoṣṭhau tīrṇamudrā prakīrtitā ||
prasāritau bhujau kṛtvā bhujāgrau kuñcitau tathā || 79 ||
agrāgrau kuñcitāṃśena karapallavasannibhau ||
tarjanīṃ kuñcitāṃ kṛtvā aṅguṣṭhopari vinyaset || 3.33.80 ||
nikubjau bāhudaṇḍau tu badhnīyācca pṛthakpṛthaka ||
kairiṇī nāma mudraiṣā kathitā sūryadevatā || 81 ||
padmākārau karau kṛtvā saṃśliṣṭau grathitāṅgulīḥ ||
aṅgulyā kārayettasminviśvamudreti kīrtitā || 82 ||
sammukhau tu karau kṛtvā saṃśliṣṭau grathitāṅgulīḥ ||
kaniṣṭhāmadhyame yojyau tarjanyau madhyame tathā || 83 ||
hṛdi mūrdhni śikhābandhe mudreyaṃ vyomasaṃjñitā ||
muṣṭibandhocchritāṃ kṛtvā madhyahastasya tarjanīm || 84 ||
tālaśabdakṛtāsau tu mudrā cāsrasya kīrtitā ||
madhyamā tarjanī caiva savyahastasya cocchrite || 85 ||
kaniṣṭhānāmike kubje sāṅguṣṭhenaiva saṃjñitā ||
netrapradarśitā jñeyā govṛṣāgamane tathā || 86 ||
uttānau tu karau kṛtvā sarvāṅgulyo vikuñjitāḥ ||
kṛtvā copari cāṅguṣṭhaṃ cālayeta punaḥ punaḥ || 87 ||
sarvāsāmeva śaktīnāṃ mudraiṣā samprakīrtitā ||
dīptā sūkṣmā jayā bhadrā vibhūtirvimalā tathā ||88 ||
amoghā vidyutā caiva navamā sarvatomukhī ||
iti nāmāni śaktīnāṃ sarahasyāni nirdiśet || 89 ||
sammukhau tu karau kṛtvā śliṣṭau caiva prasāritau ||
iyaṃ mudrā namaskāre ravi sānnidhyakāriṇī || 3.33.90 ||
anāmikāyāḥ sandeśo ravimudrā prakīrtitā ||
somamudrā madhyamayā bhīmamudrā hyanāmayā || 91 ||
kanīyasyā budhe mudrā unmūle jīvasaṃjñitā ||
anāmikāmūlagā tu śukramudrā prakīrtitā||92 ||
madhyamāmūlagāṃguṣṭhā śanimudrā prakīrtitā ||
tarjanīmūlagāṃguṣṭhe rāhu mudrā prakīrtitā || 93 ||
tarjanī calanājjñeyā ketumudrā narādhipa ||
vāmahastena śikharāḥ krodhamudrā prakīrtitā || 94 ||
sampuṭau tu karau kṛtvā vāmocchrāyāṃ narādhipa ||
eṣā varāhamudrā tu sarvakarmakarī śubhā || 95 ||
vāmena veṣṭitau hastau dakṣiṇā bhairavī bhavet ||
hastāvadho mukhau yatra dṛṣṭiścādhastathā bhavet || 96 ||
pātālabhañjinī nāma mudraiṣā kathitā nṛpa ||
kapitthānāmikā dīrghāstambhanī tu nigadyate || 97 ||
karamadhyāttu saṃgrāhyamiva madhyena cāhatam ||
evaṃ madhyena saṃyuktā krodhinī parikīrtitā || 98 ||
sahamadhyā tu saṃgrāhyā khātamadhye dvidhāhatam ||
evamadhyena saṃyuktā vīryamudrā prakīrtitā || 99 ||
evamadhyena saṃyuktaṃ bhairavī cāparā bhavet ||
kṣetramadhyā tu saṃgrāhyā iramadhyena saṃyutam || 3.33.100 ||
evamadhyena ca krāntaṃ stambhanī cāparā bhavet ||
lasanmadhyā tu saṃgrāhyamekamadhyena cāhatam || 101 ||
pātālabhañjanī nāma mudreyamaparā bhavet ||
tarjanī madhyamā caiva tathā caiva tvanāmikām || 102 ||
madhyaparvasu saṃyuktāṃ pṛṣṭhataḥ kārayetkare ||
śaṅkhamudreyamuddiṣṭā pūrvamuktā tathāparā ||] 03 ||
prasārya sarvāścāṅgulyaḥ karapṛṣṭhe ca yojayet ||
cakramudrā bhavetteṣāṃ pūrvamuktā tathāparā || 104 ||
kaṭibhāgapradeśe tu muṣṭistu triśirā bhavet ||
gadā mudrā bhavatyeṣā sarvavighnapraṇāśinī || 105 ||
anyonyābhimukhau kṛtvā karau saṃveṣṭitāṅgulī ||
sahāṅguṣṭhena nirdiṣṭā mudrā kaustubhasaṃjñitā || 106 ||
saṃspṛśya vahniśikhirāṃ vāmacakriṇakaṃ spṛśet ||
iyaṃ mudrā vinirdiṣṭā vanamāleti pārthiva || 107 ||
anāmīnāśane dravye srajaṃ dravya karadvaye ||
jaṃghāmadhyagatau kṛtvā cibuko'rdhasamāyutau || 108 ||
mukhaṃ vivṛtakaṃ kuryājjvalajjihvā tu lelihā ||
eṣā mudrā mahāmudrā nārasiṃhīti kīrtitā||109||
aṅguṣṭhaṃ muṣṭinā grāhyaṃ hyamudrāṃ parikīrtitā ||
muṣṭiṃ baddhvaiva cāṅguṣṭhe tiryaggaṃ kārayecchiraḥ||3.33.110||
spaṣṭā tu tarjanīmuṣṭau śikhāmudrā prakīrtitā ||
tarjanīprāntasaṃlagne dve muṣṭī kavacaṃ bhavet || 111||
muṣṭī baddhvā tu hastau dve ubhayāṃguliyojitau||
gadāmudrā samākhyātā sarvakarmakarī śubhā || 1 12||
tarjanī tu tathāṅguṣṭhe pratiśliṣṭau tu kārayet ||
śeṣāḥ prasāritāṅgulyo netramudrā bhavennṛpa||113||
anāmyo'rdhelinau kṛtvā tarjanyānāṃ samaṃ śrayāt||
madhyamau connatau kṛtvā aṅguṣṭhau śleṣitau samau|||114||
eṣā mudā samākhyātā divyā ca śiraso nṛpa||
añjaliḥ pramadā mudrā kṣipraṃ devaprasāritam || 115||
vandane hṛdayā mattā pūrvadakṣiṇato'parā ||
ūrdhvo'ṅguṣṭho vāmamuṣṭirdakṣiṇo'ṅguṣṭhabandhanaḥ || 116||
saṃnyasya tasya cāṅguṣṭhau yasyā ūrdhvaṃ pratiṣṭhitam ||
vāsudevasya mudraiṣā baddhā syātpāpanāśinī || 117 ||
hastayorubhayoreva kaniṣṭhāṅguṣṭhagarbhakau ||
śeṣāḥ prasāritaḥ śliṣṭā mudrā saṅkarṣaṇasya tu || 118 ||
tarjanyāvardhataḥ śliṣṭau samāṅguṣṭhau tu śleṣitau ||
eṣā muṣṭiṃ tataḥ kṛtvā mudrā pradyumnasaṃjñikā || 119 ||
aṅgulyau valitau yatra sūrpākārau tu kārayet||
veṣṭyāṅguṣṭhāvadhaḥ kāryāvaniruddhe tu kārayeta || 3.33.120 ||
ityuddeśe tu te proktā mudrāhastā mayā nṛpa ||
darśayenmantrayuktānāṃ parāṃ siddhimabhīpsitām ||121||
yathāmantraṃ yathādevaṃ yathāvidhirataṃdritaḥ ||
mantreṣu devatāyuktā bahvyo mudrāḥ prakīrtitāḥ ||122||
tāsāṃ mantraṃ tu vijñeyaṃ niyogo nṛpa iṣyate || 123 ||
etāvaduktaṃ nṛpa nṛttaśāstraṃ samāsataḥ pārthivavaṃśamukhya ||
niḥśeṣametadgaditaṃ mahārthaṃ vistārataḥ sarvajagatpradhānam ||124||
iti śrīviṣṇudharmottare tṛtīyakhaṇḍe mārkaṇḍeyavajrasaṃvāde nṛttaśāstramudrā nāma trayastriṃśattamo'dhyāyaḥ || 33 ||
<3.34>
vajra uvāca ||
nṛttamutpāditaṃ kena ṛṣiṇā daivatena vā ||
etanme saṃśayaṃ chindhi tvaṃ hi sarvaviducyase || 1 ||
mārkaṇḍeya uvāca ||
ekārṇave purā loke naṣṭasthāvarajaṃgame ||
śeṣaparyaṅkaśayane supte madhuniṣūdane || 2 ||
saṃvāhyamānacaraṇe lakṣmyā yadukulodvaha ||
nābhyāṃ tasya samutpannaṃ padmaṃ padmanibhekṣaṇa || || 3 ||
tatra jātaḥ svayaṃ brahmā devaḥ śubhacaturmukhaḥ ||
daivaiḥ saha mahābhāga saśarīrairnarādhipa || 4 ||
padmodabindu sambhūtau tatraiva madhukaiṭabhau ||
rajastamomayau corau dānavau tau bhayānakau || 5 ||
tato jagṛhaturvedānbrahmaṇastau narādhipa ||
hata vedastato brahmā tuṣṭāva madhusūdanam || 6 ||
vedā me paramaṃ cakṣurvedā me paramaṃ balam ||
dānavāpahṛtā vedā andho jātosmi śatruhan || 7 ||
evamuktastadā viṣṇurbrahmaṇā puruṣottamaḥ ||
utthāya salilāttasmādbabhrāma salilāśaye || 8 ||
aṅgahāraiḥ sulalitaistathā padaparikramaiḥ ||
tathā bhramantantaṃ devaṃ dadarśāyatalocanā || 9 ||
atīva lalitaṃ lakṣmīrjātarāgā viśeṣataḥ ||
devopyaśvaśiro bhūtvā pātālatalagaḥ kṣaṇāta || 3.34.10 ||
sa daityāndṛṣṭavānvedāṃstyaktvāśvaśirasastanum ||
jaghāna tau mahākāyau dānavau madhukaiṭabhau || 11 ||
hatvā vedasahāyastu samprāpto brahmaṇo'ntike ||
dadau vedāṃstatastasmai devadevaḥ svayambhuve || 12 ||
dattvā vedāṃstataḥ prāha sṛṣṭiṃ kuru pitāmaha||
vedagrāmayuto brahmā sargaṃ cakre tataḥ prabhuḥ || 13 ||
śeṣasyāṅkagataṃ devaṃ lakṣmīḥ papraccha pārthiva||
lakṣmyuvāca ||
devadeva jagannātha śaṅkhacakragadādhara || 14 ||
parikramaṃ mayā toye dṛṣṭaḥ salalitaḥ prabho ||
atīva ramaṇīyāṅga kiṃ tatprabrūhi me prabho||15||
śrībhagavānuvāca||
nṛttamutpāditaṃ hyetanmayā padmanibhekṣaṇe||
aṅgahāraiḥ sakaraṇaiḥ saṃyuktaṃ saparikramaiḥ||16||
nṛttenārādhayiṣyanti bhaktimantastu māṃ śubhe ||
trailokyasyānukaraṇaṃ nṛtte devi pratiṣṭhitam || 17 ||
etāvaduktvā tāṃ devo brahmāṇaṃ vākyamavravīta ||
gṛhāṇa nṛttaṃ dharmajña lakṣyalakṣaṇasaṃyutam || 18 ||
etāvaduktvā brahmāṇaṃ grāhayāmāsa keśavaḥ ||
tadgṛhītvā tato brahmā dadau rudrāya vedhase ||
gṛhītvā tacca rudropi toṣayāmāsa keśavam ||
tena nṛttena satataṃ deveśaṃ bhaktavatsalam || 3.34.20 ||
evamutpāditaṃ nṛttaṃ vāsudevena pārthiva ||
ekārṇave purā loke naṣṭasthāvarajaṅgame || 21 ||
tataḥ prabhṛti deveśaḥ śaṅkaraḥ śaṅkaro nṛṇām ||
nṛttenārādhayannāste devaṃ cakragadādharam || 22 ||
nṛtteśvaratvaṃ cāvāpa tuṣṭāva madhusūdanam ||
so'pi tuṣyati nṛttena samyagārādhito haraḥ || 22 ||
anye ca devāstuṣyanti samyaṅ nṛttena toṣitāḥ ||
āpyāyanaṃ paraṃ hyetatkathitaṃ tu divaukasām ||24||
etadeva hi devatvaṃ dīvyatāṃ satataṃ divi ||
puṣpanaivedyadānebhyo nṛttadānaṃ viśiṣyate|| 25 ||
svayaṃ nṛttena yaḥ kuryāddevadevasya pūjanam ||
viśeṣeṇa mahābhāga tasya tuṣyati keśavaḥ || 26 ||
nṛttaṃ gītaṃ tathā vādyaṃ dattvā devāya viṣṇave ||
sarvakāmasamṛddhasya yajñasya phalamaśnute || 27 ||
nṛttena vṛttiṃ yaḥ kuryātsa tu varjyaḥ prayatnataḥ ||
kuśīlavādyairyaḥ kuryānnṛttavikrayakārakaḥ || 28 ||
devatārādhanaṃ kuryādyastu nṛttena dharmavit ||
sa sarvakāmānāpnoti mokṣopāyaṃ ca vindati || 29 ||
dhanyaṃ yaśasyamāyuṣyaṃ svargalokapradaṃ tathā ||
īśvarāṇāṃ vilāsaṃ tu cārtānāṃ duḥkhanāśanam || 3.34.30 ||
mūḍhānāmupadeśaṃ tatstrīṇāṃ saubhāgyavardhanam ||
śāntikaṃ pauṣṭikaṃ kāmyaṃ vāsudevena nirmitam ||31||
etāvaduktaṃ tava nṛttaśāstraṃ samāsato lokahitāya rājana ||
nṛttena yatnaḥ puruṣeṇa kāryo lokadvayañjetumabhīpsatā vai || 32 ||
iti śrīviṣṇudharmottare tṛtīyakhaṇḍe mārkaṇḍeyavajrasaṃvāde nṛttaśāstravarṇano nāma catustriṃśattamo'dhyāyaḥ||34||
<3.35>
mārkaṇḍeya uvāca ||
ataḥ paraṃ pravakṣyāmi citrasūtraṃ tavānagha||
urvaśīṃ sṛjataḥ pūrve citrasūtraṃ nṛpātmaja||1||
nārāyaṇena muninā lokānāṃ hitakāmyayā||
prāptānāṃ vañcanārthāya devastrīṇāṃ mahāmuniḥ || 2 ||
sahakārarasaṃ gṛhya urvyāṃ cakre varastriyam ||
citreṇa sā tato jātā rūpayuktā varāpsarāḥ || 3 ||
yāṃ dṛṣṭvā vrīḍitāḥ sarvā jagmustā devayoṣitaḥ ||
evaṃ mahāmuniḥ kṛtvā citraṃ lakṣaṇasaṃyutam || 4 ||
grāhayāmāsa sa tadā viśvakarmāṇamacyutam ||
yathā nṛtte tathā citre trailokyānukṛtiḥ smṛtā || 5 ||
dṛṣṭayaśca tathā bhāvā aṅgopāṅgāni sarvaśaḥ ||
karāśca ye mahānṛtte pūrvoktā nṛpasattama || 6 ||
ta eva citre vijñeyā nṛttaṃ citraṃ paraṃ matam ||
nṛtte pramāṇaṃ yenoktaṃ tatpravakṣyāmyataḥ śṛṇu || 7 ||
haṃso bhadro'tha mālavyo rucakaḥ śaśakastathā ||
vijñeyāḥ puruṣāḥ pañca teṣāṃ vakṣyāmi lakṣaṇam || 8 ||
ucchrāyāyāmatulyāste sarve jñeyāḥ pramāṇataḥ ||
svenevāṅgulamānena śatamaṣṭādhikaṃ bhavet ||9 ||
pramāṇaṃ nṛpa haṃsasya bhadrasya tu ṣaḍuttaram ||
caturbhiradhikaṃ jñeyaṃ mālavyasya tathā nṛpa || 3.35.10 ||
śataṃ ca rucakasyoktaṃ daśonaṃ śaśakasya ca ||
dvādaśāḍulavistārastāla ityabhidhīyate || 11 ||
aṅgulphantaccaturbhāgaṃ pādocchrāyaḥ prakīrtitaḥ ||
dvau ca tālau tathā jaṅghe pādatulye ca jānunī || 12 ||
jaṃghātulyau tathā corū nābhistālaṃ tu meḍhrataḥ ||
tāvacca nābhihadayaṃ hṛdayātkaṇṭha eva ca || 13 ||
kaṇṭhastālatribhāgaḥ syāttālaṃ ca vadanaṃ bhavet ||
tālaṣaḍbhāgamapyuktaṃ lalāṭopari mastakam || 14 ||
madhye meḍhraṃ tu vijñeyamiti dairghyaṃ prakīrtitam ||
tālaḥ proktaḥ karo rājanbāhū saptadaśāṅgulau || 15 ||
prabāhū tāvadevoktau vakṣasordhamathāṣṭakam|
etadāyāmataḥ proktaṃ mānaṃ haṃsasya pārthiva||16||
anenaivānusāreṇa śeṣāṇāmapi kalpayet||
āyāmapariṇāhābhyāṃ samāḥ sarve narādhipa||17||
sāmānyataste nṛpavarya mānaṃ proktaṃ mayā haṃsa narādhipasya ||
pratyaṅgamānaṃ ca mayocyamānaṃ samāsatastvaṃ śṛṇu rājasiṃha|| 18||
iti śrīviṣṇudhamottare tṛtīyakhaṇḍe mārkaṇḍeyavajrasaṃvāde citrasūtre āyāmocchrāyamānavarṇano nāma pañcatriṃśattamo'dhyāyaḥ || 35 ||
<3.36>
mārkaṇḍeya uvāca ||
atha pratyaṅgavibhāgo bhavati tatra dvādaśāṅgulaparīṇāho mūrdhā |
caturaṃgulocchrāyaṃ aṣṭāṅgulaṃ lalāṭam |
śaṅkhau caturaṅgulau dvyaṅgulocchrāyau pañcāṅgulau gaṇḍau caturaṅgulo hanuḥ |
dvyaṅgulau karṇau caturaṅgulocchrāyau |
karṇamadhyāṅgulaṃ tadrandhramudakam ||
pāliraniyamena | (karṇasya luṭikāpāliḥ)
nāsā caturaṅgulā agre dvyaṅgulocchrāyā trikāyāmā ca nāsāpuṭāvaṃgulīvistārau dviguṇadviguṇāyāmau |
nāsauṣṭhamadhyamardhāṅgulam |
oṣṭhaścāṅgulaḥ |
caturaṅgulāyāmamāsyam|
adharoṅgulaṃ dvyaṃgulaṃ cibukam |
catvāriṃśaddantāḥ |
teṣvaṣṭau daṃṣṭrāḥ |
adhārṅgulocchritā dantāḥ |
aṅguladvādaśabhāgikā daṃṣṭrā |
aṅgula vistṛtā tryaṅgulāyāme netre |
netratribhāgaṃ kṛṣṇamaṃḍalam|
pañcabhāgāstārā |
ardhāṅgulivistṛte tryāṅgulāyāme bhruvau |
tayordvyaṃgulamantaram |
caturaṅgulaṃ nnetrāntakarṇavivaram |
daśāṅgulavistṛtā grīvā|
ekaviṃśatyaṅgulapariṇāhā ṣoḍaśāṅgulastanāntaram |
ṣaḍaṅgulaṃ stanajatrvantaram |
ṣoḍaśa bāhumūlapariṇāhaḥ|
dvādaśāgre saptāṅgulaṃ karatalam |
paṃcāṅgulaṃ vistṛtaṃ paṃcāṅgulapramāṇā madhyamikā |
tatpūrvadalahīnā pradeśinī |
tattulyā cānāmikā |
tatparihīnā kaniṣṭhikā sarvāḥ samatribhāgaparvāḥ |
parvārdhā nakhāḥ |
tryaṅgulamaṅguṣṭhaṃ dviparvaḥ|
jaṭharapariṇāho dvācatvāriṃśāṃgulaḥ |
vedyapramāṇābhyāmaṅgulaṃ nābhiḥ |
kaṭiraṣṭādaśāṃgulā vipulā |
tatparidhiścatuścatvāriṃśat |
caturaṃgulavistṛtau vṛṣaṇau |
ṣaḍaṅgulaṃ tāvatpariṇāhaṃ meḍhram |
tanmadhyata ūrū caturaṅgulau |
taddviguṇapariṇāhāṅgulavipule jānunī |
tattriguṇapariṇāhaṃ jaṅghāgram |
pañcāgulaṃ caturdaśapariṇāhaṃ dvādaśa dīrghau ṣaḍaṅgulavistṛtau padau trikāyatāṅguṣṭhau | aṅguṣṭhatulyā pradeśinī |
tadaṣṭāṃgonāḥ śeṣāḥ |
aṅgulacaturbhāgahīno'ṅguṣṭhanakhaḥ |
tadardhapramāṇaṃ pradeśinyāḥ |
tadaṣṭabhāgaḥ śeṣāṇām |
sarvapādamaṅgulamaṣṭāṅgulotsedhaḥ |
tryaṅgule pārṣṇīḥ|
caturaṅgulocchrāyau iti haṃsapramāṇaṃ bhavati|
bhavaṃti cātra ||
śeṣāṇāṃ pārthivendrāṇāṃ mānaṃ yuktyā prakalpayeta||
anenaivānusāreṇa svamānasyānusārataḥ||1||
madhakṣaścandragaurastu nāgarājabhujo balī||
haṃsagāmī sumadhyaśca haṃsaśca sumukho bhavet || ||2||
romaruddhakapolastu gajagāmī mahāmatiḥ ||
vṛttopacitabāhustu bhadraḥ padmanibho bhavet ||3||
mudgaśyāmastu mālavyaḥ kṛśamadhyastanucchaviḥ ||
ājānubāhuḥ pīnāṃso dantighoṇo mahāhanuḥ || 4 ||
śaradgaurastu rucakaḥ kambugrīvo mahāmatiḥ ||
satyastu sikataścaiva balavāṃśca prakīrtitaḥ || 5 ||
raktaśyāmastu śaśakaḥ kiṃcitkarburakastathā ||
pūrṇagaṇḍaśca caturo madhvakṣaśca prakīrtitaḥ || 6 ||
iti śrīviṣṇudharmottare tṛtīyakhaṇḍe mārkaṇḍeyavajra saṃvāde citrasūtre pramāṇavarṇano nāma ṣaṭtriṃśattamamo'dhyāyaḥ || 36 ||
<3.37>
mārkaṇḍeya uvāca ||
aṅgapratyaṅgamānena yathā pañca narāḥ smṛtāḥ ||
striyaḥ pañca tathā jñeyāstā eva manujottama || 1 ||
puruṣasya samīpasthā kartavyā yoṣidīśvara ||
naraskandhapramāṇena kāryaikā sā yathāmiti || 2 ||
aṅgulī dvau narātkṣāmaṃ striyo madhyaṃ vidhīyate ||
adhikā ca kaṭiḥ kāryā tathaiva caturaḍulam || 3 ||
uraḥpramāṇataḥ kāryau stanau nṛpa manoharau ||
nṛpāśca sarve kartavyā mahāpuruṣalakṣaṇāḥ || 4 ||
jālapādakarāḥ kāryāstathā vai cakravartinaḥ ||
ūrṇeyaṃ ca bhuvormadhye teṣāṃ kāryaṃ tathā śubham || || 5 ||
rekhāśca karayoḥ kāryāstisro rājñāṃ manoharāḥ ||
śaśakṣatajasaṅkāśāḥ śastrā vai kṣāmakoṭakāḥ || 6 ||
tadaṅgabhaṅginaḥ sūkṣmā nijasnehābhyalaṃkṛtāḥ ||
ghanendranīlasadṛthāḥ keśāḥ kāryāstathā śubhāḥ || 7 ||
kuntalā dakṣiṇāvartāstaraṅgā siṃhakesarāḥ ||
vardharā jūṭaṭasarā ityetāḥ keśajātayaḥ || 8 ||
cāpākāraṃ bhavennetraṃ matsyodaramathāpi vā ||
netramutpalapatrābhaṃ padmapatranibhaṃ tathā || 9 ||
śānākṛtirmahārāja pañcamaṃ parikīrtitam ||
cāpākāraṃ bhavennetraṃ pramāṇena yathā striyaḥ || 3.37.10 ||
matsyodarākhyaṃ kathitaṃ tathā yavacatuṣṭayam ||
netramutpalapatrākhyaṃ pramāṇātṣaḍyavaṃ smṛtam || 11 ||
padmapatranibhaṃ netraṃ pramāṇena yavā nava ||
śaśākṛtiṃ ca vijñeyaṃ tathaiva ca yavā daśa || 12 ||
svamānā'ṅgulamānasya yavamānaṃ prakalpayet ||
cāpākāraṃ bhavennetraṃ yogabhūminirīkṣaṇāt || 13 ||
matsyodarākṛti kāryaṃ nārīṇāṃ kāmināṃ tathā ||
netramutpalapatrābhaṃ nirvikārasya śasyate || 14 ||
trastassa rudataścaiva padmapatranibhaṃ bhavet ||
kruddhasya vedanāntasya netraṃ śārākṛtirbhavet || 15 ||
ṛṣayaḥ pitaraścaiva devatāśca narādhipa ||
svaprabhābharaṇāḥ kāryā dyutimantastathaiva ca ||
muṣṇantastejasāṃ tejaḥ pareṣāṃ nṛpasattama || 16||
samyagvicārya nṛpatiḥ svadhiyā yathoktaṃ hyetatpramāṇamanurūpamaninditaṃ ca || sthānairanekakiraṇaiḥ sthirabhūmilambhaiḥ kāryaṃ tadeva sakumāramajihmarekham || 17 ||
iti śrīviṣṇudharmottare tṛtīyakhaṇḍe mā0 va0 saṃ0 sāmānyamānavarṇano nāma saptatriṃśattamo'dhyāyaḥ || 37 ||
<3.38>
|| mārkaṇḍeya uvāca||
netramutpalapatrābhaṃ raktāntaṃ kṛṣṇatārakama||
prasannaṃ dīrghapakṣmāntaṃ manojñaṃ mṛdu sattama| 1||
devatānāṃ karaṃ rājanprajāhitakaraṃ bhavet||
same gokṣīravarṇābhe snigdhe jihmāgrapakṣmale || 2 ||
prasanne padmanetrānte manojñe priyadarśane ||
kṛṣṇatāre viśāle ca nayane śrīsukhaprade || 3 ||
caturasraṃ susampūrṇaṃ prasannaṃ śubhalakṣaṇam ||
atrikoṇamavakraṃ ca adhikāramukhaṃ bhavet || 4 ||
dīrghamaṇḍalacakrāṇi trikoṇādīni yāni ca ||
varjyāni tāni devānāṃ prajāsu śivamicchatā || 5 ||
kāryā hasapramāṇena devā yadukulodvaha ||
teṣāṃ ca loma karttavyamakṣipakṣmasu ca bhruvoḥ || 6 ||
ataḥ śeṣeṣu gātreṣu devāḥ syurlomavarjitāḥ ||
dviraṣṭavarṣākārāśca tathā kāryā divaukasaḥ || 7 ||
prasannavadanā nityaṃ tathā ca smitadṛṣṭayaḥ ||
mukuṭaiḥ kuṇḍalairhāraiḥ keyūrairaṅgadaistathā || 8 ||
bhūṣitāste'tha kartavyāḥ śubhasragdāmadhāriṇaḥ||
śroṇīsūtreṇa mahatā pādābharaṇacāriṇā || 9 ||
yajñopavītavaṃtaśca sāvataṃsāstathaiva ca ||
jānvadholambinā kāryāḥ śobhinā kaṭivāsasā || 3.38.1 0||
vāme manujaśārdūla dakṣiṇaṃ jānu darśayet ||
aṃśukaṃ ca tathā kāryaṃ devatānāṃ manoharam || 11 ||
prabhā ca teṣāṃ kartavyā mūrdhni mūrdhnaḥ pramāṇataḥ ||
maṇḍalābhā mahārāja devatāto'nukāriṇī || 12 ||
ūrdhvā dṛṣṭiradhodṛṣṭistiryak teṣāṃ vivarjayeta ||
hīnādhikā vā dīnā vā kruddhā rūkṣā tathaiva ca || 13 ||
ūrdhvā tu maraṇāyoktā śokāyādhaḥ prakīrtitā ||
tiryagdhanavināśāya hīnā bhavati mṛtyave || 14 ||
adhikā śokajananī dīnā ca nṛpasattama ||
rūkṣā dhanakṣayāya syātkruddhā bhayavivardhinī || 15 ||
śātodarī na kartavyā na kāryā cādhikodarī ||
sakṣatā ca na kartavyā tathā yadukulodvaha || 16 ||
hīnādhikapramāṇā ca rūkṣavarṇā tathaiva ca ||
vivṛtena ca vaktreṇa natā ca yadunandana || 17 ||
pramāṇahīnairaṅgaiśca tvadhikairapi pārthiva ||
śātodarī kṣudbhayadā maraṇāyādhikodarī || 18 ||
sakṣatā maraṇāyoktā hīnā dhanavināśinī ||
adhikā śokajananī rūkṣavarṇā bhayapradā || 19 ||
vivṛttena ca vaktreṇa kulanāśakarī bhavet ||
prācyābhā dhananāśāya dakṣiṇena ca mṛtyave || 3.38.20 ||
paścimena sutaghnī ca codagbhayavivṛddhaye ||
pramāṇahīnā nāśāya adhikā deśanāśinī || 21 ||
aślakṣṇā maraṇāyoktā kruddhā rūpavināśinī ||
pramāṇahīnāṃ pratimāṃ tathā lakṣaṇavarjitām || 22 ||
āvāhitāñca viprendrairnāviśanti divaukasaḥ ||23||
āviśaṃti tu tāṃ nityaṃ piśācā daityadānavāḥ ||
tasmātsarvaprayatnena mānahīnāṃ vivarjayet || 24 ||
citralakṣaṇasaṃyuktaṃ praśastaṃ sarvamucyate ||
āyuṣyaṃ ca yaśasyaṃ ca dhanadhānyavivardhanam || 25 ||
tadeva lakṣaṇāpetaṃ dhanadhānyavināśanam ||
devā narendra karttavyāḥ śobhāvantaḥ sadaiva tu ||
mṛgeṃdravṛṣanāgānāṃ haṃsānāṃ gatibhiḥ samāḥ || 26 ||
salakṣaṇaṃ citramuśanti dhanyaṃ deśasya karturvasudhādhipasya ||
tasmātprayatnena salakṣaṇaṃ tatkāryaṃ narairyatnaparairyathāvata || 27 || ||
iti śrīviṣṇudharmottare tṛtīyakhaṇḍe mārkaṇḍeyavajrasaṃvāde pratimālakṣaṇavarṇanannāmāṣṭatriṃśattamo'dhyāyaḥ || 38 ||
<3.39>
mārkaṇḍeya uvāca ||

bāhuvakṣaḥkaṭitaṭasthitigu............... || 21 ||
dvikalaṃ dvikalaṃ jñātvā yathābhāgaṃ kṛṣīkṛtam ||

<3.40>
|| mārkaṇḍeya uvāca || ||
triprakāreṣṭikācūrṇaṃ tryaṃśaṃ kṣiptvā mṛdastataḥ ||

bilvajaṃ dvyaṃśamiśraṃ tatprakṣipya maṣakaṃ kaṣam||
vālukāṃśaṃ tato dadyātsvānurūpeṇa buddhimān||3||
tataḥ śakalatoyena plāvayetpicchilena tam||
pariklinnaṃ samagraṃ tanmāsamātraṃ nidhāpayet||4||
mārdavaṃ māsamātreṇa gatamuddhṛtya yatnataḥ ||
dadyātpralepaṃ nipuṇaḥ śuṣkaṃ kuḍye vimṛśya tu ||5 ||

api varṣaśatasyānte na praṇaśyettu karhicit || 9 ||

kartavyāścitravapuṣā vividhā maṇibhūmayaḥ || 3.40.10 ||
kuḍye śuṣke tithau śaste rūkṣe ca guṇasaṃyute ||
citrāyoge viśeṣeṇa śvetavāsā yatātmavān|| || 11 ||
brāhmaṇānpūjayitvā tu svasti vācya praṇamya ca ||

prāṅmukho devatādhyāyī citrakarma samācaret ||

ālikhya sthāpayedvidvānpramāṇe sthānake tathā ||

śyāmā gaurī tathā tasya cchaviḥ syāttāṃ pradarśayet ||
tasyāśca lakṣaṇaṃ proktaṃ prāptāyā nṛpa vistare || 15 ||
mūlaraṅgāḥ smṛtāḥ pañca śvetaḥ pīto vilomataḥ ||
kṛṣṇo nīlaśca rājendra śataśontarataḥ smṛtāḥ || 16||
pūrṇaraṅgavibhāgena bhāvakalpanayā tathā||
svabuddhyā kārayedraṅgāñśataśo'tha sahasraśaḥ || 17 ||
nīleṣvativyatikṛtiḥ pālāśa iti śasyate ||
sa śuddhaḥ śvetamiśraśca nīlābhyadhika eva ca || 18 ||
ekādhikaṃ ca bhavati cchavīnāmanurūpataḥ ||
śvetādhiko vā nyūno vā samāṃśaśceti sa tridhā ||19||
sa ekastambhanāyukto bahudhaivaṃ vikalpate ||
tena dūrvāṃkurā pītaḥ kapitthaharitaḥ śubhāḥ || 3.40.20 ||
mudgaśyāmaprakṛtayaḥ kartavyāśchavayo nṛpa ||
nīlaḥ pāṇḍurasampṛkto viraṅgaḥ so'pyanekadhā || 21 ||
anyonyābhyadhikaṃ nyūnaṃ samāṃśavaśakalpanā ||
tena nīlotpalanibhā māṣasacchāyasuprabhā || 22 ||
kriyate cchavayo ramyā yathāyogaviniścayāt ||
lākṣayā śvetayā yuktā lākṣārodhapinaddhayā|
raktā raktotpalaśyāmā cchavirbhavati śobhanā||
sāpi nānāvidhānanyānvarṇānvikurute bahūn ||24||

<3.41>
mārkaṇḍeya uvāca ||
satyaṃ ca vaiṇikaṃ caiva nāgaraṃ miśrameva ca ||

rekhā ca vartanā caiva bhūṣaṇaṃ varṇameva ca ||
vijñeyā manujaśreṣṭha citrakarmasu bhūṣaṇam || 3.41.10 ||

iti matvā tathā yatnaḥ kartavyaścitrakarmaṇi ||

iti śrīviṣṇudharmottare tṛ0 mā0 saṃ0 vartano nāmaikacatvāriṃśattamo'dhyāyaḥ || 41 ||
<3.42>
mārkaṇḍeya uvāca ||
yathā devastathā citre kartavyaḥ pṛthivīśvara ||
ekaikaṃ rūpake loma kartavyaṃ pṛthivīkṣitām || 1 ||
ṛṣayaśca sagandharvā daityāśca saha dānavāḥ ||
mantriṇaśca mahārāja saṃvatsarapurohitau || 2 ||
kāryā bhadrapramāṇena brāhmaṇāśca nareśvara ||
ṛṣayastatra kartavyā jaṭājūṭopaśobhitāḥ || 3 ||
kṛṣṇājinottarāsaṅgā durbalāstejasā yutāḥ ||
devatāścāpi gandharvā mukuṭena vivarjitāḥ || 4 ||
kartavyāste mahārāja śikharairupaśobhitāḥ ||
brahmavarcasvino viprāḥ śuklāmbaradharāstathā || 5 ||
mantriṇaśca mahārāja sāṃvatsarapurohitau ||
sarvālaṅkārasaṃyuktānevolbaṇavibhūṣaṇān || 6 ||
mukuṭena vihīnāṃstu soṣṇīṣānkārayecca tān ||
daityāśca dānavāścaiva kartavyā bhrukuṭīmukhāḥ || '7 ||
vartulākṣāstathā kāryā bhīmavaktrāstathaiva ca ||
teṣāmabhyuddhato veṣaḥ kartavyaḥ pṛthivīpate || 8 ||
rudrapramāṇāḥ kartavyāstathā vidyādharā nṛpa ||
sapatnīkāśca te kāryā mālyālaṅkāradhāriṇaḥ || 9 ||
khaḍgahastāśca te kāryā gagane vātha vā bhuvi ||
mālavyaparimāṇena kinnaroragarākṣasāḥ || 3.42.10 ||
rucakasya pramāṇena yakṣāḥ kāryā narādhipa ||
śaśakasya pramāṇena pradhānaṃ mānavaṃ likhet || 11 ||
piśācā vāmanāḥ kubjāḥ pramathāśca mahībhujaḥ ||
mānanniyamataḥ kāryaṃ rūpanniyamatastathā || 12||
svānurūpapramāṇāśca sarveṣāṃ yoṣitaḥ smṛtāḥ||
kinnarā dvividhāḥ proktā nṛvaktrā hayavigrahāḥ || 13 ||
nṛdehāścāśvavaktrāśca tathānye parikīrtitāḥ ||
aśvavaktrāstu kartavyāḥ sarvālaṅkāradhāriṇaḥ || 14 ||
gītavādyasamāyuktā dyutimantastathaiva ca ||
utkacā rākṣasāḥ kāryā vikalākṣā vibhīṣaṇāḥ || 15 ||
devakārāśca kartavyā nāgāḥ phaṇavirājitāḥ ||
sālaṃkārā smṛtāḥ sarve yakṣāste'bhihitā mayā || 16 ||
surāṇāṃ pramathāḥ kāryāḥ pramāṇena vivarjitāḥ ||
piśācāśca tathā kāryā pramāṇena vivarjitāḥ || 17 ||
nānāsattvamukhāḥ kāryā devatānāṃ tathā gaṇāḥ ||
nānāveśā mahārāja nānāyudhadharāstathā || 18 ||
nānākrīḍāprasaktāśca nānākarmakarāstathā ||
ekarūpāstu kartavyā vaiṣṇavānāṃ tathā gaṇāḥ || 19 ||
tatrāpi teṣāṃ kartavyā bhedāścatvāra eva ca ||
vāsudevasamāḥ kāryā vāsudevagaṇāḥ śubhāḥ || 3.42.20 ||
saṃkarṣaṇena sadṛśāstadgaṇāśca tathā smṛtāḥ ||
pradyumnenāniruddhena tadgaṇāḥ sadṛśāstathā || 21 ||
tatprabhāvāḥ smṛtāḥ sarve tadāyudhadharāstathā ||
nīlotpaladalaśyāmāścandraśubhrāstathaiva ca || 22 ||
tathā marakatākārā sindūrasadṛśaprabhāḥ ||
rucakasya tu mānena veśyāḥ kāryāstathā striyaḥ || 23 ||
veśyānāmuddhataṃ veśaṃ kāryaṃ śṛṅgārasammatam ||
mālavyāmānataḥ kāryā lajjāvatyaḥ kulastriyaḥ || 24 ||
nātyunnatena veśena sālaṃkārāstathaiva ca ||
daityadānavayakṣāṇāṃ rākṣasānāṃ tathaiva ca || 25 ||
rūpavatyastathā kāryāḥ patayo manujasattama ||
mātaraḥ svena rūpeṇa tathā kāryā narādhipa || 26 ||
piśācānāṃ ca patnyo'pi kāryāstadrūpasaṃyutā ||
vibhartṛkāstu kartavyā striyaḥ palitasaṃyutāḥ || 27 ||
śuklavastraparīdhānāḥ sarvālaṅkāravarjitāḥ ||
kubjā vāmanikā vṛddhā tathā rūpavatī bhavet || 28 ||
rājastrīṇāṃ parīvāre vṛddhaḥ syātkañcukī punaḥ ||
rucakasya tu mānena vaiśyamānaṃ vidhīyate || 29 ||
śaśakasya tu mānena śūdramānaṃ tathaiva ca ||
yathā jātyanurūpeṇa veṣeṇa manujeśvara || 3.42.30 ||
daityādiyoṣitāṃ kāryāḥ paricārastriyaḥ sadā ||
mahāśirā mahorasko mahānāso mahāhanuḥ || 31 ||
pīta skandhabhujagrīvaḥ parimāṇena cocchritaḥ ||
tritaraṅgalalāṭaśca vyomadṛṣṭirmahākaṭiḥ || 32 ||
dṛptaścitravidā kāryaḥ senāyāḥ patirūrjitaḥ || |
yodhāḥ kāryā mahārāja prāyaśo bhrukuṭīmukhāḥ || 33 ||
kiṃciduddhatavegāśca kāryāścoddhatadarśanāḥ ||
abhyudgatāśca kartavyā āyudhīyāḥ padātayaḥ || 34 ||
khaḍgacarmadharāḥ kāryāḥ karṇāṭakavapurdharāḥ ||
varabāṇadharāḥ kāryā nagnajaṅghāśca dhanvinaḥ || 35 ||
nātyuddhatena veṣeṇa sopānatkāstathaiva te ||
yathoktalakṣaṇāḥ kāryāḥ kuñjarāsturagādayaḥ || 36 ||
hastyārohāstu kartavyā muhuḥ śyāmāstu varṇataḥ ||
keśaiśca jūṭaṭamaraiḥ sālaṃkārāstathaiva ca| || 37 ||
udīcyaveśāḥ kartavyāsturagāṇāṃ tu sādinaḥ ||
uddhatena tu veṣeṇa kartavyā bandinastathā ||38 ||
sirādarśitakaṇṭhāśca tathaivonmukhadṛṣṭayaḥ ||
āhvānakāstu kartavyāḥ kapilāḥ kekarekṣaṇāḥ || 39 ||
kiṃciddānavasaṅkāśāḥ prāyaśo daṇḍapāṇayaḥ ||
na kekarānna kapilānyuddhe dvaṃdvānsamālikhet || 3.42.40 ||
nātyunnatena veṣeṇa na ca śāntena śasyate ||
pārśvabaddhena khaḍgena pratīhārastu daṇḍavān || 41 ||
saṃveṣṭitaśiraskāśca kartavyā vaṇijastathā ||
gāyanā nartakā ye vā vādyavādaviśuddhaye || 42 ||
uddhatena tu veṣeṇa kāryāste manujottama ||
āsannapalitā kāryāḥ svabhūṣaṇavibhūṣitāḥ || 43 ||
paurajānapadāḥ śreṣṭhāḥ śubhavastravibhūṣaṇāḥ ||
prasṛtapravaṇāḥ prahvāḥ svabhāvapriyadarśinaḥ || 44 |||
svakarmopaskaravyagraḥ kāryaḥ karmakaro janaḥ||
prāṃśavaḥ pīnagātrāśca pīnagrīvaśirodharāḥ || 45 ||
ugrāśca nīcakeśāśca mallāḥ kāryāstathoddhatāḥ ||
vṛṣāḥ kesariṇaścaiva yāścānyāḥ sattvajātayaḥ || 46 ||
yathābhūminiveśāste lokaṃ dṛṣṭvā narādhipa ||
etadrūpasamuddeśamadṛṣṭānāṃ taveritam || 47 |
dṛṣṭaṃ susadṛśaṃ kāryaṃ sarveṣāmaviśeṣataḥ ||
citre sādṛśyakaraṇaṃ pradhānaṃ parikīrtitam || 48 ||
buddhyā rūpaṃ yathāveśaṃ varṇaṃ ca manujottama ||
deśedeśe narāḥ kāryā yathāvattatsamudbhavāḥ ||49||
deśaṃ niyogaṃ sthānaṃ ca karma buddhvā ca yatnataḥ ||
āsanaṃ śayanaṃ yānaṃ veśaṃ kāryaṃ narādhipa || 3.42.50 ||
saritāṃ saśarīrāṇāṃ vāhanāni pradarśayet ||
pūrṇakumbhakarāḥ kāryāstathā nāmitajānavaḥ || 51 ||
śailānāṃ śikharaṃ mūrdhni darśayenmanujottama ||
dvīpānāṃ ca karaiḥ kāryaṃ tathā bhūmaṇḍalaṃ śubham || 92 ||
. .. ... ..........rājaṃstathā śikharapāṇayaḥ ||
ratnapātrakarāḥ kāryāḥ sāgarā manujottama || 53 ||
samudrāṇāṃ prabhāsthāne salilaṃ tu pradarśayet ||
āyudhānāṃ ca taccihnaṃ kiñcinmūrdhani darśayet || 54 ||
nidhīnāṃ darśayetkumbhaṃ śaṃkhaṃ śaṃkhasya darśayet ||
padmaṃ padmasya rājendra śeṣāṇāmanurūpataḥ || 55 ||
kāryasyāvayavāḥ kāryāḥ svaddehasadṛśāḥ pṛthak ||
divyānāṃ darśayeccihnamakṣamālāṃ ca pustakam || 56 ||
ataḥ paraṃ pravakṣyāmi rūpaṃ yadyasya dṛśyate ||
ākāśaṃ daśaryedvidvānvivarṇaṃ khagamākulam || 57 ||
tathaiva darśayedrājaṃstārakāmaṇḍitaṃ divam |
bhūmiṃ ca jāṅgalānūpamiśrāṃ svaiḥsvaistathā guṇaiḥ || 58 ||
parvataṃ tu śilājālaiḥ śikharairdhātubhirdrumaiḥ ||
nirjharairbhujagaiścaiva darśayennṛpasattama || 59 ||
vanaṃ nānāvidhairvṛkṣairvihaṅgaiḥ śvāpadaistathā ||
toyaṃ ca darśayedvidvānanantairmatsyakacchapaiḥ || 3.42.60 ||
padmākṣaiśca mahārāja tathānyairjalajairguṇaiḥ ||
devatāveśmabhiścitraiḥ prāsādāpaṇaveśmabhiḥ || 61 ||
nagaraṃ darśayedvidvān rājamārgaiśca śobhanaiḥ ||
vamatyā darśayedgrāmaṃ kiñcidudyānabhūṣitam || 62 ||
sarveṣāmatha durgāṇāṃ kartavyaṃ darśanaṃ tathā ||
svabhūmiviniveśena vaprāṭṭālakaparvataiḥ || 63 ||
paṇyayuktāstu kartavyāstathaivāpaṇabhūmayaḥ ||
ādhānabhūmiḥ kartavyā pānayuktā narākulā || 64 ||
uttarīyavihīnāṃśca dyūtasaktānpradarśayet||
jitāñśokasamāyuktānhṛṣṭāṃllabdhajayāṃstathā || 65 ||
caturaṅgabalopetāṃ praharadbhirnarairyutām ||
mṛtāvayavaraktāḍhyāṃ raṇabhūmiṃ pradarśayet || 66 ||
citākuṇapasaṃyuktaṃ śmaśānaṃ ca tathā nṛpa ||
yuktaṃ sabhārairuṣṭrādyairmārgaṃ sārthaṃ pradarśayet || 67 ||
sacandragrahanakṣatrāṃ tathā darśitalaukikām ||
āsannataskarāṃ rātriṃ darśayetsuptamānavām || 68
prāgrātre darśayettatra tathā caivābhisārikām ||
sāruṇo mlānadīpaśca pratyūṣo rutakukkuṭaḥ ||69||
karmavyagrajanaprāyaḥ kartavyo vānarastathā ||
dvijairniyamabhiryuktāṃ gāṃ sandhyāṃ pradarśayet || 3.42.70 ||
tamaso darśanaṃ kāryaṃ vāse saṃsarpakairnaraiḥ ||
kumudānāṃ vikāśe ca jyotsnāṃ candre pradarśayet ||71||
darśayetsarajasyaṃ ca śayyāṃ karṇotkarāvṛtām ||
sadvṛttamānavaprāyāṃ vṛṣṭiṃ vṛṣṭyāmpradarśayet||72||
prāṇināṃ kleśataptānāmādityena nidarśanam ||
vṛkṣairvasantajaiḥ phullaiḥ kokilāmadhupotkaṭaiḥ || 73 ||
prahṛṣṭanaranārīkaṃ vasantaṃ ca pradarśayet ||
klāntaiḥ kāryaṃ narairgrīṣmaṃ mṛgaiśchāyāgataistathā || 74 ||
mahiṣaiḥ paṅkamalinaistathā śuṣkajalāśayam ||
vihaṅgairdrumasaṃlīnaiḥ siṃhavyāghrairguhāgataiḥ || 75||
toyanamraghanairyuktaṃ sendracāpavibhūṣaṇaiḥ ||
vidyudvidyotanairyuktāṃ prāvṛṣaṃ darśayettathā || 76 ||
saphaladrumasaṃyuktāṃ pakvasasyāṃ vasundharām ||
sahasapadmasalilāṃ śaradaṃ tu tathā likhet || 77 ||
savāṣpasalilasthānaṃ tathā lūnavasundharam ||
sanīhāradigantaṃ ca hemantaṃ darśayedbudhaḥ || 78 ||
hṛṣṭavāyasamātaṅgaṃ śītārtajanasaṅkulam ||
śiśiraṃ tu likhedvidvānhimacchannadigantarama || 79 ||
vṛkṣāṇāṃ puṣpaphalataḥ prāṇināṃ madatastathā|| ||
ṛtūnāṃ darśanaṃ kāryaṃ lokāndṛṣṭvā narādhipa || 3.42.80 ||
rasabhāvāśca kartavyā yathāpūrvamudāhṛtāḥ ||
yathāyogaṃ tu yuñjīta nṛttābhihitamatra ca ||83 ||
śuṣkaṃ vartanayā vastu citraṃ tanmadhyamaṃ smṛtam ||
śuṣkārdramadhamaṃ proktaṃ cārdrameva tathottamam || 82 ||
yathādeyaṃ yathākālaṃ yathādeśaṃ yathāvayaḥ ||
kriyamāṇaṃ bhaveddhanyaṃ viparītamato'nyathā || 83 ||
itivicakṣaṇabuddhivikalpitaiḥ karaṇakāntivilāsarasādibhiḥ || 
likhitamīkṣaṇalocanamādarādbhavati citramamīpsitakāmadam || 84 ||
iti śrīviṣṇudharmottare tṛtīyakhaṇḍe mā0 va0 saṃ0 rūpanirmāṇaṃ nāma dvicatvāriṃśattamo'dhyāyaḥ || 42 ||
<3.43>
mārkaṇḍeya uvāca ||
śṛṅgārahāsakaruṇavīraraudrabhayānakāḥ ||
bībhatsādbhutaśāntāśca nava citrarasāḥ smṛtāḥ || 1 ||
tatra yatkāṃtilāvaṇyalekhāmādhuryasundaram ||
vidagdhavaśābharaṇaṃ śṛṅgāre tu rase bhavet || 2 ||
yatkujjavāmanaprāyamīṣadvikaṭadarśanam ||
vṛthā ca hastaṃ saṃkocya tatsyāddhāsyakaraṃ rase || 3 || 
yāñcāvirahāsantyāgavikrayavyasanādiṣu ||
anukampitakaṃ yatsyāllikhettatkaruṇārase ||4 ||
pāruṣyavikṛtikrodhaviṣastyarthā na dūṣaṇam ||
dīpraśastrābharaṇavatkṛtaṃ raudrarase bhavet || 5 ||
pratijñāgarbhaśauryādiṣvartheṣvau dāryadarśanam ||
sasmayaṃ sabhrukuṭivadvīraṃ vīrarase'dbhutam ||6||
duṣṭadurdarśanonmattahiṃsravyāpādakādi yata ||
tatsyādbhayānakarase prayoge citrakarmaṇaḥ ||7||
śmaśānagarhitaṃ ghātakaraṇaṃ sthānadāruṇam||
yaccitraṃ citravacchreṣṭhaṃ tadbībhatsarase bhaveta ||8||
yadā vinītaromāñcacintāṃ tārkṣyamukhānatam ||
pradarśayati cānyo'nyaṃ tadadbhutarasāśrayam ||9 || 
yadyatsaumyākṛtidhyānadhāraṇāsanabandhanam ||
tapasvijanabhūyiṣṭhaṃ tattu śānte rase bhavet || 3.43.10 ||
śṛṅgārahāsya śāntyākhyā lekhanīyā gṛheṣu te || 11 ||
paraśeṣā na kartavyā kadācidapi kasyacit ||
devaveśmani kartavyā rasāḥ sarve nṛpālaya || 12 ||
rājaveśmani no kāryā rājñāṃ vāsagṛheṣu te ||
sabhāveśmasu kartavyā rājñāṃ sarvarasā gṛhe || 13 ||
varjayitvā sabhāṃ rājño devaveśma tathaiva ca ||
yuddhaśmaśāna karuṇāmṛtaduḥkhārtakutsitān || 14 ||
amaṅgalyāṃśca na likhetkadācidapi veśmasu || 
nidhiśṛṃgānvṛṣānrājannidhihastānnatāṅgajān || 15 ||
nidhi vidyādharā rājanṛṣayo garuḍastathā ||
hanūmāṃśca sumaṅgalyā ye ca loke prakīrtitāḥ || 16 ||
likhitavyā mahārāja gṛheṣu satataṃ nṛṇām ||
citrakarma na kartavyamātmanā svagṛhe nṛpa || 17 ||
daurbalyaṃ sthūlarekhatvamavibhaktatvameva ca ||
varṇānāṃ saṅkaraścātra citradīpāḥ prakīrtitāḥ ||18 ||
|sthānapramāṇaṃ bhūlambho madhuratvaṃ vibhaktatā ||
sādṛśyaṃ kṣayavṛddhī ca guṇāṣṭakamidaṃ smṛtam || 19 ||
sthānahīnaṃ gatarasaṃ śunyadṛṣṭimalīmasam ||
cetanārahitaṃ vā syāttadaśastaṃ prakīrtitam || 3.43.20 ||
lasatīva ca bhūlambo bibhyatīva tathā nṛpa||
hasatīva ca mādhuryaṃ sajīva iva dṛśyate || 21 ||
saśvāsa iva yaccitraṃ taccitraṃ śubhalakṣaṇam ||
hīnāṅgaṃ malinaṃ śūnyaṃ baddhavyādhibhayākulaiḥ || 22 ||
vṛttaṃ prakīrṇakeśaiśca sumaṃgalyairvivarjayet ||
pratītaṃ ca likheddhīmānnāpratīteḥ kathaṃcana || 23 ||
śāstrajñaiḥ sukṛtairdakṣaiścitraṃ hi manujādhipa ||
śriyamāvahati kṣipramalakṣmīṃ cāpakarṣati || 24 ||
nirṇejayati cotkaṇṭhāṃ niruṇaddhyāgataṃ śubham ||
śuddhāṃ prathayati prītiṃ janayatyatulāmapi || 25 ||
duḥsvapnadarśanaṃ hanti prīṇāti gṛhadaivatam ||
na tu śūnyamivābhāti yatra citraṃ pratiṣṭhitam || 26 ||
..............dakarṇaṃ cāpyanalaṃkṛtam ||
śalyaviddhaṃ ca vṛddhaṃ ca yaḥ karoti sa citravit || 27 ||
taraṅgāgnikhikhādhūmaṃ vaijayantyasvarādikam ||
vāyugatyā likhedyastu vijñeyo mattacitravit || 28 ||
suptaṃ ca cetanāyuktaṃ mṛtaṃ caitanyavarjitam ||
nimnonnatavibhāgaṃ ca yaḥ karoti sa citravit || 29 ||
eteṣāṃ khalu sarveṣāmānulomyaṃ praśasyate ||
sammukhatvamathaiteṣāṃ citre yatnādvivarjayet || 3.43.30 ||
yathācitraṃ tathaivoktaṃ khātapūrvaṃ narādhipa ||
suvarṇarūpyatāmrādi tacca loheṣu darśayet || 31 ||
śilādāruṣu loheṣu pratimākaraṇaṃ bhavet||
anenaiva vidhānena yathā citramudāhṛtam||32||
tathānena vidhānena pustakarma vidhīyate ||
dvividhaṃ tacca kathitaṃ ghanaṃ suṣirameva ca||33||
ghanarlauṃhaiḥ śilābhiśca dārumṛdbhiḥ sadā bhavet ||
carmaṇā suṣiraḥ kāryo dārulohaistathaiva ca || 34 ||
deyo mṛttikayā lepaḥ puste carmakṛte dṛḍhaḥ ||
sūtre'nena vidhānena citravastre tathā likhet || 35 ||
etaduddeśamātreṇa yadā te kathitaṃ nṛpa ||

nṛte'pi noktaṃ taccitraṃ nātra yojyaṃ narādhipa || 37 ||
kalānāṃ pravaraṃ citraṃ dharmakāmārtha mokṣadam ||
