yathā narāṇāṃ pravaraḥ kṣitīśastathā kalānāmiha citrakalpaḥ || 39 ||
iti śrīviṣṇudharmottare tṛtīyakhaṇḍe mārkaṇḍeyavajrasaṃvāde śṛṅgārādibhāvakathanaṃ nāma tricatvāriṃśattamo'dhyāyaḥ || 43 ||
samāptaṃ citrasūtram ||
<3.44>
vajra uvāca||
devatārūpanirmāṇaṃ kathayasya mamānagha||
yasmātsannihitā nityaṃ śāstravatsā kṛtā bhavet||1||
mārkaṇḍeya uvāca||
devatārūpanirmāṇaṃ vadataḥ śṛṇu pārthiva||
ādāveva pravakṣyāmi trimūrtestava pārthiva || 2 ||
viṣṇoramitavīryasya pratimālakṣaṇaṃ śubham ||
brāhmī tu rājasī mṛtistasya sarvapravartinī || 3 ||
sāttvikī vaiṣṇavī jñeyā saṃsāraparipālinī ||
tāmasī ca tathā raudrī jñeyā saṃhārakāriṇī || 4 ||
brahmāṇaṃ kārayedvidvāndevaṃ saumyaṃ caturmukham ||
baddhapadmāsanaṃ toṣyaṃ tathā kṛṣṇājināmbaram || 5 ||
jaṭādharaṃ caturbāhuṃ saptahaṃse rathe sthitam ||
vāme nyastaṃ karatale tasyaikaṃ doryugaṃ bhavet || 6 ||
ekasmindakṣiṇe pāṇāvakṣamālā tathā śubhā ||
kamaṇḍalurdvitīye ca sarvābharaṇadhāriṇaḥ || 7 ||
sarvalakṣaṇayuktasya śāntarūpasya pārthiva ||
padmapatradalāgrābhaṃ dhyānasammīlitekṣaṇam || 8 ||
arcāyāṃ kārayeddevaṃ citre vā pustakarmaṇi ||
devadevaṃ tathā viṣṇuṃ kārayedgaruḍasthitam || 9 ||
kaustubhodbhāsitoraskaṃ sarvābharaṇadhāriṇam ||
sajalāmbudasacchāyaṃ pītadivyāmbaraṃ tathā || 3.44.10 ||
mukhāśca kāyāścatvāro bāhavo dviguṇāstathā ||
saumyaṃ tu vadanaṃ pūrvaṃ nārasiṃhaṃ tu dakṣiṇam ||11||
kāpilaṃ paścimaṃ vaktraṃ tathā vārāhamuttaram ||
tasya dakṣiṇahasteṣu bāṇākṣamusalādayaḥ ||12 ||
carma cīraṃ dhanuścendracāpeṣū vanamālinaḥ ||
kāryāṇi viṣṇordharmajña śṛṇu rūpaṃ pinākinaḥ||13||
devadevaṃ mahādevaṃ vṛṣārūḍhaṃ tu kārayet ||
tasya vakatrāṇi kāryāṇi pañca yādavanandana || 14 ||
sarvāṇi saumyarūpāṇi dakṣiṇaṃ vikaṭaṃ mukham ||
kapālamālinaṃ bhīmaṃ jagatsaṃhārakārakam || 15||
trinetrāṇi ca sarvāṇi vadanaṃ hyuttaraṃ vinā ||
jaṭākalāpe mahati tasya candrakalā bhavet || 16 ||
tasyopariṣṭādvadanaṃ pañcamaṃ tu vidhīyate ||
yajñopavītaṃ ca tathā vāsukiṃ tasya kārayet || 17 ||
daśabāhustathā kāryo devadevo maheśvaraḥ ||
akṣamālāṃ triśūlaṃ ca śaradaṇḍamathotpalam || 18 ||
tasya dakṣiṇahasteṣu kartavyāni mahābhuja ||
vāmeṣu mātuliṅgaṃ ca cāpādarśau kamaṇḍalum || 19 ||
tathā carma ca kartavyaṃ devadevasya śūlinaḥ ||
varṇāstathā ca kartavyāścandrāṃśusadṛśaprabhāḥ||3.44.20||
etattrimūrtestava rūpamuktaṃ māhānubhāvasya janārdanasya||
padmasvarūpaṃ tava vacmi rājanyatrārcanaṃ kāryamatheśvarāṇām ||21||
iti śrīviṣṇudharmottare tṛtīyakhaṇḍe mā0 va0 saṃ0 trimūrtinirmāṇannāma catuścatvāriṃśattamo'dhyāyaḥ || 44 ||
<3.45>
mārkaṇḍeya uvāca ||
sauvarṇaṃ rājataṃ tāmramārakūṭamayaṃ tathā ||
dvihastamātraṃ kartavyaṃ kamalaṃ cārukesaram || 1 ||
tasyāṣṭabhāgāḥ kartavyāḥ karṇikā nṛpa vartulā ||
aṣṭabhāgocchritā caiva patre nyastā tu kārayet ||2||
ekatonatripañcāśatkarṇikāyāṃ tu kārayet ||
vartulā karṇikācchedā yavamātrasamanvitāḥ || 3 ||
ṣoḍaśāṃśena kartavyaṃ taddhi vistaratastathā ||
śeṣaṃ patraistu susnigdhaiḥ pūrayedaṣṭabhistathā || 4 ||
tasya pratiṣṭhā kartavyā tatra devāṃstu pūjayet ||
brahmāṇaṃ pūjayettatra tatra sampūjayeddharim || 5 ||
tatra sampūjayedrudraṃ tatra sampūjayecchriyam ||
tatra sampūjayecchakraṃ devarājaṃ jagatpatima || 6 ||
tatra sampūjayetsūryaṃ śaśinaṃ tatra pūjayet ||
yameva manasoddiśya devaṃ padmaṃ pratiṣṭhitam ||
tameva pūjayettatra nānyaṃ devaṃ kathañcana || 7 ||
padmasya rūpaṃ kathitaṃ tavaitatpadmaṃ samagrā vasudhā niruktā ||
tatrārcanaṃ kāryamatheśvarāṇāṃ tatrārcitāste varadā bhavanti || 8 ||
iti śrīviṣṇudharmottare tṛtīyakhaṇḍe mārkaṇḍeyavajrasaṃvāde padmarūpanirūpaṇo nāma pañcacatvāriṃśattamo'dhyāyaḥ || 45 ||
<3.46>
|| vajra uvāca || ||
rūpagandharasairhīnaḥ śabdasparśavivarjitaḥ ||
puruṣastu tvayā proktastasya rūpamidaṃ katham || 1 ||
mārkaṇḍeya uvāca ||
prakṛtirvikṛtistasya rūpeṇa paramātmanaḥ ||
alakṣyaṃ tasya tadrūpaṃ prakṛtissā prakīrtitā || || 2 ||
sākārā vikṛtirjñeyā tasya sarvaṃ jagatsmṛtam ||
pūjādhyānādikaṃ kartuṃ sākārasyaiva śakyate || 3 ||
svatastu devasyākāraḥ pūjanīyo yathāvidhi ||
avyaktā hi gatirdukhaṃ dehabhṛdbhiravāpyate || 4 ||
ato bhagavatānena svecchayā tatpradarśitam ||
prādurbhāveṣvathākāraṃ taṃ diśanti divaukasaḥ || 5 ||
etasmātkāraṇātpūjā sākārasya vidhīyate ||
hetumacca tadākāraṃ tanme nigadataḥ śṛṇu || 6 ||
aruṇaṃ rajasā varṇaṃ tena padmāgrasannibhaḥ ||
brahmā devavaro jñeyassarvabhūtanamaskṛtaḥ || 7 ||
ṛgvedaḥ pūrvavadanaṃ yajurvedastu dakṣiṇam ||
paścimaṃ sāmavedaḥ syādātharvaṇamathottaram || 8 ||
ye vedāste mukhā jñeyāścatasro bāhavo diśaḥ ||
āpa eva jagatsarvaṃ sthāvaraṃ jaṅgamaṃ tathā || 9 ||
tāśca dhārayate brahmā tena haste kamaṇḍaluḥ ||
akṣamālā vinirdiṣṭā kālastu brahmaṇaḥ kare || 3.46.10 ||
kalanātsarvabhūtānāṃ kāla ityabhidhīyate ||
yajño vistīryate sarvaḥ śuklāśuklena karmaṇā || 11 ||
śuklāśuklamato jñeyaṃ vāsaḥ kṛṣṇājinaṃ vibhoḥ ||
bhūlokaśca bhuvo lokaḥ svaloko'tha mahattathā||12||
janastapaśca satyaṃ ca saptalokāḥ prakīrtitāḥ ||
ye lokāste rathe haṃsā brahmaṇaḥ parameṣṭhinaḥ||13||
viṣṇunābhau samutpannaṃ yatpadmaṃ sā mahībhujaḥ ||
merustu karṇikā tasya vijñeyā gajasattama || 14 ||
sarvatra pārthiva sthairyaṃ dhyānabandhamataḥ sthitam ||
padmāsanena bhagavānvidhatte pārthivena tu || 15 ||
ātmanaḥ paramaṃ dhāma rūpahīna vicintayet ||
dṛṣṭyarthaṃ jagatāmāste dhyānasammīlitekṣaṇaḥ || 16||
tathaivoṣadhayo rājañjagaddhāraṇakāraṇāḥ ||
brahmaṇastā jaṭā jñeyāḥ sarvagasya mahātmanaḥ || 17 ||
prakāśakāni lokasya vidyāsthānāni yāni ca||
tasyābharaṇajātāni jñeyāni parameṣṭhinaḥ || 18 ||
etaddhi tasyāpratimasya rūpaṃ taveritaṃ sarvajaganmayasya ||
evaṃ śarīreṇa jagatsamagraṃ sandhārayatyeva jagatpradhānaḥ ||19||
iti śrīviṣṇudharmottare tṛtīyakhaṇḍe mā0 saṃ0 brahmarūpanirmāṇo nāma ṣaṭcatvāriṃśattamo'dhyāyaḥ ||46||
<3.47>
mārkaṇḍeya uvāca||
parasya puruṣasyaiṣā saṃsāre vikṛtirmatā ||
sarvā ca vikṛtiḥ kṛṣṇā tena saṃsārapālanam || 1 ||
kṛṣṇarūpaṃ sa bhagavānvidhatte bhūtabhāvanaḥ ||
brahmaṇaiva hareḥ proktaṃ sarvābharaṇadhāraṇam || 2 ||
bibharti vakṣasā jñānaṃ kaustubhaṃ vimalaṃ hariḥ ||
kṛṣṇā dīrghā vicitrā ca vanamālā prakīrtitā || 3 ||
yathā sarvamidaṃ baddhaṃ jagadrājaṃścarācaram ||
avidyā vasanaṃ tasya saṃsāraparipālinī || 4 ||
vidyā śuklā vinirdiṣṭā kṛṣṇamajñānamucyate ||
ajñānavidyā madhyasthā tvavidyā parikīrtitā || 5 ||
na kṛṣṇā na tathā śuklā tena vidyeyamuttamā ||
antarālaṃ bibhartyeva suvarṇakanakopamam || 6||
manastu garuḍo jñeyaḥ sarvabhūtaśarīragam ||
tasmācchīghrataraṃ nāsti tathaiva balavattaram || 7 ||
diśaścatasro dharmajña tāvatyo vidiśastathā ||
bāhavo'ṣṭau vinirdiṣṭāstasya devasya śārṅgiṇaḥ || 8. ||
balaṃ jñānaṃ tathaiśvaryaṃ śaktiśca yadunandana ||
vijñeyaṃ devadevasya tasya vaktracatuṣṭayam || 9 ||
vāsudevaśca bhagavāṃstathā saṅkarṣaṇaḥ prabhuḥ ||
pradyumnaścāniruddhaśca balādyāḥ parikīrtitāḥ || 3.47.10 ||
dvaudvau bhujau tu vijñeyau prativaktraṃ mahātmanaḥ ||
vāsudevasya karayorjñātavyau sūryarātripau || 11 ||
saṅkarṣaṇasya kārayostathā musalalāṅgale ||
pradyumnasya tathā jñeyau cāpabāṇau mahābhuja || 12 ||
āniruddhamya vijñeyo carmakhaṅgau vicakṣaṇaiḥ ||
puruṣaprakṛtī jñeyau sūryācandramasāvubhau || 13 ||
ete ca vāsudevasya kare cakragade mate ||
kālaṃ ca lāṅgalaṃ viddhi mṛtyuṃ ca musalaṃ tathā|| 14||
tābhyāṃ saṃkarṣaṇo rudraḥ karṣatīdaṃ carācaram||
pradyumnasya kare vahneḥ śārṅgaṃ cāpaṃ tu yatsmṛtam|| 15||
dhyeyaṃ tu paramaṃ lakṣyaṃ tābhyāṃ chindanti yoginaḥ ||
brahmaṇastvaniruddhasya kare carma mahābhuja || 16 ||
ajñānāvaraṇaṃ jeyaṃ jagatsṛṣṭiprayojanam ||
vairāgyaṃ nandakaṃ khaṅgaṃ chittvā vai tena bandhanam ||
nadanti yogino yasmāttasmāttannandakaṃ smṛtam || 17 ||
etaddhi tasyāpratimasya rūpaṃ taveritaṃ sarvajaganmayasya ||
evaṃ śarīreṇa jagatsamagraṃ bibharti devaḥ sa jagatpradhānaḥ || 18 ||
iti śrīviṣṇudharmottare tṛtīyakhaṇḍe mārkaṇḍeyavajrasaṃvāde viṣṇurūpanirmāṇo nāma saptacatvāriṃśattamo'dhyāyaḥ || 47 ||
<3.48>
|| mārkaṇḍeya uvāca ||
sadyojātaṃ vāmadevamaghoraṃ ca mahābhuja ||
tathā taṃ puruṣaṃ jñeyamīśānaṃ pañcamaṃ mukham || 1 ||
sadyojātaṃ mahī proktā vāmadevaṃ tathā jalam ||
tejastvaghoraṃ vidyāttaṃ vāyustatpuruṣaṃ matam || 2 ||
īśānaṃ ca tathākāśamūrddhvasthaṃ pañcamaṃ mukham ||
vibhāgenātha vakṣyāmi śambhorvadanapañcakam || 3 ||
mahādevamukhaṃ jeyaṃ pūrvaṃ śambhormahātmanaḥ ||
netrāṇi trīṇi tasyāṅga somasūryahutāśanāḥ || 4 ||
dakṣiṇaṃ tu mukhaṃ raudraṃ bhairavaṃ tatprakīrtitama ||
paścimaṃ yanmukhaṃ tasya nandivaktraṃ taducyate || 5 ||
umā vaktraṃ ca vijñeyaṃ tasya devasya cottaram ||
sadāśivākhyaṃ vijñeyaṃ pāvanaṃ tasya pañcamam || 6 ||
trilocanāni sarvāṇi vāmadevaṃ dvilocanam ||
mahādevamukhaṃ bhūmistataḥ syādbhairavaṃ mukham || 7 ||
nandivaktraṃ tathā vāyu raudreyaṃ cāpa ucyate ||
sadāśivamukhaṃ jñeyamākāśaṃ yadunandana || 8 ||
diśo daśa bhujāstasya vijñeyaṃ vadanaṃ prati ||
mahādevakare jñeyā tvakṣamālākamaṇḍalū || 9 ||
sadāśivamukhe jñeyau cāpabāṇau mahābhuja ||
māheśvaraṃ tataścāpaṃ pinākamiti śabditam || 3.48.10 ||
teṣāṃ tu pūrvamevoktaṃ vyākhyānaṃ ripusūdana ||
daṇḍaśca mātuluṅgaśca karayorbhairavasya tu || 11 ||
mṛtyu daṇḍau vinirdiṣṭau mātuluṅgastathā kare ||
jagadbījasya sarvasya ye rājanparamāṇavaḥ || 12 ||
taiḥ pūrṇaṃ bījaratnaṃ tu bhairavasya kare smṛtam ||
carmaśūle kare jñeye nandino yadunandana|| 13 ||
pūrvameva ca te proktaṃ dharmavyākaraṇaṃ mayā ||
triśalūdaṇḍamavyaktaṃ śūleṣu vyaktatāṃ gatam || 14 ||
sattvaṃ rajastamaścaiva vijñātavyaṃ nṛpottama ||
darpaṇendīvare jñeye tathā devyāḥ karadvaye || 15||
ādarśaṃ nirmalaṃ jñānaṃ vairāgyaṃ ca tathotpalam ||
mahādevasya devasya vyākhyātā brāhmaṇā jaṭāḥ || 16||
aiśvaryaṃ tu kalā cāndrī mūrdhni śambhoḥ prakīrtitā ||
trailokyaśamanaḥ krodho vāsukirnāmataḥ smṛtaḥ || 17 ||
tṛṣṇā viśālā citrā ca vyāghracarma prakīrtitam ||
vṛṣo hi bhagavāndharmaścatuṣpādaḥ prakīrtitaḥ ||18||
jagadutpādakaṃ vastu prakṛtiḥ sā prakīrtitā ||
śuklā ca prakṛtiḥ sarvā tena śuklo maheśvaraḥ || 19||
etaddhi tasyāpratimasya rūpaṃ taveritaṃ sarvajaganmayasya ||
evaṃ śarīreṇa jagatsamagraṃ sa dhārayatyeva jagatpradhānaḥ || 3.48.20 ||
iti śrīviṣṇudharmottare tṛtīyakhaṇḍe mārkaṇḍeyavajrasaṃvāde mahādevarūpanirmāṇo nāmāṣṭācatvāriṃśattamo'dhyāyaḥ || 48 ||
<3.49>
vajra uvāca ||
nāsatyayormamācakṣva rūpanirmāṇamacyuta ||
nāsatyarūpayoścaiva tvaṃ hi sarvaviducyase || 1 ||
mārkaṇḍeya uvāca ||
padmapatrasavarṇābhau padmapatrasamāmbarau ||
dvibhujau devabhiṣajau kartavyau devasaṃyutau ||2|
sarvābharaṇasaṃpannau viśeṣāccārulocanau ||
tayoroṣadhayaḥ kāryā divyā dakṣiṇahastayoḥ || 3 ||
vāmayoḥ pustakau kāryau darśanīyau tathā dvija ||
ekasya dakṣiṇe pārśve vāme cānyasya yādava || 4 ||
nārīyugaṃ tu kartavyaṃ surūpaṃ cārudarśanam ||
tayośca nāmanī prokte rūpasaṃpattathākṛtiḥ || 5 ||
madhūkapuṣpasaṃkāśā rūpasaṃpatprakīrtitā||
prākṛtiḥ kathitā loke śarakāṇḍanibhā tathā ||
na tu bhāṇḍakare kārye candraśuklāmbare tathā ||6||
nāsatyayo rūpamidaṃ tavoktaṃ mayādya mukhyaṃ yaduvaṃśamukhya ||
dhyānena sarvāmayanāśakāri sampūjitaṃ vā bhuvi mānavānām ||7||
iti śrīviṣṇudharmottare tṛtīyakhaṇḍe mārkaṇḍeyavajrasaṃvāde nāsatyarūpanirmāṇo nāmaikonapañcāśattamo'dhyāyaḥ ||49||
<3.50>
vajra uvāca||
śakrakīnāśavaruṇadhanadānāṃ bhṛgūttama ||
sahetukaṃ samācakṣva rūpanirmāṇamacyuta ||1 ||
mārkaṇḍeya uvāca ||
caturdante gaje śakraḥ śvetaḥ kāryaḥ sureśvaraḥ ||
vāmotsaṅgagatā kāryā rakṣamāṇā tathā śacī ||2||
nīlavastraḥ suvarṇābhaḥ sarvābharaṇavām̐ntathā ||
tiryaglalāṭagenākṣṇā kartavyaśca vibhūṣitaḥ||3||
śakraścaturbhujaḥ kāryo dvibhujā ca tathā śacī ||
padmāṅkuśau tu kartavyau śakradakṣiṇahastayoḥ ||4||
vāmaṃ śacīpṛṣṭhagataṃ dvitīyaṃ vajrasaṃyutam ||
vāme śacyāḥ kare kāryā ramyā santānamañjarī ||5||
dakṣiṇaṃ pṛṣṭhavinyastaṃ devanāthasya kārayet ||
brahmaṇaiva vinirdiṣṭaṃ tasyābharaṇadhāraṇam || 6 ||
tenaiva tasya kathitaṃ tathā bāhucatuṣṭayam ||
trinetratā ca tasyoktā devadevena śambhunā || 7 ||
rukmābhavarṇatā tasya tejasāṃ dhāmakāraṇāt ||
yannīlaṃ vasanaṃ tasya tadākāśaṃ prakīrtitam || 8 ||
ājñā tasyāṅkuśo haste sarvabhūtavaśaṅgarī ||
padmasthāṃ sahapadmena vibhūtiṃ bhuvanādhipa || 9 ||
kareṇa dhārayañchrīmānmaghavānsurasattamaḥ ||
duṣṭasaṃśamanaṃ krodho vajraḥ śakrakaraḥ smṛtaḥ || 3.50.10 ||
vāsudevaḥ smṛtaḥ śakrastena lakṣmīḥ śacī matā ||
tasyāḥ kare ca santānaṃ jñeyā santānamañjarī || 11 ||
arthastvairāvaṇo jñeyo dantāstasya tu kīrtitāḥ ||
daivamantraprabhūtsāhaśaktayastu mahābalāḥ || 12 |
etadrūpaṃ vāsudevasya śākraṃ tubhyaṃ proktaṃ devarājasya rājan ||
yāmyaṃ rūpaṃ te'dhunāhaṃ bravīmi yasyāyatte sukha duḥkhe narāṇām || 13 ||
iti śrīviṣṇudharmottare tṛtīyakhaṇḍe mārkaṇḍeyavajrasamvāde śakrarūpanirmāṇo nāma pañcāśattamo'dhyāyaḥ || 50 ||
<3.51>
mārkaṇḍeya uvāca ||
sajalāmbudasacchāyastaptacāmīkarāmbaraḥ ||
mahiṣasthaśca kartavyaḥ sarvābharaṇavānyataḥ || 1 ||
nīlotpalābhā dhūmorṇā vāmotsaṅge ca kārayet ||
dhūmorṇā dvibhujā kāryā yamaḥ kāryaścaturbhujaḥ || 2 ||
daṇḍakhaḍgāvubhau kāryau yamadakṣiṇahastayoḥ ||
daṇḍopari mukhaṃ kāryaṃ jvālamālāvibhūṣaṇam ||3||
dhūmrorṇāpṛṣṭhagaṃ vāmaṃ carmayuktaṃ tathāparam ||
dhūmorṇādakṣiṇaṃ hastaṃ yamapṛṣṭhagataṃ bhavet || 4 ||
vāme tasyāḥ kare kāryaṃ mātuluṅgaṃ sudarśanam ||
pārśve tu dakṣiṇe tasya citraguptaṃ ca kārayet || 5 ||
uddiśya veśaṃ svākāraṃ dvibhujaṃ saumyadarśanam ||
dakṣiṇe lekhinī tasya vāme patraṃ tu kārayet || 6 ||
vāme pāśadharaḥ kāryaḥ kālo vikaṭadarśanaḥ ||
yamaṃ saṅkarṣaṇaṃ viddhi tāmasīṃ tanumāśritam || 7 ||
maryādāpālanārthāya lokasaṃhārakāraṇam ||
nīlotpaladalacchāyastāmasatvātprakīrtitaḥ || 8 ||
vasanaṃ tasya vikhyātaṃ vāsudevena śatruhan ||
caturbhujatvaṃ vikhyātaṃ brahmaṇā paramātmanaḥ || 9 ||
brahmaṇā tasya nirdiṣṭaṃ sarvābharaṇadhāraṇam ||
yo moho maraṇaṃ nṝṇāṃ vijñeyo mahiṣastu saḥ ||3.51.10||
amoghaṃ tu kare daṇḍaṃ mṛtyuṃ dhārayate yamaḥ ||
aniruddhena tasyoktaṃ dhāraṇaṃ khaḍgacarmaṇoḥ || 11 ||
kālarātrī tu vijñeyā dhūmorṇā yadunandana ||
tasyāstu śūlinā proktaṃ bījapūrakadhāraṇam || 12 ||
citragupto vinirdiṣṭastathātmā sarvadehagaḥ ||
patraṃ dharmamadharmaṃ ca karasthā tasya lekhinī || 13 ||
kāla eva svarūpeṇa kālo yamasamīpagaḥ ||
tasya pāśaḥ kare ghoro yamamārgaḥ suduṣkaraḥ || 14 ||
vajra uvāca ||
saṃhārahetukī mūrtirviṣṇoḥ saṅkarṣaṇo mataḥ ||
sa tu devastvayā proktaścandraśuklavapurmahān || 12 ||
talpe saṃhārakartṛtve kṛṣṇamūrtiḥ kathaṃ yamaḥ ||
etaṃ me saṃśayaṃ chindhi bhṛguvaṃśavivardhana ||16||
mārkaṃḍeya uvāca ||
mūrtiḥ sāṅkarṣiṇī viṣṇo rudraḥ saṃhārakārakaḥ ||
kalpakṣaye tu saṃhāre karoti jagatāṃ hi sā || 17 ||
prakṛto yāti dharmajña saṃhṛtaṃ tu tadā jagata ||
tena prakṛtivarṇasthaḥ karoti jagatāṃ kṣayam || 18 ||
kṛtvāpi prāṇisaṃhāraṃ yamarūpī punaḥpunaḥ ||
prāṇināṃ prakṛtau yogaṃ na vidhatte kadācana || 19 ||
vikāre yojayatyeva sukhaduḥkhātmake tadā ||
vikārarūpavarṇaṃ tu tena saṃharate jagat || 3.51.20 ||
rūpamidaṃ kathitaṃ tava yāmyaṃ pāpavināśakaraṃ vibudhānām ||
rūpamataḥ kathayāmi mahātmaṃstoyapatervaruṇasya tavāham || 21 ||
iti śrīviṣṇudharmottare tṛtīyakhaṇḍe mārkaṇḍeyavajrasaṃvāde yamarūpanirmāṇo nāmaikapañcāśattamo'dhyāyaḥ || 51||
<3.52>
mārkaṇḍeya uvāca ||
saptahaṃse rathe kāryo varuṇo yādasāmpatiḥ ||
snigdhavaiḍūryasaṅkāśaḥ śvetāmbaradharastathā ||1||
kiñcitpralambajaṭharo muktāhāravibhūṣaṇaḥ ||
sarvābharaṇavānrājā tayā devaścaturbhujaḥ || 2 ||
vāmabhāgagataṃ ketuṃ makarasya tu kārayet ||
chatraṃ ca susitaṃ mūrdhni bhāryā sarvāṅgasundarī || 3 ||
vāmotsaṃgagatākāryā gaurī taddvibhujā nṛpa ||
utpalaṃ tu kare vāme dakṣiṇaṃ devapṛṣṭhagam || 4 ||
padmapāśau kare kāryau devadakṣiṇahastayoḥ ||
śaṅkhaṃ ca ratnapātraṃ ca vāmayostasya kārayet || 5 ||
bhāge tu dakṣiṇe gaṅgā makarasthā sacāmarā ||
devī padmakarā kāryā candragaurī varānanā || 80 ||
vāme tu yamunā kāryā kūrmasaṃsthā sacāmarā ||
nīlotpalakarā saumyā nalinīrajasannibhā || 7 ||
snigdhavaiḍūrya saṅkāśastvāpovarṇo yato nṛpa ||
tayoranugrahārthāya tadvarṇo varuṇastataḥ || 8 ||
svarūpavarṇāḥ susitāstā eva dvijapuṃgava ||
vasanaṃ tasya kathitaṃ tā eva susitaṃ nṛpa ||9||
vajra uvāca ||
ambhasastu kathaṃ varṇaḥ satyāsatye tvayerite ||
etattu śrotumicchāmi paraṃ kautūhalaṃ hi me ||3.52.10||
mārkaṇḍeya uvāca ||
vaiḍūryābhamapāṃ varṇamatathyaṃ yadunandana ||
chāyāgatasya nabhasastadrūpaṃ tāsu dṛśyate || 11||
prapātapātairyadrūpamaṃbhasāṃ nṛpa dṛśyate ||
śaśāṅkāṃśupratīkāśaṃ tatsvarūpe pratiṣṭhitam || 12 ||
pradyumno vāsudevastu varuṇo yādasāṃ patiḥ ||
tasya bhāryā ratirjñeyā gaurī yādavanandana ||13||
saubhāgyamutpalaṃ tasyāḥ kare suratagaṃ vibho ||
varuṇasya kare padmaṃ vidhidharme mahābhuja || 14 ||
śaṅkhamarthaṃ vijānīhi pāśaṃ saṃsārabandhanam ||
ratnapātraṃ kare jñeyaṃ sarvaratnadharā dharā || 15 ||
yaśaśca susitaṃ chatraṃ saukhyaṃ makara eva ca ||
brahmaṇā kathitaṃ tasya bhujābharaṇadhāraṇam || 16 ||
lavaṇakṣīraājyādi dadhimaṇḍasurodakāḥ ||
tathaivekṣurasodaśca surodaśca tathāparaḥ | 17 ||
samudrāste tu vikhyātā loke naravarottama ||
saptahaṃsā rathe tasya varuṇasya mahātmanaḥ || 18 ||
jāyā tu yamunā jñeyā siddhirbhāgīrathī dvija ||
vīryakālau vinirdiṣṭau tathā makarakacchapau || 19 ||
svargagaṅgākare padmaṃ vṛddhayāmunamutpalam || 3.52.20 ||
rūpaṃ jaleśasya tavaitaduktaṃ mayā śiraḥpāśadharasya tasya ||
ataḥ paraṃ vittapaterbravīmi rūpaṃ tavāhaṃ yaduvaṃśacandra || 21 ||
iti śrīviṣṇudharmottare tṛtīyakhaṇḍe mārkaṇḍeyavajrasaṃvāde varuṇarūpanirmāṇo nāma dvipañcāśattamodhyāyaḥ || 52 ||
<3.53>
mārkaṇḍeya uvāca ||
kartavyaḥ padmapatrābho dhanado naravāhanaḥ ||
cāmīkarābho dhanadaḥ sarvābharaṇabhūṣaṇaḥ || 1 ||
lambodaraścaturbāhurvāmapiṅgalalocanaḥ ||
apīcyaveśaḥ kavacī hārabhārārpitodaraḥ || 2 ||
dve ca daṃṣṭre mukhe tasya kartavye śmaśrudhāriṇaḥ ||
vāmena vinatā kāryā maulistasyārimardinī || 3 ||
vāmotsaṃgagatā kāryā ṛddhirdevī varapradā ||
devapṛṣṭhagataṃ pāṇiṃ dvibhujāyāstu dakṣiṇam || 4 ||
ratnapātrakaraṃ kāryaṃ vāmaṃ ripuniṣūdana ||
gadāśaktī ca kartavye tasya dakṣiṇahastayoḥ || 5 ||
siṃhāṅkalakṣaṇaṃ vṛttaṃ śibikāmapi pādayoḥ ||
śaṅkhapadmau nidhī kāryau svarūpau nidhisaṃsthitau || 6 ||
śaṅkhapadmāntaniṣkrāntaṃ vadanaṃ tasya pārśvataḥ ||
aniruddhastu vijñeyastvayā devo dhanādhipaḥ || || 7 ||
brahmaṇā kathitaṃ tasya sarvābharaṇasādhanama ||
suvarṇaṃ nāma vikhyātaṃ dhanānāmuttamaṃ dhanam ||
tasyānugrahato dhatte vāsaḥ kanakasannibham ||
śaktireva smṛtā śaktirdaṇḍanītistathā gadā || 9 ||
lokayātrā ca kathitā ṛddhirjñeyā svabhāvataḥ || 8 ||
tatkare ratnapātraṃ tu guṇādhāraṃ pracakṣate || 3.53.10 ||
naraṃ rājyaṃ vijānīhi yatrasthastu sadā vibhuḥ ||
svarūpatastu vijñeyau śaṅkhapadmau tathā nidhī || 11 ||
vittatoṃśo jayatyasmiṃstābhyāṃ prasavate'yutaḥ ||
arthaḥ sakṣamayā jñeyaḥ ketuḥ siṃhāṅkalakṣaṇaḥ ||
nigrahānugrahe daṃṣṭre tasya tāta mahātmanaḥ || 12 ||
yakṣādhipasyetadanantadaṃṣṭraṃ nāme vane yakṣanadasya rājan ||
ataḥ paraṃ kiṃ kathayāmi tubhyaṃ tanme vadasvāyatalohitākṣa ||13 ||
iti śrīviṣṇudharmottare tṛtīyakhaṇḍe mārkaṇḍeyavajrasaṃvāde vaiśravaṇarūpanirmāṇo nāma tripañcāśattamo'dhyāyaḥ||53||
<3.54>
vajra uvāca ||
suparṇasya ca tālasya makarasya mṛgasya ca||
marutāṃ rūpanirmāṇaṃ śrotumicchāmi bhārgava ||1 ||
mārkaṇḍeya uvāca ||
tārkṣyo mārakataprakhyaḥ kauśikākāranāsikaḥ ||
caturbhujastu kartavyo vṛttanetramukhastataḥ||2||
gṛdhrorujānucaraṇaḥ pakṣadvyavibhūṣaṇaḥ||3||
prabhāsaṃsthānarsauvarṇaḥ kalāpena vivarjitaḥ||
chatraṃ ca pūrṇakumbhaṃ ca karayostasya kārayet||4||
karadvaye tu kartavyaṃ tathāsya racitāñjaliḥ ||
tathāsya bhagavānpṛṣṭhe chatrakumbhadharau karau || 5 ||
na kartavyau tu kartavyau devapādavarāvubhau ||
kiñcillambodaraḥ kāryaḥ sarvābharaṇabhūṣitaḥ || 6 ||
tālastālākṛtiḥ kāryo makaro makarākṛtiḥ ||
upariṣṭāttathaivāsya kāryo ṛgyavadacyutaḥ ||7 ||
mano jagattathā kāmaṃ karma caite kramātsmṛtāḥ ||
vāsudevocyutaścaiva pradyumnaśca mahābhujaḥ ||
tathā caivāniruddhaśca krameṇaite prakīrtitāḥ || 8 ||
tārkṣyaḥ satālo makarastathārcyo deveśa kīnāśajalārthanāthaḥ || 
agnyarkatoyādhipapatrināthasta eva lokeṣu budhaiḥ pradiṣṭāḥ || 9 ||
iti śrīviṣṇudharmottare tṛtīyakhaṇḍe mārkaṇḍeyavajrasaṃvāde garuḍarūpanirmāṇo nāma catuḥpañcāśattamo'dhyāyaḥ ||54 ||
<3.55>
vajra uvāca ||
śivasya rūpanirmāṇaṃ vahnervai nirṛtestathā ||
vāyośca brūhi dharmajña tatra me saṃśayo mahān ||1||
mārkaṃḍeya uvāca ||
vāmārdhe pārvatī kāryā śivaḥ kāryaścaturbhujaḥ||
akṣamālāṃ triśūlaṃ ca tasya dakṣiṇahastayoḥ || 2 ||
darpaṇendīvarau kāryau vāmayoryadunandana ||
ekavaktro bhavecchambhurvāmā ca dayitā tanuḥ || 3 ||
dvinetraśca mahābhāga sarvābharaṇabhūṣitaḥ ||
abhedabhinnā prakṛtiḥ puruṣeṇa mahābhuja || 4 ||
gaurīśarveti vikhyātā sarvalokanamaskṛtā ||
kāraṇaṃ kathitaṃ pūrvaṃ śūlādīnāṃ mayā tava || 5 ||
īśānarūpaṃ kathitaṃ tavedaṃ rūpaṃ tathāgneḥ śṛṇu dharmanityam ||
vedā yadarthaṃ jagati pravṛttā mukhaṃ ca yatsarvasurā'surāṇām || 6 ||
iti śrīviṣṇudharmottare tṛtīyakhaṇḍe mārkaṇḍeyavajrasaṃvāde gaurīśarvavarṇano nāma pañcapañcāśattamo'dhyāyaḥ || 55 ||
<3.56>
mārkaṇḍeya uvāca ||
raktaṃ jaṭādharaṃ vahniṃ kuryādvai dhrūmravāsasam ||
jvālāmālākulaṃ saumyaṃ trinetraṃ śmaśrudhāriṇam || 1 ||
caturbāhuṃ caturdaṃṣṭraṃ deveśaṃ vātasārathim ||
caturbhiśca śukairyuktaṃ dhūmacihnarathe sthitam || 2 ||
vāmotsaṅgagatā svāhā śakrasyeva śacī bhavet ||
ratnapātrakarā devī vahnerdakṣiṇahastayoḥ || 3 ||
jvālātriśūlau kartavyau cākṣamālā tu vāmake ||
raktaṃ hi tejaso rūpaṃ raktavarṇaṃ tataḥ smṛtam || 4 ||
vātasārathitā tasya pratyakṣaṃ dhūmrakṣetratā ||
pratyakṣā ca tathā proktā yāgadhūmrābhavastratā || 5 ||
akṣamālāṃ triśūlaṃ ca jaṭājūṭatrinetratā ||
sarvābharaṇadhāritvaṃ vyākhyātaṃ tasya śambhunā || 6 ||
jvālākāraṃ paraṃ dhāma hutaṃ tena pratīcchati ||
gṛhītvā sarvadevebhyo tato nayati śatruhan || 7 ||
vāgdaṇḍamatha dhigdaṇḍaṃ dhanadaṇḍaṃ tathaiva ca ||
caturthaṃ vadhadaṇḍaṃ ca daṃṣṭrāstasya prakīrtitāḥ || 8 ||
śmaśru tasya vinirdiṣṭaṃ darbhāḥ paramapāvanam ||
ye vedāste śukāstasya rathayuktā mahātmanaḥ || 9 ||
āgneyametattava rūpamuktaṃ pāpāpahaṃ siddhikaraṃ narāṇām ||
dhyeyaṃ tavaitannṛpa homakāle sarvāgnikarmaṇyaparājitena || 3.56.10 ||
iti śrīviṣṇudharmottare tṛtīyakhaṇḍe mā0 va0 saṃ0 agnirūpanirmāṇo nāma ṣaṭpañcāśattamo'dhyāyaḥ ||56||
<3.57>
mārkaṇḍeya uvāca ||
virūpākṣo vivṛttākṣaḥ prāṃśudaṇḍojjvalānanaḥ ||
ūrdhvakeśo hariśchamaśrurdvibāhurbhīṣaṇānanaḥ ||1||
varṇena raktakṛṣṇāṅgaḥ kṛṣṇāmbaradharastathā ||
sarvābharaṇadhārī ca daṇḍaraśmikarastathā || 2 ||
bhāryā ca tasya kartavyā devī ninṛtitastathā ||
kṛṣṇāṅgī kṛṣṇavadanā pāśahastā tu vāmataḥ || 3 |
kālaḥ prokto virūpākṣo mṛtyurhi ninṛtistathā ||
bibharti tāmasaṃ rūpaṃ vāsāṃsi ca tato nṛpa || 4 ||
māraṇaṃ tatkare daṇḍaṃ bandhanaṃ coṣṭraraśmayaḥ ||
mahāmohastathaivoṣṭraḥ kathitastasya vāmataḥ ||9||
vapurvirūpākṣamidaṃ pradiṣṭaṃ rakṣodhipasyāpratimasya tasya |
ataḥ paraṃ vacmi tavādya rājanvāyosturūpaṃ paramasya dhāmnaḥ || 9 ||
iti śrīviṣṇudharmottare tṛtīyakhaṇḍe mā0 va0 saṃ0 virūpākṣarūpanirmāṇo nāma saptapañcāśattamo'dhyāyaḥ ||57||
<3.58>
mārkaṇḍeya uvāca ||
vāyurambaravarṇastu tadākārombaro bhavet ||
vāyvāpūritavastraśca dvibhujo rūpasaṃyutaḥ || 1 ||
gamanecchā śivā bhāryā tasya kāryā ca vāmataḥ ||
kāryo gṛhīta vastrāntaḥ karābhyāṃ pavano dvija || 2 ||
tathaiva devī kartavyā śivā paramasundarī ||
vyādhitāsyastathā kāryo devo vyākulamūrdhajaḥ || 3 ||
vāyurāśrayato dhatte gandhavarṇādikaṃ yataḥ ||
tasmādañjanavarṇastu gamanasya samāśrayāt || 4||
tadeva vasanaṃ tasya gaganaṃ parikīrtitam ||
yā gatiḥ sā śivā devī yo'niruddhaśca so'nilaḥ || 9 ||
etaddhi rūpaṃ pavanasya rājam̐stavoditaṃ sa vai jaganmayasya |||
devyāḥ śivāyāśca mahānubhāva paraṃ pavitraṃ viditaṃ dvijānāma ||6||
iti śrīviṣṇudharmottare tṛtīyakhaṇḍe mā0 va0 saṃ0 vāyurūpanirmāṇonāmāṣṭapañcāśattamo'dhyāyaḥ||58||
<3.59>
mārkaṇḍeya uvāca
athāto rūpanirmāṇaṃ vacmyahaṃ bhairavasya te ||
lambodaraṃ tathā kuryādvṛttapiṅgalalocanam || 1 ||
daṃṣṭrākarālavadanaṃ phullanāsāpuṭaṃ tathā ||
kapālamālinaṃ raudraṃ sarvataḥ sarpabhūṣaṇam || 2 ||
vyālena trāsayantaṃ ca devīṃ parvatanandinīm ||
sajalāmbudasaṃkāśaṃ gajacarmottaracchadam || 3 ||
bāhubhirbahubhivyāptaṃ sarvāyudhavibhūṣaṇaiḥ ||
bṛhatsālapratīkāśaistathā tīkṣṇanakhaiḥ śubhaiḥ || 4 ||
sācīkṛtamidaṃ rūpaṃ bhairavasya prakīrtitam ||
mahākālasya kathitametadeva ca sanmukham || 5 ||
devī tu vāmataḥ kāryā kare kāryastu pannagaḥ |
na cāsya purataḥ kāryā devī parvatanandinī || 6||
śuklā na kāryā na tathāsya raktā samīpato mātṛgaṇapradhāne ||
kāryastvathānyaḥ paribarhamasya gaṇāśca kāryā bahurūparūpāḥ || 7||
iti śrīviṣṇudharmottare tṛtīyakhaṇḍe mārkaṇḍayavajrasaṃvāde bhairavarūpanirmāṇo nāmaikonaṣaṣṭitamo'dhyāyaḥ || 59 ||
<3.60>
||vajra uvāca||
viṣṇubhūmyambarāṇāṃ tvaṃ brahmaṇaśca tathā vibho ||
rūpanirmāṇamācakṣva bhṛguvaṃśavivardhana || 1 ||
mārkaṇḍeya uvāca ||
ekavaktro dvibāhuśca gadācakradharaḥ prabhuḥ ||
dehavinyāsamaparaṃ prāguktaṃ kīrtitaṃ hareḥ || 2 ||
gāndhārī tu smṛtā māyī jagadbandhāya vaiṣṇavī ||
gadā devakare nityaṃ sarvabhūtavaśaṅkarī || 3 ||
saiva lakṣmīrdhṛtiḥ kīrtiḥ puṣṭiḥ śraddhā sarasvatī ||
gāyatrī vedajananī kālarātristathaiva sā || 4 ||
saṃsārabhramaṇaṃ cakraṃ cakraṃ viṣṇukare sthitam ||
dharmacakraṃ kālacakraṃ bhacakaṃ ca mahābhuja || 5 ||
dhruvo hi vipṇurbhagavānpradiṣṭacakraṃ sadā bhrāmayate sa vīraḥ ||
etena kāryeṇa jagatpradhānaḥ sa cakrahasto bhagavānpradiṣṭaḥ||6||
iti śrīviṣṇudharmottare tṛtīyakhaṇḍe mārkaṇḍeyavajrasaṃvāde viṣṇurūpanirmāṇo nāma ṣaṣṭitamo'dhyāyaḥ ||60||
<3.61>
mārkaṇḍeya uvāca ||
śukavarṇā mahī kāryā divyābharaṇabhūṣitā ||
caturbhujā saumyavapuścandrāṃśusadṛśāmbarā ||1||
ratnapātraṃ sasyapātraṃ pātramoṣadhisaṃyutam ||
padmaṃ kare ca kartavyaṃ bhuvo yādavanandana || 2 ||
diṅnāgānāṃ caturṇāṃ sā kāryā pṛṣṭhagatā tathā ||
sarvauṣadhiyutā devī śuklavarṇā tataḥ smṛtā||3||
dharmaṃ vastraṃ sitaṃ tasyāḥ padmamarthaṃ tathā kare ||4||
hetuśca śeṣo vidito budhānāmato mayā te kathito'dya rājan ||
rūpaṃ tvarūpasya tu vacmyatohaṃ prakarṣitaṃ yadgaganasya siddhaiḥ || 5 ||
iti viṣṇudharmottare tṛtīyakhaṇḍe mārkaṇḍeyavajrasaṃvāde bhūmirūpanirmāṇo nāmaikaṣaṣṭitamo'dhyāyaḥ || 61 ||
<3.62>
mārkaṇḍeya uvāca ||
nīlotpalābhaṃ gaganaṃ tadvarṇāmbaradhāriṇam ||
candrārkahastaṃ kartavyaṃ dvibhujaṃ saumyadarśanam ||1||
etaddhi rūpaṃ gaganasya rājanproktaṃ mayā te parikalpitaṃ yata ||
rūpaṃ pravakṣyāmyatha te nibodha pitāmahasyāpratimasya tasya || 2 ||
iti śrīviṣṇudharmottare tṛtīyakhaṇḍe mārkaṇḍeyavajrasaṃvāde gaganarūpavarṇano nāma dviṣaṣṭitamo'dhyāyaḥ || 62 ||
<3.63>
mārkaṇḍeya uvāca ||
padmapatrāsanasthastu brahmā kāryaścaturmukhaḥ ||
sāvitrī tasya kartavyā vāmotsaṅgagatā tathā || 1 ||
ādityavarṇā dharmajña sākṣamālākarā tathā ||
rūpaṃ pūrvoditaṃ kāryaṃ sarvamanyajjagatpate || 2 ||
etaddhi rūpaṃ varadasya kāryaṃ pitāmahasyāpratimasya saumya ||
pūrvoditaṃ vā varadasya tasya lokaṃ samagraṃ vaśagaṃ hi yasya || 3 ||
iti śrīviṣṇudharmottare tṛtīya khaṇḍe mārkaṇḍeyavajrasaṃvāde brahmarūpanirmāṇo nāma triṣaṣṭitamo'dhyāyaḥ || 63 ||
<3.64>
mārkaṇḍeya uvāca ||
devī sarasvatī kāryā sarvābharaṇabhūṣitā ||
caturbhujā sā kartavyā tathaiva ca samutthitā || 1 ||
pustakaṃ cākṣamālāṃ ca tasyā dakṣiṇahastayoḥ ||
vāmayośca tathā kāryā vaiṣṇavī ca kamaṇḍaluḥ || 2 ||
samapādapratiṣṭhā ca kāryā sāmamukhī tathā ||
vedāstasya bhujā jñeyāḥ sarvaśāstrāṇi pustakam || 3 ||
sarvaśāstrāmṛtaraso devyā jñeyaḥ kamaṇḍaluḥ ||
akṣamālā kare tasyāḥ kālo bhavati pārthiva || 4 ||
siddhirmūrtimatī jñeyā vaiṣṇavī nātra saṃśayaḥ ||
sāvitrī vadanaṃ tasyāḥ sarvādyā parikīrtitā || 5 ||
candrārkalocanā jñeyā sā ca rājīvalocanā || 6 ||
sārasvataṃ te kathitaṃ mayaitadrūpaṃ pavitraṃ paramaṃ manojñam ||
dhyeyaṃ ca kāryaṃ ca mahīpamukhya savārthasiddhiṃ samabhīpsamānaiḥ || 7||
iti viṣṇudharmottare tṛtīyakhaṇḍe mārkaṇḍeyavajrasaṃvāde sagsvatīrūpanirmāṇo nāma catuḥṣaṣṭi tamo'dhyāyaḥ || 64 ||
<3.65>
vajra uvāca ||
ācakṣva rūpanirmāṇaṃ mamānantasya devaja ||
viṣṇoramitavīryasya śeṣasya dharaṇībhṛtaḥ || 1 ||
mārkaṃḍeya uvāca ||
kuryācchaśāṅkasaṅkāśaṃ ratnojjvalaphaṇānvitam||
nīlavastraṃ caturbāhuṃ sarvābharaṇadhāriṇam ||2||
phaṇāśca bahavaḥ kāryā yatphaṇaṃ tasya madhyamama||
tatra rūpavatī kāryā vasudhā yadunandana||3||
padmaṃ samusalaṃ kāryaṃ devadakṣiṇahastayoḥ ||
vāmayossīraśaṃkhe ca kare tasya surāmbudhiḥ ||4||
tālavṛkṣaṃ jagatsarvaṃ kathitaṃ tu mahābhuja ||
vikhyātau sīramusalau pūrvameva mayā tava || 5 ||
vanamālā ca vikhyātā tathā yadukulodvaha ||
pṛthivī dhāryate tena saśailavanakānanā || 6 ||
pṛthivī tena kartavyā phaṇe tasya tu madhyame || 7 ||
phaṇāvaliptasya tayā pratiṣṭhā kāmāstu te yādavavaṃśamukhya ||
etaddhi rūpaṃ parameśvarasya viṣṇoracintyasya mayeritaṃ te || 8 ||
iti śrīviṣṇudhamottare tṛtīyakhaṇḍe mārkaṇḍeyavajrasaṃvāde'nantarūpanirūpaṇo nāma pañcaṣaṣṭitamo'dhyāyaḥ ||65||
<3.66>
mārkaṃḍeya uvāca ||
kartavyastumbururdevo mātṛmadhyagataḥ prabhuḥ ||
upaviṣṭo vṛṣe kāryaḥ śarvavatsa caturmukhaḥ || 1 ||
upaviṣṭastu kartavyaḥ sa tu pārthivasattama ||
mahādevena tasyoktaṃ rūpaṃ te sakalaṃ mayā || 2 ||
kapālaṃ tasya kartavyaṃ mātuluṅgadhare kare ||
mātuluṅgaṃ ca kartavyaṃ tryambakeṇa tadīritam || 3 ||
tasya dakṣiṇataḥ kāryaṃ tathā mātṛdvayaṃ bhavet ||
vāmatastasya kartavyaṃ mātṛdvitayameva ca || 4 ||
jayā ca vijayā caiva kartavyā dakṣiṇena tu ||
jayantī vāmataḥ kāryā tathā caivāparājitā || 5 ||
sarvāśca dvibhujāḥ kāryāścaturvaktrāstathaiva ca ||
sarvāsāṃ vāmahasteṣu kapālāni tu kārayet || 6 ||
daṇḍaḥ kāryo mahārāja dakṣiṇe tu jayā kare ||
vijayāyāḥ kare khaḍgaḥ kāryo bhinnāñjanaprabhaḥ || 7 ||
akṣamālādharakarāṃ jayantīṃ nṛpa kārayeta ||
bhindipālakarā kāryā tathā devyaparājitā || 8 ||
pādapīṭhagataṃ pādaṃ sarvāsāmeva kārayet ||
dakṣiṇaṃ yānagaṃ vāmaṃ kartavyaṃ nṛpa kuñcitam || 9 ||
jayā puruṣagā kāryā vijayā kauśikopamā ||
jayantī turagārūḍhā meghagā cāparājitā || || 3.66.10 ||
candrāṃśugauraḥ kartavyo mahādevastu tumburuḥ || 11 ||
devīpriyārthaṃ bhuvanasya goptā tāsāṃ sa madhye bhagavānatheśaḥ ||
bhakteṣu kāmānvibhadhannathāste sa pūjanīyastridaśārihantā || 12 ||
iti śrīviṣṇudharmottare tṛtīyakhaṇḍe mārkaṇḍeyavajrasaṃvāde devīcatuṣṭayasahi tatuṃburūpanirmāṇannāma ṣaṭṣaṣṭitamo'dhyāyaḥ || 66 ||

<3.67>
vajra uvāca ||
prāguktaṃ rūpanirmāṇaṃ tvayā candrāmbunāthayoḥ ||
tayorevāparaṃ brūhi rūpamakendurūpayoḥ || 1 ||
mārkaṇḍeya uvāca ||
raviḥ kāryaḥ śubhaśmaśruḥ sindūrābharaṇaprabhaḥ ||
sudīptaveśaḥ svākāraḥ sarvābharaṇasaṃyutaḥ || 2 ||
caturbāhurmahātejāḥ kavacenābhisaṃvṛtaḥ ||
kartavyā raśanā cāsya yāviyāṅgeti saṃjñitā || 3 ||
raśmayastasya kartavyā vāmadakṣiṇahastayoḥ ||
ūrdhvasragdāmasaṃsthānāḥ sarvapuṣpāñcitāḥ śubhāḥ||4||
surūparūpaḥ svākāro daṇḍī kāryosya vāmataḥ||
dakṣiṇe piṅgalo bhāge kartavyaścātipiṅgalaḥ ||5||
uddīptaveśau kartavyau tāvubhāvapi yādava ||
tayormūrdhani vinyastau karau kāryau vibhāvasoḥ || 6 ||
lekhanīpatrakakaraḥ kāryo bhavati piṅgalaḥ ||
carmaśūladharodevastathā yatnādvidhīyate || 7 ||
siṃho dhvajaśca kartavyastathā sūryasya vāmataḥ ||
catvāraścāsya kartavyāstanayāstasya pāśvaryoḥ ||8||
revantaśca yamaścaiva manudvitayameva ca ||
graharājo raviḥ kāryo grahairvā parivāritaḥ || 9 ||
rājñī ca rikṣubhā cchāyā tathā devī suvarcasā ||
catasraścāsya kartavyā patnyaśca paripārśvayoḥ || 3.67.10 ||
ekacakre'tha saptāśve ṣaḍrekhāṅke rathottame ||
upaviṣṭastu kartavyo devo vāruṇasārathiḥ||11||
gāyatryuṣṇiganuṣṭup ca bṛhatī paṅktireva ca ||
triṣṭup ca jagatī sapta chandāṃsyarkarathe hayāḥ || 12 ||
raśmibhiḥ karasaṃsthaistu dhārayatyakhilaṃ jagat ||
siṃho dhvajagataścāsya sākṣāddharmaḥ prakīrtitaḥ || 13 ||
raśanāsthaṃ jagatsarvaṃ devo dhārayate tathā ||
rājñī bhūrikṣubhā dyauśca cchāyā chāyā prakīrtitā ||14||
prabhā suvarcasā proktāstasya devasya patnayaḥ ||
raktavarṇaḥ sa bhagavāṃstejasā dhāma kāraṇāt||15||
asahyatejodhāritvād gūḍhagātrastathaiva ca ||
evaṃ sarvamayaṃ dhāma sūrya eva prakīrtitaḥ || 16 ||
rūpaṃ tathaitaddivasasya bharturmayeritaṃ sarvajaganmayasya ||
tejonidhānasya parojjvalasya sarvādhināthasya sanātanasya || 17 ||
iti śrīviṣṇudharmottare tṛtīyakhaṇḍe mā0 va0 saṃ0 ādityarūpavarṇanaṃ nāma saptaṣaṣṭitamo'dhyāyaḥ ||67 ||
<3.68>
mārkaṃḍeya uvāca||
candraḥ śvetavapuḥ kāryastathā śvetāmbaraḥ prabhuḥ ||
caturbāhurmahātejāḥ sarvābharaṇavāṃstathā || 1 ||
kumudau ca sitau kāryau tasya devasya hastayoḥ ||
kāntirmūrtimatī kāryā tasya pārśve tu dakṣiṇe || 2 ||
vāme śobhā tathā kāryā rūpeṇāpratimā bhuvi ||
cihnaṃ tathāsya siṃhāṃkaṃ vāmapārśvaikavadbhavet || 3 ||
daśāśvo vā rathaḥ kāryo dvicakrombarasārathī ||
srajaśca trimanāścaiva vṛṣo vādī naro hayaḥ || 4 ||
atho vāksaptadhātuśca haṃso vyomo mṛga stathā ||
ete te vāmataḥ sarve daśa candramaso hayāḥ ||5||
atha vaivāsya kartavyā aṣṭaviṃśatisaṅkhyakāḥ|
atyarthaṃ rūpasaṃyuktāḥ patnyo nakṣatrasaṃjñitāḥ || 6 ||
aśītidvyadhikāḥ kāryā bhāvā vā dehabhedayoḥ ||
purā devāsure yuddhe prākāmyena nṛpoḍubhiḥ || 7 ||
kṛtāni svaśarīrāṇi bahūni yaduna ndana ||
svanāthadevarūpāṇi tataste dānavā hatāḥ || 8 ||
nakṣatreṇa kṛtaṃ rūpaṃ yasya yasya surasya tu ||
sā caiva devatā tasya nakṣatrasya prakīrtitā || 9 ||
devāsure tato vṛtte prākāmyenaiva yādava ||
strīrūpadhāriṇo devāstāvantastu tataḥ kṛtāḥ || 3.68.10 ||
sarvalokasya dharmajña candramā vai pitā smṛtaḥ ||
tanminsṛṣṭe jagatsarvaṃ paraṃ harṣamupaiti ca || 11||
saumyamūrtiḥ parā jñeyā sā ca viṣṇormahātmanaḥ ||
harṣaprasādau vijñeyau kumudau candrahastayoḥ || 12 ||
śobhākāntī vinirdiṣṭe svarūpaṃ bhṛgunandana ||
apāṃ sāramayatvāddhi śuklavarṇastu candramāḥ || 13 ||
apāṃ hi dhāmāmṛtamāhurādyā mavāstu taccandrasamaḥ pradiṣṭam ||
siṃhadhvajaṃ dharmamuśanti rājandiśasturaṅgāḥ śaśinaḥ pradiṣṭāḥ || 14 ||
iti śrīviṣṇudharmottare tṛtīyakhaṇḍe mārkaṇḍeyavajrasaṃvāde śaśirūpanirūpaṇo nāmāṣṭaṣaṣṭitamo'dhyāyaḥ || 68 ||
<3.69>
vajra uvāca ||
grahāṇāṃ rūpanirmāṇaṃ samācakṣva mahābhuja ||
anenaiva prasaṅgena sarvajñastvaṃ mato mama || 1 ||
mārkaṇḍeya uvāca ||
bhaumo'gnitalpaḥ kartavyaścāṣṭāśve kāñcane rathe ||
viṣṇutalpo budhaḥ kāryo bhaumatalpe tathā rathe || 2 ||
taptajāmbūnadaḥ kāryo dvibhujaśca bṛhaspatiḥ ||
pustakaṃ cākṣamālāṃ ca karayostasya kārayet || 3 ||
sarvābharaṇayuktaśca tathā pītāmbaro guruḥ ||
aṣṭāśve kāñcane divye rathe dṛṣṭimanohare || 4 ||
śukraḥ śvetavapuḥ kāryaḥ śvetāmbaradharastathā ||
dvau karau kathitau tasya nidhipustakasaṃyutau || 5 ||
daśāśve ca rathe kāryo rājate bhṛgunandana ||
kṛṣṇavāsāstathā kṛṣṇaḥ śaniḥ kāryassirātataḥ ||6||
daṇḍākṣamālāsaṃyuktaḥ karadvitayabhūṣitaḥ||
kārṣṇāyase rathe kāryastathaivāṣṭabhujaṅgame || 7 ||
raupyai rathe tathāṣṭāśvo rāhuḥ kāryo vicakṣaṇaḥ ||
kevalaṃ mastakaṃ kāryaṃ bhujenaikena saṃyutam || 8 ||
karamekaṃ tu kartavyaṃ tasya śūnyaṃ tu dakṣiṇam ||
bhaumasya ca tathā kāryaṃ keto rūpaṃ vijānatā || 9 ||
kevalaṃ cāsya kartavyā daśarājaṃsturaṅgamāḥ ||3.69.10||
iti śrīviṣṇudharmottare tṛtīyakhaṇḍe mārkaṇḍeyavajrasaṃvāde bhaumādigraharūpanirmāṇo nāmaikonasaptatitamo'dhyāyaḥ || 69 ||
<3.70>
vajra uvāca ||
manudvayasya me rūpaṃ kathayasvāmitadyute ||
devendrasya ca dharmajña yamasyābhihitaṃ tvayā ||1||
mārkaṃḍeya uvāca ||
vartamāno manuḥ kāryo rājalakṣaṇasaṃyutaḥ ||
bhaviṣyantu tathā kāryaḥ sarvābharaṇavarjitaḥ || 2 ||
jaṭādharo'kṣamālī ca kamaṇḍaludharastathā ||
kṛśo'pi tejasā yuktastapasyabhiratastathā || 3 ||
kāryā yogena cānye'pi bhaviṣyā manavaśca ye ||
sāvarṇamanutulyena kāryo rūpeṇa yādava || 4 ||
atītā manavaḥ kāryā rājalakṣaṇalakṣitāḥ ||
pṛṣṭheśve sūryavatkāryo revantaśca tathā prabhuḥ || 5 ||
iti śrīvi0 tṛ0 kha0 mā0 saṃ0 manurūpanirūpaṇo nāma saptatitamo'dhyāyaḥ || 70 ||
<3.71>
vajra uvāca ||
grahāṇāṃ devatā proktā yāstvayā pūrvameva tu ||
tāsāṃ svarūpanirmāṇaṃ kathayasvāmitadyute || 1 ||
mārkaṇḍeya uvāca ||
pūrvameva mayā proktaṃ rūpaṃ vahnyambunāthayoḥ ||
tathā ca kathitaṃ tubhyaṃ viṣṇudevendrayoḥ purā||2||
caturmūrteḥ kumārasya rūpaṃ te vacmi yādava ||
kumāraśca tathā skando viśākhaśca gurustathā || 3 ||
kumāraḥ ṣaṇmukhaḥ kāryaḥ śikhaṇḍakavibhūṣaṇaḥ ||
raktāmbaradharaḥ kāryo mayūravaravāhanaḥ || 4 ||
kukkuṭaśca tathā ghaṇṭā tasya dakṣiṇahastayoḥ ||
patākāvaijayantī ca śaktiḥ kāryā ca vāmayoḥ || 5 ||
skando viśākhaśca guhaḥ kartavyaśca kumāravat ||
ṣaṇmukhāste na kartavyā na mayūragatāstathā || 6 ||
caturātmā hi bhagavānvāsudevaḥ sanātanaḥ ||
prādurbhūtaḥ kumārastu devasenāninīṣayā || 7 ||
aṣṭādaśabhujā kāryā bhadrakālī manoharā ||
ālīḍhasthānasaṃsthānā catuḥsiṃhe rathe sthitā || 8 ||
akṣamālā triśūlaṃ ca khaṅgaṃ carma ca yādava ||
bāṇacāpe ca kartavye śaṅkhapadmau tathaiva ca || 9 ||
suksrucau ca tathā kāryau tathā vedīkamaṇḍalū ||
daṇḍaśaktī ca kartavye kṛṣṇājinahutāśanau || 3.71.10 ||
hastānāṃ bhadrakālyāstu bhavecchāntikaraḥ karaḥ ||
ekaścaiva mahābhāga ratnapātradharo bhavet || 11 ||
haṃsayānena kartavyo na ca kāryaścaturmukhaḥ ||
brahmoktamaparaṃ rūpaṃ sarvaṃ kāryaṃ prajāpateḥ || 12 ||
vināyakastu kartavyo gajavaktraścaturbhujaḥ ||
śūlakaṃ cākṣamālāṃ ca tasya dakṣiṇahastayoḥ || 13 ||
pātraṃ modakapūrṇaṃ tu paraśuścaiva vāmataḥ ||
dantaścāsya na kartavyo vāmo ripuniṣūdana || 14 ||
pādapīṭhakṛtaḥ pāda eka āsanago bhavet ||
pūrṇamodakapātraṃ tu karāgre tasya kārayeta || 15 ||
lambodarastathā kāryaḥ stabdhakarṇaśca yādava ||
vyāghracarmāmbaradharaḥ sarpayajñopavītivān || 16 ||
viśvakarmā nu kartavyaḥ sūryarūpadharaḥ prabhuḥ ||
sa daśapāṇirdvibhujastejomūrtidharo bhavet || 17 ||
viśvaṃ karoti vai yasmādviśvakarmā tataḥ smṛtaḥ ||
viśvakṛdbhagavānviṣṇurviśvakarmā sa ucyate || 18 ||
iti śrīviṣṇudharmottare tṛtīyakhaṇḍe mārkaṇḍeyavajrasaṃvāde kumārabhadrakālīcaturvaktragajavaktraviśvakarmarūpanirūpaṇo nāmaikasaptatitamo'dhyāyaḥ || 71 ||
<3.72>
|| vajra uvāca || ||
vasūnāṃ deva me rūpaṃ tathaivāṅgirasaṃ śubham ||
viśveṣāṃ caiva devānāṃ rudrāṇāmapi devatāḥ||1||
bhṛgūṇāmatha sādhyānāṃ cādityānāṃ tathaiva ca ||
marutāṃ ca tathā rūpamācakṣva surapūjita|| 2||
mārkaḍeya uvāca ||
prājāpatyena rūpeṇa dharo nāma vasurbhavet ||
vaiṣṇavena ca rūpeṇa dhruvo nāma prakīrtitaḥ||3||
somaścāndreṇa rūpeṇa vāyavyena tathā'nilaḥ ||
āgneyenānalaḥ kāryaḥ prabhāso vāruṇena ca||4||
viśvedevāstathā kāryāḥ śakrarūpadharāḥ surāḥ||
tiryaglalāṭenākṣṇā ca nāgarājo vivarjitaḥ || 5 ||
jīvarūpeṇa kartavyā devāścāṅgirasastathā ||
māheśvareṇa rudrāśca sādhyākṣā vaiṣṇavena tu || 6 ||
ādityāḥ sūryarūpeṇa ceti dvādaśa yādava ||
pṛthakkriyāyāṃ teṣāṃ tu navārkasadṛśāḥ smṛtāḥ ||7||
viṇvindravaruṇāḥ kāryāḥ yathā pūrvaṃ mayeritāḥ ||
kartavyāḥ śukrarūpeṇa maruto nāma devatāḥ || 8 ||
iti śrīviṣṇudharmottare tṛtīyakhaṇḍe mārkaṇḍeyavajrasaṃvāde vasurūpanirmāṇo nāma dvisaptatitamo 'dhyāyaḥ || 72 ||
<3.73>
|| vajra uvāca ||
anyeṣāmapi devānāṃ rūpanirmoṇameva ca ||
kathayasva mahābhāga tvaṃ hi sarvaviducyase || 1 ||
mārkaṇḍeya uvāca ||
prājāpatyena rūpeṇa kartavyaḥ kaśyapaḥ prabhuḥ ||
aditirditirdanuḥ kāṣṭhā danāyuḥ siṃhikā muniḥ||2||
kadrūḥ krodhā darā prādhā vinatā surabhiḥ khaśā ||
sarvāḥ surūpāḥ kartavyā dvibhujāḥ pūrvamātaraḥ || 3 ||
aditiśca ditiścaiva vinatā surabhistathā ||
dṛṣṭirāsāṃ tu kartavyā saumyā yādavanandana || 4 ||
anenaiva tu rūpeṇa sarvā vai devamātaraḥ ||
kartavyā yādavaśreṣṭha rūpanirmāṇakarmaṇā || 5 ||
kaśyapasyāparāḥ patnyaḥ kāryā vyākulalocanāḥ ||
viṣṇurūpadharaḥ kāryo dhruvo grahagaṇeśvaraḥ || 6 ||
cakraraśmikaraḥ śrīmāndvibhujaḥ saumyadarśanaḥ ||
bhaviṣyamanurūpeṇa kāryo'gastyo mahāmuniḥ || 7 ||
rūpaṃ tadevaṃ kathitaṃ bhṛgvādīnāṃ mahātmanām ||
śukraputrau jayantaśca kartavyo rūpasaṃyutaḥ || 8 ||
cāpabāṇakaraḥ śrīmāndvibhujaḥ saumyadarśanaḥ ||
balo yamasutaḥ kāryaścarmaśūladharaḥ prabhuḥ || 9 ||
puṣkaraśca tathā kāryaḥ padmapatrasamaprabhaḥ ||
khaḍgaṃ ca pustakaṃ cobhau karayostasya kārayeta || 3.73.10 ||
candraputrī tathā jyotsnā rūpeṇāpratimā bhuvi ||
vāmapārśve tu kartavyā sarvābharaṇabhūṣaṇā || 11 ||
surūparūpaḥ kartavyo ratnapātrakaro vibhuḥ ||
dvidaṃṣṭrāyuktavadano dhanādirnalakūbaraḥ ||12||
dhanadasya tu rūpeṇa maṇibhadraṃ vidurbudhāḥ ||
na kāryā śibikā tasya sabhāryo naravāhanaḥ || 13 ||
vāyuputrastu kartavyo vāyurūpaḥ purojavaḥ ||
atīva rūpavānkāryo varcāḥ somasutaḥ prabhuḥ || 14 ||
līlākamalapāṇistu varcasvī yena jāyate ||
nandī kāryastrinetrastu caturbāhurmahābhujaḥ || 15 ||
sindūrāruṇasaṅkāśo vyāghracarmāmbaracchadaḥ ||
triśūlabhindipālau ca karayostasya kārayet || 16 ||
śirogataṃ tṛtīyantu tarjayantaṃ tathā parama ||
ālokamānaṃ kartavyaṃ dūrādāgāmikaṃ janam || 17 ||
anenaiva ca rūpeṇa vīrabhadraṃ vidurbudhāḥ ||
upariṣṭātpravakṣyāmi dharmarūpamahaṃ tava || 18 ||
arthaḥ kāryastu rūpeṇa dhanadasya mahātmanaḥ ||
kāmadevastu kartavyo rūpeṇāpratimo bhuvi || 19 ||
aṣṭabāhuḥ sa kartavyaḥ śaṅkhapadmavibhūṣaṇaḥ ||
cāpabāṇakaraścaiva madādañcitalocanaḥ || 3.73.20 ||
ratiḥ prītistathā śaktirmadaśaktistathojjvalā ||
catasrastasya kartavyāḥ patnyo rūpamanoharāḥ || 21 ||
catvāraśca karāstasya kāryā bhāryāstanopagāḥ ||
ketuśca makaraḥ kāryaḥ pañcabāṇa mukho mahān || 22 ||
kareṇa kaṭigenātha nidrāṃ vāmena kārayet ||
dakṣiṇe tu tathā pārśve tasyāḥ saṅkarṣaṇo bhavet || 23 ||
vāsudevastathā vāme maikāneśā tathā sutaḥ ||
rūpaṃ lakṣmyāḥ pravakṣyāmi upariṣṭāttavānagha || 294 ||
caturbhujā ca kartavyā tathā devī sarasvatī| ||
akṣamālā triśūlaṃ ca pustakaṃ ca kamaṇḍalum || 25 ||
tasyā kareṣu kāryāṇi yathāvadyadunandana ||
kāryā sakalaśā devī vāruṇī cātisundarī || 26 ||
lambodarī tu kartavyā raktāmbarapayodharā ||
śūlahastā mahābhāgā bhujapraharaṇā tathā || 27 ||
bṛhadrathā ca kartavyā bahubāhustathaiva sā ||
cāmuṇḍā kathitā saiva sarvasattvavaśaṅkarī || 28 ||
tathaivāntramukhī śuṣkā śuṣkā kāryā viśeṣataḥ ||
bahubāhuyutā devī bhujagaiḥ pariveṣṭitā || 29 ||
kapālamālinī bhīmā tathā khaṭvāṅgadhāriṇī ||
śivā dūtī tu kartavyā śṛgālavadanā śubhā || 3.73.30 ||
ālīḍhasthānasaṃsthānā tathā rājaṃścaturbhujā||
asṛkpātradharā devī khaḍgaśūladharā tathā || 31 ||
caturthaṃ tu karaṃ tasyāstathā kāryaṃ tu sāmiṣam ||
devatulyena cihnena yathārūpeṇa mātaraḥ || 32 ||
kartavyā devanāmāṅkā yā mayā te puroditāḥ ||
asṛkpātrakarāḥ kāryāḥ sāmiṣaiśca tathā karaiḥ || 33 ||
nṛtyamānā mahābhāgā yāsāṃ rūpaṃ tu noditam||
pūrvā gajagatā bālā raktavarṇā tu digbhavet || 34 ||
kareṇugā bṛhatkanyā padmābhā pūrvadakṣiṇā ||
rathasthā dakṣiṇā pītā tathā syātprāptayauvanā || 35 ||
uṣṭragā kṛṣṇapītā ca varuṇā yāmyapaścimā ||
yauvanādvicyutā kṛṣṇā paścimā turagasthitā || 36 ||
āsannapalitā nīlā vaḍavā yadunandana ||
śvetā narabhṛtā vṛddhā tathā bhavati cottarā || 37 ||
ativṛddhā vṛṣasthā ca śuklā pūrvottarā bhavet ||
adhastātpṛthivī tulyā cordhvā gaganasaṃsthitā || 38 ||
kālaḥ karālavadano nityagaśca vibhīṣaṇaḥ |||
pāśahastaśca kartavyaḥ sarpavṛścikaromavān || 39 ||
jvarastripādaḥ kartavyastrinetrairvadanaistribhiḥ ||
bhasmapraharaṇo raudrastribāhurvyākulekṣaṇaḥ || 3.73.40 ||
dhanvantariśca kartavyaḥ surūpaḥ priyadarśanaḥ ||
karadvayagataṃ cāsya sāmṛtaṃ kalaśaṃ bhavet || 41 ||
sāmavedastu kartavyaḥ kiṃ tu śvavadanaḥ prabhuḥ ||
atha vā devarūpeṇa vedāḥ kāryā vicakṣaṇaiḥ || 42 ||
ṛgvedastu smṛto brahmā yajurvedastu vāsavaḥ ||
sāmavedastathā viṣṇuḥ śambhuścātharvaṇo bhavet || 43 ||
śikṣā prajāpatirjñeyā kalpo brahmā prakīrtitaḥ ||
sarasvatī vyākaraṇaṃ niruktaṃ varuṇaḥ prabhuḥ || 44 ||
chando kṣitistathaivāgnirjyotiṣaṃ bhagavānraviḥ ||
mīmāṃsā bhagavānsomo nyāyamārgaḥ samīraṇaḥ || 45 ||
dharmaśca dharmaśāstrāṇi purāṇaṃ ca tathā manuḥ ||
itihāsaṃ prajādhyakṣo dhanurvedaṃ śatakratuḥ || 46 ||
āyurvedastathā sākṣāddevo dhanvantariḥ prabhuḥ ||
phalavedaṃ mahī devī nṛtyaśāstraṃ maheśvaraḥ || 47 ||
saṅkarṣaṇaḥ pāñcarātraṃ rudraḥ pāśupataṃ tathā ||
pātañjalamanantaśca sāṃkhyaśca kapilo muniḥ || 48||
arthaśāstrāṇi sarvāṇi dhanādhyakṣaḥ prakīrtitaḥ ||
kalāśāstrāṇi sarvāṇi kāmadevo jagadguruḥ || 49 ||
anyāni yāni śāstrāṇi yatkartṝṇi pracakṣate||
sa caiva devatā tasya śāstra kāvyasya dehavat||3.73.50||
rūpaṃ tu kālāvayavasya kāryaṃ svadevatārūpapasamaṃ ripughna ||
etaṃ tavoktaṃ surasattamānāṃ rūpaṃ mayā te yaduvaṃśacandra || 51||
iti śrīviṣṇudharmottare tṛtīyakhaṇḍe mārkaṇḍeyavajrasaṃvāde devatārūpanirmāṇo nāma trisaptatitamo'dhyāyaḥ || 73 ||
<3.74>
vajra uvāca ||
liṅgasya rūpanirmāṇaṃ kathayasva mamānagha || 1 ||
mārkaṇḍeya uvāca ||
liṅge hi pūjitaṃ pūjā kṛtā tu jagato bhavet ||
bhogosya vṛttaḥ kartavyo bhāgamaṣṭāsrameva tu || 2 ||
caturasraṃ tathā bhāgaṃ kartavyaṃ bhūridakṣiṇama ||
vṛttaṃ dṛśyaṃ ta kartavyaṃ aṣṭāsraṃ piṇḍikāgatam || 3 ||
caturasraṃ tu kartavyaṃ brahmapīṭhagataṃ tathā ||
adhastādbhadrapīṭhasya brahmapīṭhaṃ vidurbudhāḥ || 4 ||
liṅgopariṣṭāttu gatā purastāllekhā suvṛttā tu tathaiva kāryā ||
ūrdhvāyatā buddhiguṇā tathāsyā yā brahmasūtre vibudhaiḥ pradiṣṭā||5||
iti śrīviṣṇudharmottare tṛtīyakhaṇḍe mārkaṇḍeyavajrasaṃvāde liṅgarūpanirmāṇo nāma catuḥsaptatitamo 'dhyāya ||74||
<3.75>
vajra uvāca ||
vyomarūpaṃ samācakṣva sarvadharmabhṛtāṃ vara||
vyomni tu pūjite pūjā kṛtā syājjagato bhavet|| 1 ||
mārkaṇḍeya uvāca ||
caturasraṃ bhavenmūle tato vṛttaṃ mahābhuja ||
tatolpacaturasraṃ ca caturasraṃ tato bhavet || 2 ||
tatolpacaturasraṃ ca meruvatsaṃsthitaṃ tataḥ ||
bhadrapīṭhamidaṃ proktaṃ vyomabhāgaṃ tṛtīyakam || 3 ||
sarveṣāṃ bhadrapīṭhānāmetallakṣaṇamucyate ||
sambhavaścaturasraṃ tu madhyabhāgaṃ prakīrtitam || 4 ||
bhadrapīṭhaṃ tato bhāgaṃ tatra padma niveśayet ||
śubhāṣṭapatraṃ tanmadhye karṇikāyāṃ divākaram ||85||
patreṣu kalpayettatra dikpālām̐śca yathādiśam ||
bhadrapīṭhamadhastāttu pṛthivīṃ parikalpayet || 6 ||

tasya sampūjanaṃ kṛtvā sarvānkāmānavāpnuyāta || 8 ||

iti śrīviṣṇudharmottare tṛtīyakhaṇḍe mārkaṇḍeyavajrasaṃvāde vyomarūpanirmāṇo nāma pañcasaptatitamo'dhyāyaḥ||75||
<3.76>
vajra uvāca||
naranārāyaṇau kāryau kena rūpeṇa saṃyutau||

iti śrīvipṇudha0tṛ0kha0mā0 saṃ0 naranārāyaṇarūpanirmāṇo nāma ṣaṭsaptatitamo'dhyāyaḥ ||76||
<3.77>
vajra uvāca ||
dharmasya rūpanirmāṇaṃ jānasya ca parantapa ||
vairāgyasya va dharmajña tathaiśvaryasya me vada || 1 ||
mārkaṃḍeya uvāca ||
caturvaktraścatuṣpādaścaturbāhussitāmbaraḥ ||
sarvābharaṇavāñchveto dharmaḥ kāryo vijānatā || 2 ||
dakṣiṇe cākṣamālāṃ ca tasya vāme tu pustakam ||
mūrtimānvyavasāyastu kāryo dakṣiṇabhāgagaḥ || 3 ||
vāmabhāgagataḥ kāryaḥ sukhaḥ parama rūpavān ||
kāryau dharmakarau mūrdhni vinyastau ca tathā tayoḥ || 4 ||
akṣamālā smṛtaḥ kālaḥ pustakaṃ cāgamaṃ smṛtam ||
yajñaḥ satyaṃ tapo dānaṃ tasya vaktracatuṣṭayam || 5 ||

dharmaḥ sthitaḥ syādyadi caika eva bhāryānvitaścedatha copadiṣṭaḥ || 3.77.10 ||
iti śrīviṣṇudha0 tṛ0 kha0 mā0 saṃ0 dharmarūpanirmāṇo nāma saptasaptatitamo'dhyāyaḥ || 77 ||
<3.78>
mārkaṇḍeya uvāca ||
ya eva bhagavānviṣṇurnarasiṃha vapurdharaḥ ||

pīnaskandakaṭigrīvaḥ kṛśamadhyaḥ kṛśodaraḥ ||

gadāmastakavinyastakaradvitayabhūṣaṇaḥ || 9 ||
jvālāmālākulavapuḥ śaṅkhapadmadharaḥ prabhuḥ ||
mūrtimatpṛthivīhastanyastapādo'tha votthitaḥ || 3.78.10 ||
śaṅkhacakragadāpadmalāñchanaiḥ śobhitaḥ karaiḥ ||
agnijvālākulāvartavibhūṣitatanūruhaḥ || 11 ||

iti śrīvi0 dha0 tṛ0 kha0 mā0 saṃ0 nṛsiṃharūpanirmāṇo nāmāṣṭasaptatitamo'dhyāyaḥ || 78 ||
<3.79>
|| mārkaṃḍeya uvāca ||
aiśvaryamaniruddhastu varāho bhagavānhariḥ ||

iti śrīvi0 dha0 tṛ0 kha0 mā0 va0 saṃ0 varāharūpanirmāṇo nāmaikonāśītitamo'dhyāyaḥ || 79 ||
<3.80>
vajra uvāca ||

<3.81>
vajra uvāca ||
padmanābhasya me rūpaṃ prabrūhi jagatāmpateḥ ||
yatrāsya padmasambhūto devadevaḥ pitāmahaḥ||1||
mārkaṃḍeya uvāca||
jalamadhyagataḥ kāryaḥ śeṣaḥ pannagadarśanaḥ|
phaṇapuñjamahāratnadurnirīkṣyaśirodharaḥ || 2 ||
devadevastu kartavyastatra suptaścaturbhujaḥ |9
ekaḥ pādo'sya kartavyo lakṣmyutsaṅgagataḥ prabho || 3 ||

iti śrīviṣṇudha0 tṛ0 kha0 mā0 va0 saṃ padmanābharūpanirmāṇo nāmaikāśītitamo'dhyāyaḥ || 81 ||
<3.82>
vajra uvāca ||
ācakṣva rūpaṃ lakṣmyā me bhṛguvaṃśavivardhana ||
yā mātā sarvalokasya patnī viṣṇormahātmanaḥ || || 1 ||
mārkaṇḍeya uvāca ||
hareḥ samīpe kartavyā lakṣmīstu dvibhujā nṛpa ||

siṃhāsane'syāḥ kartavyaṃ kamalaṃ cārukarṇikam ||
