padmaṃ lakṣmīkare viddhi vibhavaṃ dvijapuṅgava || 9 ||
hastidvayaṃ vijānīhi śaṃkhapadmāvubhau nidhī ||
samutthitā vā kartavyā śaṃkhāmbujakarā tathā||3.82.10||
samukṣitā mahābhāgā padme padmāntaraprabhā ||
dvibhujā cārusarvāṃgī sarvābharaṇabhūṣaṇā || 11 ||
dvau ca maulicarau mūrdhni kāryā vidyādharau śubhau || 12 ||
karābhyāṃ maulilagnābhyāṃ dakṣiṇābhyāṃ virājitau ||
karābhyāṃ khaḍgadhāribhyāṃ devīvīkṣaṇatatparau || 13 ||
rājaśrīḥ svargalakṣmīśca brāhmī lakṣmīstathaiva ca ||
jayalakṣmīśca kartavyā tasyā devyāḥ samīpagāḥ || 14 ||
sarvāḥ surūpāḥ kartavyāstathā ca suvibhūṣaṇāḥ || 15 ||
lakṣmīḥ sthitā sā kamale tu yasmim̐stāṃ keśavaṃ viddhi mahānubhāva ||
vinā kṛtā sā madhusūdanena kṣaṇaṃ na santiṣṭhati lokamātā || 16 ||
iti śrīviṣṇudharmottare tṛtīyakhaṇḍe mārkaṇḍeyavajrasaṃvāde lakṣmīrūpanirmāṇo nāma dvyaśītitamo'dhyāyaḥ ||82||
<3.83>
vajra uvāca ||
rūpeṇa kena kartavyo viśvarūpadharo hariḥ ||
etaṃ me saṃśayaṃ chindhi tvaṃ hi sarvaviducyase || 1 ||
mārkaṇḍeya uvāca ||
ādau devasya kartavyāścatvāro vai stavonmukhāḥ ||
teṣāmupari kartavyāstathā māheśvarāḥ punaḥ || 2 ||
īśānaṃ vaktrahīnāste tathā proktā mayā purā ||
teṣāmupari kartavyā mukhyā brāhmī yatheritā || 3 ||
tataścānyamukhāḥ kāryāstiryagūrdhve tathaiva ca ||
sarveṣāmapi devānāṃ tathānyānapi kārayet || 4 ||
ye mukhāḥ sattvajātānāṃ nānārūpā vibhāgaśaḥ ||
yāvanto dṛṣṭayaḥ proktāścitrasūtre mahātmabhiḥ || 5 ||
darśanīyāstu tāḥ sarvāstasya mūrdhasu bhāgaśaḥ ||
nānāvidhāni sattvāni mukhairanyaistathaiva ca || 6 ||
grasamānaḥ sa kartavyaḥ sarvaiḥ sattvabhayaṅkaraiḥ ||
kāryāṇyuddhūyamānāni mukhāḥ kecana te śubhāḥ || 7 ||
yathā śaktyā ca kartavyāstasya devasya bāhavaḥ ||
hastāni yāni dṛṣṭāni nṛttaśāstre mahātmabhiḥ || 8 ||
tāni sarvāṇi kāryāṇi tasya devasya bāhuṣu ||
hastāḥ kāryāstathaivānye sarvāyudhavibhūṣaṇāḥ || 9 ||
yajñadaṇḍadharāścānye śilpabhāṇḍadharāstathā ||
kālabhāṇḍadharāścānye vādyabhāṇḍadharāḥ pare || 3.83.10 ||
rūpamanyatprakartavyaṃ vaikuṇṭhavadato'cyute ||
tatrāpi gātragāstasya darśayeccitrakarmaṇā || 11 ||
trailokyaṃ sakalaṃ rājanyathāśāstrānusārataḥ ||
darśanīyāni varṇāni sarvāṇyeva mahātmanaḥ || 12 ||
bahurūpasya devasya vahumastakagāni tu || 13 ||
evaṃ hi śaktyā jagatāṃ pradhānaḥ samagrarūpo bhagavānhi kāryaḥ ||
kārtsnyena rūpaṃ puruṣottamasya vaktuṃ na śakyaṃ kuta eva kartum || 14 ||
iti śrīviṣṇudharmottare tṛtīyakhaṇḍe mā0 va0 saṃ0 viśvarūpanirmāṇo nāma tryaśītitamo'dhyāyaḥ || 83 ||
<3.84>
mārkaṇḍeya uvāca ||
aiḍūkarūpanirmāṇaṃ śṛṇuṣva gadato mama ||
aiḍūkapūjanātpūjā kṛtāsya jagato bhavet || 1 ||
bhadrapīṭhaṃ budhaḥ kuryātsopānaiḥ śobhanairyutam ||
caturbhiryādavaśreṣṭha yathādiśamarindama || 2 ||
tasyopariṣṭādaparaṃ bhadrapīṭhaṃ tu kārayet ||
tasyopariṣṭādaparaṃ tādṛgvidhamarindama || 3 ||
tasyopariṣṭātkartavyaṃ liṅgarūpaṃ vijānatā ||
tattu tatrāpi kartavyaṃ liṃgarekhā virājitam || 4 ||
tasya madhye dhruvāṃ yaṣṭiṃ caturasrāṃ tu kārayet ||
tasyopariṣṭātkartavyā bhūmikāstu trayodaśa || 5 ||
tasyopariṣṭātkartavyaṃ tathaivāmalatārakam ||
tasyopari punaryaṣṭiḥ kāryā rājansuvartulā || 6 ||
samārdhacandramadhyasthacandrakeṇa virājitā ||
bhūmikā yā mayā proktā tathaivāmalatārakam || 7 ||
bhuvanāste tvayā jñeyāstathā rājam̐ścaturdaśa||
liṃge maheśvaro devo vṛttā yaṣṭiḥ pitāmahaḥ || 8 ||
caturasrā tu yā yaṣṭiḥ sa ca devo janārdanaḥ ||
guṇarūpeṇa vijñeyaṃ bhadrapīṭhatrayaṃ tathā || 9 ||
guṇādhānamiti proktaṃ trailokyaṃ sacarācaram ||
adhastādbhuvanānāṃ tu liṅgopari tathā nṛpa || 3.84.10 ||
lokapālāśca kartavyāḥ śūlahastāścaturdiśam ||
virūḍho dhṛtarāṣṭraśca virūpākṣaśca yādavaḥ || || 11 ||
kuberaśca mahātejāḥ sūryaveśadharāḥ śubhāḥ ||
sarve kavacinaḥ kāryāḥ śubhābharaṇabhūṣitāḥ || 12 ||
virūpākṣaṃ vijānīhi śakradeva gaṇeśvaram ||
dhṛtarāṣṭraṃ vijānīhi yamaṃ bhuvananāyakam || 13 ||
virūpākṣaṃ vijānīhi varuṇaṃ yādasāmpatim ||
rājarājaṃ vijānīhi kuberaṃ dhanadaṃ prabhum || 14 ||
aiḍūkarūpaṃ kathitaṃ mayaitatprajāhitākhyaṃ yaduvaṃśamukhya ||15||
iti śrīviṣṇudharmottare tṛtīyakhaṇḍe mārkaṇḍeyavajrasaṃvāde aiḍūkarūpanirmāṇo nāma caturaśītitamo'dhyāyaḥ||84||
<3.85>
vajra uvāca ||
vāsudevasya devasya bahurūpasya bhārgava ||
brūhi me rūpanirmāṇaṃ śāśvatasya mahātmanaḥ || 1 ||
mārkaṇḍeya uvāca ||
ekavaktraścaturbāhuḥ saumyarūpaḥ sudarśanaḥ ||
salilādhmātameghābhassarvābharaṇabhūṣitaḥ ||2 ||
kaṇṭhena śubharekheṇa kambutulyena rājatā ||
varābharaṇayuktena kuṇḍalottamabhūṣiṇā || 3 ||
aṅgadī baddhakeyūro vanamālāvibhūṣaṇaḥ ||
urasā kaustubhaṃ bibhratkirīṭaṃ śirasā tathā || 4 ||
śiraḥpadmastathaivāsya kartavyaścārukarṇikaḥ ||
muṣṭiśliṣṭāyatabhujastanustāmranakhāṅguliḥ || 5 ||
madhyena trivalībhaṅgaḥśobhitena sudhāruṇā ||
strīrūpadhāriṇī kṣoṇī kāryā tatpādamadhyagā || 6 ||
tatkarasthāṃghriyugulo devaḥ kāryo janārdanaḥ ||
tālāntarapadanyāsaḥ kiñcinniṣkrāntadakṣiṇaḥ || 7 ||
antardṛśā mahī kāryā devadarśanavismitā ||
devaśca kaṭivāsena kāryo jānvavalambinā || 8 ||
vanamālā ca kartavyā devajānvavalambinī ||
yajñopavītaḥ kartavyo nābhideśamupāgataḥ || 9 ||
utphullakamalaṃ pāṇau kuryāddevasya dakṣiṇe ||
vāma pāṇigataṃ śaṅkhaṃ śaṅkhākāraṃ tu kārayet || 3.85.10 ||
dakṣiṇe tu gadā devī tanumadhyā sulocanā ||
strīrūpadhāriṇī mugdhā sarvābharaṇabhūṣaṇā || 11 ||
paśyantī devadeveśaṃ kāryā cāmaradhāriṇī ||
kāryaṃ tanmūrdhnivinyastaṃ devahastaṃ tu dakṣiṇam||12||
vāmabhāgagataścakraḥ kāryo lambodarastathā|||
sarvābharaṇa saṃyukto nṛttavisphāritekṣaṇaḥ||13||
kartavyaścāmarakaro devavīkṣaṇatatparaḥ||
kāryo devakaro vāmo vinyastastasya mūrdhani||14||
vajra uvāca ||
kiṃ bhayaṃ tasya devasya nityaṃ sarvāyudhodyataḥ ||
yatastiṣṭhati sarvātmā bhayahā tridivaukasām || 15 ||
mārkaṇḍeya uvāca ||
naitānyāyudhajātāni paramā rthena yādava ||
mahābhūtānyathaitāni harirdhārayate prabhuḥ || 16 ||
khaṃ vijānīhi devasya kare śaṃkho mahābhujaḥ ||
cakraṃ jānīhi pavanaṃ gadāṃ tejastathā vibhoḥ || 17||
āpaḥ padmaṃ vijānīhi pādamadhye vyavasthitam ||
mahābhūtānyathaitāni tyaktāni hariṇā nṛpa || 18 ||
kṣiprameva viśīryante tena dhārayate hariḥ ||
viṣṇurjīvaḥ samākhyātastena tyaktāḥ śarīragāḥ ||19||
bhūtāni paśya pratyakṣaṃ śīryamāṇāni sarvaśaḥ ||
bhūtairdevadhṛtailoko dhāryate yadunandana ||
teṣāṃ dhāraṇaśaktiryā sā jñeyā viṣṇukāritā || 3.85.20 ||
vāsudevasya rūpeṇa kāryaḥ saṅkarṣaṇaḥ prabhuḥ ||
sa tu śuklavapuḥ kāryo nīlavāsā yadūttama || || 21 ||
gadāsthāne ca musalaṃ cakrasthāne ca lāṅgalam||
kartavyau tanumadhyau tau nṛrūpau rūpasaṃyutau|| 22||
vāsudevasya rūpeṇa pradyumnaśca tathā bhavet ||
sa tu dūrvāṅkuraśyāmassitavāsā vidhīyate || 23 ||
cakrasthāne bhaveccāpaṃ gadāsthāne tathā śaram ||
tathāvidhau tu kartavyau tathā musasalāṅgalau ||24||
etadeva tathā rūpamaniruddhasya kārayet ||
padmapatrābhavapuṣo raktāmbaradharasya tu || 25 ||
cakrasthāne bhaveccarma gadāsthāne'sireva ca ||
carma syāccakrarūpeṇa prāṃśuḥ khaṅgo vidhīyate || 26 ||
cakrādīnāṃ svarūpāṇi kiñcinmūrdhasu darśayet ||
ramyāṇyāyudharūpāṇi cakrādīnyeva yādava || 27 ||
vāmapārśvagatāḥ kāryā devānāṃ pravarā dhvajāḥ ||
svapatākāyutā rājanyaṣṭisthāste yatheritāḥ || 28 ||
pītavarṇaṃ pratīhāraṃ vāsudevasya kārayet ||
subhadravasubhadrākhyau vīrau prāsakarāvubhau || 29 ||
āṣāḍho yajñatātaśca kāryaḥ saṅkarṣaṇasya tu ||
nalivarṇau mahābhāgau tathā mudgaradhāriṇau || 3.85.30 ||
pradyumnasya pratīhārau jayo vijaya eva ca ||
śvetavarṇau mahābhāga tathā khaṅgadharāvubhau || 31 ||
āmodaśca pramodaśca mahābalaparākramau ||
aniruddhasya vijñeyau pratīhārau sureśvarau || 32 ||
raktavarṇau mahābhāga tathobhau śaktidhāriṇau ||
sarve surūpāḥ kartavyāḥ sarvābharaṇadhāriṇaḥ || 33 ||
dvibāhavastu kartavyāstarjanyucchritapāṇayaḥ ||
dṛṣṭisteṣāṃ tu kartavyā dvāranyastā mahābhuja || 34 ||
śakraṃ subhadraṃ jānīhi vasubhadraṃ hutāśanam ||
āṣāḍhaṃ devadeveśaṃ yamaṃ jānīhi yādava || 35 ||
yajñatātaṃ virūpākṣaṃ viddhi devamaninditam ||
jayaṃ jānīhi varuṇaṃ yādogaṇamaheśvaram || 36 ||
pavanaṃ ca vijānīhi vijayaṃ yadunandana ||
āmodaṃ dhanadaṃ viddhi pramodaṃ śivameva ca || 37 ||
ete kāryāśca vā sarve dinagoktavapurdharāḥ ||
eta eva tathā proktā bhūya eva mahātmabhiḥ || 38 ||
aṣṭau devagaṇā rājam̐stanme nigadataḥ śṛṇu ||
vāsudevapratīhārāvaṇimā laghimā smṛtau || 39 ||
saṃkarṣaṇapratīhārau mahimāprāptisaṃjñitau ||
prākāmyaṃ ca tatheśitvaṃ pradyumne kathitāvubhau || 3.85.40 ||
vaśitvaṃ viddhi cāmodaṃ yatra kāmāvasāyitā ||
pramodamatha jānīhi sarvalokanamaskṛtam || 41 ||
etatte rūpanirmāṇaṃ caturmūrtermayeritam ||
ekamūrtidharaḥ kāryā vaikuṇṭhetyabhiśabditāḥ || 42 ||
caturmukhaḥ sa kartavyaḥ prāguktavadanaḥ prabhuḥ ||
caturmūrtiḥ sa bhavati kṛte mukhacatuṣṭaye || 43 ||
pūrvaṃ saumyamukhaṃ kāryaṃ yattu mukhyatamaṃ viduḥ ||
kartavyaṃ siṃhavaktrābhaṃ jñānavaktraṃ tu dakṣiṇam || 44 ||
paścimaṃ vadanaṃ raudraṃ yattadaiśvaryamucyate ||
caturvaktrasya kartavyaṃ rūpamanyattatheritam || 45 ||
caturbhujo vā kartavyastārkṣyo yādavanandana ||
gāruḍaśca tathā kāryo dharmajña racitāñjaliḥ || 46 ||
sukhopaviṣṭaistatpṛṣṭhe tatkarastho hi paṅkajaḥ ||
upaviṣṭau gadā cakrau kartavyau tārkṣyapakṣayoḥ || 47 ||
tārkṣyāsanasthā śrīḥ kāryā vāmotsaṅgagatāpi vā ||
śeṣabhogopaviṣṭo vā kāryo devo manoharaḥ || 48 ||
tatphaṇaireva racitaṃ durnirīkṣyaṃ prabhormukham ||
śeṣabhogopaviṣṭasya śūnyaṃ karacatuṣṭayam || 49 ||
kāryaṃ cakraṃ gadā kāryā sadehā tatsamīpagā ||
lakṣmīḥ kāryā tathā tasya śeṣabhogagatāpi vā || 3.85.50 ||
śeṣaparyaṅkaśayane kāryo vā bhagavānhariḥ ||
airāvatī sumudbhūte yathārūpo mayeritaḥ || 51||
nṛsiṃha rūpaṃ kathitaṃ vārāhaṃ kāpilaṃ tathā ||
viśvarūpaṃ hayagrīvaṃ padmanābhaṃ tathaiva ca || 52 ||
brāhmyaṃ raudraṃ ca rāmīya puṣkareṇa mahātmanā ||
kartavyo vāmano devaḥ saṅkaṭairgātraparvabhiḥ || 53 ||
pīnagātraśca kartavyo daṇḍī cādhyayanodyataḥ ||
dūrvāśyāmaśca kartavyaḥ kṛṣṇājinadharastathā || 54 ||
sajalāmbudasaṅkāśastathā kāryastrivikramaḥ ||
daṇḍapāśadharaḥ kāryaḥ śaṅkhasañcumbitādharaḥ || 55 ||
śaṅkhacakragadāpadmāḥ kāryāstasya svarūpiṇaḥ ||
nṛdehāste na kartavyā śeṣaṃ kāryaṃ tu pūrvavat || 56 ||
ekordhvavadanaḥ kāryo devo visphāritekṣaṇaḥ ||
rūpaṃ narasya kathitaṃ tathā nārāyaṇasya te || 57 ||
kṛṣṇasya hariṇā sārdhaṃ pūrvaṃ varuṇasūnunā ||
haṃso matsyastathā kūrmaḥ kāryāstadrūpadhāriṇaḥ || 58 ||
śṛṅgamatsyastu kartavyau devadevo janārdanaḥ ||
strīrūpaśca tathā kāryaḥ sarvābharaṇabhūṣitaḥ || 59 ||
kare'mṛtaghaṭaścāsya kartavyo bhūridakṣiṇaḥ ||
pṛthuḥ kāryastathā rājā cakravatyanta lakṣaṇaḥ || 3.85.60 ||
kāryastu bhārgavo rāmo jaṭāmaṇḍaladurdṛśaḥ ||
haste'sya paraśuḥ kāryaḥ kṛṣṇājinadharasya tu || 61 ||
rāmo dāśarathiḥ kāryo rājalakṣaṇalakṣitaḥ ||
bharato lakṣmaṇaścaiva śatrughnaśca mahāyaśāḥ ||62||
tathaiva sarve kartavyāḥ kintu maulivivarjitāḥ ||
gaurastu kāryo vālmīkirjaṭā maṇḍaladurdṛśaḥ || 63 ||
tapasyabhirataḥ śānto na kṛśo na ca pīvaraḥ ||
vālmīkirūpaṃ sakalaṃ dattātreyasya kārayet || 64 ||
kṛṣṇaśāntatanu rvyāsaḥ piṅgalo'tijaṭādharaḥ ||
sumanturjaiminiḥ pailo vaiśampāyana eva ca || 65 ||
tasya śiṣyāstu kartavyāścatvāraḥ paripārśvayoḥ ||
yudhiṣṭhiro'tha karttavyo rājalakṣaṇalakṣitaḥ || 66 ||
śmaśruhīno'tipīnāṅgaḥ kṣāmamadhyo vṛkodaraḥ ||
tiryakprekṣī saṃhatabhruḥ kartavyaśca tathā gadī || 67 ||
dūrvāśyāmorjunaḥ kāryaḥ kirīṭī lohitāṅgadaḥ ||
bāṇacāpadharaḥ śrīmānsarvābharaṇabhūṣitaḥ || 68 ||
nakulaḥ sahadevaśca kāryāvaśvisamāvubhau ||
khaṅgacarmadharau kāryau na tvevauṣadhipāṇinau || 69 ||
nīlanīrajapatrābhā kṛṣṇā kāryātirūpiṇī ||
padmapatrāgragaurā tu karttavyā devakī tathā || 3.85.70 ||
madhūkapuṣpasacchāyā yaśodāpi tathā bhavet ||
ekānaṃśāpi kartavyā devī padmakarā tathā || 71 ||
kaṭisthavāmahastā sā madhyasthā rāmakṛṣṇayoḥ ||
sīrapāṇirbalaḥ kāryo musalī caiva kuṇḍalī || 72 ||
śveto'tinīlavasano madādañcitalocanaḥ ||
kṛṣṇaścakradharaḥ kāryo nīlotpaladalacchaviḥ || 73 ||
indīvarakarā kāryā tathā śyāmā ca rukmiṇī ||
tārkṣyasthā sā ca kartavyā satyabhāmā surūpiṇī || 74 ||
anyāśca devyaḥ kartavyāḥ surūpāḥ sumanoharāḥ ||
cāpabāṇadharaḥ kāryaḥ pradyumnaśca sudarśanaḥ || || 75 ||
rājandūrvādalaśyāmaḥ śvetavāsā madotkaṭaḥ ||
kartavyaścāniruddho'pi khaḍgacarmadharaḥ prabhuḥ || 76 ||
sāmbaḥ kāryo gadāhastaḥ surūpaśca viśeṣataḥ ||
sāmbāniruddhau kartavyau padmābhau raktavāsasau ||
khaṅgahastau ca kartavyau striyo veṣau tu pārśvayoḥ || 77 ||
yuyudhānastathā prāṃśurbāṇacāpadharo bhaveta ||
varṇenotpalagarbhābho dīrghabāhuḥ sulocanaḥ||78||
prādurbhāvā devadevasya viṣṇorvaktuṃ śakyā vistarānnaiva rājan ||
buddhyā teṣāṃ karmayogaṃ yathā vacchāstraṃ dṛṣṭvā te tu kāryā budhena||79||
iti śrīviṣṇudharmo0tṛ0kha0mā0va0 saṃ0devodyānarūpanirmāṇonāma pañcāśītitamo'dhyāyaḥ ||85||
<3.86>
||vajra uvāca||
proktaṃ tvayā dvijaśreṣṭha pratimālakṣaṇaṃ śubham ||
prāsādalakṣaṇaṃ samyakkathayasvāmitadyute ||1 ||
mārkaṇḍeya uvāca ||
hastapramāṇantatproktaṃ prāsādānāṃ tu lakṣaṇam ||
saṃsthānato vinirdiṣṭaṃ hastamānānukīrtanam||2||
tadahaṃ te pravakṣyāmi tanme nigadataḥ śṛṇu ||
prāsādalakṣaṇaṃ śreṣṭhaṃ vijayārogyavardhanam ||3||
bhāgaṃ tu jagatī kāryā prāsādo bhāgameva ca ||
kartavyā jagatī mātratalpocchrāyatribhṛmikā ||4 ||
ucchrāyā tena kartavyā hyekaikā bhūmikā gatiḥ ||
bhadrapīṭhākṛtiḥ kāryā bhūmikā bhūmikā punaḥ || 5 ||
prasādārthaṃ kaṭiḥ kāryā kūṭameva tathaiva ca ||
kaṭimūlāṣṭabhāgena sopānavistarā bhavet || 6 ||
samasaṃkhyaṃ tu kartavyaṃ sopānaṃ bhūmikāṃ prati ||
kūṭaḥ kāryastridhocchedaḥ śubhāmalakasārakaḥ || 7 ||
caturasrastathā rājankramopacayasaṃyutaḥ ||
vicchedāścātra kartavyāḥ siṃhamālāvibhūṣitāḥ || 8 ||
dvārocchrāyāśca kartavyā devāścāṣṭāṃśasaṃyutāḥ ||
vistāraṃ dviguṇaṃ cātra dvārocchrāyaṃ tu kārayet || 9 ||
dvāraśobhāvatī kāryā candraśālā samucchritā ||
kūṭaprathamavicchede śubhāmalakasārakaiḥ || 3.86.10 ||
dvāraiścaturbhirbhagnaistu na bhagnairvā mahīpate ||
prāsādametatkartavyaṃ kūṭacchedadvaye tathā || 11 ||
vicchinna candraśālābhiścatasṛbhirivāvṛtam ||
himavānnāma vikhyātaḥ prāsādo'yaṃ manoharaḥ || 12 ||
kūṭacchedadvayopeto mālyavānabhidhīyate ||
ekakūṭastathaivāyaṃ śṛṅgavāniti kīrtitaḥ || 13 ||
āgārākhyo bhavatyeṣa dvāreṇaikena śṛṅgavān ||
agāro bhavanākhyaḥ syātprāsādastu dvimekhalaḥ || 14 ||
gṛhākhyaścaiṣa nirdiṣṭaḥ prāsādaścaikamekhalaḥ ||
śṛṅgavānniṣadhākhyaḥ syātprāsādastu dvimekhalaḥ || 15 ||
sa eva nīlo bhavati tathā caivaikamekhalaḥ||
mālyavāñśvetasaṃjñaḥ syātprāsādastu dvimekhalaḥ || 16
vindhyanāmā bhavatyeṣa tathā caivaikamekhalaḥ ||
agārabhedarahitaḥ sarvaḥ sarvadivaukasām ||17 ||
rājandevagṛhāḥ kāryā yatheṣṭāśāmukhāḥ śubhā||
āgārabhedāḥ kartavyāḥ pūrvapaścimadiṅmukhāḥ ||18||
teṣāṃ liṅgapratiṣṭhaikā śastā bhavati pārthiva ||
dvimekhalā ye kathitāstathā caivaikamekhalāḥ || 19 ||
bhāgasteṣāṃ tu jagatī bhāgasteṣāṃ tathā kaṭiḥ ||
bhāgamekaṃ tathā kāryaṃ kūṭaṃ prāgvanmahīpate || || 3.86.20 ||
valabhīsaṃsthitaṃ ramyaṃ prāsādaṃ tu caturdiśam ||
vistāratriguṇāyāmaṃ tathā caivaikamekhalam||21||
pārśvayorvalabhīpṛṣṭhe candraśālāyutaṃ bhavet ||
prāsāde tatra kartavyastrayāścāmalasārakāḥ || 22 ||
bhāgastu mekhalā tatra kaṭibhāgastathā bhavet ||
bhāga ekastu valabhī tathā tatra prakīrtitā || 23||
prāsādasyāṣṭamo bhāgaḥ kartavyo mekhalāgatiḥ ||
kaṣṭyaṣṭabhāgo dvau hitvā sopānaṃ vistarādbhavet || 24 ||
ekasyā diśi caivātra dvāraḥ kāryaḥ samucchrayaḥ ||
svecchāśābhimukhaḥ kāryo valabhīsaṃjñitastvayam || 25 ||
prāsāde'smim̐stu kartavyā brahmaviṣṇumaheśvarāḥ ||
ekānaṃśātha vā kāryā madhyagā rāmakṛṣṇayoḥ||26||
gaṇeśaskandayormadhye kartavyo vā trilocanaḥ ||
madhyerkacandrayoḥ kāryastvaṣṭā vāpi janārdanaḥ||27||
ekaiva durgā kartavyā bahubāhu vibhūṣitā ||
tatra kāryātha vā lakṣmīrjagattritayabhūṣaṇā ||28||
viṣṇurbhogaśayaḥ kāryaḥ prāsāde tatra vā nṛpa ||
caturātmāthavā kāryo devadevo janārdanaḥ || 29 ||
śakrakīnāśavaruṇaiḥ sahito vā dhaneśvaraḥ ||
anye devā na kartavyā ye mayā nānukīrtitāḥ || 3.86.30 ||
ekāśābhimukhairdvāraiścaturbhiḥ saṃyutastvayama ||
brahmaṇā vṛddhido nāma prāsādaḥ parikīrtitaḥ ||31 ||
kartavyastatra lokeśaścaturātmā haristvatha ||
vistāratriguṇaiśchāyāṃ dvārāṃstatra niveśayet || 32 ||
vistāratriguṇocchrāyairdvārairvā saṃyutastribhiḥ ||
prāsādo'yaṃ bhavedrājam̐striguṇo nāmataḥ śubhaḥ || 33 || 
ekāśābhimukhadvāragarbhabhittisamanvi taḥ ||
brahmā viṣṇuśca rudraśca prāsāde'tra niveśayet || 34 ||
ādityaviṣṇucandrārkāḥ pṛthagbhūpālasattama ||
gaṇeśarudraskandānvā prāsāde'sminniveśayet||35||
pārśvasthavalabhīhīno valabhīsaṃjñito bhavet ||
prāsādaḥ pārśvasaṃyuktaḥ śikharo nāmato nṛpa||36||
pārśvasthavalabhīhīnaṃ vṛttidaṃ kāmadaṃ bhaveta ||
pārśvasthavalabhīhīnaṃ triguṇaṃ nṛgṛhaṃ bhavet || 37 ||
kṛtavistṛtadigdvāraṃ prāsādaṃ valabhīgatam||
turagākhyaṃ vinirdiṣṭaṃ retomandagṛhaṃ hitam || 38 ||
vināyakasya tatkāryaṃ bhadrakālyastathaiva ca ||
skandasya kāryaṃ tadrājam̐llakṣmyā kāryamathāpi vā || 39 ||
nāgānāṃ pramathānāṃ vā nānyasya tu vidhīyate ||
kṛtavistṛtadigdvāraṃ turagaṃ kuñjaraṃ smṛtam || 3.86.40 ||
turagābhihatānāṃ ca devānāṃ tatpraśasyate ||
śakrasya ca mahābhāga nānyeṣāṃ tadvidhīyate || 41 ||
yatheṣṭasaṃkhyāgarbhaṃ tu prāsādaṃ valabhīgatam ||
yatheṣṭannāma vikhyātaṃ yatheṣṭāyāmasaṃjñitam || 42 ||
yatheṣṭamānaṃ dvārantad dvāreṇaikena saṃyutam ||
ekagarbhaṃ tadevoktaṃ viśālaṃ nāma pārthiva ||43 ||
yatheṣṭe ca viśāle ca kartavyā mātaro nṛpa ||
grahā vā rājaśārdūla nakṣatrāṇyatha vā punaḥ || 44 ||
ādityā vasavo rudrā viśvedevā marudgaṇāḥ ||
bhṛgavoṅgirasaḥ sādhyā devo vā yadi vāśvinau || 45 ||
aṣṭau vātha diśāṃ pālā yadi vā bhūtapañcakam ||
evaṃ devagaṇaḥ kāryo rājanprāsādayostayoḥ||46 ||
āyatā mālyavatpṛṣṭhe valabhī tu yadā bhavet ||
kartavyo bhadrakālyarthe prāsādo bhadrasaṃjñitaḥ || 47 ||
tiryaksthā mālyavatpṛṣṭhe valabhī tu yadā bhaveta ||
dvārapāleti sa proktaḥ prāsādo vibudhapriyaḥ || 48 ||
subhadrākhyasya nirdiṣṭaḥ śvetākhyo bhadravatkṛtaḥ ||
kṛtastu dvāraśālāvatsaumyaḥ syādgandhamādanaḥ || 49 ||
saumyaḥ subhadraśca tathā sarveṣāmeva śasyate ||
aṣṭatrikūṭaṃ tricchedaṃ śubhāmalakasārakam || 3.86.50 ||
aṣṭāṃśajagatībandhaṃ tathā caivaikamekhalam ||
aṣṭagarbhāṣṭadigdvāraṃ kamalaṃ parikīrtitam || 51 ||
aṣṭau tatra tu kartavyā grahāḥ ketuvivarjitāḥ ||
sūryaḥ śukraḥ kujo rāhuḥ sauraścandro budho guruḥ || 52 ||
krameṇānena kartavyaṃ tatra rājangṛhāṣṭakam ||
bhūjalākāśavahnīndudīkṣitārkamaruttanuḥ || 53 ||
mahādevo'tha vā kāryastatra devo jagadguruḥ ||
aṣṭātmā vāsudevo vā prabhave yāpanena ca ||54 ||
prāsāde'smiṃstu kartavyo vidhinānena vā hariḥ ||
caturātmā tu kartavyo digdvāreṣvatra keśavaḥ || 55 ||
aśvaśīrṣaṃ varāhaṃ ca narasiṃhaṃ trivikramam ||
īśānādiṣu koṇeṣu kāryā devā narottama || 56 ||
ucchritā jagatī kāryā bhadrapīṭhākṛtiṃ vinā ||
prāgbhāgavalabhīhīnaṃ turagaṃ tatra kārayet || 57 ||
praśastaḥ sarvadevānāṃ prāsādastvaruṇodayaḥ ||
pūrvāparāśābhimukhaḥ kāryoyaṃ nānyadiṅmukhaḥ || 58 ||
ayamevātidīrghastu prāsādo gṛhasaṃjñitaḥ ||
prāsāde tvaruṇe lagnā valyaprāsādakāvubhau || 59 ||
yāmyottarāśābhimukhau tadvajjagati bandhanau ||
sammukhau tau tu jagati valabhīcchandakau bhavet||3.86.60||
śubhā jālapraticchannā saubhadvāramukhā tathā ||
tatra syādgaruḍo nāma prāsādaḥ sumanoharaḥ || 61 ||
ādityasya pratiṣṭhānaṃ prāsāde tatra śasyate ||
daṇḍapiṅgalakau kāryau tatra prāsādayordvayoḥ || 62 ||
tayorvā nṛpa kartavyau ratau mandayamāvubhau ||
kartavyau vā mahārāja tathā keśavaśaṅkarau || 63 ||
prāsāde garuḍo vāpi kartavyaścandramā budhaiḥ ||
pārśvaprāsādayostasya kartavyau śiśirāmbupau || 64 ||
prāsāde gāruḍe vāpi kāmadevaṃ tu kārayet ||
pārśva prāsādayostasya kartavyau vanamālinau || 65 ||
prāsāde gāruḍe vāpi kartavyo devarāḍ budhaiḥ ||
pārśvaprāsādayostasya kartavyau vanamālinau||66||
prāsāde gāruḍe vāpi kartavyaśca tathā yamaḥ ||
pārśvaprāsādayostasya mṛtyukālau niveśayet || 67 ||
prāsāde gāruḍe vāpi kartavyo varuṇo bhavet ||
pārśvaprāsādayoḥ kāryā gaṅgā kālindireva ca || 68 ||
prāsāde gāruḍe vāpi dhanādhyakṣaṃ tu kārayet ||
pārśvaprāsādayostasya śaṅkhapadmau niveśayet || 69 ||
prāsāde gāruḍe vāpi brahmāṇaṃ viniveśayet ||
pārśvaprāsādayostasya kartavyau keśaveśvarau || 3.86.70 ||
prāsāde gāruḍe vāpi mahādevaṃ niveśayet ||
pārśvaprāsādayostasya kāryau viṣṇupitāmahau || 71 ||
vināyakaḥ kumāro vā kartavyo yadunandana ||
vīrabhadraśca nandī ca kartavyau vā manoharau || 72 ||
prāsāde gāruḍe vāpi garuḍaṃ viniveśayet ||
pārśvaprāsādayoḥ kāryaḥ kaśyapo vinatā tathā ||73||
prāsāde gāruḍe vāpi vāsudevaṃ niveśayet ||
pārśvaprāsādayostasya kāryau rudrapitāmahau||74||
candrārkau vā mahārāja yadi vā varuṇānilau ||
lakṣmīṃ ca kālarātrīṃ ca yadi vāpyanalānilau || 75 ||
tārkṣyānantau mahārāja yadi vā garuḍāruṇau ||
prāsāde gāruḍe vāpi dharmaḥ kāryo mahīpate || 76 ||
pārśvaprāsādayostasya kāryo'rthaḥ kāma eva ca ||
liṅgākāraṃ tu kartavyaṃ devaveśma trimekhalam || 77 ||
tasya nāma bhavelloke śarveti narapuṅgava ||
dvimekhalasya tasyaiva trailokyamiti nirdiśet || 78 ||
sa eva liṅganāmā syātprāsādastvekamekhalaḥ ||
kūṭavadvalabhīyuktaṃ sarvakīṭaṃ prakīrtitam || 79 ||
brahmāṇḍamiti vijñeyaṃ trailokyaṃ valabhīyutam ||
liṅgākhyaṃ valabhīyuktaṃ sāramityabhidhīyate || 3.86.80 ||
śarvādyāḥ ṣaḍime kāryāḥ pūrvapaścimadiṅmukhāḥ ||
liṅgasaṃsthāpanaṃ kāryaṃ nityameteṣu paṇḍitaiḥ || 81 ||
prāsādaṃ valabhīsaṃjñaṃ caturasraṃ vimekhalam ||
caturasramiti khyātaṃ kāryaṃ tatsumanoharam || 82 ||
vidvāraṃ staṃbhasaṃyuktaṃ purastādbhittivarjitam |||
śākraṃ devagaṇairyuktaṃ yamaṃ vā pitṛsaṃyutam || 83 ||
mātṛyuktaṃ mahākālaṃ nṛsiṃhamatha vā punaḥ ||
durgāṃ devīṃ tathā rājannakṣatraṃ śaśinaṃ tathā || 84 |||
grahairdivākaraṃ yuktaṃ nāgairvā nṛpa vāsukima ||
sumekhalaṃ tannirdiṣṭaṃ tathā nṛpa samekhalam || 85 ||
vimekhalaṃ tatkathitaṃ rājandvāraṃ dvimekhalam|||
trimekhalaṃ tatkathitaṃ mekhalāḍhyaṃ mahīpate || 86 ||
sarveṣveteṣu kartavyāḥ sabhāsthāḥ sarvakīrtitāḥ ||
anyepyanuktāḥ kartavyāḥ sabhāsthā narapuṅgava ||| || 87 ||
caturasrādayastvete dvāravanto mahīpate ||
dhiṣṇyaśalyabudhendvākhyā nāmataḥ parikīrtitāḥ || 88 ||
caturasro gṛhaḥ kāryaḥ prāsādastveka bhūmikaḥ||
gṛha ityeva vikhyāto lakṣmīṃ tatra niveśayeta||89||
sa eva kāryo devānāṃ yathaṣṭo bahubhūmikaḥ ||
bahubhūmikasaṃjñastu yatheṣṭaṃ nṛpapuṅgava||3.86.90||
bhūmikādvādaśopetaḥ ṣaḍasro merurucyate ||
caturdvārastu kartavyaḥ sarveṣāṃ tridivaukasāma || 91 ||
śuktimānnāmataḥ sarvaḥ sa caikādaśabhūmikaḥ ||
mandaro nāmataḥ proktaḥ sa taddvādaśabhūmikaḥ || 92 ||
navabhirbhūmikābhistu pāriyātraḥ prakīrtitaḥ ||
alako nāma vikhyātastathā caivāṣṭabhūmikaḥ || || 93 ||
vimānaḥ saptabhaumaḥ syātpalabhaumo nandanaḥ smṛtaḥ ||
pañcataḥ pañca maḥ syāccaturbhaumaścatuṣkakaḥ || 94 ||
tribhūmistu tribhūmiḥ syāddvibhūmyākhyo dvibhūmikaḥ ||
ekabhaumastu nirdiṣṭastathā caivaikabhūmikaḥ ||95|||
proktaḥ samudrākhyastathā vṛttaikamekhalaḥ ||
mekhalā caturasrā tu bhadrapīṭhākṛtirbhavet ||96||
kṛtastvaṣṭaparicchedaiḥ sarvasvāmalasārakaiḥ||
vṛtta eva tathā nandī prāsādaḥ parikīrtitaḥ||97||
dīrgharājaguhākāro guhārājaḥ prakīrtitaḥ||
bhadrakālyāstathā kāryā viṣṇorbhāgaśayasya vā || 98 ||
ekaśṛṅgastathā vṛtto vṛṣākhyaḥ syāddvimekhalaḥ ||
haṃsākārastathā haṃso ghaṭākāgstathā ghaṭaḥ ||99||
siṃhākāraḥ smṛtaḥ siṃho maṇḍapo maṇḍapākṛtiḥ ||
dvādaśāsriḥ ṣaḍasristu tathāṣṭāsrirmahīpate || 3.86.100 ||
prāsādāstu mayā proktāstathā proktāśrayasthitiḥ ||
pañcabhiḥ śikharairjuṣṭaścaturbhirapi maṇḍapaiḥ ||101||
dvāraiścaturbhiḥ saṃyuktaḥ kailāśaḥ parikīrtitaḥ ||
trimekhalastriśṛṅgaśca trikūṭo nāma viśrutaḥ ||102||
śṛṅgeṇaikena saṃyuktaścaturbhirapi maṇḍapaiḥ||
caturdvāraḥ smṛtaḥ saumyaḥ prāsādaḥ sumanoharaḥ||103||
prākāraṃ jānudaghnā syādyasyaikā jagatī bhavet||
prāsādatulyā tryaṃśā tu tryaṃśatulyā tathā kaṭiḥ||104||
śikharaḥ kuharopeto nānārūpakabhūṣitaḥ||
ekamaṇḍapasaṃyuktastādṛśaiḥ suramandiraiḥ ||105||
svalpairvidikṣu saṃyuktaścaturbhirbhuvi maṇḍapaiḥ||
sopānamūle saṃyukto dvābhyāmeva narādhipa||106||
prāsādo rājarājākhyo mukhyo'yaṃ parikīrtitaḥ||
prāsāde rājarājākhye catvāro yadi maṇḍapāḥ||107||
sarve śikharahīnāḥ syustathā sopānamūlagāḥ||
aṣṭau devagṛhāṇi syuḥ sa bhaveddharaṇīdharaḥ||108||
maṇḍapaiḥ śikharopetaivimānākhyaḥ sa vai bhaveta ||
vidikṣu yatra prāsādāścatvāro maṇḍapāntare || 109 ||
maṇḍapāḥ śikharopetā madhyamaṃjārisaṃyutāḥ ||
navabhiḥ śikharaireṣa prāsādaḥ surarāḍ bhavet || 3.86.110 ||
bahuvātāyanopeto bahujālagavākṣakaḥ ||
caturasro'tha vā vṛttastathaivocchritamekhalaḥ || 111||
ānandanāmā bhavati prāsādaḥ sarvatomukhaḥ ||
mekhalāpṛṣṭhabhūbhāgātkrameṇāmalasārakaiḥ || 112 ||
avicchinnaiḥ kramāddvārairbahubhiryo vinirmitaḥ ||
na muṇḍo na ca śūlāgraḥ prāsādaḥ susamaḥ smṛtaḥ || 113 ||
muṇḍamaṇḍalasaṃyuktaḥ sa evoktaḥ prabhañjanaḥ ||
caturbhirmaṇḍapairyukto viśvakarmeti kīrtyate || || 114 ||
maṇḍapaiḥ susamākāraiścaturbhirapi saṃyutaḥ ||
mahāsumana ityeṣa prāsādaḥ parikīrtitaḥ || 115 ||
bhadrākāraḥ smṛtaśchatro mṛdaṅgaḥ syāttadā kṛtiḥ |
viparīto mṛdaṅgastu vajra ityabhidhīyate ||116 ||
ekā kaṭirbhavedyasya tathaikā jagatī nṛpa ||
śikharā śreṇigāḥ pañca dvārāḥ pañca tathā nṛpa || 117 ||
prāsādo lokapālākhyo lokapāleṣu śasyate ||
śaṃsantyathānyaṃ prāsādaṃ mahābhūtagaṇasya ca || 118 ||
prāsādo vartulakaṭistathā vartulamekhalaḥ ||
aṣṭāsriḥ śikharopeto dvārāṣṭakabhūṣitaḥ || 119 ||
digbandha iti vikhyātaḥ prāsādaḥ sarvadaivataḥ ||
paścādgaviṭakopeto jagatī mānamaṇḍalaḥ || 3.86.120 ||
prāsādastvekaśikharaḥ sāmānyaḥ parikīrtitaḥ ||
maṇḍape jālakopete paścādbhāge guhā bhaveta || 121 ||
vimekhalo'yaṃ prāsādaḥ suguhaḥ parikīrtitaḥ ||
sa eva triguṇopetastriguṇaḥ parikīrtitaḥ || 122 ||
himavānbahubhistambhairyuto bhavati nandakaḥ ||
stambhavānvalabhīyuktaḥ prāsādo bhittivarjitaḥ || 123 ||
ākāśanī sā bhavati tatrākāśaṃ prakalpayeta ||
ṣoḍaśasristu kathitaḥ prāsādastāvadasrikaḥ || || 124 ||
prāsādaḥ śaṅkhasaṃjñaḥ syācchaṅkhākāraḥ suśobhanaḥ ||
padmābhaśikharopeto vaijayanto mahīpate || 125 ||
anāsriḥ śikharopeto bhavedambudasaṃjñitaḥ ||
śikharaiḥ kuharopetairbhavenmaṅgalasaṃjñitaḥ || 126 ||
prāsādānāṃ śataṃ caitatsamāsātkathitaṃ mayā ||
yathecchāśāmukhāḥ kāryā yeṣāṃ diṅ na prayojitāḥ || 127 ||
sarve devagaṇāḥ kāryā yeṣāṃ devo na kīrtitaḥ||
viṣṇordevasya kartavyaḥ sarva eva viśeṣataḥ || 128 ||
anyamiśrau na kartavyau candrārkāvekaveśmagau ||
sūryeṇa saṃyutaścandraḥ kṣīṇaḥ ketuḥ kṣayāvahaḥ || 129 ||
parasparāśābhimukhau kartavyau sūryarātripau ||
pūrvāparāśābhimukhau na tu yāmyottarāmukhau || 3.86.130 ||
pūrvāparāśābhimukhau yadvā syātāṃ tadā śaśī ||
pūjyo bhavati dharmajña kartuḥ kalyāṇakārakaḥ || 131 ||
naikaveśmagatau kāryau grahau skandavināyakau ||
na nāgagaruḍau kāryau na cāntakamaheśvarau || 132 ||
mahākālanṛsiṃhau ca raktāṃ śuṣkāṃ tathaiva ca ||
brahmarudrakṛtāntaistu bhadrakālyātha vā saha || 133 ||
kāmadevo na kartavyastathaiva varuṇānalau ||
devasyaikasya no kārye dve tathā ca tathā nṛpa || 124 ||
prādurbhāvagate kārye te ca vipṇormahātmanaḥ ||
yeṣāmapi ca devānāṃ dvandvayogo vigarhitaḥ || 135 ||
teṣāmapyanyasahitaḥ praśasto nṛpasattama ||
grahasaṃkhyāsusahitau kartavyau sūryarātripau || 136 ||
mahādevasamīpasthau tathā skandavināyakau ||
vāsudevasamīpasthau śeṣatārkṣyau tathaiva ca || 137 ||
evamanyasametānāṃ yathāyogaiḥ pṛthagvidhaiḥ ||
kartavyastu viruddhānāṃ devānāṃ cāpi saṅgamaḥ || 138 ||
prāsādalakṣaṇamidaṃ kathitaṃ samāsādālokya pūrvacaritaṃ hi pitāmahoktama ||
śāstraṃ hitāya narasattama mānuṣāṇāṃ dhāryaṃ tvayā tadatiyatnapareṇa nityam || 139 || 
iti śrīviṣṇudharmottare tṛtīyakhaṇḍe mārkaṇḍeyavajrasaṃvāde prāsādalakṣaṇo nāma ṣaḍaśītitamo'dhyāyaḥ || 86 ||
<3.87>
mārkaṇḍeya uvāca || ||
prāsādamatha vakṣyāmi sarvatobhadrasaṃjñitama ||
caturātmā hariryatra kartavyo jagatīpate || 1 ||
ekatra jagatī kāryā caturasrā manoramā ||
tasyāṃ garbhagṛhāḥ kāryā viṃśatiścaturuttarāḥ || 2 ||
yeṣāṃ pṛṣṭhe jagatyūrddhavaṃ prākāraṃ viniveśayet ||
sarvatra mekhalākāraṃ tatrāpi viniveśayeta || 3 ||
prāsādakā mahārāja caturviṃśatireva ca ||
caturṣu dikṣu sopānaṃ mekhalāyāṃ niveśayet || 4 ||
sopānastryaṃśavistāro mekhalāyā prakīrtitaḥ ||
sopānobhayato rājanprāsādāste trayastrayaḥ || 5 ||
bhavanti mekhalāpṛṣṭhe tadādau garbhamandiram ||
vimekhalaṃ tu sāmānyaṃ madhye teṣāṃ vimaṇḍapama || 6 ||
himavansaṃjñitau kāryau tasya pārśve vimekhalau ||
sopānamūle prāsāde dvedve kārye manorame || 7 ||
daṃṣṭre devakulākhye te sāmānyākhye vimaṇḍape ||
tathaiva jagatīpṛṣṭhe koṇe devakulaṃ nyaset || 8 ||
prāsādo madhyamaścātra kartavyo maṇḍapānvitaḥ ||
catvāro maṇḍapāścātra kāryāḥ śikharasaṃjñitāḥ || 9 ||
madhyaprāsādakoṇeṣu tathā maṇḍapasandhiṣu ||
dvidvigarbhaguhānramyānprāsādānviniveśayet || 3.87.10 ||
ekaikaṃ maṇḍapaṃ cātra dvāratritayabhūṣitam ||
caturthe ca tathā dvāre devagarbhagṛhaṃ bhavet || 11 ||
maṇḍapasya ca ye dvārā kāryāste stambhabhūṣitāḥ ||
prāsādasyāsya śikharā bhavantyeva navaiva tu || 12 ||
ucchritaṃ navamaṃ śṛṅgaṃ tulyaṃ syācchikharāṣṭakam ||
madhyamaṃ śikharaṃ tatra kāryaṃ śikharavarjitam || 13 ||
rūpakairvividhairyuktaṃ na maṇḍaṃ na ca śūlavat ||
aṣṭau tu śikharāstatra kartavyāḥ kuharānvitāḥ || 14 ||
jālāgavākṣakopetaiḥ kuharairnṛpa bhūṣitāḥ ||
sarvatra śikharāḥ kāryāḥ śubhāmalakasārakāḥ || 15 ||
sacakrāḥ sapatākāśca sadhvajāśca narādhipa ||
khe'bhiṣakta ivābhāti prāsādo'yaṃ samucchritaḥ || 16 ||
kramato devatānyāsa prāsāde'sminnibodha me ||
vāsudevo'tra kartavyaḥ pūrvāśābhimukho nṛpa || 17 ||
paścimāśāmukho vāpi na kāryaścānyadiṅmukhaḥ ||
vāsudevadiśaṃ cātra pūrvāṃ rājanprakalpayet || 18 ||
tasyāścaivānusāreṇa diśaḥ syātparikalpanā ||
dakṣiṇāśāmukhaḥ kāryo devaḥ saṅkarṣaṇaḥ prabhuḥ || 19 ||
paścimāśāmukhaḥ kāryaḥ pradyumnaścāpyanantaram ||
uttaraśāmukho rājannaniruddho vidhīyate || 3.87.20 ||
diggarbhā ye mayā proktā gṛhā maṇḍalasandhigāḥ ||
prāṅmaṇḍapāddakṣiṇato lakṣmīṃ tatra tu kārayeta || 21 ||
dakṣiṇādvāmato nidrāṃ kālarātrīṃ tu dakṣiṇe ||
paścimādvāmataḥ siddhiṃ ratiṃ tasyaiva dakṣiṇe || 52 ||
uttarādvāmataḥ kīrtiṃ dakṣiṇena sarasvatīm ||
pūrvasyāṃ vāmabhāge tu tathā puṣṭiṃ niveśayet || 23 ||
aiśānyādikramāḥ kāryāḥ koṇaprāsādagāstvime ||
aśvaśīrṣaṃ varāhaṃ ca narasiṃhaṃ trivikramam || 24 ||
prāsādakeṣu kartavyā mekhalā bandhakeṣu ca ||
caturviṃśatirityete vibudhā vibudhottama || 25 ||
tārkṣyaṃ śaṅkhaṃ tathā padmaṃ cakraṃ lāṅgalameva ca ||
musalaṃ cāpyanantaṃ ca gadāṃ tālaṃ tathaiva ca || 26 ||
triśūlaṃ ca tathā śārṅgaṃ śaraṃ makarameva ca ||
paraśuṃ mudgaraṃ caiva carma pāśaṃ ca pārthiva || 27 ||
paṭṭiśaṃ nandakaṃ caiva śaktimṛṣṭiṃ tathaiva ca ||
vajraṃ ca kaustubhaṃ caiva vanamālāṃ ca yādava || 28 ||
daṃṣṭrāṃ devagṛhāṇāṃ me tvaṃ vinyāsamidaṃ śuṇu ||
indrāgnī pūrvataḥ kāryau dakṣiṇena tathā nṛpa || 29||
yamaśca nirṛtiścaiva paścime varuṇānilau ||
uttareṇa ca kartavyau dhaneśvaramaheśvarau || 3.87.30 ||
grahāṣṭakaṃ vā kartavyaṃ daṃṣṭrā devagṛheṣu ca ||
ādityaśukrau pūrveṇa yāmyena ca kujāsurau || 31 ||
śanaiścaraśaśāṃkau ca tathā paścimato nṛpa||
uttareṇa ca kartavyau candraputrabṛhaspatī || 32 ||
daṃṣṭrādevagṛhāvatra vāsudevasya yāvubhau ||
vāsudevapratīhārau tayorvā kārayennṛpa || 33 ||
subhadravasubhadrākhyau trailokyasyeśvarāvubhau ||
āṣāḍhayajñatātākhyau kāryau saṅkarṣaṇasya tu || 34 ||
jayaṃ ca vijayaṃ caiva pradyumrasya tu kārayeta ||
āmodaṃ ca pramodaṃ ca aniruddhasya pārthiva || 35 ||
prāguktaṃ devatānyāsāddaṃṣṭrā devakuleṣvatha ||
pratīhārapratiṣṭhānaṃ śasyate bahubhirguṇaiḥ || 36 ||
ta eva jñeyā dikpālāsta eva ca tathā grahāḥ ||
etattvaṃ jagatībandhe śṛṇu devānyathākramam || 37 ||
gāyatrīṃ caturo vedānvaiṣṇavīṃ cāparājitām ||
mṛtyuṃ kālaṃ yamaṃ daṇḍaṃ kavacaṃ śarameva ca || 38 ||
sāṃkhyaṃ yogaṃ pañcarātraṃ jñānaṃ pāśupataṃ tathā ||
vyāsavālmīkimārkaṇḍaṃ mahābhūtānanantaram || 39 ||
evaṃvidhe'tra prāsāde vistīrṇamajiraṃ bhaveta ||
toyaiskṛtrimairdivyaiḥ sambaddhairupaśobhitam || 3.87.40 ||
dvārapālāścatasrastu tataḥ kāryā vijānatā ||
prāsādo dvāraśālākhyaḥ samarūpo manoharaḥ || 41 ||
śreṇīgatāśca prākārāḥ kāryā garbhaguhāḥ śubhāḥ ||
prādurbhāvaṃ nyasetteṣu viṣṇoramitatejasaḥ || 42 ||
atha vā pārthivaśreṣṭha sarvāndevagaṇānkramāta ||
prāsādametadvidhivadyaḥ karoti mahīpate || 43 ||
sarveṣāṃ devatānāṃ tu nityaṃ vihitapūjanam ||
kalpaṃ tretāyugasyādau cakravartyabhidhīyate || 44 ||
pariveśastathā kāryaḥ svarge vasati kāmataḥ ||
tataḥ sāyujyatāmeti devadevasya cakriṇaḥ || 45 ||
phalaṃ trailokyadānasya dhruvamāpnoti mānavaḥ ||
prāsādesminsakṛtkṛtvā sarveṣāmeva pūjanam || 46 || 
gandhamālyanamaskāradhūpadīpānnasampadā ||
yatheṣṭaṃ kāmamāpnoti nātra kāryā vicāraṇā || 47 ||
sacakraḥ sapatāko'yaṃ prāsādo'timanoharaḥ ||
yāvaddarśanamāyāti tāvannaśyantyupadravāḥ || 48 ||
vyādhiścaivāpamṛtyuśca ītayaśca tathā nṛpa ||
nāśamāyāntyasandehaṃ tatpraviṣṭasya yādava || 49 ||
nākālamṛtyavo rājanyoginyo yakṣarākṣasāḥ ||
pretā vināyakā vāpi prabhavantyapi kasyacit ||3.87.50 ||
atra sāttvatamukhyā ye sāyujyaṃ yānti te dhruvam ||
ye devagrahagāścātra dāsīdāsagaṇāśca ye|| || 51 ||
sarve te svargamāyānti yāvadindrāścaturdaśa ||
yasya rājñastu viṣaye prāsādo'yaṃ bhavatyapi ||52||
sa cāpi śakralokasthaściraṃ modati pārthivaḥ ||
tasminpatākāṃ yo dadyāttayā pāpaṃ vidhūyate || 53 ||
ītayaśca śamaṃ yānti puṇyamāpnotyanuttamam ||
kṛtvā sitaṃ taṃ prāsādaṃ cakravartyabhidhīyate || 54 ||
evaṃ vidhānavihite mayā devā yathoditāḥ ||
sarve sannihitāstatra bhavanti vasudhādhipa ||55 ||
tatra yatsalilaṃ tiṣṭhettattīrthaṃ satataṃ bhavet ||
tatra yātrāvidhāne tu sarve devāḥ savāsavāḥ || 56 ||
ṛṣayaśca mahābhāgāstraikālyāmaladarśinaḥ ||
rājarṣayaḥ purāṇāśca sarve samuditā gaṇaiḥ ||57||
diviṣṭhāḥ sumahātmānaḥ śakratulyaparākramāḥ||
gandharvāpsarasaścaiva śubhā bhūtagaṇāśca ye||58||
saśarīrāḥ samāyānti iṣṭaṃ devamahotsavam||
tatrotsavaṃ naraḥ paśyandhruvaṃ kalyāṇabhāgbhavet || 59 ||
tatra hiṃsā na kartavyā devadravyasya karhicita ||
rājā vā tanniyukto vā tatrāsyādhikṛtastu yaḥ || 3.87.60 ||
sa yāti narakaṃ ghoraṃ saputrapaśubāndhavaḥ ||
asmim̐lloke dhruvaṃ tasya sthānanāśo bhaviṣyati || 61 ||
ye tatra bhaktā manujāḥ sapaśudravyasañcayāḥ ||
nityaṃ vṛddhimupāyānti devadevaprasādataḥ || 62 ||
draṣṭavyametattridiveśaveśma kṛtaṃ yadanyena narendracandra ||
dṛṣṭvā hi pāpaiḥ sakalairmanuṣyo vimucyate puṇyamavāpnuyācca ||63||
iti śrīviṣṇudharmottare tṛtīyakhaṇḍe mārkaṇḍeyavajrasaṃvāde sarvatobhadraprāsādalakṣaṇaṃ nāma saptāśītitamo'dhyāyaḥ ||87||
<3.88>
mārkaṇḍeya uvāca ||
sāmānyamatha vakṣyāmi prāsādānāṃ tu lakṣaṇam ||
catuḥṣaṣṭipadaṃ kāryaṃ devatāyatanaṃ sadā||1||
dvāraṃ ca madhyamaṃ tasya samadiksthaṃ praśasyate ||
dvāraṃ vistarataḥ kāryaṃ bhūpāla dviguṇocchrayam||2||
gṛhasthasya tu dvāreṇa dvāradevakuladrumaiḥ ||
aviddhaṃ tacca kartavyaṃ nāmbusrāvi na kukṣimat||3||
vajramadhyaṃ nataṃ caiva dvāraṃ yatnādvivarjayeta||
maṅgalyaṃ rūpakopetaṃ tacca kāryaṃ prayatnataḥ||4||
dvāramānāṣṭabhāgānāṃ pratimāṃ tu sapiṇḍikām||
dvau bhāgau pratimā tatra tṛtīyāṃśaśca piṇḍikā||9||
kaṭiraṣṭamabhāgonaṃ dvāraṃ kāryaṃ vijānatā||
tṛtīyamaṃśaṃ vasudhā tṛtīyāṃśaḥ kaṭirbhavet || 6 ||
maṃjarī ca tṛtīyāṃśaḥ prāsādasya mahābhuja ||
garbhaṃ pādena vistīrṇa tathā dvāraṃ praśasyate || 7 ||
bhittirgarbhāṣṭabhāgonā tathā kāryā vijānatā ||
prāsādocchrāyabhāgena caturthena ca śasyate || 8 ||
vasudhāsañcaro rājankaṭyaṃśadvitayena tu ||
nātyarthaṃ saṅkaṭaṃ kāryaṃ nāvistīrṇaṃ tathaiva ca ||9 ||
mūlakramādyathā cordhvaṃ tathā vistāravarjitam ||
sopānaṃ kārayedrājanpārśvayoḥ siṃhabhūṣaṇam||3.88.1 0||
na muṇḍaṃ na ca śūlāgraṃ nānataṃ kārayettathā||
samaṃ manoharaṃ kāryaṃ tathā māṅgalyarūpavat || 11 ||
sudhāsusitaśṛṅgaṃ ca vicitrakaṭibhūṣaṇam ||
dvārayoḥ sapratīhāraṃ samagarbhagṛhaṃ samam ||
bhadrapīṭhanibhaṃ saumyaṃ ślakṣṇaṃ ghātavivarjitam || 12 ||
evaṃvidhe devagṛhe niviṣṭā śubhāvahā syātpratimā sadaiva||
nityaṃ tathā sannihitā nṛvīra tasmātprayatnādidameva cintyam || 13 ||
iti śrīviṣṇudharmottare tṛtīyakhaṇḍe mārkaṇḍeyavajrasaṃvāde sāmānyaprāsādalakṣaṇo nāmā ṣṭāśītitamo'dhyāyaḥ || 88 ||
<3.89>
mārkaṇḍeya uvāca ||
vanapraveśaṃ dharmajña nibodha gadato mama ||
śubhe divasanakṣatre muhūrte cābhipūjite || 1 ||
daivajñaṃ purataḥ kṛtvā sthapatiḥ praviśedvanam ||
tatastatra parīkṣeta cchindyādbhinyādvanaspatīna || 2 ||
sakoṭarāṃ latābaddhāṃ kṛmijagdhāṃ vivarjayet ||
vahninā ca tathā spṛṣṭāṃ pātitāṃ pavanena ca||3||
kuñjareṇa tathā bhagnāṃ pakṣiṇāṃ nilayāśca ye||
tāpasānāṃ samīpe ca siktāśca ghaṭavāriṇā||4||
sattvānāmāśrayā ye ca ye ca kubjā narādhipa ||
suṣirā ūrdhvaśuṣkāśca bālavṛddhāstathaiva ca || 5 || 
śmaśānacaityavalmīkadevatāyatanodbhavāḥ ||
udyānajānekavṛkṣānsīmāvṛkṣāṃstathaiva ca || 6 ||
mārgavṛkṣānmahārāja prayatnena vivarjayet ||
palāśaṃ kovidāraṃ ca śālmaliṃ pippalaṃ vaṭam || 7 ||
āmraṃ ca puṣpakaṃ caiva kapitthaṃ ca bibhītakam ||
varjayedvetasaṃ caiva ye cārāmāstathā drumāḥ ||8||
nandanaṃ syandanaṃ sālaṃ śiṃśapaṃ khadiraṃ dhavam ||
kiṃśukaṃ padmakaṃ caiva haridraṃ cīnakadrumama||9||
arjunaṃ ca kadambaṃ ca madhūkaṃ cāñjanaṃ tathā||
devavṛkṣām̐śca jātyām̐śca tathā vai raktacandanam||3.89.10||
ete' praśastā vijñeyā ye ca sārā mahīruhāḥ ||
śastāśastā na ye proktāste tu madhyaphalāḥ smṛtāḥ||11||
raktasārā narendrāṇāṃ śuklasārā dvijanmanām ||
pītasārā viśāṃ śastāḥ śūdrāṇāṃ kṛṣṇamadhyakāḥ||12 ||
chettavyaṃ tu drumaṃ gatvā sandhyākāle tu paścime||
drumasyāgre baliḥ kāryo bhūtānāṃ tannivāsinām||13||
kulmāṣollopikābhakṣyapuṣpadhūpādibhirnṛpa ||
kṛtvā cāsye tato mantramidaṃ yadukulodvaha||14||
vasanti yatra bhūtāni tebhyaḥ svasti namo'stu vaḥ ||
upahāraṃ gṛhītvemaṃ kriyatāṃ vāsaparyayaḥ || 15 ||
bhavadbhiścābhyanujñātaḥ pūjitaiḥ pramatheśvarāḥ ||
devāgāre niyokṣyāmi drumametamasaṃśayam || 16 ||
na cedbhavantastyakṣyanti drumametaṃ tato mama ||
svapne bhavadbhirvaktavyaṃ yathātathyamanugrahāt || 17 ||
evamuktvā tathā tatra prasvapetsthapatirnṛpa ||
daivajñaśca mahābhāga rakṣito śastrapāṇibhiḥ || 18 ||
tataḥ svapnaṃ śubhaṃ rātrau tayoreko'pi cedyadi ||
paśyedrājanna vā paśyecchindyād vṛkṣamasaṃśayam || 19 ||
svapnaṃ vāpyaśubhaṃ paśyenna taṃ bhindyātkathañcana ||
chettavyaṃ chedayed vṛkṣaṃ kalyamutthāya buddhimān || 3.89.20 ||
madhvājyāktena rājendra tataḥ paraśunā śuciḥ ||
rekhāṃ pradakṣiṇaṃ kṛtvā pātayetprayatastataḥ || 21 ||
pūrvāgramuttarāgraṃ vā patanaṃ tasya śasyate ||
aiśānyāṃ ca mahārāja dikṣu śeṣāsu garhitam || 20 ||
vāme prabhagne na grāhyaṃ prāṇihiṃsākaraṃ tathā ||
praśastaṃ patitaṃ vṛkṣaṃ grāhyaṃ bhavati yatnataḥ || 23 ||
agraṃ mūlaṃ prayatnena kartavyaṃ tasya cihnitam ||
agraṃ devasya mūrdhānaṃ pādaṃ mūlaṃ tu kārayet || 24 ||
arcākṛtā viparyastā tiryagvā maraṇāvahā ||
agramūlaviparyāsaṃ stambhānāṃ ca vivarjayet || 25 ||
agramūlaviparyāse kṛte veśma kṣayaṃ vrajet ||
pūrvāgrā cottarāgrā vā drumā yojyā gṛheṣu ca || 26 ||
mahāvaṃśeṣu vaṃśeṣu viparyastā vigarhitāḥ ||
dakṣiṇāgrāḥ parāgrā vā gṛhasvāmivipattidāḥ || 27 ||
tasmātsarvaprayatnena cihnaistaṃ kārayeddrumam ||
agre mūle ca dharmajña tataḥ samyakpraveśayet || 28 ||
śakaṭena mahārāja narairvāpyatha govṛṣaiḥ ||
āpte divasanakṣatre muhūrte cābhipūjite || 29 ||
praviśya samyaktaṃ vidvānyathāyogaṃ niyojayet ||
yathoddeśaṃ mahārāja sthapatirlakṣaṇānvitam || 3.89.30 ||
evaṃ hi kṛtvā vanasampraveśaṃ yaḥ kāṣṭhamānīya gṛhāṇi kuryāt ||
vṛddhirbhavettasya sadaiva rājandhanena dhānyena sutaiśca mukhyā || 31 ||
iti śrīviṣṇudharmottare tṛtīyakhaṇḍe mārkaṇḍeyavajrasaṃvāde dāruparīkṣā nāmaikonanavatitamo'dhyāyaḥ || 89 ||
<3.90>
mārkaṇḍeya uvāca ||
pūrvoktavidhinā gatvā śilāṃ vātha parīkṣayeta ||
śailaṃ gatvā mahārāja daivajñaḥ sthapatistathā || 1 ||
śuklā śastā dvijātīnāṃ kṣattriyāṇāṃ ca lohitā ||
viśāṃ pītā hitā kṛṣṇā śūdrāṇāṃ ca hitapradā || 2 ||
ekavarṇāṃ samāṃ snigdhāṃ nimagnāṃ ca tathā kṣitau ||
ghātātimātra sphuṭanāṃ dṛḍhāṃ mṛdvīṃ manoramām ||3 ||
komalāṃ sikatāhīnāṃ priyāṃ dṛṅgamanasorapi ||
saritsalilanirdhūtāṃ pavitrāṃ tu jaloṣitāma || 4 ||
drumacchāyopagūḍhāṃ ca tīrthāśrayasamanvitāma ||
āyāmapariṇāhāḍhyāṃ grāhyāṃ prāhurmanīṣiṇaḥ || 5 ||
agrāhyāṃ jvalanālīḍhāṃ taptāṃ bhāskararaśmibhiḥ ||
anyakarmopayuktāṃ ca tathā kṣārāmbusaṃyutām || 6||
atyantopahatāṃ rūkṣāmapuṇyajanasevitāma ||
tilaiḥ sambhūṣitā yā tu vicitrabindubhiścitā ||7||
rekhāmaṇḍalasaṅkīrṇāṃ viddhāṃ vimalasaṃyutāma ||
vimalaṃ trividhaṃ jñeyaṃ lohaṃ kāṃsyaṃ ca hemajam || 8 ||
yā lohavimalairjuṣṭā sā janakṣayakāriṇī ||
kāṃsyābhavimalopetā janamānavināśinī || 9 ||
haimena yuktā durbhikṣaṃ tathā kuryādavagraham ||
dharṣaṇe cchedane caiva maṇḍalaṃ yatpradṛśyate || 3.90.10 ||
sagarbhāṃ tāṃ vijānīyād yatnena ca vivarjayeta ||
māṃjiṣṭhavarṇasaṃkāśe garbhe bhavati darduraḥ || 11 ||
pītake maṇḍale godhā kṛṣṇe vidyādbhujaṅgamam ||
kapile mūṣakaṃ vidyātkṛkalāsaṃ tathāruṇe || 12 ||
guḍavarṇe tu pāṣāṇaṃ kāpote gṛhagodhikā ||
āpo nistriṃśavarṇābhe bhasmavarṇe tu vālukā || 13 ||
atastvekatamenāpi yuktā karturviparyaye ||
evaṃ garbhāstu vijñeyā bāhyalakṣaṇalakṣitāḥ || 14 ||
bāhyato lakṣaṇaṃ nāsti teṣāṃ lepāni dāpayeta ||
brāhmīṃ māheśvarīṃ śākrīṃ vaiṣṇavīṃ takṣaṇocitām || 15 ||
ajāstanyena saṃyojya śilālepaṃ tu dāpayet ||
ahorātre gate yatra śilālepo na jāyate || 16 ||
sagarbhāṃ tāṃ vijānīyātprayatnena vivarjayeta ||
kāsīsaṃ pītakāsīsaṃ gavyakṣīreṇa peṣayet || 17 ||
pāṣāṇaṃ lepitaṃ tena bahuvarṇaṃ yadā bhaveta ||
sā śilā na praśastā syātprāṇigarbhā tu sā smṛtā || 18 ||
mustakaṃ karavīraṃ ca kuṣṭhaṃ tālīśapatrakama ||
strīstanyapiṣṭairetaistu pāṣāṇaṃ lepayedbudhaḥ || 19 ||
ebhirlepitamaśmānaṃ yadā simisimāyate ||
kālakūṭaṃ viṣaṃ tatra na taṃ hastena saṃspṛśet || 3.90.20 ||
śvetaśca padmavarṇaśca kusumoṣaṇasannibhaḥ ||
pāṇḍuro mudgavarṇaśca kāpoto bhṛṅgasannibhaḥ || 21 ||
jñeyāḥ praśastāḥ pāṣāṇāḥ aṣṭāvete na saṃśayaḥ ||
kṛṣṇavarṇā śilā yā tu śuklā hīrakasaṃyutā || 22 ||
sā śilā śrīkarī jñeyā putrapautravivardhinī ||
sitavarṇā tu yā kṛṣṇairhīrakaiḥ sabalīkṛtā|| 23 ||
bahudoṣakarī sā tu kṛṣṇā vā raktahīrakaiḥ ||
sarvavarṇeṣu śukleṣu praśastaṃ hīrakaṃ viduḥ|| 24 ||
sarveṣāmeva varṇānāmanyavarṇaṃ vigarhitam ||
brāhmīṃ śilāṃ dine gatvā śobhane dhūpayedbudhaḥ || 25 ||
kṣīreṇa nṛpa gavyena sakaṣāyaistathā jalaiḥ ||
ācchādya kusumairdarbhaiḥ prāgvatkuryāttathā balim||26||
svapnārthaṃ ca svapettatra daivajñaḥ sthapatistathā ||
prāgvaddṛṣṭe śubhe svapne śilā neyā tu sā bhavet || 27 ||
adṛṣṭe tu tathā svapne na duṣṭe śobhanaṃ bhavet ||
pūjanaṃ tu dvitīye'hni kṛtvā bhāṇḍagaṇasya tu || 28 ||
cchedakasya tataḥ kṛtvā saṃvāhyaṃ taṃ praveśayet ||
prāgvadeva mahīpāla yathā vṛkṣeṣu kīrtitam || 29 ||
śilānāṃ garbhavijñānaṃ yaduktaṃ nṛpasattama ||
tadeva sarvaṃ jānīhi drumāṇāmapi yatnataḥ || 3.90.30 ||
anena samyagvidhinā gṛhītā śilā bhavetsarvasukhāvahā sā ||
tasmātprayatnena yathoktametadvanapraveśaṃ sakalaṃ hi kuryāt || 31 ||
iti śrīviṣṇudharmottare tṛtīyakhaṇḍe mārkaṇḍeyavajrasaṃvāde śilāparīkṣā nāma navatitamo'dhyāyaḥ ||90||
<3.91>
mārkaṇḍeya uvāca ||
ataḥ paraṃ pravakṣyāmi pakveṣṭanicayaṃ tava ||
brāhmaṇānāṃ śubhā śvetā kṣatriyāṇāṃ tu lohitā || 1 ||
mṛtpītā vaiśyajātīnāṃ kṛṣṇā caivāntyajanmanām ||
yasya varṇasya yā śastā tāṃ mṛdaṃ grāhayedbudhaḥ || 2 ||
asaṅkīrṇācchubhāddeśādgṛhītvā śoṣayenmṛdam ||
śoṣitāṃ tāṃ vimṛdnīyātsaśaivālena cāmbhasā ||3||
hastadīrgheṇa yantreṇa tadardhavistṛtena ca ||
tadardhamucchritenātha kartavyā veṣṭakā matāḥ || 4||
ātape tāstathā śuṣkā vipacennṛpasattama ||
kāṣṭhairasārairbhūpāla gokarīṣaistṛṇaistathā || 5 ||
mṛdunā nṛpa pākena na śāntyarthaṃ pacecca tāḥ ||
apakvāṃśca na saṃgrāhyā na vilīnāḥ kathañcana || 6 ||
tāsāmatyarthapakvānāṃ bhaṅgo bhavati rūpake ||
bhavantyasārāścāpakvā vilīnāḥ kardanāśadāḥ || 7 ||
iṣṭakānicaye vahniṃ yo naraḥ saṃprayacchati ||
somavikrayiṇā tulyaḥ sa tu pāpaḥ prakīrtitaḥ || 8 ||
tasmācchuddhena mūlena tatrāgniṃ dāpayennaraḥ ||
vartamānamukhaḥ satyaṃ saṃvibhajya prayatnataḥ || 9 ||
pakvāstu śītalībhūtāḥ svānurūpaistvarūpakaiḥ ||
samyaksaṃyojitā yojyā yathāsthāne narādhipa || 3.91.10 ||
iṣṭakāsañcaye naivamekaikaṃ rūpakaṃ budhaḥ ||
darśayettu suyuktena yamasyājñāṃ vijānatā || 11 ||
evaṃ rūpakavinyāsaṃ śilāsvapi vidhīyate||
evaṃ suyuktyā kurvaṃti kāṣṭhasaṃghātakaṃ tathā || 12 ||
kāṣṭhānāmāyasaṃ bandhaṃ samyakkāryaṃ vijānatā ||
iṣṭakānāmapakvānāṃ paṅkabandho vidhīyate ||13||
iṣṭakānāṃ tu pakvānāṃ śilānāṃ ca narādhipa ||
bandhaḥ kāryaḥ prayatnena vajralepakṛtaḥ śubhaḥ || 14 ||
pakveṣṭakānāṃ racanā mayoktā sudhānvitā saiva bhavennarendra ||
sudhāśilānāṃ tridaśādhivāse sudhāśilā naiva gṛheṣu deyā ||15||
iti śrīviṣṇudharmottare tṛtīyakhaṇḍe mā0 vaḥ saṃ0 iṣṭakāparīkṣā nāmaikanavatitamo'dhyāyaḥ|| 91 ||
<3.92>
mārkaṇḍeya uvāca ||
ataḥ paraṃ pravakṣyāmi vajralepavidhiṃ tava ||
bindukāṃśca kapitthāṃśca āmrānsaṃhṛtya yatnataḥ || 1 ||
śālmalīnāṃ tathā puṣpaṃ sallakībījameva ca ||
dhanuṣaśca tathā kalkaṃ vacāṃ ca manujeśvara || 2||
eteṣāṃ samavetānāṃ samānāṃ manujottama ||
toyamaṣṭaguṇaṃ kvāthamaṣṭabhāgā vaśeṣitam || 3 ||
tatastatra kṣipeddravyaṃ śuṇu tacca samāsataḥ ||
mallakīnāṃ tu vinyāsaṃ vatsalasya ca guggulam||4||
bhallātakāni bilvāni kurmuruṃ sarjameva ca ||
atasīṃ vasutapto'yaṃ vajralepaḥ prakīrtitaḥ || 5 ||
lākṣāṃ ca kundaruṃ caiva gṛhadhūmaṃ tathaiva ca ||
kapitthabilvamadhyāni tathā nāgaphalaṃ nṛpa || 6 ||
balāṃ ca madhukaṃ caiva kiṃjāpaṃ madanaṃ tathā ||
mañjiṣṭhāsallakaṃ sarjaṃ vajralepaḥ prakīrtitaḥ || 7 ||
viṣāṇāni gavāṃ rājanmahiṣāṇāṃ tathaiva ca ||
ajānāṃ ca tathā carma māhiṣyaṃ gavyameva ca || 8 ||
rasabiṃbakapitthāstu vajralepaḥ prakīrtitaḥ ||
aṣṭau bhāgāstu nāgasya kāṃsyabhāgadvayaṃ tathā || 9 ||
rītibhāgaṃ ca kathito vajralepastathaiva ca ||
sudhāpakvā tataḥ klinnā gavyacarmasamanvitā || 3.92.10 ||
khararomayutā rājanvajralepastathaiva ca ||
teṣāmekaikasaṃyuktāḥ kāryāḥ śailagṛhā nṛpa || 11 ||
pakveṣṭakagatāścaiva sthāyino manujottama ||
vajralepayutā hyete bhavantyapi dṛḍhāstathā || 12 ||
tiṣṭhanti sumahārāja saṃvatsaraśatānyapi ||
sahasrāṇi ca dharmajña cāyutānyarbudāni ca || 13 ||
sudhāvarṇena sarvatra lepaṃ dadyāttathā bahiḥ ||
tataśca tāṃ sudhāṃ dadyācchobhārthamarimardana || 14 ||
vajralepavidhānaṃ tu proktaṃ samyaktayā tava ||
devāyatanamukhyeṣu tathā ca bhavaneṣu ca || 15 ||
iti śrīviṣṇudharmottare tṛtīyakhaṇḍe mārkaṇḍeyavajrasaṃvāde vajralepanirūpaṇannāma dvinavatitamo'dhyāyaḥ || 92 ||
<3.93>
|| mārkaṇḍeya uvāca || ||
kṛte tu devāyatane vidhānaṃ nābhavatkṣitau ||
kaivalaṃ hi tadā hyāsītpratyakṣeṇa narādhipa || 1 ||
pratyakṣeṇāpi dṛśyante tretā dvāparayorjanāḥ ||
devānāṃ pratimāḥ kṛtvā pūjayanti yathāvidhim || 2 ||
gṛhe pratiṣṭhā tatrāpi tretāyāṃ tu pravartitā ||
dvāpare cāpyaraṇyeṣu ṛṣibhistu tataḥ kṛtā || 3 ||
teṣāṃ sampūjanaṃ nityaṃ siddhaistu kriyate nṛpa ||
pratiṣṭhāmātrakaraṇānnirvṛtā ṛṣayastu te ||4||
kalau pravṛttaḥ sarvo'yaṃ pratiṣṭhākaraṇo janaḥ ||
jñānaṃ paraṃ kṛtayuge tretāyāṃ ca tapaḥ parama || 5 ||
dvāpare tu tathā jñeyaṃ pratiṣṭhā tu kalau yuge ||
devatāveśmapūrvāṇi nagarāṇi kalau yuge || 6 ||
kartavyāni mahīpālaiḥ svargalokamabhīpsubhiḥ ||
rājā tu sarvanagaraṃ na kuryānnṛpasattama || 7 ||
yo na rājā hato yuddhe sa puraṃ kartumarhati ||
rūpakaṃ ca sunāmākhyaṃ kuryādrājā tathāpyasau || 8 ||
durgaṃ sarveṇa kartavyaṃ rājñā bhūpatisattama ||
bhūmidānādibhiryastu kuryātsuvihitaṃ mudā || 9 ||
pratiṣṭhāṃ sakalāṃ kuryādanyathā pāpabhāgbhavet ||
tatkṛtāyāṃ surārcāyāmaniṣṭaṃ syātkalau yuge || 3.93.10 ||
kṛtaṃ tu tadaniṣṭaṃ syājjanmāntaraśateṣvapi ||
kṛtatretādvāpareṣu pratiṣṭhākaraṇātphalama || 11 ||
yasmātteṣu kṛtāmarcāṃ siddhāḥ sampūjayantyalam ||
kalau kṛtāyāmarcāyāṃ siddhapūjā na vidyate || 12 ||
tasmātsuvihitā kuryādbhūmidānādibhiḥ kalau ||
yena rājñā hato rājā nānyo yudhi bhavetkalau || 13 ||
pratiṣṭhā tena kartavyā nagare pūrvanirmite ||
anujñāṃ nṛpateḥ prāpya kuyādanyo'pi yādava || 14 ||
sthirāṃ pratiṣṭhāṃ gṛhyāyāṃ nṛpānujñānakāraṇam ||
parapraṇītāṃ yastvarcāṃ sa kuryātpunareva tu || 15 ||
vidhānaṃ bhūmidānādyaṃ kuryāttatra na śaktitaḥ ||
dviguṇaṃ phalamāpnoti kulaṃ caiva samuddharet || 16 ||
kṛtvā suvihitāmarcāṃ pratiṣṭhāpya yathāvidhi ||
iṣṭāpūrtena dharmeṇa naro yujyatyasaṃśayam || 17 ||
rājasūyāśvamedhānāṃ phalena ca narādhipa ||
kṛte tretāyuge rājandvāpare ca narottamāḥ || 18 ||
āgacchanti nṛloke'sminsaśarīrā divaukasaḥ ||
nāgacchanti kalau yasmātsaśarīrāḥ kathaṃcana || 19 ||
āgacchanti pratiṣṭhāsu dvijairāvāhitāstataḥ ||
tasmātkalau prayatnena pratiṣṭhāṃ kārayedbudhaḥ || 3.93.20 ||
arcāsthaistridaśairlokaḥ kalau sarvo hi pālyate||
arcāgatāśca gṛhṇanti kṛtadevapratiṣṭhitāḥ || 21 ||
tasmātkalau prayatnena pratiṣṭhākaraṇaṃ hitam ||
yatnātsuvihitaṃ kuryādbhūmidānādibhissadā || 22 ||
iṣṭvā yajñairyathā yānti mānavāstridivālayam ||
tathaiva rājan gacchanti kṛtadevapratiṣṭhakāḥ || 23 ||
tasmātkalau pratiṣṭhānaṃ kartavyaṃ bhūridakṣiṇam ||
yatnātsuvihitaṃ kṛtvā bhūmidānādibhiḥ śubhā || 24 ||
durge pratiṣṭhā kartavyā nagare ca tathā śubhe ||
nityamāpaṇavīthyagre nānyatreti narādhipa || 25 ||
grāme vā yadi vā ghoṣe nāsti yatrāpaṇaṃ nṛpa ||
tatrodyāne bahiḥ kāryā grāmamadhye na kārayet || 26 ||
nadītīreṣu kartavyā vaneṣūpavaneṣu ca ||
sarasāṃ caiva tīreṣu girīṇāṃ śikhareṣu ca || 57 ||
upatyakāsu ramyāsu guhāsu ca viśeṣataḥ ||
eteṣu yānti sānnidhyaṃ sthāneṣu tridivaukasaḥ || 28 ||
jalāśayavihīneṣu deśeṣu manujottama ||
sānnidhyaṃ naiva kurvanti kadācittridivaukasaḥ || 29 ||
tathā suragṛhaṃ kāryaṃ yathā vāme jalāśayam ||
purastādvā bhavettasya nānyathā manujādhipa || 3.93.30 ||
samaṃtāttu jalaṃ śastaṃ dvīpe devagṛhe kṛte ||
yatra devagṛhaṃ kāryaṃ tatra bhūmiṃ parīkṣayeta || 31 ||
śvetā raktā tathā pītā kṛṣṇā varṇakramānvitā ||
kuśābhiśca śaraiḥ kāśairdūrvābhiśca tathā yutā || 32 |||
madhurā ca kaṣāyā ca tathā tu lavaṇā ca yā ||
varṇakramāddhitā bhūmiḥ sarveṣāṃ śṛṇvataḥ param || 33 ||
kaṇṭakidrumasaṅkīrṇā śarkarāloṣṭasaṃyutā ||
aśubhrā viṣamā durgā savalmīkā tathā ca yā || 34 ||
mūṣikāvāsabahulā pipīlakaguṇānvitā ||
nityaṃ ca śakaṭacchinnā plutā yā ca purodakaiḥ || 35 ||
aghātasūnā bhūmiśca bandhanāgārasaṃyutā ||
vasatiryatra dagdhā ca vidyutā vahnināpi vā || 36 ||
vasatāṃ dṛṣṭidoṣā ca kūrmapṛṣṭhā tathaiva ca ||
trikoṇā sragvihīnā ca śūrpākārā tathā ca yā || 39||
dakṣiṇena tathā nimnā nimnā pṛṣṭhata eva ca |
purāmbugrāhiṇī yā ca suśirā ca tathā nṛpa || 38 ||
khātaṃ ca sumṛdā yatra pūryate na punastathā ||
khāte yatra kṛtaṃ dīpaṃ pramlānimupagacchati || 39 ||
mālyaṃ ca mlāyate khāte tāṃ bhūmiṃ parivarjayeta ||
khāte toyena pūrṇe ca yatra toyaṃ na tiṣṭhati || 3.93.40 ||
tāṃ bhūmiṃ varjayadyatnāddurgandhā ca tathāpi yā ||
prāgudakpravaṇā śastā snigdhā ca sudṛḍhā tathā || 41 ||
khāte'dhikāṃ ca mṛdyatra pūryamāṇe bhavennṛpa ||
dīpo na mlāyate yatra nyastaṃ ca kusumaṃ tathā || 42||
toyaṃ santiṣṭhate khāte yatra kālāntaraṃ bahu ||
sā praśastā mahī jñeyā sugandhā susvanā tathā || 43 ||
bhuvaḥ parīkṣaṇaṃ kuryāddivase'tiśubhe tathā ||
bhuvaḥ parīkṣaṇaṃ yatra maṅgalyadravyadarśanam || 44 ||
śravaṇaṃ vā tathaiva syātsā śubhā syādvasundharā ||
na tāṃ rājanparīkṣeta praśastā sā prakīrtitā || 45 ||
amaṅgalyaṃ tathā dṛṣṭvā śrutaṃ vā nṛpasattama ||
bhuvaḥ parīkṣaṇaṃ tatra na kāryamaśubhā hi sā || 46 ||
śubhā mahī yatra bhavennṛvīra kāryā pratiṣṭhā bhavatīha tatra ||
kṛtā bhavettatra narendracandra hitāya kartuḥ svavivṛddhaye ca || 47 ||
iti śrīviṣṇudharmottare tṛtīyakhaṇḍe mārkaṇḍeyavajrasaṃvāde bhūmi parīkṣā nāma trinavatimo'dhyāyaḥ || 93 ||
<3.94>
|| mārkaṇḍeya uvāca ||
śubhāṃ parīkṣya vasudhāṃ tatra kuryātsurālayama ||
ādau tāṃ śodhayedvidvāndivase guṇasaṃyute || 1 ||
tatra deśaṃ śubhaṃ vidvāngomayenopalepayeta ||
tataḥ sampūjayettatra devadevaṃ vināyakam || 4 ||
tathā ca viśvakarmāṇaṃ bhāṇḍaṃ tadupayogi ca ||
palalollapikā bhakṣyakulmāṣaudanaparpaṭaiḥ || 3 ||
paramānnena satyaiśca puṣpadhūpānulepanaiḥ ||
pūjayitvā ca daivajñaṃ sāttvataṃ sthapatiṃ tathā || 4 ||
bhuvaḥ saṃśodhanaṃ kuryādyathāvatpṛthivīpatiḥ ||
tāvatsaṃśodhayedbhūmiṃ nijāṃ yāvatpradṛśyate || 5 ||
tadā saṃśodhayedvidvānyāvatsyācchalyavarjitā ||
tatastu pūrayetpaścāddṛḍhaṃ mṛttikayā punaḥ || 6 ||
tadvarṇayā tayā śastā yasya varṇasya kīrtitā ||
aśalyayā mṛttikayā pūrayetsudṛḍhaṃ punaḥ || 7 ||
kṛtvā tu pūritaṃ deśaṃ sudṛḍhaṃ susamantataḥ ||
susūkṣmaśalyajñānārthaṃ kuryācchalyaparīkṣaṇam || 8 ||
yathā vakṣyāmyahaṃ rājaṃstathā yatnena buddhimān ||
yadi syācchalyasaṃyuktā śalyamuddhṛtya yatnataḥ || 9 ||
punaḥ sampūrayedvidvānyatnācchubhamṛdā nṛpa ||
evaṃ saṃśodhya bhūmiṃ tu kumbhaṃ saṃsthāpayettataḥ || 3.94.10 ||
śilānyāsaṃ tu kurvīta tatraivā'hni caturdiśam ||
pakveṣṭāracite kāryāstā eva tu caturdiśam || 11 ||
mṛnmaye ceṣṭakānyāsaṃ kartavyaṃ na ca dāruṇe ||
śubhe divasanakṣatre muhūrte cābhipūjite || 12 ||
kumbhākārāṃ śilāṃ kuryācchailadevagṛhe śubham ||
anyatra tāmrakalaśaṃ sudṛḍhaṃ yatra saṃyutam || 13 ||
catuḥṣaṣṭipadaṃ kṛtvā same sthāne tu maṇḍalam ||
kṛtvā tu devatānyāsaṃ tatra maṇḍalake dvija || 14 ||
sarveṣāṃ vāstuvidyoktā devatānāṃ yathāvidhi ||
śriyāḥ sampūjanaṃ kṛtvā vāsudevasya cāpyatha || 15 ||
pūjanaṃ maṇḍale kārye vāstudevagaṇasya ca ||
vināyakasya devasya viśvakarmaṇa eva ca || 16 ||
bhāṇḍānāṃ ca mahārāja tathā tadupayoginām ||
gandhārghapuṣpanaivedyadhūpadīpairyathāvidhi || 17 ||
oṃkārapūtamājyaṃ ca juhuyācchrīdharasya ca ||
śriyāḥ kṛtvā tato homaṃ brahmaṇaḥ kārayettadā || 18 ||
brahmāṇaṃ purataḥ kṛtvā vāsudevagaṇasya ca ||
madhye kṛtvā tataḥ kumbhaṃ śilānyāsaṃ tu kārayeta || 19 ||
aiśāne tu tataḥ koṇe śilāṃ pūrvaṃ pratiṣṭhitām ||
pradakṣiṇaṃ tato rājañchilānyāsaṃ vidhīyate || 3.94.20 ||
sūtranyāsaṃ tu kurvīta tasminneva tathāhani ||
yathāniveśaṃ kartavyaṃ prāsādasya suniścitam || 21 ||
kṛtvā tu sūtravinyāsaṃ nādhikaṃ taṃ tu kārayet ||
nūnaṃ manujaśārdūla karturdoṣakarāvubhau|| 22 ||
kumbhasya ca śilānāṃ ca tataḥ sthānaṃ vidhīyate ||
vaṭāśvatthakaṣāyeṇa sarvauṣadhijalaistataḥ || 23 ||
tato'nulepanaṃ kuryāccandanena sugandhinā ||
ācchādanaṃ tataḥ kāryaṃ vāsobhiḥ kusumaistathā || 24 ||
dhūpaṃ dīpaṃ sunaivedyaṃ teṣāṃ nāmnā nivedayet ||
dakṣiṇābhirdvijendrāṇāṃ tataḥ pūjā vidhīyate || 25 ||
kālavitsthapatiḥ pūjyo tato rāma vijānatā ||
tataḥ kartā japenmantraṃ sthapatiṃ vātha kālavit || 26 ||
nande nandaya vāsiṣṭhe vasubhiḥ pūjayā saha ||
jaye bhārgavadāyāde prajābhyo jayamāvaha || 27 ||
pūrṇeṅgirasadāyāde pūrṇakāmaṃ kuruṣva mām ||
bhadre kaśyapadāyāde kuru bhadrāṃ gatiṃ mama || 28 ||
sarvabījasamāyukte sarvaratnauṣadhairvṛte ||
rucire nandite nande vāsiṣṭhe ramyatāmiha || 29 ||
prajāpatisute devi caturasre mahīmayi ||
subhage suvrate bhadre iha kāśyapi ramyatām || 3.94.30 ||
avyaṅge cākṣate pūrṇe muneraṃgirasaḥ sute ||
iṣṭake tvaṃ prayaccheṣṭaṃ pratiṣṭhāṃ kāmayāmyaham || 31 || 
deśasvāmipurasvāmipaśusvāmiparigrahe ||
manuṣyapaśuhastyaśvadhana vṛddhikarī bhava || 32 ||
anenaiva vidhānena kāryā bhūmiḥ samīkṣayā ||
dvāranyāsaṃ mahārāja dvārocchrāyastathaiva ca || 33 ||
stambhocchrāyaṃ tathā vaṃśavinyāsaṃ ca yadūttama ||
tathāmalakasārasya cihnasya tu samucchrayam ||34||
āyudhaṃ vāhanaṃ cihnaṃ yasya devasya yadbhavet||
tasmādekatamaṃ cihnaṃ kāryaṃ cāmalasārake || 35 ||
sudhāsite samāpte tu citrakarma vidhīyate ||
kaṭiḥ sucitritā kāryā pṛṣṭhaṃ kāryaṃ sudhāsitam || 36 ||
citralakṣaṇavatkāryaṃ sarvakāmasamṛddhidam ||
āścaryāṇi tathānyāni tatra yatnānniveśayeta || 37 ||
cihnaṃ viṣṇugṛhe tatra garuḍaṃ vāpi kārayeta ||
durgāgṛhe tathā siṃhaṃ triśūlaṃ haraveśmani || 38 ||
padmaṃ lakṣmīgṛhe cihnaṃ haṃsaṃ syādbrahmaṇo gṛhe ||
makaraṃ kāmadevasya tālaṃ saṅkarṣaṇasya tu || 39 ||
ardhacandrasthitaṃ siṃhamādityasya gṛhe bhavet ||
vṛttamaṇḍalamadhyasthaṃ mṛgaṃ candrasamo bhavet || 3.94.40 ||
vajraṃ śakrasya kurvīta daṇḍaṃ vaivasvatasya ca ||
varuṇasya tathā pāśaṃ gadāṃ ca dhanadasya vai || 41 ||
skandasya śaktiḥ kartavyā gaṇādhyakṣasya pārthiva ||
kartavyaṃ paraśościhnaṃ vahnerjvālā vidhīyate || 42 ||
vāyorambarabhaṅgantu uṣṭraṃ nirṛtaye tathā ||
anyeṣāmapi devānāṃ cihnaṃ syādāyudhaṃ svakam || 43 ||
tato dhvajasya vinyāsaḥ kartavyaḥ pṛthivīpate ||
asurā vāsamicchanti dhvajahīne surālaye || 44 ||
dhvajena sakalaṃ pāpaṃ dhvajadasya vinaśyati ||
tasmātsarvaprayatnena dadyāddevagṛhe dhvajam || 45 ||
evaṃ kṛte devagṛhe narendra vṛddhiḥ sadā syānna hi saṃśayo'tra ||
tasmātprayatnena vidhānayuktaṃ devālayaṃ kāryamadīnasattva || 46 ||
iti śrīvi0 dha0 tṛ0 kha0 mā0 va0 saṃ0 brahmaśilpadhvajavidhirnāma caturṇavatitamo'dhyāyaḥ || 94 ||
<3.95>
evaṃ vādinaṃ śrībhagavantamamalatapasamatiyaśa sañcirajīvinaṃ śrīmārkaṇḍeyaṃ rājā vajraḥ papraccha |
bhagavannaṣṭaśalyajñānaṃ kathaṃ bhavati tamuvāca śrībhagavānmārkaṇḍeyaḥ |
kadācitkamapi mahadbhūtaṃ trailokyanāśāyārthitamabhavat |
tadbhūtaṃ sasaṃbhramaistridaśairnigṛhya vegenādhomukhaṃ nyastam ||
tasya cotthānabhītā devā aṅgeṣūpaviṣṭāḥ |
tasya parjanyādyāḥ krameṇa dakṣiṇe'ṅgabhāge, vyutkrameṇa nāgādyā vāme, parjanyahutāśanau śirasi, karagrahau vadane,dṛśośca yajñakāmau, stanayormahendrāditī, śravaṇayoḥ ravyaditī, uraḥsthale aṃsayośca satyabhṛśāntarikṣapavanāḥ |
bhuje candrabhallāṭamukhyā nāgarājānaḥ, dvitīye bhuje sāvitrasavitārau karau rudravyādhī dvitīye kare, aryamā pṛthivīdharaśca karasya kakṣyayoḥ, puṣpavitathabahakṣatāḥ pārśve, śoṣaśokayakṣāśca dvitīye pārśve, hṛdi brahmā, vivasvānmitraśca jaṭhare, jayendrau meḍhre, vṛṣaṇayośca yamavaruṇau, sphiji vṛṣāsurau, ūrvoḥ gandharvapuṣpadantau, jānvoḥ mṛgagrīvau, jaṅghayoḥ pitṛdauvārikau caraṇayoḥ, evamayameśānī dik śiraḥ nairṛtī digādau vāstupuruṣaḥ ||
tatra ca yatsvāṅgaṃ spṛśapraṣṭā prayacchati tatraiva vāstvaṅge śalyaṃ vidyāt |
athavā yatraivāṅge gṛhapateḥ bhūyobhūyo rugbhavati ||
atha vāstudevatāmekaikāmuddiśya ghṛtaṃ juhuyāt |
yadvājyaṃ hūyamāne agnilakṣaṇamaśarabhaṃ bhavettaddevadeśe vā ||
athaitaddevatābhāge sukhena śalyajñānamahaṃ catuṣṣaṣṭipadavibhāgena vakṣyāmi |
tatra bahiḥ pūrveṇārdhapadeśvaraḥ |
parjanyaḥ adhyardhapadeśvaraḥ, parjanyaḥ adhyardhapadeśvarakaragrahaḥ mahendraravisatyavṛṣāḥ yathākramaṃ dvidvigraheśāḥ; antarikṣodhyardhapadeśaḥ |

vitatho'dhyardhapadeśaḥ |

mukhyo'rdhapadeśaḥ |
bhallāṭacandrādiditayo dvipadeśāḥ |
rāgo'dhyardhapadeśaḥ |

vastuto navadhā vibhaktasya madhye vakāraḥ akanata evaṃ saga ityete krameṇa prācyādiṣu varṇāḥ |
varṇaprathamaśravaṇādvāstuni taddiśi śalyam |

pāpayukteṣu saśalyaṃ ca kendre rāśivaśā digbhavati balinigrahe asthiśalyam |
balahīne śarkarāṅgārakāṣṭhatuṣabhasmādi tatra sarvepvasthiṣu karturmaraṇaṃ bhavati śarkarātuṣakeśāṅgārabhasmaśalyeṣu śokānarthavyādhimaraṇacauropadravāḥ saśalye ca devatāyatane devatāsannidhānaṃ na bhavati |

vajra uvāca ||
kasminkāle tu kartavyā pratiṣṭhā bhṛgunandana ||
kasminkasminkṛtā kāle tadā bhavati kīdṛśī || 1 ||
mārkaṇḍeya uvāca ||
śṛṇu kālaṃ pravakṣyāmi pratiṣṭhākaraṇe tava ||
yasminyasminkṛtā kāle yathā bhavati yādava || 2 ||
nityaṃ sannihitā loke vaiṣṇave ta yuge kṛtā ||
dhanadhānyavatī sphītā varadā ca tathā bhavet || 3 ||
dṛṣṭā bhavati vikhyātā tridaśeśaguroryuge ||
aindre tejovatī sphītā bhūtanigrahakāriṇī ||4||
tathāvidhā tathāgneye bhūtadāhamavāpnuyāta ||
nityāścaryayutā tvāṣṭre gamyā lokaistathaiva ca || 5 ||
āhirbudhnye dṛḍhā nityaṃ lokānugrahakāriṇī |||
pitrye vināśamāpnoti kartuśca maraṇāvahā ||6||
vaiśve lokahitā karturlokagamyā kṛtā bhaveta ||
saumyā saumye tathā kāntā varadā ca tathā bhavet || 7 ||

lokābhigamyā bhāgye syātkarturdoṃṣavatī bhaveta ||

saṃvatsarākhye kartavyā nityamājñāyutā bhaveta ||

yasya pakṣasya vṛddhiḥ syāttasmiñchubhavahā bhavet ||

bhaume'gninā pradahyeta budhena dhanadā bhavet ||
guruṇā ca dṛḍhā nityaṃ lokānandakarī site || 26||

nityāścaryayutā loke citrāyāṃ ca hitapradā ||

ugrā syātsūryahorāyāṃ saumyā somasya kīrtitā ||
pāpagrahasya dreṣkāṇe karturnāśāya kīrtitā || 97 ||
somagrahasya suśubhā karturbhavati vṛddhidā ||
phalaṃ yadeva lagneṣu navāṃśeṣu tadeva tu || 98 ||
dvādaśāṃśeṣu vijñeyastadeva manujottama ||
karturnāśāya vijñeyā triṃśāṃśe bhaumasaurayoḥ || 99 ||
sitajīvenduputrāṇāṃ kartuḥ kalyāṇadā bhaveta ||
śubhamaṃśaṃ mahārāja lagne pāpaphale śubham || 3.96.100 ||
lagne śubhatare jñeyaḥ pāpāṃśaḥ pāpado nṛṇāma ||
lagnasthe śaṅkarā kartuḥ kṛtā bhavati bhāskare || 101 ||
dvitīye'rthasya hāniḥ syāttṛtīye dhanasaṃyutā ||
āyuḥkṣayaṃ caturthasthe pañcame svatvanāśanam ||102||
ṣaṣṭhe śatruvighātaṃ tu dāridryaṃ saptame bhavet ||
maraṇaṃ cāṣṭame bhānau navame dharmapīḍanam|| ||103||
daśame sutavṛddhiśca lābhe lābhayutā bhavet ||
dvādaśe vyayasaṃyuktā nirdhanā tu tathā bhavet||1 04||
candre lagnagate jñeyā sudṛḍhā manujottama ||
dhanānvitā dvitīye tu tṛtīye lābhasaṃyutā ||105||
caturthe bāndhavahitā pañcame sutavṛddhidā ||
ṣaṣṭhe ripuvināśāya saptame ca dhanāvahā || 106 ||
aṣṭame maraṇāyoktā navame dharmapīḍanam ||
daśame karmadā proktā lābhe lābhapradā bhavet || 107 ||
vyaye vyayavatī rājaṃstathā dhanavivarjitā ||
ardhabimbādhike kṣīṇe kramādatha nibodha me || 108 ||
candre lagnagate jñeyā kṣipranāśā mahābhuja ||
dvitīye ca daridrā syāttṛtīye dhanasaṃyutā || 109 ||
bahvanarthā caturthe syātputranāśāya pañcame ||
ṣaṣṭhe ripuvināśāya bhāryānāśāya saptame || 3.96.110 ||
aṣṭame maraṇāyoktā navame dharmaghātinī ||
dhanānvitā syāddaśame lābhe lābhavatī bhavet || 111 ||
vyaye vyayavatī nitye daridrā ca tathā bhavet ||
lagne mṛtyukaro bhaumastathā dāridryado dhane || 112 ||
kīrtiṃ tribhuvane śubhrāṃ tṛtīyasthaḥ karotyasau ||
caturthe bandhunāśaḥ syātsutanāśastathā sute || 113 ||
arināśastathā ṣaṣṭhe saptame'gnau vinaśyati ||
aṣṭame kartṛmaraṇaṃ navame deśavicyutiḥ || 114 ||
daśame karmasiddhiḥ syāllābhe lābhavatī bhavet ||
dhanajāyātmajairnityaṃ viyogo vyayage bhavet || 115 ||
budho lagne dhanayuto dvitīye ca tathā bhavet ||
tṛtīye varadā proktā caturthe dhanasaṃyutā || 116 ||
kīrtiśca pañcame jñeyā ṣaṣṭhe ripuvināśinī ||
sukhadā saptame jñeyā harṣadā ca tathāṣṭame || 117 ||
saukhyadā navame rājandaśame karmavṛddhidā ||
sthirā lābhagate jñeyā vyaye dhanasamanvitā ||118||
jīve dhanāḍhyā lagnasthe dvitīye ca tathā bhavet ||
dṛḍhāspadā tṛtīye syāccaturthe dhanasaṃyutā || 119 ||
pañcame sutadā proktā ṣaṣṭhe śatruvināśinī ||
saptame rājyadā karturaṣṭame ca samṛddhidā || 3.96.120 ||
kīrtidā navame jñeyā daśame ca samṛddhidā ||
lābhasthe lābhadā jñeyā vyaye lābhasamanvitā || 121 ||
lagnasthe niścalā śukre sarvakāmapradā bhavet ||
dhanage ca dhanaṃ dadyāttṛtīye bhrātṛvṛddhidā || 122 ||
caturthe subhagā jñeyā pañcame sutavṛddhidā ||
ṣaṣṭhe pūjyā tathā loke saptame dhanavṛddhidā || 123 ||
aṣṭame sukhadā proktā navame ca sukhārthadā ||
daśame lokakāntā syādarināśastathāyuge || 124 ||
tathā vyayasthe vijñeyā savyayā sadhanā tayā ||
mṛtyudā lagnage saurau daridrā syāddvitīyage || 125 ||
tṛtīye dhanalābhāya caturthe kartṛmṛtyudā ||
sutage sutanāśaḥ syādarināśastathārige || 126 ||
jāyānāśastu jāyāsthe mṛtyurmṛtyugṛhopage ||
dharmage dharmahāniḥ syātkarmavṛddhistu karmage || 127 ||
mahīlābhastu lābhasthe vyayage ca vyayo bhavet ||
śūnyaṃ vivarjayetkendraṃ śubhākendre niyojayeta || 128 ||
evaṃ jñātvā śubhaṃ kālaṃ prayatnena tu daivavit ||
chāyāmbubhiḥ prayatnena gṛhṇīyādavadhānataḥ || 129 ||
śubhe'hni pūrvayantrasthaṃ tatkālaṃ pratimāṃ svayam ||
samāṃ tu pīṭhikāṃ garte sthapatirviniveśayet || 3.96.130 ||
tatkālameva kurvīta nāma cāsyāḥ samāhitaḥ ||
svasthāṃ tāṃ tu svanāṃ nānte vaiṣṇavī pratimā bhaveta || 131 ||
keśavāntāḥ striyaḥ kāryā nāthānto brāhmaṇo bhavet ||
īśvarāntā harasyoktā mihirāntā raveḥ smṛtā || 132 ||
anyeṣāṃ devanāmāntāḥ surāṇāṃ pratimāḥ smṛtāḥ ||
sūkṣmā tu pratimā kāryā dvitīyā cādhivāsane || 133
citrakarmakarā yā ca mṛnmayī yā tathā bhavet ||
tasyāḥ pavitrake kāryaṃ tathā caivādhivāsanam || 134 ||
saṃvatsaraṃ tu kartavyaṃ ṣaṇmāsānatha vā punaḥ ||
pakṣaṃ dvādaśarātraṃ vā saptāhaṃ tryahameva vā || 135 ||
ekāhamekarātraṃ vā yathāvadadhivāsanam ||
vibhavenātimahatā gītairnṛtyaiḥ suśobhanaiḥ || 136 ||
nityamambupradānaiśca vahnipūjābhireva ca ||
nijapuṇyāhaghoṣeṇa kartavyamadhivāsanam || 137 ||
tataḥ pratiṣṭhā kartavyā kāle saṃvatsarocite ||
śubhe kāle kṛtā sā tu parā vṛddhikarī bhavet || 138 ||
rājñāṃ prajānāṃ kartuśca tathā kālavido nṛpa ||
ṛtvijāṃ ca mahārāja śubhakālakṛtā hitā || 139 ||

ghṛtavatī ghṛtena viṃśatitamam ||
aghamarṣaṇena ratnairekaviṃśam ||
evamekaviṃśatyātmakena somena tāṃ śocayitvā paramapāvanā mantrā japtavyāḥ || aghamarṣaṇaṃ daivakṛtaṃ śuddhavatyastaratsamāḥ ||
kūṣmāṇḍāḥ pāvamānyaśca durgā devī sarasvatī || 1 ||
cāndrāyaṇaṃ ca ṛṣabhaṃ vāmadevaṃ rathantaram ||
pauruṣaṃ ca tathā sūktaṃ śrīsūktamathavāpsaram|| 2 ||
abhijñāṃ ca pavitraṃ ca caritaṃ vaiṣṇavaṃ tathā ||
vāditraśabdairjayaśabdamiśrairvṛtaistathā bhūpavarāṅganānām |
śauce nivṛtte tvadhivāsanaṃ vai kāryaṃ surārcāsu yathābhidhāsye || 3 ||
iti śrīviṣṇu0 dha0 tṛ0 kha0 mā0 va0 sa0 arcāśaucakathanaṃ nāma śatatamo'dhyāyaḥ || 100 ||
<3.101>
mārkaṇḍeya uvāca ||
athārcāśaucānantaraṃ pavitrakasyārcāyāḥ kuryāt ||
tatra kamalakarṇikāyāṃ dhruvā dyauriti mantreṇa dakṣiṇaśiraskaṃ prākchirasaṃ vā śayyāyāṃ sthāpayet||
ataḥ pañcavarṇaiḥ pañcabhirāstaraṇairāstarayet|
śuddhāvatyaścodīrayet|
tato yuvā suvāsā iti pracchādanapaṭaṃ dadyāt ||
gaṇḍopadhānaṃ ca |
tato garbhamayaṃ saptaśīrṣamanantaṃ nāgaṃ kuryāt |
taṃ ca śayane sthāpayet ||
tato jīvasyāvāhanaṃ kuryāta śivasaṃkalpaṃ codīrayet |
tatastvanantasyāvāhanaṃ prayojayet | tatorghyapādyamācamanīyamanulepanamālyadīpadhūpamadhuparkaprāpaṇakamātrābhiḥ pratyekaṃ sāvitreṇānantaramarcayet ||
tataḥ sarpasāma |
namostu sarpebhya iti yogeyogeti mantraṃ codīrayet ||
anantamāhātmyaṃ ca vā vadet ||
tato dhruvā dyauriti mantreṇānantopari śrībhagavadarcā pavitrake sthāpayet ||
tato jīvasyāvāhanaṃ kuryāt |
śivasaṅkalpaṃ japet |
tato bhagavantamāvāhayet |
tataḥ sarve bhūnyastajānavaḥ praṇāmaṃ kuryuḥ āgataśca bhagavāniti brūyuḥ |
tataḥ śaṅkhapaṭahabherījayaśabdaiḥ pūjayeyuḥ |
tato'rghyapādyācamanīya snānīyānulepanamālyadhūpadīpamadhuparkaprāpaṇakamātrādānaiḥ śrībhagavantaṃ pratyekaṃ savitreṇārcayet |
tataḥ svasti na indra iti japet |
tato yuvāsuvāsā iti śrībhagavantaṃ mahatā vāsoyugenācchādayet yuñjate mana iti puṣpairavakiret |
bodhaścamā pratibodhaśceti siddhārthairavakiret |
prādurabhāvāniti vācayet |
evamadhivāsanaṃ kāryam ||
adhivāsite ca bhagavati arcāsthaṇḍile vediṃ pratiṣṭhāpya āsanāni pādapīṭhāni ca arcāśaucavidhānena vedimāsanāni ca śodhayet |
tataḥ śaṅkaragītoktavidhinā ijyāṃ sāttvataḥ kuryāt |
ijyāsamakālameva homasthaṇḍile hotā homaṃ vedīśaucamāsanānāṃ sakṛdeva vidhatte ||
ijyāhomo tu kartavyo pratyahaṃ tvadhivāsite |
pūjanaṃ gītanṛtyaiśca pratiṣṭhā yāvadāgatā |
adhivāsanametatte mayā samyagudāhṛtam || 1 ||
iti śrīviṣṇu0 dha0 tṛ0 kha0 mā0 va0 saṃ0 adhivāsanavarṇano nāmaikādhikaśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ ||101||
<3.102>
|| mārkaṇḍeya uvāca ||
āvāhayāmyahaṃ jīvaṃ bījaṃ sarvagataṃ prabhum ||
pañcabhāvasthitaṃ dehe pañcadhā ca sthitaṃ punaḥ || 1 ||
vāyavyena tu bhāvena pañcadhāvasthitaṃ punaḥ ||
āgneyena tu bhāvena pañcadhāvasthitaṃ tathā || 2 ||
vāruṇena ca bhāvena pañcadhāvasthitaṃ prabhum ||
bhūtātmakatvena tathā pañcadhāvasthitaṃ punaḥ || 3 ||
paramātmānamatyugramanugraṃ jagadīśvaram ||
sarvagaṃ sarvadhātāraṃ sarveśamaparājitam || 4 ||
atyarthasūkṣmaṃ varadaṃ kṣetrajñaṃ prabhumīśvaram ||
|bhūtabhavyabhaviṣyasya prabhuṃ lokanamaskṛtam || 5 ||
guṇādhāraṃ jagadyoniṃ mahadbhyopi mahattaram ||
jīvadeva tvamabhyehi praviśārcāmimāṃ svayam || 6 ||
kuruṣva devadeveśa sajīvaṃ ca tathānagha ||
idamarghyamidaṃ pādyaṃ dhūpo'yaṃ pratigṛhyatām || 7 ||
iti śrīvi0 dha0 tṛ0 kha0 mā0 va0 saṃ0 jīvāvāhanaṃ nāma dvayuttaraśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ || 102 ||
<3.103>
vajra uvāca ||
āvāhanāni me brūhi devatānāṃ bhṛgūttama ||
bhavantyāvāhitā mantrairyasmātsannihitāḥ surāḥ || 1 ||
mārkaṇḍeya uvāca ||
āvāhanāni te vacmi devānāṃ yadunandana ||
athoktavarjaṃ keṣāṃcitkeṣāṃcitpunareva te || 2 ||
sarvasyāvāhanasyānte ślokārdhaṃ parikīrtitam ||
idamarghyaṃ ca pādyaṃ ca dhūpo'yaṃ pratigṛhyatām || 3 ||
āvāhayāmyahaṃ devau nāsatyau sūryanandanau ||
āgacchetāṃ mahābhāgau varadau cāśvinau śubhau || 4 ||
vasūnāvāhayiṣyāmi devānaṣṭau varapradān ||
āyāntu varadā devā vasavaḥ pāpanāśanāḥ || 5 ||
viśvānāvāhayiṣyāmi devānadbhutatejasaḥ ||
āyāntu varadāḥ sarve viśvedevā mahābalāḥ || 6 ||
āvāhayāmyaṅgiraso devānadbhutatejasaḥ ||
āyāntvaṅgiraso devā mahābala parākramāḥ || 7 ||
rudrānāvāhayiṣyāmi trinetrānvṛṣavāhanān ||
āyāntu varadā rudrāstrailokyavaradāḥ śubhāḥ || 8 ||
āvāhayiṣyāmyādityān varadāndīptatejasaḥ ||
āyāntu varadāḥ sūryāḥ sarve te tejasolbaṇāḥ || 9 ||
bhṛgūnāvāhayiṣyāmi devāndīptatapodhanān ||
āyāntu bhṛgavo devā varadā dīptatejasaḥ || 3.103.10 ||
surānāvāhayiṣyāmi marutastejasādhikāḥ ||
āyāntu maruto devā varadā bhuvaneśvarāḥ || 11 ||
surānāvāhayiṣyāmi sarvāneva divaukasaḥ ||
āgacchantu surāḥ sarve mantrapūjābhilāṣiṇaḥ || 12 ||
daityānāvāhayiṣyāmi mahābalaparākramān ||
āyāntu daityā dharmiṣṭhā devadevānugā mama || 13 ||
āvāhayiṣye gandharvānrūpadraviṇasaṃyutān ||
āyāntu sarvagandharvā varadāḥ kāmarūpiṇaḥ || 14 ||
yakṣānāvāhayiṣyāmi dhanādhyakṣasamanvitān ||
āyāntu varadā yakṣāstrailokyaviditā mama || 15||
āvāhayiṣyāmi tathā rākṣasānvividhāyudhān||
āyāntu rākṣasā vīrā devadevānuyāyinaḥ || 16 ||
āvāhayiṣyāmi tathā vidyādharagaṇānaham ||
vidyādharāḥ samāyāntu vividhāḥ gaganaṅgamāḥ||17||
āvāhayiṣyāmi tathā kinnarāndevagāyanān||
āyāntu kinnarāḥ sarve susvarāstu sulocanāḥ || 18||
nāgānāvāhayiṣyāmi bhujagānsarpagocarāna||
āyāntu varadā nāgāstrailokyaviditā mama || 19 ||
ahamāvāhayiṣyāmi tathaivāpsarasaḥ śubhāḥ ||
samāyāntu mahābhāgā devayoṣā mamojjvalāḥ || ||| 3.103.20 ||
ahamāvāhayiṣyāmi sarvāstridaśapatnayaḥ ||
āyāntu varadāḥ sarvāstaptacāmīkaraprabhāḥ || 21 ||
āvāhayiṣyāmi tathā varadā devamātaraḥ ||
āyāntu varadāḥ sarvā suveśāḥ sukhadāḥ śivāḥ || 22 ||
pitṝnāvāhayiṣyāmi sūkṣmarūpadharānaham ||
āyāntu pitaraḥ sarve gaṇasthāstu pṛthakpṛthak || 23 ||
āvāhayiṣyāmi tathā suparṇānbalavattarān ||
suparṇāstu samāyāntu viṣṇutejopabṛṃhitāḥ || 24 ||
āvāhayāmi varadānsarvāneva prajāpatīn ||
prajādhyakṣāḥ samāyāntu trailokyaparipālakāḥ || 25 ||
āvāhayāmi devendrāndevānbhuvananāyakān ||
āyāntu devanāthā me varadāḥ kāmarūpiṇaḥ || 26 ||
manūnāvāhayiṣyāmi prabhutve jagataḥ sthitān ||
āyāntu manavaḥ sarve tapasā naṣṭakilbiṣāḥ || 27 ||
āvāhayiṣyāmyoṣadhyaḥ sarvaprāṇidharāḥ śubhāḥ ||
oṣadhyaśca samāyāntu sarvāḥ kāmapradāḥ śubhāḥ || 28 ||
ṛṣīnāvāhayiṣyāmi tapasā dyotitaprabhān ||
āyāntu ṛṣayaḥ siddhāḥ sarve me dīptatejasaḥ ||
śailānāvahāyiṣyāmi sarvauṣadhisamanvitān || 29 ||
āyāntu bhūdharāḥ śailāḥ sarve me sthāvareśvarāḥ ||
drumānāvāhayiṣyāmi nānārūpadharānahama || 3.103.30 ||
drumāḥ sarve samāyāntu manomārutaraṃhasaḥ ||
āvāhayiṣyāmi tathā pātālānsarvagocarān || 31||
āyāntu sarve pātālā daityasthānā hyavinditāḥ ||
dvīpānāvāhayiṣyāmi sarvānadbhutadhāriṇaḥ || 32 ||
āyāntu dvīpā lokānāṃ sthānabhūtā jagatpriyāḥ ||
lokānāvāhayiṣyāmi sthānabhūtānmanoharān || 33 ||
āyāntu lokāḥ sakalāḥ sarve me bhūtabhāvanāḥ ||
āvāhayiṣyāmi tathā samudrāñjagataḥ priyān || 34 ||
samudrā me samāyāntu rasādhārā jagatpriyāḥ ||
varṣānāvāhayiṣyāmi sarvabhūtāśrayāniha || 35 ||
āgacchantu tathā ceha varṣāḥ sarvamanoharāḥ ||
āvāhayiṣyāmi tathā caturaḥ sāgarānaham || 36 ||
sāgarāḥ śīghramāyāntu sarve toyadharā mama ||
nadīstvāvāhayiṣyāmi varadā lokamātaraḥ || 37 ||
āyāntu varadā nadyaḥ sarvapāpaharāḥ śubhāḥ ||
āvāhayiṣyāmi tathā sarvānabhragaṇānaham || 38 ||
āyāntvabhrā mahābhāgāḥ sarve cāśucarā mama ||
āvāhayiṣyāmi tathā piśācānsarvagānaham||
piśācāstu samāyāntu sarve me kāmarūpiṇaḥ||39||

nānārūpāḥ samāyāntu skandasya pārśvagā mama ||

ahamāvāhayiṣyāmi muhūrtānsūkṣmarūpiṇaḥ || 48 ||
muhūrtāḥ śīghramāyāntu kālasyāvayavāḥ śubhāḥ ||
ṛtūnāvāhayiṣyāmi ṣaḍahaṃ bhuvaneśvarān || 49 ||

āvāhayiṣyāmi tathā pañcasaṃvatsarānaham||3.103.50||

iha sarve samāyāntu sarve tridaśavāhanāḥ ||
