iti śrīviṣṇudhamottare tṛtīyakhaṇḍe mārkaṇḍeyavajrasaṃvāde pratiṣṭhākalpe devasaṃvāhvānaṃ nāma tryuttaraśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ || 103 ||
<3.104>
mārkaṇḍeya uvāca ||
āvāhayāmyahaṃ devaṃ brahmāṇaṃ parameṣṭhinam ||
jagato'sya samutpattisthitisaṃhārakāraṇam || 1 ||
caturvedaṃ caturvaktraṃ cāturvarṇyaprabhuṃ prabhum ||
caturāśramagoptāraṃ varadaṃ bhūtabhāvanam || 2 ||
padmayoniṃ jagadyoniṃ yajñayoniṃ jagatpatim ||
ātmayonimanādhṛṣyaṃ yajñavāhaṃ jagatpatim || 3 ||
yajñeśaṃ yajñamūrtiṃ ca trailokyasyaikakāraṇam ||
sarvagaṃ nāradaṃ saumyaṃ paramaṃ parameśvaram || 4 ||
bhūtabhavyabhavannāthaṃ yogajñeyaṃ sanātanam ||
āgaccha bhagavanbrahmanyajamānasya vṛddhaye || 5 ||
idamarghyamidaṃ pādyaṃ dhūpaṃ cedaṃ pragṛhyatām ||
āvāhayāmyahaṃ devaṃ devadevaṃ hutāśanam ||6 ||
jagato'sya samutpattisthiti saṃhārakārakam ||
devamūrtiṃ tadādhāraṃ sarvadevamukhaṃ prabhum || 7 ||
piṅgekṣaṇaṃ piṅgajaṭaṃ dhūmaketuṃ vibhāvasum ||
śukayānagamaṃ devaṃ sarvābharaṇabhūṣitam || 8 ||
sarvagaṃ varadaṃ bhānuṃ jaṭharasthaṃ ca dehinām ||
tejomūrtiṃ durādharṣaṃ saptajihvaṃ mahābalam || 9 ||
saptārciṣaṃ saptamuniṃ samitsaptakamacyutam ||
viṣṇoraṃśamanirdeśyaṃ bhānavaṃ jātavedasam || 3.104.10 ||
pāvanaṃ puṣṭidaṃ saumyaṃ bhaktānāṃ bhūtivardhanam ||
yājakaṃ yajamānaṃ ca puṣṭyā vibhaja sarvaśaḥ || 11 ||
idamarghyamidaṃ pādyaṃ dhūpaṃ cedaṃ pragṛhyatām ||
āvāhanānyato vakṣye sarveṣāṃ pārthivottama || 12 ||
sarvasyāvāhanasyānte śloko'yaṃ parikīrtitaḥ ||
praviśyārcāmimāṃ samyaṅ mayā bhaktyā vinirmitām || 13 ||
idamarghyamidaṃ pādyaṃ dhūpaṃ cedaṃ pragṛhyatām ||
dhruvamāvāhayiṣyāmi sarvadeva gaṇeśvaram || 14 ||
nibaddhaṃ bhrāmyate yena bhacakraṃ vātarajjubhiḥ ||
ehi me dhruva deveśa keśavācintyavikrama || 15 ||
sarvagrahagaṇādhyakṣa sarvalokanamaskṛta ||
āvāhayiṣyāmyākāśaṃ vimānaśatamaṇḍitam || 16 ||
tṛtīyaṃ devadevasya padaṃ viṣṇormahātmanaḥ ||
ehi me bhagavansaumya nirālamba nabhastala || 17||
aprameyātigambhīra vimānaśatamaṇḍita ||
ūrdhvamāvāhayiṣyāmi cānantāṃ mahatīṃ diśam || 18 ||
aprameyāṃ nirālambāṃ candrasūryāṃśuvarjitām ||
āgaccheha mahābhāge satataṃ siddhasevite || 19 ||
anante vipule devi nirmale hitakāriṇi ||
āvāhayāmyadhastāttu diśaṃ śeṣeṇa pālitām || 3.104.20 ||
nāgadaityoragagaṇaiḥ satataṃ ca niṣevitām ||
gambhīre vipule bhīme bahusthānātimaṇḍite || 21 ||
ehi me ratnabahule bahurūpe mahābale||
āvāhayiṣye varadaṃ sahasrāṃśuṃ divākaram ||
tejomūrtiṃ durādharṣaṃ bhaktānāmabhayapradam||
ehi deva jagannātha ṛksāmayajuṣāṃ pate ||23||
trailokyamaṇḍale dīpa sarvavyādhivināśana ||
āvāhayāmyahaṃ devamādityamudakeśayam || 24 ||
snigdhavaidūryasaṅkāśaṃ varuṇaṃ sumahādyutim ||
ehi deva jalādhyakṣa yādogaṇamaheśvara || 25 ||
nāgadaityoragagaṇaiḥ satataṃ sevitācyuta ||
āvāhayāmyahaṃ candraṃ śītāṃśumamṛtaprabham || 26 ||
oṣadhīśaṃ dvijādhyakṣaṃ nayanānandakārakama ||
ehi me bhagavañcandra śaśāṅka mṛgalāñchana || 27 ||
bhaktānukampinsatataṃ sarvanakṣatrapūjita ||
bhaumamāvāhayiṣyāmi tejomūrtiṃ durāsadam || 28 ||
bhadramūrtimanirdeśyaṃ vaktraṃ rudhirasaprabham ||
ehi me bhagavanbhauma aṅgārakasamaprabha || 29 ||
tvayi sarvaṃ samāyattaṃ bhūtale'smiñśubhāśubham ||
skandamāvāhayiṣyāmi ṣaṇmukhaṃ varadaṃ śiśum || 3.104.30 ||
devārisenāmathanaṃ pārvatyānandavardhanama ||
ehi deva kumārādya mahiṣāsurataskara || 31 ||
kārtikeya jagannātha mayūravaravāhana ||
budhamāvāhayiṣyāmi bodhanaṃ jagadīśvaram || 32 ||
cāndriṃ grahagaṇādhyakṣaṃ tejomūrtiṃ durāsadam ||
ehi śītāṃśujācintya jagajjiṣṇo janārdana || 33 ||
mahābala mahābhāga mahābāho mahādyute ||
viṣṇumāvāhayiṣyāmi śaṅkhacakragadādharam || 34 ||
atasīkusumaśyāmaṃ pītavāsasamacyutam ||
ehi me devadeveśa prajānirmāṇakāraka || 35 ||
nārāyaṇa suduṣpāra maheṣvāsa dhanurdhara ||
śakramāvāhayiṣyāmi devaṃ suragaṇeśvaram || 36 ||
vajrapāṇiṃ mahābāhuṃ gobrāhmaṇahite ratam ||
ehi deva sahasrākṣa devāribalasūdana || 37||
airāvataga dharmajña śacīhṛdayanandana ||
jīvamāvāhayiṣyāmi deveśvarapurohitam || 38 ||
bṛhaspatiṃ bṛhadvākyaṃ vedavedāṃgapāragam ||
ehi jīva mahābhāga jīvabhūta mahītale||39||
samyagvṛddhistavāyattā satataṃ bhūmivardhana ||
devīmāvāhayiṣyāmi pārvatīṃ varadāmumām ||3.104.40||
harasya dayitāṃ bhāryāṃ cārvaṅgīṃ bhūtivardhinīm ||
ehi devi jaganmātarmenāhṛdayanandini||41||
pavitre varade saumye nityaṃ bhaktajanapriye||
śukramāvāhayiṣyāmi bhārgavaṃ jagadīśvaram||42||
nityaṃ sarvadhanādhyakṣaṃ tapasā dyotitaprabhama ||
ehi śukra mahābhāga ṣoḍaśārce varaprada||43||
prabhustvaṃ varado nityaṃ varṣāvarṣasya nigrahe||
āvāhayāmyahaṃ devaṃ prajādhyakṣamakalmaṣam || 44 ||
bhaktānukaṃpinaṃ devaṃ prajānirmāṇakārakam ||
ehi deva prajādhyakṣa prajānirmāṇakāraka ||45||
tvayi deva samāyattaṃ prajānāmabhavodbhavam ||
saurimāvāhayiṣyāmi śanaiścāriṇamacyutam || 46 ||
tapasvinamanādhṛṣyaṃ bhaktānāmabhayapradam ||
ehi tīkṣṇāṃśujācintya bhāvābhāvapradarśaka ||47||
tvayi sarvaṃ mamāyattaṃ rājñāṃ bhūmau śubhāśubham ||
āvāhayāmyahaṃ devaṃ gajavaktraṃ gaṇeśvaram ||48||
vighneśaṃ vighnahartāraṃ pārvatī hṛdayapriyam ||
ehi deva gaṇādhyakṣa lambodara mahābhuja || 49 ||
kāryasiddhiḥ samāyattā tvayi sarveśa dehinām||
rāhumāvāhayiṣyāmi daityeṃdraṃ grahatāṃ gatam ||3.104.50||
keśavāptavaraṃ vīraṃ tapasā dagdhakilbiṣam ||
ehyehi daityapravara varadācintyavikrama || 51 ||
tamomūrte durādharṣaṃ viparītāmbarāmbara ||
āvāhayāmyahaṃ devaṃ viśvakarmāṇamacyutam || 52 ||
sarvāsāṃ devatānāṃ tu sadā śilpapravartakam ||
ehi devavarācintya sarvaśilpapravartaka || 53 ||
ehi devavarācintya sadā śilpapravartaka ||
tvayi sarveśa sarveṣāṃ karmaṇāmāśritā gatiḥ || 54 ||
ketumāvāhayiṣyāmi ketuṃ sarvadivaukasām ||
brāhmaṇyaṃ sarvadharmajñaṃ bhaktānāmabhayapradam || 55 ||
dhūmaketo ihābhyehi hutāśanasamaprabha||
śikhākarālavikaca pūjakārtivināśana||56||
āvāhayiṣyāmi śubhāṃ kṛttikāṃ devapūjitām ||
ehi sādhāraṇe devi jyeṣṭhe dakṣasute śubhe||57||
āvāhayāmi varadāṃ rohiṇīṃ candravallabhām ||
ehi rohiṇi dharmajñe dhruvakarmasu śobhane||58||
āvāhayāmi varadāmilvilāṃ śaśivallabhām ||
ehi me ilvile devi mṛdukarmasu śobhane||59||
rudramāvāhayiṣyāmi trinetraṃ śūlapāṇinam ||
ehi śaṅkara sarvātmanmahāgaṇagaṇeśvara ||3.104.60||
ārdrāmāvāhayiṣyāmi nakṣatraṃ bāhusaṃjñitam ||
ehyārdre cārusarvāṅgi dāruṇe rudrasammite ||61 ||
āvāhayiṣyāmi ditiṃ tapasā dagdhakilbiṣām ||
ādityāraṇi dharmajñe ehi devi mahāvrate || 62 ||
ṛkṣamāvāhayiṣyāmi dharmajñaṃ tu punarvasum ||
punarvasau ihāgaccha carakarmaprasādhaka || 63 ||
tiṣyamāvāhayiṣyāmi nakṣatraṃ kṣiprasaṃjñitama ||
ehi puṣya mahābhāga poṣaṃ vardhaya sarvataḥ || 64 ||
sarpānāvāhayiṣyāmi trailokyānantagocarān ||
āyāntu sarvataḥ sarpāḥ saumyarūpā bhavantu te || 65 ||
āvāhayāmi cāśleṣāṃ bhaktānāṃ śrīvivardhinīm ||
āśleṣe tvamihābhyehi dāruṇe vijayaprade || 66 ||
pitṝnāvāhayiṣyāmi martyamūrtidharānaham ||
āyāntu pitaraḥ śīghraṃ sudhākavyabhujo'vyayāḥ || 67 ||
maghāmāvāhayiṣyāmi ugranakṣatramañjasā ||
ehi me subhage devi sarvāghaviniṣūdani || 68 ||
bhagamāvāhayiṣyāmi bhaktasaubhāgyavardhanam||
ehi devavarācintya bhaktasaṃkaṭanāśaka || 69 ||
ṛkṣamāvāhayiṣyāmi pūrvaphalgunasaṃjñakam ||
ehi bhāgi mahābhāga ugrakarmaprasādhaka || 3.104.70 ||
āvāhayiṣyāmyaryamṇamādityaṃ tejasāṃ nidhim ||
aryamaṃstvaṃ samabhyehi bhaktasaṅkaṭanāśana || 71 ||
ṛkṣamāvāhayiṣyāmi uttarāphalgunīṃ śubhām ||
ehi tvaṃ subhage devi dhruve sarvāṅgasundari || 72 ||
hastamāvāhayiṣyāmi sāvitraṃ kṣipramañjasā ||
ehi sāvitra dharmajña bhaktānāṃ pāpanāśaka || 73 ||
āvāhayāmyahaṃ devaṃ tvaṣṭāramasitadyutim ||
ehi me bhagavaṃstvaṣṭaḥ prajāpālanatatpara || 74 ||
citrāmāvāhayiṣyāmi citramūrtiṃ manoharām ||
ehi me varade devi mṛdukarmaprasādhike || 75 ||
vāyumāvāhayiṣyāmi sarvagaṃ dīptatejasam ||
deva vāyo tvamabhyehi sarvabhūtajagatpriya || 76 ||
svātimāvāhayiṣyāmi nityamuttarasarvagām ||
devi svāti tvamabhyehi carakarmasu śobhane || 77 ||
āvāhayiṣyāmīndrāgnī sahitau dīptatejasau ||
iṃdrātrehi jagannātha ehyagne vijayaprada || 78 ||
ṛkṣamāvāhayiṣyāmi viśākhāmagratejasam ||
viśākhe tvamihābhyehi devi sādhāraṇe śubhe || 79 ||
mitramāvāhayiṣyāmi devaṃ dīptāṃśutejasam ||
ehi mitra mahābhāga bhaktānāmabhayaṃ kuru || 3.104.80 ||
ṛkṣamāvāhayiṣyāmi tvanurādhāṃ varapradām ||
anurādhe tvamabhyehi mṛdukarmasu śobhane || 81 ||
śakrosyāvāhanaṃ proktaṃ jyeṣṭhāyāḥ śṛṇu pārthiva ||
jyeṣṭhāmāvāhayiṣyāmi nakṣatraṃ śakradaivatam || 82 ||
jyeṣṭhe devi tvamabhyehi dāruṇe cārulocane ||
devamāvāhayiṣyāmi nirṛtiṃ vīravanditam || 83 ||
ehi deva virūpākṣa mahābala sadāśraya ||
mūlamāvāhayiṣyāmi nakṣatraṃ dāruṇaṃ mahat || 84 ||
ehi mūla mahābhāga bhaktānāmabhayaprada ||
āpastvāvāhayiṣyāmi sarvagā varadāḥ śubhāḥ ||85||
āpastvāvāhayiṣyāmi pūrvāṣāḍheti saṃjñitam ||
ehi tvamugre varade pūrvāṣāḍhetisaṃjñite || 86 ||
ṛkṣamāvāhayiṣyāmi yattadabhijiducyate ||
ehi dhiṣṇyavariṣṭhāya kṣiprakarmaprasādhaka || 87 ||
ṛkṣamāvāhayiṣyāmi śravaṇaṃ sarvakāmadam ||
prasannastvaṃ samabhyehi carakarmaprasādhaka || 88 ||
dhaniṣṭhe tvamihābhyehi carakarmaprasādhike || 89 ||
ṛkṣamāvāhayiṣyāmi nāmnā śatabhiṣāṃ śubhām ||
āgaccha tvaṃ śatabhiṣe carakarmasu śobhane || 3.104.90 ||
ajaikapādaṃ varadaṃ rudramāvāhayāmyaham ||
āgaccha rudra varada triśūlaparamāyudha || 91 ||
ṛkṣamāvāhayiṣyāmi pūrvabhadrapadāmaham ||
ehi bhadrapade pūrve ugrakarmaprasādhani || 92 ||
āhirbudhnyaṃ mahābhāgaṃ rudramāvāhayāmyaham ||
āhirbudhnya samabhyehi jaṭājūṭopaśobhita ||93||
dhruvamāvāhayiṣyāmi deśe bhadrapadottarām ||
ehi tvaṃ sumahābhāge mama bhadrapadottare || 94 ||
āvāhayāmyahaṃ devaṃ pūṣaṇaṃ pāpanāśakam ||
pūṣansamyagihābhyehi sarvakalmaṣanāśaka || 95 ||
ṛkṣamāvāhayiṣyāmi revatīṃ bhīrudarśanām ||
ehi revati dharmajñe mṛdukarmaprasādhake || 96 ||
ṛkṣamāvāhayiṣyāmi kṣipramaśvini saṃjñakam ||
ehyaśvini mahābhāge varade kāmadāyini || 97 ||
yamamāvāhayiṣyāmi dharmarājamathācyutam ||
ehi dharmabhṛtāṃ śreṣṭha vaivasvata mahābhuja || 98 ||
ṛkṣamāvāhayiṣyāmi ugraṃ bharaṇisaṃjñakam ||
ehi tvaṃ devi bharaṇi subhage cārudarśane ||99||
prācīmāvāhayiṣyāmi sūryodayavibhūṣaṇāma ||
ehi tvaṃ varade pūrve śubhakarmaprasādhani || 3.104.100 ||
diśamāvāhayiṣyāmi varadāṃ pūrvadakṣiṇām ||
vahnipriye samabhyehi varade pūrvadakṣiṇe || 101 ||
ahamāvāhayiṣyāmi dakṣiṇāṃ dakṣiṇāṃ diśam ||
dakṣiṇe tvaṃ samabhyehi sarvakarmaśubhaprade || 102 ||
diśamāvāhayiṣyāmi śubhāṃ dakṣiṇapaścimām ||
ehi tvaṃ nairṛte devi satataṃ bhūtivardhini || 103 ||
diśamāvāhayiṣyāmi vāruṇīṃ varadāṃ sadā ||
āgaccha paścime devi varade varuṇapriye ||104||
āvāhayiṣyāmi diśaṃ vāyavīṃ paścimottarām ||
varade tvaṃ samabhyehi vāyavye paścimottare || 105 ||
āvāhayāmi dhanadaṃ devaṃ vaiśravaṇaṃ prabhum ||
rājarāja samabhyehi sarvayajñeśvarādhipa || 106 ||
diśamāvāhayiṣyāmi saumyāṃ dhanadapālitām ||
uttare tvaṃ samabhyehi sarvadharmaphalaprade || 107 ||
diśamāvāhayiṣyāmi śubhāṃ prāguttarāmaham ||
ehi tvaṃ subhage devi satataṃ śivapālite || 108 ||
sarvānāvāhayiṣyāmi pūrṇakumbheṣu sāgarān ||
ratnādhyakṣāḥ samāyāntu catvāra iha sāgarāḥ || 109 ||
aiśānyāṃ diśi yaḥ kumbhaḥ pūrvastasyāṃ tu sāgaraḥ ||
āyātu śīghrameveha taraṅgormisamākula || 3.104.110 ||
śīghramabhyehi nilaya yādasāṃ pūrvasāgara ||
taraṅgaśatasaṃkrāṃta bālabhāskaramaṇḍala || 111 ||
āgneye ca samāyāta dakṣiṇe dakṣiṇaḥ śivaḥ ||
bahuratnaprabhāpuñjavimiśra salilāśaya || 112 ||
yādasāṃ nilayābhyehi kṣipraṃ dakṣiṇasāgara ||
taraṅgadhauta pādāgra trikūṭaśikharācyuta || 113 ||
paścimāyāḥ samāyātu kumbhe dakṣiṇapaścime ||
ratnapuñjamahājvālāvirājitamahodaka || 114 ||
taraṅgamaṇḍitābhyehi kṣipraṃ paścimasāgara ||
astārkabimbasaṃkrāntagatamaṇḍaladurdṛśa || 115 ||
uttaraśca tathābhyetu kumbhe vai paścimottare ||
himādriśikharacchāyākṣīrodasadṛśaḥ prabhuḥ || 116 ||
mahāpavitrasalila śīghramuttarasāgara ||
ihābhyehi mahābhāga yajamānābhivṛddhaye || 117 ||
āvāhitānāṃ kartavyaṃ bhūmau kṛtvā tataḥ śiraḥ ||
praṇāmamabhivādaṃ ca tato mantramudīrayet || 118 ||
bhavatāṃ hi prasādena bhavatāṃ pratipūjane ||
prayatiṣye yathāśaktyā tanme'nujñātumarhatha || 119 ||
yuṣmākaṃ pūjanaṃ kartuṃ kena śakyaṃ yathāvidhi ||
yuṣmatpūjāvihīnena kena labhyaṃ mahatphalam ||3.104.120||
tataḥ kartuṃ yathāśaktyā bhavatāṃ pratipūjanam ||
varadāḥ prayatiṣyāmi tanme'nujñātumarhatha ||121 ||
svāṃsvāṃ diśamadhiṣṭhāya dikpālā dīptatejasaḥ ||
utsādayantu sakalānvighnānyānsamupasthitāna || 125 ||
vināyakā vighnakarāḥ samogrā yajñadviṣo ye piśitāśanāśca || 
siddhārthakairvajrasamānakalpairmayā nirastā vidiśaḥ prayāntu || 126 ||
iti śrīviṣṇudharmottare tṛtīyakhaṇḍe mārkaṇḍeyavajrasaṃvāde brahmādidevāhvāna varṇano nāma caturuttaraśatatamodhyāyaḥ ||104||
<3.105>
mārkaṇḍeya uvāca ||
lokānāvāhayiṣyāmi haṃsarūpānmanoharān||
āyāntu vāhanatvena ye sthitāḥ parameṣṭhinaḥ ||1||
vṛṣamāvāhayiṣyāmi catuṣpādaṃ kakudminam ||
vṛṣābhyehi mahābhāga dharmamūrte varaprada||2||
śūlamāvāhayiṣyāmi guṇādhāraṃ mahādyutim||
ehi śūla mahābhāga daityanāthagaṇāntaka || 3 ||
ahamāvāhayiṣyāmi devaṃ nandīśvaraṃ prabhum ||
abhyehi nandinbhagavan sarvalokanamaskṛta || 4 ||
āvāhayāmi varadaṃ vīrabhadraṃ mahābalam ||
abhyehi vīrabhadrāya mahāgaṇagaṇeśvara || 5 ||
jvaramāvāhayiṣyāmi tripādaṃ tribhujaṃ prabhum ||
jvarābhyehi mahābhāga satataṃ caraṇojjvala || 6 ||
devīmāvāhayiṣyāmi bhadrakālīṃ sukhapradām ||
āgaccha varade durge sarvalokanamaskṛte || 7 ||
āvāhayāmi paulomīṃ varadāṃ nityanandinīma ||
śakrapatni tvamabhyehi varade kāmarūpiṇi || 8 ||
nāgamāvāhayipyāmi tathairāvaṇasaṃjñitam ||
ihābhyehi mahābhāga śakranāga jayāvaha || 9 ||
vajramāvāhayiṣyāmi sarvabhedakamāśugam ||
śīghraṃ vajra tvamabhyehi sarvaduṣṭanibarhaṇa || 3.105.10||
hayānāvāhayiṣyāmi chandorū pānmanoharān ||
āgacchantu mamādyeha turagā vyomagocarāḥ || 11 ||
ahamāvāhayiṣyāmi daṇḍasaṃjñaṃ balotkaṭam ||
daṇḍa śīghramihābhyehi mahābalaparākrama ||12||
ahamāvāhayiṣyāmi piṅgalaṃ sumahābalam ||
lokasākṣistvamabhyehi piṅgalāya mahābala ||13 ||
dhvajamāvāhayiṣyāmi citraṃ sūryasamaprabham ||
dharma śīghraṃ tvamabhyehi sūryadhvajagatācyuta || 14 ||
devīmāvāhayiṣyāmi sūryabhāṃ kāmarūpiṇīm ||
devi tvaṃ tūrṇamāgaccha sarvalokanamaskṛte ||| || 15 ||
rājñīmāvāhayiṣyāmi sūryapatnīṃ surūpiṇīm||
āgaccha varade rājñi jagaddhāriṇi pūjite ||16||
ahamāvāhayiṣyāmi sūryapatnīṃ suvarcasām||
dhyāye devi tvamāgaccha kṛtākṛtavicāriṇi ||17||
ahamāvāhayiṣyāmi daśacandramaso hayān ||
sarve śīghramihāyāntu candrāśvāḥ kāmarūpiṇaḥ||18||
ahamāvāhayiṣyāmi kāntiṃ tridaśavallabhām ||
kānte śīghraṃ tvamabhyehi śaśāṅkadayite śubhe ||19||
śobhāmāvāhayiṣyāmi kāṅkṣitāṃ bhuvaneśvarīm ||
śobhe śīghraṃ tvamabhyehi sarvalokaikasundari|| 3.105.20 ||
dinamāvāhayiṣyāmi samastadhvāntanāśakam ||
dina śīghraṃ tvamabhyehi darśitākhilamaṇḍala || ||
rātrimāvāhayiṣyāmi sarvabhūtasukhāvahām ||
rātri devi tvamabhyehi sarvabhūtavaraprade ||22||
sandhyāmāvāhayiṣyāmi devīṃ brāhmaṇapūjitām ||
āgaccha varade sandhye sarvabhūtanamaskṛte || 23 ||
svāhāmāvāhayiṣyāmi vahnerbhāgaṃ vibhāvarīm ||
svāhebhyehi mahābhāge varade kāmarūpiṇi || 24||
vedānāvāhayiṣyāmi śubharūpadharānaham ||

āvāhayiṣye mahiṣaṃ mahābalaparākramam ||

mṛtyumāvāhayiṣyāmi devīṃ sarvaharāmaham ||
devi mṛtyo tvamabhyehi sarvage kāmarūpiṇi || 3.105.30 ||
āvāhayāmi varadaṃ citraguptaṃ sukhapradam ||
citragupta samabhyehi sarvatrasamadarśana || 31 ||
ahamāvāhayiṣyāmi virūpākṣaṃ mahābalam ||

gaṅgāmāvāhayiṣyāmi sarvakalmaṣanāśinīm ||

devīmāvāhayiṣyāmi pārvatīṃ haravallabhām ||
devi śīghraṃ tvamabhyehi mahāvibhavakārike || 44 ||
ahamāvāhayiṣyāmi jayantaṃ śakranandanam ||
jayanta śīghramabhyehi sarvāsuravimardana || 45 ||
āvāhayiṣye varadaṃ puṣkaraṃ kāmagaṃ śubham ||
puṣkara tvamihābhyehi sarvajñānaprakāśaka||46||
jyotsnāmāvāhaviṣyāmi trailokyasyaikasundari ||
āgaccha varade jyotsne sarvabhūtasukhaṅkari ||47||
ahamāvāhayiṣyāmi tvāmeva nalakūbaram ||
samabhyehi mahābhāga dhanadasyātmasambhava || 48||
āvāhayiṣyāmi tathā raviputraṃ yamāgrajam ||
sūryaputra samabhyehi bhaktakalyāṇakāraka ||49||
dharmamāvāhayiṣyāmi sarvalokasukhāvaham ||
dharmābhyehi mahābhāga jagatāṃ paripālaka ||3.105.50||
arthamāvāhayiṣyāmi sarvalokasukhāvaham ||
arthabhyehi mahābhāga sarvabādhāvināśaka||51||
kāmamāvāhayiṣyāmi phalaṃ sarvasya kāraṇa ||
kāma śīghraṃ tvama bhyehi sarvaprāṇihṛdi sthita||52||
rātrimāvāhayiṣyāmi devīṃ sarvajagatpriyām ||
abhyehi varade devi rati tvamaparājite||53 ||
nidrāmāvāhayiṣyāmi sarvalokasukhapradām ||

āvāhayiṣye varadāṃ mahādevīṃ sarasvatīm||

ahamāvāhayiṣyāmi tathā dhanvatariṃ prabhum ||
dhanvantare tvamabhyehi kṣīrodārṇavasambhava ||56||
ahamāvāhayiṣyāmi uccaiḥśravasamaśvapam ||
hayarāja samabhyehi kṣīrodārṇavasaṃbhava || 57||
yeṣāmāvāhanaṃ proktaṃ teṣāṃ mantramidaṃ paṭhet ||
aśvinau praṇataḥ staumi darbhapāṇiratandritaḥ || 58 ||
āvāhanānāṃ ca gaṇaṃ tathoktaṃ mahāśubhaṃ pāpaharaṃ pavitram ||
āvāhitāḥ sannihitāstvavaśyaṃ devā bhavantyuttamavaṃśajātāḥ || 59 ||
iti śrīviṣṇudharmottare tṛtīye khaṇḍe mārkaṇḍeyavajrasaṃvāde lokādyāvāhanavarṇano nāma pañcottaraśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ || 105||
<3.106>
mārkaṇḍeya uvāca ||
āvāhanānyato vacmi yānyahaṃ yadunandana ||
teṣāmante paṭhecchlokaṃ pratyekasya mahābhuja || 1 ||
yājakaṃ yajamānaṃ ca śreyasābhyetya yojaya ||
idamarghyamidaṃ pādyaṃ dhūpoyaṃ pratigṛhyatām || 2 ||
āvāhayāmyahaṃ devaṃ vāsudevaṃ jagatpatim ||
amṛtādhmātameghābhaṃ vidyudṛśavasāsasam || 3 ||
caturvaktraṃ mahābāhuṃ caturvedadharaṃ prabhum ||
caturāśramagoptāraṃ cāturvarṇyadharaṃ tathā || 4 ||
caturyugaṃ catuṣketuṃ caturmūrtidharaṃ param ||
caturaṃghritanuṃ nāthaṃ caturvidhavibhāvanama || 5 ||
jarāyujasya kartavyaṃ tathā cāṇḍodbhavasya ca ||
sarvagaṃ varadaṃ nityaṃ sūkṣmaṃ sthūlamajaṃ vibhum || 6 ||
ātmasthaṃ ca bahisthaṃ ca dūrasthaṃ cāntikaṃ ca tam ||
guṇādhāraṃ guṇairhīnaṃ guṇātītaṃ guṇottaram || 7 ||
saguṇaṃ nirguṇātmānaṃ cājeyaṃ jagataḥ parama ||

cāpakṛṣṭivinirmuktaśarāhatajagattraya ||

ākāśatulyasadvajravidāritasurāhita ||

(sarveṣāṃ prāṇināṃ karmma saṃsāraparipālaka ||24||)

(jayamāvāhayiṣyāmi dīptatejasamacyutam ||
vijayaṃ ca viśālaṃ ca pāṭalāmbaradhāriṇau || 138 ||
śaśāṃkāṃśupratīkāśau tathobhau khaḍgadhāriṇau ||)
pradyumnasya pratīhārau kāmadevau suvarcasau || 139 ||
jaya śīghraṃ tvamabhyehi paścimadvāravāmataḥ ||
gaṇaiḥ sānucaraiḥ sārdhaṃ bhava saṃnihitaḥ sadā || 3.106.140 ||
vijaya tvamihābhyehi dvāradakṣiṇato bhava ||
gaṇaiḥ sānucaraiḥ sārdhaṃ bhava sannihitaḥ sadā || 141 ||
ahamāvāhayiṣyāmi tathā modaṃ jagatpriyam ||
pramodaṃ ca mahāvīryaṃ śvetāmbaradharāvubhau || 142 ||
sindūrāruṇasaṅkāśau śaktihastau balotkaṭau ||
aniruddhapratīhārau mahābalaparākramau || 143 ||
āmoda śīghramabhyehi dvāradakṣiṇato bhava ||
gaṇaiḥ sānucaraiḥ sārdhaṃ bhava sannihitaḥ sadā || 144 ||
sarvānāvāhayiṣyāmi vāsudevagaṇānaham ||
vāsudevasamākārānvāsudevaparākramān || 145 ||
pradyumnasya samākārānpradyumnābhaparākramān ||
pradyumnasya gaṇāḥ śīghramāyāṃtu bhuvaneśvarāḥ || 146 ||
sarvakāmapradā vīrā sarveśāḥ sarvaśaktayaḥ ||
sarvānāvāhayiṣyāmi aniruddhagaṇānaham || 147 ||
aniruddhasamākārānaniruddhaparākramān ||
aniruddhagaṇāḥ śīghramāyāntu bhuvaneśvarāḥ || 148 ||
sarvakāmapradā vīrā sarveśāḥ sarvaśaktayaḥ ||
prādurbhāvagatasyeva viṣṇoramitatejasaḥ || 149 ||
noktamāvāhanaṃ yatra vāsudevoktamañjasā ||
kāryamāvāhanaṃ tatra sarvagasya mahātmanaḥ ||
atha vā viṣṇurūpoktametatsāraṃ taveritam ||3.106.150 ||
āvāhane ye kathitāstu mantrā jñeyāsta eveha vibodhane'pi ||
āvāhane tatra vibodhane tu budhaiḥ prayojyaṃ yaduvaṃśanātha|| 151 ||
iti śrīviṣṇudharmottare tṛtīyakhaṇḍe mārkaṇḍeyavajrasaṃvāde śeṣasarvadevāvāhanavarṇano nāma ṣaḍuttaraśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ || 106 ||
<3.107>
mārkaṇḍeya uvāca ||
prokto vibodhane vīra maṃtro hi na divaukasām ||
vāsudevasya vakṣyāmi tava rājannibodhanam || 1 ||
devaṃ vibodhayiṣyāmi vāsudevamajaṃ vibhum ||
anādimadhyaparyantaṃ mahābalaparākramam || 2 ||
anantaṃ sarvagaṃ nāthaṃ mahadbhyopi mahattaram ||
sūkṣmātisūkṣmaṃ dūrasthaṃ sannikarṣagataṃ tathā || 3 ||
nārāyaṇaṃ mṛdukaraṃ devaṃ śārṅgadhanurdharam ||
atasīkusumaśyāmaṃ pītavāsasamacyutam || 4 ||
vibuddhaḥ puṇḍarīkākṣaḥ śaraṇāgatavatsalaḥ ||
saṃsārasarasīmagnabhagnanāgakṛtakṛpa || 5 ||
cakraparyantacchinnāgra duḥsvapnagrāhadurdhara ||
saṃsevitamahāyoganidrāsammīlitekṣaṇa || 6 ||
śeṣāhibhogaparyantavistīrṇaśayanācyuta ||
tatphaṇāvaliratnāṃśuvitānakakṛtottara || 7 ||

bhaktānāṃ narakaśvabhranipātabhayanāśana || 9 ||
teṣāṃ svalokagamanadattahastāvalambana ||
saṃsārasāgarottāramahāpota janārdana || 3.107.10 ||
nāmagrahaṇamātreṇa ghanāvakṣayakāraka ||
svatimātrakṛtāśeṣa manorathamahāphala || 11 ||
trivikramakramākrāntabrahmāṇḍottaramaṇḍapa ||
bālendukalpadaṃṣṭrāgra līloddhṛtavasundhara || 12 ||
ete vibodhayanti tvāṃ devāḥ sendrapurogamāḥ ||
ṛṣayaśca mahābhāgāḥ trailokyāmaladarśinaḥ || 13 ||
vibodhayati deva tvāṃ brahmā śubhacaturmukhaḥ ||
yajurvedo gadābhistu gīrbhidevavarācyuta || 14 ||
tyaja vibudhagaṇārisūdanādya nidrāṃ bhava bhuvanapateghasaṃcayāpaho me ||
tvayi bhavati hi yasya nātha bhaktirugrā gatiratiśayanī tu tasya daivatebhyaḥ || 15 || iti śrīviṣṇudharmottare tṛ0 khaṇḍe mārkaṇḍeyavajrasaṃvāde vāsudevavibodhano nāma saptottaraśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ || 107 ||
<3.108>
|| vavra uvāca || ||
bhagavansarvadevānāṃ mūrtayaḥ parikīrtitāḥ ||
pañcabhūtayutā yasmāttasmātteṣāṃ bhṛgūttama || 1 ||
āvāhanavidhiḥprokto yattadyuktatamaṃ matam ||
āvāhitāḥ samāyānti yatra kāmāvasāyitām || 2 ||
āśritya mahimāṃ prāptiṃ laghimāṃ ca tathā surāḥ ||
viṣṇuḥ sarvagato devo mahadbhyopi mahattaraḥ || 3 ||
sūkṣmebhyaścātisūkṣmaśca sarvavyāpī jaganmayaḥ ||
nāsti kiñcijjagatyasminyatra nāsti janārdanaḥ || 4 ||

mārkaṇḍeya uvāca ||
sarvadevagaṇā yadvadvajra piṇḍitamūrtayaḥ ||

ādyantamadhyasaṃyuktā sarvasyaiva yadūttama||8||
prādurbhāvagato viṣṇuryathā devagaṇāstathā ||
āhitaḥ sannihito bhavatīti viniścayaḥ ||9 ||
devasya dvividhā mūrtiḥ parā caiva tathāparā ||
parāyāḥ pauruṣī mūrtiḥ pañcabhūtavirājitā || 3.108.10 ||
tayā samūrtirbhavati sarvagaḥ parameśvaraḥ ||
puruṣeṇa vinā loke na kiñcidiha vidyate || 11 || 
anādimadhyanidhanastvaprameyatanustathā ||
sarvendriyamanāḥ śaktiḥ sarvataḥ sa tathā nṛpa ||12||
prādurbhāvagatasyāpi tasya yā sarvaśaktitā ||
icchataḥ sā vinirdiṣṭā na tveṣānicchato nṛpa || 13 ||
puruṣatve hyanantasya yā rājansarvaśaktitā ||
nisargasiddhā sā jñeyā sadā sarvatra yādava || || 14 ||
āvāhanamathātrāpi kriyate yajjagatpateḥ ||
nityaṃ saṃnihitasyāpi sarvatra parameṣṭhinaḥ || 15 ||
kevalaṃ kāraṇaṃ tatra manasastuṣṭikāraṇam ||
āvāhito mayā devaḥ sthāne sannihito'pi ca || 16 ||
āvāhanaṃ svatṛptyarthaṃ puruṣasya yathā sthitam ||
tathāpyarcā mahābhāga svatuṣṭyarthaṃ prayojanam || 17 ||
nityatṛptasya kiṃ tasya kāryaṃ bhavati cārcayā || 18 ||
kevalaṃ bhaktituṣṭyarthaṃ sa bhaktebhyaḥ pratīcchati ||
yosāvarcayate devo yatra devorcyate budhaiḥ || 19 ||
abhyarcate ca yairbhāvaiḥ sarve tadviddhi tanmayam ||
tasyārcākaraṇaṃ viddhi kartṛprītiprayojanam || 3.108.20 ||
jñeyaṃ tathaiva cātmānaṃ tasya sarvagatasya tu||
anādikāraṇaṃ tena nityatṛptena yādava || 21 ||
anugrahārthaṃ bhaktānāṃ cātmanaḥ saṃpravartitam ||
aśarīraḥ śarīrastho yathā bhavati bhūpate || 22 ||
bhaktalakṣaṇabodhārthaṃ tathaivāvāhyate tvasau ||
anākāraṃ mahārāja lakṣyabandhastu duṣkaraḥ||23||
sākārairlakṣabandhastu kartuṃ vai śakyate sukham||
vidhivacca tathākāraḥ prādurbhāvagatasya hi||24||
mayokto rājaśārdūla purastādeva śāstrataḥ ||
bhāvo bhavatu yo yasminbhāvanāyāṃ bhavennaraḥ || 25 ||
tatraiva bhāvanānena kāryā cārcā ca yādava ||
ādau kṛtvā mahābhāga sākāre lakṣabandhanam || 26 ||
tataḥ samartho bhavati śūnye dhyāne narottama||
evamāvāhanaṃ tasya tathā pūjā ca yādava || 27 ||
prabuddhaiḥ kriyate yatnādātmānugrahakāraṇam ||
nityatṛpto na tṛptyarthamupahāraṃ prayacchati || 28 ||
pratīcchati mahārāja bhaktānugrahakārakaḥ ||
mokṣa prāptāvubhāvetau tvayā jñeyau mahābhuja || 29 ||
jñānamārgaḥ kriyāmārgaḥ kṛto yaśca nirāśiṣaḥ ||
vajrabandhāya bhavati sakāmena kriyā kṛtā||
niṣkāmena kṛtā saiva mokṣāyaiva vidhīyate||3.108.30 ||
āvāhitāḥ sannihitāstadeva bhavatyavaśyaṃ nṛpa mantrayuktāḥ||
svatuṣṭaye deva varasya viṣṇorāvāhana vajra budhaiḥ pradiṣṭam || 31 ||
iti śrīviṣṇudharmottare tṛtīyakhaṇḍe mārkaṇḍeyavajrasaṃvāde āvāhanaprayojanavarṇano nāmāṣṭottaraśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ || 108 ||

mahābhāgaḥ ||1||
bhāsaḥ ||
trisuparṇaḥ ||

<3.111>
mārkaṇḍeya uvāca ||
atha pratiṣṭhitasya bhagavato bṛhatsnapanamārabhet tato gāyatrīṃ paṭhet ||
iṣetvetyanena patadgrahaṃ dadyāt ||
vaiṣṇavagāyatryā pāduke |

gandhadvāreti gandhaiḥ |
oṃkāranamaskārapavitraiḥ śiraḥsnānauṣadhībhiḥ yathā kuṣṭham, rodhram, mustā, vacā, sarṣapāḥ, jayantī, viṣṇukrāntā, punarnavā, brāhmī, tagaram, atibalā, navabalā, nāgabalā, tvakpatram ,agurubālakam, hareṇukam, lavaṅgam, aguram, uśīram, nālikā cūrakam, dve haridre ceti ||
evaṃ snānasya kalaśadānaṃ bhavati ||
tatrādāvevoṅkāranamaskārapavitrābhirjñātvā daśādhyātmamṛtpiṇḍamādhuraiḥ sāttvato dadyāt ||
tataḥ sāmavinmahāvāmadevalakṣarathantaraśākvarabhāruṇḍabṛhatsāmabhiḥ ||
atharvavidatharvaśirāḥ pratyaṅgiraḥ śaṅkhamaṇiprāṇasūktamaṇipratisaramaṇibhiḥ || ṛgvedavit gāyatrīsāvitrīgosūktāśca ||
sūryasūktapuruṣasūktaśrīsūktairyajurvedavit || kṛṣṇājinacamasaṣaṭkaṣaḍṛtukūṣmāṇḍarudradraviṇaiḥ |
tataḥ svaśaktyā kalaśasahasrakalaśāṣṭaśata pañca vā śatāni catvāri vā śatamaṣṭādhikaṃ vā aṣṭāviṃśatirvāṣṭau vā svarcitānvanaspatikālāpagarbhaśaṅkhadhvanisvanena vādyaśabdena jayajayakāravaṃdiśabdena prasubhagānartitena deyāḥ ||
tataḥ pavitreṇopakalaśānviśrāmya vedyāṃ kṣipet ||
ghṛtavatīti ghṛtāvekṣaṃ darśayet ||
sahasraśīrṣeṇa śikhaṇḍodagrahaṇaśāṭakaṃ dadyāt ||
yuvāsuvāsa iti pratinirvasanaśāṭakam ||
idamāpaḥ pravahata ityācamanīyaṃ tato jānubhyāṃ pāṇibhyāṃ śirasā ca namaskāraṃ kuryāt ||
bṛhatsnapanametaddhi yaḥ karoti naro hareḥ||
sarvapāpavinirmukto viṣṇuloke mahīyate||
na kevalaṃ pratiṣṭhāyāṃ sarvadaivaṃ samācaret ||
śāntidaṃ pauṣṭikaṃ kāmyaṃ bṛhatsnapanamuttamama ||
divyāntarikṣabhaumānāmutpātānāṃ samudbhave ||
devadevasya rājendra bṛhatsnapanamācaret ||
nāsti loke samutpāto yo hyanena na śāmyati ||
ārogyakāmaḥ śrīkāmo mokṣakāmaśca bandhanāt ||
saubhāgyaśāntikāmaśca bṛhatsnapanamācaret ||
vidyārthī prāpnuyādvidyāṃ putrakāmastathātmajān ||
priyāśca mukhyāḥ prāpnoti yāśca kāmayate yadi ||
akāmakāmaḥ prāpnoti tadviṣṇoḥ paramaṃ padam ||
iti śrīviṣṇudharmottare tṛtīyakhaṇḍe mārkaṇḍeyavajrasaṃvāde bṛhatsnapanapraśaṃsāvarṇano nāmaikādaśottaraśatatamo 'dhyāyaḥ || 111 ||
