|| mārkaṇḍeya uvāca || ||
snātaṃ śrībhagavantaṃ samarcayet ||
candanabahulaniryāsajātīphalamṛgamadakarpūrāṇi samastāni vyaktāni vānulepanaṃ kuryāt || gandhadvāreti śrībhagavantamanulimpet| ||
vāyuragregā iti tālavṛntaṃ nivedayet ||
vāta āvātabheṣajamiti vījayet ||
sāvitrāṇi sāvitrasyeti cāmaraṃ nivedayet ||
śrīviṣṇugāyatryā bījāni nivedayet ||
yuvāsuvāsa iti mahārhairvastraiḥ śrībhagavantaṃ ācchādayet ||
nopabhuktairna nīlaiḥ hiraṇyavarṇairābharaṇairarcayet |
kaṅkato na kaṅkata iti kaṅkatena indrāviśveti kaṅkatena dūṣyādūśirasi pratisaṃkhyā añjana maṇinā praijanena ātmavyūhena kūrcaprasādanena puṣpavatīti puṣpaiḥ prathitairagrāsthitaiśca ||
prajāpate na tvadetānyanya iti paṭavāsena||
agnimūrdheti dīpena||
dhūrasi dhūrvamiti dhūpena ||
abhitvā śūra nonuma iti cchatreṇa||
viṣṇugāyatryā upānadbhyām ||
idaṃ viṣṇurvicakrame iti yānena tenaiva pravahaṇena |
ketuṃ kṛṇvanniti dhvajaiḥ tenaiva patākādibhiḥ |
tataḥ stotraiḥ śrībhagavantamarcayet ||
tato agnirmūrdheti sarvabhogapūraṇārthīyamātrayā ||
tatastantrīvādyaiḥ tataḥ śaṃkhapaṭahabherīninādaiḥ subhagātenaveti ||
anena vidhinā bhogaiḥ samabhyarcya janārdanam ||
sarvānkāmānavāpnoti viṣṇulokaṃ ca gacchati || 1 ||
iti śrīviṣṇudharmottare tṛtīyakhaṇḍe mārkaṇḍeyavajrasaṃvāde bhogadānavarṇano nāma dvādaśottaraśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ || 112 ||
<3.113>
mārkaṇḍeya uvāca ||
atha madhuparkeṇa śrībhagavantamabhyarcayet ||
hiraṇyavarṇetyarhaṇāṃ dadyāt |
dadhikrāvṇeti dadhi |
ghṛtavatīti ghṛtam ||
madhuvātāṛtāyate iti madhu ||
trisuvarṇena saṃyojya sāvitreṇa niyojayeta ||
āpohiṣṭheti tarpaṇaṃ dadyāt||
vāta āvātu bheṣajamiti niṣpuṃsanam||
śanna āpa iti ācamanīyam||
aṣṭakavidhinā paśum hiraṇyavarṇeti vā mātrām |
tena bimbaśaṃkhapadmādyāni maṅgalyāni nivedayet||
madhuparkavidhistveṣa kathitaste mayānagha ||
etatkṛtvā mahārāja viṣṇuloke mahīyate ||
iti śrīviṣṇudharmottare tṛtīyakhaṇḍe mārkaṇḍeyavajrasaṃvāde madhuparkanivedano nāma trayodaśottaraśatatamo 'dhyāyaḥ || 113 ||
<3.114>
mārkaṇḍeya uvāca ||
hiraṇyavarṇetyarhaṇāṃ dadyāt ||
tataḥ sabhakṣyaṃ savyañjanaṃ sahacaritakaṃ sāvadaṃśaṃ saphalaṃ sāvitreṇa nivedayet||
śaktyā sugandhīni susvādūni ambupānāni ca āpohiṣṭheti ca tarpaṇena, vāta āvātu bheṣajamiti niṣpuṃsanena, śanna āpa ityācamanīyena, agnirmūrdheti punaranenaiva vidhānena, saṃvibhāgārthīyena anuyajñena śrībhagavantamarcayet ||
mukhādindraśca tāṃbūlaṃ dadyāt ||
tenaiva sugandhīni, mukhavāsāṃsi, tenaiva darpaṇam ||
punarapi śrībhagavantaṃ gītavāditranṛttaiḥ samabhyarcayet ||
annayajñena saṃpūjya devadevaṃ sanātanam ||
sarvānkāmānavāpnoti viṣṇulokaṃ ca gacchati ||
iti śrīviṣṇudharmottare tṛ0 khaṇḍe mārkaṇḍeyavajrasaṃvāde ijyāvarṇano nāma caturdaśottaraśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ || 114||
<3.115>
mārkaṇḍeya uvāca ||
tataḥ śaṅkaragītoktena vidhānenejyāṃ sāttvataḥ kuryāt ||
kāriṇaśca bhogaiḥ śaciṃ bhajet ||
devapurata evartvijāṃ pratyekaṃ suvarṇa mahatvaṃ vāso govatsakṛtaṃ kāsyaṃ dadyāta || svaśaktyā ca tānabhyarcayet ||
tato brāhmaṇānbhojayet ||
bhuñjānāṃśca śrībhagavanmāhātmyaṃ śrāvayet ||
tato haviṣyaṃ yajamānastvaśnīyāt ||
tato devāgāre gītanṛtyavādyapustakaśravaṇe tiṣṭhet ||
anena vidhinā kṛtvā pratiṣṭhāṃ śārṅgiṇo naraḥ ||
sarvānkāmānavāpnoti viṣṇulokaṃ ca gacchati ||
iti śrīviṣṇudharmottare tṛ0 kha0 mārkaṇḍeyavajrasaṃvāde sāttvatejyāvarṇano nāma pañcadaśottaraśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ || || 115 ||
<3.116>
mārkaṇḍeya uvāca ||
vaidikenejyāvidhānena sāttvatoktena darśanavidhihomena saptāhaṃ pratyahamarcāṃ kuryāt | tataḥ saptamehni bṛhatsnapanaṃ kṛtvā kalpakaḥ kralaśapāṇiḥ yā oṣadhaya iti toraṇānyabhiṣiṃcet ||
gandhadvāreṇetyanulepanenārcayet |
puṣpavatīti puṣpaiḥ ||
agnirmūrdheti dīpena |
dhūrasīti dhūpena|
sāvitrāṇi sāvitrasyeti naivedyena ||
tataḥ prāguttaraṃ tataḥ pūrvaṃ tato dakṣiṇataḥ paścime śrīviṣṇugāyatryā toraṇānyuddharet || sarva eṣa ṛtvijatoraṇānyādāya gajapṛṣṭhagā aśvarathapṛṣṭhagā gorathapṛṣṭhagāḥ śibikāgatā vā nadīṃ gaccheyuḥ |
tatra ca svasti na indra iti mantreṇākṣipeyuḥ ||
bhadraṅkarṇeti śrīdevatāgṛhaṃ vrajeyuḥ yaśceha bhagavāniti praviśeyuḥ ||
tato bhogavidhipūrvakeṇa sarveṇa pratiṣṭhoktena vidhinā śrībhagavantamanvayeyuḥ |
tato bhuktavatsu vipreṣu muñcāmi tveti mantreṇa yajamānasya dīkṣākaraṇaṃ baddhaṃ muñcet ||
kavacena ca rakṣaṇaṃ kuryāt ||
toraṇoddharaṇavarjaṃ māsimāsi saptāhoktaṃ vidhiṃ kuryāta ||
yāvatsaṃvatsaraṃ nityamevaṃ sarvakāmadaṃ karma tat ||
pratiṣṭhākalpametatte mayoktaṃ pārthivottama ||
sarvapāpaharaṃ jñeyamāyuṣyamaparājitam ||
anena vidhinā kṛtvā sthāpanaṃ śārṅgiṇo naraḥ||
sarvakāmānavāpnoti viṣṇulokaṃ ca gacchati ||
oṣadhyaḥ pavanaṃ vṛkṣā yatkiṃcidupayujyate ||
pratiṣṭhāyāṃ hi tatsarvaṃ viṣṇuloke mahīyate ||
pratiṣṭhāṃ ye'pi paśyanti kriyamāṇāṃ narādhipa ||
abhinandanti ye bhaktāstepi svargārjino narāḥ ||
sāttvatoktena vidhinā nityameva pratiṣṭhitam ||
pūjayeddevadeveśaṃ ya icchecchāśvataṃ padam || 1 ||
iti śrīviṣṇudharmottare tṛtīyakhaṇḍe mārkaṇḍeyavajrasaṃvāde toraṇoddhāravidhivarṇano nāma ṣoḍaśottaraśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ || 116 ||
<3.117>
mārkaṇḍeya uvāca ||
atha devayātrāvidhirbhavati |
yasya devasya yā tithiḥ svakīyā uktā tasya tasyāṃ tithau yātrāṃ kuryāt ||
yasya tithirna jñāyate tasya paurṇamāsyāṃ kuryāt ||
śrībhagavato vāsudevasya sarvā eva tithayaḥ nāsya tithirnāma tatrādāveva sudhāvadātaṃ sucitritaṃ devakulaṃ kṛtvā śubhehni vināyaka pūjanaṃ kuryāt ||
dvitīyehni graharkṣapūjanaṃ, tṛtīyehni nāgapūjanam,
caturthehni pramathapūjanam,
pañcamehni brāhmaṇapūjanam, dīnānāthadānaṃ ca ṣaṣṭhehni snapanam ||
tatra bṛhatsnapanaṃ kṛtvā sarve eva nāgarajanāḥ suśuklavāsasaḥ pavitrakṛtapāṇayaḥ mahatā vādyaghoṣeṇa subhagānartitena saraḥsaritprasravaṇāgāraṃ nagarasaṃnikarṣamāsannatamaṃ gaccheyuḥ | tasmādudakakalaśānādāya gajapṛṣṭhagatān vā vāsasā śubhenācchāditānpraveśayet |
praveśya śrībhagavantaṃ snāpayeta, bhogavidhipūrvakena pratiṣṭhoktena vidhinā pūjayet, nṛtyavādyagītaiśca śrībhagavantamarcayet, tato yātrādine prāpte svalpārcāṃ pratimākhyām | kūṭāgāre manohare nānāprakāravasanācchādite sakiṅkiṇīke ratnamālyapatākopaśobhite rathe dṛḍhīkṛtvā aśvairdā ntairvyāyatairvā puruṣairnagaraṃ bhrāmayet |
nāgareṇa ca janena suveśena cāpamātrapāṇinā kūṭāgārasya purastādbhramaṇaṃ kartavyam || anyaiśca kusumamālāvikiraṇaṃ kartavyam |
stutīśca kuryuḥ |
vandino maṅgalapāṭhakāśca purato bhaveyuḥ |
vāditrāṇi vādayaṃścaturaṅgabalānuyāto rājā cānugamanaṃ kuryāt ||
nagarādhikṛto vā tadadhikṛto vā gajapṛṣṭhagataḥ sāṃvatsareṇānugamanaṃ kuryāt || nimittāni ca paśyet |
vināvātaṃ dhvajabhaṅge rājñāṃ maraṇamādiśet |
chatrabhaṅge janapadavināśaḥ |
īṣadbhaṅge rājapatnīmaraṇam ||
rajjucchede bālapīḍā ||
aśvadāntanaravipattau janapada pīḍā, mātṛkācchede rājamātuḥ pīḍā ||
arcābhaṅgapatane lokanāśaḥ, samagrakūṭāgārasya patane sarājakasya rāṣṭrasya vināśaḥ, visvareṣu vāditreṣu paracakrāgamaḥ, dhvajastho gavyādeḥ samarabhayam |
apṛṣṭajanapade durbhikṣabhayam ||
śubhabhe śubhaṃ bālakā yatra kuryuḥ tat tathāvidhameva bhavati |
yasya ca puruṣasya tasminnahani yādṛśe sukhaduḥkha bhavatāma ||
tādṛśepi saṃvatsare tasya jñeye |
tasmāttatrāhni svāśitena suvāsasā prakṛṣṭena nāgareṇa bhavitavyama ||
evaṃ nagare saṃbhrāmya cihnāgasaṃ jñātvā praviṣṭāyāṃ pratimārcāyāṃ mahadutsavaṃ kuryāt | dvitīyadivasādārabhya yāvatsāmarthyaṃ pratyahaṃ naṭanartakanartakīmallendrajālikādīnāṃ prekṣāpūrvakaṃ dhanaṃ dadyāt ||
prekṣākāle ca prekṣakaṃ janaṃ mālyatāmbūlānulepanā didānenārcayet |
adṛśyānāṃ prekṣakāṇāṃ bhūtānāṃ raṅgādidigvidikṣu sodakamannādyaṃ phalāni puṣpāṇi palalollopikāmodakāni vikiret ||
prekṣakāṇāṃ yathāgatānāṃ ca śreyaścā'stvityudīrayet |
yathāgatāśca prekṣakā āgāminīṃ yātrāṃ paśyaṃtvityudīrayeta ||
aśubhanimittotpattau tadupaśamanāyānena vidhinā punaryātrāṃ samyakkuryāt ||
anena vidhinā yastu yātrāṃ kuryātsurālaye ||
sa sarvakāmānāpnoti viṣṇulokaṃ ca gacchati ||
dhanyaṃ yaśasyamāyuṣyaṃ rājñāṃ ca vijayapradam ||
nāgarāṇāṃ hitakaraṃ yātrākarma prakīrtitam ||
tuṣṭyarthaṃ triśendrāṇāṃ hitārthaṃ nagarasya ca||
janasya rājankartavyaṃ yātrākarma jayāvaham||
ativṛṣṭikaraṃ proktaṃ tvanāvṛṣṭivināśanam ||
yātrākarma tvidaṃ kāryaṃ devatāyatanaṃ sadā||
pratisaṃvatsaraṃ rājanrājā vijayamicchatā ||
iti śrīviṣṇudharmottare tṛtīyakhaṇḍe mārkaṇḍeyavajrasaṃvāde yātrāvidhivarṇano nāma saptadaśottaraśata tamodhyāyaḥ | 117 ||
<3.118>
vajra uvāca ||
kāṃkāṃ vipṇostanuṃ bhaktaḥ kena kāmena pūjayeta ||
etanme saṃśayaṃ chindhi tvaṃ hi sarvaṃ vijānase || 1 || ||
mārkaṇḍeya uvāca ||
sarvakāmapradaṃ devaṃ caturmūrtiṃ tu pūjayet ||
pūjayedaniruddhantu dharmakāmo naraḥ sadā||2||
tathā saṅkarṣaṇaṃ devamarthakāmastu pūjayeta||
kāmakāmo'pi rājendra pradyumnaṃ pūjayedvibhum||3||
pūjayenmokṣakāmastu vāsudevaṃ jagatprabhum ||
putrakāmastathā rājanpadmanābhaṃ ca pūjayet||4||
tathāśvaśirasaṃ devaṃ vidyākāmastu pūjayet||
bhogānāmāptakāmastu pūjayedbhogaśāyinama||5||
bhogibhogāsanāsīnaṃ sthānakāmastu pūjayet ||
matsyaṃ saṃpūjayeddevaṃ dhānyakāmo naraḥ sadā || 6 ||
tathaivārogyakāmastu kūrmadevaṃ ca pūjayet ||
jña kāmastathā haṃsaṃ nṛsiṃhamatha vā vibhum||7||
vidyākāmo'tha vālmīkiṃ vyāsaṃ vāpyatha pūjayet||
sāṃkhyajñānaparijñānahetave kapilaṃ tathā||8||
bhūtikāmo varāhaṃ tu nṛvarāhamathārcayet||
vyavahāre raṇe dyūte jayakāmastathā pṛthak || 9 ||
dharmakāmastathā dharmaṃ brahmāṇaṃ cāpi pūjayeta ||
śatrūṇāṃ nāśakāmastu mahādevaṃ tu pūjayet || 3.118.10 ||
pūjayedbhārgavaṃ rāmaṃ pratijñāpāraṇecchayā ||
atha vā rājaśārdūlaṃ rāmaṃ daśarathātmajam || 11 ||
śrīkāmastu mahārāja śrīsahāyaṃ ca pūjayet ||
balakāmastathā devaṃ balabhadraṃ prapūjayet || 12 ||
tameva rājaśārdūlaṃ kṛṣikarmaprasiddhaye ||
sarvakāmapradaṃ devaṃ yatheṣṭīṃ tanumāsthitam || 13 || pūjayetsarvaśaktitvātsarvakāmaphalapradān ||
nirāśiṣaḥ pūjayito vareṇyaṃ deveśvaraṃ vāsudevaṃ purāṇam ||
yatheṣṭadehopagataṃ nṛvāraṃ sthānaṃ taduktaṃ paramaṃ hi yatsyāt || 14 ||
iti śrīviṣṇudharmottare tṛtīyakhaṇḍe mārkaṇḍeyavajrasaṃvāde tattatkāmapūjanavarṇano nāmāṣṭādaśottaraśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ || 118 ||
<3.119>
vajra uvāca||
kasminkarmaṇi saṃprāpte kāṃ kāṃ saṃpūjayettanum ||
kāryasiddhimabhiprepsustanme vada mahāmune || 1 ||
mārkaṇḍeya uvāca ||
samudrapotayātrāyāṃ matsyaṃ saṃpūjayedvibhum ||
varāhaṃ vā mahābhāga nadyuttāre tathaiva ca || 2 ||
saṃpūjayedaśvaśiro vidyārambhe tu mānavaḥ ||
trivikramaṃ tathā devaṃ yātrākāle tu pūjayet || 3 ||
vivāhakāle saṃprāpte pradyumnaṃ pūjayennṛpa ||
saṃskāreṣu ca sarveṣu dattātreyaṃ ca pūjayet || 4 ||
kāvyārambhe tu kartavyaṃ vālmīkiṃ vyāsameva ca ||
abhiṣeke tathā rājā mṛtyuṃ vainyaṃ ca pūjayeta || 5 ||
avaśyaṃ vyavahāre ca saṃgrāme dyūta eva vā ||
varāhaṃ pūjayeddevaṃ prārambhe kṛṣikarmaṇi || 6 ||
atha vāpi mahārāja devaṃ saṅkarṣaṇaṃ vibhum ||
nṛsiṃhaṃ pūjayedeva karmaṇyarivināśane || 7 ||
pratijñāpāraṇe rāmaṃ bhārgavaṃ vātha rāghavama ||
auṣadhasya samārambhe karma strīrūpameva vā || 8 ||
tīrthayātrāsamārambhe haṃsaṃ saṃpūjayedvibhum ||
vāṇijyasya samārambhe tathaiva garuḍadhvajam || 9 ||
śilpa karmasamārambhe viśvarūpaṃ samarcayet ||
tapasaśca samārambhe naranārāyaṇāvubhau || 3.119.10 ||
gṛhapraveśe ca tathā devaṃ bhogiśayaṃ vibhum ||
dharmaṃ saṃpūjayeddevaṃ dharmakāryapravartane || 11 ||
varāhaṃ pūjayecchrāddhe dānavānyajñakarmaṇi ||
vyālakuñjaradurgeṣu ripucorabhayeṣu ca || 12 ||
pūjayennarasiṃhaṃ tu sarvabādhā vināśanam ||
yatheṣṭarūpaṃ dharmajña sarvakāryeṣu cārcayet || 13 ||
yenayeneha kāmena yāṃyāṃ saṃpūjayettanuma ||
sāsā tasya dhruvaṃ dadyāttaṃtaṃ kāmamasaṃśayam ||14||
saṃpūjito devavarassakāmairyathepsitaṃ vajra dadāti kāmam ||
sarvāsvathārcāsu yatheṣṭasaṃsthāstvakāmakāmairvidadhāti mokṣam || 15 ||
iti śrīviṣṇudharmottare tṛtīyakhaṇḍe mārkaṇḍeyavajrasaṃvāde kāryārambhaprādurbhāvapūjanaṃ nāmaikonaviṃśaśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ || 119 ||
<3.120>
vajra uvāca || ||
kasminkāle tu devasya prādurbhāvaṃ kamarcayet ||
etanme saṃśayaṃ chindhi bhṛguvaṃśavivardhana || 1 ||
|| mārkaṇḍeya uvāca || ||
nṛhayaṃ nṛvarāhaṃ ca narasiṃhaṃ ca vāmanam ||
trivikramaṃ tu dharmajña pañcasvabdeṣu pūjayet || 2 ||
vāsudevaṃ haladharaṃ pūjayedayanadvaye ||
pradyumnamaniruddhaṃ ca tathā bhogaśayaṃ prabhum || 3 ||
padmanābhaṃ dāśarathiṃ rāmaṃ bhārgavameva ca || 4 ||
vasantādiṣu dharmajña pūjayedṛtuṣu kramāt ||
gajendramokṣaṇaṃ caivaṃ pṛthuṃ vālmīkimeva ca || 5 ||
vyāsaṃ ca kṛṣṇaṃ ca naraṃ naranārāyaṇau tathā ||
rāmaṃ rāmaṃ tathā rāmaṃ hariṃ caitrādiṣu kramāt || 6 ||
vahniṃ prajāpatiṃ somaṃ rudraṃ strīrūpiṇaṃ tathā||
vyāsaṃ śeṣaṃ varāhaṃ ca pradyumnaṃ cārjunaṃ nṛpa || 7 ||
ādityaṃ viśvakarmāṇaṃ cakraṃ devaṃ nṛkesarim ||
garuḍaṃ puruhūtaṃ ca kapilaṃ padmameva ca || 8 ||
viśvarūpaṃ ca brahmāṇa tathā devaṃ trivikrama ||
gavāṃ kūrmaṃ naraṃ caiva tathā nārāyaṇaṃ harim ||
rāmaṃ kṛṣṇaṃ ca varadaṃ kṛttikādiṣu pūjayet ||
nṛhayaṃ nṛvarāhaṃ ca tathā devaṃ trivikramama || 3.120.10 ||
kapilaṃ bhogiśayanaṃ varāhaṃ matsyameva ca ||
rāmaṃ rāmaṃ ca dharmajña kūrmaṃ haṃsaṃ ca yādava || 11 ||
pradyumnaṃ baladevaṃ ca varāhaṃ ca tathā kramāt ||
tithiṣu prathamādyāsu krameṇaiva samarcayeta || 12 ||
yatheṣṭamūrtigaṃ devaṃ sarvakālamathārcayeta ||
tasya sarvātithiryasmānmahāśakterjagadguroḥ || 13 | |
sa sarvaśaktirbhagavānmahātmā pūjyaḥ sadā bhaktimupetya rājan ||
saṃpūjanaṃ devavarasya tasya bhakti pradhānā na hi kālayogaḥ || 14 | |
iti viṣṇudharmottare tṛtīyakhaṇḍe mārkaṇḍeyavajra saṃvāde kālaniyamapūjāvarṇano nāma viṃśatyuttaraśatatamo 'dhyāya || 120 ||

<3.121>
vajra uvāca ||
kasmindeśe mahābhāga prādurbhāvaṃ kamarcayeta ||
etanme saṃśayaṃ chindhi tvaṃ hi sarvaṃ vijānase || 1 ||
mārkaṇḍeya uvāca ||
brahmāṇaṃ puṣkare devaṃ prayāge yogaśāyinama | |
kālaṃjare mahādevaṃ śakratīrthe śatakratuma || 2 ||
sindhukūle varāhaṃ ca śāligrāme trivikramam | |
kāśmīreṣu tathā matsyaṃ sāgare vaḍavānalam | | 3 | |
ayodhyāyāṃ tathā rāmaṃ naimiṣe dharmameva ca ||
karṇāṭe cāśvaśirasaṃ madradeśe nṛkesarim || 4 ||
śatrughnaṃ mathurāyāṃ ca daṇḍakeṣu ca lakṣmaṇam ||
bharataṃ kekayeṣveva yamunāyāṃ tathā balim || 5 ||
prāgjyotiṣe'niruddhaṃ ca pradyumnaṃ dakṣiṇāpathe ||
tathā bhārgavarāmaṃ ca malaye yadunandanam || 6 ||
pāñcālaviṣaye rājanpṛthuṃ vainyaṃ ca pūjayeta ||
tamasātīramāsādya vālmīkiṃ pūjayennaraḥ || 7 ||
vārāṇasyāṃ pare pāre vyāsaṃ yadukulodvaha ||
gaṅgākūle mahārāja vāsudevaṃ ca pūjayet || 8 ||
gajendramokṣaṇaṃ devamaṅgeṣu yadunandana ||
dvāravatyāṃ tathā kṛṣṇaṃ naranārāyaṇāvubhau || 9 ||
badaryāśramamāsādya pūjayettu vicakṣaṇaḥ ||
kurukṣetrepi dharmajña rāmaṃ bhṛgukulodvahama || 3.121.10 ||
atha vā yādavaśreṣṭha rathasthau kṛṣṇaphālgunau ||
haṃsaprapatane haṃsaṃ matsyaṃ matsyeṣu yādava || 11 ||
gayāśīrṣe varāhaṃ tu mūlasthāne divākaram ||
prabhāse ca tathā somaṃ himavatyalakādhipama || 12 ||
airāvatyāṃ bhogamayaṃ sarayvāṃ rāmameva ca ||
bhogibhogāsanāsīnaṃ sarayūtaṭasaṃsthitama || 13 ||
sarvamūrtidharaṃ devaṃ sarvatrāpyatha vārcayeta ||
sarvatra sarva śaktirhi sarvātmā sa nigadyata || 14 ||
sarvatra devaḥ sa tu sarvaśaktiḥ sarveśvaraḥ sarvaśarīrasaṃsthaḥ ||
pūjyaḥ sadā sarvaphalapradātā loke sakāmānvidadhāti rājan || 15 ||
iti śrīvipṇudharmauttare tṛ0 khaṇḍe mārkaṇḍeyavajrasaṃvāde śrīviṣṇudeśādhideśaprādurbhāvapūjāvarṇano nāmaikaviṃśatyuttaśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ | 121 ||
<3.122>
vajra uvāca ||
kena kāryeṇa devasya kiṃkiṃ nāma prakīrtayet ||
etanme saṃśayaṃ chindhi bhṛguvaṃśavivardhana || 1 ||
mārkaṇḍeya uvāca ||
vāsudevācyutānantasatyājapuruṣottama ||
paramātmeśvarādyaiśca tato mokṣaṃ prayacchati || 2 ||
dharmaviddharmakṛddharmī viśvātmā viśvakṛcchuciḥ ||
śucimadviṣṇurabjākhyo puṣkarākhyo hyadhokṣajaḥ || 3 ||
śuciḥśravā śipiviṣṭo yajñeśo yajñabhāvanaḥ ||
nāmnāmityevamādīnāṃ samuccāraṇato naraḥ || 4 ||
dharmaṃ mahāntamāpnoti pāpabandhakṣayaṃ tathā ||
śrīdaḥ śrīśaḥ śrīnivāsaḥ śrīdharaḥ śrīniketanaḥ || 5 ||
śriyaḥ patiḥ śrīparamaḥ śrīmāñchrīvatsalācchanaḥ ||
nṛsiṃho duṣṭadamano jayo viṣṇustrivikramaḥ || 6 ||
stutaḥ prayacchate cātha aṇimādibhiracyutaḥ ||
kāmyaḥ kāmapradaḥ kāntaḥ kāmayātmā tathā hariḥ || 7 ||
ānando mādhavaścaiva kāmasaṃvṛddhaye japet ||
rāmaḥ paraśurāmaśca nṛsiṃho viṣṇureva ca || 8 ||
vikramaścaivamādīni japyānyarijigīṣubhiḥ ||
dāmodarāniruddhau ca jñeyau bandhanamokṣadau || 9 ||
japtavyobhyasatā vidyāṃ mahāpuruṣa ityapi ||
vidyārthinā vā japtavyaṃ tathāśvaśiraseti ca || 3.122.10 ||
balārthī balabhadreti saukhyārthī sukhadeti ca ||
aśvārthī cāśvaśirasaṃ gajārthī nāgamokṣaṇam || 11 ||
gobhirarthī japennāma vāsudeveti kīrtitam ||
yajñārthī yajñapuruṣaṃ kīrtayetprayato naraḥ ||12||
nārāyaṇeti ca tathā viṣṇaveti yadūdvaha||
yadvābhirocato rāmaṃ sarvārtheṣu hyudīrayet ||
sarvāṇyetāni nāmāni parasya brahmaṇo yataḥ ||13 ||
sarvāṇi nāmāni hi tasya rājan bhavanti kāmākhiladāyakāni ||
yadeva saṃkīrtayate sa śaktyā tasmādavāpnoti hi kāmasiddhim || 14 ||
iti śrīviṣṇudharmottare tṛ0 khaṇḍe mārkaṇḍeyavajrasaṃvāde kāmaprāptivarṇano nāma dvāviṃśatyuttaraśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ || 122 ||
<3.123>
vajra uvāca ||
kāryārambhe tathā kasminko nāma ddvija kīrtayet ||
etanme saṃśayaṃ chindhi tvaṃ hi sarvaṃ vijānase || 1 ||
mārkaṇḍeya uvāca ||
cakriṇaṃ halinaṃ caiva śārṅgiṇaṃ khaḍginaṃ tathā ||
kṣemārthī pravasanrājandikṣu prācyādiṣu smaret || 2 ||
arcitaṃ cādhikaṃ caiva sarvaṃ sarveśvaraṃ prabhum ||
saṃsmaretpuruṣaṃ bhaktyā vyavahāreṣu sarvadā || 3 ||
kūrmaṃ varāhaṃ matsyaṃ ca jalaprataraṇe smaret ||
bhrājiṣṇumagnijanane japedrāmamatandritaḥ || 4||
saṅgrāmābhimukhe gacchansaṃsmaredaparājitam ||
keśavaṃ puṇḍarīkākṣaṃ puṣkarārthaṃ tathā japet||5||
netrabādhāsu sarvāsu hṛṣīkeśaṃ tathaiva ca ||
acyutaṃ cāmṛtaṃ caiva japedauṣadhakarmaṇi || 6 ||
garuḍadhvajānusmaraṇādāpado mucyate naraḥ ||
jvaraduṣṭa śirorogaviṣavīryaṃ praśāmyati || 7 ||
grahanakṣatrapīḍāsu devabādhāṭavīṣu ca ||
dasyuvairinirodheṣu vyāghrasiṃhādisaṃkaṭe || 8 ||
andhakāre tathā tīvre narasiṃheti kīrtayet ||
vittasthāpanakāle ca dhānyasaṃsthāpane tathā || 9 ||
kurvīta tanmanā bhūtvā anantācyutakīrtanam ||
nārāyaṇaṃ śārṅgadharaṃ śrīdharaṃ gajamokṣaṇam || 3.123.10 ||
vāmanaṃ khaṅginaṃ caiva duḥsvapneṣu tu saṃsmaret ||
agnidāhe samutpanne saṃsmarejjalaśāyinam||11||
balabhadraṃ samṛddhārthe kṛṣyāraṃbhe halāyudham ||
uttāraṇaṃ vaṇijyārthī śrīśamabhyudaye nṛpa ||12 ||
maṅgalyaṃ maṅgale viṣṇumamaṅgalyeṣu kīrtayet ||
ariṣṭānteṣu śeṣeṣu viśokeṣu tathā japet || 13 ||
uttiṣṭhan kīrtayedviṣṇuṃ prasupto mādhavaṃ naraḥ ||
bhojane caiva govindaṃ sarvatra madhusūdanam || 14 ||
nārāyaṇaṃ sarvakāle kṣutapraskhala nādiṣu ||
snāne devārcane home praṇipāte pradakṣiṇe || 15 ||
kīrtayedvāsudeveti anukteṣvapi yādava ||
kāryārambhe'tha vā rājanyatheṣṭaṃ nāma kīrtayeta |||| 16 ||
sarvāṇi nāmāni hi tasya rājansarvārthasiddhāni bhavanti puṃsaḥ ||
tasmādyatheṣṭaṃ khalu devanāma sarveṣu kāryeṣu japettu bhaktyā || 17 ||
iti śrīviṣṇudharmottare tṛtīyakhaṇḍe mārkaṇḍeyavajrasaṃvāde tattatkāryaprāptitattannāmakīrtanavarṇano nāma trayoviṃśatyuttaraśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ || 123 ||
<3.124>
vajra uvāca ||
kasminkāle tu devasya kiṃkiṃ nāma prakīrtayet ||
etanme saṃśayaṃ chindhi tvaṃ hi sarvaṃ vijānase || 1 ||
mārkaṇḍeya uvāca ||
puruṣaṃ vāmadevaṃ ca tathā saṅkarṣaṇaṃ prabhum ||
pradyumnamaniruddhaṃ ca kramācchabdeṣu varjayet || 2||
viṣṇuṃ madhunihantāraṃ tathā devaṃ trivikramam ||
vāmanaṃ śrīdharaṃ caiva hṛṣīkeśaṃ tathaiva ca||3||
dāmodaraṃ padmanābhaṃ keśavaṃ ca yadūttama||
nārāyaṇaṃ mādhavaṃ ca govindaṃ ca tathā kramāt ||4||
caitrādiṣu tu māseṣu devadevamanusmaret ||
pradyumnamaniruddhaṃ ca pakṣayoḥ śuklakṛṣṇayoḥ||5||
sarvaḥ śarvaḥ śivaḥ sthāṇurbhūtānāṃ nidhiravyayaḥ||
ādityādiṣu vāreṣu kramādevamanusmaret || 6 ||
viśvaṃ viṣṇurvaṣaṭkāro bhūtabhavyabhavaḥ prabhuḥ||
bhūtakṛt bhūtabhṛdbhāvo bhūtātmā bhūtabhāvanaḥ||7||
avyaktaḥ puṃḍarīkākṣo viśvakarmā śuciśravāḥ ||
sambhavo bhāvano bhartā prabhavaḥ prabhurīśvaraḥ || 8 ||
aprameyo hṛṣīkeśaḥ padmanābhaḥ paraḥ prabhuḥ ||
agrāhyaḥ śāśvato dhātā kṛṣṇaścaitānyanusmaret||9||
devadevasya nāmāni kṛttikādiṣu yādava ||
brahmāṇaṃ śrīpatiṃ viṣṇuṃ kapilaṃ śrīdharaṃ prabhum || 3.124.10 ||
dāmodaraṃ hṛṣīkeśaṃ govindaṃ madhusūdanam ||
bhūdharaṃ gadinaṃ caiva śikhinaṃ padminaṃ tathā || 11 ||
cakriṇaṃ ca mahārāja prathamādiṣu saṃsmaret ||
sarvaṃ vā sarvadā rāma devadevasya yādava || 12 ||
nāmāni sarvāṇi janārdanasya kālaśca sarvaḥ puruṣapravīra ||
tasmātsadā sarvagatasya nāma grāhyaṃ yatheṣṭaṃ varadasya rājan || 13 ||
iti śrīviṣṇudharmottare tṛtīyakhaṇḍe mārkaṇḍeyavajrasaṃvāde kālanāmākhyāno nāma caturviṃśatyuttaraśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ || 124 ||
<3.125>
vajra uvāca ||
kasminkṣetre tu devasya kiṃkiṃ nāma prakīrtayet ||
etanme saṃśayaṃ chindhi bhṛguvaṃśavivardhana || 1 ||
mārkaṇḍeya uvāca ||
atrāpyudāharantīmamitihāsaṃ purātanam||
vāsudevasya saṃvādamarjunasya ca yādava || 2 ||
vṛtte bhāratasaṃgrāme rājyaṃ prāpte yudhiṣṭhire|
asminneva sabhoddeśe vijahrāte yathāsukham ||3||
vāsudevārjunau vīrau lakṣīkṛtavasundharau ||
tataḥ kathānte kasmiṃścitkṛṣṇaṃ papraccha phālgunaḥ || 4 ||
arjuna uvāca ||
kṣetreṣu yeṣuyeṣu tvaṃ kīrtanīyo mayācyuta ||
cetasā praṇidhānārthaṃ tvaṃ mamākhyātumarhasi || 5 ||
śrībhagavānuvāca ||
sarvagaḥ sarvabhūto'haṃ na hi kiñcinmayā vinā ||
carācare jagatyasminvidyate kurusattama || 6 ||
tathāpi yeṣu sthāneṣu cintanīyohamarjuna ||
stotavyo nāmabhiryaiśca śṛṇu tvaṃ tadvadāmi te || 7 ||
puṣkare puṇḍarīkākṣaṃ gayāyāṃ ca gadādharam ||
lohadaṇḍe tathā viṣṇuṃ stuvaṃstarati duṣkṛtam || 8 ||
rāghavaṃ citrakūṭe tu prabhāse daityasūdanam ||
vṛndāvane ca govindaṃ māṃ stuvanpuṇyabhāgbhavet || 9 ||
jayaṃ jayantyāṃ stunvaṃśca jayantaṃ hastināpure ||
varāhaṃ kandamāle ca kaśmīreṣu ca cakriṇam || 3.125.10 ||
janārdanastu kubjāmre mathurāyāṃ ca keśavam ||
śrīkubje śrīdharaṃ tadvadgaṅgādvāre surottamam || 11 ||
śāligrāme mahāyogyaṃ hariṃ govardhanācale ||
piṇḍārake caturbāhuṃ śaṃkhadvāre ca śaṃkhinam || 12 ||
vāmanaṃ tu kurukṣetre yamunāyāṃ trivikramam ||
viśveśvaraṃ tathā śoṇe kapilaṃ pūrvasāgare || 13 ||
śvetadvīpapatiṃ cāpi gaṅgāsāgarasaṃgame ||
bhūdharaṃ devikātīre prayāge yogaśāyinam || 14 ||
naranāraṇāyākhyau ca tathā badarikāśrame ||
samudre dakṣiṇe nāma padmanābheti phālguna || 15 ||
dvārakāyāṃ tathā kṛṣṇaṃ stuvaṃstarati durgatim ||
evaṃ rāmaṃ mahendrādrau hṛṣīkeśaṃ tathā budha || 16 ||
aśvatīrthe hayagrīvaṃ viśvarūpaṃ himācale ||
nṛsiṃhaṃ kṛtaśauce tu vipāśāyāṃ dvijapriyam || 17 ||
naimiṣe yajñapuruṣaṃ jambumāle tathācyutam ||
anantaṃ saindhavāraṇye daṇḍake śārṅgadhāriṇam || 18 ||
utpalāvartake śauriṃ narmadāyāṃ śriyaḥ patim ||
dāmodaraṃ raivatake nandāyāṃ jalaśāyinam || 19 ||
sarvayogeśvaraṃ caiva sindhusāgarasaṃgame ||
sahyādrau devadeveśaṃ vaikuṇṭhaṃ mādhave vane ||3.125.20 ||
sarvapāpaharaṃ viṃdhye coḍreṣu puruṣottamam ||
hradaye cāpi kaunteya paramātmā sanātanaḥ || 21 ||
vaṭevaṭe vaiśravaṇaṃ catvarecatvare śivam ||
parvateparvate rāmaṃ sarvatra madhusūdanam || 22 ||
naraṃ bhūmau tathā vyomni kaunteya garuḍadhvajam ||
vāsudevaṃ ca sarvatra saṃsmarejjyotiṣāṃ patim || 23 ||
arcayanpraṇamanstunvansaṃsmareśca dhanañjaya ||
eteṣvetāni nāmāni naraḥ pāpātpramucyate || 24 ||
sthāneṣveteṣu mannāmnāmeteṣāṃ prīṇayennaraḥ ||
dvijānāṃ prīṇanaṃ kṛtvā svargaloke mahīyate || 25 ||
nāmānyetāni kaunteya sthānānyetāni cātmavān ||
japanvai pañca pañcāśattrisandhyaṃ matparāyaṇāḥ || 26 ||
trīṇi janmāni yatpāpaṃ cāvasthātritaye kṛtam ||
tatkṣālayatyasandigdhaṃ jāyate ca satāṃ kule || 27 ||
dvikālaṃ vā japanvaitaddivārātrau ca yatkṛtam ||
tasmādvimucyate pāpātsaṃstuvanparamo naraḥ || 28 ||
japtānyetāni kaunteya sakṛcchraddhāsamanvitam ||
mocayanti naraṃ pāpādyattatraiva dine kṛtam || 29 ||
dhanyo yaśasyaḥ śatrughno jayaḥ kurukulodvaha ||
grahānukūlatāṃ caiva karotyāśu na saṃśayaḥ || 3.125.30||
upoṣitāśca manasaḥ sthāneṣveteṣu mānavaḥ ||
kṛtāyatanavāsaśca prāpnotyabhimataṃ phalam || 31 ||
utkrāntirapyaśeṣeṣu sthāneṣveteṣu śasyate ||
anyasthānācchataguṇameteṣvanaśanādikam || 32 || ||
yastu matparamaḥ kālaṃ karotyeteṣu mānavaḥ||
devānāmapi pūjyo'sau mama loke mahīyate||33||
sthāneṣvathaiteṣu ca ye vasanti saṃpūjayante mama sarvakālam ||
tadeha cānte tridivaṃ prayānti nākaṃ ca lokaṃ samavāpnuvanti||34||
iti śrīviṣṇudharmottare tṛtīyakhaṇḍe mārkaṇḍeyavajrasaṃvāde arjunaṃ prati kṛṣṇopadeśe sthānaviśeṣakīrtanamāhātmyavarṇano nāma pañcaviṃśatyuttaraśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ || 125 ||
<3.126>
|| vajra uvāca || ||
nāmnāṃ saṃpūjanaṃ viṣṇostvayā kāryaṃ prakīrtitam||
prādurbhāveṣu kāmyaṃ ca pūjanaṃ ca tvayeritam || 1 ||
kāla nāmāni ca tathā sthānanāmāni cāpyatha ||
kāmyāni caiva nāmāni tvayā proktāni bhārgava || 2 ||
tasyaiva devadevasya viṣṇoramitatejasaḥ ||
sarvadevamayasyeha sarvarūpadharasya ca || 3 ||
ārādhanavidhānāni kathayasva mamānagha ||
tvaṃ hi sarvakathārāmo yathā devaḥ pitāmahaḥ || 4 ||
bhūya eva samācakṣva upavāsaphalaṃ vibho ||
devatāpūjanārthāya vidhinā pūjanasya ca || 5||
mārkaṇḍeya uvāca ||
aṣṭapatraṃ tu kamalaṃ vinyasedvarṇakaiḥ śubhaiḥ ||
brahmāṇaṃ karṇikāyāṃ tu tasya saṃpūjayedvibhum || 6 ||
ṛgvedaṃ pūrvapatre tu yajurvedaṃ tu dakṣiṇe ||
paścime sāmavedaṃ tu uttare'tharvaṇaṃ tathā || 7 ||
āgneye ca tathāṅgāni dharmaśāstrāṇi nairṛte ||
purāṇānyeva vāyavye caiśānyāṃ nyāyavistaram || 8 ||
evaṃ vinyasya dharmajña sopavāsastu pūjayet ||
caitraśuklādathārabhya sopavāso jitendriyaḥ || 9 ||
sadā pratipadaṃ prāpya śuklapakṣasya yādava ||
saṃvatsaraṃ mahābhāga śuklagandhānulepanaḥ || 3.126.10 ||
bhūriṇā paramānnena dhūpadīpairatandritaḥ ||
saṃvatsarānte gāṃ dadyāddhute cīrṇe narottama ||11||
idaṃ vrataṃ yastu karoti rājansa vedavitsyādbhuvi dharmanityaḥ ||
kṛtvā tadā dvādaśavatsarāṇi vidheśca loka puruṣaḥ prayāti|| 12 ||
iti śrīviṣṇudharmottare tṛtīyakāṇḍe mārkaṇḍeyavajrasaṃvāde brāhmīpratipallābho nāma ṣaḍviṃśatyuttaraśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ || 126 ||
<3.127>
|| mārkaṇḍeya uvāca || ||
ārādhanaṃ tridevasya tasya vakṣyāmi yādava ||
viṣṇoramitavīryasya dvitīyamarimardana || 1 ||
brāhmī tu rājasī mūrtistasya vargapravartinī ||
sāttvikī vaiṣṇavī jñeyā saṃsāraparipālinī || 2 ||
tāmasī ca tathā raudrī jñeyā saṃhārakāriṇī ||
caitraśuklapratipadi brahmāṇaṃ pūjayennaraḥ || 3 ||
gandhamālyanamaskāradhūpadīpānnasampadā ||
kṣīraṃ brāhmaistato mantrairjuhuyājjātavedasi || 4 ||
lohabhāṇḍaṃ dvije dadyātkṣīrapūrṇaṃ sakāñcanam ||
dadhnā ca bhojanaṃ kāryaṃ madhyaṃ prāpte divākare || 5 ||
ekavastrāṃcite deśe tathā svapyātkuśāstṛte ||
tataścaturthe divase kūpanādeyasārasaiḥ ||
tribhirekagataistoyaiḥ samyaksnānaṃ samācaret || 6 ||
pūjayetpūrtavatsamyakpuratritayameva tat ||
lohatrayaṃ dvije dadyātsamālyaṃ nṛpa dakṣiṇām || 7 ||
tṛtīyehni tataḥ pūjā kāryā rudrasya pārthiva ||
gandhamālyanamaskāradhūpa dīpaistathaiva ca ||
mānastoketi maṃtreṇa ghṛtahomo vidhīyate ||8||
saghṛtaṃ lohapātraṃ ca sahiraṇyaṃ sadakṣiṇam ||
tṛtīye dinabhāge tu vātāhāraṃ samācaret || 9 ||
tathā trimadhuraṃ dadyādbhojanaṃ tacca bhakṣayet ||
vratametannaraḥ kṛtvā māsimāsi dinatrayam ||
saṃvatsareṇa dharmajña gatimiṣṭāmavāpnuyāt || 3.127.10 ||
mānuṣyamāsādya bhavatyarogo jitendriyaḥ satyaparo vinītaḥ||
saubhāgyarūpadraviṇopapannaḥ sākṣācchivo lokadhurandharaḥ syāt||11||
iti śrīviṣṇudharmottare tṛtīyakhaṇḍe mārkaṇḍeyavajrasaṃvāde trimūrtivrataṃ nāma saptaviṃśatyuttaraśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ || 127||
<3.128>
mārkaṇḍeya uvāca ||
ekameva jagatsarvaṃ prakṛtiḥ puruṣaḥ smṛtaḥ ||
caitraśuklasamāraṃbhe sopavāso jitendriyaḥ||1||
puruṣaṃ pūjayedviṣṇuṃ sthale vā yadi vā jale || 
gandhamālyanamaskāradhūpadīpānnasaṃpadā||2||
pauruṣaṃ tu tathā sūktaṃ japedantarjale naraḥ ||
tathā cainaṃ ca pratyaṣṭaṃ budho dadyājjalāñjalim ||3 ||
tathā puṣpāṇi dharmajñaḥ phalāni ca mahābhuja ||
dhūpaṃ dadyātsanaivedyaṃ japecchaktyā tathā ca tam || 4 ||
juhuyācca tathā''jyena dvije dadyācca kāñcanam ||
āhāraṃ payasā kuryānniśākāle ca bhārgava|| || 5 ||
etatsaṃvatsaraṃ kṛtvā nityavratamatandritaḥ ||
pakṣayorubhayorvīra sarvapāpaiḥ pramucyate || 6 ||
prasādamāsādya sa vāsudevātsarveśvarātsarvagaṇādacintyāt ||
lokeśvarānmokṣapadaṃ prayāti ye yānti siddhā munayaḥ pratītāḥ || 7 ||
iti śrīviṣṇudharmottare tṛ0 khaṇḍe mārkaṇḍeyavajrasaṃvāde pauruṣīpratipadvarṇano nāmāṣṭāviṃśatyuttaraśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ || 128 ||
<3.129>
mārkaṇḍeya uvāca ||
puruṣaḥ prakṛtiścobhau jagatsarvaṃ prakīrtitam ||
agnīṣomātmakaṃ sarvaṃ tathā tasya prakīrtitam ||1||
vāsudevaśca lakṣmīśca tāveva parikīrtitau ||
caitraśuklasamāyāṃ ca sopavāso jitendriyaḥ ||2||
pauruṣeṇa sa sūktena vahniṃ saṃpūjayennaraḥ ||
gandhamālyanamaskāradhūpadīpānnasapradā || 3 ||
lakṣmīṃ ca varadāṃ devīṃ pūjayedudakena tu ||
śrīsūktena ca dharmajña yathāvanmanujeśvara || 4 ||
kāñcanaṃ rājataṃ cobhau dadyādvipreṣu dakṣiṇām ||
prāṇayātrāṃ budhaḥ kuryātkṣīreṇa saghṛtena ca || 5 ||
saṃvatsaramidaṃ kṛtvā vrataṃ samyagupoṣitaḥ ||
mucyate pātakaiḥ sarvairmokṣopāyāṃśca vindati || 5||
kāmānavāpnotyatha vā vicitrām̐llokāṃśca mukhyānvasudhāṃ samagrām ||
virūpato rūpamathāpi cāgryaṃ yaśastathāgryaṃ tanayāṃśca mukhyān||6||
iti śrīviṣṇudharmottare tṛ0 khaṇḍe mārkaṇḍeyavajrasaṃvāde prakṛti puruṣanirūpaṇo nāmaikonatriṃśaduttaraśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ || 129 ||
<3.130>
mārkaṇḍeya uvāca ||
nāsatyau devabhiṣajāvaśvinau parikīrtitau ||
tāveva kathitau loke sūryatārādhipau nṛpa || 1 ||
aśvayānarato yasmādaśvinau parikīrtitau ||
caitraśukladvitīyāyāṃ sopavāso jitendriyaḥ || 2 ||
nāsatyau devabhiṣajau pūjayetprayataḥ śuciḥ ||
gandhamālyanamaskāradhūpadīpānnasampadā || 3 ||
kṛtvā ca rūpanirmāṇaṃ nāsatyau pūjayennaraḥ |||
dīpamālāṃ tato dadyāttayorniśi viśeṣataḥ || 4 ||
kanakaṃ rajataṃ cobhe dadyādvipreṣu dakṣiṇām ||
saṃmīlya netrayugalaṃ viṣṇuṃ ca saṃsmarennaraḥ || 5 ||
pradīptatejā bhavati cakṣuṣmāṃścaiva jāyate ||
prāṇayātrāṃ tu kurvīta dadhnā ghṛtayutena ca || 6 ||
netravrataṃ dvādaśavatsarāṇi kṛtvā bhavedbhūmipatiḥ pratītaḥ ||
sarūparūporigaṇapramāthī dharmābhirāmo nṛpavaryamukhyaḥ || 7 ||
iti śrīviṣṇudharmottare tṛ0 kha0 mārkaṇḍeyavajrasaṃvāde nāsatyapūjācakṣurvrataṃ nāma triṃśaduttaraśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ || 130 ||
<3.131>
|| mārkaṇḍeya uvāca ||
imāṃ tathānyāṃ vakṣyāmi dvitīyāṃ sarvakāmadām ||
yāmupoṣya naraḥ kāmānsarvānāpnotyabhīpsitān || 1 ||
caitradvitīyāṃ saṃprāpya śuklapakṣasya mānavaḥ ||
dināvasāne kurvīta samyak snānaṃ nadījale || 2 ||.
bālendumaṇḍalaṃ kṛtvā pūjayecchvetavarṇakaiḥ ||
śvetaiḥ puṣpaiḥ phalaiścaiva paramānnena bhūriṇā || 3 |||
īkṣunekṣuvikārairvā guḍena lavaṇena vā ||
dināvasāne deveśaṃ pūjayitvā niśākaram ||4||
ghṛtena havanaṃ kṛtvā naktaṃ bhuñjīta vāgyataḥ ||
tatastailena haritaṃ bhakṣayetsakṛdeva ca ||5||
etadvrataṃ naraḥ kṛtvā samyak saṃvatsaraṃ śuciḥ ||
saubhāgyaṃ mahadāpnoti svargalokaṃ ca gacchati ||6||
etatpavitraṃ ripu nāśakāri saubhāgyadaṃ rogaharaṃ ca rājan||
proktaṃ vrataṃ yādavavaṃśamukhya kāryaṃ prayatnena tathā striyāpi||7||
iti śrīviṣṇudharmottare tṛtīyakhaṇḍe mārkaṇḍeyavajrasaṃvāde bālendudvitīyāvratavarṇano nāmaikatriṃśaduttaraśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ || 131 ||
<3.132>
mārkaṇḍeya uvāca ||
aśūnyaśayanāṃ rājandvitīyāṃ śṛṇvato mama ||
yāmupoṣya na vaidhavyaṃ prayāti strī kathañcana || 1 ||
patnīviyogaṃ ca naro nāsādayati karhicit ||
kṛṣṇapakṣadvitīyāyāṃ grāhyā sā śravaṇe tithiḥ || 2 ||
svāstīrṇe śayane viṣṇuṃ lakṣmīṃ devīṃ tathaiva ca ||
saṃpūjayetphalaiḥ puṣpairgandhairarghaiśca bhārgava || 3 ||
naivedyaṃ ca tato dadyāddīpaṃ dhūpaṃ ca śaktitaḥ ||
tataḥ saṃpūjya deveśaṃ japenmantramimaṃ śuciḥ ||4||
śrīvatsadhāriñśrīkānta śrīvāsa śrīpate'cyuta ||
gārhasthaṃ mā praṇāśaṃ me yātu dharmārtha kāmadam || 5 ||
agnayo mā praṇaśyantu mā praṇaśyantu devatāḥ ||
pitaro mā praṇaśyantu matto dāṃpatyabhedataḥ || 6 ||
lakṣmyā viyujyate deva na kadācidyathā bhavān ||
tathā kalatrasaṃbandho deva mā me vinaśyatu || 7 ||
lakṣmyā na śūnyaṃ varada yathā te śayanaṃ sadā ||
śayyā mamāpyaśūnyāstu tathaiva madhusūdana || 8 ||
evaṃ prasādya deveśaṃ naktaṃ bhuñjīta vāgyataḥ ||
brāhmaṇāya dvitīyehni pradadyāccaiva dakṣiṇām||9||
śayane ca dvijendrāya gate māsa catuṣṭaye||
tasya janmatrayaṃ rājangṛhabhaṅgo na jāyate||3.132.10||
aśūnyaśayanaścāsau dharmakarmārthasādhakaḥ ||
bhavatyavyāhataiśvaryaḥ puruṣo nātra saṃśayaḥ||11||
vaidhavyahīnā subhagā virogā pativratā dharmaparā ca sādhvī ||
rūpeṇa yuktā na bhayānvitā ca nārī bhavetsarvaguṇopapannā ||12||
iti śrīviṣṇudhamottare tṛtīyakhaṇḍe mārkaṇḍeyavajrasaṃvāde aśūnyaśayanadvitīyāvratakathano nāma dvātriṃśaduttaraśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ || 132 ||
<3.133>
mārkaṇḍeya uvāca ||
śuklapakṣa tṛtīyāyāṃ sopavāso jitendriyaḥ ||
maṇḍalatritayaṃ kuryādvarṇakena pṛthakpṛthak || 1 ||
maṇḍalaṃ dakṣiṇe bhāge śvetaṃ kuryānnaro bhaveta ||
raktaṃ madhye'pyadhaḥ kuryādudakchvetaṃ dhruvasya tu|| 2 ||
viṣṇoḥ padatrayaṃ teṣu pūjyaṃ syānmaṇḍaleṣu ca ||
bhūmau ca prathamaṃ pādaṃ dvitīyaṃ sūryamaṇḍale || 3 ||
tṛtīyaṃ tu dhruve deve pūjayetprayataḥ śuciḥ ||
yathā maṇḍalavarṇāṅko gandhamālyādibhirdvija || 4 ||
tadviṣṇoḥ paramityeva homamantro vidhīyate ||
trivikramāyeti tathā strīśūdrasya dvijottama || 5 ||
akṣatāni tilānyājyaṃ homayetsaṃyutaṃ trayam ||
tāmraraupyasuvarṇāni śaktyā dadyāttu dakṣiṇām || 6 ||
bhojanaṃ ca trimadhuraṃ bhojayedbrāhmaṇottamam ||
prāṇayātrāṃ ca kurvīta trigavyena ca taddinam || 7 ||
evaṃ varṣatrayaṃ kṛtvā vratānte vai trihāyanam ||
brāhmaṇāya tu gāṃ dattvā sarvānkāmānavāpnuyāt || 8 ||
bhūmau tathāntarikṣe ca divi caiva narottama ||
gatistasyā pratihatā bhavatyariniṣūdana || 9 ||
trīṇi varṣasahasrāṇi sarvagasyāvicāriṇaḥ ||
mānasaṃ vācikaṃ caiva kāyikaṃ ca vimuñcati || 3.133.10 ||
sarvapāpaṃ mahābhāga manuṣyo jāyate tayā ||
kule dhanāḍhyo bhavati rūpadraviṇasaṃyute || 11 |
virogaḥ sarvasiddhārthastrivargasya ca sādhakaḥ ||
kṛtvā dvādaśavarṣāṇi viṣṇuloke mahīyate || 12 ||
tatrāpi nityaṃ varadaṃ vareṇyaṃ lakṣmīsahāyaṃ puruṣaṃ purāṇam ||
paśyatyamoghaṃ vibhumapratarkyaṃ sanātanaṃ duḥkhavināśahetuma|| || 13 ||
iti śrīviṣṇudharmottare tṛtīyakhaṇḍe mārkaṇḍeyavajrasaṃvāde trivikramavratavarṇano nāma trayastriṃśaduttaraśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ || 133 ||
<3.134>
|| mārkaṇḍeya uvāca ||
dvitīyaṃ tava vakṣyāmi śṛṇu traivikramaṃ vratam ||
bhūmistu prathamaḥ pādo hyantarikṣaṃ tathā param || 1 ||
tṛtīyaśca divaṃ jñeyo devadevasya cakriṇaḥ ||
bhuvaḥ patiḥ smṛto vahnirantarikṣaṃ tathā param || 2 ||
divaḥ patistathā sūryastacca viṣṇoḥ padatrayam ||
jyeṣṭhaśuklatṛtīyāyāṃ sopavāso jitendriyaḥ || 3 ||
kalye kūpajalasnāto vahniṃ sampūjayennaraḥ ||
gandhamālyanamaskāradhūpadīpānnasaṃpadā || 4 ||
nadījale tataḥ snātvā madhyaṃ prāpte divākare ||
vāyoḥ saṃpūjanaṃ kṛtvā saktūndadyāddvijātaye || 5 ||
snātvā ca sarasītoye sāyaṃ sūryaṃ samarcayet ||
tataśca naktaṃ cāśnīyāddhaviṣyaṃ vāgyataḥ śuciḥ || 6 ||
evaṃ saṃvatsare rājankṛtvā vratamatandritaḥ ||
samāpayitvā vaiśākhe dadyādvipreṣu dakṣiṇām || 7 ||
tāmraṃ rūpyaṃ suvarṇaṃ ca trīṇi lohāni yādava ||
vāsoyugaṃ ca soṣṇīṣaṃ trīṇi vāsāṃsi cāpyatha || 8 ||
upānaho yugaṃ chatraṃ trīṇi cemāni bhaktitaḥ ||
etatsaṃvatsaraṃ kṛtvā narastraivikramaṃ vratam || 9 ||
sarvakāmasamṛddhasya yajñasya phalamaśnute ||
vimānenārkavarṇena kiṅkiṇījālamālinā || 3.134.10 ||
devarājagaṇāḍhyena vīṇāmurajanādinā ||
annamāyuḥ sutāścaiva prasanne garuḍadhvaje ||11||
ākrāntalokatritayasya tasya padatrayaṃ dharmabhṛtāṃ variṣṭha ||
saṃpūjya kāmānmanujā labhante gatiṃ yatheṣṭāṃ puruṣapravīra || 12 ||
iti śrīviṣṇudharmottare tṛtīyakhaṇḍe mārkaṇḍeyavajrasaṃvāde dvitīyatraivikramavratakathano nāma catustriṃśaduttaraśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ || 134 ||
<3.135>
mārkaṇḍeya uvāca ||
vyākhyātaṃ te mahābhāga tava traivikamaṃ vratama ||
tṛtīyaṃ nṛpaśārdūla tanme nigadataḥ śṛṇu || 1 ||
jyeṣṭha śuklatṛtīyāyāṃ naraḥ samyagupoṣitaḥ ||
bhuvassaṃpūjanaṃ kuryāddhūpamālyānulepanaiḥ || 2 ||
dīpairannaiśca vividhairdadyādvipreṣu dakṣiṇām ||
bījapūrṇāni pātrāṇi sahiraṇyāni śaktitaḥ || 3 ||
pūjaivamantarikṣasya hyāṣāḍheti vidhīyate ||
dakṣiṇā tatra dātavyā vāsoyugmaṃ dvijātaye || 4 ||
divaḥ pūjā ca kartavyā śrāvaṇe prāgvadeva tu ||
chatraṃ copānaho yugmaṃ dakṣiṇāṃ tatra dāpayet || 5 ||
evaṃ māsatrayeṇeha pāraṇaṃ prathamaṃ bhavet ||
māsatrayeṇa cānyattu māsatrayyaṃ punastataḥ || 6 ||
tato māsatrayeṇānyamenamabdena cānagha ||
vijñeyaṃ dharmanityasya pāraṇānāṃ catuṣṭayam || 7 ||
prathamaṃ pāraṇaṃ kṛtvā agniṣṭomaphalaṃ labheta ||
atirātraphalaṃ rājandvitīye pāraṇe tathā || 8 ||
tṛtīye pāraṇe vidvānvājapeyaphalaṃ labheta ||
caturthe pāraṇe proktaṃ rājasūyasya yatphalam || 9 ||
vimānenārkavarṇena kiṅkiṇījālamālinā ||
haṃsayuktena divyena vīṇāmurajanādinā || 3.135.10 ||
varāpsarogaṇāḍhyena kāmukenāpi cāruṇā ||
yatheṣṭagāmī bhavati bahukālamasaṃśayam || 11 ||
mānuṣye janma cāsādya triśuklatvamavāpnuyāt ||
vidyā janma tathā karma nātra kāryā vicāraṇā || 12 ||
trivikramasyāpratimasya tasya carācareśasya padatrayaṃ ca ||
yaḥ pūjayettasya bhavanti kāmāḥ sarve samṛddhā na hi saṃśayo'tra || 13 ||
iti śrīviṣṇudharmottare tṛtīyakhaṇḍe mārkaṇḍeyavajrasaṃvāde tṛtīyatraivikramavratavarṇano nāma pañcatriṃśaduttaraśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ || 135 ||
<3.136>
mārkaṇḍeya uvāca ||
ārādhanaṃ hi devasya tasya vakṣyāmi yādava ||
viṣṇoramitavīryasya tannibodha narādhipa || 1 ||
vāyvarkacandradehastu trimūrtirbhagavānhariḥ ||
agniṣṭomo jagatyasmim̐ścandrārkau parikīrtitau || 2 ||
sarvādhāraśca bhagavānvāyuḥ sarvagatastathā ||
pañca bhūtātmakaṃ sarvaṃ jagadetaccarācaram || 3 ||
ādhārabhūtau dvāvetau pṛthivī gaganaṃ tathā ||
ādheyo viddhi dharmajña tathā bhūtatrayaṃ sadā || 4 ||
candrādityau ca vāyuśca sarvasya jagato dvija ||
ādhārādheyabhāvena bhūtairvṛtamidaṃ jagata || 5 ||
naiva cāsti jagatyasminkiñcidbhūtavivarjitam ||
tasmādvā yādavaśreṣṭha bhūtairvṛtamidaṃ jagat ||
brahmāṇaṃ viddhi bhūmiṃ ca gaganaṃ tadanantaram ||
aṇḍasyāntaḥ sthitā devā vāyvarkaṃ tuhināṃśavaḥ || 6 ||
pālayanti jagatsarvaṃ sadevāsuramānuṣam ||
tathaivāṇḍasthitāḥ sarve pālayanti jagattrayam || 7 ||
tathaiva dehināṃ dehe pālayanti śarīragāḥ ||
vātapitta kaphatvena vāyvarkaśiśirāṃśavaḥ || 8 ||
ādhāraṃ jagato yadvad brahmāṇḍaṃ parikīrtitam ||
tathā dehe śarīrasthā dehināṃ hi tvagucyate || 9 ||
brahmāṇḍe dehidehe'pi gaganaṃ dṛśyate dvayoḥ ||
brahmāṇḍavaccharīrāṇi vāyvarkaśiśirāṃśavaḥ || 3.136.10 ||
pālayanti mahīpāla vātapittakaphāṃśakāḥ ||
vāyvarkacandra dehastu tasmādviṣṇustrimūrtidhṛt || 11 ||
tasya saṃpūjanaṃ kāryaṃ yathāvacchṛṇu bhārgava ||
jyeṣṭhaśuklatṛtīyāyāṃ nirāhāro naraḥ śuciḥ || 12 ||
trimūrtipūjanaṃ kuryāddvitīyāyāṃ yathāvidhi ||
kūpanādeyatāḍākamiśraiḥ snātaḥ śucirjalaiḥ || 13 ||
pratyūṣe pūjayedvāyumanulipte śubhe sthale ||
gandha mālyanamaskāradhūpadīpānnasaṃpadā || 14 ||
homaṃ kuryādyavairmukhyairvastraṃ dadyāddvijātaye ||
madhyāhne pūjayedvahnau tathā sūryamatandritaḥ || 15 ||
tilāṃśca juhuyādvahnau dadyāddvipreṣu dakṣiṇām ||
sūryāstamanavelāyāṃ jale candraṃ ca pūjayet || 15 ||
ghṛtena homaṃ kurvīta rajataṃ dakṣiṇā smṛtā ||
naktaṃ bhuñjīta dharmajña tailahīnaṃ tato naraḥ || 17 ||
pūrṇaṃ saṃvatsaraṃ kṛtvā vratametadatandritaḥ ||
svargalokamavāpnoti sahasraparivatsarān || 18 ||
mānuṣyamāsādya tato rājā bhavati gahvare ||
vrataṃ kṛtvā mahārāja pūrṇaṃ saṃvatsaratrayam || 19 ||
pañcavarṣasahasrāṇi svargaloke mahīyate ||
mānuṣyaṃ ca samāsādya rājā bhavati dhārmikaḥ || 3.136.20 ||
vīro māṇḍalikaḥ śūraḥ paracakrabhayaṃkaraḥ ||
virogo darśanīyaśca subhago janavallabhaḥ || 21 ||
kṛtvā dvādaśavarṣāṇi vratametadanuttamam ||
vratāvasāne viprāṇāṃ sahasraṃ bhojayettataḥ || 22 ||
bhojyaṃ trimadhuraṃ peyaṃ dadyācchaktyā ca dakṣiṇām ||
tataḥ svargamavāpnoti varṣāṇāṃ ca śataṃ vratī || 23 ||
tato mānuṣyamāsādya rājā bhavati pārthivaḥ ||
pradīptacakro balavāndharmarājo janapriyaḥ || 24 ||
janmāntaraṃ samāsādya tato vipratvamāpnuyāt ||
tato brāhmaṇyamāsādya mokṣopāyaṃ ca vindati || 25 ||
ārādhanaṃ te kathitaṃ mayaitadviṣṇostridehasya mahānubhāva ||
ārādhito yena jagatpradhāna kāmānabhīṣṭānviduṣo dadāti || 26 ||
iti śrīviṣṇudharmottare tṛtīyakhaṇḍe mārkaṇḍeyavajrasaṃvāde śrīviṣṇutrimūrtivratavarṇano nāma ṣaṭtriṃśaduttaraśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ || 136 ||
<3.137>
|| mārkaṇḍeya uvāca ||
ataḥ paraṃ pravakṣyāmi viṣṇumūrtivrataṃ tava ||
dvidhā tu devadevasya mṛrtirbhavati yādava || 1 ||
ghorā cānyā śivā cānyā ghorā bhavati pāvakaḥ ||
śivā cāmbupatiryasmādagnīṣomātmakaṃ jagat || 2 ||
dvidhā ghorā vinirdiṣṭā dvidhā saumyā tataḥ punaḥ ||
ghorā vahniśca sūryaśca saumyā somajalādhipo || 3 ||
teṣāṃ ca pūjanaṃ kāryaṃ prativatprabhṛti kramāt ||
śuklapakṣādathārabhya phālgunasya dvijottama ||4||
ādityaṃ pūjayedrājā prathamehni paraḥ śuciḥ ||
dvitīyehni tathā vahni tṛtīyehni jalādhipam|| 5 ||
caturthehni śaśāṃkaṃ ca yathāvanmānavottama ||
teṣāṃ tu rūpanirmāṇaṃ kṛtvā tānarcayedbudhaḥ ||6||
gandhamālyanamaskāradhūpadīpānnasaṃpadā ||
vacāharidrayā snānaṃ prathamehni samācaret || 7 ||
dvitīye yaduśārdūla snānamāmalakaiḥ śubham ||
priyaṅguṇā tṛtīye ca caturthe gaurasarṣapaiḥ || 8||
godhūmatiladhānyaiśca yavaiśca divasaṃ kramāt ||
homaśca kāryo dharmajña dakṣiṇāṃ śṛṇu cāpyatha || 9 ||
kuṃkumaṃ raktavastraṃ ca candanaṃ chatrameva ca ||
kṣīreṇa cātra kartavyaṃ pratyahaṃ prāṇadhāraṇam || 3.137.10 ||
etatsaṃvatsaraṃ kṛtvā vrataṃ pūrṇaṃ narottama ||
sarvakāmasamṛddhasya yajñasthaṃ phalamaśnute || 11 ||
vimānenārkavarṇena svargalokaṃ ca gacchati ||
tatroṣya suciraṃ kālaṃ kule mahati jāyate || 12 ||
mānuṣyamāsādya bhavatyarogo vasundhareśo vijitāripakṣaḥ ||
dharme sthitaḥ satyaparo vinīto jitendriyaḥ sarvajanābhirāmaḥ||13||
iti śrīviṣṇudharmottare tṛtīyakhaṇḍe mārkaṇḍeyavajrasaṃvāde caturmūrtivratavarṇano nāma saptatriṃśaduttaraśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ || 137 ||
<3.138>
|| mārkaṇḍeya uvāca || ||
idamanyatpravakṣyāmi caturmūrtivrataṃ tava ||
śakrakīnāśavaruṇadhanādhyakṣānyadūttama || 1 ||
caturātmā vinirdiṣṭo vāsudevo jagatpatiḥ ||
teṣāṃ tu rūpanirmāṇaṃ kṛtvā tānarcayedbudhaḥ || 2||
gandhamālyanamaskāradhūpadīpānnasaṃpadā ||
ādyehni caitraśuklasya yajeta tridaśeśvaram || 3 ||
dvitīyehni yamaṃ devaṃ tṛtīye salilādhipam ||
caturthehni dhanādhyakṣaṃ pratyahaṃ snānamācaret || 4 ||
nadīpradeśamāsādya devadṛk pratyahaṃ kramāt ||
yavaistilaistathājyena homaḥ syāttilataṇḍulaiḥ || 5 ||
raktaṃ pītaṃ tathā kṛṣṇaṃ śvetaṃ vastraṃ dinakramāt ||
śubhametadvrataṃ kṛtvā pūrṇaṃ saṃvatsaraṃ naraḥ ||
nākalokamavāpnoti yāvadindrāścaturdaśa || 6 ||
mānuṣyamāsādya bhavatyarogo vasundhareśo vijitāripakṣaḥ ||
janābhirāmaḥ subhagaḥ prakṛtyā tatopi vipratvamupaiti bhūyaḥ || 7 ||
iti śrīviṣṇudharmottare tṛtīyakhaṇḍe mārkaṇḍevajrasaṃvāde caturmūrtivratavarṇano nāmā ṣṭātriṃśaduttaraśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ || 138 ||
<3.139>
|| mārkaṇḍeya uvāca || ||
idamanyatpravakṣyāmi caturmūrtivrataṃ tava ||
viṣṇurbhūmirnabho brahmā tasya rūpacatuṣṭayam || 1||
teṣāṃ tu rūpanirmāṇaṃ kṛtvā tānarcayettataḥ ||
gandhamālyanamaskāradhūpadīpānnasaṃpadā ||2||
ādyehni caitraśuklasya viṣṇuṃ devaṃ samarcayet ||
dvitīyādiṣu dharmajña bhuvaṃ tvāṃ ca pitāmaham || 3 ||
pūrvavratoktaṃ sakalaṃ vidhānamaparaṃ bhavet ||
vratametaṃ naraḥ kṛtvā svargalokamavāpnuyāt || 4 ||
mānuṣyamāsādya bhavatyarogo dharmābhirāmo draviṇopapannaḥ ||
dhanena rūpeṇa sukhena yukto janābhirāmo vijitāripakṣaḥ || 5 ||
iti śrīviṣṇudharmottare tṛtīyakhaṇḍe mārkaṇḍeyavajrasaṃvāde tārtīyakacaturmūrtivratavarṇano nāmaikonacatvāriṃśaduttaraśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ || 139 ||
<3.140>
mārkaṇḍeya uvāca ||
idamanyatpravakṣyāmi caturmūrtivrataṃ tava ||
balaṃ jñānaṃ tathaiśvaryaṃ śaktiśca yadunandana || 1 ||
vikhyātaṃ devadevasya tasya mūrticatuṣṭayam ||
yadeva rūpaṃ dharmasya balasyoktaṃ tadeva tu || 2 ||
rūpaṃ jñānasya te proktaṃ narasiṃhaṃ tathā nṛpa ||
rudrarūpamathaiśvaryaṃ kathitaṃ te mayā nṛpa || 3 ||
śaktirvarāhaḥ kathito devo madhuniṣūdanaḥ ||
evametanmukhātproktaṃ caturmūrtidharasya te || 4 ||
pūrvaṃ balamukhaṃ tasya vāsudevamukhaṃ bhavet ||
dakṣiṇaṃ vadanaṃ jñānaṃ devaṃ saṃkarṣaṇaṃ viduḥ || 5 ||
aiśvaryaṃ paścimaṃ vaktraṃ raudraṃ pāpaharaṃ tathā ||
varāhamuttaraṃ vaktramaniruddhaṃ prakīrtitama ||6||
trirātropoṣitaścaitre pūrvaṃ saṃpūjayenmukham ||
tathā vaiśākhamāse tu pūjayeddakṣiṇāmukham ||
jyeṣṭhe ca paścimaṃ vaktramāṣāḍheti tathottaram || 7||
gṛhopayogi dātavyaṃ caitre māsi dvijātaye ||
raṇopayogi dātavyaṃ vaiśākhe yādavottama||8||
yāgopayogi dātavyaṃ tathā jyeṣṭhe dvijātaye|
yajñopayogi dātavyaṃ māsyāṣāḍhe tathaiva ca||9||
vratametannaraḥ kṛtvā pūrṇaṃ māsacatuṣṭayam ||
pāraṇe prathame prāpte bhavatīti viniścayaḥ|| 3.140.10 ||
pāraṇaṃ prathamaṃ kṛtvā svargaloke mahīyate||
daśavarṣasahasrāṇi svargaṃ bhuktvā yathoditam || 11 ||
saumyādiṣu ca yāmeṣu dvitīyaṃ pāraṇaṃ bhavet ||
dvitīyaṃ pāraṇaṃ kṛtvā bhojayedbrāhmaṇāñśuciḥ ||
bhojanaṃ gorasaprāyaṃ mṛdvīkāśarkarāyutam || 12||
prāpte tṛtīye dvijapāraṇe ca prāpnoti devasya salokatāṃ saḥ ||
svargendulokau ca tathoktakālaṃ bhuktvā sukhī sarvasamṛddhikāmaḥ || 13 ||
iti śrīviṣṇudharmottare tṛ0 kha0 mārkaṇḍeyavajrasaṃvāde caturthaḥ caturmūrtivratavarṇano nāma catvāriṃśaduttaraśatamo'dhyāyaḥ || 140 ||
<3.141>
mārkaṇḍeya uvāca ||
idamanyatpravakṣyāmi caturmūrtivrataṃ tava ||
vidyākāmena kartavyaṃ nareṇa suvipaścitā || 1 ||
bahiḥ snānaṃ naraḥ kṛtvā kṛtaṛgvedapūjanaḥ ||
ṛgvedaṃ śṛṇuyānnityaṃ māsamātramatandritaḥ || 2 ||
caitrādārabhya dharmajñaḥ nityaṃ naktāśano dvijaḥ ||
yasmācchrutaṃ dvijāttasya jyeṣṭhasya caramehani || 3 ||
vāsoyugaṃ hiraṇyaṃ ca tathā dhenuṃ payasvinīma ||
ghṛtapūrṇaṃ kāṃsyapātraṃ sahiraṇyaṃ ca dakṣiṇā || 4 ||
āṣāḍhādiṣu māseṣu yajurvedavrataṃ bhaveta ||
āśvinādiṣu māseṣu sāmavedavrataṃ bhavet || 5 ||
tathā sarvavrataṃ nāma pauṣādiṣu vidhīyate ||
sarveṣu sarvaṃ kartavyamṛgvedavratakīrtitam || 6 ||
vedātmano vāsudevasya pūjāṃ kṛtvā naro drādaśavatsarāṇi ||
viṣṇolokaṃ yāti loke viśiṣṭaṃ yastaṃ prāptaḥ sarvaduḥkhaṃ jahāti || 7 ||
iti śrīviṣṇudharmottare tṛ0 kha0 mārkaṇḍeyavajrasaṃvāde pañcamacaturmūrtikalpe vedavrato nāmaikacatvāriṃśaduttaraśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ || 141 ||
<3.142>
mārkaṇḍeya uvāca ||
idamanyatpravakṣyāmi caturmūrti(caturvyūha)vrataṃ tava ||
sarvātmanāṃ tu kartavyā yathā pūjā jagadguroḥ||
caitraśuklacaturthyāṃ tu sopavāso jitendriyaḥ || 1 ||
caturthyāṃ vāsudevasya kṛtvā saṃpūjanaṃ śubhama ||
kāñcanaṃ dakṣiṇāṃ dadyāddvijāya brahmacāriṇe || 2 ||
tathā saṅkarṣaṇaṃ devaṃ pūjayitvā jagadgurum ||
vaiśākhe tu gṛhasthāya dadyācchayyāṃ susaṃskṛtām || 3 ||
saṃpūjya devaṃ pradyumnaṃ jyeṣṭhe māsi yathāvidhi ||
vanasthāya tathā dadyātphalamūlaṃ sagorasam || 4 ||
aniruddhaṃ tathāṣāḍhe pūjayitvā jagadgurum ||
dadyādalābupātraṃ ca yogasthāya dvijātaye || 5 ||
ityevaṃ pāraṇaṃ proktaṃ svargaloke mahīyate ||
dvitīye pāraṇe prāpte śakraloke mahīyate || 6 ||
sālokyamāyātyatha keśavasya prāpte tṛtīye tvatha pāraṇe ca ||
ityāśramākhyaṃ vratamuttamaṃ te mayeti kalmaṣanāśakāri || 7 ||
iti śrīviṣṇudharmottare tṛtīyakhaṇḍe mārkaṇḍeyavajrasaṃvāde caturmūrtikalpe āśramavratavarṇano nāma dvicatvāriṃśaduttaraśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ || 142 ||
<3.143>
mārkaṇḍeya uvāca ||
idamanyatpravakṣyāmi caturmūrtivrataṃ tava ||
caturātmā hariḥ proktaścatvārastu hutāśanāḥ || 1 ||
āhitāgnirdvijo yasya vidyate'gnicatuṣṭayam ||
sopavāsaścaturthyāṃ tu śuklapakṣasya phālgune || 2||
abhyarcya caturātmānaṃ vāsudevamatandritaḥ ||
tasmai dadyāddvijendrāya tilaprasthāni ṣoḍaśa || 3 ||
suvarṇasya suvarṇaṃ ca vastraṃ ghṛtatulāmapi ||
evaṃ saṃvatsaraṃ kṛtvā vratametadatandritaḥ ||4 ||
sarvakāmasamṛddhasya yajñasya phalamaśnute ||
vimānenārkavarṇena svargalokaṃ ca gacchati || 5 ||
mānuṣo dīptatejāḥ syāddīptāgniḥ pramadāpriyaḥ ||
ripūñjayati saṃgrāme dhanavāṃśca tathā bhavet || 6 ||
ye tvagnayo vai caturaḥ pradiṣṭāḥ sa vāsudevaḥ kathitaścaturdhā ||
yaḥ pūjayedbrāhmaṇamāhitā'gniṃ devaḥ sa tenāpyatha pūjitaḥ syāt || ||7 ||
iti śrīviṣṇudharmottare tṛ0 kha0 mārkaṇḍeyavajrasaṃvāde saptamacaturmūrtikalpe agnivratavarṇano nāma tricatvāriṃśaduttaraśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ || 143 ||
<3.144>
mārkaṇḍeya uvāca ||
idamanyatpravakṣyāmi caturmūrtivrataṃ tava ||
caitraśuklasamārambhe prathamehni pūjayeta || 1 ||
kṛtaṃ śuklena sarveṇa gandhamālyādinā dvijaḥ ||
dvitīyehani raktena tathā tretāṃ tu pūjayet || 2 ||
tṛtīyehani pītena dvāparaṃ pūjayedbudhaḥ ||
caturthehani kṛṣṇena tiṣyaṃ saṃpūjayedyugam || 3 ||
siddhārthakaiḥ kuṃkumena tathaiva ca haridrayā ||
tathaivāmalakasnānamācareddivasakramāt || 4 ||
siddhārthakākṣatatilairghṛtena juhuyāttathā ||
muktāphalaṃ tathā vajraṃ padmarāgaṃ ca yādava || || 5 ||
indranīlaṃ tathā dadyādvipreṣu divasakramāt ||
kṣīreṇa prāṇayātrāṃ tu kuryātpratyahameva tu || 6 ||
kṛtvā vrataṃ vatsarametadekaṃ caturyugaṃ modati nākapṛṣṭhe ||
saṃpūjya devaṃ yugamūrtisaṃjñaṃ caturyugaṃ śāsti mahīṃ samagrām ||7||
iti śrīviṣṇudharmottare tṛ0 kha0 mākaṇḍeyavajrasaṃvāde aṣṭamacaturmūrti kalpe caturyugavratavarṇano nāma catuścatvāriṃduttaraśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ || 144 ||
<3.145>
mārkaṇḍeya uvāca ||
idamanyatpravakṣyāmi caturmūrtivrataṃ tava ||
nityaṃ caturṣu māseṣu śrāvaṇādyeṣu yādava || 1 ||
catuḥsāgaracihnāni pūrṇakumbhāni pūjayeta ||
caturātmā harirjñeyaḥ sāgarātmā vicakṣaṇaiḥ ||
snānaṃ samācarennityaṃ nadītoyeṣu yādava || 2 ||
homaṃ ca pratyahaṃ kuryād gavyena payasā tathā ||
tāni kumbhāni viprebhyaḥ pratyahaṃ vinivedayet || 3 ||
sopahārāṇi dharmajña bhaktyā śaktyā tathaiva ca ||
naktaṃ ca prāśanaṃ kuryyātsatataṃ tailavarjitam || 4 ||
kārtikasyāvasāne'hni bhojayitvā dvijottamān ||
dhenuṃ dattvā ca viprāya nākapṛṣṭhe mahīyate ||
sarvakāmasamṛddhasya yajñasya phalamaśnute ||5||
mānuṣyamāsādya mahīpatiḥ syādbhuktvā mahīṃ sāgaramekhalāntām ||
tatrāpi dharmasya patirniviṣṭo bhavatyarogaśca balena yuktaḥ || 6 ||
iti śrīviṣṇudharmottare tṛtīyakhaṇḍe mārkaṇḍeyavajrasaṃvāde viṣṇucaturmūrtikalpe sāgaravratavarṇano nāma pañcacatvāriṃśaduttaraśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ||145||
<3.146>
mārkaṇḍeya uvāca ||
idamanyatpravakṣyāmi caturmūrtivrataṃ tava ||
vāsudevasya garuḍastālaḥ saṅkarṣaṇasya ca||1||
pradyumnasya tathā cihnaṃ makaro vyāditānanaḥ||
devo'niruddho dharmajña ṛṣyaketuḥ prakīrtataḥ||2||
pītaṃ nīlaṃ tathā śvetaṃ raktaṃ ca yadunandana||
teṣāṃ tu kathitaṃ vāsaḥ patākā tādṛgiṣyate||3||
yasya devasya yaccihnaṃ sa cātmā nāma kīrtitaḥ ||
patākā yādṛśī yasya vasanaṃ tasya tādṛśam|| 4 ||
caitre tu pratyahaṃ māsi garuḍaṃ pūjayennaraḥ ||
pītena gaṃdhanaivedyamālyavastrādinā dvijaḥ||5||
vaiśākhe ca tathā māsi tālaṃ saṃpūjayetsadā ||
nīlena gaṃdhanaivedyamālyavastrādinā dvijaḥ||6||
ṛṣyaṃ saṃpūjayeddevaṃ māsyāṣāḍhe yathāvidhi ||
raktena gandhanaivedyamālyavastrādinā dvijaḥ||7|| |
bahiḥ snānaṃ tathā kuryādvahnisaṃpūjanaṃ tataḥ ||
nityaṃ ca kuryāddharmajña tathā brāhmaṇatarpaṇam || 8 ||
prāṇayātrā tathā kuryāduktaṃ tailavivarjitam ||
adhaḥśāyī tathā ca syād brahmacārī tathā bhavet || 9 ||
vratamettathā kuryānnityaṃ māsacatuṣṭayam ||
brāhmaṇānpūjayecchaktyā cāṣāḍhacaramehani || 3.146.10 ||
vastrāṇyarthāni dharmajña dadyādvipreṣu dakṣiṇām ||
kṛtvaikaṃ pāraṇaṃ rājansvargaloke mahīyate || 11 ||
dvitīyaṃ pāraṇaṃ kṛtvā śakraloke mahīyate ||
tṛtīyaṃ pāraṇaṃ kṛtvā brahmaloke mahīyate || 12 ||
kṛtvā pāraṇaṣaṭkaṃ tu rudraloke mahīyate ||
viṣṇulokamavāpnoti kṛtvā dvādaśapāraṇam || 13 ||
dhvajavrataṃ dvādaśavatsarāṇi kṛtvā naro bhārgavavaṃśamukhya ||
sāyujyamāyāti janārdanasya devasya viṣṇoḥ parameśvarasya || 14 ||
iti śrīviṣṇudharmottare tṛtīyakhaṇḍe mārkaṇḍeyavajrasaṃvāde caturmūrtikalpe dhvajavratavarṇano nāma ṣaṭcatvāriṃśaduttaraśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ | 146 ||
<3.147>
mārkaṇḍeya uvāca ||
idamanyatpravakṣyāmi caturmūrtivrataṃ tava ||
īśānaṃ ca tathā vahniṃ virūpākṣaṃ samīraṇam || 1||
vijñeyaṃ yaduśārdūla devamūrticatuṣṭayam ||
teṣāṃ tu rūpanirmāṇaṃ kṛtvā tānarcayedbudhaḥ || 2 || 
gandhamālyanamaskāradhūpadīpānnasaṃpadā ||
caitraśukle mahābhāgaṃ pratipatprabhṛti kramāt || 3 ||
bhaumanādeyatāḍākasārasaiḥ snānamācaret ||
dadhnā tilairyavairhomo ghṛtena ca tathā bhavet || 4 ||
karpūrakuṅkumaṃ caiva tathaivāgurucaṃdane ||
brāhmaṇeṣu pradātavyaṃ tathā rājandina kramāta || 5 ||
dinatrayaṃ tathāśnīyātsāyaṃprātarayācitam ||
dinamekaṃ tathāśnīyādbrahmacārī narottama || 6 ||
evaṃ saṃvatsaraṃ kṛtvā vrataṃ puruṣasattama ||
sarvakāmasamṛddhasya yajñasya phalamaśnute || 7 ||
mānuṣyamāsādya bhavatyarogo vasundhareśo vijitāripakṣaḥ ||
janmāntare syāddvijavaryamukhyo vedaprasakto bahuyajñayājī||8||
iti śrīṣṇudharmottare tṛ0 kha0 mārkaṇḍeyavajrasaṃvāde caturmūrtikalpe devamūrtivratavarṇanonāma saptacatvāriṃśaduttaraśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ|| 147||
<3.148>
mārkaṇḍeya uvāca ||
idamanyatpravakṣyāmi caturmūrtivrataṃ tava ||
śaṅkhacakragadāpadmaścaturātmā prakīrtitaḥ|| 1 ||
vāsudevaḥ smṛtaḥ śaṅkhaścakraṃ saṃkarṣaṇastathā ||
pradyumnaśca gadā padmamaniruddho jagadguruḥ||2||
śrāvaṇādiṣu māseṣu bahiḥ snānastu bhaktabhuk ||
teṣāṃ saṃpūjanaṃ kuryātpratimāsamataḥ kramāt||3||
gandhamālya namaskāradhūpadīpānnasaṃpadā ||
tatastu kārtikasyānte samāpte tu tathā vrate||4||
brāhmaṇānbhojayedbhaktyā dadyācchaktyā ca dakṣiṇām ||
kāṃsyapātraṃ ca saghṛtaṃ sasuvarṇaṃ tathaiva ca||5||
kṛtvā vrataṃ māsacatuṣṭayaṃ tu prāpnoti lokaṃ tridaśeśvarasya||
mānuṣyamāsādya tatheha paścādvasundhareśo bhavatīha vīraḥ ||6||
iti śrīviṣṇudharmottare tṛtīyakhaṇḍe mārkaṇḍeyavajrasaṃvāde caturmūrtivratakalpe āyudhavratavarṇano nāmāṣṭācatvāriṃśaduttaraśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ || 148 ||
<3.149>
mārkaṇḍeya uvāca ||
idamanyatpravakṣyāsi caturmūrtivrataṃ tava ||
trirātropoṣitaḥ samyagvasante viṣuvaddine || 1 ||
pūjāṃ kṛtvā yathāśakti vāsudevasya śatruhan ||
tato māsatrayaṃ nityaṃ vāsudevaṃ tu pūjayeta || 2 ||
tacca māsatrayaṃ tiṣṭhetphalāhāro vicakṣaṇaḥ ||
tataḥ śāradamāsādya dharmajñaṃ viṣuvaddinam || || 3 ||
trirātropoṣitaḥ snātvā pradyumnaṃ pūjayetpriyam ||
homaṃ kṛtvā tatośnīyāddattvā dānaṃ ca śaktitaḥ || 4 ||
prāṇayātrāṃ tu kurvīta yāvakena mahāmate||
vratāvasānamāsādya vāsantaṃ viṣuvaddinam ||
brāhmaṇaṃ bhojayecchaktyā dadyādvipreṣu dakṣiṇām ||5||
kāñcanaṃ rajataṃ vastraṃ gāṃ ca yādavanandana ||
etatsaṃvatsaraṃ kṛtvā vrataṃ nāma haripriyam || 6 ||
svargalokamavāpnoti bahukālamasaṃśayam ||
tato'śvilokānprāpnoti vasulokaṃ tato dvijaḥ || 7 ||
viśveṣāṃ caiva devānāṃ tathaivāṅgirasāṃ dvijaḥ ||
rudrāṇāmatha sādhyānāṃ bhṛgūṇāṃ savitustathā ||8 ||
marutāmapi dharmajña śiśirāṃśostathaiva ca ||
prāpnoti devalokaṃ ca kāmacārī vihaṅgamaḥ || 9 ||
prāpnoti lokaṃ sa hutāśanasya śakrasya devasya tathā harasya ||
lokaṃ samāsādya pitāmahasya tataḥ sa viṣṇoḥ puruṣaḥ prayāti || 3.149.10 ||
iti śrīviṣṇudharmottare tṛtīyakhaṇḍe mārkaṇḍeyavajrasaṃvāde caturmūrtivratakalpe phalāhāraharipriyavratavarṇano nāmaikonapañcāśaduttaraśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ || 149 ||
<3.150>
mārkaṇḍeya uvāca ||
idamanyatpravakṣyāmi caturmūrtivrataṃ tava ||
tasminneva vrate rājanmayā pūrvodite śubhe ||1||
viṣṇoḥ sthāne tvanantasya pūjā kāryā vijānatā ||
tasya saṃpūjanaṃ kṛtvā mahīgaganavedhasām || 2 ||
dvitīyādiṣu kurvīta yathāvadvijitendriyaḥ ||
anantaṃ pūjayedvidvānprathamehni yathāvidhi || 3 ||
vratametannaraḥ kṛtvā pūrṇaṃ saṃvatsaraṃ tataḥ||
viṣṇulokamavāpnoti yāvadindrāścaturdaśa || 4 ||
mānuṣyamāsādya bhavatyarogo jitendriyaḥ satyaparo vinītaḥ ||
dhanena rūpeṇa sukhena yukto janābhirāmaḥ pramadāpriyaśca || 5 ||
iti śrīviṣṇudharmottare tṛtīyakhaṇḍe mārkaṇḍeyavajrasaṃvāde caturmūrtikalpe'nantatavarṇano nāma pañcāśaduttaraśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ || 150 ||
<3.151>
mārkaṇḍeya uvāca ||
idamanyatpravakṣyāmi caturmūrtivrataṃ tava ||
vāsudevāṃśakājjātāḥ sarve devagaṇā nṛpa || 1 ||
adhikena tadaṃśena sādhyā jātāstathāsurāḥ ||
tatrāpi cādhikāṃśena caturātmā hariḥ smṛtaḥ || 2 ||
naro nārāyaṇaścaiva hayo haṃsaśca vīryavān ||
hayo haririti khyāto haṃsaḥ kṛṣṇaśca kīrtitaḥ || 3 ||
caturātmā hari rjāto gṛhe dharmasya yādava ||
ādityeṣu tathā yukto mitrāvaruṇasaṃjñakau || 4 ||
tāveva sādhyau jānīhi hariṃ kṛṣṇaṃ ca yādava ||
ādityeṣu tu yā yuktau śakraviṣṇū surottamau || 5 ||
tāveva siddhasādhyeṣu naranārāyaṇau punaḥ ||
caitraśuklacaturthyāṃ tu sopavāsastu pūjayet || 6 ||
deveśaṃ caturātmānaṃ vittaśaktyā narādhipa ||
vratametannaraḥ kṛtvā pūrṇaṃ dvādaśavatsaram||
na durgatimavāpnoti mokṣopāyaṃ ca vindati||7||
tataḥ samāsādya ca niṣkalatvaṃ pareṇa puṃsā ca samatvameti ||
sarveśvaraścāpratimaprabhāvo vimuktaduḥkho bhuvanasya goptā || 8 ||
iti śrīviṣṇudharmottare tṛtīyakhaṇḍe mārkaṇḍeyavajrasaṃvāde caturmūrtivrate śrīviṣṇuvratavarṇano nāmaikapañcāśaduttaraśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ ||151 ||
samāptaṃ caturmūrtivratam ||
<3.152>
mārkaṇḍeya uvāca ||
ataḥ paraṃ pravakṣyāmi pañcamūrtestathārcanam ||
pṛthivyāpastathā tejo vāyurākāśameva ca || 1 ||
vāsudevasya devasya kathitāḥ pañcamūrtayaḥ ||
caitre tu pañcamīṃ śuklāṃ samāsādya vicakṣaṇaḥ || 2 ||
sopavāso hariṃ devaṃ pañcātmānaṃ samarcayet ||
pañcamaṇḍalagāḥ kāryāḥ pañcabhirvaṇakaiḥ pṛthak || 3 ||
pārthivaṃ maṇḍalaṃ kāryaṃ śukla varṇaṃ mahīpate ||
vāruṇaṃ ca tathā śvetaṃ raktamāgneyamiṣyate || 4 ||
pītaṃ bhavati vāyavyaṃ kṛṣṇamākāśadaivatam ||
samānavarṇairgaṃdhaistu puṣpaistānarcayetpṛthak || 5 ||
śaktyā ca dhūpadīpādyairyathālābhamarindama ||
yavairvānyaistilaiścaiva sarṣapaiśca ghṛtena ca || 6 ||
pañcabhirjuhuyānmiśraiḥ sarveṣāṃ ca pṛthakpṛthak ||
talliṅgairatha mantrairvā tvatha vā nṛpa nāmabhiḥ || 7||
oṃkārapūrvakairhutvā śaktyā viprām̐stu pūjayet ||
evaṃ saṃvatsaraṃ kṛtvā pūrṇe saṃvatsare tataḥ || 8 ||
dattvā vipreṣu vastrāṇi devaraṅgasamāni ca ||
mahābhūtavratamidaṃ yaḥ karotyatha pañcakam || 9 ||
pañcayajñamavāpnoti kramaśo ye'nukīrtitāḥ ||
bahūnyabdasahasrāṇi svargaloke mahīyate || 3.152.10 ||
mānuṣyamāsādya bhavatyarogo balānvito vairigaṇāpahartā ||
śrutena rūpeṇa guṇena yukto janābhirāmaḥ pramadā priyaśca || 11 ||
iti śrīviṣṇudharmottare tṛtīyakhaṇḍe mārkaṇḍeyavajrasaṃvāde pañcamūrtipañcamahābhūtavratavarṇano nāma dvipañcāśaduttaraśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ || 152 ||
<3.153>
mārkaṇḍeya uvāca ||
idamanyatpravakṣyāmi pañcamūrtivrataṃ tava ||
saṃvatsaraḥ smṛto vahnistathārkaḥ parivatsaraḥ || 1 ||
iḍāpūrvastathā soma anupūrvaḥ prajāpatiḥ ||
tatpūrvaśca tathā prokto devadevo maheśvaraḥ || 2 ||
teṣāṃ maṇḍalavinyāsaiḥ prāgvadeva vidhīyate ||
yāgaśca pūjanaṃ kāryaṃ homaḥ kāryastathāvidhaḥ || 3 ||
tilavrīhiyavaiścaiva ghṛtena sitasarṣapaiḥ ||
talliṅgairatha vā mantrairnāmabhiḥ pratyahaṃ kramāt || 4 ||
naktāśanastathā tiṣṭhetprāgvaddivasapañcakam ||
saṃvatsarākhye varṣe tu vratametatsamārabhet || 5 ||
vratāvasāne dātavyaṃ suvarṇaṃ pañca yādava ||
caturvedavidāṃ deyaṃ śākhābhedena yādava || 6 ||
ekaikaṃ pañcamaṃ deyaṃ tathā kālavide bhavet ||
aśvamedhaphalaṃ hyetadvrataṃ tava mayeritam || 7 ||
yatheṣṭaṃ lokamāpnoti kāmacārī vihaṅgamaḥ ||
kṛtaṃ nānena dharmajña pūjyamānaḥ surāsuraiḥ|8||
mānuṣyamāsādya bhavatyarogo varṇena rūpeṇa balena yuktaḥ ||
nṛpaḥ pratāpānataśatrusaṃgho devottamo vā bahuyajñayājī || 9 ||
iti śrīviṣṇudharmottare tṛtīyakhaṇḍe mārkaṇḍeyavajrasaṃvāde pañcavratavidhau saṃvatsarapūjāyāmaśvamedhaphalavarṇano nāma triṣpañcāśaduttaraikaśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ || 153 ||
<3.154>
mārkaṇḍeya uvāca ||
athātaḥ saṃpravakṣyāmi śrīvrataṃ nāma te vratam ||
caitraśuklatṛtīyāyāṃ snānamabhyaṅgapūrvakam || 1 ||
kṛtvā śuklāmbaro rājañśuklamālyānulepanaḥ ||
tiṣṭhed ghṛtaudanāhāro bhūmau svapyācca tāṃ niśām||2||
caturthyāṃ ca tathā snānaṃ bahirgatvā samācareta||
padminyāṃ ca viśeṣeṇa tataḥ śuklāmbaraḥ śuciḥ || 3 ||
lakṣmīṃ sampūjayetpadme kṛtake'kṛtake'pi vā ||
śuklena gandhamālyena ghṛtadīpena cā pyatha || 4 ||
dadhnā ca paramānnena ārdrakeṇa guḍena ca ||
ikṣuṇekṣuvikāraiśca lavaṇena ca bhūriṇā || 5 ||
svaśaktyā ca mahārāja bhūyasā balika rmaṇā ||
śrīsūktena tato vahnau padmāni juhuyācchuciḥ || 6 ||
tadalābhe ca bilvāni tadalābhe tathā ghṛtam||
brāhmaṇāngorasaprāyaṃ ghṛtaṃ bhūyiṣṭhamāśayet| || 7 ||
suvarṇamāṣakaṃ dadyādbrāhmaṇebhyaśca dakṣiṇām ||
anāhārastataḥ svapyācchucau deśe yathāvidhi || 8 ||
tatastu pañcamīṃ prāpya pūrvāhṇe padminī jale ||
snātvā sampūjanaṃ kuryātprāgvadeva tathā priyaḥ || 9 ||
bhūya eva dvije dadyātpūrvaṃ kanakamāṣakam ||
padmākṣamatha vā bilvaṃ prāśnīyāttadanantaram| || 3.154.10 ||
tato haviṣyamaśnīyādvāgyato mānavottamaḥ ||
saṃvatsaramidaṃ bilvaṃ prāśnīyāttadanantaram ||
saṃvatsaramidaṃ kṛtvā vrataṃ pārthivasattama ||11||
phalamāpnoti vipulaṃ rājasūyāśvamedhayoḥ ||
vinā kanakadānena vratametatsamācaret || 12 ||
vratānte māṣakaṃ dadyādvahniṣṭomaphalaṃ labhet ||
sampūjya sopavāsastu śuklapakṣasya pañcamīm || 13 ||
nityameva śriyaṃ devīṃ śriyamāpnotyanuttamām ||
balamuttamamāpnoti rūpamārogyameva ca ||14 || |
jagatpradhānā varadā vareṇyā vibhāvarī sarvagatā narendra ||
śraddhānvitaḥ pūjayatīha yastu kāmānavāpnoti sa sarvakālam || 19 ||
iti śrīviṣṇudharmottare tṛtīyakhaṇḍe mārkaṇḍeyavajrasaṃvāde pañcamūrtividhau lakṣmīvratavarṇano nāma catuṣpañcāśaduttaraśatatamo'dhyāyu|| 154 ||
<3.155>
mārkaṇḍeya uvāca ||
athāparaṃ pravakṣyāmi pañcamūrtivrataṃ tava ||
śaṅkhaṃ cakraṃ gadāṃ padmaṃ pṛthivīṃ ca mahābhuja || 1 ||
gandhairmaṇḍalakānkṛtvā pañcapañcasu pūjayet ||
caitraśuklāntamārabhya pañcamīprabhṛtīrnṛpa||2||
sopavāso bahiḥ snātastathā śuklāmbaraḥ śuciḥ || 
gandhamālyanamaskāradhūpadīpānnasaṃpadā||3|||
sarveṣāṃ pūjanaṃ kṛtvā juhuyājjātavedasi ||
sarveṣāmeva devānāṃ nāmabhistu tathā ghṛtam || 4 ||
brāhmaṇānbhojayeccātra tadā madhurabhojanam |||
saṃvatsaramidaṃ kṛtvā vratānte vastra pañcakam || 5 ||
aśvamedhamavāpnoti pañcarātravide dadat ||
pañcavarṇaṃ ca rājendra rājasūyaphalaṃ labhet || 6 ||
mānuṣyamāsādya bhavatyarogo balānvito dharmaparo vinītaḥ ||
dhanena rūpeṇa sukhena yukto rājādhirājo'pyatha vā narendraḥ || 7 ||
iti śrīviṣṇudharmottare tṛtīyakhaṇḍe mārkaṇḍeyavajrasaṃvāde pañcamūrtivrate āyudhādivratanirūpaṇo nāma pañcapañcāśaduttaraśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ || 155 ||
<3.156>
mārkaṇḍeya uvāca ||
athātaḥ saṃpravakṣyāmi ṣaṇmūrterarcanaṃ tava ||
vasante pūjayennityaṃ dvau māsau madhumādhavau || 1 ||
phalaiḥ puṣpaiḥ kaṣāyaistu grīṣmegrīṣme ca pūjayet ||
madhureṇa mahārāja prāvṛṭkāle hite ca tam ||2 ||
amlena pūjayennityaṃ śaradaṃ lavaṇena ca||
baṭukena ca hemante tiktena śiśire tathā ||3||
naktāśanastathā tiṣṭhedṛtūktaṃ varjayedrasam ||
brāhmaṇānbhojayeccātra ṛtūktaṃ rasabhojanam|| || 4 ||
saṃvatsaramidaṃ kṛtvā vrataṃ paramapāvanam ||
aśvamedhamavāpnoti rājasūyaṃ ca vindati ||5||
sarvānkāmānavāpnoti nātra kāryā vicāraṇā ||
phalamakṣayyamāpnoti vratasyāsya narottama || 6 ||
caitrādathārabhya site ca ṣaṣṭhīṃ saṃpūjayedyo ṛtuṣaṭkamevam ||
kṛtopavāsastu yathoktametatphalaṃ labhetpūrṇamitīdamuktam || 7 ||
iti śrīviṣṇudharmottare tṛtīyakhaṇḍe mārkaṇḍeyavajrasaṃvāde ṣaṇmūrtivratavarṇano nāma ṣaṭpañcāśaduttaraśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ || 156||
<3.157>
mārkaṇḍeya uvāca ||
ataḥ paraṃ pravakṣyāmi saptamūrtivrataṃ tava ||
caitramāsādathārabhya pratipatprabhṛti kramāt || 1 |||
subhāsvarā varhiṣado hyagniṣvāttāstathaiva ca ||
kravyādānupahūtām̐śca ājyapāṃśca sukālinaḥ || 2 ||
pūjayetpratyahaṃ rājangandhamālyānulepanaiḥ ||
naivedyaṃ kṛsaraṃ kuryāttilānagnau ca homayet || 3 ||
kṛsaraṃ bhojayedviprāṃstilāndadyācca dakṣiṇām ||
naktāśanastathā tiṣṭheddhaviṣyāśī narādhipa ||4||
saṃvatsaramidaṃ kṛtvā vrataṃ puruṣasattama ||
vratāvasāne dadyācca rajatasya palaṃ dvije ||5||
vratenānena cīrṇena sūryalokagatirbhavet ||
tridaśaiḥ pūjyamānastu kāmacārī vihaṅgamaḥ || 6||
varṣaṃ samagraṃ puruṣastu kṛtvā saṃsāramokṣaṃ labhate narendra||
kṛtvā tathā dvādaśavatsarāṇi mānuṣyamāsādya mahīpatiḥ syāt ||7||
iti śrīviṣṇudharmottare tṛtīyakhaṇḍe mā0 saṃvāde subhāsurādipitṛvratavarṇano nāma saptapañcāśaduttaraśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ || 157 ||
<3.158>
mārkaṇḍeya uvāca ||
caitramāsādathārabhya kṛṣṇapakṣadine dine ||
pātālapūjanaṃ kuryātpratipatprabhṛti kramāt ||1||
rukmabhaumaṃ tathā bhaumaṃ pātālaṃ nīlamṛttikam ||
raktabhaumaṃ pītabhaumaṃ śvetakṛṣṇamṛdāvapi||2||
suvarṇairgandhamālyaiśca naivedyena ca bhūriṇā ||
ghṛtadīpapradānena vahnisantarpaṇena ca || 3 ||
evaṃ naktāśanaṃ kṛtvā vrataṃ saṃvatsaraṃ tadā ||
vratāvasāne dadyācca dīpakaṃ dvijaveśmasu || 4 ||
sa tu vastrāṇi rājendra yathāvarṇāni cāpyatha ||
vratametannaraḥ kṛtvā nṛṇāmadhipatirbhavet ||5||
patiṃ labhante nara devakanyāḥ śatasahasraśaḥ ||
ramayanti mahārāja yathaiveḍaviḍastathā||6||
kālena cāsādya manuṣyalokaṃ rājā bhavecchatrugaṇapramāthī||
balena rūpeṇa dhanena yukto mahāmatiḥ sarvajagatpradhānaḥ||7||
iti śrīviṣṇudha0 tṛ0 khaṇḍe mā0 saṃ0 pātālānāṃ vratavarṇano nāmāṣṭapañcāśadadhikaśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ || 158 ||
<3.159>
mārkaṇḍeya uvāca ||
athātaḥ saṃpravakṣyāmi saptadvīpavrataṃ śubham ||
caitraśuklādathārabhya pratyahaṃ dinasaptakam || 1 ||
jambuśākakuśakrauñcaśālmalidvīpasaṃjñitam ||
gomedaṃ puṣkaraṃ caiva pratyahaṃ pūjayetkramāt || 2||
nityameva tathā snānaṃ bahireva samācaret ||
ghṛtena homaṃ kurvīta saptasasyaṃ ca dāpayet || 3||
ekamannaṃ tathā naktaṃ sakṛdeva samācaret ||
adhaḥśāyī bhavennityaṃ taddeve divasatrayam ||4||
pūrṇe saṃvatsare dadyādrajatasya vinirmitam ||
palānāntu śatenaiva saṃsthānaṃ dvīpavatkṛtam || 5 ||
vratametannaraḥ kṛtvā pūrṇaṃ saṃvatsaraṃ śuciḥ ||
svargalokamavāpnoti yāvadābhūtasaṃplavam || 6 ||
mānuṣyamāsādya samastadvīpānbhunakti bhūmiṃ vijitāripakṣaḥ ||
saṃpūjyamānastridaśaiḥ sadaiva maharṣibhirbrāhmaṇapuṅgavaiśca || 7 ||
iti śrīviṣṇudharmottare tṛ0 kha0 mārkaṇḍeyavajrasaṃvāde jambūdvīpādisaptadvīpavratavarṇano nāmaikonaṣaṣṭyuttaraśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ||159||
<3.160>
mārkaṇḍeya uvāca ||
athātaḥ saṃpravakṣyāmi samudravratameva te ||
caitraśuklādathārabhya pratyahaṃ divasatrayam || 1 ||
lavaṇakṣīraājyaughadadhimaṇḍasurodakam ||
tathaivekṣusurodaṃ ca svādūdaṃ caiva pūjayet || 2 ||
ācaretpratyahaṃ snānaṃ nityameva gṛhādbahiḥ ||
ghṛtena homaṃ kurvīta saptasasyaṃ ca dāpayet || 3 ||
haviṣyāśī bhavennaktaṃ kuryātsaṃvatsaraṃ vratam ||
saṃvatsarānte dadyācca tathā dhenuṃ payasvinīm || 4 ||
vratenānena cīrṇena saptasāgaramekhalām ||
bhunakti vasudhāṃ rājā saptajanmāntarāṇi ca || 5 ||
ārogyakāmaḥ kurvīta vratametattathottamam ||
dharmakāmo yaśaḥkāmaḥ śrīkāmaśca tathāparaḥ || 6 ||
maṅgalyametatparamaṃ pavitraṃ śrīvardhanaṃ dharmavivṛddhikāri ||
kartavyametatprayatairmanuṣyairye rājyakāmāstu viśeṣatastaiḥ ||7||
iti śrīviṣṇudharmottare tṛ0 kha0 mārkaṇḍeyavajrasaṃvāde lavaṇasamudrādi saptasamudravratavarṇano nāma ṣaṣṭyuttaraśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ || 160 ||
<3.161>
mārkaṇḍeya uvāca ||
athātaḥ saṃpravakṣyāmi tava śailavrataṃ śubham ||
mahendro malayaḥ sahyaḥ śuktimānṛkṣavānapi || 1 ||
vindhyaśca pāriyātraśca saptaite kulaparvatāḥ ||
caitraśuklasamārambhātpratyahaṃ dinasaptakam||2||
teṣāṃ saṃpūjanaṃ kuryādbahiḥ snānaṃ samācaret ||
gandhamālyanamaskāradhūpadīpānnasaṃpadā || 3 ||
yavairhomastathā kāryo dadyādvipreṣu dakṣiṇām ||
naktaṃ yavānnamaśnīyātkuryātsaṃvatsaraṃ vratam || 4||
vratāvasāne dadyācca yavasthālīśca viṃśatim ||
vācakāya dvijendrāya suvarṇaṃ gāṃ ca tasya tu|| 5 ||
dratenānena cīrṇena sarvāṃ sāgaramekhalām ||
bhunakti vasudhāṃ rājā vaśīkṛtvā ripūnnṛpa || 6 ||
bhogāṃśca bhuktvā tridive ca rājanmānuṣyamāsādya yathoktametat ||
prāpnoti sarvaṃ hi mayeritaṃ yajjanmāntarāṇyeva narendra sapta || 7 ||
iti śrīviṣṇudharmottare tṛ0 kha0 mārkaṇḍeyavajrasaṃvāde saptaśailavratavarṇano nāmaikaṣaṣṭyuttaraśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ || 161 ||
<3.162>
mārkaṇḍeya uvāca ||
ataḥ paraṃ pravakṣyāmi tava lokavrataṃ śubham ||
yāstu vyāhatayaḥ sapta trailokyāṃ tu prakīrtitāḥ || || 1 ||
ācaretpratyahaṃ snānaṃ bahirnityamatandritaḥ ||
caitraśuklādathārabhya krameṇa dinasaptakam || 2 ||
gomūtraṃ gomayaṃ kṣīraṃ dadhi sarpiḥ kuśodakam ||
ekarātropavāsaśca krameṇaivaṃ samācaret || 3 ||
mahāvyāhatibhirhomastilaiḥ kāryo dinedine ||
saṃvatsarānte dadyācca tathā vipreṣu dakṣiṇām || 4 ||
suvarṇamahataṃ vāsaḥ kāṃsyaṃ dhenuṃ payasvinīm ||
saṃvatsaramidaṃ kṛtvā vrataṃ puruṣasattama|| 5 ||
sarva lokacaraḥ śrīmānsvecchayā syānnarādhipa ||
yāvatkalpāvasānaṃ tu kalpādau pārthivo bhavet || 6 ||
sa cakravartī nṛpavaryapūjyaḥ surāsurāṇāmadhikaprabhāvaḥ ||
saṃvatsarāṇāmayutaṃ śatānāṃ sampāti pṛthvīṃ sakalābhirāmaḥ || 7 ||
iti śrīviṣṇudharmottare tṛtīyakhaṇḍe mārkaṇḍeyavajrasaṃvāde saptavyāhṛti saptalokavratavarṇano nāma dvāṣaṣṭyuttaraśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ || 162 ||
<3.163>
|| mārkaṇḍeya uvāca || ||
caitraśuklādathārabhya pratyahaṃ dinasaptakam ||
hlādinīṃ hrādinīṃ caiva pāvanīṃ caiva pūjayet || 1 ||
sītāṃ rāmaṃ tathā sindhuṃ tathā bhāgīrathīṃ kramāt ||
bahiḥsnānaṃ tataḥ kuryānnityaṃ naktāśano bhavet || 2 ||
jale tu juhuyātkṣīraṃ tāsāṃ nāmnā dinedine ||
kṣīrapūrṇā ca dātavyā vāridhānyo dvijātiṣu || 3 ||
kṣīrāśanastu tiṣṭhettu tattathā dinasaptakam ||
evaṃ saṃvatsaraṃ kṛtvā pūrṇe saṃvatsare naraḥ || 4||
dvijātiṣu tato dadyādrajatasya palaṃpalam ||
phālgunasya site pakṣe saptamyāṃ divasa kramāt || 5 ||
tallokamāpnoti naro yatra pāyasakardamam ||
nadyaḥ kṣīravahā divyāḥ sarvakāmaphalapradāḥ||6||
tatroṣya kālaṃ suciraṃ mahātmā mānuṣyamāsādya bhavatyarogaḥ||
rūpeṇa śīlena dhanena yukto rājātha vā brāhmaṇapuṅgavaśca||7||
iti śrīviṣṇudharmettare tṛtīyakhaṇḍe mārkaṇḍeyavajrasaṃvāde nadīvratavarṇanonāma triṣaṣṭyuttaraśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ || 163 ||
<3.164>
|| mārkaṇḍeya uvāca || ||
caitraśuklādathārabhya pratyahaṃ dinasaptakam ||
suprabhāṃ kāñcanākṣīṃ ca viśālāṃ mānasaṃ hradam || 1 ||
tāṃ trinādāṃ suveṇuṃ ca tathaiva vimalodakām ||
nityaṃ saṃpūjayedbhaktyā bahiḥ snānaṃ samācaret || 2 ||
tāsāṃ ca pratyahaṃ nāmnā dadhnā homaṃ samācaret ||
brāhmaṇānbhojayeccātra dadhnā miśraṃ ca bhojanam || 3 ||
ghṛtaudanaṃ tathāśnīyātsakṛdeva tathā niśi ||
evaṃ saṃvatsaraṃ kṛtvā pūrṇe saṃvatsare naraḥ || 4 ||
phālgunāmalapakṣe tu pratyahaṃ divasakramāt ||
vastrāṇāṃ saptakaṃ dadyāttīrthasnāte dvijottame || 5 ||
evaṃ saṃvatsaraṃ kṛtvā vrataṃ sārasvataṃ japet ||
tatroṣya suciraṃ kālaṃ manuṣyo jāyate tadā || 6 ||
tadā narendro vijitāripakṣo dvijottamo vā bahu yajñayājī ||
rūpeṇa śīlena dhanena yuktaḥ śrutānvitaḥ syācca janābhirāmaḥ ||7||
iti śrīviṣṇudharmottare tṛtīyakhaṇḍe mārkaṇḍeyavajrasaṃvāde sārasvata vratavarṇanonāma catuḥṣaṣṭyuttaraśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ || 164 ||
<3.165>
|| mārkaṇḍeya uvāca || ||
caitraśuklādathārabhya pratyahaṃ dinasaptakam ||
marīcimatryaṅgirasau pulastyaṃ pulahaṃ kratum || 1 ||
vasiṣṭhaṃ ca mahābhāga pūjayeddivasakramāt ||
kālodbhavaiḥ phalaiḥ puṣpairgorasaiśca pṛthakpṛthak || 2 ||
ācaretpratyahaṃ snānaṃ bahirnaktāśano bhavet ||
mahāvyāhṛtibhirhomaṃ phalairmūlaiḥ sagorasaiḥ || 3 ||
vāridhānyaśca dātavyāḥ kṣīrapūrṇā dvijātiṣu ||
evaṃ saṃvatsaraṃ kṛtvā vratānte cāhitāgnaye ||4||
dadyātkṛṣṇājinaṃ rājanyathāpūrvaṃ mayeritam ||
vratametannaraḥ kṛtvā mokṣopāyaṃ ca vindati || 5 ||
mokṣopāyaṃ samāsādya mokṣaṃ prāpnotyasaṃśayam ||6||
prāpnoti lokaṃ yadi vā munīnāṃ devasya viṣṇoryadi veśvarasya ||
pitāmahasyāpratimasya vāpi vratena cīrṇena mahānubhāva || 7 ||
iti śrīviṣṇudharmottare tṛtīyakhaṇḍe mārkaṇḍeyavajrasaṃvāde saptarṣivratavarṇano nāma pañcaṣaṣṭyuttaraśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ ||165|| ||
<3.166>
|| mārkaṇḍeya uvāca || ||
caitraśuklasya pakṣe tu samyak ṣaṣṭhyāmupoṣitaḥ ||
saptamyāmarcanaṃ kuryānmarutāṃ pārthivottama || 1 ||
saptaśreṇīgataṃ saptamaṇḍalaṃ nṛpa kārayet ||
śreṇīśreṇī tathā kāryā saptamaṇḍalakānvitā || 2 ||
gandhairmaṇḍalakāḥ kāryā nāmacihnāḥ pṛthakpṛthak ||
ekajyotiśca dvijyotistrijyotiśca mahābala || 3 ||
ekadvitricatuḥśaktikrameṇa ca tathā nṛpa ||
vinyasetprathamaṃ śreṇyāṃ yathāvannṛpasattama || 4 ||
indraśca gatyā dṛśyaśca tataḥ pratiśakṛttathā ||
mitaśca samitaścaiva amitaśca mahābalaḥ || 5 ||
dvitīyāyāṃ tathā śreṇyāṃ devānetāṃśca vinyaset ||
ṛtajitsatyajiccaiva suṣeṇaścaiva vīryavān || 6 ||
atimitronamitraśca purumitrastathaiva ca ||
tṛtīyāyāṃ tathā śreṇyāṃ devānetāṃśca vinyaset || 7 ||
ṛtaśca ṛtavāndhartā vidhartā varuṇo dhruvaḥ ||
vidhāraṇaṃ caturthyāṃ tu śreṇyā pārthiva vinyaset || 8 ||
īdṛkṣaśca sadṛkṣaśca etādṛgamitāśanaḥ ||
kīrtitaḥ prasadṛkṣaśca śarabhaśca mahāyaśāḥ || 9 ||
vinyasetpañcamaśreṇyāṃ saptadevānnarādhipa ||
dhartā dugno dhvanirbhīmo atiyuktaḥ kṣamāsahaḥ || 3.166.10 ||
ṣaṣṭhyāṃ ca vinyasecchreṇyāṃ saptadevānyathākramam ||
dyutirvasuranādhṛṣyo vāsaḥ kāmo jayo virāṭ || 11 ||
saptamyāṃ ca tathā śreṇyāṃ vinyasetsapta pārthivān ||
prathamā tu bhavecchreṇī śuklā pārthivasattama || 12 ||
dvitīyā padmapatrābhā tṛtīyā rudhiropamā ||
pītavarṇā caturthī syātpañcamā śukasannibhā || 13 ||
ākāśasannibhā ṣaṣṭhī kṛṣṇavarṇā ca saptamī ||
mālyānulepanaṃ deyaṃ tāsāṃ varṇasamaṃ nṛpa || 14 ||
ekonāstatra dātavyā dīpāḥ pañcāśadeva tu ||
pṛthakpṛthaktu devānāṃ naivedyaṃ vinivedayet || 15 ||
ghṛtaṃ ca juhuyādvahnau nāmabhiśca pṛthakpṛthak ||
bhojayedbrāhmaṇām̐ścātra surasaṃkhyā samanvitān || 16 ||
saṃvatsaramidaṃ kṛtvā vrataṃ puruṣasattama ||
suvarṇasahitaṃ vāso gāṃ ca dadyāttathaiva ca || 17 ||
paurāṇikāya viprāya vratānte vinivedayet ||
vratasyāsya tu rājendra samyagvipro vidhānavit || 18 ||
ārogyakāmaḥ kurvīta vratametannarottama ||
putrakāmorthakāmaśca dhanakāmotha vā punaḥ || 19 ||
sarvakāmapradaṃ hyetatpavitraṃ pāpanāśanam ||
maṅgalyaṃ svargadaṃ proktaṃ vratānāmuttamaṃ vratam ||3.166.20 ||
vratenānena cīrṇena ciraṃ svargaṃ samaśnute ||
mānuṣyamāsādya bhavetsvargabhraṣṭastathācirāt || 21 ||
dhanena rūpeṇa balena yukto janābhirāmaḥ pramadāpriyaśca ||
virogadehaḥ kṣataśatrupakṣo vāgmī tathā śāstradharaśca loke || 22 ||
iti śrīvi0 dha0 tṛ0kha0 mā0 va0 saṃ0 marudvratavarṇano nāma ṣaṭṣaṣṭyuttaraśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ || 166 ||
<3.167>
|| mārkaṇḍeya uvāca ||
caitraśuklasya pakṣe tu samyak ṣaṣṭhyāmupoṣitaḥ ||
saptamyāmarcanaṃ kuryāddevadevasya gopateḥ || 1 ||
bahiḥ snānaṃ naraḥ kṛtvā gomayenopalepitam ||
lepayetsthaṇḍilaṃ samyakkuto gauramṛdā nṛpa || 2 ||
tatrāṣṭapatraṃ kamalaṃ varṇakaistu samālikhet ||
karṇikāyāṃ nyasettatra devadevaṃ vibhāvasum || 3 ||
pūrvapakṣe nyaseddevo dvau rājannuttare dale ||
āgneye ca nyasetpatre gandharvau ṛtukārikau || 4||
dakṣiṇe ca nyasetpatre tathaivāpsarasordvayam ||
rākṣasau dvau mahārāja patre nairṛtake nyaseta || 5 ||
kādraveyau mahānāgau paścime ṛtavārikau ||
vāyavye yātudhānau dvau tathaiva nṛpasattama || 6 ||
uttare ca tathā patre vinyasecca ṛṣidvayam ||
aiśānye vinyasetpatre grahamekaṃ narottama || 7 ||
teṣāṃ saṃpūjanaṃ kāryaṃ gandhamālyānulepanaiḥ ||
dhūpairdīpaiśca naivedyaiḥ pṛthakpṛthagarindama || 8 ||
evaṃ sampūjanaṃ kṛtvā sarveṣāṃ tadanantaram ||
ghṛtena homaṃ kurvīta sūryasyāṣṭaśatena ca || 9 ||
anyeṣāṃ va tathā dadyādaṣṭāvaṣṭau nareśvara ||
nāmnā tathaiva sarveṣāṃ caikaikaṃ bhojayeddvijam || 3.167.10 ||
śaktyā ca dakṣiṇā deyā teṣāmeva yadūttama ||
evaṃ saṃvatsaraṃ kṛtvā vratametannarottama || 11 ||
paurāṇikāya viprāya vratasyānte payasvinīm ||
vidhivacca tato dadyātsuvarṇaṃ ca yadūttama ||12||
sarvakāmapradaṃ caiva vratamuktaṃ svayambhuvā ||
vratenānena cīrṇena sūryalokamavāpnuyāt ||13||
atha dvādaśavarṣāṇi karotyetanmahāvratam ||
bhittvārkamaṇḍalaṃ rājā viṣṇoḥ sāyujyatāṃ vrajet ||14||
etadvrataṃ pāpavināśakāri dhanyaṃ yaśasyaṃ ripunāśakāri||
loke tathāsmiṃśca pare ca rājansvargapradaṃ mokṣakaraṃ tathaiva||15||
iti śrīviṣṇudharmottare tṛ0kha0 mā0saṃvāde sūryavratavarṇano nāma saptaṣaṣṭyuttaraśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ || 167 ||
<3.168>
vajra uvāca ||
yasminyasminkratau brahmannanuyānti raviṃ prabhum ||
yeye devaprabhṛtayastanme tvaṃ vaktumarhasi || 1 ||
mārkaṇḍeya uvāca ||
dhātāryamā ca rājendra vasante devatādvayam ||
tumbururnāradaścaiva gandharvau gāyanau varau || 2 ||
kṛtasthalāpsarāścaiva tathā yā puñjikasthalā ||
hotā prahotā ca tathā rakṣoghnau maṇipuṅgavau ||3||
urago vāsukiścaiva vāṣṭakarṇī ca tāvubhau ||
rathakṛtsnā rathaujāśca yātudhānavarau tathā || 4 ||
mitraśca pulahaścaiva tathaivamṛṣisattamau ||
anuyāti sitaścaiva vasante ca yathā grahaḥ || 5 ||
yenuyānti raviṃ devaṃ nidāghe tānnibodhase ||
mitraśca varuṇaścaiva grīṣme devau vasanti ca || 6 ||
ṛṣiratrirvasiṣṭhaśca nāgau raṃbhakatakṣakau ||
menakā sahajanyā ca gandharvau ca hahāhuhū || 7 ||
rathasvanapramāṇaśca rathakṛtsaśca tāvubhau ||
pauruṣādo vadhaścaiva yātudhānau tu tau smṛtau || 8 ||
anuyāti kujaścaiva nidāghe ca tathā grahaḥ ||
yenuyānti raviṃ devaṃ prāvṛṭkāle nibodha me || 9 ||
indraścaiva vivasvāṃśca aṅgirā bhṛgureva ca ||
elapatrastathā sarpaḥ śaṅkhapālaśca pannagaḥ || 3.168.10 ||
śucisenograsenau ca vrātaścaivāruṇaśca vai ||
pramlocetyapsarāścaiva nimlocantī ca te ubhe || 11 ||
yātudhānastathā sarpo vyāghraśca manujeśvara ||
prāvṛṭkāle tu yātyenaṃ graho devapurohitaḥ || 12 ||
ataḥ paraṃ nibodhasva śaratkāle narādhipa ||
parjanyaścaiva pūṣā ca bharadvājaśca gautamaḥ || 13 ||
citrasenaśca gaṃdharvastathā vasuruciśca vai ||
viśvācī ca ghṛtācī ca tathā caivāpsarodvayam || 14 ||
nāgaścairāvataścaiva viśrutaśca dhanañjayaḥ ||
senajicca suṣeṇaśca rākṣasau bhīmavikramau || 15 ||
vātodvātau ca tathā yātudhānau mahābalau ||
grahaḥ śanaiścaraścaivamanuyānti divākaram || 16 ||
ataḥ paraṃ pravakṣyāmi hemante tava pārthiva ||
aṃśo bhagaśca dvāvetau kaśyapaśca kratuśca vai || 17 ||
bhujagaśca mahāpadmaḥ sarpaḥ karkoṭakastathā ||
citrasenaśca gandharva ūrṇāyuśca mahābalaḥ || 18 ||
apsarāḥ pūrvacittiśca gandharvī urvaśī tathā ||
tārkṣyaścāriṣṭanemiśca rākṣasau bhīmavikramau|| 19 ||
vidyutsphūrjastathaivograu yātudhānau mahābalau ||
anuyāti grahaścaiva budho rājandivākaram ||3.168.20||
ataḥparaṃ ca dharmajña śiśire gadataḥ śṛṇu||
tvaṣṭā viṣṇurjamadagnirviśvāmitrastathaiva ca||21||
kādraveyau tathā nāgau kaṃbalāśvatarāvubhau||
tilottamāpsarāścaiva devī rambhā manoramā||22||
kāraṇī śitiśaścaiva satyajicca mahāyaśāḥ||
brahmopetaśca rakṣo vai yajñopetastathaiva ca ||23||
gandharvo dhṛtarāṣṭraśca sūryavarcāstathāparaḥ||
candramā graharājaśca anuyāti divākaram||24||
evaṃ hi śiśire rājannanuyānaṃ prakurvate ||
sthānābhimānino hyete saptadvādaśakā gaṇāḥ || 25 ||
gaṇaṣaṭkastathaivaikamanuyāti divākaram ||
sūryamāpyāyayantyete tejasā teja uttamam || 26 ||
grathitaistavacobhiśca kurvanti ṛṣayastavam ||
gandharvāpsarasaścaiva gītairnṛtyairupāsate || 27 ||
vidyāgrāmaṇinaścaiva kurvantyatrābhiṣugraham ||
sarpā vahanti vai sūryaṃ yātudhāno'nuyāti ca || 28 ||
parivārya grahaścaiva nayatyastaṃ yathāvidhi ||
eteṣāmeva devānāṃ yathāvīryaṃ yathātapaḥ || 29 ||
yathāsatvamasau sūryasteṣāṃ siddhastu tejasā ||
bhūtānāmaśubhaṃ karma vināśayati tejasā || 3.168.30 ||
grīṣme hime ca varṣāsu janayansa divākaraḥ ||
dharmaṃ himaṃ ca varṣaṃ ca dinaṃ ca niśayā saha || 31 ||
iti śrīviṣṇudharmo0 tṛ0 kha0 mārkaṇḍeyavajrasaṃvāde ādityānucaravarṇano nāma aṣṭaṣaṣṭyuttaraśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ || 168 ||
<3.169>
mārkaṇḍeya uvāca ||
caitramāsasya saptamyāṃ śuklapakṣe narādhipa ||
gomayenopalipte tu mṛdā kuryāttu maṇḍalam || 1 ||
tatrāṣṭapatraṃ kamalaṃ kartavyaṃ varṇakaiḥ śubhaiḥ ||
kṛtopavāsastanmadhya bhāskaraṃ pūjayennaraḥ || 2 ||
aruṇaṃ caiva dantākaṃ māṭharaṃ ca tathā damam ||
yamunāṃ ca yamaṃ kālaṃ dvitīyaṃ manumeva ca || 3 ||
śanaiścaraṃ tathā rājñīṃ chāyāṃ revantameva ca ||
saptacchandāṃsi varṣaṃ ca dyāṃ ca piṅgalameva ca || 4 ||
keśareṣu yajedvātha patreṣūktāśca devatāḥ ||
dikkālapūjanaṃ kāryaṃ bahiḥ padmasya pārthiva || 5 || 
gandhamālyanamaskāradhūpadīpānnasampadā ||
vrate samāpte dātavyasturago brāhmaṇāya ca || 6 ||
prāpyāśvamedhasya phalaṃ yathāvallokānavāpyātha purandarasya ||
tatroṣya rājansuciraṃ ca kālaṃ sāyujyamāyāti divākarasya || 7 ||
iti śrīviṣṇudharmottare tṛtīyakhaṇḍe mārkaṇḍeyavajrasaṃvāde saptavratakalpe saptamīpūjāpraśaṃsā nāmaikonasaptatyuttaraśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ || 169 ||

|| mārkaṇḍeya uvāca || ||

iti śrīviṣṇudharmottare tṛtīyakhaṇḍe mārkaṇḍeyavajrasaṃvāde raktasaptamīvratavarṇano nāma saptatyuttaraśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ||170||
<3.171>
|| mārkaṇḍeya uvāca || ||

iti śrīviṣṇudharmottare tṛtīyakhaṇḍe mārkaṇḍeyavajrasaṃvāde sūryapūjāpraśaṃsāvarṇano nāmaikasaptatyuttaraśatatamo 'dhyāyaḥ || 171 ||

|| mārkaṇḍeya uvāca || ||
