<3.174>
mārkaṇḍeya uvāca ||
himavānhemakūṭaśca niṣadho nīla eva ca ||
śvetaśca śṛṅgavānmerurmālyavāngandhamādanaḥ || 1 ||
navaitāñśailanṛpatīnnavamyāṃ pūjayennaraḥ ||
uttarāḥ kuravaḥ puṇyā ramyo hairaṇvatastathā || 2 ||
bhadrāśvaḥ ketumālaśca tathaiva ca ilāvṛtaḥ ||
harivarṣaḥ kiṃpuruṣo varṣo bhārata eva ca || 3 ||
pūjanīyāstathaivaite yathā saṃsthānato nṛpa ||
śuklapakṣanavamyāṃ tu caitrādārabhya pārthiva || 4 ||
sopavāsena dharmajña gandhamālyānnasaṃpadā ||
jambūdvīpasya saṃsthānaṃ vratānte rajataṃ naraḥ || 5 ||
tulāpramāṇaṃ dadyāttu sarvānkāmānupāśnute ||
aśvamedhamavāpnoti svargalokaṃ ca gacchati || 6 ||
mānuṣyamāsādya tu cakravartī nṛpo bhaveddevagaṇābhirāmaḥ ||
ciraṃ ca kālaṃ vasudhāṃ praśāsya lokānavāpnoti tatopi kāmān || 7 ||
iti śrīviṣṇudharmottare tṛtīyakhaṇḍe mārkaṇḍeyavajrasaṃvāde parvatāṣṭamīvratavarṇano nāma catussaptatyuttaraśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ || 174 ||
<3.175>
mārkaṇḍeya uvāca ||
navamyāṃ sopavāsastu bhadrakālīṃ tu pūjayet||
śuklapakṣe mahārāja kārtikaprabhṛti kramāt||1||
gandhamālyanamaskāradhūpadīpānnasaṃpadā||
saṃvatsarānte saṃpūjya vratānte brāhmaṇāya ca || 2 ||
vastrayugmaṃ naro dattvā yatheṣṭaṃ kāmamāpnuyāt ||
rogārto mucyate rogādbaddho mucyeta bandhanāt || 3 ||
rājakāryābhiyuktaśca mucyate mahato bhayāt ||
na caurebhyo bhayaṃ tasya nāraṇyebhyaḥ kadācana || 4 ||
putrānavāpnoti dhanaṃ yatheṣṭaṃ striyaśca mukhyā vividhaṃ ca kupyam ||
pūjāṃ tu kṛtvā vidhivadbhavānyā kāmānavāpnoti tathā yatheṣṭān || 5 ||
iti śrīviṣṇudharmottare tṛtīyakhaṇḍe mārkaṇḍeyavajrasaṃvāde bhadrakālīpūjāvarṇano nāma pañcasaptatyuttaraśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ || 175 ||
<3.176>
mārkaṇḍeya uvāca ||
kraturdakṣo ripuḥ satyaḥ kālakāmau dhvanistathā ||
kulavānmanujo viśvo rocamānaśca te daśa || 1 ||
viśvedevā samākhyātā daśātmā keśavo vibhuḥ ||
tasya saṃpūjanaṃ kāryaṃ sitapakṣe narādhipa || 2 ||
ārabhya kārtikānmāsādṛśamyāṃ nṛpapuṅgava ||
maṇḍaleṣu ca mukhyeṣu yadi vārcāsu yādava || 3 || 
gandhamālyanamaskāradhūpadīpānnasaṃpadā ||
vratānte kanakaṃ dadyādyathāśakti dvijātaye || 4 ||
kṛtvā vrataṃ vatsarametadiṣṭaṃ prāpnoti teṣāṃ suciraṃ ca lokān ||
tatroṣya loke puruṣatvametya rājā bhavedbrāhmaṇapuṅgavo vā || 5 ||
iti śrīviṣṇudharmottare tṛtīyakhaṇḍe mārkaṇḍeyavajrasaṃvāde viśvedevadaśamīpūjāvarṇano nāma ṣaṭsaptatyuttaraśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ ||176||
<3.177>
mārkaṇḍeya uvāca||
ātmā hyāyurmano dakṣo madaḥ prāṇastathaiva ca ||
haviṣmāṃśca gatīṣṭaśca ṛtaḥ satyaśca te daśa ||1||
devastvaṅgiraso nāma daśamyāṃ pūjayennaraḥ||
sopavāsaḥ kṛṣṇapakṣe pūrvoktavidhinā naraḥ ||2 ||
kṛtvā vrataṃ vatsarametadiṣṭānprāpnoti teṣāṃ suciraṃ ca lokān ||
tatroṣya loke puruṣatvametya rājā bhavedbrāhmaṇa puṅgavo vā||3||
iti śrīviṣṇudharmottare tṛtīyakhaṇḍe mā0 vajrasaṃvāde aṅgirāvratavarṇano nāma saptasaptatyuttaraśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ||177||
<3.178>
mārkaṇḍeya uvāca ||
śuklapakṣe daśamyāṃ tu sopavāsastathā naraḥ ||
mārgaśīrṣādathārabhya nityametadarindama || 1 ||
gandhamālyanamaskāradhūpadīpānnasaṃpadā ||
ghṛtena juhuyādvahniṃ brāhmaṇām̐ścātra ca pūjayet||2|
vratāvasāne dadyācca tathā dhenuṃ payasvinīm||
vratametadvinirdiṣṭamaśvamedhaphalapradam|| 3||
kṛṣṇapakṣe tathāpyetatkṛtvā saṃvatsaraṃ vratam||
rājasūyamavāpnoti phalamuddharati svakam||4||
kṛtvā pakṣadvayepyetadvrataṃ manujasattama||
rājasūyāśvamedhānāṃ phalaṃ prāpnotyasaṃśayam|5|
svargalokamavāpnoti kulamuddharati svakam||
dharme matirbhavettasya dharmamāpnotyasaṃśayam|6||
yatrayatrābhijāyeta tatra dharmaparo bhavet||
pradhānaṃ taṃ tayā rājanyatra dharmaparo bhavet ||7||
ārogyamāyuṣyakaraṃ yaśasyaṃ sthānapradaṃ pāpavināśakāri ||
kartavyametatpuruṣairyathāvatpūjyo hi viṣṇurbhagavānsadharmaḥ ||8||
iti śrīviṣṇudharmottare tṛtīyakhaṇḍe mārkaṇḍeyavajrasaṃvāde dharmavratavarṇano nāmāṣṭasaptatyuttaraśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ ||178||
<3.179>
mārkaṇḍeya uvāca || aṅgārakaṃ tathā sūryaṃ nirṛtiṃ vādyaśastathā ||
havanaṃ ceśvaraṃ mṛtyuṃ kapālimatha kiṃkiṇam || 1 ||
tatra caikādaśāṃśaistu devāṃstribhuvaneśvarān ||
ekādaśyāṃ sopavāsaḥ somaṃ saṃpūjayettathā || 2 || gandhamālyanamaskāradhūpadīpānnasampadā ||
mārgaśīrṣādathārabhya yāvatsaṃvatsaraṃ bhavet || 3 ||
saṃvatsarānte dadyācca tathā dhenuṃ payasvinīm ||
kṛtvā vrataṃ vatsarametadiṣṭaṃ bhadratvamāpnoti narastu rājan ||
rudreṇa sārdha suciraṃ ramitvā kāmānavāpyātha manobhirāmān || 4 ||
iti śrīviṣṇudharmottare tṛ0 kha0 mā0va0saṃ0 rudravratavarṇano nāmaikonāśītyuttaraśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ||179||
<3.180>
mārkaṇḍeya uvāca ||
bhuvano bhānavaścaiva sujanyaḥ sujanastathā ||
kratuḥ sarvaśca mūrdhā ca tyājaśca vyamnakastathā || 1 ||
prasavaścāpyayaścaiva dakṣo dvādaśakastathā ||
bhṛgavo nāma nirdiṣṭā devā dvādaśa yājñiyāḥ || 2 ||
teṣāṃ sampūjanaṃ kuryāddvādaśyāṃ nityameva tu ||
mārgaśīrṣādathārabhya kṛṣṇapakṣe sadaiva tu || 3 ||
gandhamālyanamaskāradhūpadīpānnasampadā ||
saṃvatsarānte dadyācca brāhmaṇāya payasvinīm || 4 ||
kṛtvā vrataṃ vatsarametadiṣṭaṃ prāpnoti teṣāṃ ca salokameva ||
tatroṣya kālaṃ suciraṃ manuṣyo rājā bhavedvā dvijapuṅgavo vā || 5 ||
iti śrīviṣṇudharmottare tṛtīyakhaṇḍe mā0 va0 saṃ0 bhṛguvratavarṇano nāmāśītyuttaraśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ ||| 180 ||
<3.181>
mārkaṇḍeya uvāca ||
mano mantastathā prāṇo naro yānaśca vīryavān ||
citirhayo nayaścaiva haṃso nārāyaṇastathā || 1 ||
vibhuścāpi prabhuścaiva sādhyā dvādaśa kīrtitāḥ ||
pūjayecchuklalapakṣe tāndvādaśyāṃ mārgaśīrṣataḥ ||2 ||
kṛtvā vrataṃ vatsarametadiṣṭaṃ prāpnoti teṣāṃ tu salokameva ||
tatroṣya kālaṃ suciraṃ narendra rājā bhavedbāhmaṇapuṅgavo vā || 3 ||
iti śrīviṣṇu0dha0 tṛtīyakhaṇḍe mā0va0saṃ0 sādhyavratavarṇano nāmaikāśītyuttara śatatamo'dhyāyaḥ || 181 ||
<3.182>
mārkaṇḍeya uvāca ||
dhātā mitroryamā pūṣā śakreśo varuṇo bhagaḥ ||
tvaṣṭā vivasvānsavitā viṣṇurdvādaśakastathā|| 1 ||
pūjayeddvādaśādityāñchuklapakṣe hyupoṣitaḥ ||
mārgaśīrṣādathārabhya gandhamālyānnasampadā || 2 ||
dattvā vratānte puruṣaḥ suvarṇamāpnoti lokānsaviturnṛvīra ||
mānuṣyamāsādya bhavatyarogo jitendriyaścaiva dhanānvitaśca || 3 ||
iti śrīviṣṇudharmottare tṛtīyakhaṇḍe mārkaṇḍeyavajrasaṃvāde kāmadeva vratavarṇano nāma dvyaśītyuttaraśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ || 182 ||
<3.183>
mārkaṇḍeya uvāca ||
śuklapakṣe mahārāja trayodaśyāmupoṣitaḥ ||
pūjayetkāmadevantu vaiśākhātprabhṛti prabho || 1 || gandhamālyanamaskāradhūpadīpānnasampadā ||
dadyādvratānte viprāya gandhavastrayugaṃ tathā || 2 ||
kṛtvā vrataṃ vatsarametadiṣṭamāsādya lokaṃ suciraṃ manuṣyaḥ ||
mānuṣyamāsādya bhavatyarogo sukhānvito rūpasarūpatā ca || 3 ||
iti śrī vi0dha0 tu0kha0 mā0va0saṃ0 kāmadevavratavarṇano nāma tryaśītyuttaraśatatamodhyāyaḥ || 183 ||
<3.184>
mārkaṇḍeya uvāca ||
śuklapakṣe mahārāja trayodaśyāmupoṣitaḥ ||
phālgunāttu samārabhya nityaṃ saṃpūjayennaraḥ || 1 || 
gandhamālyanamaskāradhūpadīpānnasampadā ||
suvarṇaṃ brāhmaṇendrāya vratānte pratipādayet || 2 ||
kṛtvā vrataṃ vatsarametadiṣṭaṃ yakṣeṣu rājansuciraṃ tathoṣya ||
mānuṣyamāsādya dhanānvitaḥ syātsaubhāgyayuktaśca tathā virogaḥ || 3 ||
iti śrīviṣṇudharmottare tṛtīyakhaṇḍe mārkaṇḍeyavajrasaṃvāde dhanavratavarṇano nāma caturaśītyuttaraśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ || 184 ||
<3.185>
śuklapakṣacaturdaśyāṃ jyeṣṭhā dārabhya yādava||
vāyuṃ saṃpūjayeddevaṃ sopavāso jitendriyaḥ ||1||
gandhamālyanamaskāradhūpadīpānnasaṃpadā||
saṃvatsarānte dātavyaṃ vastrayugmaṃ dvijātaye || 2||
kṛtvā vrataṃ vatsarametadiṣṭaṃ cāsādya lokaṃ marutāṃ manuṣyaḥ |
sukhāni bhuktvā suciraṃ mahīpa mānuṣyamāsādya bhavatyarogaḥ || 3 ||
iti śrīviṣṇudharmottare tṛtīyakhaṇḍe mārkaṇḍeyavajrasaṃvāde vāyuvratavarṇano nāma pañcāśītyuttarāśītitamo'dhyāyaḥ||185||
<3.186>
mārkaṇḍeya uvāca ||
kṛṣṇapakṣacaturdaśyāṃ virūpākṣaṃ tu pūjayet||
pauṣamāsādathārabhya yāvatsaṃvatsaraṃ vratam ||
gandhamālyanamaskāradhūpadīpānnasampadā||
uṣṭraṃ dvijātaye dadyādvratānte ca parantapa ||2||
kṛtvā vrataṃ vatsarametadiṣṭaṃ bhayaṃ na cāpnoti sa rākṣasebhyaḥ ||
kāmānavāpnoti bhavatyarogo bhayaṃ ca rājanna ca vetti kiṃcit || 3 ||
iti śrīviṣṇudharmottare tṛtīyakhaṇḍe mārkaṇḍeyavajrasaṃvāde virūpākṣavratavarṇano nāma ṣaḍaśītyuttaraśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ || 186 ||
<3.187>
mārkaṇḍeya uvāca ||
yatra kvacana nadyāṃ ca yatra kṛṣṇacatudarśī ||
anarkābhyudite kāle devaṃ saṃpūjayedyamam ||1||
dhūmrorṇaṃ citraguptaṃ ca kālapāśau ca yādava ||
mṛtyuṃ svargaṃ ca dharmajñaṃ gandhamālyānnasampadā || 2||
yamo dadhāra ityarcāṃ tilām̐śca juhuyāttataḥ ||
namo yamāyeti tathā strīśūdrasya vidhīyate || 3 ||
kṛsaraṃ bhojanaṃ viprānyathā śakti narottama ||
dadyādvratānte viprāya tathaiva ca payasvinīm || 4 ||
kṛtvā vrataṃ vatsarametadiṣṭaṃ na yāti rājannarakaṃ manuṣyaḥ ||
prāṇakṣayaṃ prāpya sa yāti nākaṃ mānuṣyamāsādya ca dharmavānsyāt || 5 ||
iti śrīviṣṇudharmottare tṛtīyakhaṇḍe mārkaṇḍeyavajrasaṃvāde yamavratavarṇano nāma saptāśītyu ttaraśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ || 187 ||
<3.188>
śuklapakṣādathārabhya phālgunasya narādhipa ||
pūjayettu caturdaśyāṃ sopavāso maheśvaram ||1||
gandhamālyanamaskāra dhūpadīpānnasaṃpadā ||
vratānte gāṃ tathā dadyādagniṣṭomaphalaṃ labhet || 2 ||
etadeva vrataṃ kṛtvā śuklapakṣe tu vatsaram ||
puṇḍarīkamavāpnoti kulamuddharati svakam || 3 ||
caturdaśīdvayaṃ caitatkṛtvā saṃvatsaraṃ naraḥ ||
māsimāsi tathā bhaktyā sarvānkāmānavāpnuyāt || 4 ||
āsādya kāmāṃśca maheśvarasya tatroṣya kālaṃ suciraṃ ca rājan ||
sāyujyamāyāti maheśvarasya sarveśvasyāpratimasya tasya || ||
iti śrīviṣṇudharmottare tṛtīyakhaṇḍe mārkaṇḍeyavajrasaṃvāde maheśvaravratavarṇano nāmāṣṭāśītyuttara śatatamo'dhyāyaḥ||188||
<3.189>
|| mārkaṇḍeya uvāca ||
prabhāsvarā barhiṣada agniṣvāttāstathaiva ca ||
kravyādaścopahūtāśca ājyapāśca sukālinaḥ || 1 ||
pūjyāḥ pitṛgaṇā rājannupavāsena nityaśaḥ ||
caitraśuklādathārabhya pañcadaśyāṃ narādhipa ||2 ||
śrāddhaṃ tadahni kurvīta yāvatsaṃvatsaraṃ bhavet ||
gandhamālyanamaskāradhūpadīpānnasampadā || 3 ||
saṃvatsarānte dadyācca tathā dhenuṃ payasvinīm ||
brāhmaṇāya mahābhāga pitṛbhaktāya bhaktitaḥ || 4 ||
kṛtvā vrataṃ vatsarametadiṣṭaṃ prāpnoti lokaṃ ca tathā sa teṣām ||
tatroṣya rājansuciraṃ sukālaṃ prāpnoti mokṣaṃ puruṣapradhāna ||5||
iti śrīviṣṇudharmottare tṛtīyakhaṇḍe mārkaṇḍeyavajrasaṃvāde pitṛvratavarṇano nāmaikonanavatyuttara śatatamo'dhyāyaḥ|| 189||
<3.190>
mārkaṇḍeya uvāca ||
kṛṣṇapakṣe pañcadaśyāṃ caitrādārabhya yādava ||
vahnisampūjanaṃ kṛtvā gandhamālyānnasampadā || 1 ||
tilairhomastataḥ kuryānnāmnā vahnernarādhipa ||
saṃvatsarānte dadyācca suvarṇaṃ brāhmaṇāya ca || 2 ||
kṛtvā vrataṃ vatsarametadiṣṭaṃ prāpnoti vittaṃ satataṃ yaśaśca ||
dharme matiṃ rūpamanuttamaṃ ca kāmānyatheṣṭānpuruṣapradhāna || 3 ||
iti śrīviṣṇudharmottare tṛ0 kha0 mārkaṇḍeyavajrasaṃvāde vahnivratavarṇano nāma navatyuttaraśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ || 190 ||
<3.191>
mārkaṇḍeya uvāca ||
amāvāsyāṃ tu velāyāṃ sopavāso narottama ||
padmadvayaṃ pūjayeta caṃdrārkāvekarāśigau || || 1 ||
ādityamaṣṭakamale candraṃ ṣoḍaśake tathā ||
ādityaṃ sarvaraktena candraṃ śuklena yādava || 2 ||
mālyādinā mahābhāga homayettilataṇḍulaiḥ ||
ghṛtakṣīrayuto rājantayornāmnā yathāvidhi || 3||
vratānte brāhmaṇendrāya kanakaṃ pratipādayet ||
rajataṃ ca mahābhāga ya icchedbhūtimātmanaḥ || 4 ||
kṛtvā vrataṃ vatmarametadiṣṭaṃ dattvā ca dīpānvidhivatprabhūtān ||
cakṣuḥ paraṃ prāpya vivṛddhatejā dhanānvitaḥ syāttridivādihasthaḥ || 5 ||
iti śrīviṣṇudharmottare tṛ0 kha0 mārkaṇḍeyavajrasaṃvāde candravratavarṇano nāmaikanavatyadhikaśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ || 191 ||
<3.192>
mārkaṇḍeya uvāca ||
kārtikāttu tathārabhya sampūrṇaśaśalakṣaṇam ||
pūjayedudare rājansadā naktāśano bhavet || 1 ||
lāvaṇaṃ maṇḍalaṃ kṛtvā candanenānulepite ||
daśanakṣatrasahitaṃ tatra somaṃ tu pūjayet || 2 ||
kṛttikārohiṇīyuktaṃ kārtike māsi pūjayet ||
saumyārdrāsahitaṃ rājanmāsi saumye tathaiva ca || 3 ||
ādityapuṣyasahitaṃ māsi pauṣe ca yādava ||
maghāsarpayutaṃ māghe phālgune śṛṇu pārthiva || 4 ||
aryamṇabhāgyasāvitraiḥ sahitaṃ pūjayedvibhum ||
citrāsvātiyutaṃ caitraṃ vaiśākhe śṛṇu pārthiva || 5 ||
viśākhayā ca maitreṇa yutaṃ saṃpūjayettathā||
jyeṣṭhāmūlayutaṃ jyeṣṭhe āṣāḍhādyāmanantaram || 6 ||
śrāvaṇe śravaṇopetaṃ sahitaṃ ca śraviṣṭhayā ||
tathā bhādrapade pauṣṇāmājāhirbudhnyasaṃyutam ||7||
aśvinībharaṇīyuktaṃ tathā cāśvayuje vibhum ||
gandhamālyanamaskāradhūpadīpānnasaṃpadā ||8 ||
guḍena paramānnena lavaṇena ghṛtena ca||
ikṣuṇekṣuvikāraiśca payasā pāyasena ca ||9||
pūjyāścāvidhavānāryastathā tallakṣaṇaiḥ śubhaiḥ ||
tato'nantaramaśnīyāddhaviṣyaṃ prayato naraḥ || 10 ||
brāhmaṇānāṃ vratānte tu mahārajataraṃjitam ||
śaktyā ca vasanaṃ dadyānnārī vā yadi vā naraḥ || 11 ||
rūpasaubhāgyalāvaṇyadhanayukto bhavennaraḥ ||
vratenānena cīrṇena svargalokaṃ ca gacchati || 12 ||
sopavāsastu yaḥ kuryādvratametadanuttamam ||
aśvamedhasahasrasya phalaṃ prāpnoti mānavaḥ || 13 ||
saubhāgyādi ca yatproktaṃ tadāpnoti viśeṣavat ||
manasā kāṃkṣitānkāmānsarvānprāpnotyasaṃśayam || 14 ||
janābhirāmaśca śaśāṅkavatsyāddhanādilābhaśca tathaiva lakṣmyā ||
śakreṇa tulyaśca tathaiva śaktyā mānuṣyamāsādya bhavecca rājan || 19 ||
iti śrī viṣṇudharmottare tṛ0 kha0 mārkaṇḍeyavajrasaṃvāde māsarkṣapaurṇamāsīvratavarṇano nāma dvinavatyuttaraśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ || 192 ||
<3.193>
mārkaṇḍeya uvāca ||
kārtike paurṇamāsyāṃ tu pūrṇaṃ śiśiradīdhitim||
padme ṣoḍaśapatreṣu karṇikāyāṃ tu pūjayet || 1 ||
kesare pūjayettatra nakṣatrāṇyaṣṭaviṃśatiḥ ||
patreṣu tithidevāṃśca tathā jyotsnāṃ ca pūjayet || 2 || 
gandhamālyanamaskāradhūpadīpānnasaṃpadā ||
guḍena paramānnena dadhnā ca lavaṇena ca || 3 ||
apūpaiśca mahābhāga phalaiḥ kālodbhavaistathā||
vratāvasāne dadyācca vāsoyugmaṃ dvijātaye || 4 ||
brāhmaṇyai ca mahābhāga mahārajatarañjitam ||
pūjyau ca vidhinā śaktyā kālavidvācakāvubhau || 6 |
sopavāso'tha naktāśī vratametatsamācaret ||
naktāśano vā dharmajña tathaiva ca haviṣyabhuk || 6 ||
saubhāgyadaṃ rūpavivardhanaṃ ca lāvaṇyadaṃ strīratibhogadaṃ ca ||
kāryaṃ prayatnena narendra puṃsā kāryaṃ tathā strībhiradīnasattva || 7 ||
iti śrīviṣṇudharmottare tṛ0 kha0 mārkaṇḍeyavajrasaṃvāde paurṇamāsīvratavarṇano nāma trinavatyuttaraśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ || 193 ||
<3.194>
mārkaṇḍeya uvāca ||
mārgaśīrṣādathārabhya candramaṇḍalake naraḥ ||
sopavāsastu saṃpūjya pauṇḍarīkaphalaṃ labhet || 1 ||
kṛtvā vrataṃ vatsarametadiṣṭaṃ prāpnoti lokaṃ sa niśākarasya ||
tatroṣya rājansuciraṃ ca kālaṃ sāyujyamāpnoti tataśca tasya || 2 ||
iti śrīviṣṇudharmottare tṛ0kha0 mārkaṇḍeyavajrasaṃvāde candravratavarṇano nāma caturnavatyuttaraśatatamo 'dhyāyaḥ || 194 ||
<3.195>
mārkaṇḍeya uvāca ||
proṣṭhapādādathārabhya saṃpūrṇe śaśalāñchane ||
saṃpūjya varuṇaṃ devaṃ gandhamālyānnasaṃpadā || 1 ||
dattvā vratāvasāne tu jaladhenuṃ dvijātaye ||
chatropānahasaṃyuktāṃ vāsoyugavibhūṣitām || 2 ||
prāpnoti lokaṃ varuṇasya rājaṃstatroṣya kālaṃ suciraṃ manuṣyaḥ ||
mānuṣyamāsādya bhavatyarogī rūpānvito vittayutastathaiva || 3 ||
iti śrīviṣṇudharmottare tṛ0 kha0 mārkaṇḍeyavajrasaṃvāde varuṇavratavarṇano nāma pañcanavatyuttaraśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ || 195 ||
<3.196>
|| mārkaṇḍeya uvāca || ||
aśvayuṅmāsi śukle tu paurṇamāsyāṃ naraḥ śuciḥ ||
sopavāso narendrastu devaṃ sampūjayettathā || 1 ||
śacīmairāvataṃ vajraṃ mātaliṃ ca narādhipa ||
gandhamālyanamaskāradhūpa dīpānnasaṃpadā || 2 ||
saṃvatsarānte kanakaṃ tu dattvā prāpnoti lokaṃ sa purandarasya ||
mānuṣyamāsādya narendrapūjyo rājā bhavedvā dvijapuṅgavo vā || 3 ||
iti śrīviṣṇudharmottare tṛtīyakhaṇḍe mārkaṇḍeyavajrasaṃvāde śakravratavarṇano nāma ṣaṇṇavatyuttaraśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ || 196 || ||
<3.197>
|| mārkaṇḍeya uvāca || ||
upoṣitaścaturdaśyāṃ paurṇamāsyāṃ narottamaḥ ||
pañcagavyaṃ pibetpaścāddhaviṣyāśī tathā bhavet || 1 ||
saṃvatsaraṃ tathā kṛtvā māsapāpātpramucyate ||
tasmātsarvaprayatnena māsimāsi samācaret ||2||
saṃvatsaraṃ prāpya narendralokaṃ tatroṣya rājansuciraṃ dvijendraḥ ||
mānuṣyamāsādya narendrapūjyo rājā bhavedvā dvijapuṅgavo vā || 3 ||
iti śrīviṣṇudharmottare tṛtīyakhaṇḍe mārkaṇḍeyavajrasaṃvāde brahmakūrcavarṇano nāma saptanavatyuttaraśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ || 197 ||
<3.198>
|| mārkaṇḍeya uvāca || ||
yogabhūtaṃ hariṃ devaṃ caturdaśyā mupoṣitaḥ ||
arcayetpaurṇamāsyāṃ tu so'śvamedhaphalaṃ labhet || 1 ||
brahmabhūtamamāvasyāṃ paurṇamāsyāṃ tathaiva ca ||
rājasūyamavāpnoti kulamuddharati svakam || 2 ||
brahmabhūtamamāvasyāṃ paurṇamāsyāṃ ca pūjayet ||
yogabhūtaṃ paricarankeśavaṃ mahadāpnuyāt || 3 ||
atyarthaṃ prītimāyāti māsapakṣāntayoḥ sadā ||
pūjitaḥ sopavāsena bhaktyā devavaro hariḥ || 4 ||
mahāvratamidaṃ khyātaṃ sarvakalmaṣanāśanam ||
saṃvatsaramidaṃ kṛtvā nākapṛṣṭhe mahīyate || 5 ||
sarvapāpapraśamanaṃ vratametatprakīrtitam ||
svavittaśaktirevātra sarvakarmasu kāraṇam || 6 ||
kṛtvā vrataṃ dvādaśavatsarāṇi prāpnoti lokaṃ puruṣottamasya ||
tatroṣya rājansuciraṃ sukālaṃ prāpnoti mokṣaṃ puruṣastatonte || 7 ||
iti śrīviṣṇudharmottare tṛtīyakhaṇḍe mārkaṇḍeyavajrasaṃvāde mahāvrataṃ nāmāṣṭa navatyuttaraśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ || 198 ||
<3.199>
vajra uvāca ||
bhagavankarmaṇā kena tiryagyonau na jāyate ||
mlecchadeśe ca puruṣastaṃ mamācakṣva pṛcchataḥ || 1 ||
mārkaṇḍeya uvāca ||
meṣasaṃkramaṇe bhānoḥ sopavāso narottamaḥ ||
pūjayedbhārgavaṃ rāmaṃ devaṃ śaktyā yathāvidhi || 2 ||
kanyāsaṃkramaṇe prāpte tathā kṛṣṇaṃ ca pūjayet ||
tathā mithunasaṃkrāntau pūjayedbhogaśāyinam || 3 ||
tathā kulīrasaṃkrāntau varāhamaparājitam ||
narasiṃhaṃ tathā devaṃ siṃhasaṃkramaṇe vibhum ||
kanyāsaṃkramaṇe devaṃ strīrūpaṃ pūjayennaraḥ || 4 ||
tulāsaṃkramaṇe prāpte vāmanaṃ nṛpa pūjayet ||
tathā vṛścikasaṃkrāntau devadevaṃ trivikramam || 5 ||
dhanuḥsaṃkramaṇe devaṃ tathāśvaśirasaṃ nṛpa ||
tathā makarasaṃkrāntau rāmaṃ daśarathātmajam || 6 ||
kuṃbhasaṃkramaṇe rājanrāmaṃ yādavanandanam ||
mīnasaṃkraṇe matsyaṃ vāsudevaṃ tu pūjayet ||7||
paṭe vā yadi vārcāyāṃ gandhamālyānnasaṃpadā ||
prādurbhāvasya nāmnāśca homaṃ kurvīta pārthiva||8||
vratānte jaladhenuṃ ca cchatropānatsamanvitām||
vastrayugmayutāṃ dadyātpratimāsaṃ ca kāñcanam ||
rātrau ca dīpamālābhirdevadevaṃ tu pūjayet || 9 ||
kṛtvā vrataṃ vatsarametadiṣṭaṃ mleccheṣu tiryakṣu na cāpi janma ||
prāpnotyavāpnoti ciraṃ ca nākaṃ kāmaṃ tathāpnoti manobhirāmam || 30 ||
iti śrīviṣṇudharmottare tṛtīyakhaṇḍe mārkaṇḍeyavajrasaṃvāde sudeśajanmāvāptivratavarṇano nāma navanavatyuttaraśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ||199||
<3.200>
vajra uvāca ||
iṣṭāṃ jātimavāpnoti karmaṇā yena mānavaḥ ||
tanmamācakṣva dharmajña tvaṃ hi sarvaviducyase || 1 ||
mārkaṇḍeya uvāca ||
pauruṣeṣu sadā te vai trirātropoṣito naraḥ ||
caitramāsādathārabhya kārtike pūjayeddharim || 2 ||
pauruṣeṇa ca sūktena ṛgbhiḥ puṣpaiḥ phalaistathā ||
dhūpairdīpaistathānnena ghṛtāhutibhireva ca || 3 ||
dhenurvratānte dātavyā kṛtvā saṃvatsaraṃ vratam ||
yatheṣṭāṃ jātimāpnoti nātra kāryā vicāraṇā || 4 ||
na kevalaṃ jātikaraṃ pradiṣṭaṃ vratottamaṃ yāvadadīnasattva ||
kāmānabhīṣṭānpuruṣasya dadyānmokṣaṃ ca dadyātpuruṣapradhāna || 5 ||
iti śrīviṣṇudharmottare tṛtīyakhaṇḍe mārkaṇḍeyavajrasaṃvāde iṣṭajātyavāptivratavarṇano nāma dviśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ || 200 ||
<3.201>
vajra uvāca ||
bhagavankarmaṇā kena kule janma samaśnute ||
etanme saṃśayaṃ chindhi tvaṃ hi sarvaviducyase || 1 ||
mārkaṇḍeya uvāca ||
bṛhaddiśi tu saṃprāpya trirātropoṣito naraḥ ||
mārgaśīrṣādathārabhya pūjayettu trivikramam || 2 ||
trivarṇaiḥ kusumairdevaṃ tribhiḥ prayatamānasaḥ ||
trayo'nulepanā deyā trisāraṃ dhūpameva tu || 3 ||
baliṃ trimadhuraṃ dadyāttrīm̐śca dīpānnṛpottama ||
yavaistilaistathā homaḥ kartavyaḥ sarṣapāyutaiḥ || 4 ||
dadyāttrilohaṃ ca tathā dvijebhyastāmraṃ suvarṇaṃ rajataṃ ca rājan ||
na kevalaṃ satkuladaṃ vrataṃ tu yatheṣṭakāmāptikaraṃ pradiṣṭam || 5 ||
iti śrīviṣṇudharmottare tṛtīyakhaṇḍe mārkaṇḍeyavajrasaṃvāde satkulāvāptivratavarṇano nāmaikottaradviśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ || 201 ||
<3.202>
vajra uvāca ||
bhavankarmaṇā kena rūpavāñjāyate naraḥ ||
etanme saṃśayaṃ chindhi tvaṃ hi sarvaviducyase || 1 ||
mārkaṇḍeya uvāca ||
phālgunyāṃ samatītāyāṃ pratipatprabhṛti kramāt ||
yāvaccaitrī mahārāja tāvatsnāto dinedine||2||
bahiḥ sampūjayeddevaṃ keśavaṃ bhogaśāyinam ||
ekabhaktāśano naktamadhaḥśāyī tathā bhaveta || 3 ||
trirātropoṣitaḥ kuryātpūjāṃ caitryāṃ tathaiva ca ||
svaśaktyā rajataṃ dadyādvastrayugmaṃ tathaiva ca || 4 ||
rūpārthino māsamidaṃ mayoktaṃ vratottamaṃ nityamadīnasattva ||
kṛtvā ca nākaṃ manujastvavāpya mānuṣyamāsādya ca rūpavānsyāt || 5 ||
iti śrīviṣṇu0 dha0 tṛ0 kha0 mā0 va0 saṃ0 rūpāvāptivratavarṇano nāma dvyuttaradviśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ ||202||
<3.203>
vajra uvāca ||
bhagavankarmaṇā kena naro lāvaṇyamāpnuyāt ||
lāvaṇyarahitaṃ rūpaṃ niṣphalaṃ pratibhāti me ||1||
mārkaṇḍeya uvāca ||
kārtikyāṃ samatītāyāṃ pratipatprabhṛti kramāt ||
paṭe vā yadi vārcāyāṃ pradyumnaṃ pūjayetprabhum||2||
bahiḥ snānaṃ sadā kuryānnaktamaśnīta vāgyataḥ ||
ekabhuktaṃ mahārāja haviṣyaprayataḥ sadā||3||
mārgaśīrṣaṃ tathā prāpya trirātropoṣitaḥ śuciḥ ||
saṃpūjya deva pradyumnaṃ hutvāgnau ghṛtameva ca ||4||
bhojayedbrāhmaṇāṃścātra bhojanaṃ lavaṇotkaṭam ||
cūrṇitasya tataḥ prasthaṃ lavaṇasya dvijātaye|5||
mahārajataraktaṃ ca vastrayugmaṃ tathā guroḥ ||
dadyācca kanakaṃ rājankāṃsyapātraṃ rasairyutam||6||
etaddhi lāvaṇyakaraṃ pradiṣṭaṃ vratottamaṃ nākagatipradaṃ ca ||
na kevalaṃ yādavavīra kāmānnarāya dadyātpuruṣapradhānaḥ ||7||
iti śrīviṣṇudharmottare tṛtīyakāṇḍe mā0 va0 saṃ0 lāvaṇyavratavarṇano nāma tryuttaradviśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ||203||
<3.204>
vajra uvāca ||
bhagavankarmaṇā kena saubhāgyaṃ mahadāpnuyāt||
lāvaṇyarūpe saubhāgyaṃ vinā jñeyaṃ nirarthakam ||1||
mārkaṇḍeya uvāca||
māghyāṃ tu samatītāyāṃ pratipatprabhṛti kramāt ||
paṭe vā yadi vārcāyāṃ kṛṣṇaṃ sampūjayetsadā || 2 ||
pūrvoktaṃ sakalaṃ kuryāddhaviṣyaṃ ca dharādhipa ||
nityaṃ samācaretsnānaṃ tathā gandhapriyaṅgunā || 3 ||
phālgunyāṃ tāṃ tato dadyāttrirāpoṣitaḥ śuciḥ||4||
vastre ca deye nṛpa kuṃkumākte kṣaudrasya pātraṃ ca tathaiva kāṃsyam ||
saubhāgyadaṃ caitadanuttamaṃ te vrataṃ mayaitatkathitaṃ nṛvīra ||5||
iti śrīviṣṇudharmottare tṛtīyakhaṇḍe mārkaṇḍeyavajrasaṃvāde saubhāgyāvāptivratavarṇano nāma caturuttaradviśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ||204||
<3.205>
vajra uvāca||
bhagavankarmaṇā kena narastvārogyamāpnuyāt||
rūpasaubhāgyalāvaṇyāḥ sarogasya nirarthakāḥ || 1 ||
|| mārkaṇḍeya uvāca ||
proṣṭhapadyāmatītāyāṃ pratipatprabhṛtikramāt ||
paṭe vā yadi vārcāyāmaniruddhaṃ tu pūjayet || 2 ||
pūrvoktena vidhānena yāvadāśvayujī bhavet ||
sārasairarcayeddevaṃ jātīpuṣpairdinedine || 3 ||
ghṛtena juhuyādvahniṃ ghṛtaṃ dadyāddvijātaye ||
bhojanaṃ gorasaprāyaṃ tathā viprāṃśca bhojayet || 4 ||
trirātropoṣitaḥ samyagāśvayujyāṃ tato naraḥ ||
saghṛtaṃ sasuvarṇaṃ ca kāṃsyapātraṃ dvijātaye || 5 ||
dadyānnṛpatiśārdūla narastvārogyavṛddhaye ||
vratametadvinirdiṣṭaṃ svagarlokapradaṃ nṛṇām || 6 ||
na kevalaṃ rogaharaṃ pradiṣṭamājñākaraṃ rūpavivṛddhidaṃ ca ||
vratottamaṃ te kathitaṃ nṛvīra tatheṣṭakāmāptikaraṃ nṛloke || 7 ||
iti śrīviṣṇudharmottare tṛtīyakhaṇḍe mārkaṇḍeyavajrasaṃvāde ārogyavratavarṇano nāma pañcottaradviśatatamo' dhyāya || 205 ||
<3.206>
vajra uvāca||
bhagavankarmaṇā kena prajñāyukto bhavennaraḥ ||
etadeva manuṣyāṇāṃ manuṣyatvamudāhṛtam||1||
mārkaṇḍeya uvāca ||
caitryāṃ tu samatītāyāṃ yāvanmāsaṃ dinedine ||
pūrvavatpūjayeddevaṃ nṛsiṃhamaparājitam || 2 ||
homaṃ ca pratyahaṃ kuryāttathā siddhārthakairnṛpa ||
brāhmaṇānbhojayeccātra tathā trimadhuraṃ sadā|| 3 ||
vaiśākhyāṃ kanakaṃ dadyātrirātropoṣito naraḥ ||
jñānāvāptipradaṃ hyetadvrataṃ buddhivivardhanam || 4 ||
kṛtvā vrataṃ māsamidaṃ mayoktamāsādya nākaṃ suciraṃ manuṣyaḥ ||
mānuṣyamāsādya ca buddhiyukto jñānena yuktaśca tathā bhavettu || 5 ||
iti śrīviṣṇudharmottare tṛtīyakhaṇḍe mārkaṇḍeyavajrasaṃvāde buddhyāvāptivratavarṇano nāma ṣaḍuttaradviśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ || 206 ||
<3.207>
vajra uvāca ||
bhagavankarmaṇā kena vidyāvānpuruṣo bhavet ||
suvidya eva vijñeyaḥ puruṣaḥ paśuranyathā || 1 ||
mārkaṇḍeya uvāca ||
pauṣyāntu samatītāyāṃ pratipatprabhṛti kramāt ||
prāgvatsaṃpūjayeddevaṃ turaṃgaśirasaṃ harim||2||
tilām̐śca juhuyādvahnau tilairdevaṃ samarcayet||
trirātropoṣito māghyāṃ tilānkanakameva ca||3||
dadyādbrāhmaṇamukhyāya samyakprayatamānasaḥ||
mukhyaṃ yajñopavītañca prabhūtamapi cendhanam||4||
kṛtvā vrataṃ māsamidaṃ mayoktaṃ vidyānvitaḥ syātpuruṣaḥ sadaiva||
svarlokamāsādya sukhāni bhuktvā kāmānabhīṣṭānpuruṣośnute ca||5||
iti śrīviṣṇudharmottare tṛtīyakhaṇḍe mā0 vajrasaṃvāde vidyāvāptivratavarṇano nāma saptottaradviśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ||207||
<3.208>
vajra uvāca||
bhagavankarmaṇā kena śīlavānpuruṣo bhavet ||
kulajātiśrutibhyaśca śīlameva viśiṣyate || 1 ||
mārkaṇḍeya uvāca ||
āgrāyaṇyāmatītāyāṃ māsamekaṃ dinedine ||
pūrvavatpūjayeddevaṃ varāhamaparājitam||2||
ghṛtena snāpayeddevaṃ ghṛtena juhuyāddhaviḥ ||
ghṛtaṃ dvijebhyo dadyācca ghṛtameva nivedayet ||3||
trirātropoṣitaḥ pauṣyāṃ ghṛtapātreṇa ca dvijān ||
pūjayetsasuvarṇena yathāśakti narādhipa || 4 ||
kṛtvā vrataṃ māsamidaṃ mayoktaṃ cāsādya nākaṃ suciraṃ manuṣyaḥ ||
mānuṣyamāsādya ca śīlavānsyātprāpnoti puṣṭiṃ cirajīvitaṃ ca ||5||
iti śrīviṣṇudharmottare tṛ0 kha0 mārkaṇḍeyavajrasaṃvāde śīlāvāptivratavarṇano nāmāṣṭottaradviśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ || 208 ||
<3.209>
vajra uvāca ||
bhagavankarmaṇā kena nityaṃ dharmaparo bhavet ||
dharmmavicca mahārāja dharmmasā phalyakāraṇāt || 1 ||
mārkaṇḍeya uvāca ||
āṣāḍhyāṃ samatītāyāṃ pratipatprabhṛti kramāt ||
pūrvvavatpūjayeddevaṃ dharmmavigrahadhāriṇam ||2||
māsasya cānte nṛpa paurṇamāsyāṃ kṛtvā trirātraṃ kanakañca dadyāt ||
vratottamaṃ dharmmakaraṃ tavoktaṃ sarvvārthadaṃ nātra vicāraṇāsti || 3 ||
iti śrīviṣṇudharmmottare tṛ0 kha0 mārkaṇḍeyavajrasaṃvāde dharmmaprāptivratavarṇano nāma navottaradviśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ || 209 ||
<3.210>
vajra uvāca ||
bhagavankarmaṇā kena dhanavānpuruṣo bhavet ||
dhanavāneva lokeṣu pūjyo bhavati mānavaḥ || 1 ||
mārkaṇḍeya uvāca ||
śrāvaṇyāṃ samatītāyāṃ pratipatprabhṛti kramāt ||
pūrvavatpūjayedviṣṇuṃ devaṃ saṅkarṣaṇaṃ prabhum || 2 ||
nīlotpalaistathā patrairnṛpa bhṛṃgirajasya ca ||
ghṛtena paramānnena tathā bilvaiśca śaktitaḥ || 2 ||
trirātropoṣitaḥ samyak proṣṭhapadyāṃ tato naraḥ ||
gāṃ ca dadyāddvijendrāya vratānte manujottamaḥ || 4 ||
kṛtvā vrataṃ māsamidaṃ yathoktaṃ cāsādya nākaṃ suciraṃ manuṣyaḥ ||
mānuṣyamāsādya dhanānvitaḥ syādvratena cīrṇena narendrasiṃha || 5 ||
iti śrīviṣṇudharmottare tṛ0 kha0 mārkaṇḍeyavajrasaṃvāde dhanāvāptivratavarṇano nāma daśottara dviśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ || 210 ||
<3.211>
vajra uvāca ||
śrīvihīnasya lokesminkiṃ dhanena mahādyute ||
tasmādvrataṃ samācakṣva yena syācchrīyuto naraḥ || 1 ||
mārkaṇḍeya uvāca ||
vaiśākhyāṃ samatītāyāṃ pratipatprabhṛti kramāt ||
pūrvavatpūjayeddevaṃ śrīsahāyaṃ dinedine ||2 ||
puṣpairmūlaiḥ phalaiścaiva juhuyādakṣatāṃstathā ||
bilvāṃśca vahnau satataṃ gorasairbhojayeddvijān || 3 ||
trirātropoṣito jyeṣṭhe kanakaṃ pratipādayet ||
vastrayugmaṃ ca rājendra tena sāphalyamaśnute||4||
kṛtvā vrataṃ māsamidaṃ yathoktaṃ cāsādya nākaṃ suciraṃ manuṣyaḥ ||
mānuṣyamāsādya vivṛddhatejā śriyā yutaḥ syājjagati pradhānaḥ||5||
iti śrīviṣṇudharmottare tṛ0kha0 mārkaṇḍeyavajrasaṃvāde śrīlabdhivratavarṇano nāmaikādaśottaradviśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ ||211 ||
<3.212>
vajra uvāca ||
bhagavankarmaṇā kena bhogānāpnoti mānavaḥ ||
kinnu bhogavihīnasya kāryamasti dhanairdvija ||1||
mārkaṇḍeya uvāca ||
jyeṣṭhāyāṃ samatītāyāṃ pratipatprabhṛti kramāt||
pūrvavatpūjayeddevaṃ viśvarūpadharaṃ harim ||2||
kṛtvā vratānte ca tathā trirātraṃ dattvā suyuktaṃ śayanaṃ dvijāya||
svarlokamāsādya ciraṃ tathoṣya mānuṣyamāsādya ca bhogavānsyāt || 3 ||
iti śrīviṣṇudharmottare tṛ0kha0 mārkaṇḍeyavajrasaṃvāde bhogāvāptivratavaṇarno nāma dvādaśottaradviśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ || 212 ||
<3.213>
vajra uvāca ||
bhagavankarmaṇā kena sarvatra jayamāpnuyāt ||
vyavahāre raṇe dyūte vivāde ca dvijottama ||1||
jayāvāpteḥ paraṃ nāsti saukhyaṃ lokeṣu sattama ||
jayāvāptiḥ paraṃ saukhyaṃ tadāptivratamucyatām || 2 ||
mārkaṇḍeya uvāca ||
āśvayujyāmatītāyāṃ pratipatprabhṛti kramāt ||
pūrvavatpūjayedviṣṇuṃ devadevaṃ trivikramam || 3 ||
trirātrānte tu kārtikyāṃ dadyādukṣāṇamuttamam ||
sarvasasyadharaṃ kṛtvā śaktyā ratnairalaṅkṛtam || 4 ||
kṛtvā vrataṃ māsamidaṃ yathoktaṃ prāpnoti nākaṃ suciraṃ nṛvīraḥ||
tatroṣya kālaṃ suciraṃ manuṣyaḥ prāpnoti sarvatra jayaṃ nṛloke || 5 ||
iti śrīviṣṇudharmottare tṛ0 kha0 mārkaṇḍeyavajrasaṃvāde jayāvāptivratavarṇano nāma trayodaśottaradviśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ||213||
<3.214>
vajra uvāca ||
vratānyanyāni me brūhi kāmyāni dvijapuṅgava ||
nārīṇāṃ puruṣāṇāṃ vā sarvajñastvaṃ mato mama || 1 ||
mārkaṇḍeya uvāca ||
kṛttikāsvarcayeddevaṃ kārtikīprabhṛtikramāt ||
yāvatsyātkārtikaṃ bhūyo narasiṃhamupoṣitaḥ ||2 ||
anulepanapuṣpādyaiḥ sarvadoktaissadaiva tu ||
vratāvasāne dattvā ca candanaṃ tagaraṃ dvije || 3 ||
carma caivāyudhaṃ mukhyaṃ vāsāṃsi vividhāni ca ||
sarvatra jayamāpnoti yatrayatrābhijāyate || 4 ||
mārgaśīrṣādathārabhya mṛgaśīrṣe tathārcayet ||
yāvadāgrāyaṇī bhūyo rāmaṃ daśarathātmajam || 5 ||
anulepanapuṣpādyaiḥ sarvaratnaiḥ sadaiva tu ||
vratāvasāne dadyādgāṃ tathā śvetāṃ dvijātaye || 6 ||
śvetaṃ vastrayugaṃ caiva rajataṃ ca tathā nṛpa ||
upoṣitaḥ sadā kṛtvā vrataṃ syācchatruvarjitaḥ || 7 ||
pauṣamāsādathārabhya puṣpairnityamupoṣitaḥ ||
yāvatpauṣī bhavedbhūyo balabhadramathārcayet || 8 ||
anulepanapuṣpānnaiḥ sarvaratnaistathaiva ca ||
vratāvasāne saghṛtaṃ kāṃsyaṃ kanakameva ca || 9 ||
dattvā viprāya bhavati nityaṃ puṣṭiyuto naraḥ ||
māghamāsādathārabhya maghāsu satataṃ naraḥ || 3.214.10 ||
vārāhamarcayeddevaṃ tathā nityamupoṣitaḥ ||
ghṛtābhyaṅgena vidhivaccandanena sugandhinā || 11 ||
tathā ca paramānnena ghṛtahomena cāpyatha ||
dadyādvratāvasāne ca kṣitiṃ gāṃ vā narādhipa || 12 ||
pitṛprasādamāpnoti kṛtvaitadvratamuttamam ||
phālgunāttu tathārabhya phālgunīṣu samarcayet || 13 ||
naranārāyaṇau devau yāvatsyātphālgunī punaḥ ||
vratāvasāne śayanaṃ svāstīrṇe prati pādayeta || 14 ||
vratāvasāne nārī syātsubhartrā samalaṃkṛtā ||
bhāryāṃ narastathāpnoti rūpadraviṇasaṃyutām || 15 ||
anukūlāṃ śriyaṃ nityaṃ tathā pakṣavatīṃ nṛpa ||
aviyogamavaidhavyaṃ karotyetanmahāvratam || 16 ||
caitramāsādathārabhya nityaṃ citrāsvathārcayet ||
vratāvasāne dadyācca citraṃ vastraṃ dvijanmane || 17 ||
vratenānena puruṣaḥ putrānāpnotyathepsitān||
nārī vā puruṣavyāghra nātra kāryā vicāraṇā || 18||
vaiśākhītastathārabhya viśākhāsu tathārcayet ||
yāvadbhūyastu vaiśākhī sopavāsaḥ pṛthuṃ vibhum|| 19||
dattvā vratānte kanakaṃ jñātiśraiṣṭhyamavāpnuyāt||
jyeṣṭhamāsādathārabhya jyeṣṭhāsu satataṃ naraḥ||3.214.20 ||
arcayedbhārgavaṃ rāmaṃ yāvajjyeṣṭhaṃ punarbhavet ||
vratāvasāne dadyācca cchatropānahameva ca ||21 ||
vratenānena bhavati sarvaśreṣṭho narottama ||
āṣāḍhītastathārabhya dinadvayamupoṣitaḥ || 22 ||
āṣāḍhyāṃ tvarcayeddevaṃ pradyumnamaparājitam ||
bhūyaḥ syādyāvadāṣāḍhī dadyācca śayanaṃ tataḥ || 23 ||
svāstīrṇaṃ tena cāpnoti nityaṃ rūpayutāḥ striyaḥ ||
śrāvaṇītastathārabhya śravaṇe satataṃ harim || 24 ||
arcayetsopavāsaṃ taṃ yāvatsyācchrāvaṇī punaḥ ||
vratāvasāne dadyācca kāṃsyaṃ rūpyaṃ ghṛtaṃ tathā||25||
vratānyathaitāni mayā narendra proktāni te pāpaharāṇi nityam ||
nākapradānyuttamapūryamāṇānkāmānpradātṝṇi yatheṣṭamatra||26||
iti śrīviṣṇudharmottare tṛ0kha0mārkaṇḍeyavajrasaṃvāde dvādaśamāsarkṣavratavarṇano nāma catudarśottaradviśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ || 214 ||
<3.215>
vajra uvāca ||
ārādhanārthaṃ devasya viṣṇoramitatejasaḥ ||
vratānyanyāni me brūhi bhṛguvaṃśavivardhana || 1 ||

gaṅgādvāre sukhāsīnaḥ pulastyaṛṣisattamaḥ ||
vratāni yāni dālbhyāya jagāda śṛṇu tāni me || 2 ||
dālbhya uvāca || ārādhanārthaṃ devasya viṣṇoramitatejasaḥ ||

pulastya uvāca ||
ekādaśyāṃ śuklapakṣe phālgune māsi yo naraḥ ||
japankṛṣṇeti devasya nāma bhaktyā punaḥpunaḥ || 4 ||

snātaḥ prasthānakāle ca utthāne skhalite kṣute || 5 ||
pāṣaṇḍānpatitāṃścaiva tathaivāntāvasāyinaḥ ||
nālapeta tathā kṛṣṇamarcayecchraddhayānvitaḥ || 6 ||
idaṃ codāharetkṛṣṇe manaḥ sandhāya tatparaḥ ||
kṛṣṇakṛṣṇa kṛpālustvamagatīnāṃ gatirbhava || 7 ||
saṃsārārṇavamagnānāṃ prasīda madhusūdana ||
evaṃ prasādyopavasetkṛtvā niyatamānasaḥ || 8 ||
pūrvehni cānyedyuścāpi gavyaṃ saṃprāśya vai sakṛt ||
snātorcayitvā kṛṣṇeti punarnāma prakīrtayet || 9 ||
vāridhārāvrate caiva vikṣipeddevapādayoḥ ||
caitravaiśākhayoścaiva tadvajjyeṣṭhe ca pūjayet || 3.215.10 ||
martyaloke gatiṃ śreṣṭhāṃ dālbhya prāpnoti vai naraḥ ||
utkrāntikāle kṛṣṇasya smaraṇaṃ ca tathāśnute || 11 ||
āṣāḍhe śrāvaṇe caiva māsi bhādrapade tathā ||
tathaivāśvayuje māsi anena vidhinā naraḥ || 12 ||
upoṣya saṃpūjya tathā keśaveti ca pūjayet ||
gomūtraprāśanātpūtaḥ svargalokagatiṃ vrajet || 13 ||
ārādhitasya jagatāmīśvarasyāvyayasya ca ||
utkrāntikāle smaraṇaṃ keśavasya tathāpnuyāt || 14 ||
kṣīrasya prāśanaṃ yastu vidhiṃ cemaṃ yathoditam ||
kārtikādi yathānyāyaṃ kuryānmāsacatuṣṭayam || 15 ||
tenaiva vidhinā brahmanvipṇornāma prakīrtayet ||
sa yāti viṣṇusālokyaṃ viṣṇuṃ smarati cākṣayam || 16 ||
pratimāsaṃ dvijātibhyo dadyāddānaṃ yathāvidhi ||
cāturmāsye ca saṃpūrṇe puṇyaśravaṇakīrtanam || 17 ||
atha vā vāsudevasya tadgītaṃ cāpi kārayet ||
evameti gatiṃ śreṣṭhāṃ devamānuṣyakīrtanāt || 18 ||
kathitaṃ pāraṇaṃ yatte kāryaṃ māsacatuṣṭayam ||
ādhipatyaṃ tathā bhogāṃstena prāpnoti cākṣayān || 19 ||
dvitīyena tathā bhogānaindrānāpnoti mānavaḥ ||
viṣṇulokaṃ tṛtīyena pāraṇena tathāpnuyāt || 3.215.20 ||
evametatsamākhyātaṃ gatiprāpakamuttamam ||
vidhānaṃ dvijaśārdūla kṛṣṇatuṣṭipradaṃ nṛṇām || 21 ||
sugatidvādaśīmetāṃ śraddadhānastu yo naraḥ ||
upoṣyati tathā nārī prāpnoti vividhāṃ gatim || 22 ||
eṣā tithiḥ pāpaharātidhanyā nākapradā nākakarī tathoktā||

iti śrīviṣṇudharmottare tṛtīyakhaṇḍe mārkaṇḍeyavajrasaṃvāde sugatidvādaśīvratavarṇano nāma pañcadaśottaradviśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ || 215 ||
<3.216>
pulastya uvāca ||
pañcadaśyāṃ tu śuklasya phālgunasyaiva sattama ||
pāṣaṇḍānpatitāṃścaiva tathaivāntyāvasāyinaḥ || 1 ||
nāstikāñcitravṛttīṃśca pāpānanyāṃśca nālapet ||
nārāyaṇaṃ tvekamanāḥ puruṣo niyatendriyaḥ ||2||
tiṣṭhanvrajanpraskhalite kṣute cāpi janārdanam ||
kīrtanaṃ tatkriyākāle saptakṛtvaḥ prakīrtayet ||3 ||

sandhyādyaparame cendusvarūpaṃ harimīśvaram || 4 ||

naktaṃ ca bhuñjīta narastailakṣāravivarjitam || 5 ||
tathaiva caitravaiśākhajyeṣṭheṣu munisattama ||

niṣpāditaṃ bhavedekaṃ pāraṇaṃ dālbhya bhaktitaḥ ||
dvitīyaṃ cāpi vakṣyāmi pāraṇaṃ dvijasattama || 7 ||

tathaivāśvayujebhyarcya śrīdharaṃ vai śriyā saha ||
samyakcandramaso dattvā bhuñjītārghaṃ yathāvidhi || 8 ||
dvitīyametadākhyātaṃ tṛtīyaṃ pāraṇaṃ śṛṇu ||
kārtikādiṣu māseṣu tathaivābhyarcya keśavam || 9 ||
bhūmyā samanvitaṃ dadyācchaśāṅkāyārhaṇaṃ niśi ||
bhuñjīta ca yathākhyātaṃ tṛtīyamiti pāraṇam || 3.216.10 ||
pratipūjāsu dadyācca brāhmaṇebhyaśca dakṣiṇām ||
pratimāsaṃ ca vakṣyāmi prāśanaṃ kāyaśuddhaye || || 11 ||

gītavādyādikaṃ pāṭhyaṃ tathā kṛṣṇasya vā kathām || 13 ||
kārayettu ca devasya pāraṇaṃ pāraṇe gate ||
evaṃ saṃpūjya vidhivatsapatnīkaṃ janārdanam|| || 14 ||

janārdanaṃ salakṣmīkamarcayetprathamaṃ tataḥ || 15 ||
saśrīkaṃ śrīdharaṃ bhaktyā tṛtīye bhūtikeśavau ||
yāvantyetadvidhānena pāraṇānyarcayetprabhum ||
tāvanti janmāni sukhaṃ prāpnotīṣṭaviyogajam || 16 ||
prāpnoti devasmaraṇe ca mṛtyau tadāpi cāpnoti manonukūlām ||
gatiṃ dvijaśreṣṭha tatopi kāle sāyujyamāyāti janārdanasya || 17 ||
iti śrīviṣṇudharmottare tṛtīyakhaṇḍe mārkaṇḍeyavajrasaṃvāde sugatipauṣamāsīkalpavarṇano nāma ṣoḍaśottaradviśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ ||216||
<3.217>
|| pulastya uvāca ||
śṛṇu dālbhya paraṃ kāmaṃ vrataṃ santatidaṃ nṛṇām ||
yadupoṣya na vicchedaḥ pitṛpiṇḍasya jāyate || 1 ||
kṛṣṇāṣṭamyāṃ caitramāse snāto niyatamānasaḥ ||
kṛṣṇamabhyarcya pūjāṃ ca devakyāḥ kurute naraḥ || 2 ||
nirāhāro japannāma kṛṣṇasya jagataḥ pateḥ||
upaviṣṭo vrajan snātaḥ kṣutapraskhalanādiṣu || 3 ||
pūjāyāṃ cāpi kṛṣṇasya saptavārānprakīrtayet ||
pāṣaṇḍino vikarmasthānnālapeccaiva nāstikān || 4 ||
prabhāte ca tataḥ snāto dattvā viprāya dakṣiṇām ||
bhuñjīta kṛtapūjastu kṛṣṇasyaiva jagatpateḥ || 5||
vaiśākhajyeṣṭhayoścaivaṃ pāraṇaṃ hi trimāsikam ||
upoṣya devadeveśaṃ ghṛtena snāpayeddharim || 6 ||
āṣāḍhe śrāvaṇe caiva māsi bhādrapade tathā ||
upoṣyate dvitīyaṃ vai ghṛtena snapayeddharim ||7 ||
āṣāḍhe śrāvaṇe caiva māsi bhādrapade tathā ||
upoṣyate dvitīyaṃ vai pāraṇaṃ pūrvavadbhavet || 8 ||
āśvine kārtike saumye tṛtīyaṃ pāraṇaṃ tathā ||
pauṣe māghe phālgune ca caturthaṃ dvijasattama || 9 ||
pāraṇe pāraṇe pūrṇe ghṛtena snāpayeddharim ||
brāhmaṇebhyo ghṛtaṃ dadyāttathaiva pratipāraṇam || 3.217.10 ||
kṛtvā vrataṃ nākamanuprayāti mānuṣyamāsādya ca nirvṛtaḥ syāt||
santānavṛddhiśca tathāpnute'sau yāvanmahī sāgaramekhalāntā||11||
iti śrīviṣṇudharmottare tṛtīyakhaṇḍe mārkaṇḍeyavajrasaṃvāde santānāṣṭamīvratavarṇanonāma saptadaśottaradviśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ || 217 ||
<3.218>
pulastya uvāca||āṣāḍhaśuklapakṣe tu bhagavānmadhusūdanaḥ ||
bhogibhāge nijāṃ māyāṃ yoganidrāṃ ca mānayet||1||
śete'sau caturomāsānyāvadbhavati kārtikam||
viśiṣṭā na pravartante tadā yajñādikāḥ kriyāḥ||2||
tatrāṣāḍhasitānte ca yo naro dinapañcakam ||
adhaḥśāyī bahiḥ snāyī māṃsābhyaṅgavivarjitaḥ || 3 ||
vṛntākālābukūṣmāṇḍamadhukṣāravivarjitaḥ ||
samastamadirāvarjī sakṛnnaktāśano bhavet ||4||
brahmacārī jitakrodho japahomaparāyaṇaḥ||
hariṃ saṃpūjayennityaṃ gandhamālyānnasaṃpadā || 5 ||
gītairvādyaistathā nṛttairdīpamālābhireva ca ||
sānnānāṃ jaladhenūnāṃ pradānena tathaiva ca || 6 ||
tathā kārtikaśuklānte tṛtīyaṃ pāraṇaṃ bhaveta ||7||
hiṃsātmakaistu kiṃ tasya yajñaiḥ kāryaṃ mahātmanaḥ ||
prasvāpe ca prabodhe ca pūjito yena keśavaḥ || 8 ||
daśāhametatkṛtvā tu vrataṃ viṣṇuparo naraḥ ||
agniṣṭomamavāpnoti kulaṃ caiva samuddharet || 9 ||
kārtikaścāgnidaivatyo māso devamukhaḥ smṛtaḥ ||
āśvayujyāmatītāyāṃ yāvatsyāddvija kārtikī || 3.218.10 ||
vrataṃ daśāhābhihitaṃ kṛtvā svarge mahīyate || ||
pauṇḍarīkamavāpnoti kulamuddharati svakam || 11 ||
pratyahaṃ dīpadānena kārtikābhimukho bhavet ||
cakṣuṣmānbrāhmaṇaśreṣṭha tathā sarvatra pūjitaḥ || 12 ||
etāvantaṃ tathā kālaṃ sarvamāṃsavivarjitaḥ ||
svargalokātparibhraṣṭo mānuṣye sukhamāpnuyāt || 13 ||
ārogyasukhasampattyā yuktaśca subhago bhavet ||
prasupte devadeveśe sarvānkāmānavāpnuyāt || 14 ||
daśāhamuditaṃ kṛtvā khaḍgadhārāvrataṃ naraḥ ||
sarvakāmasamṛddhasya yajñasya phalamaśnute || 15 ||
māsaṃ ca kārtikaṃ kṛtvā pauṇḍarīkamavāpnuyāt ||
kṛtvā māsaṃ yatheṣṭaṃ ca vahniṣṭomamavāpnuyāt ||| 16 ||
kṛtvā ca caturo māsānsupte madhuniṣūdane ||
aśvamedhaphalaṃ prāpya nākapṛṣṭhe mahīyate || 17 ||
asidhārāvrataṃ kṛtvā tathā saṃvatsaraṃ naraḥ ||
sarvayajñaphalaṃ prāpya viṣṇuloke ca gacchati || 18 ||
yenayena tu kāmena khaḍgadhārāvrataṃ caret ||
taṃtaṃ kāmamavāpnoti viṣṇuloke mahīyate || 19 ||
tathā samartho bhavati dāne ca varaśāpayoḥ ||
ādityatejā bhavati nātra kāryā vicāraṇā ||3.218.20||
dālbhya uvāca ||
asidhārāvratavidhiḥ samācakṣva mahādyute ||
etanme saṃśayaṃ cchindhi tvaṃ hi sarvaviducyase || 21 ||
pulastya uvāca ||
snātaḥ svalaṃkṛtaḥ sragvī bhuktavānmāṃsavarjitam ||

atīva duṣkaramidaṃ khaḍgadhārāvrataṃ smṛtam || 24 ||

bhuktvā ciraṃ taddvijamukhya cānte sāyujyamāyāti janārdanasya ||25||
iti śrīviṣṇudharmottare tṛtīyakhaṃḍe mā0 va0 saṃ0 dālabhyaṃ prati pulastyakathā svadhārāvratavarṇano nāmāṣṭādaśottaradviśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ || 218 ||
<3.219>
pulastya uvāca ||
māsi proṣṭhapapade śukle dvādaśyāṃ jalaśāyinam ||
praṇamyānantamabhyarcya puṣpadhūpādibhiḥ śuciḥ || 1 || 
pāṣaṇḍādibhirālāpamakurvanniyamātmavān ||
jaladhenuṃ dvije dattvā naktaṃ bhuñjīta vāgyataḥ || 2 ||

samyakkṛtānāṃ niyamavratānāṃ cānantamārādhya na saṃśayotra || 5 ||
iti śrīviṣṇudharmottare tṛtīyakhaṇḍe mārkaṇḍeyavajrasaṃvāde dālbhyaṃ prati pulastyavākyeṣvanantadvādaśīvratavarṇano nāmaikonaviṃśatyuttaradviśatatamodhyāyaḥ||219||
<3.220>
pulastya uvāca ||
pauṣamāse site pakṣe dvādaśyāṃ śakradaivatam ||
nakṣatrayogaṃ viṣṇuṃ ca prathamaṃ tu samarcayet ||1||
tataḥ prabhṛti viprendra māsimāsi janārdanam ||
upoṣitaḥ pūjayate yāvatsaṃvatsaraṃ gatam || 2 ||
māsetha māse vidhinoditena ramyāṃ tithau dānamatha bravīmi ||
prācyaṃ yathāvadvidhivatkrameṇa taducyamānaṃ nikhilaṃ nibodha || 3||
ghṛtaṃ yathā vrīhiyavaṃ hiraṇyaṃ yavānnamaṃbhaḥ kanakānupānam ||
chatraṃ payonnaṃ guḍaphāṇitāḍhyaṃ srakcandane vastramanukrameṇa || 4 ||
gomūtramaṃbho ghṛtamāṃsaśākaṃ dūrvādadhivrīhiyavaṃ tilāṃśca ||
sūryāṃśutaptaṃ jalamāśu darbhaṃ kṣīraṃ ca māsaṃ kramaśaḥ prayuñjyāta||5||
kule pradhāne dhanadhānyapūrṇaṃ vivekavānyastu samastaduḥkhe||
prāpnoti janmāvikalendriyaśca bhavatyarogo matimānsukhī ca || 6 ||
iti śrīviṣṇudharmottare tṛtīyakhaṇḍe mārkaṇḍeyavajrasaṃvāde dālbhyaṃ prati pulastyavākyeṣu brahmadvādaśīvratavarṇano nāma viṃśatyuttaradviśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ || 220 ||
samāptaḥ pulastyadālbhyasaṃvādaḥ ||

|| vajra uvāca ||
kiṃ nu kṛtyatamaṃ loke manuṣyāṇāṃ prakīrtitam ||
etatpṛṣṭo mamācakṣva sarvadharmabhṛtāṃ vara || 1||
mārkaṇḍeya uvāca ||
antarvedi bahirvedi pūjā kāryā divaukasām ||
etatkṛtyatamaṃ loke puruṣasya vipaścitaḥ || || 2 ||
antarvedyāṃ ca yajanaṃ bahuvittasya kīrtitam||
svalpavittasya dharmajña bahirvedi prakīrtitam || 3|| 
puṣpānnagandhanaivedyastotrādhyayanakarmaṇā ||
dhūpadīpanamaskārairnityaṃ pūjyā divaukasaḥ ||4||
tato'pi niyamaiścaiva brāhmaṇānāṃ ca tarpaṇaiḥ ||
bahiḥ śuśrūṣayā caiva dānena vividhena ca|| 5 ||
sadaiva pūjitā devāstuṣṭimāyānti yādava ||
svesve kālaviśeṣeṇa kālaḥ pūrvodito mayā || 6 ||
vajra uvāca ||
devatāḥ kāśca kasminnu kāle saṃpūjayetsadā ||
devatāpūjane kāryaṃ viśeṣānmama kīrtaya || 7 ||
mārkaṇḍeya uvāca ||
kālaṃ tathāhaṃ vakṣyāmi devatāpūjane pṛthak ||
saṃvatsareṇa dharmajña sopavāsasya sarvadā || 8 ||
brahmāṇaṃ pūjayeddevaṃ satataṃ prathamehani ||
pakṣadvaye mahābhāga pañcadaśyāmupoṣitaḥ || 9 ||
saṃvatsareṇa dharmajña vidyādbahusuvarṇakam||
haṃsayuktena yānena brahmaloke ca gacchati||3.221.10||
caitramāsasya yā śukle prathamā pratipadbhavet|
tadahni brahmaṇaḥ kṛtvā sopavāsastu pūjanam||11||
saṃvatsaramavāpnoti saukhyāni bhṛgunandana ||
kālasyāvayavāḥ sarve tasminnahani pūjitāḥ||12|
graharkṣāṇi ca dharmajña saukhyaṃ dadyādanuttamam||
saṃvatsarādhipaṃ nāgaṃ tasminnahani pūjayet||13||
grahebhyaḥ saukhyamāpnoti svāsthyamagryaṃ tathaiva ca||
tatrāhni brahmaṇā sṛṣṭaṃ trailokyaṃ prathamaṃ dvija||14||
dakṣaṃ prajāpatiṃ nityaṃ dvitīyāyāmathārcayet ||
phalamāpnoti dharmajña gosavasya na saṃśayaḥ ||15||
tathā nāsatyayoḥ kṛtvā tasminnahani pūjanam ||
nityamārogyamāpnoti tathā rūpaṃ ca bhārgava||16||
śuklapakṣadvitīyāyāṃ bālacandrasya pūjanam||
kṛtvā dattvā ca lavaṇaṃ prāgrātrau subhago bhavet||17||
sādhyā dvādaśa ye proktāsteṣāṃ kṛtvā tu pūjanam||
tṛtīyasyāṃ mahābhāga dvādaśāhaphalaṃ labhet||18||
tṛtīyāyāṃ tathābhyarcya brahmaviṣṇumaheśvarān||
pṛthakpṛthaṅmahābhāga trivargaphalabhāgbhaveta || || 19||
trīṃllokāṃśca tadā rāma samyaksaṃpūjayennaraḥ ||
aiśvaryaṃ mahadāpnoti gatimagryāṃ ca vindati ||3.221.20||
tṛtīyā śrāvaṇe kṛṣṇā yā syācchravaṇasaṃyutā ||
tasyāṃ saṃpūjya govindaṃ puṣṭimagryāmavāpnuyāt ||21||
vaiśākhaśuklapakṣe tu tṛtīyāyāmupoṣitaḥ||
akṣayaṃ phalamāpnoti sarvasya sukṛtasya tu || 22||
sā tathā kṛttikopetā viśeṣeṇa ca pūjitā ||
tatra dattaṃ hutaṃ taptaṃ sarvaṃ saphalamucyate || 23 ||
akṣayā sā tithiryasmāttasmātsukṛtamakṣayam ||
akṣataiḥ pūjyate viṣṇustena sāpyakṣatā smṛtā ||24||
akṣataistu naraḥ snāto viṣṇordattvā tathākṣatān ||
saktūnsusaṃskṛtām̐ścaiva hutvā caiva tathākṣatān || 25||
vipreṣu dattvā tāneva tathā saktuṃ susaṃskṛtam ||
yavānnabhuṅ mahārāja phalamakṣayyamaśnute ||26 ||
ekāmapyuktavatkṛtvā tṛtīyāṃ bhṛgunandana ||
eṣāmabdatṛtīyānāṃ sarvāsāṃ tatphalaṃ bhavet ||27||
proktāstu bhṛgavo nāma devā dvādaśa ye purā ||
caturthyāṃ pūjanaṃ teṣāṃ kṛtvā divamavāpnuyāt || 28 ||
yamasya pūjanaṃ kṛtvā caturthyāmeva dhārmikaḥ ||
nāpnoti nārakaṃ duḥkhaṃ tuṣṭe ravisute naraḥ || 29 ||
vināyakamathābhyarcya caturthyāṃ yadunandana ||
sarvavighnavinirmuktaḥ kāryasiddhimavāpnuyāt || 3.221.30 ||
nidrāṃ ratiṃ tathā śraddhāṃ kīrtiṃ medhāṃ sarasvatīm ||
prajñāṃ tuṣṭiṃ tathā kāntiṃ tasminnahani pūjayet || 31 ||
tatprasādamavāpnoti yatheṣṭaṃ manujottama ||
vidyākāmo viśeṣeṇa pūjayettu sarasvatīm || 32 ||
devānāṃ mātaraḥ sarvāstathā tatrāhni pūjitāḥ ||
sarvakāmapradā jñeyāḥ puruṣasya vipaścitaḥ || 33 ||
pañcamyāṃ pūjanaṃ kṛtvā tathā candramaso naraḥ ||
saubhāgyaṃ mahadāpnoti yaśaścāgryaṃ ca vindati || 34 ||
pañcamyāṃ pṛthivīṃ devīṃ tathā sampūjayennaraḥ ||
tāmevāpnotyayatnena nātra kāryā vicāraṇā || 35 ||
viśvedevāstu ye proktāḥ pūrvameva mayā daśa ||
teṣāṃ sampūjanaṃ kṛtvā pañcamyāṃ divamāpnuyāt || 36 ||
iṣṭagandharvamabhyarcya pañcamyāṃ subhago bhavet ||
tathā citrarathaṃ teṣāṃ rājānaṃ ca viśeṣataḥ || 37 ||
devabhāryāṃ tathā bhāryāṃ sampūjya subhago bhavet ||
iṣṭamapsarasaṃ caiva tathā rūpamavāpnuyāt || 38 ||
iṣṭaṃ nāgaṃ tathābhyarcya śriyamāpnotyanuttamām ||
vāruṇaiḥ puṣkarasyārcāṃ pañcamyāṃ yaḥ samācaret || 39 ||
sarvakāmasamṛddhasya somayajñaphalaṃ bhavet ||
tathā ca pūjayanrājaṃstathā vai nalakūbaram || 3.221.40 ||
śriyaḥ sampūjanaṃ kṛtvā pañcamyāṃ prayataḥ sadā ||
śriyamāpnoti vipulāṃ matimagryāṃ ca vindati || 41||
caitraśuklasya pañcamyāṃ pūjayitvā tathā śriyam||42||
sakṛdevāpnuyādrājanphalaṃ saṃvatsaroditam ||
sarvasyāṃ nṛpa pañcamyāṃ ratiṃ prītiṃ sarasvatīm||43||
umāṃ menāṃ bhadrakālīṃ tathā kātyāyanīmapi ||
dhṛtiṃ svāhāṃ sudhāmṛddhimanusūyāṃ tathā kṣamām||44||
subhīmāṃ devasenāṃ ca velāṃ jyotsnāṃ tathā śacīm||
gaurīṃ varuṇapatnīṃ ca yamapatnīṃ tathaiva ca || 45 ||
dhūmorṇāṃ sumahābhāgāṃ mṛtyucchāyāṃ tathaiva ca || 46 ||
abhīṣṭāṃ devajananīṃ devapatnīṃ tathaiva ca ||
pūjayankāmamāpnoti tasyā hyekamasaṃśayam ||
airāvataṃ vā garuḍamuccaiḥśravasameva vā||47||
harasya vṛṣabhaṃ caiva śastrāṇyastrāṇi yāni ca||
tadā sampūjayanrājā vijayaṃ tamupāśnute ||48||
tathā bhādrapade māsi śuklapakṣasya pañcamīm ||
nāgasaṃpūjanaṃ kṛtvā dhanabhāgī bhavennaraḥ || 49 ||
kumāraśca tathā skando viśākhaśca guhastathā ||
caturātmā vinirdiṣṭo bhagavānkrauñcadāraṇaḥ|| 3.221.50 ||
tamabhyarcya naraḥ ṣaṣṭhyāṃ putrānāpnotyabhīpsitān ||
bālakānāṃ gṛhe śreyo naraḥ prāpnotyasaṃśayam || 51 ||
ṛtūnāṃ pūjanaṃ kṛtvā tatra kṣemamavāpnuyāt ||
skandapārśvacarānrājanbhīmapārśvacarāṃstathā || 52 ||
yamapārśvacarām̐ścaiva rogamuktimavāpnuyāt ||
kālapāśau tathābhyarcya jvaravyādhīśameva ca || 53 ||
rogamokṣamavāpnoti vāyuvahnīndavastathā ||
caitraṣaṣṭhyāṃ viśeṣeṇa skandamabhyarcayennaraḥ||54||
pūrvoktaphalamāpnoti svargalokaṃ ca gacchati ||
saptamyāṃ ca samabhyarcya sūryapatnīṃ suvarcalām ||55||
iṣṭānkāmānavāpnoti nātra kāryā vicāraṇā ||
devā ekonapañcāśanmaruto nāma kīrtitāḥ || 56 ||
teṣāṃ saṃpūjanaṃ kṛtvā saptamyāṃ divamāpnuyāt ||
iṣṭapūjāṃ ca śailasya tadā kṛtvā sukhī bhavet || 57 ||
pūjayitvā tathābhīṣṭāṃ saritaṃ puṇyabhāgbhavet ||
vahnisaṃpūjanaṃ kṛtvā vahniṣṭomaphalaṃ labhet || 58 ||
vāyoḥ saṃpūjanaṃ kṛtvā prāpnoti paramāṅgatim ||
saṃpūjya ca ṛṣīnsapta somasaṃsthāstu ye smṛtāḥ || 59 ||
sapta prāpnoti lokānvai gatimagryāṃ ca vindati ||
saptamyāṃ yaduśārdūla iṣṭamabhyarcayanmunim || 3.221.60 ||
svādhyāyaphalamāpnoti tadvatsāphalyamaśnute ||
pūjayitvā samudrāṃśca drīpānatha tathā naraḥ || 61 ||
pātālānvā mahābhāga bhuvamāpnotyabhīpsitām ||

kāmamekamavāpnoti yaṃ kañcinmanasepsitam ||
catuṣpatheṣu ramyeṣu tathaivāṭṭālakeṣu ca || 3.221.70 ||
giriśṛṅgeṣu ramyeṣu nadītīreṣu cāpyatha ||
guhāsu caiva ramyāsu piśācānāṃ baliṃ haret || 71 ||

teṣāṃ balimupākurvankṣemamāpnoti mānavaḥ || 72 ||
āyudhaṃ pūjayitvā ca tathā vijayamāpnuyāt ||
vidyāmāpnoti vipulāṃ pūjayitvā sarasvatīm || 73 ||
vidyākāmastu kurvīta tadā pustakapūjanam ||
tathaivāśvayuje śukle bhadrakālīṃ viśeṣataḥ || 74 ||
navamyāṃ pūjayitvā tu sarvānkāmānavāpnuyāt ||
daśamyāṃ pūjayanrājanviśvedevāṃstathā daśa || 75 ||
sarvakāmasamṛddhasya yajñasya phalamaśnute ||
tadā saṃpūjayanrājaṃstathaiva ca diśo daśa || 76 ||
kriyāsāphalyamāpnoti yatra yatrābhijāyate ||
dharmaṃ saṃpūjya deveśaṃ sarvasattvasukhāvaham || 77 ||
dharme matimavāpnoti dharmasāphalyameva ca||
ekādaśyāṃ tu saṃpūjya rudrānekādaśaiva ca || 78 ||
sarvakāmasamṛddhasya yajñasya phalamaśnute||
tathā sarvagatānrudrāṃstadā sarvatra pūjayet || 79 ||
sarvānkāmānavāpnoti sarvagānaparājitān ||
tathā ca dvādaśādityāndvādaśyāṃ pūjayennaraḥ || 3.221.80 ||
dvādaśāhamavāpnoti gatimagryāṃ ca vindati ||
dvādaśyāṃ devadeveśaṃ pūjayitvā jalādhipam || 81 ||
pauṇḍarīkamavāpnoti varuṇaṃ yādasāṃ patim ||
sarvadeveśvaraṃ śakraṃ pūjayitvā tathā naraḥ || 82 ||
sarvānkāmānavāpnoti svargalokaṃ ca gacchati ||
viṣṇoḥ saṃpūjanaṃ kṛtvā kāmānāpnotyabhīpsitān|| 83 ||
iṣṭayajñaphalaṃ prāpya gatimagryāṃ ca vindati ||
trayodaśyāmathābhyarcya kāmadevaṃ samarcayet || 84 ||
ayatnātsarvamāpnoti phalaṃ saṃvatsaroditam ||
yakṣāṇāṃ rākṣasānāṃ ca caturdaśyāṃ tu pūjanam || 85 ||
kṛtvā kṣemamavāpnoti kriyāsāphalyameva ca ||
pūjayitvā dhanādhyakṣaṃ tadā vaiśravaṇaṃ prabhum || 86 ||

śaṃkha padmau tathābhyarcya nidhānau yakṣapūjitau || 87 ||

pūjayitvā mahādevaṃ sarvānkāmānavāpnuyāt || || 88 ||
māghamāsacaturdaśyāṃ kṛṣṇapakṣe viśeṣataḥ ||
tathā pitṝngaṇānrājankṣīṇe candre ca pūjayet || 89 ||
sarvakāmasamṛddhasya yajñasya phalamaśnute ||
śraddhāṃ kṛtvā tathā rājansarvānkāmānavāpnuyāt || 3.221.90 ||
pūrṇaminduṃ tathābhyarcya saubhāgyaṃ mahadāpnuyāt ||
iṣṭagrahamathābhyarcya nakṣatramatha vārcayet || 91 ||
tasmātkṣemamavāpnoti kāmaṃ ca yadunandana ||
māsanāma sanakṣatra pūrṇimāyogi yaśca vā||92||

sopavāsastu nakṣatraṃ nakṣatreśamathārcayet || 93 ||
nakṣatraṃ vā mahābhāga tasmātkāmamavāpnuyāt ||
kārtikeyaṃ mahābhāga kṛttikāsvarcayennaraḥ || 94 ||
vahniṣṭomamavāpnoti kulaṃ caiva samuddharet ||
kṛttikāsu tathābhyarcya khaḍgaṃ vijayamāpnuyāt || 95 ||
bhaujaṅge nāgamabhyarcya śriyaṃ prāpnotyanuttamām ||
kāmadevamathābhyarcya bhāgye saubhāgyavānbhavet || 96 ||
sāvitraṃ ca tathā haste śriyaṃ prāpnotyanuttamām ||
iṣṭagrahābhitaddehaṃ tannāmādhyakṣasevayā || 97 ||
gandharvaṃ vā grahaṃ vāpi tasmātkāmānavāpnuyāt ||
kṛṣṇapakṣacaturdaśyāṃ mahākālamathārcayet || 98 ||
tasmātkāmamavāpnoti yatheṣṭaṃ nātra saṃśayaḥ ||
māheśvaraṃ mātṛgaṇaṃ tathā tatrāgnipūjanam || 99 ||
ekavāsāntaraṃ rājankāmamāpnotyabhīpsitam || 3.221.100 ||
tathā tumbarusambaddhaṃ pañcakaṃ pūjayennaraḥ ||
sarvatra jayamāpnoti nātra kāryā vicāraṇā || 101 ||
kṛttikāsu naro'bhyarcya skandapārśvacarām̐stathā ||
ārogyaṃ mahadāpnoti sadā vā manasepsitam||102||
nṛsiṃhapratibaddhāṃ tu pūrṇe candre samarcayet||
naro mātṛgaṇaṃ cārcya sarvānkāmānavāpnuyāt||103||
ekāṃ vā mātaraṃ rājankāmānāpnotyabhīpsitān ||
vānaspatyamavāpnoti pūjayitvā vanaspatīn || 104 ||
daityānāṃ dānavānāṃ vā tatpakṣā ye tathāpare ||

karaṇe vā muhurte vā yathāvaddevamarcayet ||
tasmātkāmamavāpnoti gatimagryāṃ ca vindati || 106 ||
caitramāsādathārabhya yathoktena naraḥ sadā ||

saṃvatsareṇa dharmajña nakṣatre yadi vā tithau ||
grahe vā sarvadharmajña sopavāsassamāhitaḥ || 108 ||
saṃvatsare vāpyayane ṛtau māse ca taddale ||
naktāśī satataṃ pūjāṃ kṛtvā tatsvāminaḥ sadā ||

vajra uvāca ||
bhagavañśrotumicchāmi roceṣu ca pṛthakpṛthak ||
upoṣitena kiṃ kāryaṃ kiṃ phalaṃ ca tathā bhavet || || 1 ||
mārkaṇḍeya uvāca| ||
itihāsaṃ tathātremaṃ nibodha gadato mama ||
dhanādhyakṣasya saṃvādamaṣṭāvakrasya cāpyatha || 2 ||
aṣṭāvakraḥ purā kanyāṃ prārthayāmāsa dharmavit||
ṛṣerbhārgavamukhyasya vadānyasya tu suprabhām||3||
rūpadraviṇasampannāṃ sa covāca muniṃ muniḥ||
strīsvabhāvo yadi jñātastvayā dharmavidāṃ vara || 4 ||
tatte'haṃ tava dāsyāmi kanyāṃ bālamṛgekṣaṇām ||
|| aṣṭāvakra uvāca || ||
strīsvabhāvo na vijñāto mayā brāhmaṇasattama || 5 ||
kutaśca tanmayā jñātastanmamācakṣva pṛcchataḥ ||
|| bhārgava uvāca || ||
uttarāṃ tvaṃ diśaṃ paśya sā te vakṣyati tattvataḥ || 6 ||
strīsvabhāvaṃ mahābhāga gaccha śīghramatandritaḥ ||
|| mārkaṇḍeya uvāca || ||
evamuktaḥ sa dharmātmā prayayāvuttarāṃ diśam ||7 ||
gaṅgādvārātkrameṇātha tvarito munipuṅgavaḥ ||
tīrthāni saritaścaiva śailāni nagarāṇi ca ||8 ||
vilaṅghya dhanadāvāsamāsasāda mahātapāḥ ||
kailāse parvataśreṣṭhe sarvakāmasamṛddhimat || 9 ||
tatrāsīnaṃ mahābhāgaṃ sa dadarśa dhanādhipam ||
varāsanagataṃ vīraṃ hārabhārāñcitodaram || 3.222.10 ||
vāmabhāge kirīṭena vinītena virājitam ||
kuṇḍalābhyāṃ vicitrābhyāṃ keyūrairaṃgadaistathā || 11 ||
gadādharaṃ mahākāyamekalocanapiṅgalam ||
tathā ca śaṅkhapadmābhyāṃ nidhibhyāṃ ca virājitam || 12 ||
yakṣaiśca sumahābhāgairbhīmarūpaparākramaiḥ ||
dīrghabhadreṇa vīreṇa pūrṇabhadreṇa cāpyatha ||13||
maṇibhadreṇa vīreṇa yakṣabhadreṇa cāpyatha ||
dīrghabāhumahābāhupadmakiṃjalkasṛñjayaiḥ || 14 ||
svabhadreṇa supārśvena tathā maṇidhareṇa ca ||
yakṣaiścānyaiśca dharmajña mahā balaparākramaiḥ || 15 ||
nānāveśaiśca gandharvairmahāsattvairmahābalaiḥ ||
citrāṅgadaścitrarathaścitraseno'tha tumburuḥ || 16 ||
pūrṇāyuranaghaścaiva tathā śāliśirāḥ prabhuḥ ||
śṛṅgārakarṇo'tibalo bhīmo bhīmaparākramaḥ || 17 ||
ete cānye ca gandharvāstathaivāpsarasaḥ śubhāḥ ||
urvaśī menakā rambhā miśrakeśī hyalambusā 18 ||
viśvācī ca ghṛtācī ca pañcacūḍā manoramā ||
etābhiśca tathānyābhirdevastrībhiḥ sahasraśaḥ || 19 ||
sa dadarśa dhanādhyakṣaṃ samantātparivāritam ||
pādārghyācamanīyādyairdhanādhyakṣeṇa pūjitaḥ || 3.222.20 ||
varāsanagato rājaṃstuṣṭāva dhanadaṃ prabhum ||
|| aṣṭāvakra uvāca || ||
namo'stu te dhanādhyakṣa yajñādhyakṣa namo'stu te || 21 ||
mahābāho mahāsattva rājarājāmaraprabho ||
ṛṣisaṅghastutācintya vāmamaule varaprada ||| || 22 ||
piṅgākṣa vipulagrīva deveśa naravāhana ||
gadādhara viśālāṃsa sūryatejaḥsamaprabha || 23 ||
bhavāñcharvasakho nityaṃ viṣṇoraṃśastathā bhavān ||
devāsure'tha saṃgrāme bhavatā vinipātitāḥ || 24 ||
bahavo dānavā vīrā mahābalaparākramāḥ ||
lokāndhārayase sarvāṃstvameko yakṣapārthivaḥ || 25 ||
bhaktānukampī satataṃ cottarāśāprabhuḥ prabhuḥ ||
dharmaseturjagatyasmiṃstvayi sarvaṃ pratiṣṭhitam || 26 ||
mārkaṇḍeya uvāca ||
evaṃ dhanādhipenoktaḥ pratyuvāca mahāmuniḥ ||
dharmamahāphalaṃ tvattaḥ śrotumicchāmi vittapa || 27||
|| vaiśravaṇa uvāca || ||
brahmansvīyasya ca rucau devatāyāśca pūjanam ||
dharmaṃ mahāphalaṃ vipra tanme nigadataḥ śṛṇu || 28 ||
sarveṣu vipra roceṣu sopavāso jitendriyaḥ ||
devatāpūjanaṃ kṛtvā yathoktaṃ phalamaśnute || || 29 ||
caitraśuklasamāraṃbhādroce vratamanuttamam ||
roce yatheṣṭaṃ gṛhṇīyātsaṃvatsaramatandritaḥ || 3.222.30 ||
triṃśadrocāni vipreṃdra māsamekaṃ samāhitaḥ ||
saṃvatsareṇa naktāśī pūjayedrocadevatām || 31 ||
pratyahaṃ sumahābhāga tena kāmamavāpnuyāt ||
āṣāḍhamāse yaḥ kuryādrocavratamanuttamam || 32 ||
naktabhojī pratidinaṃ caikakālāśano dvijaḥ ||
āhārakālādanyatra toyapānaṃ vivarjayet || 33 ||
brāhmarocādathārabhya yāvadrocaṃ tu pauruṣam ||
roce tu pauruṣe prāpte kṛtvā brāhmaṇatarpaṇam || 34 ||
vāsāṃsi dattvā viprebhyaḥ suvarṇaṃ rajataṃ tathā ||
svargalokamavāpnoti dīrghakālaṃ dvijottama || 35 ||
janma cāsādya mānuṣyaṃ rūpavānabhijāyate ||
virogo balavānnityaṃ śatrujidbalavānapi || 36 ||
striyaśca mukhyāḥ prāpnoti kule janma tathottame ||
saubhāgyaṃ mahadāpnoti lāvaṇyamapi cottamam || 37 ||
sadbhirmaitrīṃ tathā vidyāṃ dharme caivottamāṃ gatim ||
vāhanānyapi mukhyāni yaścānyadabhivāñchati || 38 ||
roceṣu māse saphalaṃ tavoktaṃ naktāśino devavarasya vipra ||
ataḥ paraṃ te kathayāmi roce pṛthakphalaṃ samyagupoṣitasya || || 39 ||
iti śrīviṣṇudharmottare tṛtīyakhaṇḍemārkaṇḍeyavajrasaṃvāde roceṣu māsopavāsaphalanirūpaṇonāma dvāviṃśatyuttaraśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ || 222 || ||
<3.223>
|| vaiśravaṇa uvāca || ||
brāhme brahmāṇamabhyarcya brahmalokaṃ prapadyate ||
aśvamedhamavāpnoti prājāpatye prajāpatim || 1 ||
svargaroce tathā svargaṃ svargalokaṃ prapadyate ||
cakraroce tathā cakraṃ vaiṣṇavantu sudarśanam || 2 ||
dvādaśāraṃ naraḥ kṛtvā varṇakaiḥ sasugandhibhiḥ ||
sampūjya vatsarasyāṃte sauvarṇaṃ vinivedayet || 3 ||
vāsudevāya devāya dvitīyaṃ brāhmaṇāya ca ||
ripūnsa jayate sarvānbāhyānābhyantarānapi || 4 ||
cakramapratimaṃ tasya sarvagaṃ ca tathā bhavet ||
nāpnoti nārakaṃ duḥkhaṃ svargalokaṃ ca gacchati || 5 ||
vānaspatye tathā roce pūjayitvā vanaspatim || 6 ||
udyānaphalamāpnoti vājapeyasya mānavaḥ ||
vānaspatyaṃ ca saphalaṃ tathā sarvatra vindati ||7 ||
annaroce tathaivānnaṃ vāsudevaṃ samarcayet ||
kṣetramadhyagatastvannaṃ prāpnoti bahu mānavaḥ || 8 ||
paraloke tathā tṛptiṃ svargaloke tathaiva ca ||
ahate vāsare cāhni vāsaraṃ pūjya mānavaḥ || 9 ||
nivedya vāsudevāya devaṃ saṅkarṣaṇaṃ vibhum ||
ārogyarūpalāvaṇyasaubhāgyāni samaśnute || 3.223.10 ||
kālaroce naraḥ kālaṃ samyaksampūjya dharmavit ||
maraṇe smṛtimāpnoti mṛtaśca paramāṃ gatim || 11 ||
āgneye pūjayedvahniṃ sarvāṃllokānsamāpnuyāt ||
sarvāṇi vāgnikarmāṇi sidhyantyasya na saṃśayaḥ ||12||
apaḥ saṃpūjayannāthe ratnamāpnotyathāmbujam ||
jalakarmāṇi sarvāṇi siddhyantyasya na saṃśayaḥ || 13 ||
saure sūryamathābhyarcya virogatvamavāpnuyāt ||
candre candraṃ samabhyarcya paramāṃ nirvṛtiṃ labheta|14|
goroce gāṃ samabhyarcya surabhiṃ lokamātaram ||
gāḥ samāpnoti dharmajña tathā tallokameva ca||15||
rocabhaṃ niyateḥ prāpya niyatiṃ tu samarcayan ||
dīrghajīvitamāpnoti yatrayatrābhijāyate ||16||
vaiṣṇave viṣṇumabhyarcya viṣṇulokaṃ prapadyate ||
raudre rudramathābhyarcya tallokaṃ pratipadyate||17||
putrānāpnoti kaumāre kumāraṃ cārcayannaraḥ||
pitṝṇāmarcanaṃ kṛtvā pitṛroce samāhitaḥ ||18||
pūjāmabhīṣṭāmāpnoti pitṛlokaṃ ca gacchati ||
vāruṇe varuṇaṃ devamaśvānāpnoti pūjayan || 19 ||
anante ca tathānantaṃ pūjayitvā mahīdharam ||
anantaṃ phalamāpnoti karmaṇaḥ sukṛtasya ca || 3.223.20 ||
vāyavye vāyumabhyarcya vāṇijye lābhamāpnuyāt ||
yāmye yamamathābhyarcya nāpnoti narakādbhayam || 21 ||
sārasvatena cābhyarcya dvija devīṃ sarasvatīm||
vidyāṃ yatheṣṭāmāpnoti tayā vāgmī ca jāyate||22||
śrīroce śriyamabhyarcya śriyaṃ vindati śobhanām||
madīye ca tathā roce māṃ ca sampūjayannaraḥ || 23 ||
dhanabhāgbhavate so hi yatrayatrābhijāyate ||
śailaroce tathā śailaṃ pūjayitvā sukhī bhavet || || 24 ||
bhūroce bhuvamabhyarcya bhuvamāpnotyanuttamām ||
vedānāmarcanaṃ kṛtvā vedaroce samāhitaḥ || 25 ||
vedānāpnoti puruṣo yajñānapi narā dhipaḥ ||
pauruṣe ca tathā roce jale puruṣamarcayan || 26 ||
sarvānkāmānavāpnoti mokṣopāyaṃ ca vindati ||
rūpeṇa hīnaṃ sparśena hīnaṃ gandhena hīnaṃ ca rasena hīnam ||
śabdena hīnaṃ puruṣaṃ purāṇaṃ sampūjayanmokṣapathaṃ prayāti || 27 ||
iti śrīviṣṇudharmottare tṛ0 kha0 mārkaṇḍeyavajrasaṃvāde prati rocaphalanirūpaṇo nāma trayoviṃśatyuttaradviśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ || 223 ||
<3.224>
mārkaṇḍeya uvāca ||
iti rocavrataṃ śrutvā bhuktvā dhanadaveśmani ||
śrutvā gītaṃ ca vādyaṃ ca dṛṣṭvā nṛttaṃ manoharam || 1 ||
tatroṣya rajanīmekāṃ dhanādhyakṣeṇa pūjitaḥ ||
udagjagāma dharmātmā himaśailasya sa dvijaḥ || 2 ||
sa śailadeśe kasmiṃściddadarśa ruciraṃ gṛham ||
śītāṃśuraśmijālābhaṃ sarvakāmasamṛddhimat || 3 ||
tasmingṛhe dadarśātha vṛddhāṃ rūpavatīṃ striyam ||
sarvābharaṇasampannāṃ bahvībhiḥ parivāritām || 4 ||
taruṇībhirvarastrībhiścāruveśābhireva ca ||
sā ca taṃ pūjayāmāsa pādyādibhiranukramāt || 5 ||
gandhatailena cābhyarcya snāpayāmāsa yādava ||
sukhasparśaiśca pavanairgandhaiśca vividhaistathā || 6 ||
lilepa cāsya gātrāṇi candanena sugandhinā ||
vastrālaṅkāramālyaiśca pūjayāmāsa taṃ tathā || 7 ||
bhojayāmāsa cānnena sarvakāmamṛddhinā ||
tāmbūlaṃ pradadau cāsmai sugandhi sumanoharam || 8 ||
śvetottaracchadaṃ citraṃ svāstīrṇaṃ paramarddhimat ||
gṛhe vicitre śayanaṃ pradadau sumanoharam || 9 ||
tādṛgvidhe pare tatra sā ca suṣvāpa bhāminī ||
visarjayitvā nārīstāḥ sarvā bālamṛgekṣaṇāḥ || 3.224.10 ||
śītāpadeśena tato virātre śayanātsvakāt ||
aṣṭāvakrasya śayanaṃ jagāma varavarṇinī || 11 ||
dṛṣṭvā śayanasamprāptāṃ kimetaditi so'bravīt ||
nānurūpamidaṃ tatte vayasaścāruhāsini || 12 ||
abhagnabrahmacaryo'smi paradāravivarjakaḥ ||
svacchandaṃ māmavajñāya kimidaṃ kartumīhase || 13 ||
diguvāca ||
na vayaṃ dvija tāruṇye capalāḥ kevalaṃ striyaḥ ||
vayasonte'pi cāpalyaṃ bhajāmaḥ kāmacoditāḥ || 14 ||
na śrutaṃ tattvayā manye strīśīlaṃ dvijasattama ||
naitā vayasi rajyante na rūpe na kule śrute || 15 ||
śīle veśe dhane śaurye rajyante kvacideva tu ||
kāmayantyeva puruṣaṃ yathālābhamiti śrutiḥ || 16 ||
yāsāṃ hi rakṣā mahatī tāstriyaḥ kubjavāmanaiḥ ||
preṣyairvāpīha sajjante pumāṃsamiti bhārgava || 17 ||
nāgnistṛpyati kāṣṭhairvā nāpagābhirmahodadhiḥ ||
nāntakaḥ sarvabhūtairvā na puṃbhirvāmalocanāḥ || 18 ||
līlayaiva kulaṃ ghnanti smayamānāstu yoṣitaḥ ||
durlabhā hi strīramaṇātsvabhāvena bhṛgūttama || 19 ||
raho nāsti kṣaṇo nāsti nāsti copanimantrakaḥ ||
tena dharmajña nārīṇāṃ satītvamupajāyate || 3.224.20 ||
preṣitastvaṃ mahābhāga matsamīpaṃ mahātmanā ||
strīṇāṃ śīlamiha jñātumasmaddṛṣṭaṃ tathā śrutam ||| || 21 ||
etañjñātvā jugupsāṃ ca strīṇāṃ kāryā na ca tvayā ||
mūlaṃ dharmārthakāmānāṃ yatastā eva yoṣitaḥ || 22 ||
vahnirannaṃ jalaṃ caiva narāṇāṃ dhṛtihetavaḥ ||
ghātakāśca ta evoktāstathā jñeyāśca yoṣitaḥ || 23 ||
vṛttirloke yathā nāsti vahnayannasalilairvinā || |
gṛhāśramastathā nāsti vinā strībhiḥ kathañcana || 24 ||
tasmāttāḥ prajñayā rakṣyā nogreṇa mṛdunā na ca ||
striyo mūlamanarthānāṃ trivargasya ca sādhanam || 25 ||
tāsāṃ na gacchedviśvāsaṃ maṇivadgopayettu tāḥ ||
gṛhakāryodyatāḥ kṛtvā rakṣyāstā bhūtimicchatā || 26 ||
etacchrutvā dvitīyehni pūjitaḥ sa tayā striyā ||
jagāma dhanadāvāsaṃ tato'pi śvaśurālayam || 27 ||
tataḥ śvaśuramāsādya sarvaṃ tasmai nyavedayata ||
sa ca papraccha taṃ vipraṃ śrutvā strīṇāṃ viceṣṭitam || 28 ||
buddhirniveṣṭuṃ sañjātā na vā jātā vadasva tat ||
etacchrutvottaraṃ vākyamaṣṭāvakro'bravīnmunim ||29||
na cāpyeṣā gatiḥ kṣemyā na cānyā vidyate gatiḥ ||
tasmātprayaccha dharmajña patnyarthaṃ svasutāṃ mama || 3.224.30 ||
etacchrutvā dadau tasmai svasutāṃ bhārgavo muniḥ ||
tithau divasanakṣatre muhurte cābhipūjite || 31 ||
trivargamūlaṃ samprāpya tāṃ bhāryāmasitekṣaṇām ||
jagāma paramāṃ tuṣṭiṃ mumude ca tayā saha ||32||
rarakṣa caināṃ sa tato mahātmā kṛtvā ca nityaṃ gṛhakāryayuktām ||
ārādhayāmāsa ca sā dvijendraṃ dharme niviṣṭā paramā ca sādhvī || 33 ||
iti śrīviṣṇudharmottare tṛtīyakhaṇḍe mārkaṇḍeyavajrasaṃvāde aṣṭāvakradiksaṃvādo nāma caturviṃśatyuttaradviśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ || 224 ||
<3.225>
mārkaṇḍeya uvāca ||
vrataṃ kaṃcinmamācakṣva sarvakalmaṣanāśanam ||

mārkaṇḍeya uvāca ||
rocaṃ tu pauruṣaṃ prāpya sopavāso jitendriyaḥ || 2 ||
pauruṣeṇa ca sūktena snātaḥ prayatamānasaḥ ||
tenaiva tarpaṇaṃ kṛtvā jāpyaṃ ca manujottama || 3 ||
aṣṭapatrasya padmasya madhye haṃsaṃ janārdanam ||

dīpena ca tato rājannaivedyena tataḥ param || 5 ||
pauruṣeṇa ca sūktena tato vahniṃ samarcayet ||
tatastu dhenurdātavyā karmānte ca payasvinī || 6 ||
saṃvatsaramidaṃ kṛtvā vrataṃ parama pāvanam ||
sarvānkāmānavāpnoti ye divyā ye ca mānuṣāḥ || 7 ||
aśvamedhamavāpnoti rājasūyaṃ ca vindati||

māṅgalyametatparamaṃ pavitraṃ śrīvardhanaṃ dharmavivardhanaṃ ca ||
vratottamaṃ pāpaharaṃ nṛloke haṃsavratākhyaṃ puruṣeṇa kāryam || 9 ||
iti śrīviṣṇudharmottare tṛ0 khaṇḍe mārkaṇḍeyavajrasaṃvāde haṃsavratanirūpaṇo nāma pañcaviṃśatyuttaradviśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ || 225 ||
<3.226>
vajra uvāca ||
viṣṇunā devadevena haṃsarūpeṇa kiṃ kṛtam ||
enaṃ me saṃśayaṃ chindhi mārkaṇḍeya mahāmune ||1||
mārkaṇḍeya uvāca ||
āsītprajā jñānaniṣṭhā pūrvaṃ kṛtayuge nṛpa ||
janānāṃ jñānaniṣṭhatvāttadā yadukulodvaha || 2 ||
svayaṃ prabhutvamabhavattathā devagaṇāni ca ||
tathā kṛtayugasyānte prāvartanta parigrahe ||3||
parigrahāttato lobho lobhāddveṣo'bhavattataḥ ||
rāgadveṣābhibhūtānāṃ jñānamantaradhīyata || 4 ||
naṣṭajñāne tathā loke dharmo nāśamupāgamat ||
dharme naṣṭe tu naśyantaṃ dṛṣṭvā lokaṃ janārdanaḥ || 5 ||
bhūtvā haṃsassamo varṇe vijahāra mahīmimām ||
supakṣo devacaraṇaḥ sucañcuḥ svāyatekṣaṇaḥ || 6||
sa sadarśa tadā deśe kasmiṃścitsaṃsthitānṛṣīn ||
dhyāyanto mūḍhavijñānāḥ kiṃkartavyaparāyaṇāḥ ||7||
sa tāndṛṣṭvā tu papraccha teṣāṃ kuśalamavyayam ||
te tamūcuḥ kuto naṣṭe jñāne naḥ kuśalaṃ dvija || 8 ||
tānabravīnmunīnsarvānsa haṃsaḥ pakṣirāṭ tataḥ ||
jñānaṃ prakāśayiṣyāmi taṃ nibodhata sattamāḥ || 9 ||
etacchrutvā śubhaṃ vākyaṃ haṃsenoktaṃ mahātmanā ||
haṃsamūcurmahātmāno bhava trātā dvijottama || 3.226.10 ||

tānabravīddvijānhaṃso viṣṇurasmi dvijottamāḥ || || 12 ||
taṃ te jñātvā tadā viṣṇuṃ pūjayāmāsurañjasā ||
abruvaṃśca tathā jñānaṃ yathoktaṃ tena yādava || 13 ||

tataḥ prabhṛti loko'smiñjñāninaḥ kecideva tu || 14 ||
evaṃ kṛtvā viṣṇunā haṃsarūpa jñānaṃ naṣṭaṃ vipramukhyeṣu dattam ||
jñāne prāpte bhūtale naṣṭaśokāḥ sarve cāsanvipramukhyā nṛvīra || 15 ||
iti śrīviṣṇudharmottare tṛ0 kha0 mārkaṇḍeyavajrasaṃvāde haṃsagītāyāṃ haṃsaprādurbhāvavarṇanonāma ṣaḍviṃśatyuttaradviśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ ||226 ||
<3.227>
vajra uvāca ||
kimuktaṃ tena haṃsena ṛṣīṇāṃ bhāvitātmanām ||
etaṃ me saṃśayaṃ chindhi bhṛguvaṃśavivardhana ||1||
mārkaṇḍeya uvāca ||
bahūktaṃ tena haṃsena carṣīṇāṃ bhāvitātmanām ||

ṛṣaya ūcuḥ ||
varṇānāmāśramāṇāṃ ca brūhi dharmaṃ dvijottama||
tathā bhakṣyamabhakṣyaṃ ca śaucāśaucaṃ tathaiva ca||3||
haṃsa uvāca||
yajanaṃ yājanaṃ dānaṃ viśiṣṭaśca pratigṛhaḥ ||
adhyāpanamadhyayanaṃ viprakarma prakīrtitam || 4 ||
dānamadhyayanaṃ yajñaḥ pāśupālyaṃ vaṇikkriyā ||
amātsaryaṃ ca dharmajña dharmassādhāraṇo mataḥ || 5 ||
ānulomyena varṇānāṃ jātirmātṛsamā bhavet ||
pratilomodbhavāḥ sarve bhavantyāryavigarhitāḥ||6||

anye vaktuṃ na śakyante saṅkarātsaṅkarodbhavāḥ ||
vivāhaḥ sadṛśaścaiṣāṃ nottamairnādhamaiḥ saha || 9 ||
sūtānāmaśvasārathyaṃ māgadhānāṃ stutikriyā ||
