āyogavasya śilpāni raṃgāvataraṇaṃ tathā ||
caṇḍālānāmasaṃsparśastathā vaidhavyagāmitā || || 11 ||
gobrāhmaṇārthe samare hatānāṃ strībālahetoryadi vāpyaśeṣam ||
svāmyarthakāryaṃ yadi vā prakurvatāṃ siddhiḥ pradiṣṭā na hi saṃśayo'tra || 12 ||
iti śrīviṣṇudharmottare tṛ0 kha0 mārkaṇḍeyavajrasaṃvāde haṃsagītāyāṃ varṇadharmavarṇano nāma saptaviṃśaduttaradviśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ || 227 ||
<3.228>
haṃsa uvāca ||
upanīya guruḥ śiṣyaṃ śikṣayecchaucamāditaḥ ||
ācāramagnikāryaṃ ca sandhyopāsanameva ca || 1 ||
prayataḥ kalyamutthāya snāto hutahutāśanaḥ ||
abhigamya guroḥ kuryātprayatastvabhivādanam || 2 ||
anujñātastu guruṇā tatodhyayanamācaret ||
bhaikṣyacaryāṃ tataḥ kuryādbrāhmaṇeṣu yathāvidhi || 3 ||
guroḥ kule na bhikṣeta bhuñjīta tadanujñayā ||
hitapriye guroḥ kārye bhaktyāhaṅkāravarjitaḥ || 4 ||
madhumāṃsāñjanaṃ śrāddhaṃ nṛttaṃ gītaṃ ca sāhasam ||
hiṃsā parāpavādaṃ ca maṇḍanaṃ ca vivarjayet || 5 ||
mekhalāmajinaṃ daṇḍaṃ dhārayetprayataḥ sadā ||
vinaṣṭānyapsu vikṣipya tathānyānapi dhārayet || 6 ||
adhaḥśāyī bhavennityaṃ brahmacārī samāhitaḥ ||
evaṃ vratastu kurvīta vedasvīkaraṇaṃ budhaḥ || 7 ||
gurave vacanaṃ dattvā snāyācca tadanujñayā ||
āśarīravimokṣādvā vasadgurukule dvijāḥ||8||
evaṃ vratānte guruve ca dattvā varaṃ yathāvatparipūrṇavidyaḥ|
gṛhāśramī syādvidhivaddijendro yātrāsyalokadvitīyaṃ pradiṣṭam || 9 ||
iti śrīviṣṇudharmottare tṛ0kha0 mārkaṇḍeyavajrasaṃvāde haṃsagītāyāṃ brahmacārivratavarṇano nāmāṣṭāviṃśaduttaradviśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ ||228|| ||
<3.229>
|| haṃsa uvāca || ||
tato gṛhāśrame tiṣṭhannagnīnādhāya dharmavit ||
kṛtvā vivāhaṃ vidhivaddharmeṇa ca dhanāgamam || 1 ||
trivargaṃ tatra seveta yajñaiśca vividhairyajet ||
ācārapālanaṃ kuryādvedādhyayanameva ca || 2 ||
utsargaṃ chandasāṃ kuryācchuklānte śrāvaṇasya ca ||
pauṣamāsasya rohiṇyāmupākarma vidhīyate || 3 ||
utsargānantaraṃ vedaṃ nādhīyīta kadācana ||
tatrāpi śāstrādhyayanaṃ dvijātīnāṃ vidhīyate || 4 ||
yatnaḥ kāryaḥ pūjane cātithīnāṃ caitatkāryaṃ sarvakālaṃ gṛhasthaiḥ ||
nānyo dharmo vidyate tasya viprāścaitaddharmaṃ tasya śreṣṭhaṃ pradiṣṭam ||5||
iti śrīviṣṇudharmottare tṛtīyakhaṇḍe mārkaṇḍeyavajra saṃvāde haṃsagītāyāṃ gṛhasthadharmavarṇano nāmaikonatriṃśaduttaradviśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ || 229 ||
<3.230>
haṃsa uvāca ||
kīnāśabaddhacaurāṇāṃ klībaraṅgāvatāriṇām ||
patitavrātyaduṣṭānāṃ tathā bandhanarakṣiṇām || 1 || 
udakyāvidhavāvāpapāṣaṇḍavadhajīvinām ||
cikitsājīvakavyādhapuṃścalīnṛpavidviṣām ||2 || 
carmakārāyudhājīviśastravikrayakāriṇām ||
cailadhāvanṛśaṃsārtastrījitagrāmayājinām || 3 ||
tunnavāyakadambasya kūṭakṛcchūdrayājinām ||
eṣāmannaṃ na bhoktavyaṃ yaśca dadyādanarcitam || 4 ||
paryāyānnaṃ kṣutaṃ caiva saṃghuṣṭaṃ patitekṣitam ||
śulkaṃ paryuṣitocchiṣṭaṃ tathā cocchiṣṭajīvinaḥ || 5 ||
udakyayā tathā spṛṣṭaṃ yacca ghrātaṃ gavā śunā ||
keśakīṭāvapannaṃ ca pakṣijagdhaṃ tathaiva ca || 6 ||
dāsārdhasīri gopāla nāpitānnaṃ tathāvinām ||
śūdrasyānnaṃ tathā śrāddhamātmavikrayiṇastathā || 7 ||
annaṃ paryuṣitaṃ bhojyaṃ snehāktaṃ rāgaṣāṇḍavat ||
nisnehā api godhūmayavapaurāṇanimbakāḥ || 8 ||
ajāgomahiṣīvarjyaṃ sarvāṇyapi payāṃsi ca ||
anirdaśāhaṃ teṣāṃ vai vivatsāyāśca gostathā || 9 ||
kapiñjalastittiriśca mayūro lāvakastathā ||
ete hi pakṣiṇo bhakṣyā na tu śeṣāḥ kathañcana || 3.230.10 ||
śaśakaḥ śalyako godhā khaḍgaḥ kūrmastathaiva ca ||
bhakṣyāḥ pañcanakheṣvāhurabhakṣyāścobhayādataḥ || 11 ||
sarve caikaśaphā varjyāstathā jñātā mṛgadvijāḥ ||
laśunaṃ gṛñjanaṃ caiva palāṇḍukavakāni ca || 12 || 
lohitānvṛkṣaniryāsāñśigruśleṣmātakaṃ tathā||
abhakṣyāṇi dvijātīnāṃ khurkhaṇḍāni tathaiva ca || 13 ||
vṛthā kṛsarasaṃyāvapāyasāpūpaśaṣkulīḥ ||
anupākṛtamāṃsāni devānnāni havīṃṣi ca || 14 ||
māṃsāśanaṃ naiva budhastu kuryādvṛthā dvijendrā narakasya hetum ||
māṃsaṃ hi yo nātti sa yāti nākaṃ loke tathāsminsukhasaṅgatiḥ syāt || 15 || 
iti śrīviṣṇudharmottare tṛtīyakhaṇḍe mārkaṇḍeyavajrasaṃvāde haṃsagītāyāṃ bhakṣyābhakṣyavarjanaṃ nāma triṃśaduttaradviśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ || 230 ||
<3.231>
|| || haṃsa uvāca || ||
pāṣāṇarukmarajatasannatoyājavarmaṇām ||
patramūlaphalānāṃ ca tṛṇānāṃ ca tathā dvijāḥ || 1 ||
vaidalānāṃ ca rajjūnāṃ vāriṇā śuddhiriṣyate ||
uṣṇena pātracamasasruksruvāṇāṃ vidhīyate || 2 || 
bhūśuddhirmārjanāddāhātkālāṅgokramāṇāttathā ||
amlodakena tāmrasya tathā kāṃsyasya bhasmanā || 3 ||
nirmalīkaraṇācchuddhiḥ sarvalohasya ceṣyate ||
sphyaśūrpājinadhānyānāṃ musalolūkhalāśmanām || 4 ||
prokṣaṇaṃ saṃhatānāṃ ca bahūnāṃ dhānyavāsasām ||
gomūtreṇa ca śṛṅgāsthnāṃ takṣaṇāddāravasya ca || 5 ||
dravāṇāmapi dravyāṇāṃ śuddhirutplavanātsmṛtā ||
soṣṇairudakagomūtraiḥ śuddhyantyāvikakauśikāḥ || 6||
saśrīphalairaṃśupaṭṭaṃ māñjiṣṭhaiḥ kutapaṃ tathā ||
sagaurasarṣapaiḥ kṣaumāḥ punaḥ pākairmahīmayam || 7 ||
malairmadyaiḥ surābhiśca spṛṣṭaṃ pākānna śuddhyati || 
vasāśukramasṛṅmañjāmūtraviṭkarṇaviṇṇakhāḥ || 8 ||
śleṣmāśrudūṣikāḥ svedo dvādaśaite nṛṇāṃ malāḥ||
khārjūramaikṣavaṇṭāṅkaṃ mṛdvīkārasameva ca || 9 ||
madyānyetāni jānīyācchuktamāsavameva ca ||
gauḍī paiṣṭī ca mādhvī ca vijñeyā trividhā surā || 3.231.10 ||
amedhyāṅgasya mṛttoyaśuddhirgandhāpakarṣaṇāt ||
kṛtvā mūtrapurīṣaṃ ca bhuktvā suptotthitastathā || 11 ||
rathyāmākramya cācāmedupaviṣṭa udaṅmukhaḥ ||
śāvaṃ tadaṅgadhūpaṃ ca cāṇḍālaṃ sūtikāṃ tathā || 12 ||
śavaspṛśaṃ tathā spṛṣṭvā kaṭadhūme samaithune ||
snānameteṣu kartavyamaśrupāte ca duḥkhaje || 13 ||
sarveṣāṃ vāyunā śuddhirgṛhāṇāṃ lepanena ca ||
ākarāḥ śucayaḥ sarve mukhajāyāśca vipluṣaḥ || 14 ||
rājā na duṣṭo na tathā ca vāyurduṣṭā dvijendrā na ca makṣikā vai ||
aduṣṭamuktaṃ ca tathāpyadṛṣṭasadṛṣṭamuktaṃ ca tathāṃśujālam || 15 ||
iti śrīviṣṇudharmottare tṛtīyakhaṇḍe mārkaṇḍeyavajrasaṃvāde haṃsagītāyāṃ dravyaśuddhivarṇano nāmaikatriṃśaduttaradviśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ || 231 ||
<3.232>
haṃsa uvāca ||
daśāhaṃ śāvamāśaucaṃ sapiṇḍeṣu vidhīyate ||
brāhmaṇasya dvijaśreṣṭha dvādaśāhaṃ mahībhujaḥ || 1 ||
pakṣaṃ vaiśyasya śūdrasya māsaṃ caiva vidhīyate ||
janane caiva maraṇe naipuṇyācchu ddhimicchatām || 2 ||
prathamānte viśuddhiḥ syātsamānasya dvijottamāḥ ||
dvitīyānte tathā jñeyā asamānasya nityadā || 3 ||
avyatīte daśāhe tu śrutvā maraṇajanmanī ||
yaccheṣaṃ daśarātrasya tāvadevāśucirbhavet || 4 ||
atīte daśarātre tu varṣasyābhyantare tathā ||
trirātrācchuddhiruddiṣṭā snātasya tu tataḥ param || 5 ||
rātribhirmāsatulyābhirgarbhasrāve viśodhanam ||
ādantajanmanaḥ sadya ācūḍasyāpyaharniśam || 6 ||
trirātramāvratādeśāddaśarātramataḥ param ||
kanyānāṃ tu vratasthāne vivāhaḥ parikīrtitaḥ || 7 ||
vivāhitāsu nāśaucaṃ pitṛpakṣe vidhīyate ||
mātāmahe trirātraṃ syādbhāryāsvanyagatāsu ca || 8 ||
parapūrvāsu ca svāsu putreṣvanyagateṣu ca ||
ācārye ca tathā prete nivāsavasudhādhipe || 9 ||
ācāryapatniputreṣu śiṣye sabrahmacāriṣu ||
ekarātramaśaucaṃ syādannade tu tathā mṛte || 3.232.10 || 
bhṛgvagnināśakāmbhobhirhatānāmātmaghātinām ||
patitānāṃ ca nāśaucaṃ vidyucchastrahatāśca ye || 11 || 
chañivratibrahmacārinṛpakośakadīkṣitāḥ ||
nāśaucabhājaḥ kathitā rājājñākāriṇaśca ye || 12 ||
atīta vārṣike śūdre pañcāhācchuddhiriṣyate ||
dvādaśāhena śuddhiḥ syādatīte vatsaratraye || 13 ||
gataiḥ saṃvatsaraiḥ ṣaḍabhirmāsena parikīrtitā ||
pretānugamane snānaṃ ghṛtaprāśaśca śodhanam ||14||
jananamaraṇayordaśāhena bhavati na śaucavidhirna viprabhojyam||
na ca bhavati pratigrahepi doṣo dvipadacatuṣpadadhānya dakṣiṇāsu || 15 ||
iti śrīviṣṇudharmottare tṛtīyakhaṇḍe mārkaṇḍeyavajrasaṃvāde haṃsagītāyāmāśaucavidhivarṇano nāma dvātriṃśaduttaradviśatatamo' dhyāyaḥ || 232 ||
<3.233>
haṃsa uvāca ||
aṅgulyagre smṛtaṃ daivaṃ pitryaṃ tarjanimūlagam ||
aṅguṣṭhamūle brāhmaṃ tu kaniṣṭhāyāśca mānuṣam || 1 ||
upaspṛśettu brāhmeṇa devā divyena tarpayet ||
pitṝnpitryeṇa dharmajña mānuṣyeṇa tathā pibet||2||
avikārye tathā japye yajane yājane tathā ||
adhyāpane cādhyayane bhavennānyamanā naraḥ||3||
eteṣveva tu kāryeṣu gurūṇāmapi sannidhau ||
uddhareddakṣiṇaṃ pāṇiṃ gṛhṇīyānna ca pāduke ||4||
agnikāryaṃ devakāryamupasparśanameva ca ||
bhojanaṃ pitṛkāryaṃ ca bahirjānu na kārayet ||5||
na nāma dīkṣitasyeha gṛhṇīyād vṛṣṭayaḥ kvacit ||
gurorna kevalaṃ nāma gṛhītavyamathāpi ca||6||
yācamānaṃ yathāśaktyā na kuryādvimukhaṃ kvacit ||
daivaṃ pūrvāhṇikaṃ kāryaṃ tvaparāhṇe tu paitṛkam || 7 ||
atithīnāṃ tathā pūjā sarvakālaṃ vidhīyate ||
yadviśiṣṭatamaṃ loke yaccāsti dayitaṃ gṛhe || 8 ||
tattadguṇavate deyaṃ tadevākṣayamicchatā ||
priyāṇi labhate loke priyadaḥ priyakṛttathā || 9 ||
pratiśrutaṃ prayatnena dātavyamṛṣisattamāḥ ||
aprayacchaṃstadāpnoti mūrtīṣṭakṛtasaṃśayam || 3.233.10 ||
narakaṃ mahadāpnoti śṛgālaścaiva jāyate ||
mātā pitā guruścaiva jyeṣṭho bhrātā tathā svasā || 11 ||
pitṛvyo mātulaścaiva tathā mātṛṣvasā dvijāḥ ||
pitṛṣvasā tathā śvaśrūrgurubhāryā tathaiva ca|| || 12 ||
mātāmahaśca śvaśuraḥ pūjanīyāḥ prayatnataḥ ||
pratyutthānaśca kartavyaṃ teṣāṃ pūrvābhivādanam || 13 ||
mātulaśca pitṛvyaśca yadā syātāṃ kanī yasau ||
nābhivādyāstu te pūrvaṃ pratyutthānaṃ tu kārayet || 14 ||
ucchiṣṭo na spṛśecchīrṣaṃ sarve prāṇāstadāśrayāḥ ||
pūrvāṃ sandhyāṃ japaṃstiṣṭhedāsīnaścaiva paścimām || 15 ||
madhyandine niśākāle tvardharātre viśeṣataḥ ||
catuṣpathaṃ na seveta upasandhye tathaiva ca || 16 ||
nāstikyaṃ vedanindāñca devatānāñca kutsanam ||
dveṣastambhātimānau ca taikṣṇyaṃ caiva vivarjayeta || 17 ||
parasya daṇḍaṃ nodyacchetkruddho nainaṃ nipātayet ||
anyatra putrācchiṣyādvā śiṣṭyarthaṃ tāḍanaṃ smṛtam || 18 ||
na brāhmaṇaṃ parivadennakṣatrāṇi na nirdiśet ||
tithiṃ pakṣasya na brūyāttathāsyāyurna riṣyate || 19 ||
prāgastaśāyī na bhavenna cābhyudayaśāyikaḥ ||
avalokyo na cādarśo malino buddhimattaraiḥ || 3.233.20 ||
akṛtvā devatāpūjāṃ nānyaṃ gacchetkadācana ||
anyatra tu guruṃ vṛddhaṃ dhārmikaṃ vā vicakṣaṇam||21||
varjayecca tathā prājñāścāsanākarṣaṇaṃ sadā ||
snātvā tu nāvamājeta gātrāṇi tu vicakṣaṇaḥ || 22||
viparyayaṃ na kurvīta vāsaso buddhimānnaraḥ ||
tathā nānyadhṛtaṃ dhāryaṃ na cāpadaśameva ca || 23 ||
anyadeva bhavedvāsaḥ śayanīye dvijottamāḥ ||
anyadrathyāsu devānāṃ tvarcāyāmanyadeva tu || 24 ||
upavāsaṃ tu kurvīta snātaḥ śuciralaṃkṛtaḥ ||
parvakāleṣu sarveṣu brahmacārī tathā bhavet || 25 ||
pippalaṃ ca vaṭañcaiva śaṇaśākaṃ tathaiva ca ||
udumbaraṃ na khādeta bhavyārthī puruṣaḥ sadā ||26 ||
putramāṃsopamaṃ māṃsaṃ pṛṣṭhamāṃsaṃ ca varjayet ||
pāṇau ca lavaṇaṃ rātrau dadhisaktūṃstathaiva ca || 27 ||
sāyaṃ prātaśca bhuñjīta nāntarāle kadācana ||
sāyaṃ prātarmanuṣyāṇāmaśanaṃ devanirmitam || 28 ||
bhūmau sadaiva cāśnīyānna cāsīno ca śabdavat ||
toyapūrṇaṃ yadā pānamatithibhyo dvijottamāḥ || 29 ||
paścādbhuñjīta medhāvī na cāpyanyamanā naraḥ ||
na dhāvedvarṣamāṇe tu nadīṃ mattāṃ na laṅghayet ||3.233.30||
gāṃ carantīṃ pibantañca vatsakaṃ na niṣedhayeta ||
samānamekapaṅktyāṃ tu deyamannaṃ tathā bhavet||31||
paṅktyāntu viṣamaṃ dattvā narakaṃ pratipadyate ||
viṣaṃ hālāhalaṃ bhuṅkte yo pradāya suhṛjjane || 32 ||
loṣṭhamardī tṛṇacchedī nakhakhādī ca yo naraḥ ||
nityocchiṣṭo bhavedyaḥ sa nehāyurvindate mahat || 33 ||
paraṃ jñeyamanāyuṣyaṃ paradārābhimarśanam ||
udakyāgamanaṃ jñeyamalaṃghyaṃ paramaṃ sukham || 34 ||
purīṣamūtre nodīkṣennādhitiṣṭhetkadācana ||
udakyayā tu saṃbhāṣāṃ na tu kurvīta karhicit || 35 ||
nādhitiṣṭhettuṣaṃ jātu keśabhasmakapālikāḥ ||
anyasya cāpyupasthānaṃ dūrataḥ parivarjayet || 36 ||
trīṇi tejāṃsi nocchiṣṭo nālabheta kadācana ||
agniṃ gāṃ brāhmaṇaṃ caiva tathā hyāyurna riṣyate || 37 ||
trīṇi tejāṃsi cocchiṣṭo nodīkṣeta kadācana ||
sūryācandramasau caiva nakṣatrāṇi ca sarvaśaḥ || 38 ||
nāvamanyeta dharmajña trīṇi tejāṃsi paṇḍitaḥ ||
agniṃ ca kulaputraṃ ca brāhmaṇañca dvijottamāḥ || 39 || 
trīnkṛśānnāvajānīyāddīrghamāyurjijīviṣuḥ ||
brāhmaṇaṃ kṣatriyaṃ sarpaṃ sarve hyāśīviṣāstrayaḥ || 3.233.40 ||
nādhyāpayedathocchiṣṭo nādhīyīta kadācana ||
sarvaṃ ca tilasaṃbaddhannādyādastamite ravau || 41 ||
ūrdhvaprāṇā hyutkramanti yūnaḥ sthavira āyati ||
pratyutthānābhivādābhyāṃ punastānpratipadyate || 42 ||
abhivādayeta vṛddhāṃśca āsanaṃ caiva dāpayet ||
kṛtāñjalirupāsīta gacchataḥ pṛṣṭhato'nviyāt || 43 ||
nāsīta cāsane bhinne bhinnaṃ kāsyaṃ ca varjayet ||
naikavastreṇa bhoktavyaṃ na nagnaḥ snānamarhati || 44 ||
guruṇā vairanirbandho na karttavyaḥ kadācana ||
anumānyaḥ prasādyaśca guruḥ kruddho vijānatā || 45 ||
dūrādāvasathānmūtraṃ dūrādātmāvasecanam ||
ucchiṣṭonmārjanaṃ caiva dūrātkāryaṃ vijānatā || 46 ||
upānaho ca vastraṃ ca dhṛtamanyairna dhārayet ||
pādaṃ pādena nākrāmenna kaṇḍūyena śaucayet || 47 || 
hīnāṅgānatiriktāṅgānvidyāhīnānvigarhitān ||
rūpadraviṇahīnāṃśca sattvahīnāṃśca nākṣipet || 48 ||
bhuñjāno brāhmaṇaśreṣṭho naiva śaṅkāṃ samācaret ||
dadhi cāpyanupānaṃ ca na karttavyaṃ bhavārthinā || 49 ||
ācamya caiva hastena parisrāvyaṃ tathodakam ||
aṅguṣṭhacaraṇasyāgre dakṣiṇasya tathopari || 3.233.50 ||
parāpavādaṃ na brūyānnāpriyaśca kadācana ||
na manyuḥ kasyacitkāryaḥ kadācidapi vai dvijaḥ || 51 ||
homaścānudite sūrye dātavyo dvijapuṅgavāḥ ||
tāvadbhavati vahnistho yāvannodeti bhāskaraḥ || 52 ||
parīpsitaṃ ca vahnisthaḥ samyagdattāmathāhutim ||
tadā bhāsayate sarvaṃ trailokyaṃ sacarācaram || 53 ||
patitaistu kathāṃ neccheddarśanaṃ cāpi varjayet ||
saṃsargaṃ na ca gacchettu tathāsyāyurna riṣyate || 54 ||
vṛddho jñātistathā mitraṃ daridraṃ ca kulodgatam ||
gṛhe vāsayitavyāni dhanyāni munisattamāḥ || 55 ||
brāhmaṇasya padābhyāṃ tu vasetpūjitamālayam |||
sandhyāsvadhyayanaṃ varjyaṃ bhojanaṃ svapanakriyā || 56 ||
naktaṃ na kuryātpitryāṇi bhuktvā caiva prasādhanam ||
pānīyasya kriyā naktaṃ na kāryā bhūtimicchatā || 57 ||
animantrito na gacchettu yajñaṃ gacchettu darśakaḥ || 58||
caritāni purāṇānāṃ śrotavyāni punaḥpunaḥ ||
śrutismṛtyuditaṃ dharmaṃ tvanutiṣṭhettathā sadā || 59 ||
sukhī syāttatprasaktastu iha loke paratra ca || 3.233.60 ||
śrutistu vedo vijñeyo dharmaśāstraṃ tathā smṛtiḥ |
te sarvārtheṣu mīmāṃsye tābhyāṃ dharmo hi nirbabhau || 61 ||
yo'vamanyeta bhūtebhyo hetuśāstrāśrayāddvijāḥ ||
sādhubhiḥ sa bahiṣkāryo narakāgnestu bhājanam || 62||
vedaḥ smṛtiḥ sadācāraḥ svasya ca priyamātmanaḥ ||
etaccaturvidhaṃ prāhuḥ sādhūnāṃ dharmalakṣaṇam ||63||
cāturvarṇyavyavasthānaṃ yasmindeśe na vidyate ||
sa mlecchaviṣayo jñeya āryāvarttamataḥ param ||64|| 
sarasvatīdṛṣadvatyordevanadyoryadantaram ||
taṃ devavihitaṃ deśaṃ brahmāvartaṃ pracakṣate || 65 ||
yasmindeśe ya ācāraḥ pāramparyakramāgataḥ ||
varṇānāṃ sāntarālānāṃ sa sadācāra ucyate || 66 ||
vaidikaiḥ karmabhiḥ puṇyairniṣekādirdvijanmanām ||
kāryāḥ śarīrasaṃskārāḥ pāvanāḥ pretya ceha ca||67||
gārbhaiḥ kṛtyairjātakarmacaulamauñjīnibaṃdhanaiḥ ||
vaijikaṃ gārbhikaṃ caino dvijānāmapahanyate || 68 ||
svādhyāyena vrataihoṃmaistraividyenejyayā sutaiḥ ||
mahāyajñaiśca yajñaiśca brāhmīyaṃ kriyate tanuḥ||69||
garbhāṣṭamebde kurvīta brāhmaṇasyopanāyanam ||
garbhādekadaśe rājño garbhāttu dvādaśe viśaḥ||3.233.70||
āṣoḍaśādbrāhmaṇasya sāvitrī nātivartate||
ādvāviṃśātkṣattrabandhorācaturvijaterviśaḥ||71||
ata ūrdhvaṃ trayopyete yathākālamasaṃskṛtāḥ||
sāvitrīpatitā vrātyā bhavanti ca vigarhitāḥ||72||
brahmaṇaḥ praṇavaṃ kuryādādāvante ca sarvadā||
sravatyanoṃkṛtaṃ pūrvaṃ parastācca viśīryate || 73 ||
indriyāṇāṃ vicaratāṃ viṣayeṣvapahāriṣu ||
saṃśaye yatnamātiṣṭhedvidvānyanteva vājinām || 74 ||
vaśīkṛtvendriyagrāmaṃ saṃyamya ca manastathā ||| 
sarvānsaṃsādhayatyarthānakṣiṇvanyogatastanum || 75 ||
na jātu kāmaḥ kāmānāmupabhogena śāmyati ||
haviṣā kṛṣṇavartmeva bhūya evābhivardhate ||76||
yā dustyajā durmatibhiryā na jīryati jīryataḥ ||
yāsau prāṇāntiko rogastāṃ tṛṣṇāṃ tyajataḥ sukham ||77||
trividhaṃ narakasyedaṃ dvāraṃ nāśanamātmanaḥ||
kāmaḥ krodhastathā lobhastasmādetattrayaṃ tyajet || 78 ||
indriyāṇāṃ prasaṅgena doṣamanvetyasaṃśayam ||
saṃniyamya tu tānyeva tataḥ siddhiṃ sa gacchati || 79 ||
abhivādanaśīlasya nityaṃ vṛddhopasevinaḥ ||
catvāri saṃpravardhante hyāyurmedhā yaśo balam || 3.233.80 ||
brāhmaṇaṃ kuśalaṃ pṛcchetkṣattrabandhumanāmayam ||
vaiśyaṃ kṣemaṃ samāgamya śūdraṃ tvārogyameva ca || 81 ||
vittaṃ bandhurvayaḥ karma vidyā bhavati pañcamī ||
etāni mānyasthānāni garīyo yadyaduttaram || 82 ||
na hāyanairna palitairna vittena na bandhubhiḥ ||
ṛṣayaścakrire dharmaṃ yo'nūcānaḥ sa no mahān || 83 ||
viprāṇāṃ jñānato jyaiṣṭhyaṃ kṣatriyāṇāṃ ca vīryataḥ ||
vaiśyānāṃ dhānyadhanataḥ śūdrāṇāmeva janmataḥ || 84 ||
ahiṃsayaiva bhūtānāṃ kāryaṃ śreyo'nupālanam ||
vāk caiva madhurā hyasyāḥ prayojyā dharmakāṃkṣiṇā || 85 ||
yasya vā'ṅmanasī śuddhe samyaggupte ca sarvadā ||
sa vai sarvamavāpnoti vedāntagamanātphalam ||86||
vedameva sadābhyasyettapastaptuṃ dvijottamāḥ ||
vedābhyāso hi viprasya tapaḥ paramihocyate || 87 ||
yo'nadhītya dvijo vedānanyatra kurute śramam ||
sa jīvanneva śūdratvamāśu gacchati sānvayaḥ || 88 ||
māturagre'dhijananaṃ dvitīyaṃ mauñjibandhanam ||
tṛtīyaṃ yajñadīkṣāyāṃ dvijasya śruticodanāt || 89 ||
na hyasminsajjate karma kiñcidāmauñjibandhanāta ||
nābhivyāhārayedbrahma svadhāniyamanādṛte ||| 3.233.90 ||
śūdreṇa hi samastāvadyāvadvedaṃ na jānate ||
kṛtopanayanasyāsya vratādeśanamiṣyate || 91 ||
guroryatra parīvādo nindā cāpi pravartate ||
karṇau tatra pidhātavyau gantavyaṃ vā tato'nyataḥ ||92||
kharo bhavetparīvādī śvā vai bhavati nindakaḥ ||
paribhoktā krimirbhavati kīṭo bhavati matsarī ||93||
dūrastho nārcayedenaṃ na kruddho nāntike striyāḥ ||
ekāsanastho na bhavedāpanno'pi kadācana|| 94||
gośvoṣṭrayānaprāsādasvastareṣu kaṭeṣu ca ||
nāsīta guruṇā sārdhaṃ śilāphalakanauṣu ca || 95 ||
gurorgurau sannihite guruvadvṛttimācaret ||
adhyāpayangurusuto guruvanmānamarhati || 96 ||
na kuryādguruputrasya pādayoścāvanejanama ||
guruvatpratipūjyāstu savarṇā guruyoṣitaḥ||97||
asavarṇāstu sampūjyāḥ pratyutthānābhivādanaiḥ ||
abhyañjanaṃ sthāpanaṃ ca gātrotsādanameva ca||98||
gurupatnyā na kāryāṇi keśānāṃ ca prasādhanam||
gurupatnī ca yuvatirnābhivādyeha pādayoḥ||99||
pūrṇā viṃśativarṣeṇa guṇadoṣau vijānatā||
mātrā svasrā duhitrā vā na viviktāsano bhavet||3.233.100||
balavānindriyagrāmo vidvāṃsamapi karṣati ||
viproṣya pādagrahaṇaṃ tvanvahañcābhivādanam ||101||
gurudāreṣu kurvīta satāṃ dharmamanusmaran ||
striyo ratnānyatho vidyā dharmassatyaṃ subhāṣitam||102||
śilpāni cāpyaduṣṭāni samādeyāni sarvataḥ||
abrāhmaṇādadhyayanaṃ cāpatkāle vidhīyate||103||
anuvrajyā ca śuśrūṣā yāvadadhyayanaṃ gurau ||
nābrāhmaṇagurau śiṣyo vāsamātyantikaṃ vaset || 104 ||
brāhmaṇe vānanūcāne kāṅkṣangatimanuttamāt ||
pitṛbhirmātṛbhirnāryaḥ patibhirdevaraistathā||105||
bhūṣyā bhūṣayitavyāśca bahukalyāṇamīpsubhiḥ||
yatra nāryastu pūjyante ramante tatra devatāḥ || 106 ||
yatraitāstu na pūjyante sarvāstatrāphalāḥ kriyāḥ ||
santuṣṭo bhāryayā bhartā bhāryā bhartrā tathaiva ca || 107 ||
yasminnetatkule nityaṃ kalyāṇaṃ tatra vai dhuvam ||
kuvivāhaiḥ kriyālopairvedānadhyayanena ca || 108 ||
kulānyakulatāṃ yānti brāhmaṇātikrameṇa ca ||
śilpena vyavahāreṇa śūdrāputraiśca kevalaiḥ ||109 ||
gobhiraśvaiśca yānaiśca kṛṣyā rājopasevayā ||
ayājya yājanāccaiva nāstikyena ca karmaṇā || 3.233.110
kulānyakulatāṃ yānti yāni hīnāni mantrataḥ ||
mantratastu samṛddhāni kulānyalpadhanānyapi || || 111 ||
kulasaṃkhyāntu gacchanti karṣanti ca mahadyaśaḥ ||
ṛṣayaḥ pitaro devā bhūtānyatithayastathā || 112 ||
āśāsate kuṭumbibhyastebhyaḥ kāryaṃ vijānatā || 
svādhyāyenārcayetarṣīnhomairdevānyathāvidhi || 113 ||
pitṝñchrāddhena nṝnannairbhūtāni balikarmaṇā ||
dadyādaharahaḥ śrāddhamannādyenodakena ca || 114 ||
payomūlaphalairvāpi pitṛbhyaḥ prītimāharan ||
ekamapyāśayedvipra pitrarthe pāñcayajñike || 115 ||
na caivātrāśāyetkañcidvaiśvadevaṃ prati dvijam ||
vaiśvadevasya siddhasya gṛhye'gnau vidhipūrvakam || 116 ||
svasūtravihitaṃ kuryādvaiśvadevamatandritaḥ ||
praṇīte vāprāṇīte vā hutotsṛṣṭe jale'pi vā || 117 ||
vaiśvadevaṃ dhruvaṃ kāryaṃ brāhmaṇena vipaścitā ||
paraṃ sthānamavāpnoti kṛtvaivaṃ satataṃ gṛhī ||
bhojayedatithiṃ paścādbhikṣāṃ dadyācca bhikṣave || 118 ||
yatpuṇyaphalamāpnoti gāṃ dattvā vidhivadgurau ||
tatpuṇyaphalamāpnoti bhikṣāṃ dattvā yathārcitām || 119 ||
vidyātapaḥsamṛddheṣu hutaṃ vipramukhāgniṣu ||
nistārayati durgeṣu bhavatyakṣayameva ca || 3.233.120 ||
saṃprāptāya tvatithaye pradadyādāsanodakam ||
annaṃ caiva yathāśakti satkṛtya vidhipūrvakam || 121 ||
śilānyapyuñchato nityaṃ pañcāgnīnapi juhvataḥ ||
sarvaṃ sukṛtamādatte brāhmaṇo'narcito vasan || 122 ||
tṛṇāni bhūmirudakaṃ vāk caturthī ca sūnṛtā ||
etānyapi satāṃ gehe nocchidyaṃte kadācana || 123 ||
ekarātrantu nivasannatithirbrāhmaṇaḥ smṛtaḥ ||
naikagrāmīṇamatithiṃ vipraṃ sāṅgatikaṃ tathā||124||
upāsate ye gṛhasthāḥ parapākamabuddhayaḥ ||
tena te pretya paśutāṃ vrajantyannādyadāyinām ||||29||
aprīṇanyotithiḥ sāyaṃ sūryānte gṛhamedhinā ||
kāle prāptastvakāle vā nāsyānaśnangṛhe vaseta || 126 ||
na vai svayaṃ tadaśnīyādatithiṃ yanna bhojayet ||
dhanyaṃ yaśasyamāyuṣyaṃ svargyañcātithipūjanam ||127 ||
vaiśvadeve tu nirvṛtte yadyanyo'tithirāvrajet ||
tathā cānnaṃ yathāśakti pradadyācca baliṃ haret || 128 ||
na bhojanārthe sve vipraḥ kulagotre nivedayet ||
bhojanārthe tu śaṃsanvā vāntāśītyucyate budhaiḥ || 129 ||
yadi tvatithidharmeṇa kṣattriyo gṛhamāvrajet ||
bhuktavatsu ca vipreṣu kāmaṃ tamapi pūjayet ||3.233.130||
vaiśyaśūdrāvapi prāptau kuṭumbe 'tithidharmiṇau ||
bhojayetsaha bhṛtyaistānānṛśaṃsyaṃ prayojayan ||131||
sakhyādīnbhāryayā sārdhaṃ bhojayedgṛhamāgatān ||
patitaśvapacebhyastu śvabhyastu bhuvi nirvapet || 132 ||
suvāsinīḥ kumārāṃśca rogiṇo garbhiṇīstathā ||
atithibhyo'gra evainānbhojayedavicārayan || 133 ||
adattvā tu ya etebhyaḥ pūrvaṃ bhuṃkte vicakṣaṇaḥ ||
sa bhuñjāno na jānāti śvagṛddhairjagdhamātmanaḥ || 134||
bhuktavatsu tu vipreṣu sveṣu bhṛtyeṣu caiva hi ||
bhuñjīyātāṃ tataḥ paścādavaśiṣṭaṃ tu dampatī || 135||
gṛhasthairdhāryate viprāstairlokyaṃ sacarācaram ||
pākaḥ kāryo gṛhasthena bhūtoddharaṇakāmyayā || 136 ||
aghaṃ sa kevalaṃ bhuṃkte yaḥ pacatyātmakāraṇāt||
vighasāśī bhavennityaṃ nityaṃ cāmṛtabhojanaḥ ||137||
vighasaṃ bhuktaśeṣaṃ tu yajñaśeṣaṃ tathāmṛtam ||
rājarṣisnātakācāryapriyaśvaśuramātulān ||138||
arhayenmadhuparkeṇa parisaṃvatsaroṣitān ||
dvau kālau gṛhiṇaḥ pāko vaiśvadevaśca kīrtyate || 139 ||
vedoditaṃ svakaṃ karma nityaṃ kuryādataṃdritaḥ ||
sarvānsamutsṛjedarthāsvāndhyāyasya virodhinaḥ ||3.233.140||
buddhivṛddhikarāṇyāśu dhanyāni ca hitāni ca ||
nityaṃ śāstrāṇyavekṣeta nigamāṃścaiva vaidikān ||141||
ṛṣiyajñaṃ devayajñaṃ bhūtayajñaṃ tathaiva ca ||
nṛyajñaṃ pitṛyajñaṃ ca pañcayajñānna hāpayet ||142||
agnihotraṃ ca juhuyādādyaṃte dyuniśoḥ sadā ||
darśena cārdhamāsāṃte paurṇamāse tathaiva ca ||143||
tayoḥ kālaḥ smṛtaḥ kṣīṇe pūrṇe ca śaśalakṣaṇe ||
paśunāpyāyanānte ca māsānte saumikairmakhaiḥ||144||
nāniṣṭvā navasasyeṣṭyā paśunā vāgnimāndvijaḥ||
navānnamadyānmāsaṃ vā dīrghamāyurjijīviṣuḥ||145||
navenānarcito hyasya paśuhavyena cāgnayaḥ||
prāṇānevāttumicchanti navānnāmiṣagarddhinaḥ||146||
āsanāśanaśayyābhiradbhirmūlaphalena vā||
nāsya kaścidvasedgehe śaktito 'narcito'tithiḥ||147||
pāṣaṇḍino vikarmasthānvaiḍālavratikāñśaṭhān||
haitukānbakavṛttīṃśca vāṅmātreṇāpi nārcayet||148||
na dānaṃ yaśase dadyānno bhayānnopakāriṇe ||
na nṛttagītaśīlebhyo hāsakebhyaśca dhārmikaḥ || 149 || 
vedavidyāvratasnātāñśrotriyāngṛhamedhinaḥ ||
pūjayeddhavyakavyeṣu viparītām̐stu varjayet || 3.233.150 ||
śaktito yajamānasya dātavyo gṛhamedhinaḥ ||
saṃvibhāgaśca bhūtebhyaḥ kartavyo'nuparodhataḥ || 151 ||
rājato dhanamanvicchetsaṃsīdansnātakaḥ kṣudhā ||
yājyāntevāsino vāpi na tvanyata iti sthitiḥ || 192 ||
na jīrṇamalavadvāsā bhavecca vibhave sati ||
svādhyāye nityayuktaḥ syānnityamātmahiteṣu ca||153||
vaiṇavīṃ dhārayedyaṣṭiṃ sodakaṃ ca kamaṇḍalum ||
yajñopavītaṃ vedaṃ ca śubhe raukme ca kuṇḍale || 154 ||
śuklavāsā bhavennityaṃ parimṛṣṭaparicchadaḥ ||
na caiva pralikhedbhūmiṃ nātmanopaharetsrajam || 155 ||
nāpsu mūtraṃ purīṣaṃ vā ṣṭhīvanaṃ vā samutsṛjet ||
amedhyaliptamanyadvā lohitaṃ vā viṣāṇi vā || 156 ||
etānyeva tathā vahnau nikṣipennaiva ca dvijaḥ ||
naikaḥ śūnyagṛhe svapyācchayānaṃ naiva bodhayet || 157 ||
na divīndrāyudhaṃ dṛṣṭvā kasyaciddarśayedbudhaḥ ||
na śūdrarājye nivasennādhārmikajanāvṛte || 158||
na pāṣaṇḍijanākīrṇe nopasṛṣṭe na parvate ||
vaidyasāṃvatsarairhīne tathā prabalanāyake || 159 ||
na kurvīta vṛthā ceṣṭāṃ na vāryañjalinā pibet ||
notsaṅge bhakṣayedbhakṣyānna jātu syātkutūhalī || 3.233.160 ||
na nṛtyedatha vā gāyedvāditrāṇi na vādayet ||
nāsphoṭayenna ca kṣveḍenna ca raktāṃ virāgayet||161||
na pādau dhāvayetkāṃsye na ca pādau pratāpayet ||
na cchindyānnakharomāṇi dantairnotpāṭayennakhān || 162 ||
pādayornaiva kurvīta darbheṣu parimārjanam ||
pālāśamāsanaṃ varjyaṃ pāduke dantadhāvanam || 163 ||
na karmaviphalaṃ kuryādāyatyāṃ vā sukhodayam ||
na vigarhyakathāḥ kuryādbahirmālyaṃ na dhārayet||164||
advāreṇa na cātīyādgrāmaṃ vā veśma caiva vā||
rātrau ca vṛkṣamūlāni dūrataḥ parivarjayet ||169||
ārdrapādastu bhuñjīta nārdrapādastu saṃvi śeta ||
na śūdrāya matiṃ dadyānnocchiṣṭaṃ na haviṣkṛtam ||166||
na cāsyopadiśeddharmaṃ na cāsya vratamādiśet ||
na rājñaḥ pratigṛhṇīyāllubdhasyocchāstravartinaḥ||167||
narakāṇi ca sarvāṇi dadyādrājapratigrahaḥ ||
daśasūnāsamaścakrī daśacakrasamo dhvajī||168||
daśadhvajasamā veśyā daśaveśyāsamo nṛpaḥ ||
etadvijñāya vai rājñāṃ na pragṛhṇanti sādhavaḥ || 169 ||
upākarmaṇi cotsarge trirātraṃ na paṭhed dvijaḥ ||
vedāṅgāni paṭhetkṛṣṇe śukle vedamatandritaḥ || || 3.233.170 ||
nāvispaṣṭamadhīyīta na śūdrajanasannidhau||
na niśānte pariśrānto brahmādhītya punaḥ svapet||171||
sāyaṃ niśīthe nādhyayanaṃ vāditre na ca vādyati||
atithiñcānanujñāpya mārute vāti vā bhṛśam||172|| 
paśumaṇḍūkamārjāraśvasarpanakulādibhiḥ||
kṛte'ntarālagamane rudhire ca tathā srute||173||
dvāvetau varjayennityamanadhyāyau dvijottamaḥ ||
svādhyāyabhūmiṃ cāśuddhāṃ cātmānamaśuciṃ dvijāḥ ||174||
devatānāṃ guro rājñaḥ snātakācāryayostathā||
nākrametkāmataśchāyāṃ babhruṇo dīkṣitasya ca||175||
nātmānamavamanyeta pūrvābhirasamṛddhibhiḥ||
āmṛtyoḥ śriyamanvicchenna tāṃ manyeta durlabhām||176||
satyaṃ brūyātpriyaṃ brūyānna brūyātsatyamapriyam ||
priyaṃ ca nānṛtaṃ brūyādeṣa dharmaḥ sanātanaḥ || 177 ||
bhadraṃ bhadramiti brūyādbhadramityeva vā vadet ||
śuṣkavairaṃ vivādaṃ ca na kuryātkenacitsaha || 178 ||
anāturaḥ svāni khāni na spṛśedanimittataḥ ||
gaṇānnaṃ gaṇikānnaṃ ca sūtakānnaṃ ca varjayet ||
sarvaṃ paravaśaṃ duḥkhaṃ sarvamātmavaśaṃ sukham ||179||
sāvitraśāntihomāṃśca kuryātsarvatra nityaśaḥ ||
pitṛṃścaivāṣṭakāsvarcennityamanvaṣṭakāsu ca || 3.233.180 ||
maṅgalācārayuktānāṃ nityañca prayatātmanām ||
japatāṃ juhvatāṃ caiva vinipāto na vidyate || 181 ||
na pādapāṇicapalo na netracapalo dvijaḥ ||
na syādvākca palaścaiva na paradrohakarmadhīḥ || 182 ||
yenāsya pitaro yātā yena yātāḥ pitāmahāḥ ||
tena yāyātsatāṃ mārgaṃ tena gacchanna riṣyate || 183 || 
ṛtvikpurohitācāryamātulātithisaṃśritaiḥ ||
bālavṛddhāturairvaidyairjñātisambandhibāndhavaiḥ || 184 ||
mātāpitṛbhyāṃ jāmībhirbhrātrā putreṇa bhāryayā ||
duhitrā dāsavargeṇa vivādaṃ na samācaret || 185 ||
etairvivādaṃ santyajya sarvāṃllokāñjayed gṛhī ||
tasmādetairadhikṣiptaḥ sahetāsaṃjvaraṃ sadā || 186 ||
pratigrahaṇasamartho'pi prasaṃgaṃ tatra varjayet ||
pratigraheṇa viprāṇāṃ brāhmaṃ tejo vinaśyati || 187 ||
na ca dravyamavijñāya vidhiṃ dharmyaṃ pratigrahe ||
prājñaḥ pratigrahaṃ kuryādavasīdannapi kṣudhā || 188||
atapāstvanadhīyānaḥ pratigraharucirdvijaḥ ||
ambhasya saṃplavenaiva saha tainaiva majjati || 189 ||
tasmānna vidvāngṛhṇīyādyasmāttasmātpratigraham ||
alpakenāpyavidvāṃstu paṅke gauriva sīdati || 3.233.190 ||
pratigrahasamartho'pi nādatte yaḥ pratigraham ||
ye lokā dānaśīlānāṃ sa tānāpnoti śāśvatān || 191 || 
gurūnbhṛtyānujjihīṣurarciṣyandevatātithīn ||
sarvataḥ pratigṛhṇīyānna tu tṛpyetsvayaṃ tataḥ || 192 ||
guruṣvabhyavatīteṣu vinā vātairgṛhe vasan ||
ātmano vṛttimanvicchangṛhṇīyātsādhutaḥ sadā || 193 ||
dharmadhvajo'tha vā lubdhaśchadmastho lokanindakaḥ ||
vaiḍālavratiko jñeyo hiṃsraḥ sarvātisādhakaḥ || 194 ||
adhodṛṣṭirnaikṛtikaḥ svārthasādhanatatparaḥ ||
śaṭho mithyāvinītaśca bakavratacaro dvijaḥ || 195 ||
ye bakavratino viprā ye ca mārjāraliṅginaḥ ||
te patantyandhatāmisraṃ saha pāpena karmaṇā || 196 ||
na dharmasyāpadeśena pāpaṃ kṛtvā vrataṃ caret ||
vratena pāpaṃ pracchādya kurvanstrīśūdradambhanama ||
pretyeha tādṛśo vipro gṛhyate brahmavādibhiḥ || 197 ||
chadmanā caritaṃ tacca vrataṃ rakṣāṃsi gacchati ||
aliṅgo liṅgiveśena yo vṛttimupajīvati || 198 ||
sa liṅgināṃ haratyenastiryagyonau ca jāyate ||
parakīyanipāneṣu na snāyāddhi kadācana ||
nipānakartuḥ snātvā tu duṣkṛtāṃśena lipyate || 199 ||
yānaṃ śayyāsanaṃ vastraṃ kūpodyānagṛhāṇi tu||
adattānyupabhuñjāno hyenasaḥ syātturīyabhāk ||3.233.200||
yamānseveta satataṃ na nityaṃ niyamānbudhaḥ ||
yamānsaṃtyajya kurvāṇo niyamānkevalānbhajan ||201||
śaucamijyā tapo dānaṃ svādhyāyopasthanigrahaḥ ||
vratopavāso maunaṃ ca snānaṃ ca niyamā daśa ||202 ||
ānṛśaṃsyaṃ kṣamā satyamahiṃsā ca damaḥ spṛhā ||
dhyānaṃ prasādo mādhuryaṃ cārjavaṃ ca yamā daśa ||203||
nāśrotriyatate yajñe grāmayājihute tathā ||
striyā klībena ca hute bhuñjīyādbrāhmaṇaḥ kvacit ||204||
yajño'nṛtena kṣarati tapaḥ kṣarati vismayāt ||205||
āyurviprāpavādena dānaṃ ca parikīrttanāt ||
dharmaṃ śanaiḥ sañcinuyādvalmīkamiva puttikāḥ || 206 ||
paralokasahāyārthaṃ parapīḍāṃ vivarjayet ||
uttamairuttamairni'tyaṃ sambandhānācaretsaha ||207 ||
ninīṣuḥ kulamutkarṣamadhamānadhamām̐styajet ||
edhodakaṃ mūlaphalamannamabhyuddhṛtaṃ ca yat || 208 ||
sarvataḥ pratigṛhṇīyānmadhvathābhayadakṣiṇām ||
āhṛtyābhyudyatāṃ bhikṣāṃ gṛhṇīyādapracoditām ||
anyatra kulaṭāṣaṇḍhapatitastenavidviṣaḥ || 209 ||
śayyāṃ gṛhāñśubhāngandhānāpaḥ puṣpamaṇīrdadhi ||
dhānā matsyāḥ payo māṃsaṃ śākaṃ caiva tu nirṇudeta || 3.233.210 || 
yo'nyathāsantamātmānamanyathā satsu bhāṣate ||
kiṃ tena na kṛtaṃ pāpaṃ caureṇātmāpahāriṇā || 211 ||
vācyarthā niyatāssarve vāṅmūlā vāgvinissṛtāḥ ||
tāṃ tu yaḥ stenayedvācaṃ sa sarvasteyakṛnnaraḥ || || 212 ||
santānikaṃ yakṣyamāṇamadhvagaṃ sarvavedagam ||
gurvarthaṃ pitṛmātrarthaṃ svādhyāyārthamatāpinaḥ || 213 ||
na caitānsnātakānvindyādbrāhmaṇāndharmabhaikṣukān ||
niḥsvebhyo deyametebhyo dānaṃ vidyā viśeṣataḥ || 214 ||
etebhyo hi dvijāgrebhyo deyamannaṃ sadakṣiṇam ||
itarebhyo bahirvedyāṃ kṛtānnaṃ deyamucyate || 215 ||
kṛtadāro'parāndārānbhikṣitvā yo'dhigacchati ||
ratimātraṃ phalaṃ tasya dravyadātustu santatiḥ || 216 ||
yasya traivārṣikaṃ bhaktaṃ paryāptaṃ bhṛtyavṛttaye ||
adhikaṃ vāpi vidyeta sa somaṃ pātumarhati || 217 ||
ataḥ svalpīyasi dravye yaḥ somaṃ pibati dvijaḥ ||
sa pītasomapūrvo'pi tasya nāpnoti tatphalam || 218 ||
saktaḥ parajane dātā svajane duḥkhajīvini ||
madhvāpītau viṣāsvādaḥ sa dharmapratirūpakaḥ|| 219 ||
bhṛtyānāmuparodhena yaḥ karotyaurdhvadaihikam ||
tadbhavatyamukhodarkaṃ jīvato'sya mṛtasya vā || 3.233.220 ||
na yajñārthe dvijaḥ śūdrādbhikṣāṃ lipseta dharmavit ||
yajamāno hi bhikṣitvā caṇḍālaḥ pretya jāyate || 221 ||
yajñārthamarthaṃ bhikṣitvā yo na sarvaṃ prayacchati ||
sa yāti bhāsatāṃ vipraḥ kākatāṃ vā śataṃ samāḥ || 222 ||
devasvaṃ brāhmaṇasvaṃ ca yo lobhānnopajīvati ||
sa pāpātmā pare loke gṛddhrocchiṣṭena jīvati || 223 ||
iṣṭiṃ vaiśvānarīṃ vipro nirvapedabdaparyaye ||
kṛtānāṃ paśusomānāṃ niṣkṛtyarthamasambhave || 224 ||
āpatkalpena yo dharmaṃ kurute'nāpadi dvijaḥ ||
samāpnoti phalaṃ tasya paratreti vicāritam || 225 ||
prabhuḥ prathamakalpasya yo'nukalpeṣu vartate ||
dharmeṇa yogaṃ nāpnoti narakaṃ caiva gacchati || 226 ||
kṣattriyo bāhuvīryeṇa taredāpadamātmanaḥ ||
dhanena vaiśyaśūdrau tu japahomairdvijātayaḥ || 227 ||
na vai kanyā na yuvatirnālpavidyo na bāliśaḥ ||
hotā syādagnihotreṣu nārto nāsaṃskṛtastathā || 228 || 
prājāpatyamadattvāśvamagnyādheyasya dakṣiṇām ||
anāhitāgnirbhavati brāhmaṇo vibhave sati ||229||
puṇyānyanyāni kurvīta śraddadhāno jitendriyaḥ ||
na tvalpadakṣiṇairyajñairyajeteha kathaṃcana || 3.233.230 ||
hantyalpadakṣiṇo yajñastasmānnālpadhano yajet ||
bālātapaṃ na seveta na divā maithunaṃ vrajet || 231 ||
prāyaścittīyatāṃ prāpya prāyaścittaṃ samācaret ||
mātuḥ svasāraṃ vā putrīṃ parabhāryāṃ prakīrtayet ||232||
nadīṃ tarenna bāhubhyāṃ varjayetkūpalaṅghanam ||
yathākathaṃcinnodīkṣedādityaṃ tejasāṃ nidhim || 233 ||
na kruddhasya gurorvaktraṃ naivādarśamamārjitam ||
snānabhojanapānāni vāhebhyo nācaretpunaḥ || 234 ||
yatnena bibhriyāddārānvāgmino'kṛpaṇāndvijān || 235 ||
baddhaṅgavādikaṃ yastu yavasenodakena ca||
pratijāgarti no vipraḥ sa yāti narakaṃ mahat || || 236 ||
nārohedviṣamaṃ śailaṃ nāvaṃ saṃśayitāṃstarūn ||
na pātayeta loṣṭena na kāṣṭhena phalaṃ drumāt || 237 ||
śiraḥsnātastu tailena nāṅgaṃ kiñcidapi spṛśet ||
vahenna bhāraṃ śirasā yugapannāgnivāriṇī || 338 ||
na toyaṃ prakṣipedvahnau vahniṃ toye tathaiva ca ||
varjayedatisauhārdaṃ bhojyānnasya ca kutsanam || 239 ||
rājadviṣṭaṃ gaṇadviṣṭaṃ lokadviṣṭaṃ vivarjayet ||
sāyaṃ prātaśca viduṣāṃ śṛṇuyātpuṣkalā giraḥ || 3.233.240 ||
saṃskṛtaṃ pāyasaṃ nityaṃ yavāgvāṃ kṛsaraṃ vadet ||
śmaśrukarmaṇi cāyuṣyaṃ jīvitena tathā kṛtiḥ || 241 ||
vyādhitānāṃ tu sarveṣāmāyuṣaḥ pratinandanam ||
śrāntasya viśramaśceti cāḍhyatāṃ sīriṇastathā || 242 ||
āsīnasya sukhaṃ cāpi puṇyāhamapi gacchataḥ ||
devakarmapravṛttasya prāsādaṃ parikīrtayet || 243 ||
na jātu tvamiti brūyādāpannopi mahattaram ||
tvaṃkāro vā vadho veti vidvatsu na viśiṣyate || 244 ||
avarāṇāṃ samānānāṃ śiṣyāṇāñcaitadiṣyate ||
eka eva careddharmaṃ na dharmadhvajiko bhavet || 245 ||
arceddevānna dambhena sevetāmāyayā gurūn ||
nidhiṃ vidadhyātpārakyaṃ yatrārthaḥ śabdavāniti || 246 ||
karma vai saphalaṃ kṛtvā gurūṇāṃ pratipādayet ||
vṛddhānnāti vadejjātu na tu sampreṣayetkvacit ||247||
na vegito'nyakāryaḥ syānnāditvānnāni kutsayet||
muktakeśo na gacchettu svapnāhārasabhāstriyaḥ||248||
pāṇinālabhya niṣkrāmedratnapūjyājyamaṇḍalam ||
pradakṣiṇantu kurvīta maṃgalyaṃ sarvadā yatiḥ||249||
sūnāñca bandhanāgāraṃ nṛpanṛtyagṛhe tathā ||
antaḥ puraṃ ca ṛṣayo dūrataḥ parivarjayet || 3.233.250 ||
triḥ pakṣasya kṛtaśmaśrurnakhalomāni kalpayet ||
na pratisphālayedambu pāṇinā caraṇena vā || 251 ||
nāsaṃvṛtamukhaḥ kuryātkṣutamaṃgavijṛmbhaṇam ||
nādvāreṇa suhṛdveśma na dvāreṇa riporviśet || 252 ||
na sarpaśastraiḥ krīḍettu nadīvegaṃ vivarjayet ||
vidyātpratikṣaṇaṃ mṛtyorayathātathaceṣṭitam || 253 ||
āharejjñānamarthāṃśca naro hyamaravatsadā ||
keśairiva gṛhītastu mṛtyunā dharmamācaret || 254 ||
praviśejjanasaṃbādhaṃ tathātigahanaṃ na ca ||
hema nāsaṃskṛtaṃ dhāryaṃ niśi loṣṭaṃ na ca kṣipet ||255||
ādau paṅktyāṃ na cāśnīyādvarjayedbhūmitāḍanam||
varjayecchātraśabalaṃ divā nidrāṃ tathaiva ca || 256 ||
kāmaḥ krodho bhayo harṣaṃ lobho darpastathaiva ca ||
ripavaḥ ṣaḍ vijetavyā puruṣeṇa vipaścitā || 257 || 
ariṣaḍvargasaṃnyāsātkuryādindriyanigrahama ||
yogakṣemaṃ sadotthānātprajñāṃ vṛddhopasevanāt || 258 ||
praśrayeṇa jayenmānaṃ dainyaṃ dṛṣṭāṃta saṃśrayāt ||
akrodhena jayetkrodhaṃ vinayena tvanāryatām || 259 ||
śīghratvāddhastapādaṃ ca vācamuccāraṇājjayet ||
ajñānaṃ jñānasaṃyogātkāmaṃ kāmāṃgavarjanāt || 3.233.260 ||
dharmiṣṭhairāgamairlobhaṃ krodhaṃ maitryādvivarjayet ||
kāruṇyena jayenmānaṃ kālaṃ kāryopapattibhiḥ || 261 ||
ātmānaṃ sukhaduḥkhādyaṃ tvanabhijñāya yojayet ||
dhṛtyā śiśnodaraṃ rakṣetpāṇipādaṃ ca cakṣuṣā || 262 ||
cakṣuḥśrotre ca manasā mano vācaṃ ca karmaṇā ||
deśa kālānubandhārthaṃ dharmāṇāmavirodhi yat || 263 ||
tattatsukhaṃ niṣeveta nākasmānniḥsukhī bhavet ||
rūpasaṃdarśanaśravyarasagandhānsadā jayet||264||
vasannapi sadomadhye hyasakto manasā bhavet ||
pareṣāntatra saṃdadhyātpratikūlaṃ yadātmanaḥ || 265 ||
kṛcchreṣvapi na tatkuryātsādhūnāṃ yadasammatam ||
jīrṇāśī ca mitāśī ca hitāśī ca tathā bhavet ||266||
śasyate yairguṇairanyairyeranyairarjitaṃ yaśaḥ ||
uñchavṛttirbhavatyeva viparītamatastyajet || 267 ||
sukāruṇikamātmānaṃ khyāpayennāpi nirghṛṇam ||
deśakālau ca śaktiṃ ca janatāñcāvalokayet || 268 ||
na mṛduḥ syānna ca krūro hyubhāveva samāśrayet ||
mṛdurbhavatyavajñātaḥ tīkṣṇādudvijate janaḥ || 269 ||
anālakṣitacittaḥ syānnaikānte kiñcidācaret ||
vidyātparasya randhrāṇi svaraṃdhraṃ chādayetpunaḥ || 3.233.270 ||
gūhetkūrma ivāṅgāni rakṣedātmānamātmanā ||
vāgādīni sahāyāni kṛtvā prajñāṃ ca mantriṇam || 271 ||
dharmakāmasamṛddhyarthaṃ dāsādyarthaḥ pumānbhavet ||
arthānarthau samāsādya harṣadainye vivarjayet||272||
duḥkhe duḥkhādhikānpaśyetsukhe paśyetsukhādhikāna ||
natvevaṃ mūḍhavaddadhyādātmānaṃ sukhaduḥkhayoḥ||273||
harṣasthānasahasrāṇi śokasthānaśatāni ca ||
divasedivase mūḍhamāviśanti na paṇḍitam||274 ||
na tatkurvīta yasyānte harṣopālambhamāpnuyāt ||
ajñānaprabhavo mohastasmādrāgaḥ pravartate ||275||
rāgādveṣastataḥ krodhaḥ krodhānmāno'bhijāyate ||
mānādapārthakaṃ karma karmato'śubhalakṣaṇam || 276 || 
janmamṛtyujarāvyādhiduḥkhaśokapravarttanam ||
apramādo vṛddhasevā tatprajñāmūlamiṣyate || 277 ||
jñānāddharmaḥ sukhaṃ dharmānniḥśreyo'bhyudayātmakam ||
iṣṭendriyārthasaṃyogo bhavedudayalakṣaṇam || 278 ||
nāruntudaḥ syādanṛśaṃsavādī nahīnataḥ paramabhyādadīta ||
yayāsya vācā para udvijeta na tāṃ vadeduṣatīṃ pāpalokām || 279 ||
vāksāyakā vadanānniṣpatanti yairāhataḥ śocati rātryahāni||
parasya nurmarmasu te patanti na tāndhīro'vasṛjedvai pareṣu||3.233.280||
priyāṇi loke śṛṇute priyaṃvadaḥ priyāṇi cāpnoti priyāṇi cāśnute ||
priyaśca sarvasya janasya jāyate janānurāgaprabhavā hi saṃpadaḥ || 281|| 
ācārayuktastridivaṃ prayāti ācāravāneva bhavatyarogaḥ ||
ācāravāneva cirantu jīvedācāravāneva bhunakti lakṣaṇam || 282 ||
iti śrīviṣṇudharmottare tṛtīyakhaṇḍe mārkaṇḍeyavajrasaṃvāde haṃsagītāsu ācāravarṇano nāma trayastriṃśadadhikadviśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ || 233||
<3.234>
haṃsa uvāca ||
prāyaścittīyatāṃ prāpya daivātpūrvakṛtena vā ||
na saṃsargaṃ vrajetsadbhiḥ prāyaścitte'kṛte naraḥ || 1 ||
brahmahatyā surāpānaṃ steyaṃ gurvāṅganāgamaḥ ||
mahānti pātakānyāhuḥ saṃyogamapi taiḥ saha || 2 ||
anṛtaṃ ca samutkarṣe rājagāmi ca paiśunam ||
guroścālīkanirbandhaḥ samāni brahmahatyayā||3||
brahmojjhatā vedanindā kauṭasākṣyaṃ suhṛdvadhaḥ||
garhitānnādyayorjagdhiḥ surāpānasamāni ṣaṭ ||4||
nikṣepasyāpaharaṇaṃ narāśvarajatasya ca||
bhūmivajramaṇīnāṃ ca rukmasteyasamaṃ smṛtam ||5||
retasekaḥ sayonīṣu kumārīṣvantyajāsu ca||
sakhyuḥ putrasya ca strīṣu gurutalpasamaṃ viduḥ || 6 ||
govadho'yājyasaṃyājyaṃ pāradāryātmavikrayaḥ ||
gurumātṛparityāgaḥ svādhyāyāgnyoḥ sutasya ca ||7||
parivittitānujenūḍhe paravedanameva ca ||
tayordānaṃ ca kanyāyāstayoreva ca yājanam || 8 ||
kanyāyādūṣaṇaṃ caiva vārdhuṣyaṃ vratalopanam ||
taḍāgāgamadārāṇāmapatyasya ca vikrayaḥ|| 9 ||
vrātyatā bāndhavatyāgo bhṛtādhyāpanameva ca ||
bhṛtāccādhyayanādānamapaṇyānāṃ ca vikrayaḥ||3.234.10||
sarvakāryeṣvadhīkāro mahāyantrapravartanam ||
hiṃsroṣadhistriyājīvo'bhicāro mūlakarma ca||11||
indhanārthamaśuṣkāṇāṃ drumāṇāmavapātanam ||
ātmārthaṃ ca kriyārambho nindinānnādanaṃ tathā ||12||
anāhitāgnitā steyamṛṇānāṃ cānapākriyā ||
asacchāstrābhigamanaṃ kauśīlavyasya ca kriyā || 13||
dhānyakupyapaśusteyaṃ madyapastrīniṣevaṇam ||
strīśūdraviṭkṣattravadho nāstikyaṃ copapātakam || 14 ||
brāhmaṇasya rujaḥkṛtyaṃ ghrātiraghreyamadyayoḥ ||
jaihmyaṃ puṃsi ca maithunyaṃ jātibhraṃśakaraṃ smṛtam||15|| 
kharāśvoṣṭramṛgebhānāmajāvikavadhastathā ||
saṃkarīkaraṇaṃ jñeyaṃ mīnāhimahiṣasya ca || 16 ||
ninditebhyo dhanādānaṃ vāṇijyaṃ śūdra sevanam ||
apātrīkaraṇaṃ jñeyamasatyasya ca bhāṣaṇam || 17 ||
kṛmikīṭavayohatyā madyānugatabhojanam ||
phalaidhaḥkusumasteyamadhairyaṃ ca malāpaham || || 18 ||
etānyanyāni sarvāṇi yathoktāni pṛthakpṛthak ||
dhairyavratairapohyante tāni me sannibodhata || 19 ||
brahmahā dvādaśasamāḥ kuṭiṃ kṛtvā vane vaset ||
bhaikṣyāśyātmaviśuddhyarthaṃ kṛtvā śavaśirodhvajam || 3.234.20 ||
lakṣaṃ śastrabhṛtāṃ vā syādviduṣāmicchayātmanaḥ ||
prāśyedātmānamagnau vā samiddhe triravākśirāḥ || 21 ||
yajeta vāśvamedhena svarjitā gosavena vā ||
abhijidviśvajidbhyāṃ vā trivṛtāgniṣṭutāpi vā || 22 ||
japanvānyatamaṃ vedaṃ yojanānāṃ śataṃ vrajet ||
brahmahatyāpanodāya mitabhuṅa niyatendriyaḥ || 23 ||
sarvasvaṃ vā vedavide brāhmaṇāścopapādayet ||
dhanaṃ hi jīvanāyālaṃ gṛhaṃ vā saparicchadam || 24||
haviṣyabhugvānusaretpratisrotaḥ sarasvatīm ||
japedvā niyatāhārastrirvai vedasya saṃhitām || 25 ||
kṛtavāpano nivasedgrāmānte govraje'pi vā ||
āśrame vṛkṣamūle vā gobrāhmaṇahite rataḥ || 26 ||
brāhmaṇārthe gavārthe vā samyak prāṇānparityajet ||
mucyate brahmahatyāyā gobrāhmaṇahite rataḥ || 27 ||
trivāraṃ pratiroddhā vā sarvasvamavajitya vā ||
viprasya tannimitte vā prāṇālābhe vimucyate || 28 ||
evaṃ dṛḍhavrato nityaṃ brahmacārī samāhitaḥ ||
samāpte dvādaśe varṣe brahmahatyāṃ vimuñcati || 29 ||
śiṣṭvā vā bhūmidevānāṃ naradevasamāgame ||
svameno'vabhṛthasnāto hayamedhe vimucyate || 3.234.30 ||
dharmasya brāhmaṇo mūlamagraṃ rājanya ucyate ||
tasmātsamāgame teṣāmeno vikhyāpya śudhyati || 31 ||
brāhmaṇaḥ sambhavenaiva devānāmapi daivatam ||
pramāṇaṃ caiva lokasya brahmātraiva hi kāraṇam || 32 ||
teṣāṃ vedavido brūyustrayo'pyenaḥsu niṣkṛtim ||
sa teṣāṃ pāvanāya syātpavitrā viduṣāṃ hi vāk || 33 ||
ato'nyatamamāsthāya vidhiṃ vipraḥ samāhitaḥ ||
brahmahatyākṛtaṃ pāpaṃ vyapohatyātmavattayā ||34||
hatvā garbhamavijñātaṃ caitadevavrataṃ caret||
rājanyavaiśyau cejjñānāvātreyīmeva ca striyam ||35||
uktvā caivānṛtaṃ sākṣye pratirudhya gurūṃstathā ||
apahṛtya ca nikṣepaṃ kṛtvā ca strīsuhṛdvadham || 36 ||
iyaṃ viśuddhiruditā pramāpyākāmato dvijam ||
kāmato brāhmaṇavadhe niṣkṛtirna vidhīyate || 37 ||
surāṃ pītvā dvijo mohādagnivarṇā surāṃ pibet ||
tayā sa kāye nirdagdhe mucyate kilbiṣāttataḥ|| ||38||
gomūtramagnivarṇaṃ vā pibedudakameva vā ||
payo ghṛtaṃ vā maraṇādgośakṛdrasameva vā || 39 ||
kaṇānvā bhakṣayedabdaṃ piṇyākaṃ vā sakṛnniśi ||
surāpānopanodārthaṃ vālavāsā jaṭī dhvajī || 3.234.40 ||
surā vai malamannānāṃ pāpmā ca malamucyate ||
tasmādbrāhmaṇarājanyau vaiśyastu na surāṃ pibet || 41 ||
gauḍī mādhvī ca paiṣṭī ca vijñeyā trividhā surā ||
yathaivaikā tathaivānyā na pātavyā dvijātibhiḥ || 42 ||
yakṣarakṣaḥpiśācānnaṃ madyaṃ māṃsaṃ surāsavam ||
tadbrāhmaṇena nāktavyaṃ devānāmaśnatā haviḥ || 43 ||
amedhye vā patenmatto vaidikaṃ vāpyudāharet ||
akāryamanyatkuryādvā brāhmaṇo madamohitaḥ || 44 ||
yasya kāyagataṃ brahma madyenāplāvyate sakṛt ||
tasya vyapaiti brāhmaṇyaṃ śūdratvaṃ ca sa gacchati || 45 ||
eṣā vicitrābhihitā surāpānasya niṣkṛtiḥ ||
ata ūrdhvaṃ pravakṣyāmi suvarṇasteyaniṣkṛtim || 46 ||
suvarṇasteyavānvipro rājānamabhigamya tu ||
svakarma khyāpayanbrūyādbhavānmāmanuśāstviti || 47 ||
gṛhītvā musalaṃ rājā sakṛddhanyāttu taṃ svayam ||
vadhena śudhyati steno brāhmaṇastapasaiva vā || 48 | |
tapasāpanunutsustu suvarṇasteyajaṃ malam ||
cīravāsā dvijoraṇye caredbrahmahaṇo vratam || 49 ||
etairvratairapoheta pāpaṃ steyakṛtaṃ dvijaḥ ||
gurustrīgamanīyaṃ tu vratairetairapānudet || 3.234.50 ||
gurutalpyabhibhāṣyainaḥ kuṃbhe svapyādayomaye ||
sūrmīṃ jvalantīṃ vāśliṣya mṛtyunā sa viśudhyati || 23 ||
svayaṃ vā śiśnavṛṣaṇāvutkṛtyādhāya cāñjalau ||
nairṛtīṃ diśamātiṣṭhedānipātādajihmagaḥ || 52 ||
khaṭvāṃgī cīravāsā vā śmaśrulo vijane vane ||
prājāpatyaṃ caretkṛcchraṃ tvabdamekaṃ samāhitaḥ || 53 ||
cāndrāyaṇaṃ vā trīnmāsānabhyasenniyatendriyaḥ ||
haviṣyeṇa yavāgvāhavā gurutalpāpanuttaye || 54 ||
ebhirvratairapoheyurmahāpātakino malān ||
upapātakinastvevaṃ vividhairniyamairapi || 55 ||
upapātakasaṃyukto goghno māsaṃ yavānpibet ||
kṛtavāpo vasedgoṣṭhe carmaṇā tena saṃvṛtaḥ || 56 ||
caturthakālamaśnīyādakṣāralavaṇaṃ mitam ||
gomūtreṇācaretsnānaṃ dvau māsau niyatendriyaḥ || 57 ||
divānugacchettā gāstu tiṣṭhannūrdhvaṃ rajaḥ pibet ||
śubhrūṣitvā namaskṛtya rātrau vīrāsanaṃ vaset || 58 ||
tiṣṭhantīṣveva tiṣṭheta carantīṣvapyanuvrajet ||
āsīnāsu tathāsīta niyato vītamatsaraḥ || 59 ||
āturāmabhiśastāṃ vā siṃhavyāghrādibhirbhayaiḥ ||
patitāṃ paṃkamagnāṃ ca sarvaprāṇairvimocayet || 3.234.60 ||
uṣṇe varṣati śīte vā mārute vāti vā bhṛśam ||
na kurvītātmanastrāṇaṃ gorakṛtvā tu śaktitaḥ ||61||
ātmano yadi vānyeṣāṃ gehe kṣetre'tha vā khale ||
bhakṣayantīṃ na kathayetpibantīṃ caiva vatsakam || 62 ||
anena vidhinā yastu goghno gā anugacchati ||
sa gohatyākṛtaṃ pāpaṃ tribhirmāsairvyapohati || 63 ||
vṛṣabhaikādaśā gāśca dadyātsucaritavrataḥ ||
avidyamāne sarvasvaṃ vedavidbhyo nivedayet || 64 ||
etadeva vrataṃ kuryurupapātakino dvijāḥ ||
avakīrṇiviśuddhyarthaṃ cāndrāyaṇamathāpi vā ||65||
avakīrṇī tu kāṇena gardabhena catuṣpathe||
pākayajñavidhānena yajeta nirṛtiṃ niśi||66||
hutvāgnau vidhivaddhomānantataśca samityṛcā ||
vātendraguruvahnīnāṃ juhuyātsarpiṣāhutīḥ ||67||
kāmato retasassekaṃ vratasthasya dvijanmanaḥ ||
atikramaṃ vratasyāhurdharmajñā brahmavādinaḥ || 68 ||
marutaḥ puruhūtaṃ ca guruṃ pāvakameva ca ||
caturo vrati'nobhyeti brāhmaṃ tejo'vakīrṇinaḥ || 69 ||
etasminnenasi prāpte vasitvā gardabhājinam ||
saptāgārāṃścaredbhaikṣyaṃ svakarma parikīrtayet|| 3.234.70 ||
tebhyo labdhena bhaikṣyeṇa vartayannekakālikam ||
upaspṛśaṃstriṣavaṇaṃ cābdena sa viśuddhyati ||71||
jātibhraṃśakaraṃ karma kṛtvānyatamamicchayā ||
caretsāntapanaṃ kṛcchraṃ prājāpatyamanicchrayā ||72||
saṅkarāpātrakrṛtyāsu māsaṃ godhanamaindavam ||
malinīkaraṇīyeṣu taptaḥ syādyāvakastryaham || 73 ||
turīyaṃ brahmahatyāyāḥ kṣattriyasya vadhe smṛtam ||
vaiśye'ṣṭamāṃśaṃ vṛttasthe śūdre jñeyaṃ tu ṣoḍaśam|| 74 ||
akāmatastu rājanyaṃ vinipātya dvijottamaḥ ||
vṛṣabhaikaṃ sahasraṃ gā dadyācchuddhyarthamātmanaḥ||75||
tryabdaṃ caredvā niyato jaṭī brahmahaṇo vratam ||
vasandūratare grāme vṛkṣamūlaniketanaḥ ||76||
etadeva caredabdaṃ prāyaścittaṃ dvijottamaḥ ||
pramāpya vaiśyaṃ vṛttasthaṃ dadyādvai śatakaṃ gavām || 77||
etadeva vrataṃ kṛtsnaṃ ṣaṇmāsāñchūdrahā caret ||
vṛṣabhaikādaśā vāpi dadyādviprāya gāḥ sitāḥ ||78||
mārjāranakulaṃ hatvā cāṣaṃ maṇḍūkameva ca||
śvagodholūkakākāṃśca śūdrahatyāvrataṃ caret ||79||
payaḥ pibettrirātraṃ vā yojanaṃ vādhvano vrajet ||
upaspṛśetsravantyāṃ vā sūktaṃ vābdaivataṃ japet ||3.234.80||
abhriṃ kārṣṇāyasīṃ dadyātsarpaṃ hatvā dvijottamaḥ ||
palālabhārakaṃ śaṇḍhe saisakaṃ caikamāṣakam || 81 ||
ghṛtakumbhaṃ varāhe tu tiladroṇantu tittirau |||
śuke dvihāyanaṃ vatsaṃ kauñcaṃ hatvā trihāyaṇam||82||
hatvā haṃsaṃ balākāṃ ca bakaṃ barhiṇameva ca ||
vānaraṃ śyenabhāsau ca sparśayedbrāhmaṇāya gām ||83|||
vāso dadyāddhayaṃ hatvā pañca nīlānvṛṣām̐śca vā ||
ajameṣāvanaḍvāhaṃ kharaṃ hatvaikahāyanam || 84 ||
kravyādaṃ tu mṛgaṃ hatvā dhenuṃ dadyātpayasvinīm |||
akravyādaṃ vatsatarīmuṣṭraṃ hatvā tu kṛṣṇalam ||85|| 
jīnākārmukavastāvīnpṛthagdayādviśuddhaye ||
caturṇāmapi varṇānāṃ nārīṃ hatvā vanasthitām ||86||
dānena vadhanirṇekaḥ sarpādīnāmaśaknuvan ||
ekaikaśaścaretkṛcchraṃ dvijaḥ pāpāpanuttaye ||87||
asthimatāṃ tu sattvānāṃ sahasrasya pramāpaṇe||
pūrṇe cānasyanasthnāṃ tu śūdrahatyāvrataṃ careta||88||
kiñcideva tu viprāya dadyādasthimatāṃ vadhe ||
anasthnāṃ caiva sattvānāṃ prāṇāyāmo viśodhanam ||89||
phaladānāṃ tu vṛkṣāṇāṃ cchedane japyamṛkcchatam ||
gulmavallīlatānāṃ ca puṣpitānāṃ ca vīrudhām ||3.234.90||
annādyajātasattvānāṃ rasajānāṃ ca sarvaśaḥ ||
phalapuṣpodbhavānāṃ ca ghṛtaprāśo viśodhanam|| 91||
kṛṣṭajānāmauṣadhānāṃ jātānāñca svayaṃ vane||
vṛthālambhenugacchedvā dinamekaṃ payovratam||92||
etairvratairapohyaṃ syādeno hiṃsāsamudbhavam||
jñānājñānakṛtaṃ kṛtsnaṃ śṛṇutānnādyabhakṣaṇe ||93||
ajñānādvāruṇīṃ pītvā saṃskāreṇaiva śuddhyati||
matipūrvamanirdeśyaṃ prāṇāntikamiti sthitiḥ ||94||
apaḥ surābhājanasthā madyabhāṇḍasthitāstathā ||
pañcarātraṃ vasetpītvā śaṅkhapuṣpīśṛtaṃ payaḥ ||95 ||
spṛṣṭvā dattvā ca madirāṃ vidhivanpratigṛhya ca ||
śūdrocchiṣṭāśca pītvāpaḥ kuśavāri pibettryaham||96||
brāhmaṇastu surāpasya gandhamāghrāya somapaḥ ||
prāṇānapsu trirācamya ghṛtaṃ prāśya viśuddhyati ||97||
ajñānātprāśya viṇmūtraṃ surāsaṃspṛṣṭameva ca ||
punaḥ saṃskāramarhanti trayo varṇā dvijātayaḥ||98||
vasanaṃ mekhalā daṇḍaṃ bhaikṣyacaryā vratāni ca ||
nivartante dvijātīnāṃ punaḥsaṃskārakarmaṇi ||99||
abhojyānāntu bhuktānnaṃ strīśūdrocchiṣṭameva ca ||
jagdhvā māṃsamabhojyaṃ ca saptarātraṃ payaḥ pibet || 3.234.100 ||
śuktāni ca kaṣāyām̐śca pītvāmedhyānapi dvijaḥ ||
tāvadbhavatyaprayato yāvattanna vrajatyadhaḥ || 101 ||
viḍvarāha kharoṣṭrāṇāṃ gomāyoḥ kāpakākayoḥ ||
prāśya mūtrapurīṣāṇi dvitriścāndrāyaṇaṃ caret || 102 ||
śuṣkāṇi bhuktvā māṃsāni bhaumāni kavakāni vā ||
ajñātaṃ caiva sūpasthaṃ caitadeva vrataṃ caret || 103 ||
kravyādasūkarāṇāṃ ca kukkuṭānāṃ ca bhakṣaṇe ||
narakākakharāṇāñca taptakṛcchraṃ viśodhanam || || 104 ||
māsikānnañca yo'śnīyādavṛttyā karṣito dvijaḥ ||
sa trīṇyahānyupavasedekāhaṃ codake vaset || 105 ||
vratacārī tu yo'śnīyānmadhumāse kathañcana ||
sa kṛtvā prākṛtaṃ kṛcchraṃ vrataśeṣaṃ samāpayet || 106 ||
biḍālakākākhūcchiṣṭaṃ jagdhvā śvanakulasya ca||
keśakīṭāvapannañca pibedbrāhmasuvarcalam || 107 ||
abhojyamannaṃ nāttavyaṃ cātmanaḥ śuddhimicchatā||
ajñānabhuktaṃ tūttāryaṃ śodhyaṃ vāpyāśu śodhanaiḥ || 108 ||
eṣo'nādyādanasyokto vratānāṃ vividho vidhiḥ ||
steyadoṣāpahartṝṇāṃ vratānāṃ śrūyatāṃ vidhiḥ || 109 ||
dhānyānnadhanacauryāṇi kṛtvā kāmāddvijottamaḥ ||
svajātīyagṛhādeva kṛcchrābdena viśuddhati || 3.234.110 ||
manuṣyāṇāntu haraṇe strīṇāṃ kṣetragṛhasya ca ||
kūpavāpījalānāñca śuddhiścāndrāyaṇaṃ smṛtam || 111 ||
dravyāṇāmalpasārāṇāṃ steyaṃ kṛtvānyaveśmataḥ ||
caretsāntapanaṃ kṛcchrantanniryātyātmaśuddhaye || 112 ||
bhakṣyabhojyāpaharaṇe yānaśayyāsanasya ca ||
puṣpamūlaphalānāñca pañcagavyaṃ viśodhanam|| 113 ||
tṛṇakāṣṭhadrumāṇāñca śuṣkānnasya guḍasya ca ||
cailacarmāmiṣāṇāñca trirātraṃ syādabhojanam || 114 ||
maṇimuktāpravālānāṃ tāmrasya rajatasya ca ||
ayaḥkāṃsyopalānāñca dvādaśāhaṃ kaṇānnataḥ || 115 ||
kārpāsakīṭajīrṇānāṃ dviśaphaikaśaphasya ca ||
pakṣigandhauṣadhīnāñca rajjvāścaiva tryahaṃ payaḥ || 116 ||
ebhirvratairapohyeta pāpaṃ steyakṛtaṃ dvijaḥ ||
agamyagamanīyantu vratairebhirapānudet || 117 ||
gurutalpavrataṃ kuryādretaḥ siktvā svayoniṣu ||
sakhyuḥ putrasya ca strīṣu kumārīṣvantyajāsu ca||118||
paitṛṣvasreyāṃ bhaginīṃ svasrīyāṃ mātureva ca||
mātuśca bhrāturāptasya gatvā cāndrāyaṇaṃ caret ||119||
etāḥ striyastu bhāryārthe nopayacchettu buddhimān||
jñātitvenānupeyāstāḥ patanti hyupayannadhaḥ ||3.234.120||
amānuṣīṣu puruṣa udakyāyāmayoniṣu ||
retaḥ siktvā jale caiva kṛcchraṃ sāntapanaṃ caret || 121 ||
maithunaṃ tu samāsevya puṃsi yoṣiti vā dvijaḥ ||
goyānepsu divā caiva savāsā snānamācaret || 122 ||
cāṇḍālāntya striyaṃ gatvā bhuktvā ca pratigṛhya ca ||
patatyajñānato vipro jñānātsāmyantu gacchati || 123 ||
vipraduṣṭāṃ striyaṃ bhartā nirundhyādekaveśmani |||
yatpuṃsaḥ paradāreṣu taccaināṃ cārayedvratam||124||
sā cetpunaḥ praduṣyeta sadṛśenopayantritā||
kṛcchraṃ cāndrāyaṇaṃ caiva tadasyāḥ pāvanaṃ smṛtam ||125||
yatkarotyaikarātreṇa vṛṣalīsevanaṃ dvijaḥ ||
tadbhaikṣyabhugjapannityaṃ tribhirvaṣairvyapohati ||126||
eṣā pāpakṛtāmuktā caturṇāmapi niṣkṛtiḥ ||
patitaiḥ saṃprayuktānāṃ cemāḥ śṛṇuta niṣkṛtīḥ || 127 ||
saṃvatsareṇa patati patitena sahācaran ||
yājanādhyāpanādyaunānna tu yānāsanāśanāt || 128 ||
yo yena patitenaivaṃ saṃsargaṃ yāti mānavaḥ ||
sa tasyaiva vrataṃ kuryāttatsaṃsargasya śuddhaye || 129||
patitasyodakaṃ kāryaṃ sapiṇḍairbāndhavairbahiḥ ||
nindite 'hani sāyāhne jñātyṛtviggurusannidhau || 3.234.130 ||
dāsīghaṭamapāṃ pūrṇaṃ paryasyetpretavatsadā ||
ahorātramupāsīrannaśaucaṃ bāndhavaiḥ saha || 131 ||
nivartayeraṃstasmāttu sambhāṣaṇasahāśane ||
dāyādasya pradānaśca yātrāmeva ca laukike ||132||
jyeṣṭhatā ca nivartteta jyeṣṭhaṃ vāpi ca yadvasu ||
jyeṣṭhāṃśaṃ prāpnuyāccāsya yavīyānguṇato'dhikaḥ || 133||
prāyaścitte tu carite pūrṇakumbhamapāṃ navam ||
tenaiva sārdhaṃ prāśyeyuḥ snātvā puṇyajalāśaya ||134 ||
sa tvapsu taṃ ghaṭaṃ prāsya praviśya bhavanaṃ svakam ||
sarvāṇi jñātikāryāṇi yathāpūrvaṃ samācaret || 135 ||
etameva vidhiṃ kuyārdyoṣitsu patitāsvapi ||
vastrānnapānaṃ deyaṃ tu vaseyuśca gṛhāntike || 136 ||
enasvibhiranirṇiktairnārthaṃ kañcitsamācaret ||
kṛtanirṇejanāścainānna jugupseta karhicit ||137||
bālaghnāṃśca kṛtaghnāṃśca viśuddhānapi dharmataḥ ||
śaraṇāgatahantṝm̐śca strīhantṝm̐śca na saṃvaset||138||
yeṣāṃ dvijānāṃ sāvitrī nānūcyeta yathāvidhi ||
tām̐ścārayitvā trīnkṛcchrānyathāvidhyupanāyayet || 139 ||
prāyaścittaṃ cikīrṣanti vikarmasthāstu ye dvijāḥ ||
brahmaṇā ca parityaktāsteṣāmapyetadādiśet || 3.234.140 ||
yadgarhitenārjayanti karmaṇā brāhmaṇā dhanam ||
tasyotsargeṇa śuddhyanti japyena tapasaiva ca || 141 ||
japitvā trīṇi sāvitryāḥ sahasrāṇi samāhitaḥ ||
māsaṃ goṣṭhe payaḥ pītvā mucyate'satpratigrahāt || 142 ||
upavāsakṛśantaṃ tu govrajātpunarāgatam ||
praṇataṃ paripṛccheyuḥ sāmyaṃ saumyecchasīti kim || 143 ||
satyamuktvā tu viprebhyo vikiredyavasaṅgavām ||
gobhiḥ pravartite tīrthe kuryuste'sya parigraham || 144 ||
vrātyānāṃ yājanaṃ kṛtvā pareṣāmantyakarma va ||
abhicāramahīnaṃ ca tribhiḥ kṛcchrairviśuddhyati || 145 ||
śaraṇāgataṃ parityajya vedaṃ viplāvya ca dvijaḥ ||
saṃvatsaraṃ yavāhārastatpāpamapasedhati || 146 ||
śvasṛgālakharairdaṣṭo grāmyaiḥ kravyādbhireva ca ||
kharāśvoṣṭrairvarāhaiśca prāṇāyāmena śuddhyati || 147||
ṣaṣṭhānnakālatā māsaṃ saṃhitājapa eva vā ||
homāśca sakalā nityamapāṅktyānāṃ viśodhanam || 148 ||
uṣṭrayānaṃ samāruhya kharayānañca kāmataḥ ||
snātvā ca vipro digvāsāḥ prāṇāyāmena śuddhyati || 149 ||
vinādbhirapsu vāpyantaḥ śarīre saṃniveśya tu ||
sacailo bahirāplutya gāmālabhya viśuddhyati || 3.234.150 ||
vedoditānāṃ nityānāṃ karmaṇāṃ samatikrame ||
snātakavratalope ca prāyaścittamabhojanam || || 151 ||
huṅkāraṃ brāhmaṇasyoktā tvaṅkāraṃ tu garīyasaḥ ||
snātvānaśnannahaḥśeṣaṃ cābhivādya prasādayet || 152 ||
tāḍayitvā tṛṇenāpi kaṇṭhe baddhvā ca vāsasā ||
vivāde vāpi nirjitya praṇipatya prasādayet || 153 ||
avagūrya tvabdaśataṃ sahasramabhihanya tu ||
jighāṃsayā brāhmaṇasya narakaṃ pratipadyate || 154 ||
śoṇitaṃ yāvataḥ pāṃsūnsaṃgṛhṇāti dvijanmanaḥ ||
tāvantyabdasahasrāṇi tatkarttā narakaṃ vaset || 155 ||
avagūryācaretkṛcchramatikṛcchraṃ nipātane ||
kṛcchrātikṛcchraṃ kurvīta viprasyotpādya śoṇitam || 156 ||
vedābhyāsonvahaṃ śaktyā mahāyajñakriyā kṣamā ||
nāśayantyāśu pāpāni mahāpātakajānyapi || 157 ||
yathaiva tejasā vahniḥ prāptānnirdahati kṣaṇāt ||
tathā jñānāgninā pāpaṃ kṛtsnaṃ dahati vedavit || 158 ||
pāpānubandhaṃ puruṣasya śaktiṃ deśaṃ ca kālaṃ ca tathā viditvā ||
anuktaśuddhiḥ pravicārya deyā ukte tathaitacca nirūpaṇīyam ||159||
iti śrīviṣṇudharmottare tṛ0 kha0 mārkaṇḍeyavajrasaṃvāde haṃsagītāsu prāyaścittavarṇano nāma catustriṃśadadhikadviśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ || 234 ||

haṃsa uvāca ||
ityetadenasāmuktaṃ prāyaścittaṃ yadhāvidhi ||
ata ūrdhvaṃ rahasyānāṃ prāyaścittaṃ nibodhata || 1 ||
savyāhṛtīkāpraṇavāḥ prāṇāyāmāstu ṣoḍaśa ||
api bhrūṇahanaṃ māsātpunantyaharahaḥ kṛtāḥ || 2 ||
kautsaṃ japtvāja ityetadvāsiṣṭhaṃ ca tryṛcaṃ prati ||
māhitraṃ śuddhavatyaśca surāpo'pi viśudhyati || 3 ||
sakṛjjaptvāsyavāmīyaṃ śivasaṃkalpameva ca ||
apahṛtya suvarṇaṃ tu kṣaṇādbhavati nirmalaḥ || 4 ||
haviṣmantīyamabhyasya na tamaṃha itīti ca ||
japtvā tu pauruṣaṃ sūktaṃ mucyate gurutalpagaḥ || 5 ||
enasāṃ sthūlasūkṣmāṇāṃ cikīrṣannapanodanam ||
apetyṛcaṃ japedabdaṃ yatkiñcedamitīti ca || 6 ||
pratigṛhyāpratigrāhyaṃ bhuktvā cānnaṃ vigarhitam ||
japaṃstaratsamandīyaṃ pūyate mānavastryahāt ||7 ||
somāraudraṃ tu bahvenā māsamabhyasya śudhyati ||
sravantyāmācaransnānamaryamṇamiti ca tyṛcam || 8 ||
abdārdhamindramityetadenasvī saptakaṃ japet ||
apraśastantu kṛtvāpsu māsamāsīta bhaikṣyabhuk || 9 ||
mantraiḥ śākalahomīyairabdaṃ hutvā ghṛtaṃ dvijaḥ ||
sugurvapyapahantyeno japtvā vā nama ityṛcam || 3.235.10 ||
upapātakasaṃyuktonugacchedgāḥ samāhitaḥ ||
abhyasyābdaṃ pāvamānīrbhaikṣyāhāro viśudhyati || 11 ||
araṇye vā trirabhyasya prayato vedasaṃhitām ||
mucyate pātakaiḥ sarvaiḥ parākaiḥ śodhitastribhiḥ || 12 ||
tryahaṃ tūpavasedyastu trirahnobhyupayannapaḥ ||
mucyate pātakaiḥ sarvaistrijapitvāghamarṣaṇam || 13 ||
ṛksaṃhitāṃ trirabhyasya yajuṣāṃ vā samāhitaḥ ||
sāmnāṃ vā sarahasyānāṃ sarvapāpaiḥ pramucyate || 14 ||
yadvācikaṃ tasya parasya puṃso yasminnibaddhaṃ bhuvanaṃ samagram || 
japantamoṅkāramatandritātmā pāpāni śīghraṃ prajahāti martyaḥ || 15 ||
iti śrīviṣṇudharmottare tṛ0 kha0 mārkaṇḍeya vajrasaṃvāde haṃsagītāsu rahasyaprāyaścittavarṇano nāma pañcatriṃśadadhikadviśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ || 235 ||
<3.236>
ṛṣaya ūcuḥ ||
karmāṇi śrotumicchāmo yaiḥ pāpaṃ vipraṇaśyati ||
prāpnotyabhyudayaṃ caiva puruṣaḥ pakṣisattama || 1 ||
haṃsa uvāca ||
tryahaṃ prātastryahaṃ sāyaṃ tryahamadyādayācitam ||
tryahaṃ parañca nāśnīyātprājāpatyaṃ carandvijaḥ || 2 ||
gomūtraṃ gomayaṃ kṣīraṃ dadhi sarpiḥ kuśodakam ||
ekarātropavāsaśca kṛcchraṃ sāntapanaṃ smṛtam || 3 ||
ekaikaṃ grāsamaśnīyāttryahāṇi trīṇi pūrvavat ||
tryahaṃ copavasedanyadatikṛcchraṃ carandvijaḥ || 4 ||
taptakṛcchraṃ caranvipro dadhikṣīra ghṛtānilāna ||
pratitryahaṃ pibeduṣṇaṃ sakṛtsnāyī samāhitaḥ || 5 ||
yatātmano'pramattasya dvādaśāhamabhojanam ||
parāko nāma kṛcchro'yaṃ sarvapāpāpanodanaḥ || 6 ||
yathākathañcitpiṇḍānāṃ tisro'śītiḥ samāhitaḥ ||
māsenāśnanhaviṣyasya candrasyaiti salokatām || 7 ||
mahāvyāhṛtibhirhomaḥ kartavyaḥ svayamanvaham ||
ahiṃsāsatyamakrodhamārjavaṃ ca samācaret || 8 ||
trirahni trirniśāyāñca savāsā jalamāviśet ||
strīśūdrapatitāṃścaiva nābhibhāṣeta karhicit || 9 ||
sthānāsanābhyāṃ viharedaśakto'dhaḥśayīta vā ||
brahmacārī vratī ca syādgurudevadvijārcakaḥ || || 3.236.10 ||
sāvitrīṃ ca japennityaṃ pavitrāṇi ca śaktitaḥ ||
sarveṣveva vrateṣvevaṃ prāyaścittārthamāditaḥ || 11 ||
kṛtvā pāpaṃ hi saṃtapya tasmātpāpātpramucyate ||
nainaṃ kuryāmpunariti nivṛttyā pūyate tu saḥ || 12 ||
khyāpanenānutāpena tapasādhyayanena ca ||
pāmakṛnmucyate pāpāttathā dānena cāparaḥ||13||
yasminkarmaṇyapi kṛte manasaḥ syādalāghavam ||
tasmim̐stāvattapaḥ kuryādyāvattuṣṭikaraṃ bhavet||14||
pāpasya nāśastapasā vidheyo dharmasya vṛddhistapasā vidheyā ||
pāpapraṇāśe tvatha dharmavṛddhau kāmāḥ samṛdhyanti narasya viprāḥ || 15 ||
iti śrīviṣṇudharmottare tṛtīyakhaṇḍe mārkaṇḍeyavajrasaṃvāde munīnprati haṃsagītāsu kṛcchrasāntapanādiprāyaścittavarṇano nāma ṣaṭtriṃśadadhikadviśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ ||236||
<3.237>
|| ṛṣaya ūcuḥ || ||
karmaṇā kena puruṣaḥ kimāpnoti mahāmate ||
etannaḥ saṃśayaṃ chindhi dvijānāṃ dvijasattama || 1 ||
|| haṃsa uvāca ||
dānena bhogī bhavati tapasā vindate mahaḥ ||
vṛddhopasevayā viprāḥ prājño bhavati mānavaḥ || 2 ||
yajñena lokānāpnoti pāpanāśaṃ hutena ca ||
japyena kāmānāpnoti satyena ca parāṃ gatim ||3 ||
snānena śuddhimāpnoti prāṇāyāmādviśeṣataḥ ||
dhyānena dhāraṇābhiśca padamāpnotyanuttamam ||4 ||
damena sarvamāpnoti yatkiñcinmanasecchati ||
śaucena devāḥ prīyante prīyante copavāsataḥ || 5||
upavāsaprataptānāṃ kāmāvāptirdhruvaṃ bhavet ||
dadāti vipulānbhogānsaṃgrāmeṣvapalāyinām || 6 ||
madhumāṃsanivṛtto'pi paraṃ saukhyamupāśnute ||
ahiṃsayā tvarogī syāddīrghāyuścāpyahiṃsayā||7||
rūpalāvaṇyasaubhāgyadhanadhānyayutaḥ sadā ||
tīrthānusaraṇādviprāḥ pāpanāśamavāpnuyāt|8||
sarvakalmaṣahīnaśca yām̐llokānmanasecchati ||
pratiśrayapradānena sthānamāpnotyanuttamam ||9||
pūjyapūjayitā viprā yaśasā yujyate naraḥ ||
abhivādanaśīlasya nityaṃ vṛddhopasevinaḥ||3.237.10||
samaṃ kṛtvā vivardhante kīrtirāryuyaśo balam || 11 ||
ṛtukālādṛte bhāryā tathaiva parivarjayet ||
sarvakāmānavāpnoti brahmalokaṃ ca gacchati || 12 ||
ubhau kālau tu bhuñjānastvantarā parivarjayet ||
pānīyamapi viprendrā brāhmaṃ lokaṃ prapadyate || 13 ||
anenaiva vidhānena deśakālāśano naraḥ ||
sarvānkāmānavāpnoti gatimāpnotyabhīpsitām || 14 ||
tathā bhojanakāle tu maunaṃ yastu samācaret ||
upavāsaphalantasya pratyahaṃ bhojane smṛtam || 15 ||
sarvānkāmānavāpnoti maunī naktāśanassakṛt ||
nāpnoti nārakaṃ duḥkhaṃ nityasnāyī naro bahiḥ ||16||
yo dadyādaparikliṣṭamannamadhvani vartate ||
śrāntāyādṛṣṭapūrvāya tasya puṇyaphalaṃ mahat || 17 ||
pādyamāsanamevātha dīpamannaṃ pratiśrayam ||
dadyādatithipūjārthaṃ yajñāḥ pañca sudakṣiṇāḥ|| 18 ||
cakṣurdadyānmano dadyādvācaṃ dadyācca sūnṛtām ||
anuvrajedupāsīta yajñāḥ pañca sudakṣiṇāḥ ||19||
sthaṇḍileṣu śayānānāṃ gṛhāṇi śayanāni ca ||
cīra valkalasaṃvīta vāsāṃsyābharaṇāni ca ||3.237.20||
vāhanāsanayānāni yogātmani tapodhane ||
agnīnupaśayānasya rājapauruṣamucyate || 21||
rasānāṃ prati saṃhāre saubhāgyamadhigacchati ||
āmiṣapratisaṃhāre sarvānkāmānavāpnuyāt ||22||
arvākśirāstu yo lambettoyavāsaṃ ca yo vaset ||
satataṃ caikaśāyī ca labhate cepsitāṃ gatim ||23||
vīrāsanaṃ vīraśayyāṃ vīrasthānamupāsataḥ ||
akṣayāstasya vai lokāḥ sarvakāmapradāḥ smṛtāḥ || 24 ||
dhanaṃ bhavati dānena maunenājñāṃ prayacchati ||
upabhogām̐śca tapasā brahmacaryeṇa jīvitam || 25 ||
rūpamaiśvaryamārogyamahiṃsāphalamaśnute ||
phalaṃ phalāśināṃ rājyaṃ svargaḥ parṇāśināṃ phalam ||26||
puṣpāśināṃ tathaiśvaryaṃ dhanaṃ śākāśināṃ mahat ||
payobhakṣyā divaṃ yāṃti abbhakṣyāścāmitāṃ gatim ||27 ||
daṃtolūkhaliko vipro yaścāpyuñchena jīvati ||
kāpotīñcāśrito vṛttiṃ yatheṣṭāṅgatimāpnuyāt || 28 ||
prāyopaveśanādrājyaṃ sarvatra phalamaśnute||
svargaṃ satyena labhate dīkṣayā kulamuttamam || 29 ||
gavoḍhāḥ śākadīkṣāyāḥ svargakāmī tṛṇāśanaḥ ||3.237.30||
tryahastriṣavaṇaṃ snātvā vāyuṃ pītvā kratuṃ labhet ||
yajñayājī tathāpnoti yām̐llokānmanasecchati || 31 ||
upavāsaṃ ca dīkṣāṃ cāpyabhiṣekaṃ ca vai dvijāḥ ||
kṛtvā dvādaśavarṣāṇi vīrasthānādviśiṣyate ||32||
adhītya sarvānvedānvai sarvaduḥkhairvimucyate ||
mānasaṃ hi dhanaṃ dharmaḥ svargalokamavāpnuyāt ||33||
yena prīṇāti pitaraṃ tena prītaḥ prajāpatiḥ||
prīṇāti mātaraṃ yena pṛthivī tena pūjitā|| 34||
yena prīṇātyupādhyāyantena syādbrahma pūjitam||
sarve tasyādṛtā dharmā yasyaite traya ādṛtāḥ||35|||
anādṛtāstu yasyaite sarvāstasyāphalāḥ kriyāḥ ||
guruśuśrūṣayā vidyā nityaṃ śrāddheṣu santatiḥ||36||
nityasnāyī bhaveddakṣaḥ saṃdhye tu dve japansadā||

devaśuśrūṣayā kāmānyatheṣṭānprāpnuyānnaraḥ ||
sāntvadaḥ sarvabhūtānāṃ sarvaśokaiḥ pramucyate || 38 ||
keśaśmaśrūnvārayataḥ tvagryā bhavati santatiḥ ||

devamālyāpanayanātpādaśaucāddvijasya tu ||

jale saptasahasrāṇi ekādaśa hutāśane ||
bhṛguprapāte ca daśa saṃgrāme viṃśatistathā || 42 ||
naro varṣasahasrāṇi tanuṃ tyaktvā tu modate |
anāśake tu dharmajñāḥ parisaṃkhyā na vidyate || 43 ||
merau sādhayate rājyaṃ yatheṣṭaṃ bhuvi jāyate ||
mahāprasthānamāviśya yatheṣṭāṃ gatimāpnuyāt || 44 ||
vahnipraveśaniyatamamīṣṭaṃ lokamaśnute ||
vāruṇaṃ lokamāpnoti tyaktvāmbhasi tanuṃ naraḥ||45||
anārto vyādhirahito na tyajedātmanastanum ||
asūryā nāma te lokā andhena tamasā vṛtāḥ ||
tām̐ste pretyābhigacchanti ye ke cātmahano janāḥ ||46||
aniṣṭairātmano jñātvā mṛtyukālamupasthitam ||
vyādhito bhiṣajā tyaktaḥ pūrṇe cāyuṣi vāsake || ||47||
yathāgamānusāreṇa saṃtyajedātmanastanum ||
tasminkāle tanutyāgādyathoktaṃ phalamāpnuyāt ||48||
āyuṣastu paraṃ dṛṣṭaṃ maraṇaṃ brāhmaṇa dvayoḥ ||
kṣatriyasya tu saṅgrāme mṛte bhartari yoṣitaḥ ||49||
brāhmaṇārthe gavārthe vā yastyajedātmanastanum ||
ātmānaṃ yūpamutsṛjya sa yajño'nantadakṣiṇaḥ || || 3.237.50 ||
śaṣpaṃ mṛgakharotsṛṣṭaṃ yo mṛgaiḥ saha sevate ||
dīkṣito vai mudā yuktaḥ sa gacchatyamarāvatīm || 51 ||

vāyubhakṣyombubhakṣyo vā phalamūlāśanopi vā ||
yakṣeśaiśvaryamādāya modate'psarasāṅgaṇaiḥ || 53 ||
agniyogavaho grīṣme vidhidṛṣṭena karmaṇā ||

āhāraniyamaṃ kṛtvā munirdvādaśavārṣikam ||

sthaṇḍile śuddhamākāśaṃ parigṛhya samantataḥ ||
praviśya ca mudā yukto dīkṣāṃ dvādaśavārṣikīm || 56 ||
sthaṇḍilasya phalānyāhuryānāni śayanāni ca ||
gṛhāṇi śayanārhāṇi candraśubhrāṇi brāhmaṇaḥ || 57 |
ātmānamupajīvanyo niyato niyatāśanaḥ ||
dehaṃ cānaśane tyaktvā sa svargaṃ samupāśnute || 58 ||
ātmānamupajīvanyo dīkṣāṃ dvādaśavārṣikīm ||
aśmanā caraṇau hatvā guhyakeṣu ca modate || 59 ||
sādhayitvātmanātmānaṃ nirdvandvo niṣparigrahaḥ ||
tīrtvā dvādaśavarṣāṇi dīkṣāmekāṃmanogatām ||
svargalokamavāpnoti devaiśca saha modate || 3.237.60 ||
ātmānamupajīvanyo dīkṣāṃ dvādaśavārṣikīm ||
hutvāgnau dehamutsṛjya vahniloke mahīyate || 61 ||
yastu vipro yathānyāyaṃ dīkṣito niyatendriyaḥ ||
ātmanyātmānamādāya nirdvandvo dharmalālasaḥ || 62 ||

araṇīsahitaḥ skandhe baddhvā gacchatyanāvṛtam || 63 ||
vīrāddhvānamanānītya vīrāsanaratassadā ||

dhīralokagato vīro vīrayogavahaḥ sadā ||

śakralokagataḥ śrīmānmodate divi devavat || 65 ||
upavāsavratairdāntā ahiṃsāsatyavādinaḥ ||
saṃsiddhāḥ pretya gandharvaiḥ saha modantyanāmayāḥ || 66 ||
maṇḍūkayogaśayanā yathāsthānaṃ yathāvidhi ||
dīkṣāṃ carati dharmātmā sa nāgaiḥ saha modate || 67 ||
ārdravāsāstu śiśire vrataṃ vahati yo naraḥ ||
dvādaśābdāni niyataṃ rājā bhavati pārthivaḥ ||68||
varṣarātre tathā yaśca bhavatyākāśasādhakaḥ ||
anapastīrthaśāyī ca rājā bhavati pārthivaḥ||69 ||
tyaktvā vanagatāḥ prāṇānsaptapūrvāṃstathāparān ||
narāṃstārayitā duḥkhādātmānaṃ ca viśeṣataḥ || 3.237.70 ||
yenayena śarīreṇa yadyatkarma karoti yaḥ ||
tenatena śarīreṇa tattatphalamupāśnute || 71 ||
yasyāṃ yasyāmavasthāyāṃ yatkaroti śubhāśubham ||
tasyāṃ tasyāmavasthāyāṃ tatphalamupāśnute || 72 ||
nareṇa hi kṛtaṃ karma sadā pañcendriyairiha ||
te hyasya sākṣiṇo nityaṃ ṣaṣṭha ātmā tathaiva ca || 73 ||
yathā dhenusahasreṣu vatso vindati mātaram ||
evametatkṛtaṃ karma karttāramanuvindati||74||
avaśyaṃ hi kṛtaṃ karma bhoktavyaṃ dvijapuṅgavāḥ ||
sukṛtaṃ duṣkṛtaṃ vāpi tasmātsukṛtamācaret ||75||
acodyamānāni yathā puṣpāṇi ca phalāni ca ||
svakālaṃ nātivartante tathā karma purā kṛtam || 76 ||
yanmantre bhavati vṛthābhiyujyamāne yatsatye bhavati vṛthābhipūyamāne ||
yaccāgnau bhavati vṛthābhihūyamāne tatsarvaṃ bhavati vṛthābhidhīyamāne || 77||
iti śrīviṣṇudharmottare tṛtīyakhaṇḍe mārkaṇḍeyavajrasaṃvāde munīnprati haṃsagītāsu karmaphalaviveko nāma saptatriṃśaduttaradviśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ ||237||
<3.238>
ṛṣaya ūcuḥ ||
dṛṣṭāriṣṭasya bhavatā dehatyāgaḥ prakīrttitaḥ ||
tasmādasmākamācakṣva tvariṣṭāni mahāmate || 1 ||
haṃsa uvāca ||
pravṛttervikṛtirnṝṇāṃ buddhīndriyaśarīrajā ||
akasmāddṛśyate yeṣāṃ teṣāṃ maraṇamādiśet || 2||
etatsamāsātkathitaṃ vistaro'yamataḥ param ||
sūkṣmāriṣṭāvabodhārthaṃ tathā spaṣṭārthameva ca ||3||
na tu karmaṇi yo vyabhre prabhākarma niśābhṛtaḥ ||
chāyāṃ candrārkayorvāpi tayośca gamanaṃ tathā || 4||
devamārge prabhā vahnerdhruvaṃ tārāmarundhatīm ||
paśyatyadṛṣṭāmanyairvā mṛtyuḥ syāttasya vatsarāt ||5 ||
saprabhānāmathānyeṣāṃ prabhāṃ yastu na paśyati ||
tasyaikādaśame māse maraṇaṃ parikīrtitam || 6 ||
suvarṇaṃ rajataṃ mūtraṃ purīṣaṃ vamate niśi ||
svapne sa māse daśame prayāti yamamandiram || 7 ||
rakṣaḥpretapiśācānāṃ yakṣāṇāmapi darśanāt ||
atīndriyāṇāmanyeṣāṃ navame maraṇaṃ dhuvam || 8 ||
daurbalyaṃ jāyate yasya bahusampannabhojinaḥ ||
anaśnanto'pi pīnatvamaṣṭame syātsa mṛtyubhāk || 9||
padaṃ cāsakalaṃ yasya khaṇḍaṃ vikṛtameva vā ||
pāṃsukardamayordṛśyetsaptame māsi mṛtyubhāk ||3.238.10||
kravyādāḥ pakṣiṇo yasya mūrdhni līyanti vai dvijāḥ||
kākaśyenādayastasya paṣṭhe maraṇamādiśet|| || 11||
yasya gomayacūrṇābhaṃ śarīrānmṛjyate rajaḥ ||
svadehacchāyāvikṛtiḥ pañcame sa tu mṛtyubhāk|| 12 ||
anabhre vidyutaṃ dṛṣṭvā caturthe mriyate dhruvam|| || 13 ||
śakracāpaṃ jale dṛṣṭvā gaganaṃ vā dvijottamāḥ ||
avidyamānaṃ dharmajñastṛtīye mriyate dhruvam || 14 ||
avaghaṭṭanaṃ netrasya vinā rogaṃ yadā bhavet ||
ekasya yadi vā dṛśyetsthānabhraṃśo vidhīyate || 15 ||
māsena vikṛtā nāsā vakrā ca maraṇapradā || 16 ||
ardhamāsena durgandhaprabhavaṃ svaśarīrataḥ ||
paracakṣuṣi yaśchāyāmātmanastu na paśyati||
sa paśyati mahābhāgā dvādaśāhena vai yamam||17||
nirvāṇadīpagandhaṃ tu yastu nāghrāti mānavaḥ ||
saptāhena tu dharmajñāḥ paśyatyarkasutaṃ dhruvam ||18||
sadyaḥ snātānuliptasya hṛtpādaśirasā bhavet ||
kṣipraṃ saṃśoṣaṇaṃ tasya tryahānmaraṇamādiśet ||19||
gaṇḍayostilakānraktānvarṇavaikṛtameva ca ||
ahorātreṇa maraṇaṃ puruṣasya samādiśet ||3.238.20||
aṅgulibhyāntu pihite śrotre tu na śṛṇoti yaḥ || 21 ||
śabdaṃ na paśyettu mukhaṃ nirmale darpaṇe tathā ||
paśyecca puruṣānprāptānsadyo maraṇamādiśet || 22 ||
mūrdhadhūmavināśe tu jalārdrādvā tadudbhavet ||
nāsābhaṅgena dharmajñā dvyahānmaraṇamādiśet || 23 || 
sparśadṛṣṭirasaprāṇaśabdaceṣṭāviparyayaḥ ||
dṛśyate tu mumūrṣūṇāṃ svasthāturaśarīriṇām || 24 ||
ślakṣṇaṃ khagaṃ varaṃ lakṣma śītoṣṇasya viparyayaḥ ||
akasmādvetti yo dehī durlabhaṃ tasya jīvitam ||25||
toyavanmedinīṃ toyaṃ medinīmiva paśyati ||
atīndriyaṃ nirīkṣeta na vīkṣeta karasthitam || 26 ||
pṛthivīmiva cākāśaṃ cākāśamiva medinīm ||
ākāśamiva cātmānaṃ pūrṇamindumathātithau || 27 ||
śuklakṛṣṇaviparyāsaṃ sthūlasūkṣmaviparyayam ||
viparyāsaṃ sadasatāṃ dṛśyate jīvitakṣaye || 28 ||
ṣaṇṇāṃ rasānāṃ paryāsa iṣṭāniṣṭaviparyayaḥ||
sarvathā grahaṇaṃ paśyannigrahātīndriyagrahaḥ || 29 ||
abhakṣyalaulyamatyarthaṃ maraṇāyopajāyate ||
śobhanāśobhanaghreyagrahaṇe tu viparyayaḥ || 3.238.30 ||
iṣṭairaniṣṭaiḥ saṃyogo'niṣṭairiṣṭasamāgamaḥ ||
bhayadhairyaviparyāso jāyate prāṇasaṃkṣaye || 31 ||
atidīrgho'tihrasvo vā satamasko'tiśītalaḥ ||
ucchvāsa prāṇasantyāge gatāsūnāṃ prajāyate || 32 ||
anāhatānāṃ śabdaḥ syādāhatānāmaśabdatā ||
āhatānāmaśabdatvaṃ mumūrṣūṇāṃ nṛṇāṃ gṛhe || 33 ||
udak pravṛtte divasasya nāthe puṇye dine mohavivarjitasya ||
nārāyaṇe cetasi saṃsthite vā mṛtyurbhavetpuṇyavato dvijendrāḥ || 34 ||
iti śrīviṣṇudharmottare tṛtīyakāṇḍe mārkaṇḍeyavajrasaṃvāde ṛṣīnprati haṃsagītāsvariṣṭayogavarṇano nāmāṣṭatriṃśadadhikadviśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ || 238 ||
<3.239>
ṛṣaya ūcuḥ ||
ke doṣāḥ pāpaśamanā manuṣyāṇāṃ vināśakāḥ ||
ke guṇāśca mahādeva nityamabhyudayātmakāḥ || 1 ||
haṃsa uvāca ||
sarveṣāmeva doṣāṇāmajñānaḥ paramo mataḥ ||
ajñaścāśraddhadhānaśca saṃśayātmā vinaśyati || 2 ||
kāmaḥ krodhastathā lobho harṣo mānaḥ sadaiva ca ||
ajñānenāvṛtaṃ jantuṃ niviśante dvijottamāḥ||3||
ajñānādeva pāpāni vicinotyavicakṣaṇaḥ ||
ajñānādutpathaṃ gatvā narakaṃ pratipadyate || 4 ||
ajñānenāvṛto jantuḥ śubhe mārge na tiṣṭhati ||
ajñānaśamane yatnastataḥ kāryo vijānatā || 5 ||
śāstrānvavekṣaṇaṃ tasya vṛddhasevā tathaiva ca ||
tathā ca guruśuśrūṣā tvajñānaśamanī matā || 6 ||
lokadvaye nāsti mahānubhāvā ajñānatulyaḥ puruṣasya śatruḥ ||
ato hi lokadvitayaṃ sukhārthī jñāne tu kuryātpuruṣaḥ prayatnam || 7 ||
iti śrīviṣṇudharmottare tṛtīyakhaṇḍe mārkaṇḍeyavajrasaṃvāde haṃsagītāsu jñānavarṇano nāmaikonacatvāriṃśaduttaradviśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ || 239 ||
<3.240>
|| haṃsa uvāca || ||
pāpena sarvaduḥkhānāṃ naro bhavati bhājanam ||
pāpena narakaṃ yāti dveṣyaśceha tathā bhavet || || 1 ||
naraḥ pāpasamācāraḥ pāpādapi vinaśyati ||
pāpamekaṃ paraṃ duḥkhaṃ sarveṣāṃ dvijapuṅgavāḥ ||2||
kṛtapuṇyo'pi yaḥ pāpaṃ karoti puruṣaḥ kvacit ||
svarge'pi na sukhaṃ tasya tadbhayādupajāyate || 3 ||
sa ekaḥ puruṣo loke yasya pāpaṃ na vidyate ||
dīnāndhakṛpaṇānāthāṃstathā ca paramārbhakāḥ || 4||
tiryagyonigatāścānye narakeṣu tathā pare ||
sthāvarāsu ca yonīṣu tiryagyoniṣu cāpyatha || 5 || 
vyādhibandhavadhakleśatāḍanānyapyanekaśaḥ||
naraḥ prāpnoti dharmajñastathā pāpena karmaṇā || 6 ||
ye pāpahīnāḥ puruṣāḥ pradhānāsteṣāṃ na duḥkhaṃ bhavatīha kiñcit ||
tasmātprayatnena vivarjanīyaṃ pāpaṃ budhaiḥ puṇyacayaśca kāryaḥ || 7 ||
iti śrīviṣṇudharmottare tṛtīyakhaṇḍe mārkaṇḍeyavajrasaṃvāde munīnprati haṃsagītāsu pāpanidarśanavarṇano nāma catvāriṃśaduttaradviśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ || 240 ||
<3.241>
|| haṃsa uvāca || ||
kāmānusārī puruṣaḥ kāmādanu vinaśyati ||
yastu kevalakāmātmā so'cirādbhraśyate śriyaḥ || 1 ||
kāmānusārī dharmārthau na tiṣṭhati ciraṃ naraḥ ||
tasmātkāmaṃ na seveta naro dharmārthavarjitam || 2 ||
kṣayo dharmārthayoḥ kāme dhruvaṃ bhavati sevite ||
kāmāndhena vijānāti kartavyaṃ kiñcideva tu || 3 ||
paradāre tvagamye vā prapātaṃ nāvabudhyate ||
dhruvaṃ prāṇaviyoge'pi krīḍāmītyeva manyate || 4 ||
yastu kevalakāmātmā dharmārthaparivarjitam ||
kāmamāsevate nityaṃ vijñeyaḥ puruṣādhamaḥ || 5 ||
dharme kṣīṇe tathaivārthe kāmamāsevite dvijaḥ ||
dhruvaṃ narakapātaḥ syānnātra kāryā vicāraṇā || 6 ||
dharmārthayuktaṃ puruṣastu kāmaṃ saṃsevamāno hi na jātu kāmaiḥ ||
viyogamāpnoti tatastu vidvāndharmārthayukte satataṃ niṣevet || 7 ||
iti śrīviṣṇudharmottare tṛ0 kha0 mārkaṇḍeyavajrasaṃvāde munīnprati haṃsagītāsu kāmakṛtyavarṇanonāmaikacatvāriṃśaduttaradviśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ || 241||
<3.242>
haṃsa uvāca ||
nātyarthaharṣaḥ kāryastu kadācidapi paṇḍitaiḥ||
atyarthahṛṣṭaḥ puruṣaḥ kāryaṃ vetti na kiñcana || 1 ||
sampadañcañcalāṃ prāpya harṣaṃ prājño vivarjayet ||
dṛṣṭā hi bahavo naṣṭā loke sampatpralambitāḥ || || 2 ||
sampattau vā vipattau vā svabhāvaṃ na tyajedbudhaḥ ||
cañcalāḥ sampadaḥ sarvāḥ svabhāvādeva vai dvijāḥ || 3 ||
kastāsāṃ viśvasetprājño veśyāsstrīṇāṃ yathā tathā ||
saṃsargānmohamāpnoti tāsāṃ prāpnoti mānavaḥ || 4 ||
sarve kṣayāntā nicayāḥ patanāntāḥ samucchrayāḥ ||
saṃyogā viprayogāntā maraṇāntaṃ hi jīvitam || 5 ||
na prajñā dhanalābhāya na jāḍyamasamṛddhaye ||
lokaparyāyavṛttāntaṃ prājño jānāti netaraḥ || 6 ||
prājñastu samyakcapalāṃ viditvā tāṃ prāpya harṣaṃ parivarjayeta ||
sampadvināśe na ca duḥkhamīyādyadbhāvi tadbhāvi na tasya nāśaḥ || 7 ||
iti śrīviṣṇudharmottare tṛ0kha0mārkaṇḍeyavajrasaṃvāde haṃsagītāsu praharṣadoṣavarṇano nāma dvicatvāriṃśaduttaradviśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ||242||
<3.243>
haṃsa uvāca||
mānaṃ vivarjitaṃ sadbhirvarjanīyaṃ sadā budhaiḥ||
mānena naṣṭā bahavo rājānaḥ saparicchadāḥ|||
brāhmaṇyācca paribhraṣṭā brāhmaṇā bahavo dvijāḥ||
sarvadoṣāspadaṃ mānaṃ lokadvayavivātakam || 2 ||
yuktāyuktaṃ na jānāti mānena puruṣādhamaḥ ||
mānagrasto naro loke gurūnapyavamanyate || || 3 ||
karotyakāryaṃ puruṣo mānena ca tathā dhruvam ||
mānagrāhagṛhītasya nāsti mokṣaḥ kathañcana || 4 ||
kāmaḥ krodhaśca lobhaśca sarvajantu vināśakāḥ ||
mānagrastaṃ tu puruṣamāviśanti dvijottamāḥ || 5 ||
mānābhibhūtaśca tathā narakaṃ pratipadyate ||
apratiṣṭhannaraśreṣṭhāstasmāttaṃ parivarjayet || 6 ||
śatrurna mānena samo'sti puṃso mānena nāśaṃ vrajatīha jantuḥ ||
māno hi tasmātparivarjanīyo budhaistu nityaṃ narakasya mārgaḥ || 7 ||
iti śrīviṣṇudharmottare tṛ0 kha0 mārkaṇḍeyavajrasaṃvāde munīnprati haṃsagītāsu mānadoṣavarṇano nāma tricatvāriṃśaduttaradviśata tamo'dhyāyaḥ || 243 ||
<3.244>
haṃsa uvāca ||
mado vivarjitaḥ sadbhirvarjanīyaḥ prayatnataḥ ||
made tu bahavo doṣā lokadvayavivātakāḥ || 1 ||
mattaḥ kāryaṃ na jānāti gurūnapyavamanyate ||
mattasya narakadvāraṃ nityaṃ viprāstvapāvṛtam || 2 ||
matto bhayaṃ na jānāti maraṇe'pyatikutsite ||
matto mohasamāpanno na vijānāti kiñcana || 3 ||
madānāmapi sarveṣāṃ jñeyaḥ pānamado'dhamaḥ ||
tenābhibhūtaḥ puruṣo na mṛtādatiricyate || 4 ||
ajñānatulyaṃ na hi doṣamasti hetuśca tasyeha paro madaḥ syāt ||
tasmātprayatnena madasya heturnareṇa pānaṃ parivarjanīyam||5||
iti śrīviṣṇudharmottare tṛtīyakhaṇḍe mārkaṇḍeyavajrasaṃvāde munīnprati haṃsagītāsu madadoṣavarṇano nāma catuścatvāriṃśaduttaradviśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ || 244 ||
<3.245>
|| haṃsa uvāca || ||
lubdho doṣānna jānāti lubdhaḥ pāpaṃ na budhyate ||
naro lobhasamāviṣṭo vaśameti tathāpadām || 1 ||
jīryanti jīryataḥ keśā nakhā jīryanti jīryataḥ ||
jīryanti jīryato dantāstṛṣṇā tvekā na jīryate || 2 ||
tṛṣṇākhuniragādheyamapūrvā dvijapuṅgavāḥ||
pūryamāṇāpi satataṃ khanyate'bhyadhikaṃ tayā || 3 ||
na tāṃ pūrayituṃ śaktaḥ kadācidapi kaścana ||
na tāṃ pūrayituṃ śaktāḥ sarve santoṣamāśritāḥ || 4 ||
yatpṛthivyāṃ vrīhiyavaṃ hiraṇyaṃ paśavaḥ striyaḥ ||
nālamekasya paryāptamiti matvā śamaṃ vrajeta || 5 ||
yā dustyajā durmatibhiryā na jīryati jīryataḥ ||
yo'sau prāṇāntiko rogastāṃ tṛṣṇāṃ tyajataḥ sukham || 6 ||
lubdhasya bandhurna suto gururvā lubdhasya lokā na ca lokayātrā ||
lubdhasya doṣāḥ sakalāḥ pradiṣṭā lokadvaye duḥkhakarāḥ suraughāḥ || 7 ||
iti śrīviṣṇudharmottare tṛtīyakhaṇḍe mārkaṇḍeyavajrasaṃvāde munīnprati haṃsagītāsu lobhadoṣavarṇano nāma pañcacatvāriṃśaduttaradviśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ || 245 ||
<3.246>
haṃsa uvāca ||
krodha ekaḥ paraḥ śatruḥ krodha eko vināśakṛt ||
krodhābhibhūtaḥ puruṣastvātmānamapi ghātayet || 1 ||
arthānarthau na jānāti kāryākāryau tathaiva ca ||
naraḥ krodhasamāviṣṭo nānyaṃ mitramapi dvijān || 2 ||
gurūnākṣipati krodhānnainaṃ hi namate janaḥ ||
anarthaiḥ saha saṃsargastasmātkrodhaṃ vivarjayet || 3 ||
krodhaṃ mūlamanarthānāṃ dvāraṃ hi narakasya ca ||
āpadāmāspadaṃ ghorantaṃ prayatnena varjayet || 4 ||
krodhena vijito yastu kathamanyaṃ sa jeṣyati||
krodhe jite jitaṃ viddhi trailokyaṃ sacarācaram || 5 ||
anarthakāriṇaṃ krodhaṃ yadā tejasvitā matā ||
krodhe doṣamataḥ kṛtvā jitaḥ krodho mahātmabhiḥ || 6 ||
yajñaśca dānāni tapaḥ pavitraṃ tīrthopasevā ca tathaiva vedāḥ ||
krodhābhibhūtasya bhavanti mithyā narasya tasmātparivarjayettam || 7 ||
iti śrīviṣṇudharmottare tṛtīya khaṇḍe mārkaṇḍeyavajrasaṃvāde munīnprati haṃsagītāsu krodhadoṣavarṇano nāma ṣaṭcatvāriṃśaduttaradviśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ || 246 ||
<3.247>
|| haṃsa uvāca || ||
sarveṣāmeva pāpānāṃ nāstikyaṃ pātakaṃ param ||
nāstikyāddhi paraṃ nāsti kiñcinpāpaṃ dvijottamāḥ || 1 ||
akāryamiti kāryāṇi kṛtvā kāryavaśānnaraḥ ||
paścāttāpena saṃyuktaḥ kṣipraṃ pāpaṃ vyapohati || 2 ||
kāryabuddhyā tvakāryāṇi yaḥ karoti narādhamaḥ ||
kathaṃ nivartanaṃ tebhyo jāyate tasya vai dvijāḥ || 3||
nāstikyasya tathā mūlaṃ tvasacchāstrāvagāhanam ||
tasmātsarvaprayatnena tadvivarjyaṃ vijānatā ||4||
sarvo hi mānavo loke paralokanibandhanaḥ ||
tatrāpi nirapekṣasya kinnu loke nibandhanam ||5||
ye tu vai nāstikā loke narā lokaṃ prati dvijāḥ ||
narake te hi pacyante bahukālaṃ na saṃśayaḥ ||6||
nāstikyamekaṃ puruṣasya loke vināśanaṃ vipravarāḥ pradiṣṭam ||
tasmātprayatnena vivarjanīyo nāstikyabhāvaḥ sukhamīpsamānaiḥ||7||
iti śrīviṣṇudharmottare tṛ0 kha0 mārkaṇḍeyavajrasaṃvāde munīnprati haṃsagītāsu nāstikyadoṣavarṇano nāma saptacatvāriṃśaduttaradviśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ || 247 ||
<3.248>
haṃsa uvāca ||
ahaṅkārātparaṃ nāsti loke kiñcidvināśanam ||
ahaṅkāreṇa bhūpālāḥ śataśo nidhanaṅgatāḥ || 1 ||
ahaṃkṛtāstathā naṣṭā brāhmaṇāśca sahasraśaḥ ||
mānyāmānyanna jānanti kāryākāryaṃ tvahaṃkṛtāḥ || 2 ||
ahaṃkṛtastathā loke na vidyāmadhigacchati ||
vipannakāryaśca tathā na prājñānparipṛcchati || 3 ||
na śṛṇoti tathānyeṣāṃ darpapūrṇo vicakṣaṇaḥ ||
pātādanyannabhaiṣajyaṃ darpānnāstyeha kīrtanam || 4 ||
balādhikaistathā vīrairgurubhiśca tathaiva ca ||
maraṇe saṃnikṛṣṭepi maraṇasya vicakṣaṇaḥ || 5 ||
hetubhūtāni karmāṇi ninditāni niṣevate ||
sthite sthitāṃstathā prājñānbhāṣamāṇānvinindati || 6 ||
ahaṃkṛtānāṃ narake pradiṣṭā gatirdhruvaṃ lokavininditānām ||
prājñena tasmātparivarjanīyo darpo vināśaikakaraḥ sadaiva ||7||
iti śrīviṣṇudharmottare tṛ0 kha0 mārkaṇḍeyavajrasaṃvāde haṃsagītāsvahaṃkāradoṣavarṇano nāmāṣṭacatvāriṃśaduttaradviśatamo'dhyāyaḥ || 248 ||
<3.249>
haṃsa uvāca ||
dattaṃ yacchaucahīnena na tadgṛhṇanti devatāḥ ||
bubhutsavaśca dharmajñāḥ śucikāmāśca devatāḥ || 1 ||
yakṣaiśca rākṣasaiścaiva piśācaiśca niśācaraiḥ ||
ḍākinībhiśca dharmajñāḥ śaucahīno'bhibhūyate || 2 ||
śaucahīnamathābhyeti svayaṃ nindati kaṃ dvijāḥ ||
vyādhihīno hyaśaucaśca sarvairevābhibhūyate || 3 ||
vardhate ca tathānarthāḥ samarthāśca vrajanti ca ||
śaucahīnasya ripavo yānti vṛddhiṃ parāṃ dvijāḥ || 4 ||
strīśaucamarthaśaucaṃ ca vākchaucamapi ca dvijāḥ ||
etāni yasya śaucāni sa śuciḥ parikīrtitaḥ || 5 ||
yorthe śuciḥ sa hi śucirna mṛdvāriśuciḥ śuciḥ ||
tataścāpyadhikaṃ śaucamahiṃsā parikīrtitā || 6 ||
śaucena hīnā narakaṃ vrajanti śaucena hīnā vibhajantyanarthān ||
śaucena hīnāśca bhavanti nindyāstasmādaśaucaṃ parivarjanīyam || 7 ||
iti śrīviṣṇudharmottare tṛ0 kha0 mārkaṇḍeyavajrasaṃvāde munīnprati haṃsagītāsu śaucavarṇano nāmaikonapañcāśaduttaradviśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ || 249 ||
<3.250>
haṃsa uvāca ||
ācārahīnāḥ puruṣā bhavantyalpāyuṣo dvijāḥ ||
ācārahīnaśca tathā rākṣasairabhibhūyate || 1 ||
yakṣaiḥ piśācairgandharvaistathā cānyairniśācaraiḥ ||
ḍākinībhiḥ surāṇāṃ ca tathā pārṣagaṇāḥ pṛthaka ||2 ||
dattamācārahīnena na pratīcchanti devatāḥ ||
pitaraśca mahābhāga ṛṣayaśca tapodhanāḥ || 3 ||
sarvalakṣaṇayukto'pi narastvācāravarjitaḥ ||
na prāpnoti tathā vidyāṃ na ca kiñcidabhīpsitam ||
ācārahīnaḥ puruṣo narakaṃ pratipadyate || 4 ||
ācārahīnaṃ na punanti vedā yadyapyadhītāḥ saha ṣaḍbhiraṅgaiḥ ||
ācārahīnena tu dharmakāryaṃ kṛtaṃ hi sarvaṃ bhavatīha mithyā || 5 ||
iti śrīviṣṇudharmottare tṛ0 kha0 mā rkaṇḍeyavajrasaṃvāde munīnprati haṃsagītāsvanācāradoṣavarṇano nāma pañcāśaduttaradviśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ || 250 ||
<3.251>
haṃsa uvāca ||
anṛtaṃ varjayennityamanṛtaṃ pātakaṃ param ||
anṛtāddhi paraṃ nāsti dvāraṃ nāśanamātmanaḥ || 1 ||
sarve nibaddhā vācyārthā vāṅmūlā vāgvinissṛtāḥ||
tasmānna stenayedvācaṃ ya iccheddhitamātmanaḥ || 2 ||
anṛtaṃ na kvacidvācyaṃ tathā sākṣye viśeṣataḥ ||
anṛtaṃ hi vadansākṣye pitṛbhiḥ saha majjati || 3 ||
pañca paśvanṛte hanti daśa hanti gavānṛte ||
śatamaśvānṛte hanti sahasraṃ puruṣānṛte || 4 ||
hanti jātānajātām̐śca hiraṇyārthe'nṛtaṃ vadan ||
sarvaṃ bhūmyanṛtaṃ hanti māsma bhūmyanṛtaṃ vadet || 5 ||
apsu bhūmivadityāhuḥ strīṇāṃ bhoge ca maithune ||
abjeṣu caiva ratneṣu sarveṣvaśmamayeṣu ca || 6 ||
etāndoṣānavekṣyaiva sarvānanṛtabhāṣaṇe ||
yathāśrutaṃ yathādṛṣṭaṃ sarvamevāñjasā vadet || 7 ||
nagno muṇḍaḥ kapālī ca bhikṣārthī kṣutpipāsitaḥ ||
andhaḥ śatrugṛhaṃ gacchenna sākṣyamanṛtaṃ vadet || 8 ||
sākṣye'nṛtaṃ vadanpāśairbadhyate vāruṇairbhṛśam ||
