niviśante tamājātīstasmātsākṣye vadedṛtam || 9 ||
yanmayā gaditaṃ pāpaṃ dvijena kūṭasākṣiṇaḥ ||
tato'dhikamavāpnoni sadyo mithyā'bhiśaṃsane || 3.251.10 ||
yathā krodhādbhayāllobhādajñānādvā dvijottamāḥ ||
yo'nṛtaṃ bhāṣate sadbhyaḥ sa yāti narakaṃ dhruvam || 11 ||
avākchirāstamasyandhe kilbiṣī narake patet ||
yaḥ praśnaṃ vitathaṃ brūyātpṛṣṭaḥ saddharmaniścaye || 12 ||
andho matsyānivāśnāti nirapekṣaṃ sakaṇṭakān ||
yo bhāṣate'rtha vaikalyamapratyakṣaṃ sabhāṅgataḥ ||13||
kūṭakarmatulāmāne rasānāṃ bhedane tathā ||
viśvāsanyāsaharaṇe rahasyasamabhedane || 14 ||
mṛtiṃ kalpayate yaśca madhu māṃsena pātakam ||
dvijasya surayā vāpi lobhena ca viśeṣataḥ || 15 ||
gobrāhmaṇavadhe vāpi gurupitṛvadhe tathā ||
vayasyadāragamane gurudārapradarśane || 16 ||
vyutpādane ca śastrāṇāṃ śastrotsāde tathaiva ca ||
bhartṛpiṇḍāpaharaṇe saṃgrāmācca palāyane ||17||
śaraṇāgatasaṃtyāge gacchataśca divā striyam ||
preṣyāṃ ca gacchataḥ pāpaṃ rājadrohe tathaiva ca || 18 ||
paṅktyāṃ viṣamadātuśca śrāddhaṃ mitrasya yattathā ||
śulkena dadataḥ kanyāṃ keśavikrayikasya ca || 19 ||
viprasya pātakaṃ yacca prāṇināṃ vikraye bhavet ||
āmaśrāddhabhujaḥ pāpamayājyasya ca yājane || 3.251.20 ||
mithyā rājyasya karaṇe paradravyāpahāriṇaḥ ||
sarvāṇyetāni pāpāni dvijaḥ sabhyaḥ samāpnuyāt || 21 ||
yadyanyathā vadettasmāddharmyaṃ sabhyaḥ sadā vadet ||
etadeva tathā pāpaṃ vijñeyaṃ kūṭasākṣiṇām || 22 ||
kūṭakarmā tu narakaṃ pitṛbhiḥ saha majjati ||
daśavarṣasahasrāṇi daśavarṣaśatāni ca ||23||
tato daśaguṇaṃ kālaṃ sabhyo mithyābhibhāṣitā ||
rāgadveṣapravṛttastu vyavahāre'pi pārthivaḥ || 24 ||
tato daśaguṇaṃ kālaṃ narake vasati dhruvam ||
ukte vacasi dharmye tu sadyaḥ pāpātpramucyate || 25 ||
rājā narakamāpnoti satyavākyaṃ vilaṃghayana ||
śūdraviṭkṣatraviprāṇāṃ yatrokte prāpnuyādvadhaḥ || 26 ||
tatra vaktavyamanṛtaṃ taddhi satyādviśiṣyate ||
vāgdaivataiśca carubhiryajanaiśca sarasvatīm |||27||
kūṣmāṇḍairvāpi juhuyādghṛtamagnau yathāvidhi ||
udutyṛce vā vāruṇye ṛcā vāgdaivatena vā ||28||
anṛtasya vaśastasya kurvāṇo niṣkṛtiṃ parām||
bahavaḥ sukhamedhante yasminviprā nipātite ||
vācyaṃ tasya vadhe prāpte yathābhūtamiti śrutiḥ || 29 ||
na narmayuktaṃ vacanaṃ hinasti na strīṣu viprā na vivāhakāle ||
prāṇātyaye sarvadhanāpahāre pañcānṛtānyāhurapātakāni || 3.251.30 ||
iti śrīviṣṇudharmottare tṛtīyakhaṇḍe mārkaṇḍeyavajrasaṃvāde munīnprati haṃsagītāsvanṛtadoṣavarṇano nāmaikapañcāśaduttaradviśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ || 251 ||
<3.252>
haṃsa uvāca ||
sarveṣāmeva pāpānāṃ hiṃsā paramihocyate ||
hiṃsā balamasādhūnāṃ hiṃsā lokadvayāpahā || 1 ||
api kīṭaḥ pataṅgo vā na hantavyaḥ kathañcana ||
mahadduḥkhamavāpnoti puruṣaḥ prāṇināśanāt || 2 ||
ātatāyinamāyāntaṃ puruṣaṃ yadi vā paśum ||
upāyahīnastaṃ hatvā na tu pāpena bādhyate || 3 ||
guruṃ vā bālavṛddhau vā mānyaṃ vāpyatha vā janam ||
ātatāyinamāyāntaṃ hanyādevāvicārayan || 4 ||
ātatāyina māyāntamapi vedāntagaṃ raṇe ||
jighāṃsantaṃ jighāṃsīyāttena pāpaṃ na vidyate || 5 ||
raṇe prahārābhihate śatrau naivāsti pātakam ||
jitvā gṛhīte nihate tasminnevāsti pātakam || 6 ||
raṇe jitantu yaḥ śaktaḥ paścātpātayate naraḥ ||
sa yāti narakaṃ ghoraṃ bahūnyabdaśatāni ca || 7 ||
muktaśca nārakādduḥkhādbahūnyabdaśatāni tu ||
āmagarbheṣu sicyante ye narā naraghātakāḥ || 8 ||
yāvanti paśuromāṇi tāvatkṛtveha māraṇam ||
vṛthāpaśughnaḥ prāpnoti pretya ceha ca niṣkṛtim || 9 ||
api kīṭaṃ naro hatvā chittvā tṛṇamathāpi vā ||
narakaṃ yāti dharmajñāḥ puruṣaḥ svena karmaṇā || 3.252.10 ||
strīhiṃsā dhanahiṃsā ca prāṇihiṃsā ca dāruṇā ||
hiṃsā ca trividhā proktā varjitā paṇḍitairnaraiḥ || || 11 ||
ahiṃsakastu yatnena varjayettu parastriyam ||
vijane'pi tathā nyastaṃ paradravyaṃ prayatnataḥ || 12 ||
ato vai naiva kartavyā prāṇihiṃsā bhayāvahā ||
viyojya prāṇinaṃ prāṇaistathaikamapi nirghṛṇaḥ || 13 ||
narakāddhi vinirmukto na kiñcitsukhamedhate ||
yāṃ rātrimadhivinnāṃ strīṃ yāṃ caivākṣaparājitām || 14 ||
yāṃ ca rājyacyuto rātriṃ yāṃ ca sarvasvavicyutaḥ ||
iṣṭabandhuviyuktaśca yāṃ rātriṃ duḥkhamaśnute || 15 ||
vyavahāre jitaścaiva yad duḥkhaṃ kūṭasākṣibhiḥ ||
tatsarvaṃ duḥkhamāpnoti hatvaikamapi tu dvijāḥ || 16 ||
nāsti pāpataraṃ loke manuṣyā māṃsabhojinaḥ||
bahirmāṃsaṃ tṛṇātkāṣṭhādupalādvāpi jāyate||17||
ghorātprāṇivadhājjātaṃ puruṣaḥ parivarjayet ||
yadi cetkhādako na syānna bhavedghātakastathā || 18 ||
etasmātkāraṇānnindyo ghātakādapi khādakaḥ |||
yāvatāṃ prāṇināṃ yena māṃsaṃ bhuktaṃ bhavediha || 19 ||
tā eva te na tāvatyo gantavyā yonayo dhruvam ||
yeṣāntu prāṇināṃ bhuktaṃ tena māṃsaṃ dvijottamāḥ || 3.252.20 ||
bhoktavyaṃ tasya tairmāṃsaṃ dhruvaṃ janmanijanmani |||
māṃsabhakṣayitā'mutra yasya māṃsamihādmyaham ||21||
etanmāṃsasya māṃsatvaṃ pravadanti manīṣiṇaḥ ||
samutpattiṃ ca māṃsasya vadhabandhau ca dehinām || || 22 ||
prasamīkṣya nivartyeta sarvamāṃsasya bhakṣaṇāt ||
anumantā viśasitā nihantā kriyavikrayī || 23 ||
saṃskartā copahartā ca khādakāśca vighātakāḥ ||
svamāṃsaṃ paramāṃsena yo vardhayitumicchati || 24 ||
na pāpakṛttamo loke tasmādanyo hi vidyate ||
asaṃskṛtānpaśūnmantrairnādyādvipraḥ kathañcana || 25 ||
mantraistu saṃskṛtānadyācchāśvataṃ vidhimāsthitaḥ ||
yā vedavihitā hiṃsā na sā hiṃsā matā yataḥ || 25 ||
oṣadhyaḥ paśavo vṛkṣāstiryañcaḥ pakṣiṇastathā ||
yajñārthaṃ nidhanaṃ prāptā prāpnuvantyucchritāḥ punaḥ || 27 ||
madhuparke ca yajñe ca pitṛdaivatakarmaṇi ||
atraiva paśavo hiṃsyā nānyatreti kadācana || 28 ||
eṣvartheṣu paśūnhanti vadatattvārthaviddvijāḥ ||
ātmānaṃ ca paśūm̐ścaiva gamayantyuttamāṃ gatim || 29 ||
yajñārthaṃ paśavaḥ sṛṣṭāḥ pūrvameva svayambhuvā ||
yajñāśca bhūtaiḥ sarvasya tasmādyajñe vadho'vadhaḥ || 3.252.30 ||
vṛthā tu māṃsaṃ puruṣaḥ samaśnanprayāti viprā narakaṃ sughoram ||
nibarhaṇaṃ garhitameva jantormāṃsaśca tasmātparivarjanīyam || 31||
iti śrīviṣṇudharmottare tṛtīyakhaṇḍe mārkaṇḍeyavajrasaṃvāde haṃsagītāsu hiṃsādoṣavarṇano nāma dvipañcāśaduttaradviśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ || 252 ||
<3.253>
ṛṣaya ūcuḥ ||
brūhyasmākaṃ dvijaśreṣṭha naraḥ pāpena karmaṇā ||
kiṃkimāpnoti dharmajña vihagendra mahāmate || 1 ||
|| haṃsa uvāca || ||
pāpaṃ kṛtvā naro viprā narakaṃ pratipadyate ||
narakācca vinirmuktastiryagyonau tathā vrajet || 2||
śarīrajaiḥ karmadoṣairyāti sthāvaratāṃ naraḥ ||
vācikaiḥ pakṣimṛgatāṃ mānasairantyajātitām ||3 ||
karmajā gatayo nṝṇāmuttamādhamamadhyamāḥ ||
trividhāstu vinirdiṣṭā daśalakṣaṇalakṣitāḥ || 4 ||
paradravyeṣvabhidhyānaṃ manasā duṣṭacintanam ||
vitathābhiniveśaśca trividhaṃ karma mānasam || 5 ||
pāruṣyamanṛtaṃ caiva paiśunyaṃ caiva sarvaśaḥ ||
anibaddhapralāpaśca vāṅmayaṃ syāccaturvidham || 6 ||
adattānāmupādānaṃ hiṃsā caivāvidhānataḥ ||
paradāropasevā ca śārīraṃ trividhaṃ smṛtam || 7 ||
ekasminnapyato viprāḥ sevate daśalakṣaṇam ||
adharmātprāpya mānuṣyaṃ yadā bhavati duḥkhitaḥ || 8 ||
iha duścaritaiḥ kecitkecitpūrvakṛtaistathā ||
prāpnuvanti durātmāno narā rūpaviparyayam || 9 ||
brahmahā kṣayarogī syādduścarmā gurutalpagaḥ ||
śyāvadantaḥ surāpaśca kunakhī kanakāpahaḥ || 3.253.10 ||
vastrāpahārakaḥ śvitrī paṅguraśvāpahārakaḥ ||
paṅgureva tathā jñeyaḥ sarvayānāpahārakaḥ || 11 ||
adhaśca jāyate loke gāṃ hṛtvā mānavādhamaḥ ||
dhānyacauro'tiriktāṅgo mūko vāgapahārakaḥ || 12 ||
gurusvāmi dvijānkrośanvedanindāṃ tathaiva ca ||
śrutvā bhavati durbuddhirbadhiro mānavādhamaḥ || 13 ||
nityāmayāvī bhavati tathā cānnāpahārakaḥ ||
viyojyaikamapi prāṇaiḥ prāṇinaṃ vyādhitastathā || 14 ||
vastrabhojyāpahartārau tathā nagnabubhukṣitau ||
agnimāndyābhibhūtaśca tathā bhavati durmatiḥ || 15 ||
juhoti yo dvijaśreṣṭhā asamiddhe hutāśane ||
ratnāpahārī bhavati tathā kuṣṭhī narādhamaḥ || 16 ||
piśunaḥ pūtināsaśca pūtivaktraśca sūcakaḥ ||
ṣaṇḍaśca jāyate loke dārātikramakārakaḥ || 17 ||
parapuṃsi pradātā vā tasya bhāryā tathā bhavet ||
kuṣṭhī bhavati dharmajñāstvapahṛtya tathā striyam || 18 ||
liṅgavyādhimavāpnoti viyonau maithunaṃ gataḥ ||
gāṃ ca gatvā dvijaśreṣṭhā liṅganāśamavāpnuyāt || 19 ||
pūjyāvamantā khalvāṭo pūjyatāḍayitā kuṇiḥ ||
mārgārhāya ca yo mārgaṃ na prayacchati durmatiḥ || 3.253.20 ||
khañjo bhavati durbuddhirāsanārhasya cāsanam ||
durnāmā saṃyato nityaṃ matsarī jāyate śaṭhaḥ || 21 ||
dīpahartā bhavatyandhaḥ kāṇo nirvāpako bhavet ||
daivadattena jñānena kṛtvā karma sudurmatiḥ ||
jāyate budbudākṣo vā netrarogayuto'thavā || 22 ||
dṛṣṭvā parastriyaṃ nagnāṃ naro duṣṭena cakṣuṣā ||
jātyandhatāmavāpnoti paraloke na saṃśayaḥ || 23 ||
varṇādhikasya jīvitvā varṇahīnena karmaṇā ||
tasyaiva yonimāpnoti nātra kāryā vicāraṇā || 24 ||
tathaivādhikavarṇasya yadi jīvati karmaṇā ||
hīnavarṇastathā loke bhavatyandhaḥ sa durmatiḥ || 25 ||
yasyānnenodarasthena mṛtyumeti dvijaḥ kvacit ||
tasyaiva yonimāpnoti svakṛtenāpi karmaṇā || 26 ||
āmaśrāddhaṃ tathā bhuktvā virūpo jāyate naraḥ ||
virūpa eva bhavati tathāvikreyavikrayāt || 27 ||
vināparādhena tathā kṛtvā patnyadhivedanam ||
parājayamavāpnoti vyavahāre tathā raṇe || 28 ||
mūlakarma tathā kṛtvā vaśīkaraṇakāraṇāt ||
dveṣyo bhavati loke'smiñjagato nātra saṃśayaḥ || 29 ||
avakīrṇī ca bhavati liṅgavyādhisamanvitaḥ ||
parivettā tathā loke sarvavyādhisamanvitaḥ || 3.253.30 ||
ayājyayājakaḥ pāpo jāyate varṇasaṃkaraḥ ||
vedavikrayako mūrkhaḥ kitavaścaiva jāyate || 31 ||
vidyānarthaśca bhavati tathāvikreyavikrayī ||
ekaḥ svādvaśano loke jāyate paratarṣakaḥ || 32 ||
parivrāḍ kāmavṛttastu nityaṃ bhavati duḥkhitaḥ ||
hiṃsāratastathālpāyuryastu māṃsāśano naraḥ || 33||
nāstikastu daridraḥ syātsarvabhogavivarjitaḥ ||
atimānapravṛttastu jāyate kutsite kule || 34 ||
śvapākapukkasādīnāṃ kutsitānāmacetasām ||
kuleṣu teṣu jāyante guruvṛddhāvacāyakāḥ || 35 ||
parodvegaratirnityaṃ bahubādhaśca jāyate ||
prakīrṇamaithuno jantuḥ klībo bhavati vai dvijāḥ ||36||
naraḥ pāpasamācāro mṛtyumabhyeti kutsitam ||
śvabhre tu maraṇaṃ loke viṣaḥ sthāvarajaṅgamaiḥ || 37 ||
daṃṣṭribhyaśca nakhibhyaśca caṇḍālādbrāhmaṇāttathā||
gṛhāvapatanāccaiva drumāśvapatitaistathā || 38 ||
naubhaṃgādagnidāhācca tathodbandhanakarmaṇā ||
gobhyaśca maraṇaṃ loke vijñeyaṃ pāpakarmaṇaḥ || 39 ||
eṣāmanyatamaṃ mṛtyumāsādya narakaṃ vrajet ||
navāpi taiśca yujyante ye narāḥ śubhakāriṇaḥ || 3.253.40 ||
tāḍitā tāḍyate loke baddhā vai badhyate tathā ||
hantā vadhamavāpnoti nātra kāryā vicāraṇā || 41 ||
yadyatparasyācarati tattadasyopajāyate || 42 ||
prāpnoti yādṛśīṃ bādhāṃ yatkarmaṇi kṛte paraḥ ||
tasyāpi tādṛśī bādhā jñeyā janmanijanmani || 43 ||
tasmātsarvaprayatnena pāpabādhāṃ vivarjayet ||
śrūyatāṃ dharmasarvasvaṃ śrutvā cāpyavadhāryatām ||
ātmanaḥ pratikūlāni pareṣāṃ na samācaret || 44 ||
paropatāpakriyayā na kāryaṃ dhanārjanaṃ doṣasamūhakāri ||
paropatāpaprabhavo hi doṣaḥ śakyo na hantuṃ vibudhālaye'pi || 45 ||
iti śrīviṣṇudharmottare tṛtīyakhaṇḍe mārkaṇḍeyavajrasaṃvāde munīnprati haṃsagītāsu pāpakarmaphala nirūpaṇo nāma tripañcāśaduttaradviśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ || 253 ||
<3.254>
|| haṃsa uvāca || ||
jñānaṃ sukhāvahaṃ loke jñānaṃ cakṣuḥ paraṃ matam ||
jñānāddharmamavāpnoti jñānādarthaṃ ca mānavaḥ || 1 ||
jñānātkāmamavāpnoti jñānānmokṣaṃ ca gacchati ||
tasmātsarvaprayatnena jñāne yatnaṃ samācaret || 2 ||
jñānena sarvamāpnoti yatkiñcinmanasecchati ||
jñānaikahetuśca tathā nityaṃ śāstrānvavekṣaṇam || 3 ||
ācāryaguruśuśrūṣā vṛddhānāṃ caiva sevanam ||
anālasyamakārpaṇyaṃ divā nidrāvivarjanam || 4 ||
jñānameṣa paro bandhurjñānaṃ mārgasya deśakam ||
saṃsārabandhanakleśām̐stathā jñānena muñcati ||5|||
jñānena puruṣo vetti jñānādvairāgyamṛcchati ||
jñānena tadavāpnoti yad gatvā nānuśocati || 6 ||
etadviditvā puruṣeṇa kāryaṃ jñāne sadā yatnamadīnasattvāḥ ||
jñānaṃ samāsādya mahānubhāvā vimuktaduḥkhāḥ sukhino bhavanti || 7 ||
iti śrīviṣṇudharmottare tṛtīyakhaṇḍe mārkaṇḍeyavajrasaṃvāde munīnprati haṃsagītāsu jñānavarṇano nāma catuṣpañcāśaduttaradviśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ || 254 ||
<3.255>
|| haṃsa uvāca ||
dharme yatnaḥ sadā kāryo dharma ekaḥ sukhāvahaḥ ||
dharmeṇa pālyate sarvaṃ trailokyaṃ sacarācaram || 1 ||
dhāraṇāddharmamityāhurdharmo dhārayati prajāḥ ||
dharme saṃsevite kāmānprāpnoti manasepsitān || 2 ||
dharmeṇārthaṃ tathā loke prāpnoti manasepsitam ||
mṛtaṃ śarīramutsṛjya kāṣṭhaloṣṭasamaṃ kṣitau || 3 ||
vimukhā bāndhavā yānti dharmastvekonugacchati ||
ekasminnapyatikrānte dine dharmavivarjite || 4 ||
dasyubhirmuṣitasyeva yuktamākranditaṃ ciram ||
balādeva careddharmamanityaṃ jīvitaṃ yataḥ || 5 ||
nāsti dharmavihīnasya sukhamaṇvapi vai dvijāḥ ||
dharmeṇa devairdevatvamṛṣitvamṛṣibhistathā || 6 ||
prāptaṃ dharmeṇa sakalaṃ brahmaṇā ca kṛtaṃ jagat ||
yena sarvamidaṃ sṛṣṭaṃ sa vai dharme hariḥ sthitaḥ || 7 ||
kṛtasya dharmasya na cāsti nāśastasmāttu dharmaḥ puruṣeṇa kāryaḥ||
dharme kṛte nāsti bhayaṃ narāṇāṃ lokepare ceha tathā dvijendrāḥ || 8 ||
iti śrīviṣṇudharmāttare tṛ0 kha0 mārkaṇḍeyavajrasaṃvāde munīnprati haṃsagītāsu dharmapraśaṃsāvarṇano nāma pañcapañcāśaduttaradviśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ || 255 ||
<3.256>
haṃsa uvāca ||
jñātavyā guruśuśrūṣā paramaṃ dharmakāraṇam ||
guruśuśraṣayā vidyāṃ naraḥ samadhigacchati || 1 ||
vidyayā tadavāpnoti yatra gatvā na śocati ||
suvarṇapuṣpāṃ pṛthivīṃ vicinvanti narāstrayaḥ || 2 ||
śūraśca kṛtavidyaśca yaśca jānāti sevitum ||
sarvadharmavihīno'pi guruśuśrūṣaṇe rataḥ || 3 ||
svargalokamavāpnoti puruṣo'pi sudāruṇaḥ ||
mātaraṃ piṃtaraṃ vāpi jyeṣṭhabhrātarameva ca ||4||
guruṃ vidyāpradaṃ vāpi yaśca śuśrūṣate naraḥ ||
svargalokamavāpnoti yāvadindrāścaturdaśa ||5||
yadeva puṇyamāpnoti guruśuśrūṣayā dvijaḥ ||
dvijaśuśrūṣayā śūdrastadeva phalamaśnute ||6||
prāpnoti puṇyaṃ paramaṃ ca sadhvī śuśruṣate yā patimapramattā ||
śuśrūṣayā bhūmipateḥ prajānāṃ puṇyaṃ pradiṣṭaṃ paramaṃ nṛloke ||7||
iti śrīviṣṇudharmottare tṛ0 kha0 mārkaṇḍeyavajrasaṃvāde munīnprati haṃsagītāsu guruśuśrūṣāpraśaṃsāvarṇano nāma paṭpañcāśaduttara dviśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ || 256||
<3.257>
haṃsa uvāca ||
svādhyāyasevākarttavyā nityameva vijānatā ||
svādhyāyena tadāpnoti yatkiñcinmanasecchati || 1 ||
payasā madhunā dadhnā māṃsena ca yathākramam ||
ṛco yajūṃṣi sāmāni tathaivātharvaṇaṃ paṭhet ||2||
pitṝṇāṃ tarpaṇaṃ kuryādvedāṅgena ca sarpiṣā ||
upavedānapi paṭham̐starpayatyodanena tān || 3 ||
vinā sthānāni sarvāṇi svaireyeṇa tathā naraḥ ||
sāṃkhyayogaṃ pāñcarātraṃ tathā pāśupataṃ paṭhan || 4 ||
kṣīreṇa madhumiśreṇa pitṛṃstarpayate tathā ||
vākovākyaṃ purāṇaśca nārāśaṃsyo'tha gāthikāḥ ||5||
stotrāṇi ca paṭhannityaṃ dadhnā ca madhunā tathā ||
tasmātsarva prayatnena dvijaḥ svādhyāyamācaret || 6 ||
svādhyāya eveha tapaḥ pavitraṃ svādhyāya eveha tathaiva yajñaḥ ||
svādhyāyavāneva naraḥ prayāti triviṣṭapaṃ mokṣamathāpyabhīṣṭam||7||
iti śrīviṣṇudharmottare tṛtīyakhaṇḍe mārkaṇḍeyavajrasaṃ0 munīnprati haṃsagītāsu svādhyāyapraśaṃsāvarṇano nāma saptapañcāśaduttara dviśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ || 257 ||
<3.258>
haṃsa uvāca ||
brahmacarye'bhisaṃyukto dvijaḥ svargamupāśnute ||
brahmacaryaṃ paraṃ dhāma brahmacaryaṃ paraṃ tapaḥ || 1 ||
brahmacaryaṃ paraṃ yajño brahmacaryaṃ paraṃ yaśaḥ ||
brahmacaryeṇa saṃyuktaḥ sarvānkāmānavāpnuyāt || 2 ||
adaivaṃ daivataṃ kuryāddaivataṃ cāpyadaivatam ||
kuryāllokām̐stathaivānyānbrahmacaryeṇa mānavaḥ || 3 ||
kṛtānapi tathā pūrvānkṣiprameva praṇāśayet ||
yathābhīṣṭamavāpnoni brahmacaryeṇa mānavaḥ || 4 ||
prajāpatīnāṃ yā śaktiḥ prajāḥ kartuṃ dvijottamāḥ ||
sā jñeyā brahmacaryeṇa ṛṣīṇāmapi ca dvijāḥ || 5||
anekāni sahasrāṇi kumārabrahmacāriṇām ||
divaṃ gatāni viprāṇāmakṛtvā kulasantatim ||6||
yo brahmacaryānna paricyutaḥ syātputreṇa hīno'pi divaṃ sa yāti ||
paricyutā ye tanayairvihīnāsteṣāṃ na lokeṣu gatiḥ pradiṣṭā || 7 ||
iti śrīviṣṇudharmottare tṛtīyakhaṇḍe mārkaṇḍeyavajrasaṃvāde haṃsagītāsu brahmacaryapraśaṃsāvarṇano nāmāṣṭapañcāśa duttaradviśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ || 258 ||
<3.259>
|| haṃsa uvāca || ||
brahmacaryāccyuto yastu narassantānavarjitaḥ ||
sutaptatapasopyasya gatirloke na vidyate || 1 ||
putreṇa lokāñjayati pautreṇānantyamaśnute ||
tathā putrasya pautreṇa bradhnasyāpnoti viṣṭapam || 2 ||
nākalokamavāpnoti duhitrā dvijasattamāḥ ||
dauhitreṇa tathā lokānnaro yāti prajāpateḥ || 3 ||
punnāmno narakādyasmātpitaraṃ trāyate sutaḥ ||
tasmātputra iti proktaḥ svayameva svayambhuvā || 4 ||
putradauhitrayorloke viśeṣo nopapadyate ||
dauhitro'pi hyamutrainaṃ santārayati pautravat || 5 ||
tasmācchettā kulasya syātsa yāti narakaṃ dhruvam ||
putrapautraiḥ parivṛto nākaloke mahīyate || 6 ||
santānavāneva narastu kīrtyā yuktastathā syātparayā dvijendrāḥ ||
kīrtyā yutaḥ svargamatha prayāti santānavānyaḥ puruṣaḥ pradhānaḥ || 7 ||
iti śrīviṣṇudharmottare tṛtīyakhaṇḍe mārkaṇḍeyavajrasaṃvāde haṃsagītāsu santānapraśaṃsā varṇano nāmaikonaṣaṣṭyuttaradviśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ || 259 ||
<3.260>
|| haṃsa uvāca || ||
kīrtipradhānaḥ puruṣaściraṃ svarge mahīyate ||
kīrtiḥ śarīraṃ nirdiṣṭā mṛtasyāpi śarīriṇaḥ || 1 ||
kīrtiyuktaṃ na jānāti paratreha ca mānavaḥ ||
kīrtiyuktā bhavantīha śreyaso bhājanaṃ param || 2 ||
divaṃ spṛśati bhūmiṃ ca śabdaḥ puṇyasya karmaṇaḥ ||
yāvatsa śabdo bhavati tāvatpuruṣa ucyate || 3 ||
tāvadeva tathā lokānsarvakāmadugho'kṣayān ||
puruṣaḥ samupāśnāti divi ceha ca śāśvatam || 4 ||
yasyākīrtirmanuṣyasya narakaṃ sa tu gacchati ||
yāvallokeṣu dharmajñāstasya kīrtistu gīyate || 5 ||
tāvatsamaśnute svarge devabhogānanuttamān ||
kīrtiḥ puṇyā puṇyaloke naiva pāpaṃ prayacchati ||
kīrtyamānā manuṣyasya tasmātpuṇyaṃ samācaret || 6||
ye kīrtiyuktāḥ puruṣapradhānāste nākapṛṣṭhaṃ suciraṃ prayānti ||
akīrtiyuktā narakaṃ vrajaṃti kīrtiḥ pradhānā puruṣasya tasmāt || 7 ||
iti śrīviṣṇudharmottare tṛtīya khaṇḍe mārkaṇḍeyavajrasaṃvāde haṃsagītāsu kīrtipraśaṃsāvarṇano nāma ṣaṣṭyuttaradviśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ || 260 ||
<3.261>
|| haṃsa uvāca || ||
yaśasā saṃyuto jantuḥ śreyaso bhājanaṃ bhavet ||
yaśasā saṃyuto jantustridivaṃ pratipadyate || 1 ||
yaśasā saṃyuto jantuḥ sampadāmeti pātratām ||
yaśasā saṃyuto jantuḥ sarvakāmānupāśnute || 2 ||
sa ekaḥ puruṣo loke yaśasā yo hi saṃyutaḥ ||
yeṣāṃ sādhu samāvāpaṃ gīyate sumahadyaśaḥ || 3 ||
teṣāṃ yaśastathā puṇyaṃ nākapṛṣṭhe'pi gīyate ||
devatā yaṃ vijānaṃti tasya nāke gatirdhruvā || 4 ||
kīrtyamānaṃ tathā sadbhiryaḥ śṛṇoti yaśaḥ svakam ||
sukhamāpnoti dharmajñāḥ svargādapi paraṃ naraḥ || 5 ||
yaśaḥ śobhā manuṣyasya paramaṃ tasya maṇḍanam ||
yaśasā varjitasyeha maṇḍanaṃ lāñchanaṃ param || 6||
yaśaḥ pradhānaṃ puruṣasya loke yaśaḥpradhānāstridivaṃ prayānti ||
lokadvaye cāpi yaśo'nvitānāṃ bhavanti kāmā manasā tvabhīṣṭāḥ || 7 ||
iti śrīviṣṇudharmottare tṛ0 kha0 mārkaṇḍeyavajrasaṃvāde munīnprati haṃsagītāsu yaśaḥpraśaṃsāvaṇarno nāmaikaṣaṣṭyuttaradviśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ || 261 ||
<3.262>
haṃsa uvāca ||
yajñena devā jīvanti yajñena pitarastathā ||
devādhīnāḥ prajā sarvā yajñādhīnāśca devatāḥ || 1 ||
yajño hi bhagavānviṣṇuryatra sarvaṃ pratiṣṭhitam ||
yajñārthaṃ paśavaḥ sṛṣṭā devāstvauṣadhayastathā || 2 ||
yajñārthaṃ puruṣāḥ sṛṣṭāḥ svayameva svayaṃbhuvā ||
yajñaśca bhūtyai sarvasya tasmādyajñaparo bhavet || 3 ||
yajñaśiṣṭāśinaḥ santo mucyante sarvakilbiṣaiḥ ||
dhanaṃ yadyajñaśīlānāṃ devasvaṃ taṃ vidurbudhāḥ || 4 ||
oṣadhīnāṃ paśūnāñca yajña eva parāyaṇam ||
sayūthyaśca sakulyaśca nākapṛṣṭhe mahīyate || 5 ||
paśustu prokṣito yajñe tathevauṣadhayo dvijāḥ ||
yajñopayuktā modante tridive nātra saṃśayaḥ || 6 ||
yajñena samyakpuruṣastu nāke samṛjyamānastridaśairmahātmā ||
prāpnoti saukhyāni mahānubhāvāstasmātprayatnena yajeta yajñaiḥ || 7 ||
iti śrīviṣṇudharmottare tṛ0 kha0 mārkaṇḍeyavajrasaṃvāde munīnprati haṃsagītāsu yajñapraśaṃsāvarṇano nāma dviṣaṣṭyadhikadviśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ || 262 ||
<3.263>
haṃsa uvāca ||
śīlaṃ pradhānaṃ loke'smiṃstasminsarvaṃ pratiṣṭhitam ||
śīlena sarvamāpnoti yatkiñcinmanasecchati || 1 ||
kriyāḥ śīlavihīnasya bhavanti viphalāḥ kṛtāḥ ||
tasmācchīlavatā bhāvyaṃ puruṣeṇa vijānatā || 2 ||
śīlena devarāṭchakraḥ suraiśvaryamavāptavān ||
śīlena dharmarājo'pi dharmarājatvamāptavān ||3||
śīlena varuṇo devo rājarājatvamāptavān ||
śīlena ca kubero'pi dhanādhyakṣatvamāptavān || 4 ||
śīlena sukhamāpnoti svargaṃ ca manujottamāḥ ||
śīlena tadavāpnoti yatra gatvā na śocati || 5 ||
śīlaṃ samāsādya samastakāmānprāpnoti mukhyānpuruṣaḥ pradhānaḥ ||
tasmātprayatnena nareṇa śīlaṃ samarjanīyaṃ sukhadaṃ sadaiva || 6 ||
iti śrīviṣṇudharmottare tṛ0 kha0 mārkaṇḍeyavajrasaṃvāde munīnprati haṃsagītāsu śīlapraśaṃsāvarṇano nāma triṣaṣṭyuttaradviśatatamo 'dhyāyaḥ || 263 ||
<3.264>
haṃsa uvāca ||
damena yuktaḥ puruṣaḥ sarvānkāmānavāpnuyāt ||
tasmātsarvaprayatnena damaḥ kāryo vijānatā || 1 ||
indriyāṇāṃ jayo loke dama ityabhidhīyate ||
nādāntasya kriyāḥ kāścidbhavantīha dvijottamāḥ || 2 ||
nādāntaḥ siddhimāpnoti nādānto gatimīpsitām ||
nādāntaḥ śriyamāpnoti nādāntaḥ kiñcideva tu || 3 ||
pañcendriyasya martyasya yadyekaṃ chidramindriyam ||
tato'sya kṣarati prajñā dṛteḥ pātrādivodakam || 4 ||
puruṣārthaṃ parityajya svārthe ca satatotthitaḥ ||
indriyāṇāṃ budhasteṣāṅkaḥ svārthe prayatennaraḥ || 5 ||
indriyāṇāṃ prasaṅgena gatiṃ gacchati dāruṇām ||
vijitya tānyeva naraḥ sarvānkāmānavāpnuyāt || 6 ||
dāntaḥ pumānnākamanuprayāti dāntaḥ pumānmokṣapathaṃ prayāti ||
dāntasya duḥkhaṃ na hi kiñcidasti dāntaḥ samāpnotyakhilānhi kāmān || 7 ||
iti śrīviṣṇudharmottare tṛtīyakhaṇḍe mārkaṇḍeyavajrasaṃvāde munīnprati haṃsagītāsu damapraśaṃsāvarṇano nāma catuḥṣaṣṭyuttaradviśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ ||264|| ||
<3.265>
|| haṃsa uvāca || ||
satyameva paraṃ brahma satyameva paraṃ tapaḥ ||
satyameva paro yajñaḥ satyameva paraṃ śrutam || 1 ||
satyaṃ vedeṣu jāgarti muktiḥ satyataroḥ phalam ||
tapo yaśaśca puṇyaśca pitṛdevarṣipūjanam||2||
ādyo vidhiśca vidyā ca sarvaṃ satye pratiṣṭhitam||
satyaṃ yajñāstathā vedā mantrā devī sarasvatī || 3 ||
vratacaryā tathā satyamoṅkāraḥ satyameva ca ||
satyena vāyurabhyeti satyenābhāsate raviḥ || 4 ||
satyena cāgnirdahati svargaṃ satyena gacchati ||
satyena cāpaḥ kṣipati parjanyaḥ pṛthivītale || 5 ||
pāraṇaṃ sarvavedānāṃ sarvatīrthāvagāhanam ||
satyaṃ ca vadate loke samaṃ syātāṃ na vā samam || 6 ||
aśvamedhasahasraṃ tu satyaṃ ca tulayā dhṛtam ||
aśvamedhasahasrāddhi satyameva viśiṣyate || 7 ||
satyena devāḥ prīyante pitaro brāhmaṇāstathā ||
satyamāhuḥ paraṃ dharmaṃ tasmātsatyaṃ na lopayet || 8 ||
munayaḥ satyaniratāḥ satyasthāstridaśottamāḥ ||
svarge satyaparā nityaṃ modante devatā iva || 9 ||
apsarogaṇasaṃkīrṇairvimānairupayānti ca ||
vaktavyaṃ hi sadā satyaṃ na satyādvidyate param || 3.265.10 ||
agādhe vimale śuddhe satyatoye śucihrade ||
snātavyaṃ mānase tīrthe snānaṃ tatparamaṃ smṛtam || 11 ||
ātmārthe vā parārthe vā putrārthe vā ripoḥ kṛte ||
yenṛtaṃ nābhibhāṣante te narāḥ svargagāminaḥ || 12 ||
yadbhūtahitamatyantaṃ tatsatyamiti dhāraṇā ||
hitaṃ syādyena bhūtānāṃ tattadvācyaṃ vijānatā||13||
ādityacandrāvanilānalau ca dyaurbhūmirāpo hṛdayaṃ yamaśca ||
ahaśca rātriśca śubhe ca sandhye dharmaśca jānāti narasya vṛttam || 14 ||
na sā sabhā yatra na santi vṛddhā vṛddhā na te ye na vadanti dharmam ||
nāsau dharmo yatra no satyamasti na tatsatyaṃ yacchalenānuviddham || 15 ||
iti śrīviṣṇudharmottare tṛtīyakhaṇḍe mārkaṇḍeyavajrasaṃvāde munīnprati haṃsagītāsu satyapraśaṃsāvarṇano nāma pañcaṣaṣṭyuttaradviśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ || 265 || ||
<3.266>
|| haṃsa uvāca || ||
tapaso hi paraṃ nāsti tapasā vidante mahat ||
prajāpatīnāṃ yā śaktiḥ prajāḥ kartuṃ dvijottamāḥ || 1 ||
tapasā saṃpradāne na ṛṣīṇāṃ jñānameva ca ||
bahavo divi modante prasādāttapaso janāḥ || 2 ||
tapasā prāpyate svargastapasā prāpyate yaśaḥ ||
āyuḥprakarṣaṃ bhogāṃstu tapasā vindate mahat || 3 ||
jñānaṃ vijñānamāstikyaṃ saubhāgyaṃ rūpamuttamam ||
tapasā prāpyate sarvaṃ yatkiñcinmanasepsitam || 4 ||
rasāyanāni mantrāśca vidyāstvauṣadhayastathā ||
nātaptatapasaḥ siddhiṃ prayānti dvijapuṅgavāḥ || 5 ||
yatkāryaṃ kiñcidāsthāya puruṣastapyate tapaḥ ||
sarvaṃ tasmādavāpnoti paratreha ca mānavaḥ || 6 ||
brahmahā ca surāpaśca stenaśca gurutalpagaḥ ||
tapasā mucyate jantuḥ sarvataścāpi mucyate || 7 ||
tapasaiva sutaptena mokṣamāpnoti mānavaḥ ||
śakravaivasvatā'mbhopadhanādhipahutāśanaiḥ || 8 ||
sthānaṃ mahattaraṃ prāptaṃ tathā rudrārkarātripaiḥ ||
na cāsti tatsukhaṃ loke yadvinā tapasā bhavet || 9 ||
tapasaiva sukhaṃ sarvamiti dharmavido viduḥ ||
aṣṭāśītisahasrāṇāṃ munīnāmūrdhvaretasām || 3.266.10 ||
tapasā divi modante tridaśaiḥ sahitā dvijāḥ ||
puruṣastu samārambhānniṣphalānvīkṣya cātmanaḥ || 11 ||
tapaḥ kuryātprayatnena na hyanuptaṃ prarohati ||
kīṭāḥ pataṅgāḥ paśavo vayāṃsi ca tathā dvijāḥ || 12 ||
sthāvarāṇi ca bhūtāni divaṃ yānti tapobalāt ||
yaddustaraṃ yaddurāpaṃ yaddurgaṃ yacca duṣkaram || 13 ||
sarvaṃ tattapasā sādhyaṃ tapo hi duratikramaḥ ||
yasminkarmaṇyapi kṛte manasaḥ syādalāghavam || 14 ||
tasmiṃstāvattapaḥ kuryādyāvattuṣṭikaraṃ bhavet||
yatkiñcidenaḥ kurvanti manovākkarmabhirjanāḥ||15||
tatsarvaṃ vinudantyāśu tapasaiva tapodhanāḥ||
tapasaiva viśuddhasya brāhmaṇasya divaukasaḥ|| 16 ||
satyācca pratigṛhṇanti kāmānsaṃvardhayanti ca ||
brahmā prajeśvaro devo mahādevastathā dvijāḥ || 17 ||
nityameva tapasyanti tapaḥ parama dustaram ||
yasyedaṃ bhuvanaṃ sarvaṃ yaśca sarvamayo'cyutaḥ || 18 ||
yaśca dhātā jayatyasminsa viṣṇuḥ parameśvaraḥ ||
nityameva mahābhāgāstapaścarati duścaram ||19 ||
bhagavānvāsudevastu tapaḥ paramihocyate ||
aṣṭau devagaṇāṃlloke prāpnoti tapasā naraḥ || 3.266.20 ||
trailokyakaraṇasyāpi śaktaśca tapasā bhavet ||
divyānāñca tathāstrāṇāṃ śakto bhavati dhāraṇāt || 21 ||
loke surāṇāṃ bhuvi vā dvijendrā na vidyate tatpuruṣasya kiñcit ||
tapaḥ prakarṣeṇa pareṇa labdhirna vidyate yasya mahānubhāvāḥ ||22 ||
iti śrīviṣṇudharmottare tṛtīyakhaṇḍe mārkaṇḍeyavajrasaṃvāde munīnprati haṃsagītāsu tapaḥpraśaṃ sāvarṇanonāma ṣaṭṣaṣṭyuttaradviśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ || 266 ||
<3.267>
haṃsa uvāca ||
vaśe jagadidaṃ prāhuḥ śūrabāhuṣu lambate ||
śūrāṇāmatha sarveṣāṃ śauryameva viśiṣyate ||1||
brāhmaṇārthe gavārthe vā svāmyarthe mitrakāraṇe ||
āhṛtau ye prapadyante nākapṛṣṭhaṃ vrajaṃti te ||2||
dhanāpahāre saṃprāpte saṃprāpte dāraviplave ||
(ātmanaśca paritrāṇe yudhyamāno divaṃ vrajet || 3 ||
kṣatraviṭchūdraviprāṇāṃ yuddhameva parāyaṇam ||)
ātmanaśca paritrāṇe saṃprāpte deśaviplave ||4||
dāranāśe tu saṃprāpte dakṣiṇānāṃ ca saṅkare ||
viprastu śastraṃ gṛhṇīyātsaṃkare cāpyupasthite || 5 ||
vṛttintu kalpayedvipraḥ kṣattradharmeṇa vā na vā ||
saṃgrāmānna nivartteta tasya dharmaṃ hitaṃ tadā || 6 ||
ekaputraṃ tṛṇamukhaṃ tavāsmīti ca vādinam ||
strībālarakṣakambhīruṃ palāyanaparāyaṇam || 7 ||
vyāyudhaṃ vyasanasthaṃ ca pareṇa saha saṃgatam ||
dhuryaṃ ca dhuryavāhaṃ ca yuddhaprekṣakameva ca ||8||
na hantavyā dvijaśreṣṭhā yuddhadharmaṃ vijānatā ||
yuddhaṃ na kuryādyā nasthaḥ puruṣeṇa padātinā||9||
samena yuddhamākāṅkṣastathaivābhyadhikena vā||
naraḥ prāpnoti narakaṃ hīnahā taddhato'pi vā ||3.267.10||
avarmayitvā yo vāhaṃ cātmānaṃ varmayennaraḥ ||
sa śīghraṃ narakaṃ yāti sukṛtenāpi karmaṇā || 11||
kathito yuddhadharmo'yaṃ phalaṃ cāto nibodhata ||
dharmalābho'rthalābhaśca yaśolābhaśca tasya vai||12||
yaḥ śūro yuddhyate yuddhe vimṛndanparavāhinīm||
tasya dharmārthakāmāśca yajñāścaivāptadakṣiṇāḥ||13||
agniṣṭomādibhiryajñairiṣṭvā vipuladakṣiṇaiḥ||
na tatphalamavāpnoti saṃgrāme yadavāpyate || 14 ||
asṛgyadāsya gātrebhyo viniryāti raṇājire ||
tenaiva sārdhaṃ niryāti tasya pāpaṃ śarīrataḥ ||15||
sahate ca paraṃ kleśaṃ prahārāṇāṃ ca ropaṇe ||
yadvīraḥ samavāpnoti tapo nāstyadhikaṃ tataḥ || 16 ||
yasya yuddhe prahārā ye kintu syāddaivayogataḥ ||
yudhyamānasya dharmajñāḥ so'pi nāke mahīyate || 17 ||
raṇe yathādhikavadhaṃ kṛtvā phalamupāśnute ||
paraṃ hyabhimukhaṃ hatvā tadyānaṃ yo'dhirohati||18||
viṣṇukrāntaṃ sa jayati caivaṃ yudhyanraṇājire ||
yastu senāpatiṃ hatvā tadyānamadhirohati || 19 ||
aśvamedhānavāpnoti caturastena karmaṇā ||
yāvanti tasya śastrāṇi bhiṃdanti tvacamāhave || 3.267.20 ||
tāvato labhate lokānsarvakāmadughākṣayān ||
yatrayatra hataḥ śūraḥ śatrubhirvinipātitaḥ || || 21 ||
akṣayāṃllabhate lokānnirvṛtiṃ dīnatāṃ vinā ||
svāmī sukṛtamādatte hatānāṃ vipalāyinām || 22 ||
svāmino duṣkṛtaṃ sarvaṃ tadā ca pratipadyate ||
palāyamānaḥ puruṣastathā prāpnoti vai dvijāḥ || 23 ||
padepade durācāro govadhasya phalaṃ naraḥ ||
jīvam̐llakṣmīṃ samāpnoti mṛtaśca tridivaṃ naraḥ || 24 ||
yaśaścobhayathā viprā nāsti yuddhe nirarthatā ||
parānabhimukhaḥ śūro yāvadvrajati vai dvijāḥ || 25 ||
padepade yajñaphalamānupūrvyātsamāpnuyāt ||
abhagnañca paraṃ hanyādbhagnaṃ ca parirakṣati || 26 ||
palāyante sthitā yasminsa yāti paramāṃ gatim ||
ūrdhvaṃ tiryak ca yaścārvāk prāṇānsaṃtyajya yudhyati || 27 ||
hatāsuśca patedyuddhe sa svargānna nivartate ||
yasya yuddhe praviṣṭasya dehaṃ cihnīkṛtaṃ paraiḥ || 28 ||
ātmānaṃ yūpamutsṛjya sa yajño'nantadakṣiṇaḥ ||
nāśaucabhājastasya syurbāndhavā dvijasattamāḥ || 29 || 
śrāddhañcaivāgnisaṃskārastathaivāpyudakakriyā ||
mṛtasya nopayujyante saṃgrāme'bhimukhasya tu ||3.267.30||
kriyante tasya dharmajñā ānṛṇyārthaṃ svabandhubhiḥ ||
yasya cihnīkṛtaṃ gātraṃ śaraśaktyṛṣṭitomaraiḥ || 31 ||
devakanyāstu taṃ vīra ramayante ramanti ca ||
varāpsaraḥsahasrāṇi śūramāyodhane hatam || 32 ||
tvaritāstvabhidhāvanti mama bhartā mameti ca ||
hatasyābhimukhasyājau patitasyānivartinaḥ || 33 ||
hriyate yatparairdravyaṃ naramedhaphalaṃ tu tat ||
śakyaṃ tviha samṛddhaistu yaṣṭaṃ kratuśatairnaraiḥ || 34 ||
ātmadehaṃ tu saṃgrāme tyaktuṃ viprāḥ suduṣkaram ||
yāṃ yajñasaṃghaistapasā ca viprāḥ svargaiṣiṇaḥ sattvacayaiḥ prayānti ||
kṣaṇena tāmaiva gatiṃ prayānti mahāhave svāṃ tanumutsṛjantaḥ || 35 ||
sarve ca vedāḥ saha ṣaḍbhiraṃgaiḥ sāṃkhyaṃ ca yogaṃ ca vane ca vāsaḥ ||
etānguṇāneka evātideśetsaṃgrāmadhāmnā svatanuṃ tyajedyaḥ ||36 ||
iti śrīviṣṇudharmottare tṛtīyakhaṇḍe mārkaṇḍeyavajrasaṃvāde haṃsagītāsu śūraguṇavarṇano nāma saptaṣaṣṭyuttaradviśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ || || 267 ||
<3.268>
haṃsa uvāca ||
ahiṃsā sarvadharmāṇāṃ dharmaḥ para ihocyate ||
ahiṃsayā tadāpnoti yatkiñcinmanasepsitam || 1 ||
strīhiṃsā dhanahiṃsā ca prāṇihiṃsā tathaiva ca ||
trividhāṃ varjayanhiṃsāṃ brahmalokaṃ prapadyate || 2 ||
dākṣiṇyaṃ rūpalāvaṇyaṃ saubhāgyamapi cottamam ||
dhanaṃ dhānyamathārogyaṃ dharmaṃ vidyāṃ tathā striyaḥ || 3 ||
rājyaṃ bhogām̐śca vipulānbrāhmaṇyamapi cepsitam ||
aṣṭau caiva guṇānvāpi dīrghaṃ jīvitameva ca ||4 ||
ahiṃsakāḥ prapadyaṃte yadanyadapi durlabham||
ahiṃsakastathā janturmāṃsavarjayitā bhaveta||5||
māsimāsyaśvamedhena yo yajeta śataṃ samāḥ||
māṃsāni ca na khādedyaḥ samaṃ vā syānna vā samama||6||
sadā yajati satreṇa sadā dānaṃ prayacchati ||
sadā tapasvī bhavati madhumāṃsasya varjanāt||7||
sarvavedā na tatkuryuḥ sarvadānāni caiva hi ||
yo māṃsarasamāsvādya sarvamāṃsāni varjayet || 8 ||
duṣkaraṃ hi rasajñena māṃsasya parivarjanama ||
caturvratamidaṃ śreṣṭhaṃ prāṇināṃ mṛtyubhīruṇām || 9 ||
tadā bhavati loke'sminprāṇināṃ jīvitaiṣiṇām ||
viśvāsyaścopagamyaśca nahi hiṃsāruciryathā || 3.268.10 ||
prāṇā yathātmano'bhīṣṭā bhūtānāmapi te tathā ||
ātmaupamyena gantavyamātmavidbhirmahātmabhiḥ || 11 ||
ahiṃsā paramo dharmaḥ satyameva dvijottamāḥ ||
lobhādvā mohato vāpi yo māṃsānyatti mānavaḥ || 12 ||
nirghṛṇaḥ sa tu mantavyaḥ sarvadharmavivarjitaḥ ||
svamāṃsaṃ paramāṃsena yo vardhayitumicchati || 13 ||
udvignavāse vasati yatrayatrābhijāyate ||
dhanena krayiko hanti upabhogena khādakaḥ || 14 ||
ghātako vadhabandhābhyāmityeṣa trividho vadhaḥ ||
bhakṣayitvā tu yo māṃsaṃ paścādapi nivarttate || 15 ||
tasyāpi sumahāndharmo yaḥ pāpādvinivarttate ||
rākṣasairvā piśācairvā ḍākinībhirniśācaraiḥ || 16 ||
tathānyairnābhibhūyeta yo māṃsaṃ parivarjayet ||
khecarāścāvagacchanti jīvato'sya mṛtasya vā || 17 ||
pṛṣṭhato dvijaśārdūla yo māṃsaṃ parivarjayet ||
tathānyairnābhibhūyeta yo māṃsaṃ parivarjayet || 18 ||
citādhūmasya gandhe'pi mṛtasyāpi niśācarāḥ ||
kravyāde vipraṇaśyanti yo māṃsaṃ parivarjayet ||19||
śastrāgninṛpacaurebhyaḥ salilācca tathā viṣāt ||
bhayaṃ na vidyate tasya tathānyadapi kiñcana || 3.268.20 ||
na tām̐llokānprapadyaṃte ye lokā māṃsavarjinām ||
sa daṇḍī sa ca vikrāntaḥ sa yajvā sa tapasyati || 21 ||
sa sarvalokānāpnoti yo māṃsaṃ parivarjayeta ||
na tasya durlabhaṃ kiñcittathā lokadvaye bhavet ||
varaśāpasamarthaśca tathā syānmāṃsavarjakaḥ || 22||
vimānamāruhya śaśāṅkatulyaṃ devāṅganābhiḥ sahito nṛvīraḥ ||
sukhāni bhuktvā suciraṃ hi nāke lokānavāpnoti pitāmahasya || 23 ||
iti śrīviṣṇudharmottare tṛtīyakhaṇḍe mārkaṇḍeyavajrasaṃvāde haṃsagītāsu hiṃsādoṣavarṇano nāmāṣṭaṣaṣṭyadhikadviśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ || 268 ||
<3.269>
haṃsa uvāca ||
kṣamā dharmaḥ kṣamā satyaṃ kṣamā śaucaṃ kṣamā balam ||
kṣamā yajñāḥ kṣamā dānaṃ kṣamā ca paramaṃ tapaḥ || 1 ||
ākṛṣṭastāḍito vāpi pareṣāṃ yastitikṣate ||
tasmātsukṛtamādatte duṣkṛtaṃ ca prayacchati || 2 ||
yaḥ kṣamāvānsa dharmātmā kṣamayaiva dvijottamāḥ ||
ākroṣṭāraṃ nirdahati svargalokaṃ ca gacchati || 3 ||
ekaḥ kṣamāvatāṃ doṣo dvitīyo nopalabhyate ||
yadenaṃ kṣamayā yuktaṃ tvaśaktaṃ manyate janaḥ || 4 ||
vijñeyaḥ so'pi doṣo'sya dvijendrāḥ sa mahān guṇaḥ ||
tasmātpuṇyasamādānād duṣkṛtasya samarpaṇāt || 5 ||
mṛtaḥ kṣamāvānpuruṣo brahmalokaṃ prapadyate ||
vimānenārkavarṇena haṃsayuktena bhāsvatā || 6 ||
prāpnoti kāmāndivi ceha mukhyānsaṃvatsarāṇāṃ niyutāni viprāḥ ||
sarvaṃsahaḥ kṣāntiparo manuṣyastvataḥ kṣamāvānsatataṃ naraḥ syāt || 7 ||
iti śrīviṣṇudharmottare tṛtīya khaṇḍe mārkaṇḍeyavajrasaṃvāde munīnprati haṃsagītāsu kṣamāguṇavarṇano nāmaikonasaptatyadhikadviśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ || 269 ||
<3.270>
haṃsa uvāca ||
ānṛśaṃsyaṃ paro dharmo loke'smindvijasattamāḥ ||
tasmādātmavatā bhāvyaṃ kṛtajñena nareṇa tu || 1 ||
brahmaghne ca surāpe ca caure bhagnavrate tathā ||
niṣkṛtirvihitā rājankṛtaghne nāsti niṣkṛtiḥ || 2 ||
narakānna vimucyante ye kṛtaghnā narādhamāḥ ||
ābhūtasaṃplavaṃ ghorā nātra kāryā vicāraṇā || 3 ||
tiryagyonau hi jāyante duḥkhāvāsaṃ vasanti ca ||
labhante na kvacittrāṇaṃ tathā prāpte bhaye narāḥ || 4 ||
etāvāneva puruṣaḥ kṛtaṃ yasminna naśyati ||
kṛtajñaḥ svargamāpnoti kṛtajño vindate mahat || 5 ||
prabhuṃ vā yadi vā bhṛtyaṃ vipraṃ gurumathāpi vā ||
āpannaṃ na tyajedyastu sa tu nāke mahīyate || 6 ||
samṛddhaṃ yastu saṃsevya daridraṃ yo vimuñcati ||
sa nṛśaṃsatamo loke naro narakabhājanam || 7 ||
na tyajeta naro bhṛtyaṃ svagehe gatayauvanam ||
asamarthaṃ tu vārdhakye tyaktvā narakamāpnuyāt || 8 ||
kuñjaranturagaṃ vṛddhaṃ dhenuṃ dāntamathāpi vā ||
yastyajetsa sudurbuddhirnarakaṃ pratipadyate || 9 ||
nādriyante paśūnbaddhvā yavasenodakena vā ||
yathāvad dvijaśārdūlāste narā nirayaṅgatāḥ || 3.270.10 ||
acikitsyena rogeṇa gṛhītaṃ svagṛhe paśum ||
cikitsyaṃ na tyajedenaṃ yāvajjīvaṃ dvijottamāḥ || 11 ||
tasya tyāgādavāpnoti mānavo narakaṃ dhuvam ||
samṛddhaṃ svāminaṃ yastu sevitvā puruṣādhamaḥ ||
asamṛddhaṃ tyajetpaścād dhruvaṃ narakamṛcchati || 12 ||
yo naraḥ kleśamāpnoti hīnārthasvāmisevanāt ||
tattapo'sya vinirdaṣṭaṃ brahmalokāya vai dvijāḥ || || 13 ||
kṛtajñaḥ puruṣo loke kṛtajñaḥ sarvabhāktathā ||
kṛtajñaḥ sarvamāpnoti yatkiñcinmanasecchati || 14 ||
mātaraṃ pitaraṃ putraṃ yājyamṛtvijameva ca ||
svāminaṃ ca guruṃ bhṛtyaṃ bāndhavaṃ paśumeva ca || 15 ||
bhāryāṃ vāpi dvijaśreṣṭhā na tyajenmanasā kvacit ||
eṣāṃ tu patati tyāge dhruvaṃ narakamṛcchati ||16||
na tyajeta tathaivāgniṃ nāgnimutsādayettathā ||
devatārcāṃ tathā viprāstyaktvā narakamṛcchati||17||
chāyāyāṃ yasya viśrānto naraḥ syātpādapasya tu ||
tasmātpatraṃ na hiṃseta kṛtajñatvamanusmaran || 18 ||
kṛtajño bhūtimāpnoti kṛtajñaśca tathā yaśaḥ ||
kṛtajño nākamāpnoti brahmalokamathāpi vā ||19 ||
kṛtajñairvasudhā sarvā dhāryate sumahātmabhiḥ || 3.270.20 ||
kṛtajña eva yajate kṛtajñastapyate tapaḥ ||
yajvā dātā tathā śūraḥ kṛtajñaśca tathā bhavet ||
kṛtajñastyajate prāṇānmitrakārye tathā dhuvam || 21 ||
yo brahmalokastapasā na śakyaḥ prāptuṃ dvijendrā mahataḥ sukṛcchrāt ||
taṃ brahma lokaṃ hi naraḥ kṛtajñaḥ prāpnoti śīghraṃ na hi saṃśayo'tra || 22||
iti śrīviṣṇudharmottare tṛtīyakhaṇḍe mārkaṇḍeyavajrasaṃvāde munīnprati haṃsagītāsu kṛtajñatāguṇanirūpaṇo nāma saptatyadhikadviśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ ||270||
<3.271>
haṃsa uvāca||
ācāraḥ svargajanana ācāraḥ kīrtivardhanaḥ ||
ācāraśca tathāyuṣyo dhanyo lokasukhāvahaḥ || 1 ||
sarvalakṣaṇahīno'pi sa sadācāravānnaraḥ ||
sarvalakṣaṇalakṣaṇyairvijñeyaḥ puruṣo varaḥ ||2||
ācārastrividho loke sevitavyo vicakṣaṇaiḥ ||
śrutismṛtyuditaścaiva sadācārastathā paraḥ ||
ācāraprabhavaṃ saukhyaṃ dhanaṃ ca dvijasattamāḥ ||3||
ācārayuktastridivaṃ prayāti ācāravāneva bhavatyarogaḥ ||
ācāravāneva cirantu jīvedācāravāneva bhunakti lakṣmīm || 4 ||
iti śrīviṣṇudharmottare tṛtīyakhaṇḍe mārkaṇḍeya vajrasaṃvāde munīnprati haṃsagītāsvācāravarṇano nāmaikasaptatyadhikadviśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ || 271 ||
<3.272>
haṃsa uvāca ||
śaucaṃ devāḥ praśaṃsanti śaucaṃ brahmarṣayastathā||
śaucayuktena yaddattaṃ tadaśnanti divaukasaḥ ||1||
sarveṣāmeva dharmāṇāṃ śaucaṃ vā phalabhāgbhavet ||
śaucavāneva puruṣo yatheṣṭāṅgatimāpnuyāt || 2 ||
śarīraśaucaṃ jalamṛdbhiruktamalobhaśaucantu manaḥ pramārṣṭi ||
mano viśuddhaṃ puruṣasya yasya sa lokamāpnoti pitāmahasya || 3 ||
iti śrī viṣṇudharmottare tṛ0 kha0 mārkaṇḍeyavajrasaṃvāde munīnprati haṃsagītāsu śaucavarṇano nāma dvisaptatyadhikadviśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ || 272 ||
<3.273>
|| haṃsa uvāca ||
tīrthānusaraṇaṃ śaucaṃ tīrthānusaraṇaṃ tapaḥ ||
tīrthāmbhobhirnarāḥ pūtā yānti nākamasaṃśayam || 1 ||
tīrthānusaraṇaṃ puṇyaṃ cādhvanā prāpnuyānnaraḥ ||
adhvanā kṣīṇapāpastu tridivaṃ pratipadyate || 2 ||
pāpmanāṃ pāpaśamanaṃ vidhatte tapasā vinā ||
vipāpmanā tathā tulyaṃ vidhatte yatprakīrtitam || 3 ||
bahūpakaraṇā yajñā nānāsambhāravistarāḥ ||
na te śakyā daridreṇa prāptuṃ dharmabhṛtāṃvarāḥ || 4 ||
daridrairanyathā prāptuṃ śakyaṃ yajñaphalaṃ dvijāḥ ||
tīrthānusaraṇaṃ kṛtvā tasmāttīrthaparo bhavet || 5 ||
yasya hastau ca pādau ca manaścaiva susaṃyatam ||
vidyā tapaśca kīrtiśca sa tīrthaphalamaśnute || 6 ||
pāpānāṃ pāpaśamanaṃ dharmavṛddhistathā satām ||
vijñeyaṃ sevitaṃ tīrthaṃ tasmāttīrthaparo bhavet || 7 ||
ye sāgnayo dhanopetāstīrthānusaraṇe ratāḥ ||
teṣāṃ yakṣaphalāvāptirvināpi dhanasaṃcayaiḥ || 8 ||
sarveṣāmeva varṇānāṃ sarvāśramanivāsinām ||
tīrthaṃ phalapradaṃ jñeyaṃ nātra kāryā vicāraṇā || 9 ||
kāryāntareṇa yo gatvā snānaṃ tīrthe samācaret ||
tīrthayātrāphalaṃ tasya nāsti snānaphalaṃ tataḥ || 3.273.10 ||
adhvanāṃ kleśaduḥkhena tīrthayātrāphalaṃ bhavet ||
tīrthānusaraṇaṃ padbhyāṃ tapaḥ paramihocyate || 11 ||
tadeva kṛtvā yānena snānamātraphalaṃ labhet ||
yasyānye kurvate śaktyā tīrthayātrāṃ tathā naraḥ || 12 ||
yānāsanābhyāṃ ca tathā phalaṃ tasya caturguṇam ||
tīrthānusaraṇaṃ kṛtvā kṣetrabhugvijitendriyaḥ ||13||
phalaṃ prāpnoti viprendrāstīrthāddaśaguṇaṃ bhavet ||
chatropānahahīnastu tathā śataguṇaṃ labhet || 14 ||
chatropānahahīnastu bhaikṣyāśī vijitendriyaḥ ||
mahāpātakajairghoraiḥ kṣipraṃ pāpairvimucyate || 15 ||
na bhaikṣyaṃ parapākaṃ tu na ca bhaikṣyaṃ pratigrahaḥ ||
somapānasamaṃ bhaikṣyaṃ tasmādbhaikṣyaṃ samācaret || 16 ||
loke'smindvividhastīrthaḥ svacchando ninditastathā ||
puṣkarādyāstu ye tīrthā svaprabhāvamahattarāḥ || 17 ||
teṣāṃ tīrthaprabhāveṇa kṛtaḥ pūrvaiḥ pratigrahaḥ ||
kaṇvāśramādyā ye tīrthā kaṇvādīnāṃ prabhāvataḥ|| 18 ||
puṇyāste kathitā loke pāpaghnāḥ puṇyavṛddhidāḥ ||
dviprakārasya tīrthasya sevā jñeyā mahāphalā || 19 ||
ye tīrthasevāniratā dvijeṃdrāste pāpahīnā divamāpnuvanti ||
tatroṣya kālaṃ suciraṃ sukhante mānuṣyaloke sukhamāpnuvanti || 3.273.20 ||
iti śrīviṣṇudharmottare tṛ0kha0mārkaṇḍeyavajrasaṃvāde munīnprati haṃsagītāsu tīrthaphalavarṇano nāma trisaptatyadhikadviśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ || 273 ||
<3.274>
haṃsa uvāca ||
bahūpakaraṇā yajñā nānāsambhāravistarāḥ ||
na te śakyā daridreṇa prāptuṃ dharmabhṛtāṃvarāḥ || 1 ||
daridrairapyathāvāptuṃ śakyaṃ yajñaphalaṃ dvijāḥ ||
sadopavāsanirataistasmādupavasennaraḥ || 2 ||
yāvanmāsopavāsantu tāvattapa ihocyate ||
anāśakamata ūrdhvaṃ dvijātīnāṃ prakīrtitam || 3 ||
ṣaṣṭhakālāśanaṃ śūdre tapaḥ paramihocyate ||
varṇasaṃkarajātīnāṃ dinamekaṃ prakīrtitam || 4 ||
ṣaṣṭhakālātparaṃ śūdrastapaḥ kuryādyathā kvacit ||
rāṣṭre hānistadā jñeyā rājñaścopadravo mahān || 5 ||
śūdrastu ṣaṣṭhakālāśī yāvacchaktyā tapaścaret ||
māsopavāsādarvāgvā tathā śaktyā dvijaścaret || 6 ||
nāsti gaṅgāsamaṃ tīrthaṃ nāsti kṛṣṇasamā gatiḥ ||
gāyatrīsadṛśaṃ japyaṃ homaṃ vyāhṛtibhiḥ samam || 7 ||
antarjale tathāvāptiḥ pāpaghnamaghamarṣaṇāt ||
ahiṃsāsadṛśaṃ puṇyaṃ dānātsaṃsavanaṃ param || 8 ||
evameva dvijā nāsti tapo nānaśanātparam ||
upavāsaiḥ prataptānāṃ dīrghaṃ sukhamanantaram || 9 ||
upavāsatapaḥśīlā nākalokamupāgatāḥ ||
modante tridaśe sārdhaṃ yathā devagaṇāstathā || 3.274.10 ||
pāpānāṃ pāpaśamanaṃ satāmīpsitakāmadam ||
upavāso vinirdiṣṭo viśeṣāddevatāśrayaḥ || 11 ||
upavāsaprasaktānāṃ prasādasumukhāḥ surāḥ ||
yathecchamapi jāyante nātra kāryā vicāraṇā || 12 ||
vāsudevamathāśritya naraḥ samyagupoṣitaḥ ||
anantaphalamāpnoti tvanantaḥ saṃyato hariḥ || 13 ||
vāsudevamathāśritya brāhmaṇairyairupoṣitam ||
tedyāpi na nivartante samudrebhya ivāpagāḥ || 14 ||
upoṣitā ye vidhivanmanupyāste nākamāsādya suroṣamānāḥ ||
devāṅganābhiḥ sahitā manuṣyā bhavanti loke gatasarvaduḥkhāḥ || 15 ||
iti śrīviṣṇudharmottare tṛ0 kha0 mārkaṇḍeyavajrasaṃvāde munīnprati haṃsagītāsūpavāsapha lavarṇano nāma catuḥsaptatyuttaradviśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ || 274 ||
<3.275>
haṃsa uvāca ||
manaḥsamādhisaṃyuktastīrthānusāraṇātphalam ||
prāpnoti sumahadviprāstathā samyagupoṣitaḥ||1||
sarva eva manuṣyasya sarvakarmasu sādhakam ||
manasā tu viśuddhena yadyadācarate naraḥ ||2||
na tadbahuphalaṃ tasya pretya ceha ca jāyate||
mano hi paramaṃ bhūtaṃ tacchauce prayatetsadā||3||
manaḥśaucayuto janturnarakaṃ na prapadyate||
nākalokamavāpnoti tvatha vā paramaṃ padama||4||
guṇaistu saha yo vāso gatadoṣasya mānadāḥ||
upavāsassa vijñeyo na śarīrasya śoṣaṇam||5||
nirmale susukhe śuddhe satyakūle mahāhrade ||
snātavyaṃ mānase tīrthe taddhi tīrthamanuttamam ||6||
atrāpyudāharantīmaṃ ślokaṃ paurāṇamacyutāḥ ||
yamo vaivasvato devo yastavaiṣa hadi sthitaḥ ||
ātmanaścāvivādaścenmā gaṅgāṃ mā kurūngamaḥ||7||
mano lāghavadaṃ karma satataṃ tu samācaret ||
sarvāṇi tasya puṇyāni bhavantyabhyadhikāni tu ||8||
manaḥśaucasamāyukto yāni martyo niṣevate||
mana eva manuṣyasya pūrvarūpāṇi śaṃsati||9||
mana eva vijānāti karmaṇo gurulāghavam||
satyaṃ śaucaṃ damaḥ śaucaṃ śaucaṃ śārīrameva ca||3.275.10||
sarvāṇi tasya śaucāni yasya śuddhaṃ manaḥ sadā ||
manaḥśuddhivihīnasya sarve śaucā nirarthakāḥ ||11||
na tu sā kṛtrimā śakyā kartuṃ nāstikyavarjitaiḥ ||
yasya doṣaparityaktaṃ guṇādhīnaṃ sadā manaḥ || 12 ||
sa eva yajate yajñaiḥ sa ekastapate tapaḥ ||
sa ekaḥ sarvamāpnoti yatkiñcinmanasecchati||
tasmāddhi sarvaśaucānāṃ manaḥśaucaṃ paraṃ smṛtam ||13||
ātmā nadī puṇyataṭā sutīrthā satyodakā dhṛtikūlā dayormiḥ||
yasyāṃ snātaḥ pūyate puṇyakarmā tīrthaṃ hyātmā nityasaṃbhoga eva ||14 ||
iti śrīviṣṇudharmottare tṛ0 kha0 mā0 saṃ0 munīnprati haṃsagītāsu manaḥśaucanirūpaṇo nāma pañcasapta tyadhikadviśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ || 275 ||
<3.276>
haṃsa uvāca ||
śraddhānvitena manasā yadyatkiñcitsamācaret ||
tattadbahuphalaṃ tasya jāyate lokayordvayoḥ || || 1 ||
śraddhāmayo'yaṃ puruṣo yo yacchraddhaḥ sa eva saḥ||
devaśraddhā narā devāḥ kathitā devayājinaḥ||2||
pitṛśraddhāśca pitaro daitye śraddhā diteḥ sutāḥ||
pāpaśraddhāstathā pāpā vijñeyā narakaṅgamāḥ || 3 ||
tasmācchraddhāṃ samāsthāya dharmaṃ dharmī samācaret ||
bahudvārasya dharmasya nehāsti viphalā kriyā ||
āstikyabhāvamāsthāya naraḥ śraddhāsamanvitaḥ || 4 ||
nityaṃ puṇyāni kurvīta pāpāni parivarjayet ||
puṇyaṃ bahuphalaṃ tasya śraddhāmāsthāya yatkṛtam ||85 ||
śraddhā pavitraṃ paramaṃ hi loke śraddhā śarīraṃ puruṣasya coktam ||
śraddhāṃ samāsthāya naraśca kṛtvā puṇyāni puṇyāṃ gatimāpnuvanti || 6 ||
iti śrīviṣṇudharmottare tṛtīyakhaṇḍe mārkaṇḍeyavajrasaṃvāde munīnprati haṃsagītāsu śraddhāphalavarṇano nāma ṣaṭsaptatyuttaradviśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ || 276 ||
<3.277>
haṃsa uvāca ||
nityaṃ naimittikaṃ kāmyaṃ kriyāṅgaṃ malakarṣaṇam ||
kriyāsnānaṃ tathā ṣaṣṭhaṃ ṣoḍhā snānaṃ prakīrttitam ||1||
nitya naimittikau kāryau uṣṇodakaparodakaiḥ ||
tṛtīyaṃ ca parasnānaṃ yaduktaṃ malakarṣaṇam || 2 ||
pañca piṇḍānsamuddhṛtya snātavyaṃ paravāriṇi ||
ato' nyathā naraḥ snāto duṣkṛtāṃśena lipyate || 3 ||
snātaḥ śuddhimavāpnoti na tu snānaphalaṃ naraḥ ||
uddhṛtya pañca piṇḍāni snāto'pi paravāriṇā || ||4||
vahnitaptāmbunā snāto nāpnoti snānajaṃ phalam ||
snātaḥ śuddhimavāpnoti karmaṇyaścaiva jāyate ||5||
tasmātpuṇyeṣu tīrtheṣu sāgareṣu saraḥsu ca ||
saritsvapi ca divyāsu tathā prasravaṇeṣvapi || 6 ||
dharmārthamācaretsnānaṃ tena nāke mahīyate ||
toyālābhe tathālasye nityanaimittikāvubhau || 7 ||
malāpakarṣaṇaṃ snānaṃ tṛtīyaṃ ca samācaret ||
śuddhimātramavāpnoti taptodakaparodakaiḥ || 8 ||
sravanti rātrau chidrāṇi suptasya puruṣasya tu ||
tasmātkalyamathotthāya snānaṃ nityaṃ samācaret ||9||
kriyāvaiphalyamāpnoti snānahīnaḥ samācaran ||
yathākathañcitkurvīta snānaṃ nityamato budhaḥ || 3.277.10 ||
snātastu tarpaṇaṃ kṛtvā devānāṃ pitṛbhiḥ saha||
snāto bhavati dharmātmā nānyathā snāta ucyate || 11 ||
sūtake mṛtake snāto na kuryāttarpaṇakriyām ||
mṛtake tarpaṇaṃ kuryānmṛtasyaikasya paṇḍitaḥ || 12 ||
snātaḥ śaucamavāpnoti snānaṃ tuṣṭikaraṃ param ||
bāhyamābhyantaraṃ doṣaṃ snānena praṇudatyapi || 13 ||
duḥsvapnaśamanaṃ snānaṃ cāmaṅgalyapraṇāśanam ||
maṅgalyajananaṃ mukhyaṃ manaḥśaucakaraṃ param || 14 ||
nāpnoti nārakaṃ duḥkhaṃ nityasnāyī narottamaḥ ||
vahnivṛddhikaraṃ snānaṃ snānaṃ saubhāgyavardhanam || 15 ||
alakṣmyāḥ śamanaṃ snānaṃ snānaṃ duritanāśanam ||
snātaḥ kriyāsamarthaśca tathā bhavati mānavaḥ || 16 ||
śirasā tu vinā snāto na snātaḥ parikīrtitaḥ ||
kalye'samarthaḥ snāne tu prāpte bhojanakarmaṇi || 17 ||
dhruvaṃ samācaretsnānaṃ svargamārgapradarśakama ||
śiraḥsnātastu kurvīta divyaṃ pitryaṃ dvijottama || 18||
divyapitryāṇi karmāṇi nāsnātastu samācaret ||
vināpi tīrthasalilāddaive paitrye ca karmaṇi ||19 ||
sadā samācaretsnānaṃ dehaśuddhikaraṃ param ||
snānādeva paraṃ puṇyaṃ tīrthasnātaḥ samaśnute || 3.277.20 ||
bhūmiṣṭhantūddhṛtātpuṇyaṃ tasmātprasravaṇodakam ||
tasmācca nairjharantoyaṃ nairjharādapi sārasam || 21 ||
sārasādapi nādeyaṃ tasmātsādhuparigraham ||
sarvebhyaśca tathā puṇyaṃ kathitaṃ jāhnavījalam || 22||
gaṅgāplutāṅgā divi devabhogānbhuktvā ciraṃ prāpya manuṣyabhāvam ||
bhavanti loke sukhinaḥ samṛddhā yaśonvitā dharmaparā vidukhāḥ ||23||
iti śrīviṣṇudharmottare tṛ0 kha0 mārkaṇḍeyavajrasaṃvāde munīnprati haṃsagītāsu snānavarṇanonāma saptasaptatyuttaradviśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ||277||
<3.278>
haṃsa uvāca ||
japyaṃ siddhikaraṃ loke dvijānāṃ parikīrtitam ||
japyena siddhimāpnoti viśeṣeṇa dvijottamāḥ||1||
japyenaiva tu saṃsidhyedbrāhmaṇo nātra saṃśayaḥ ||
kuryādanyatra vā kuryānmaitro brāhmaṇa ucyate || 2 ||
dārvāsane sukhāsīnastvatha vāpi kuśāsane ||
japyaṃ samāhitaḥ kuryādatha vāpi samutthitaḥ || 3 ||
pūrvottarāśābhimukhaḥ sūryasyābhimukho'pi vā ||
darbhapāṇirdvijaśreṣṭhā jñātvā dharmasya niścayam || 4 ||
ṛṣiṃ mantrasya vijñāya cchando daivatameva ca ||
viniyogaṃ japaṃ kuryāddhyāyanvai mantradevatām || 5 ||
nātidrutena kartavyaṃ na caivātivilambitam ||
varṇānuccārayetsarvāñjapyakāle dvijottamāḥ|| 6 ||
mantramāvartayedvidvānyathāśakti hyatandritaḥ ||
svādhyāyastu bhavatyuccairupāṃśurjapya ucyate || 7 ||
manasā tu tathā jāpyaṃ tasmādbahuphalaṃ smṛtam ||
akṣasūtrastu kartavyo rudrākṣairdvijasattamāḥ || 8 ||
padmākṣairvā mahābhāgāstvatha vā putrajīvakaiḥ ||
muktāphalairvidrumeṇa rukmeṇa sphaṭikena vā ||9||
kuśāguṇena vā viprāḥ samyaggraṃthimatā satā ||
gaṇanaṃ vā tathā kuryātsamyagaṅguliparvabhiḥ ||3.278.10||
evaṃ kṛtvā dvijā japyamavaśyaṃ mantradevatām ||
ārādhya tu tadāpnoti yadyadvāñchati cetasā || 11 ||
japyena siddhimāsādya brahma lokamavāpnuyāt ||
japyena mucyate pāpājjapyena tridivaṃ vrajet || 12 ||
japyena mokṣamāpnoti tasmājjapyaparo bhavet ||
akāmakāmaṃ japyena dhruvaṃ mokṣamavāpnuyāt ||13||
kāmakāmastadāpnoti yaṃ kāmaṃ manasecchati ||
saṃsiddhā ṛṣayaḥ sarve japyenaiva dvijottamāḥ ||14||
japyaṃ pavitraṃ paramaṃ dvijānāṃ japyena pāpaṃ sakalaṃ jahāti ||
japyena lokām̐llabhate tvabhīṣṭāñjapyena mokṣaṃ labhate dvijātiḥ || 15 ||
iti śrīviṣṇudharmottare tṛtīyakhaṇḍe mārkaṇḍeyavajrasaṃvāde munīnprati haṃsagītāsu japyavarṇano nāmāṣṭasaptatyuttaradviśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ || 278 ||
<3.279>
haṃsa uvāca ||
kṛtvā jalanimagnastu mantrajāpaṃ dvijottamāḥ ||
phalamakṣayamāpnoti mucyate sarvakilbiṣaiḥ || 1 ||
mantramantarjale japyaṃ dhyāyatā mantradevatām ||
naraṃ mocayate pāpānmahāpātakajādapi || 2 ||
antarjalenātha vidhūtapāpā dvijāstu nākaṃ dhruvameva yānti ||
jale nimagnena tatastu yatnānmantrasya jāpaḥ puruṣeṇa kāryaḥ || 3 ||
iti śrīviṣṇudharmottare tṛtīyakhaṇḍe mārkaṇḍeyavajrasaṃvāde munīnprati haṃsagītāsvantarjale japya varṇano nāmaikonāśītyuttaradviśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ || 279 ||
<3.280>
|| haṃsa uvāca || ||
recakaṃ pūrakaṃ caiva kumbhakaṃ ca tathā dvijāḥ ||
ekastryavastho vijñeyaḥ prāṇāyāmo mahāphalaḥ || 1 ||
ādadānastu yaḥ śvāsaṃ maṃtrādhyayanamācaret ||
manasā brāhmaṇaśreṣṭhā recakaṃ tatprakīrtitam || 2 ||
śvāsa sandhāraṇaṃ kurvannādadāno na ca tyajan ||
pūrakaṃ tadvinirdiṣṭaṃ sarvakalmaṣanāśanam || 3 ||
maṃtrasya devatādhyānaṃ prāṇāyāmaṃ tathā caret ||
prāṇāyāmātparaṃ nāsti dvijātīnāṃ tathā tapaḥ || 4 ||
nirodhājjāyate vāyustatastvagniḥ prajāyate ||
tatastu salilaṃ dehe pūyate taistribhirnaraḥ || 5 ||
ākeśāgraṃ nakhāgraṃ svaṃ tapsyate paramaṃ tapaḥ ||
yastu kuryād dvijaśreṣṭhāḥ prāṇāyāmamatandritaḥ || 6 ||
na tatpuṇyamavāpnoti tīrthānusaraṇāddvijāḥ ||
na yajñena tathā kṛcchraiḥ prāṇāyāmādyadāpnuyāt ||7 ||
prāṇāyāmaparā viprāḥ sarvanirdhautakalmaṣāḥ ||
tridivaṃ pratipadyante mokṣaṃ vā manasepsitam ||8||
prāṇāyāmena naśyanti mahāpātakajānyapi ||
ghorāpyenāṃsi dharmajñāḥ prāṇāyāmamataścaret || 9 ||
etattapastatparamaṃ dvijānāṃ pāpāpahaṃ puṇyavivṛddhikāri ||
mokṣapradaṃ vā puruṣottamānāṃ tasmātprayatnena samācarettat || 3.280.10 ||
iti śrīviṣṇudharmottare tṛtīyakhaṇḍe mārkaṇḍeyavajrasaṃvāde munīnprati haṃsagītāsu prāṇāyāmavarṇano nāmāśītyuttaradviśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ ||280||
<3.281>
haṃsa uvāca ||
viṣayebhyaḥ pravṛttasya cetaso vinivartanam ||
pratyāhāraṃ vinirdiṣṭaṃ paramantattapaḥ smṛtam ||1||
atyantacañcalaṃ cittaṃ tasya saṃyamanaṃ dvijāḥ ||
pratyāhāreṇa kartavyaṃ puruṣeṇa vipaścitā ||2||
pratyāhṛtya naraścetaḥ kāmebhyo dvijasattamāḥ ||
kāmānāṃ bhājanaṃ loke mokṣasya ca tathā bhavet ||3||
vihāya kāmānyaḥ sarvānpumām̐ścarati nispṛhaḥ||
nirmamo nirahaṃkāraḥ sa śāntimadhigacchati ||4||
pratyāhāraṃ yathā kuryātkāmebhyaścetaso naraḥ ||
yathākāmamavāpnoti mokṣopāyaṃ ca vindati||5||
cañcalaṃ hi mano viprāḥ pramāthi balavaddṛḍham ||
tasya yatnaṃ samāsthāya kartavyo nigraho budhaiḥ||6||
yāvanna ceta āyāti vairāgyaṃ paramaṃ naraḥ||
tāvanna śakto bhavati pratyāhārasya vai dvijāḥ ||7|| 
janmamṛtyujarāvyādhiduḥkhaśokārditaṃ jagat ||
paśyankhinnastadā viprā vairāgyātsiddhimāpnuyāt || 8 ||
anityatāṃ tathā paśyañjagato'sya punaḥpunaḥ||
vairāgyabhāvamāsthāya tataḥ siddhimavāpnuyāt||9||
devatānāmṛṣīṇāṃ ca rājñāṃ bhūtagaṇasya ca||
dṛṣṭvā kṣayaṃ mahābhāgā virakto vipramucyate||3.281.10||
garbhavāse mahadduḥkhaṃ narake ca punaḥpunaḥ||
tiryagyonau ca mānuṣye tiryakkāmo vimucyate ||11||
divasedivase khinno parānneṣu ca yo dvijāḥ ||
vairāgyamāpnoti tathā khinno mūtrapurīṣayoḥ||12||
duḥkhānāṃ tu pratīghāte sukhamityeva bhāvite ||
duḥkhebhyaḥ svalpakālena yaḥ khinnaḥ sa virajyate || 13 ||
virāgamāsthāya tataḥ pratyāhāramatandritaḥ ||
kāmebhyaścetasaḥ kuryāttena sarvaṃ samaśnute ||14||
āpūryamāṇamacalapratiṣṭhaṃ samudramāpaḥ praviśanti yadvat ||
tadvatkāmā yaṃ praviśanti sarve sa śāntimāpnoti na kāmakāmī || 15 ||
iti śrīviṣṇudharmottare tṛtīyakhaṇḍe mārkaṇḍeyavajrasaṃvāde munīnprati haṃsagītāsu pratyāhāravarṇano nāmaikāśītyuttaradviśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ || 281||
<3.282>
|| haṃsa uvāca || ||
ekatra samavasthānaṃ bāhye dravye tu cetasaḥ ||
dhāraṇā sā vinirdiṣṭā manaso bandhakārikā || 1 ||
sā kāryā devatārcāyāṃ yatrāsya syātsthirā matiḥ ||
ekatra labdhvā lakṣyaṃ tu manasaścañcalasya tu || 2 ||
turyannivāraṇaṃ yatnānmano'nugrahakāraṇāt ||
atha vā lakṣyabandhantu bhuvi kāryaṃ vijānatā || 3 ||
labdhalakṣastu bhūyoge mahīyogaṃ samutsṛjet ||
labdhalakṣyo naraḥ kuryānnarāstejasi sattamāḥ || 4 ||
samutsṛjya tato vāyau tato nabhasi kārayet ||
kramaśastvevamāruhya lakṣyaṃ lakṣyatayā naraḥ || 5 ||
bāhyeṣu dhāraṇāṃ kṛtvā manasaḥ siddhimaśnute ||
manasaścañcalasyāsya yāvadbandhanadhāraṇā ||6 ||
tāvaddhyānamathārūḍhaṃ na śakyaṃ kenaciddvijāḥ ||
dhāraṇāṃ niścalāṃ prāpya tato dhyānamavāpnuyāt || 7 ||
śarīraṃ vijahātyenaṃ yatra baddhā tu dhāraṇā ||
prāpnoti tanmayībhāvaṃ nātra kāryā vicāraṇā || 8 ||
saṃprāpya dhāraṇāṃ muktvā śarīraṃ manujottamaḥ ||
kālānmokṣamavāpnoti dhyānācchīghramiti sthitiḥ || 9 ||
prāptastu yanmayībhāvaṃ dhāraṇāsthaḥ pumāṃstataḥ ||
sa yadā puruṣaṃ yāti tadā sa pratipadyate || 3.282.10 ||
dhruve mokṣamavāpnoti dhāraṇāṃ prāpya mānavaḥ ||
kintu kālena mahatā dhyānācchīghramiti sthitiḥ || 11 ||
sarvaloke tathā jñeyā bāhyadravyavināśanāḥ ||
teṣāntu dhāraṇā kāryā manograhaṇakāraṇam || 12 ||
saṃprāpya manaso bandhaṃ niścalaṃ puruṣottamaḥ ||
dhāraṇāyogatastasmāddhruvaṃ mokṣamupāśnute || 13 ||
yadasya nityaṃ hadi saṃsthitaṃ syāttatsyāttadevāsya hi mṛtyukāle ||
yadbhāvayatyeva hi mṛtyukāle bhavatyavaśyaṃ sa hi tanmayastu || 14 ||
iti śrīviṣṇudharmottare tṛtīya khaṇḍe mārkaṇḍeyavajrasaṃvāde munīnprati haṃsagītāsu dhāraṇāvarṇano nāma dvyaśītyuttaradviśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ||282||
<3.283>
haṃsa uvāca ||
dravye tu dhāraṇā bāhye dhāraṇā parikīrtitā ||
saiva cetasi nirdiṣṭā dhyāneti manujottamāḥ|| 1 ||
adhomukhaṃ hṛtkamale karṇikāyāṃ dvijottamāḥ||
dhyānaṃ sūryasya kurvīta tejorāśervicakṣaṇaḥ||2||
ravimadhye sthitaḥ somo somamadhye hutāśanaḥ||
tejomadhye sthitaṃ sattvaṃ tejasopyatitaijasam|| 3||
tanmadhye bhagavānviṣṇuḥ paramātmā sanātanaḥ ||
acyutaḥ puruṣo nityaṃ sarvavyāpī maheśvaraḥ||4||
sūkṣmātisūkṣmantaṃ paśyettejomūrtiṃ durāsadam||
dhyānayogena taṃ dṛṣṭvā siddhimāpnoti mānavaḥ || 5 ||
svastikaṃ sarvatobhadraṃ paryaṃkaṃ kamalāsanam ||
teṣāmekatamaṃ baddhvā yogāsanamatandritaḥ ||6||
ūrumūle tathānīya tiryaggau ca samau karau ||
kṛtvottānau dvijaśreṣṭhā vāmasyopari dakṣiṇam ||7||
kiñcidākuñcitāṅguṣṭhau karttavyau ca tathā karau ||
tena connāmya vitathaṃ kṛtvā bāhū pariślathau || || 8 ||
pṛṣṭhakāṃ yojayetskandhe dehamunnāmayetsudhīḥ ||
niṣkampāṃ sudṛḍhāṃ grīvāṃ śiraḥ kāryaṃ samaṃ tadā || 9 ||
sa paśyannāsikāgraṃ svaṃ diśaścānavalokayan ||
kiñcinnimīlya nayane sarvaṃ bāhyaṃ samutsṛjet ||
bāhyaṃ sarvaṃ parityajya paramātmānamātmanā || 3.283.10 ||
hṛtpadmaṃ karṇikāmadhye tejastejasi cārpitam ||
dṛṣṭvā puruṣamīśānaṃ sarvabandhaiḥ pramucyate || 11 ||
pratyāhāreṇa manasaḥ kṛtvā niyamanaṃ purā ||
dhāraṇāllabdhalakṣastu dhyānamārohate naraḥ || 12 ||
indriyāṇāṃ jayaṃ kṛtvā manaḥ saṃyamya cāpyatha||
vijitya ca tathā vāyuṃ dhyānamārohate naraḥ ||
dhyānārūḍhastadāpnoti yatra gatvā na śocati || 13 ||
niśāvasāne ca tathā pradoṣe dhyānena yatnaḥ puruṣeṇa kāryaḥ ||
dhyānena dṛṣṭvā puruṣaṃ paraṃ tu vimuktadehaḥ sakalatvameti || 14 ||
iti śrīviṣṇudharmottare tṛtīyakhaṇḍe mārkaṇḍeyavajrasaṃvāde munīnprati haṃsagītāsu dhyānavarṇano nāma tryaśītyuttaradviśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ || 283 ||
<3.284>
|| haṃsa uvāca || ||
atha dhyānamupārūḍho samādhiṃ manaso labhet ||
na kiñciccintayetpaścātsamādhiṃ prāpya mānavaḥ || 1 ||
śabdasparśarasairhīnaṃ gandharūpavivarjitam ||
taṃ paraṃ puruṣaṃ vidyādyatra gatvā na śocati 42 ||
śūnyaṃ dhyānaparaṃ viprāḥ samādhiriti kīrtyate ||
tantu prāpya naraiścānyairnābhibhūyeta karhicit || 3 ||
samādhitaḥ paraṃ nāsti sukhaṃ kiñcijjagattraye ||
samādhisthaśca duḥkhena guruṇāpi na cālyate || 4 ||
śaṅgāni śataśastvasya vādyante yadi karṇayoḥ ||
bheryaśca yadi hanyante śabdaṃ bāhyaṃ na vindati || 5 ||
tathā prahārābhihato vahnidagdhatanustathā ||
śīte sthito'tighore'pi sparśaṃ bāhyaṃ na vindati || 6 ||
jaye gandhe rase bāhye tādṛśasya tu kā kathā ||
dṛṣṭvā ya ātmanātmānaṃ samādhiṃ labhate punaḥ || 7 ||
tṛṣṇā vātha bubhukṣā vā na taṃ dhāvati karhicit ||
api santyajatastṛṣṇāmabhuñjānasya paśyataḥ || 8 ||
ekameva sa nirvedaṃ vijānāti narottamaḥ ||
samādhitaḥ samutthānaṃ tena tapyatyasau bhṛśam || 9 ||
na svarge na ca pātāle mānuṣye ca na tatsukham ||
samādhiṃ niścalaṃ prāpya yatsukhaṃ vindate naraḥ ||3.284.1 0 ||
samādhiṃ puruṣaḥ prāpya tyaktvā dehanibandhanam ||
vyāpnoti sakalām̐llokāṃstrailokyaṃ sacarācaram || 11 ||
tacca vyāpnoti puruṣastrailokyādapi yadbahiḥ ||
anantatvamavāpnoti samādhiṃ prāpya mānavaḥ || 12||
janmamṛtyujarāvyādhiśokaduḥkhavivarjitaḥ ||
sarvagaḥ sarvasaṃhārī sarvendriyaguṇātmakaḥ ||13||
sarvatra sarvadā viprāḥ sa tasyāṃ nātra saṃśayaḥ ||
puruṣatvamathābhyeti sarvaśaktitvameti ca || 14 ||
saṃyamya cetaḥ khalu dhāraṇāsu dhyānaṃ samāruhya tatastu paścāt ||
samādhimāsthāya pareṇa puṃsā tulyatvamāyāti mahānubhāvaḥ| 15||
iti śrīviṣṇudharmottare tṛtīyakhaṇḍe mārkaṇḍeyavajrasaṃvāde munīnprati haṃsagītāsu samādhivarṇano nāma caturaśītyuttaradviśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ || 284 ||
<3.285>
ṛṣaya ūcuḥ ||
sthānasya mahataḥ prāptau yanmūlaṃ dvijasattama ||
tadasmākaṃ samācakṣva sarvaṃ pratyakṣalakṣaṇam || 1 ||
|| haṃsa uvāca ||
prāpnuvaṃti narāḥ sarve vyavasāyapuraḥsarāḥ ||
vyavasāyadvitīyānāṃ nāsti loke sudurlabham || 2 ||
vyavasāyaḥ paro bandhuḥ sahāyastadanaṃtaram ||
tena yuktaḥ pumānsamyakprāpnotīti viniścitam || 3 ||
dharme cārthe ca kāme ca mokṣe ca ṛṣisattamāḥ ||
bhājanantu sadā jñeyo narastu vyavasāyavān || 4 ||
vyavasāyavihīnānāṃ svalpopyartho'sti durlabhaḥ ||
vyavasāyavihīnānāmalasānāṃ durātmanām || 5 ||
karaprāpto'pi sahasā kṣipramartho vinaśyati ||
vyavasāyavihīnastu sthāṇunā nātiricyate || 6||
tasmānnarā ye vyasanāya yuktāste kāmabhājaḥ kathitāstu loke ||
teṣāmayaṃ caiva paraśca lokasteṣāṃ tathā mokṣapatho na dūre || 7 ||
iti śrīviṣṇudharmottare tṛtīyakhaṇḍe mārkaṇḍeyavajrasaṃvāde munīnprati haṃsagītāsu vyavasāyavarṇano nāma pañcāśītyuttaradviśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ ||285||
<3.286>
haṃsa uvāca ||
sarvakarmapradhānasya vyavasāyasya mānadāḥ ||
mūlamekaṃ vinirdiṣṭaṃ saṃkalpo nātra saṃśayaḥ || 1 ||
saṃkalpamūlaḥ kāmo vai jñānaṃ saṃkalpabandhanam ||
saṃkalpabandhanā vedā dharmaḥ saṅkalpabandhanaḥ || 2 ||
saṃkalpabandhanaścārtho mokṣaḥ saṃkalpabandhanaḥ ||
saṃkalpabandhanaṃ sarvaṃ jagadetaccarācaram || 3 ||
ādau niścitya yatkarma manasā buddhimānnaraḥ ||
yunakti vyavasāyena paścājjñeyaḥ sa paṃḍitaḥ || 4 ||
yāvanto niścitāstvarthā manasā kenacitkvacit ||
tāvanna śakyate kartuṃ saṃkalpo'taḥ paro mataḥ || 5 ||
saṃkalpitānāṃ manasā tu pūrvaṃ siddhiḥ pradiṣṭā vyavasāyayuktā ||
kāryāṇi sarvāṇi tatastu loke saṃkalpamūlāni vadaṃti santaḥ || 6 ||
iti śrīviṣṇudharmottare tṛtīyakhaṇḍe mārkaṇḍeyavajrasaṃvāde munīnprati haṃsagītāsu saṃkalpanirvacano nāma ṣaḍaśītyuttaradviśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ || 286 ||
<3.287>
ṛṣaya ūcuḥ ||
naiḥśreyasapadāvāptau pratyāhārastvayeritaḥ ||
dhāraṇādhyānayogaśca samādhirapi cottamā || 1 ||
bhūya eva tvamācakṣva nṛṇāṃ saṃsārasāriṇām ||
hitārthamuttamaṃ mārgaṃ lokadvayasukhāvaham || 2 ||
|| haṃsa uvāca || ||
agnirmukhaṃ devatānāṃ sarvabhūtaparāyaṇam ||
tasya śuśrūṣaṇādeva sarvānkāmānsamaśnute || 3 ||
sā mūrtiḥ paramā viṣṇostaddattaṃ prāpnute raviḥ ||
sūryādvṛṣṭistatastvannamannātprabhavate jagat || 4 ||
adhaḥpravarṣiṇo devā bhaumāstūrdhvaṃ pravarṣiṇaḥ ||
ūrdhvapravarṣiṇaṃ mukhyaṃ bhaumaṃ dattaṃ hutāśane || 5 ||
oṃkāraṃ pūrvamuddiśya yāṃyāṃ viprastu devatām ||
havirjuhoti sā tasya prītā bhavati nityadā || 6 ||
vahnidattena haviṣā tṛptimāyānti devatāḥ ||
tāstṛptāstarpayantyenaṃ naraṃ kāmasamṛddhibhiḥ || 7 ||
sūrye homaḥ paraḥ puṇyastena svarge mahīyate ||
tasmādaṣṭaguṇaṃ puṇyamidhmabhiḥ prāpnuyāddhute || 8 ||
tasmāddaśaguṇaṃ sasyairvrīhibhirdviguṇaṃ tataḥ ||
tāvadeva tathā puṇyaṃ home siddhārthakaiḥ smṛtam || 9 ||
yavaiścaturguṇaṃ tasmāttilairdaśaguṇaṃ tataḥ ||
tāvadeva phalaṃ jñeyaṃ bilvairdaśaguṇaṃ tataḥ || || 3.287.10 ||
padmaiśca tāvadevoktaṃ dadhnā kṣīreṇa ca dvijāḥ ||
paramānnena dharmajñā ghṛtenāṣṭaguṇaṃ tataḥ || 11 ||
mantreṇoṅkārapūtena svāhāntena vicakṣaṇaḥ ||
svāhāvasāne juhuyāddhyāyanvai mantradevatām || 12 ||
yayā tvaṅguliparvāṇi pūryante tāvadāhutiḥ ||
aṅguṣṭhamātraṃ sthaulyena vitastiścedhma iṣyate || 13 ||
duḥsvapne ca duriṣṭe ca durnimitte tathaiva ca ||
nāsti homasamantrāṇaṃ tathā pāpavināśanam || 14 ||
homena pāpaṃ puruṣo jahāti homena nākaṃ ca tathā prayāti ||
homastu loke duritaṃ samagraṃ vināśayatyeva na saṃśayo'tra || 15 ||
iti śrīviṣṇudharmottare tṛtīyakhaṇḍe mārkaṇḍeyavajrasaṃvāde munīnprati haṃsagītāsu homavidhivarṇano nāma saptāśītyuttaradviśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ || 287 ||
<3.288>
haṃsa uvāca ||
daivādhīnā prajāḥ sarvā devāyattamidaṃ jagat ||
sukhaduḥkhe mānuṣāṇāṃ devāyatte tathaiva ca || 1 ||
devairvihitamaśnanti manuṣyāstu śubhāśubham ||
tasmātsarvaprayatnena devapūjāparo bhavet || 2 ||
devāśca bhaktyā tuṣyanti śraddhayā parayā tathā ||
pūjitāḥ śucinā caiva devāstuṣyanti nānyathā || 3 ||
devaśuśrūṣayā svargo devaśuśrūṣayā sukham ||
devaśuśrūṣayā rājyaṃ mokṣaṃ cāpyathavāpyate || 4 ||
devatāḥ kāmayantīha tathā bhaktaṃ janaṃ sadā ||
bhaktānukampino devāḥ paratreha tathā dhruvam || 5 ||
trāṇaṃ kurvanti bhaktasya dhruvaṃ devā dvijottamāḥ ||
devatoṣādavāpnoti yānkāmānmanasecchati || 6 ||
jātaprabhṛti yaṃ devaṃ bhaktyā nityamathārcayeta ||
antakāle tu saṃprāpte so'pi trātā bhaved dhruvam ||7||
kiṃ vā pāpavihīnasya kṣipraṃ tuṣyanti devatāḥ ||
tasmātpāpaṃ na karttavyaṃ nareṇa tridaśāśrayāt || 8 ||
devāśritānāṃ pāpānāṃ kṣipraṃ pāpaṃ vinaśyati ||
natveva pātakaṃ karma vṛthā teṣāṃ prajāyate || 9 ||
tathā bahu phalaṃ loke kathitaṃ tridaśāśrayam ||
sarvānsamarcayeddevānekaṃ vā devamāśrayet || 3.288.10 ||
devārcāṃ śraddhayā kṛtvā dhruvaṃ tallokamāpnuyāt ||
kṛtvā devagṛhaṃ viprā narastallokamāpnuyāt || 11 ||
paramāṇūni yāvanti bhavanti suraveśmanaḥ ||
tāvato'bdānavāpnoti tallokānnātra saṃśayaḥ || 12 ||
yāvadvāraṃ karotīha devaveśma sudhāsitam ||
janmāntarāṇi tāvanti yaśasā sa virājate || 13 ||
pratipādya tathā bhaktyā dhvajaṃ tridaśaveśmani ||
nirṇudatyāśu pāpāni mahāpātakajānyapi || 14 ||
citrakarma tathā kṛtvā gandharvai saha modate ||
devamālyāpanayanādgodānaphalamāpnuyāt| || 15 ||
pāpairvimucyate sarvaiḥ prokṣaṇāddevaveśmanaḥ ||
sammārjanāttathā pāpaṃ vyapohati na saṃśayaḥ || 16 ||
kāmānabhīṣṭānāpnoti devaveśmopalepanāt ||
sudhārekhānvitaṃ kṛtvā maryādāṃ nātivartate || 17 ||
varṇakaiścitritaṃ kṛtvā saubhāgyaṃ mahadāpnuyāt ||
gandharvatvamavāpnoti gandhairabhyukṣya mānavāḥ || 18 ||
puṣpairvikiraṇaṃ kṛtvā rūpavānabhijāyate ||
abhyajya devapratimāṃ sarvaduḥkhairvimucyate || 19 ||
virūkṣayitvā tāmeva pāpebhyo vipramucyate ||
oṣadhībhistathā snāpya nāsatyaiḥ saha modate || 3.288.20 ||
loke prajāpateryāti vedaiḥ saṃsnāpya devatām |||
puṣpodakena snapanātsaubhāgyaṃ mahadaśnute || 21 ||
phalodakena saṃsnāpya saphalāṃ vindate kriyām ||
nāgavallījalaiḥ snāpya nāgalokaṃ sa gacchati || 22 ||
ratnodakena snapanāddhānadaṃ lokamāpnuyāt ||
gandhodakena mukhyena gandharvaiḥ saha modate || 23 ||
tīrthodakena puṇyena svargalokamavāpnuyāt ||
tathānulipya gandhaiśca śakraloke mahīyate || 24 ||
vibhūṣaṇapradānena rājā bhavati bhūtale ||
rājaliṅgapradānena rājā bhavati hi kṣitau || 25 ||
vastradānena loke'sminsuveśastvabhijāyate ||
deve mālyapradānena parā lakṣmīṃ samaśnute || 26 ||
dhūpapradānena tathā gatimagryāmavāpnuyāt ||
dīpadānena cakṣuṣmānprakāśaścaiva jāyate || 27 ||
ūrdhvāṃ gatimavāpnoti sarvatra ca tathāgryatām ||
devatābhyastathā viprā nivedyānnamatandritaḥ || 28 ||
sarvakāmasamṛddhasya yajñasya phalamaśnute ||
paritoṣamathāyāti devatā balikarmaṇā || 29 ||
tāstuṣṭāstarpayanyenaṃ sarvakāmasamṛddhibhiḥ ||
hṛdyapānapradānena tṛptimāpnoti śāśvatīm || 3.288.30 ||
devatānāṃ namaskārātkiñcitpāpaṃ praṇaśyati ||
tathaivābhyadhikaṃ pāpaṃ praṇipātena naśyati || 31 ||
tato'pyabhyadhikaṃ kiñcitkṛtvā deve pradakṣiṇām ||
stutikriyā devatānāṃ sarvakāmaphalapradā || 32 |||
gītena devatāstoṣamupayānti dvijottamāḥ ||
tathā vādyena nṛtyena puruṣasya viśeṣataḥ ||33 ||
nṛttaṃ gītaṃ tathā vādyaṃ svayamācarate dvijā ||
devasālokyamāpnoti puruṣasya vipaścitaḥ || 34 ||
vācanāditihāsānāṃ pāpamokṣamavāpnuyāt ||
mūlyena kārayedgītaṃ gandharvatvamathāpnuyāt || 35 ||
vādyaṃ dattvātha dharmajñaṃ vasūnāṃ lokamaśnute ||
nṛttena lokamāpnoti śaṅkarasya mahātmanaḥ || 36 ||
itihāsapradānena buddhimāñjāyate naraḥ ||
āhutiṃ devamuddiśya yo dadāti hutāśane || 37 ||
sa devastarpayatyenaṃ kāmairvā tena tarpitaḥ ||
devamuddiśya viprāṇāṃ yo dadātīha bhojanam || 38 ||
sa devaḥ prītimānasya kāmāniṣṭānprayacchati ||
devamuddiśya yatkiñcidbrāhmaṇebhyaḥ pradīyate ||39||
tadeva prāpyate loke prasanne tridaśe bahu ||
yānaṃ śayyāsanaṃ chatraṃ pāduke cāpyupānahau || 3.288.40 ||
vāhanaṃ gāṃ ca dharmajñāstridaśabhyo dadāti yaḥ ||
ekaikasmādavāpnoti vahniṣṭomaphalaṃ naraḥ || 41 ||
dattvā phalāni devebhyaḥ saphalāṃ vindate kriyām ||
nivedya sasyāni tathā gatimāpnotyanuttamām || 42 ||
naiti devastathā prītiṃ dānairnṛtyaistathā phalaiḥ ||
prītimeti yathā viprāḥ svāśritānāntu pūjanaiḥ || 43 ||
devatā naiva tuṣyanti dānena bahudhā dvijāḥ ||
yathā bhaktyā tu tuṣyanti tasmādbhaktiṃ samāśrayet || 44 ||
ghṛtapūrṇāni pātrāṇi dattvā śrāddhe tathā naraḥ ||
dīrghaṃ jīvitamāpnoti na rogaiścābhibhūyate || 45 ||
madhupūrṇāni pātrāṇi dattvā saubhāgyamāpnuyāt ||
yathākathañcitpātrāṇāṃ dānādbhavati sattamāḥ ||46||
pātraṃ nu sarvakāmānāṃ nātra kāryā vicāraṇā ||
puṣpavṛkṣapradānena gṛhamāpnoti mānavaḥ || 47 ||
phalavṛkṣapradānena grāmasyādhipatirbhavet ||
gṛhapradānāddhavati kheṭakādhipatistathā || 48 ||
gṛhaṃ śayyāsanopetaṃ yānavāhanasaṃyutam ||
dhanadhānyayutaṃ sphītaṃ dāsīdāsasamākulam || 49 ||
sarvakāmayutaṃ ramyaṃ pradāya śrāddhakarmaṇi ||
svargalokātparibhraṣṭo rājā bhavati pārthivaḥ || 3.288.50 ||
pitṝnuddiśya yaddānaṃ kiñcideva prayacchati||
tasyābhyadhikamāpnoti puṇyaṃ teṣāṃ prasādataḥ || 51 ||
tasmācchrāddhāni kāryāṇi tīrtheṣvāyataneṣu ca ||
dānāni caiva deyāni pitṝnuddiśya śaktitaḥ||52||
śrāddheṣu puṣṭiṃ manujā labhante śrāddhena viprāstridive ca vāsaḥ||
śrāddhaṃ prayatnena tataśca kuryātsvakalpasūtrābhihitaṃ yathāvat ||53||
iti śrīviṣṇudharmottare tṛ0 khaṃ0 mārkaṇḍeyavajrasaṃvāde munīnprati haṃsagītāsu pitṛpūjāvidhānaṃ nāmāṣṭāśītyuttaradviśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ || 288 ||
<3.289>
haṃsa uvāca ||
śūdrāṇāṃ daivataṃ vaiśyā vaiśyānāṃ kṣatriyāstathā ||
brāhmaṇāḥ kṣatriyāṇāṃ ca teṣāmapi hutāśanaḥ || 1 ||
gurudevo brahmacārī nāryaśca patidevatāḥ ||
gṛhastho'tithidaivatyastasmāttaṃ pūjayetsadā || 2 ||
vaiśvadevaṃ gṛhe kṛtvā sāyaṃ prātaḥ samāhitaḥ ||
piṇḍanirvapaṇaṃ kṛtvā bhikṣāṃ dattvā tu bhikṣave|| ||
satkṛtya bhikṣādānena godānaphalamāpnuyāt ||
śvabhyaśca śvapacebhyaśca vayobhyaścāvapedbhuvi || 4 ||
gṛhasthamupajīvanti prajāḥ sthāvarajaṅgamāḥ ||
gṛhasthaḥ poṣaṇaṃ kuryānmṛtānāṃ jīvatāmapi || 5 ||
poṣaṇānnityameveha tena svarge mahīyate ||
kīṭaḥ pataṅgaḥ patitaḥ śatravo dvijapuṅgavāḥ||6||
gṛhiṇā nāvamantavyā vaiśvadeve hyupasthite ||
atithiṃ pūjayedyatnāgṛhastho gṛhamāgatam || 7 ||

āsanaṃ ca tathā dattvā sthānalābhaṃ narottamaḥ || 8 ||
kṛtvā ca kuśala praśnaṃ buddhimāñjāyate naraḥ ||
pādaśaucena tasyātha śrāntasaṃvāhanena ca || 9 ||
vījanāccāmareṇāpi tālavṛntena vījanāt ||
pādābhyaṅgapradānena dīpadānena vāpyatha || 3.289.10 ||
rātrau śayanadānena tathā saṃvāhanena ca ||
ekaikasmādavāpnoti godānaphalamuttamam || 11 ||
upāsyainaṃ kathābhiśca yaśasā yogamāpnuyāt ||
saṃśrāvya cetihāsāni tathainaṃ ca tadicchayā || 12 ||
nākalokamavāpnoti gītanṛtyotsavādibhiḥ ||
pūjayitvā tathāpnoti gandharvatvamasaṃśayam || 13 ||
pratiśrayapradānena sarvānkāmānsamaśnute ||
mṛdaṃ ca dantakāṣṭhaṃ ca dattvā śaucodakaṃ tathā || 14 ||
kāle yathāsukhaṃ dattvā saubhāgyaṃ mahadāpnuyāt ||
pratāpya cainaṃ śītārtaṃ sarvatra sukhamaśnute || 15 ||
abhyaṅgapūrvakaṃ dattvā tasya snānamatandritaḥ ||
ārogyarūpalāvaṇyadhanadhānyamupāśnute || 16 ||
dattvā sakalamukhyāni snānāni dvijasattamāḥ ||
nāsatyalokamāpnoti nātra kāryā vicāraṇā ||17 ||
sugandhīni tathā dattvā gandharvaiḥ saha modate ||
kaṃkaṇasya pradānena saubhāgyaṃ mahadaśnute || 18 ||
atithisnāpanātpuṇyaṃ daśagodānajaṃ bhavet ||
kimicchukena saṃyojya vājapeyaphalaṃ labhet || 19 ||
svecchitaṃ ca pradāyāsya tadevākṣayamaśnute ||
sarvaśaktyā narastasmādatithiṃ pūjayetsadā || 3.289.20 ||
tṛpto'tithiryatpuruṣasya kiñcidvicintayatyeva mahānubhāvaḥ ||
tatsarvamāpnotyacireṇa tasmātpūjyo'tithirdharmapareṇa nityam || 21 ||
iti śrīviṣṇudharmottare tṛtīyakhaṇḍe mārkaṇḍeyavajrasaṃvāde munīnprati haṃsagītāsvatithipūjā nāmaikonanavatyadhikadviśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ || 289 ||
<3.290>
|| haṃsa uvāca || ||
brāhmaṇā daivataṃ bhūmau brāhmaṇā divi daivatam ||
brāhmaṇebhyaḥ paraṃ nāsti bhūtaṃ kiṃñcijjagattraye || 1 ||
adaivaṃ daivataṃ kuryuḥ kuryurdaivamadaivatam ||
brāhmaṇā hi mahābhāgāḥ pūjyante satataṃ dvijāḥ || 2 ||
brāhmaṇebhyaḥ samutpannā devāḥ pūrvamiti śrutiḥ ||
brāhmaṇebhyo jagatsarvaṃ tasmātpūjyatamāḥ sadā || 3 ||
eṣāmaśnanti vaktreṇa devatāḥ pitarastathā ||
ṛṣayaśca mahābhāgāḥ kimbhūtamadhikaṃ tataḥ || 4 ||
yadeva manujo bhaktyā brāhmaṇebhyaḥ prayacchati ||
tadevāpnoti dharmajñā bahu janmanijanmani || 5 ||
tālavṛntānilenaivaṃ śrāntasaṃvāhanena ca ||
unmārjanena gātrāṇāṃ tathāpyañjanakarmaṇā || 6 ||
pādaśaucapradānena pādayoḥ śaucanena ca ||
paricaryayā yathākāmamekenaiva dvijottamāḥ || 7 ||
agniṣṭomamavāpnoti svargalokaṃ ca gacchati ||

yadbrāhmaṇāstuṣṭatamā vadanti taddevatāḥ pratyabhinandayanti ||
tuṣṭeṣu tuṣṭāḥ satataṃ bhavanti pratyakṣadeveṣu parokṣadevāḥ||9||
iti śrīviṣṇudharmottare tṛtīyakhaṇḍe mārkaṇḍeyavajrasaṃvāde munīnprati haṃsagītāsu brāhmaṇaśuśrūṣā nāma navatyadhikadviśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ || 290 ||

|| haṃsa uvāca ||
gāvaḥ pavitrā maṅgalyā goṣu lokāḥ pratiṣṭhitāḥ ||
yathā devā yathā vedā brāhmaṇāśca yathā dvijāḥ || 1 ||
tathā gāvo vinirdiṣṭāḥ sarvalokasukhāvahāḥ ||
ekaikaṃ brāhmaṇaṃ jñeyaṃ tayoryajñaḥ pratiṣṭhitaḥ || 2 ||
vipreṣu mantrāstiṣṭhanti goṣu santiṣṭhate haviḥ ||
gavāṃ dānaṃ dvijaśreṣṭhā duṣkṛtānyapakarṣati ||3||
gomūtraṃ gomayaṃ kṣīraṃ dadhi sarpiśca pañcamam ||
pavitrametannirdiṣṭaṃ narāṇāṃ śodhanaṃ param || 4 ||
maṅgalyāśca pavitrāśca rakṣoghnā rocanāstathā ||
tāsāṃ śṛṅgodakaṃ viprā jāhnavīsalilopamam || 5 ||
gavāṃ kāṇḍūyanānmartyaḥ sarvaṃ pāpaṃ vyapohati ||
tāsāṃ grāsapradānena mahatpuṇyamavāpnuyāt || 6 ||
tāsāṃ ca pracaraṃ kṛtvā tathaiva salilāśayam ||
svargalokamupāśnanti bahūnyabdagaṇāni tu || 7 ||
tāsāṃ pracārabhūmiṃ tu kṛtvā prāpnoti mānavaḥ ||
aśvamedhasya yajñasya phalaṃ prāpnotyasaṃśayam || 8 ||
tāsāmāvasathaṃ kṛtvā nagarādhipatirbhavet ||
tathā lavaṇadānena saubhāgyaṃ mahadaśnute || 9 ||
āturāṃ paṅkalagnāṃ vā coravyāghrabhayārditām ||
mokṣayitvā dvijaśreṣṭhāstvaśvamedhaphalaṃ labhet || 3.291.10 ||
tāsāmauṣadhadānena virogastvabhijāyate ||
vipramocya bhayebhyaśca na bhayaṃ vidyate kvacit || 11 ||
krītvā caṇḍālahastācca gomedhasya phalaṃ labhet ||
gopakastvasya cānyasya krītvā hastāttathaiva ca || 12 ||
kṛtvā śītātapatrāṇaṃ tāsāṃ svargamavāpnuyāt ||
utthitāsūtthitastiṣṭhedviṣṭhitāsu ca viṣṭhitaḥ || 13 ||
bhuktavatsu tu cāśnīyājjale pīte pibettu ca ||
gomūtreṇācaretsnānaṃ gopurīṣāttathā yavaiḥ || 14 ||
śarīrayātrāṃ kurvīta gorasairatha vā dvijāḥ ||
etaddhi govrataṃ māsātsarvakalmaṣanāśanam || 15 ||
ekāṃ gāṃ dhārayenmāsaṃ dadyāttasyāstathā yavān || 
gomayāttānsamaśnīyānmāsamekamataḥ śuciḥ || 16 ||
māsānte tāṃ tathā dhenuṃ dadyādviprāya bhaktimān ||
vratametatsamuddiṣṭaṃ sarvakalmaṣanāśanam | || 17 ||
rājasūyāśvamedhābhyāṃ vratametattathādhikam ||
vratenānena cīrṇena kāmāniṣṭānavāpnuyāt || 18 ||
vimānenārkavarṇena brahmalokaṃ ca gacchati ||
vināpi gopradānena vratametanmahāphalam||19||
trirātraṃ saptarātraṃ vā śaktiṃ jñātvā tathā svakām||
gavāṃ nirhāranirmuktairvṛttiṃ kṛtvā tathā yavaiḥ||3.291.20||
pāpamokṣamavāpnoti puṇyaṃ ca mahadaśnute ||
rajaśca gokhurodbhūtaṃ sarvakalmaṣanāśanam ||21||
yādṛśāttādṛśāddeśānnātra kāryā vicāraṇā ||
maṅgalyaṃ ca pavitraṃ ca tadalakṣmīvināśanam ||22||
gavāṃ hi parivāsena bhūmiḥ śuddhimavāpnuyāt ||
taddhi śuddhaṃ yadā veśma yatra tiṣṭhanti dhenavaḥ ||23||
tāsānniḥśvāsavātena puraṃ nīrājanaṃ bhavet ||
tāsāṃ saṃsparśanaṃ puṇyaṃ duḥsvapnāghavināśanam||24||
grīvāmastakasandhau tu tāsāṃ gaṅgā pratiṣṭhitā |
sarvadevamayā gāvaḥ sarva tīrthamayāstathā||25||
tāsāṃ lomāni puṇyāni pavitrāṇi tathā dvijāḥ ||
gomayenopaliptaṃ tu śuci sthānaṃ prakīrtitam || 26 ||
agnyāgārasurāgārāngomayenopalepayet ||
gomaye tu sadā lakṣmīḥ svayameva vyasthitā ||27||
gomūtre ca tathā gaṅgā dadhikṣīraghṛteṣu ca ||
sadā vyavasthitaṃ somaṃ rocanāyāṃ sarasvatī||28||
viṣṇuryajñaḥ samākhyātaḥ sa ca goṣu pratiṣṭhitaḥ ||
tasmādgāvo vinirdiṣṭā viṣṇureva purātanaiḥ || 29 ||
pūjyāstāstu namaskāryāḥ kīrtanīyāśca tāstathā ||
tāsāmāhāradānaṃ ca kāryaṃ śuśrūṣaṇaṃ tathā||3.291.30||
śuśrūṣaṇeneha gavāṃ dvijendrāḥ prāpnoti lokānamalānviśokān||
tasmātprayatnena gavāṃ hi kāryaṃ śuśrūṣaṇaṃ dharmaparairmanuṣyaiḥ||31||
iti śrīviṣṇudharmottare tṛtīyakhaṇḍe mā0 saṃ0 munīnprati haṃsagītāsu gośuśrūṣā nāmai kanavatyadhikadviśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ||291||
<3.292>
haṃsa uvāca ||
yo dayāvānsa puruṣo nirghṛṇo rākṣaso mataḥ ||
rākṣasānāmapi ghṛṇā vidyate dvijasattamāḥ || ||1||
nirghṛṇāḥ pratipadyante ghoraṃ narakamañjasā ||
vadhabandhaparikleśānmānuṣye'tha labhanti te ||2||
vyādhyārtā duḥkhitā dīnā daridrāśca bhavanti te ||
dayānvitā virājante nākamāsādya mānavāḥ ||3||
tasmāddayāvatā bhāvyaṃ tṛṇeṣvapi vipaścitā ||
tṛṇānyapi sajīvāni teṣāṃ kuryānna pīḍanam || || 4 ||
kīṭe pataṅge ca paśau dayā kāryā tathā mṛge ||
varṇottame varṇahīne patite varṇasaṅkare || 5 ||
sarvatraiva dayā kāryā sarvakāmaphalapradā ||
vratānāmapi sarveṣāṃ śreṣṭhamekaṃ dayāvratam || 6 ||
duḥkhārtaṃ prāṇinaṃ kiñciduḥkhānmokṣayate dvijāḥ ||
vahniṣṭomaphalaṃ proktaṃ nātra kāryā vicāraṇā || 7 ||
paricaryāṃ tathā kṛtvā sāntvayetprāṇino dvijāḥ||
brahmalokamavāpnoti dayāvāntena karmaṇā || 8 ||
śleṣmamūtrapurīṣāṇāṃ vyādhitasyājugupsakaḥ ||
sarvakāmasamṛddhasya yajñasya phalamaśnute ||9||
mṛtasya vāpyanāthasya na jugupsanti ye narāḥ ||
kurvanti caiva saṃskārānte narāḥ svargagāminaḥ ||3.292.10|| 
agnidāhājjalācchastrāttathānyasmādupadravāt ||
prāṇino mokṣaṇaṃ kṛtvā svargaloke mahīyate || 11 ||
bandhanātprāṇinaḥ kṛtvā mokṣaṇaṃ ca tathā dvijāḥ||
sarvakāmasamṛddhasya yajñasya phalamaśnute||12||
yasmāddhi prāṇinaṃ duḥkhātpuruṣo mokṣayetkvacit||
na tadduḥkhamavāpnoti yatrayatrābhijāyate ||13||
sarvabhūteṣu yo dānaṃ caikasattve ca yā dayā ||
sarvasattvapradānāddhi dayā jñeyā mahāphalā||14||
dayānvitānāṃ tridaśendralokā dayānvitānāṃ ca pitāmahasya ||
dayānvitānāṃ ca samastakāmā lokadvaye vipravarā pradiṣṭāḥ||15||
iti śrīviṣṇudharmottare tṛtīyakhaṇḍe mārkaṇḍeyavajrasaṃvāde munīnprati haṃsagītāsu dayāvarṇano nāma dvinavatyadhikadviśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ || 292 ||
<3.293>
|| haṃsa uvāca || ||
dākṣiṇyaṃ paramaṃ loke nṛṇāṃ saṃvananaṃ param ||
dākṣiṇyayuktasya tathā pareṣāṃ priyakāritā || 1 ||
priyāṇyācarate yastu pareṣāṃ manujottamaḥ ||
samāpnoti priyāṇyeva lokayorubhayordvijāḥ || 2 ||
pratyakṣaṃ ca parokṣaṃ ca pareṣāmācaretpriyam ||
priyeṇa vipriyaṃ haṃti priyeṇa priyamāpnuyāt || 3 ||
mṛdunā dāruṇaṃ hanti mṛdunā haṃti mārdavam ||
sarvaṃ hi mṛdunā hanti tasmāttīkṣṇataro mṛduḥ || 4 ||
mṛdoriha vadānyasya devā nighnanti śātravān ||
abhicāraḥ sa śatrūṇāṃ narasya mṛdutā tathā ||5||
nareṇa mṛdunā dagdhā mārdavenābhitastu ye ||
na prarohaṃti te viprā bījā dagdhā yathā tathā || 6 ||
dākṣiṇyamekaṃ subhagatvaheturdākṣiṇyamekaṃ tridivasya hetuḥ ||
dākṣiṇyamāsthāya jayedarātīndākṣiṇyayuktastu sadā bhavecca||7||
iti śrīviṣṇudharmottare tṛtīyakhaṃḍe mārkaṇḍeyavajrasaṃvāde munīnprati haṃsagītāsu dākṣiṇyavarṇano nāma trinavatyadhikadviśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ || 293 ||
<3.294>
|| haṃsa uvāca || ||
saṃrohatīṣuṇāviddhaṃ vanaṃ paraśunā hatam ||
vācā duruktaṃ bībhatsaṃ na saṃrohati vākkṛtam || 1 ||
mitrāṇi yāṃti śatrutvaṃ narasyāpriyavādinaḥ ||
anarthāḥ svayamāyāṃti mṛto narakamṛcchati || 2 ||
tasmādbhāvyaṃ manuṣyeṇa smitapūrvābhibhāṣiṇā ||
pratyakṣaṃ ca parokṣaṃ ca sarveṣāṃ priyabhāṣiṇām || 3 ||
mitratāṃ yāṃti ripavo narāṇāṃ priyabhāṣiṇām ||
rakṣāṃsyapi nigṛhyante puruṣaiḥ sāntvavādibhiḥ || 4 ||
priyavākyātparaṃ loke nāsti saṃvananaṃ param ||
priyo bhavati lokānāṃ priyavādī tathā naraḥ || 5 ||
priyaṃvadānāṃ sakalaṃ jagadetatsthitaṃ vaśe ||
priyaṃvadānāṃ śatrutve nāsti kaścijjagattraye || 6 ||
priyaṃvadāḥ saukhyamihāpnuvaṃti priyaṃvadā nākamatha prayāṃti ||
priyaṃvadāḥ sarvamathāpnuvaṃti priyaṃvadaḥ syādata eva nityam ||7||
iti śrīviṣṇudharmottare tṛ0 khaṃḍe mārkaṇḍeyavajrasaṃvāde munīnprati haṃsagītāsu priyaṃvadapraśaṃsā nāma caturnavatyadhikadviśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ || 294 ||
<3.295>
|| haṃsa uvāca ||
alasānāṃ manuṣyāṇāṃ sadā duḥkhaṃ daridratā ||
ālasyaṃ pātakaṃ loke ālasyaṃ bhūtināśanam || 1 ||
nādhigacchanti bhoktavyamalasā ye narādhamāḥ ||
alasānāṃ manuṣyāṇāṃ prāptopyartho vinaśyati ||2||
tasmānnityaṃ manuṣyeṇa dākṣyaṃ kāryaṃ vijānatā ||
dakṣaḥ sarvamavāpnoti yatkiñcinmanasecchati || 3 ||
dharme cārthe ca kāme ca mokṣe ca dvijasattamāḥ ||
bhājanaṃ sarvakāleṣu dakṣa eva sadā naraḥ||||
ākṛṣya dakṣaḥ keśeṣu vimukhāmapi ca śriyam ||
svīkaroti mahābhāgā dakṣe sarvaṃ pratiṣṭhitam || 5 ||
utthānadhīraḥ puruṣo vāgvīrānadhitiṣṭhati ||
utthānadhīraṃ vāgdhīrā ramayanta upāsate || 6 ||
utthānavāneva divaṃ prayāti utthānavāneva paraṃ padañca ||
utthānavāneva naro jayī syāttasmātsadotthānapareṇa bhāvyam || 7 ||
iti śrīviṣṇudharmottare tṛ0 kha0 mārkaṇḍeyavajrasaṃvāde munīnprati haṃsagītāsu utthānapraśaṃsā nāma pañcanavatyadhikadviśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ || 295 ||
<3.296>
ṛṣaya ūcuḥ ||
bahirveditvamācakṣva puṇyānyasmākamacyuta ||
tvayā hi jñānadānena bhūya eva sma vardhitāḥ || 1 ||
haṃsa uvāca ||
pānīyametatsakalaṃ trailokyaṃ sacarācaram ||
vikāraḥ salilasyedaṃ sthāvaraṃ jaṅgamaṃ tathā ||2||
pānīyena vinā vṛttirloke nāstīha karhicit ||
krimikīṭapataṅgānāṃ jalameva parāyaṇam || 3 ||
yatrodakaṃ tatra surāḥ sarva eva pratiṣṭhitāḥ ||
yatrodakaṃ tatra tathā ṛṣayaśca mahāvratāḥ || 4 ||
durlabhaṃ tacca lokānāṃ paraloke dvijottamāḥ ||
udakena vinā vṛttirnāsti lokadvaye sadā || 5 ||
tadā jalāśayāḥ kāryāḥ puruṣeṇa vipaścitā ||
agniṣṭomasamāḥ kūpāstvaśvamedhasamaṃ saraḥ || 6 ||
kūpaḥ pravṛttapānīyastvardhaṃ harati duṣkṛtam ||
kūpakṛtsvargamāsādya devabhogānsamaśnute || 7||
tatrāpi bhogavaikalyaṃ sthānābhyāsācca kīrtitam ||
daśakūpa samā vāpī tathā ca parikīrtitā || 8 ||
kṛtvā taḍāgaṃ ca tathā vāruṇaṃ lokamaśnute ||
daśavarṣasahasrāṇi nalvamātraṃ dvijottamāḥ ||9 ||
tatrāpi puṇyamāpnoti sthānatyāgāttathā bahu ||
gavāṃ pracāre tatkṛtvā golokaṃ prāpnuyād dhruvam || 3.296.10 ||
śakralokamavāpnoti taḍāgakaraṇātpathi ||
marau taḍāge pānīyaṃ yasya tiṣṭhati vai dvijāḥ ||11||
vimānenārkavarṇena brahmalokaṃ sa gacchati ||
prāvṛṭkāle tu pānīyaṃ taḍāge yasya tiṣṭhati || 12 ||
agniṣṭomaphalaṃ tasya puruṣasya prakīrtitam ||
dvādaśāhe phalaṃ tasya śaratkāle tu yasya vai || 13 ||
gosavasya tu hemante phalaṃ prāpnoti mānavaḥ ||
pauṇḍarīkaphalaṃ tasya śiśire yasya tiṣṭhati||14||
vājapeyaphalaṃ tasya vasante yatra ca dvijāḥ ||
aśvamedhaphalaṃ tasya nidāghe yasya tiṣṭhati || 15 ||
sasyānāṃ secanārthāya nadīvāhe tathā kṛte ||
annadaḥ prāṇadaścaiva trāṇadaśca tathā bhavet ||16||
bhagīrathasya lokāṃstānsamavāpnotyasaṃśayam ||
pānīyaśodhanaṃ kṛtvā pradeśe devanirmite||17||
puruṣaḥ phalamāpnoti daśagodānajaṃ dvijāḥ ||
dānapāṣāṇaracitaṃ sukhasevyañjalāśayam || 18 ||
purāṇaṃ mānavaḥ kṛtvā nākaloke mahīyate ||
praṇālakānāṃ karaṇāttṛptimāpnotyanuttamām || 19 ||
mahatpuṇyamavāpnoti tathā kṛtvāmalaṃ jalam ||
tṛṣṇāpanodanaṃ kṛtvā caikasyāpīha dehinaḥ || 3.296.20 ||
mahatphalamavāpnoti nākaloke ca modate ||
svabāhukhātaṃ yaḥ kuryāttaḍāgaṃ matimānnaraḥ || 21 ||
nalvātphalamavāpnoti rājasūyāśvamedhayoḥ ||
nadīsnānamavāpnoti snānaseturjalāśaye ||22||
vahukhāte tataḥ (naraḥ) snāto gaṅgāsnānamavāpnuyāt||
sarastu puṇyaṃ yaḥ kuryātkiñcittasya jalāśaye||
puṇyāṃśabhāgī bhavati yasya taddhi jalāśayam||23||
prāpnoti tṛptiṃ paramāṃ manuṣyo lokāṃstathāgryānsalilādhipasya ||
jalāśaye'lpe'pi kṛte dvijendrāstasmāttu kāryaṃ salilaṃ nṛloke || 24 ||
iti śrīviṣṇudharmottare tṛtīyakhaṃḍe mā0 saṃvāde munīnprati haṃsagītāsu jalāśayanirmāṇo nāma ṣaṇṇavatyadhikadviśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ || 296 ||
<3.297>
haṃsa uvāca ||
vṛkṣasaṃropaṇānmartyo mahatphalamupāśnute ||
gulmavallīlatānāṃ ca ropaṇātpuruṣo dvijāḥ ||1||
pratyekaṃ phalamāpnoti godāne yatprakīrtitam ||
agniṣṭomamavāpnoti puṣpavṛkṣasya ropaṇāt || 2||
pauṇḍarīkamavāpnoti phalavṛkṣasya ropaṇāt ||
ārāmamapi yaḥ kuryātpareṣāṃ nāmakāraṇāt || 3 ||
tathā haritakārthaṃ ca so'gniṣṭomaphalaṃ labhet ||
puṣpārāmaṃ naraḥ kṛtvā devatārthaṃ manoharam || 4 ||
devodyāneṣu sarveṣu nandanādiṣu modate ||
yathākāmaṃ vihārī syānnātra kāryā vicāraṇā || 5 ||
phalārāmaṃ tataḥ kṛtvā vindatyabhyadhikaṃ phalam ||
kṛtvā drumaśatārāmaṃ sośvamedhaphalaṃ labhet || 6 ||
phalaṃ daśaśatārāme lakṣārāme ca kalpayet ||
campakaṃ mallikāṃ jātīṃ śreṣṭhāḥ puṣpākarā matāḥ || 7 ||
anyo'dhikamavāpnoti dattvaitānnātra saṃkṣayaḥ ||
