gulmeṣu kathitau śreṣṭhau tathobhau bhavyadāḍimau || 8 ||
pathi śreṣṭhatamau viprā vaṭāśvatthau prakīrtitau ||
nāraṅgadānādbhavati naro rūpasamanvitaḥ || 9 ||
bījapūrakadānena saubhāgyaṃ mahadāpnuyāt ||
dattvā pālevatāmrau tu tṛptimāpnotyanuttamām || 3.297.10 ||
āmrākṣoṭapradānena sarvakāmānavāpnuyāt ||
lakṣārāmaṃ tu yaḥ kuryādāmrāṇāmiha mānavaḥ || 11 ||
devodyāneṣu ramyeṣu krīḍatyamarasannibhaḥ ||
apsarobhiḥ parivṛto na ca tasmānnivartate || 12 ||
eko'pi ropito vṛkṣaḥ putrakāryakaro bhavet ||
devānprasūnaiḥ prīṇāti chāyayā cātithīṃstathā || 13 ||
phalairmanuṣyānprīṇāti nārakyaṃ nāsti pādape ||
api puṣpaphalairhīne drume pānthasya viśramaḥ || 14 ||
chāyāyāṃ stokakālena bahupuṇyaṃ prayacchati ||
deve varṣati yadvṛkṣātpatrebhyaḥ sravate jalam || 15 ||
tena tṛptimavāpnoti paralokagato dhruvam ||
tena nākamavāpnoti vṛkṣaropayitā naraḥ || 16 ||
secanādapi vṛkṣasya ropitasya pareṇa tu ||
mahatphalamavāpnoti nātra kāryā vicāraṇā || 17 ||
vṛkṣāyurvedavidhinā vyādhitantu yathākramam ||
nīrujaṃ mānavaḥ kṛtvā svargalokamavāpnuyāt || 18 ||
śītavātātapakleśaṃ tathā cchedanabhedane ||
sahante paramaṃ vṛkṣāstaddhi teṣāṃ mahattapaḥ || 19 ||
patrapuṣpaphalairnityaṃ chāyayā caiva śākhinaḥ ||
pareṣāmupayujyante taddhi teṣāṃ mahattapaḥ || 3.297.20 ||
teṣāṃ sadā dayā kāryā bhaktiḥ kāryā tathaiva ca ||
yasteṣāṃ bhaktimānnityaṃ tasya sā vanadevatā || 21 ||
toṣamāyāti paramaṃ tathā kāmānprayacchati ||
vānaspatyaṃ tathā lokaṃ dehabhede samaśnute || 252 ||
vānaspatye śubhe loke sarveṣāmeva śākhinām ||
śarīravatpratiṣṭhanti devatā nātra saṃśayaḥ || 23 ||
yāḥ proktāstvabhimāninyaḥ parāṃ śobhāmathāsthitāḥ ||
yāmyaloke tathā lokaṃ vānaspatyaṃ prakīrtitam || 24 ||
yamalokamupāsante sarvāstā drumadevatāḥ ||
drumaropayitāraṃ ca paralokamupāgatam || 25 ||
divyena gītanṛtyena ramayanta upāsate ||
ropitasya drumasyeha yathā bhavati devatā ||26||
yāmye tu phaladā loke tathā loke tu vāruṇe ||
devatā syānmahābhāgāḥ kṛte puṇye jalāśaye || 27 ||
kṛte śāstre tathā samyagbrahmaloke tathā dvijāḥ ||
kṣiprameva mahābhāgā jāyante śāstradevatāḥ || 28 ||
śāstraṃ jalāśayaṃ vṛkṣaṃ suraveśma tathaiva ca |||
mṛtasyāpi manuṣyasya śarīramiha kīrtitam||29||
kīrtyate yena cānyena mānavaḥ svargamāpnuyāt ||
tattatkāryaṃ manuṣyeṇa svargamakṣayyamicchatā||3.297.30||
putrāḥ pradiṣṭāḥ puruṣasya vṛkṣāḥ svayaṃkṛtāsteṣu nareṇa bhāvyam ||
snehena nityaṃ puruṣaṃ mṛtante kāmaistu divyaiḥ paritarpayanti || 31 ||
iti śrīviṣṇudharmottare tṛtīyakhaṇḍe mā0saṃ0 munīnprati haṃsagītāsu vṛkṣāropaṇaphalanirūpaṇo nāma saptanavatyadhikadviśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ || 297||
<3.298>
haṃsa uvāca ||
jalena nāśamāyāti yatra samyadvijottamāḥ ||
jalaughavāraṇārthāya tatra setuṃ karoti yaḥ || 1 ||
bhūmidāne tu ye lokāḥ sa tānprāpnoti śāśvatān ||
mārgasetuṃ sukhaṃ kṛtvā vahniṣṭomaphalaṃ labhet || 2||
jalaprataraṇe setuṃ yaḥ karoti narottamaḥ ||
sa tīrṇaḥ sukhaduḥkhebhyaḥ svarga lokaṃ prapadyate || 3 ||
prapāṃ pathi tathā kṛtvā nākaloke mahīyate ||
prapāsthānaṃ śubhaṃ kṛtvā sthānamāpnoti śāśvatam || 4 ||
tasya sthānāttathā tyāgātphalavṛddhiḥ prakīrtitā ||
tasyopalepanaṃ kṛtvā sarvapāpaiḥ pramucyate || 5 ||
dattvā rajjumapāṃ sthāne godo bhavati mānavaḥ ||
vāridhānīṃ tathā dattvā tadeva phalamāpnuyāt || 6||
kumbhapradānādbhavati sadā pūrṇamanorathaḥ ||
prapāyāśca tathā kṛtvā puruṣaṃ paricārakam||7||
sarvakāmasamṛddhasya yajñasya phalamaśnute ||
vardhamānāni yo dadyātprapāyāṃ dvijasattamāḥ || 8 ||
trividhāṃ vṛddhimāpnoti tejasā yaśasā śriyā ||
lavaṇasya pradānena rasānāpnoti śāśvatān || 9 ||
tatra haritadānena gavāṃ lokamavāpnuyāt ||
phalapradānena tathā vahniṣṭomaphalaṃ labhet || 3.298.10 ||
saktūnāṃ ca pradānena gosavasya phalaṃ naraḥ ||
gorasānāṃ pradānena gavāṃ lokamavāpnuyāt || 11 ||
śayyāśca vividhāḥ kṛtvā prapāyāmāsanāni ca ||
svargalokamavāpnoti mānuṣye sthānameva ca || 12 ||
kṛtvā nivātaṃ śaraṇaṃ prapāsthāne manoharam ||
svārakṣaṃ lokamāpnoti vasūnāṃ nātra saṃśayaḥ || 13 ||
tatra vahniṃ tathā kṛtvā śītatrāṇāya mānavāḥ ||
kāyāgnidīptiṃ prākāśyaṃ saubhāgyaṃ ca tathāpnuyāt || 14 ||
indhanānāṃ pradānena ripubhirnābhibhūyate ||
tatrāpi yogyabhāṇḍānāṃ dānātsvargamavāpnuyāta || 15 ||
candralokamavāpnoti sthāpanāttuhinasya tu ||
śarkarāloṣṭapāṣāṇakaṇṭakām̐śca tathā naraḥ || || 16 ||
mārgādapāsya yatnena godānaphalamāpnuyāt ||
śrāntasaṃvāhanaṃ kṛtvā tadeva phalamaśnute || 17 ||
vījayitvā pariśrāntaṃ tālavṛntena mānavaḥ ||
skandhena śrāntamṛḍhvāpi vājapeyaphalaṃ labhet || 18 ||
vāhanenātha yānena svena śrāntaṃ tathā nayan ||
sādhyānāṃ lokamāsādya devabhogānupāśnute || 19 ||
śrāntasya bhojanaṃ dattvā kāmānāpnoti śāśvatān ||
śrāntastu tarpitastoyaistṛptimāpnoti mānavaḥ || || 3.298.20 ||
upānahābhyāṃ chatreṇa śrāntaṃ saṃyojya mānavaḥ ||
svābhyāṃ viṣamadeśeṣu kṣaṇādbahuphalaṃ labhet || 21 ||
bhāraśrāntāttathā bhāraṃ gṛhītvā vahate ca yaḥ ||
karmaṇā tena viprendrāḥ svargalokamupāśnute || 22 ||
mūlyena vāhayitvāpi parabhāraṃ vicakṣaṇaḥ ||
tasya mūlyasya dānena phalaṃ daśaguṇaṃ labhet || 23 ||
rakṣaṇaṃ pathi yaḥ kuryātpānthānāṃ mānavottamaḥ ||
prāptādvṛttimayād ghorādrudraloke mahīyate || 24 ||
nadītīreṣu santārya pānthānpathi tathā naraḥ ||
śīghraṃ tarati durgāṇi kṣuradhārām̐śca parvatān || 25 ||
sadā śaucaṃ tathā nāvaṃ yaḥ karoti nadītaṭe ||
aśvamedhaphalaṃ tena naraḥ prāpnoti karmaṇā || 26 ||
sabhāsthānāni ramyāṇi yaḥ karoti vicakṣaṇaḥ ||
udyānāni vicitrāṇi śakraloke mahīyate ||27 ||
prekṣaṇīya pradānena smṛtiṃ medhāṃ ca vindati||
pānthānāṃ hi dayā kāryā karmaṇā yena kenacit||28||
kṛpāsthānaṃ paraṃ pānthaḥ svagṛhādyo vinissṛtaḥ||
liṅgī cāpyathavā varṇī pānthaṃ prati vicakṣaṇaiḥ||
apekṣā naiva kartavyā pānthāḥ pūjyāstu sarvaśaḥ||29||
pānthasya pūjāṃ puruṣastu kṛtvā sthānaṃ samāsādya puraṃdarasya||
prāpnoti puṇyāndivi devabhogānpānthāstataḥ pūjyatamā nṛloke ||3.298.30||
iti śrīviṣṇudharmottare tṛ0 kha0 mā0 saṃ0 munīnprati haṃsagītāsu prapāpraśaṃsā nāmāṣṭanavatyuttaradviśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ || 298 ||
<3.299>
|| haṃsa uvāca || ||
caturdhā sarvapuṇyānāṃ phalamuktaṃ manīṣibhiḥ ||
kṛtāni vidhihīnāni vañcanārthaṃ parasya vā || 1 ||
krodhalobhābhibhūtena tāmasāni vinirdiśet ||
kṛtāni paramānena rājasāni bhavanti tu || 2 ||
sāttvikāni bhavantīha śraddhayā parayā dvijāḥ ||
guṇāntarāṇi vijñāya vidhikāmaṃ kṛtāni tu || 3||
tāmasānāṃ phalaṃ bhuṃkte tiryaktve mānavaḥ sadā ||
varṇasaṃkarabhāvena vārdhakye yadi vā punaḥ || 4 ||
bālye vā dāsabhāve vā nātra kāryā vicāraṇā ||
ato'nyathā tu mānuṣye rājasānāṃ phalaṃ labhet || 5 ||
sāttvikānāṃ phalaṃ bhuṃkte devatve nātra saṃśayaḥ ||
guṇottarakṛtānāñca phalamokṣa udāhṛtaḥ || 6 ||
karmaṇāṃ dhanasādhyānāṃ dhanañca trividhaṃ smṛtam ||
kṛṣṇaśca śabalaścaiva śuklaśca dvijasattamāḥ || 7 ||
trividhasya phalaṃ tasya trividhaṃ parikīrtitam ||
kṛṣṇena yatkṛtaṃ kiñcittiryaktve taddhi bhujyate || 8 ||
varṇānāṃ hi dhanaṃ śuklamarjitaṃ yatsvakarmaṇā ||
śabalantu samuddiṣṭaṃ tathānantarakarmaṇā || 9 ||
varṇottamasya hīnasya karmaṇā kṛṣṇamucyate ||
tathāpi śuklaṃ vakṣyāmi varṇānāntu dvijottamāḥ ||3.299.1 0 ||
pratigraheṇa yallabdhaṃ yājyataḥ śiṣyatastathā ||
guṇānvitebhyo viprasya śuklaṃ tattrividhaṃ dhanam || 11 ||
yuddhopalabdhaṃ kārañca daṇḍācca vyavahārataḥ ||
kṣattriyasya dhanaṃ śuklaṃ trividhaṃ parikīrtitam || 12 ||
kṛṣivāṇijyagorakṣāḥ kṛtvā śuklaṃ tathā viśaḥ ||
dvijaśuśrūṣayā labdhaṃ śuddhaṃ śūdrasya kīrtitam ||13||
na kathañcana kurvīta brāhmaṇaḥ karma vārṣalam ||
vṛṣalaḥ karma vā brāhmaṃ patanīyetaretaram || 14||
(utkṛṣṭaṃ cāpakṛṣṭaṃ ca tayoḥ karma na vidyate ||
madhyame karmaṇī hitvā sarvasādhāraṇe hite||15||
sarveṣāmeva varṇānāṃ vakṣyāmi vividhaṃ dhanam||)
pārśvika dyūtacauryāttu pratirūpakasāhasaiḥ || 16 ||
vyājenopārjitaṃ yattatsarveṣāṃ kṛṣṇamucyate ||
śilpānuvṛttyā yatprāptaṃ kusīdena tathā dhanam || 17 ||
kṛtopakārādāptaṃ ca śabalaṃ samudāhṛtam ||
kramāgataṃ prītidāyaṃ prāptaṃ ca saha bhāryayā || 18 ||
aviśeṣeṇa sarveṣāṃ dhanaṃ śuklamudāhṛtam ||
śuklena vittena kṛtaṃ puṇyaṃ bahuphalaṃ bhavet ||19||
nyāyāgatenātha dhanena vidvānpuṇyāni kṛtvā divi devabhogān ||
ciraṃ samaśnāti mahānubhāvo nyāyena tasmāddhanamajarnīyam ||3.299.20||
iti śrīviṣṇudharmottare tṛ0 kha0 mā0 saṃ0 munīnprati haṃsagītāsu dhanavicāro nāma navanavatyuttaradviśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ ||299||
<3.300>
haṃsa uvāca ||
dānaṃ devāḥ praśaṃsanti manuṣyāśca tathā dvijaḥ ||
dānena kāmānāpnoti yānkām̐ścinmanasecchati || 1 ||
adattadānātkṛpaṇā dṛṣṭveha paratantrakān ||
pāratrikaṃ dhanaṃ kuryāddānaṃ vipreṣu mānavaḥ||2||
amanuṣye samaṃ dānaṃ goṣu jñeyaṃ mahāphalam ||
dviguṇaṃ ca tadevoktaṃ tathā vai varṇasaṅkare || || 3 ||
śūdre caturguṇaṃ proktaṃ viśi cāṣṭaguṇaṃ bhavet ||
kṣattriye ṣoḍaśaguṇaṃ brahmabandhau tadeva tu || 4 ||
dvātriṃśatā smṛtaṃ dānaṃ vedādhyayanatatpare ||
śatadhā tadviśiṣṭaṃ tu prādhīte lakṣasammitam || 5 ||
anantaṃ ca tadevoktaṃ brāhmaṇe vedapārage ||
ātmanastu bhavetpātraṃ nānyasya tu purohitaḥ || 6 ||
purohite tu svaṃ dattaṃ dānamakṣayamucyate ||
yājake ṛtvije caiva gurāvapi ca mānavaḥ || 7 ||
varṇāpekṣā na kartavyā mātaraṃ pitaraṃ prati ||
mātṛṣvasāṃ svasāṃ caiva tathaiva ca pitṛṣvasām || 8 ||
mātāmahīṃ bhāgineyīṃ bhāgineyaṃ tathaiva ca ||
dauhitraṃ viṭpatiṃ caiva teṣu dattamathākṣayam || 9 ||
śrībhraṣṭe yattathā dattaṃ tadapyakṣayamucyate ||
sthānabhraṣṭasya yaḥ kuryādbhūyastvāropaṇaṃ naraḥ || 3.300.10 ||
nākalokamavāpnoti ciranteneha karmaṇā ||
bhūpālaṃ yaścyutaṃ rājyātsvarājye sthāpayetpunaḥ || 11 ||
sa yāti śakrasālokyaṃ yāvacchakrāścaturdaśa ||
tato mānuṣyamāsādya rājā bhavati dhārmikaḥ || 12 ||
tasmāddānaṃ śrīvihīnaṃ dātavyaṃ bhūtimicchatā ||
śrīvihīneṣu yaddattaṃ tadanantaṃ prakīrtitam || 13 ||
kāle pātre tathā deśe dhanaṃ nyāyāgataṃ tathā ||
yaddattaṃ brāhmaṇaśreṣṭhāstadanantaṃ prakīrtitam || 14 ||
dvādaśīṣu tu yaddattaṃ śuklāsu tu viśeṣataḥ ||
śravaṇena tu yuktāsu tatrāpi dvijasattamāḥ || 15 ||
viśeṣādbudhayuktāsu pakṣānteṣu ca yattathā ||
tṛtīyāsu tu śuklāsu sarvāsu ca viśeṣataḥ || 16 ||
vaiśākhaśuklapakṣe tu viśeṣeṇa ca mānadāḥ ||
āṣāḍhe kārtike caiva phālgune ca viśeṣataḥ || 17 ||
tisrastvetāḥ paurṇamāsyo dāne viprā mahāphalāḥ ||
vyatīpāteṣu yaddattaṃ janmarkṣe tu dvijottamāḥ || 18 ||
grahasaṃkramakāle tu tyāge ca mithunasya tu ||
ratrermahāphalaṃ dattantebhyo'pi syānmahāphalam || 19 ||
yadā praviśate bhānurmakaraṃ dvijasattamāḥ ||
āṣāḍhe'śvayuje pauṣe caitre śukle tathaiva ca || 3.300.20 ||
dvādaśīprabhṛti proktaṃ puṇyaṃ dinacatuṣṭayam ||
mithunaṃ ca tathā kanyā dhanvinaṃ mīnameva ca || 21 ||
praviṣṭe bhāskare puṇyaṃ pratyekaṃ kathitaṃ dvijāḥ ||
ṣaḍaśītimukhaṃ nāma dāne dinacatuṣṭayam || 22||
acchinnanāḍyāṃ yaddattaṃ putre jāte dvijottamāḥ ||
saṃskāreṣu ca putrasya tadakṣayyaṃ prakīrtitam || 23 ||
candre vā yadi vā sūrye dṛṣṭe rāhau mahāgrahe ||
akṣayaṃ kathitaṃ dattaṃ tatrāpyekaṃ viśeṣataḥ || 24 ||
puṣkareṣu prayāge ca prabhāse naimiṣe tathā ||
dharmāraṇye gayāyāñca tathaivāmarakaṇṭake || 25 ||
gaṅgādvāre kuśāvarte bilvake nīlaparvate ||
tathā kanakhale tīrthe śāligrāme tathaiva ca || 26 ||
vārāṇasyāṃ sarasvatyāṃ gaṅgāsāgarasaṅgame ||
sindhusāgarasandhau ca tritakūpe tathānaghāḥ || 27||
plakṣaprasravaṇe puṇye camasonmajjane tathā ||
mahālaye'tha kedāre bhṛgutuṅge tathaiva ca || 28 ||
mānase ca tathā puṇye tathaivottaramānasa ||
eteṣvanyeṣu ca tathā dadhitīrtheṣu yadbhavet || 29 ||
nimnagānāṃ ca tīreṣu jñeyaṃ bahuphalaṃ hi tat ||
dīnāndhakṛpaṇānāthavāgvihīneṣu yattathā || 3.300.30 ||
vikaleṣu tathānyeṣu jaḍavāmanapaṅguṣu ||
rogārtteṣu ca yaddattaṃ tatsyādbahuphalaṃ dhanam || 31 ||
yaddattaṃ dharmasāhāyye kriyamāṇe tathā kratau ||
vivāhakaraṇārthāya tadakṣayyaṃ prakīrtitam || 32 ||
yatprāptena tu vittena naraḥ syādutsave sukhe ||
utsavānāṃ sa bhāgī syādyatra tatrābhijāyate || 33 ||
yaddattena ca vittena vyasanāduttaretpuraḥ ||
na sa kiñcidavāpnoti vyasanaṃ manujottamaḥ || 34 ||
striyaṃ meṣamajaṃ caiva gāmaśvaṃ turagannaram ||
ekamekaṃ tu dātavyamekasya bahavo'pi vā 35 ||
ekaṃ bahūnāṃ taddattaṃ dhruvaṃ narakamṛcchati ||
ādāturnarakāya syāttadā dāturdvijottamāḥ || 36 ||
vikrayaṃ tu samāpannā yo dattvā jīvadakṣiṇām ||
evaṃ kāle ca pātre ca deśe śuddhāgamaṃ dhanam || 37 ||
dattvā dhanamavāpnoti devabrāhmaṇakṣattriyān ||
dhanaṃ bahiścarāḥ prāṇā dhane sarvaṃ pratiṣṭhitam || 38 ||
dhanādannaṃ tathānnena sarvo jīvati vai janaḥ ||
yāvanti loke puṇyāni tebhyo dātā viśiṣyate ||
dānena tadavāpnoti yatkiñcinmanasepsitam || 39 ||
dānena loke priyatāmupaiti dānena nākaṃ puruṣaḥ prayāti ||
mānuṣyamāsādya ca dānaśīlo bhogānavāpnoti manobhirāmān || 3.300.40 ||
iti śrīviṣṇudharmottare tṛtīyakhaṃḍe mārkaṇḍeyavajrasaṃvāde munīnprati haṃsagītāsu dānapraśaṃsā nāma triśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ || 300 ||
<3.301>
ṛṣaya ūcuḥ ||
sarvadharmabhṛtāṃ śreṣṭha vidhiṃ dharmyaṃ pratigrahe ||
brāhmaṇānāṃ samācakṣva tvaṃ hi vedamayo nidhiḥ || 1 ||
|| haṃsa uvāca || ||
mṛtake sūtake viprā na tu deyaḥ pratigrahaḥ ||
tatrāpi ca tathā deyā yā syādabhayadakṣiṇā || 2 ||
rātrau dānaṃ na śaṃsanti vinā tvabhayadakṣiṇām ||
vidyāṃ kānyāṃ dvijaśreṣṭhā dīpamannaṃ pratiśrayam || 3 ||
rātrāvapi hitaṃ deyaṃ yaccānyatkathitaṃ purā ||
pūjane tvatithīnāṃ ca pānthānāmapi pūjane || 4||
tacca rātrau tathā jñeyaṃ gavāmuktaṃ ca pūjane ||
kālaḥ sarvo vinirdiṣṭaḥ pātraḥ sarvaśca mānadāḥ|| 5 ||
abhayasya pradāne tu nātra kāryā vicāraṇā ||
śucirvāpyaśucirvāpi dadyādabhayadakṣiṇām || 6 ||
śucinā'śucinā vāpi grāhyā tvabhayadakṣiṇā ||
kālaḥ sa eva grahaṇe yadā bhayamupasthitam || 7 ||
pratigrahāṇyathānyāni grāhyāṇi śucinā sadā||
mṛtake sūtake caiva na grāhyāṇi dvijottamāḥ || 8 ||
abhyaktena ca dharmajñāstathā muktaśikhena ca ||
snātaḥ samyagupaspṛśya gṛhṇīyātprayataḥ śuciḥ || 9 ||
toyaṃ dadyāttathā dātā dāne vidhirayaṃ smṛtaḥ ||
pratigrahītā sāvitraṃ sarvatraiva tu kīrtayet || 3.301.10 ||
tatastu kīrtayetsārdhaṃ dravyeṇa dravyadevatām ||
samāpayettataḥ paścātkārmastutyā pratigraham || 11 ||
pratigrahaṃ paṭheduccaiḥ pratigṛhya dvijottamaḥ ||
mandaṃ paṭhettu rājanyādupāṃśu ca tathā viśaḥ || 12 ||
manasā tu tathā śūdrātsvastivācanikaṃ tathā ||
soṃkāraṃ brāhmaṇe kuryānniroṃkāraṃ mahīpatau || 13 ||
upāṃśu ca tathā vaiśye svasti śūdre prakīrtitam ||
abhayaṃ sarvadaivatyaṃ bhūmirvai viṣṇudevatā || 14 ||
kanyādānaṃ tathā dāsī prājāpatyāḥ prakīrtitāḥ ||
tathā caikaśaphaṃ sarvaṃ kathitaṃ yamadaivatam || 15 ||
māhiṣaṃ ca tathā sāmyaṃ coṣṭraṃ vai nairṛtaṃ bhavet ||
raudrī dhenurvinirdiṣṭā cchāgamāgneyamucyate || 16||
meṣaṃ tu vāruṇaṃ vidyādvarāho vaiṣṇavaḥ smṛtaḥ||
āraṇyāḥ paśavaḥ sarve kathitā vāyudaivatāḥ || 17 ||
jalāśayāni sarvāṇi vāridhānī kamaṇḍaluḥ ||
kumbhaṃ ca karakaṃ caiva vāruṇāni dvijottamāḥ || 18 ||
samudrajāni ratnāni vāruṇāni dvijottamāḥ ||
āgneyaṃ kanakaṃ proktaṃ sarvalohāni cāpyatha || 19 ||
prājāpatyāni sasyāni pakvānnamapi ca dvijāḥ ||
jñeyāni sarvagandhāni gāndharvāṇi vicakṣaṇaiḥ || 3.301.20 ||
bārhaspatyaṃ smṛtaṃ vāsaḥ saumyānyatha rasāni ca ||
pakṣiṇaśca tathā sarve vāyavyā parikīrtitāḥ || 21 ||
vidyā brāhmī vinirdiṣṭā vidyopakaraṇāni ca ||
sārasvatāni jñeyāni pustakādyāni paṃḍitaiḥ ||22||
sarveṣāṃ śilpabhāṇḍānāṃ viśvakarmā tu devatā ||
drumāṇāmatha puṣpāṇāṃ śākahāritakaiḥ saha || 23 ||
phalānāmapi sarveṣāṃ tathā jñeyo vanaspatiḥ ||
matsyamāṃsaṃ vinirdiṣṭaṃ prājāpatyaṃ tathaiva ca || 24 ||
chatraṃ kṛṣṇājinaṃ śayyā rathamāsanameva ca ||
upānahau tathā yānaṃ yaccānyatprāṇivarjitam ||25||
uttānāṅgirase tvetatpratigṛhṇīta mānavaḥ ||
parjanyāya tathā sīraṃ śastravarmadhvajādikam || 26 ||
raṇopakaraṇaṃ sarvaṃ kathitaṃ cakradaivatam ||
grahantu sarvadaivatyaṃ yadyaduktaṃ dvijottamāḥ || 27 ||
tajjñeyaṃ viṣṇudaivatyaṃ sarvaṃ vā dvijasattamāḥ ||
bhūmeḥ pratigrahaṃ kuryādbhūmiṃ kṛtvā pradakṣiṇam || 28 ||
kare gṛhya tathā kanyāṃ dāsīdāsau dvijottamāḥ ||
karantu hṛdi vinyasya dharmyo jñeyaḥ pratigrahaḥ || 29 ||
āruhya ca gajasyoktaḥ karṇe cāśvasya kīrtitaḥ ||
tathā caikaśaphānāṃ ca sarveṣāmaviśeṣataḥ || 3.301.30 ||
pratigṛhṇīta gāṃ pucche hyante kṛṣṇājinaṃ tathā ||
karṇe'jāḥ paśavaścānye grāhyāḥ pucche vicakṣaṇaiḥ || 31 ||
pratigrahastvathoṣṭrasya āruhyaiva tathā bhavet ||
bījānāṃ muṣṭimādāya ratnānyādāya sarvataḥ || 32 ||
vasuṃ daśāntādādadyātparidhāyātha vā punaḥ ||
āruhyopānahau mantramāruhyaiva ca pāduke || 33 ||
īṣādhastu rathaṃ grāhyaṃ chatraṃ daṃḍe tu vācayet ||
drumāṇāmatha sarveṣāṃ mūle nyastakaro dvijāḥ || 34 ||
āyudhāni samādāya tathonmucya vibhūṣaṇam ||
varmadhvajau tathā spṛṣṭvā praviśya ca tathā gṛham ||35||
avatīrya tu sarvāṇi jalasthānāni vai dvijāḥ||
dravyāṇyanyānyathādāya spṛṣṭvā tu brāhmaṇaḥ paṭheta || 36 ||
kanyādāne tu ca paṭheddravyāṇāntu pṛthakpṛthak ||
pratigrahe dvijaśreṣṭhāstatraivāntarbhavanti te || 37 ||
dravyāṇāmatha sarveṣāṃ dravyasaṃśravaṇānnarāḥ ||
vācayejjalamādāya kareṇātha pratigraham || 38 ||
pratigrahasya yo dharmaṃ na jānāti dvijo vidhim ||
dravyastainyasamāyukto narakaṃ pratipadyate || 39 ||
niṣkrīṇāti tu taddravyaṃ brāhmaṇastu pratigrahāt ||
vidhiṃ tu dharmyaṃ vijñāya brāhmaṇastu pratigrahe || 3.301.40 ||
ātmanaḥ śreyasā yogaṃ kuryāddātā tathaiva ca ||
bhuvaṃ dhenumathāśvaṃ ca ratnāni kanakaṃ tathā || 41 ||
tilāni kuñjaraṃ dadyānnāvijñāya guṇāguṇān ||
ānṛśaṃsyena sarvasya dadyādannaṃ vicakṣaṇaḥ ||42||
vāsaśca dadyātsarvasya sarvasādhāraṇe hite ||
vidhiṃ dharmyamavijñāya yastu kuryātpratigraham || 43 ||
dātrā saha taratyeva naiva durgāṇyasau dvijāḥ ||
tasmātsarvaprayatnena vidhirjñeyaḥ pratigrahe || 44 ||
pratigraho yo vidhinā gṛhīto dvayostu yogaṃ parameṇa kuryāt ||
paratra saṃbhṛtya tatheha loke sa śreyase nātra vicāramasti || || 45 ||
iti śrīviṣṇudharmottare tṛ0 khaṃ0 mā0 saṃ0 munīnprati haṃsagītāsu pratigrahavidhivarṇano nāmaikottaratriśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ || 301 ||
<3.302>
ṛṣaya ūcuḥ ||
pṛthakpṛthak tvamācakṣva dānānāṃ phalamuttamam ||
sarvadharmabhṛtāṃ śreṣṭha sarvajñastvaṃ mato hi naḥ || 1 ||
|| haṃsa uvāca || ||
abhayasya pradānāddhi nānyaddānaṃ viśiṣyate ||
sarveṣāmeva dānānāṃ prāṇadānaṃ viśiṣyate || 2 ||
grāhyaṃ prāṇapradānantu caṇḍālātpukkasādapi ||
jīvansarvamavāpnoti jīvandharmaṃ karoti ca || 3 ||
jīvamānastu puruṣaḥ kāmānāṃ bhājanaṃ bhavet ||
bhājanena tu sarvaṃ vai bhājanatvaṃ vinaśyati || 4 ||
śarīradharmasarvasvaṃ rakṣaṇāya prayatnataḥ ||
śarīrātsravate dharmaṃ parvatātsalilaṃ yathā || 5 ||
abhayasya pradānena naraḥ syātsarvakāmadaḥ ||
aśvamedhaphalaṃ tasya yo rakṣeccharaṇāgatam || 6 ||
śaraṇāgataṃ tu santyajya yo naraḥ puruṣādhamaḥ ||
bahūnyabdasahasrāṇi narakaṃ pratipadyate || 7 ||
narakācca tathā muktastiryagyoniṣu jāyate ||
tatrāpi ca tathāpnoti vadhabandhānyanekaśaḥ || 8 ||
daivānmānuṣyamāsādya caṇḍālatvamavāpnuyāt ||
vyādhibandhavadhairghoraistatrāpi sa ca yujyate || 9 ||
paritrāṇaṃ tu yaḥ kuryādbhītasya śaraṇaiṣiṇaḥ ||
sa ekaḥ puruṣo loke sarvadharmabhṛtāṃ varaḥ || 3.302.10 ||
vadhabhītasya yaḥ kuryātparitrāṇaṃ narottamaḥ ||
śakralokaḥ smṛtastasya yāvadindrāścaturdaśa || 11 ||
tamaḥpraveśabhītasya paritrāṇena mānavaḥ ||
brahmalokamavāpnoti nātra kāryā vicāraṇā || 12 ||
vadhādapi paraṃ pāpaṃ cakṣuṣā tu viyojayet ||
pārthivena na tatkāryaṃ kadācidapi kasyacit || || 13 ||
tamaḥpraveśanaṃ kṛtvā pareṣāṃ puruṣādhamaḥ ||
andhatvaṃ dhruvamāpnoti yatrayatrābhijāyate || 14 ||
api kalpasahasrāṇi tamasā na viyujyate ||
aṅgacchedanabhītasya kṛtvā trāṇamaninditāḥ || 15 ||
rudralokamavāpnoti kalpaśeṣamiti śrutiḥ ||
kalpādisarge bhavati jñānayukto narastataḥ || 16 ||
kṛtvā bandhanabhītasya paritrāṇannarottamaḥ ||
sarvabandhavinirmukto vāyuloke mahīyate || 17 ||
āsevanāddhi bhītasya trāṇaṃ kṛtvā tathā naraḥ ||
sarvaduḥkhavinirmuktaḥ sādhyānāṃ lokamāpnuyāt || 18 ||
tāḍanādyastu bhītasya paritrāṇaṃ samācaret ||
sarvabandhavinirmukto bhṛgūṇāṃ lokamaśnute || 19 ||
vivāsanācca bhītasya trāṇaṃ kṛtvā tathā naraḥ ||
svargalokamavāpnoti mānuṣye sthānamuttamam || || 3.302.20 ||
dhanāpahārabhītasya trāṇaṃ kṛtvā narottamaḥ ||
svargalokamavāpnoti pūjāṃ devagaṇāttathā || 21 ||
naraḥ kṛtaparitrāṇaḥ puṇyaṃ kiñcidyadācaret ||
bhāgī tasya paritrātā naraḥ puṇyasya karmaṇaḥ || 22 ||
paśūnāñca mṛgāṇāñca pakṣiṇāñca tathā dvijāḥ ||
tṛṇadrumalatānāñca trāṇādbhavati nākabhāk || 23 ||
vadhakasya hastagataṃ paśuṃ krītvā narottamaḥ ||
nākalokamavāpnoti sukhī sarvatra jāyate || 24 ||
yāvanti paśu romāṇi tāvadvarṣāṇi mānavaḥ ||
svargalokamavāpnoti yaśca trāṇaṃ karotyasau ||25||
api kīṭapataṅgasya trāṇātsarvamupāśnute ||
bahūnyabdasahasrāṇi nātra kāryā vicāraṇā ||26||
cauragrastaṃ nṛpagrastaṃ ripugrastaṃ tu mokṣayet ||
vyālagrastaṃ tathā viprāḥ so'śvamedhaphalaṃ labhet||27||
viṣame tu tathā durge salile vā hutāśane||
nimagnaṃ yaḥ paritrāyetso'śvamedhaphalaṃ labhet||28||
vyādhitasyauṣadhaṃ dattvā prāṇadānaphalaṃ labhet||
rogārtaṃ paricaryātha prāṇadānaphalaṃ labhet || 29 ||
upacāraṃ tathā kṛtvā bhiṣaṅ mūlyaṃ vinā dvijāḥ ||
prāṇadānaphalaṃ tasya nākaloke mahīyate||3.302.30||
ye rakṣyamāṇā nihatā manuṣyā bhīruṅgatāste tridaśendralokam ||
cyavanti nādyāpi ca te nṛvīrāḥ kāle'pi teṣāṃ cyavanaṃ na cāsti || 31 ||
iti śrīviṣṇudharmottare tṛ0 kha0 mārkaṇḍeyavajrasaṃvāde munīnprati haṃsagītāsvabhayadānapraśaṃsāvarṇano nāma dvyadhikatriśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ || 302 ||
<3.303>
|| haṃsa uvāca ||
vidyā kāmadughā dhenurvidyā cakṣuranuttmam ||
vidyādānātparaṃ dānaṃ na bhūtaṃ na bhaviṣyati || 1 ||
vidyāvānsarvakāmānāṃ bhājanaṃ puruṣo bhavet ||
tasmādvidyāṃ hi dadatā sarvaṃ dattaṃ bhavediha || 2 ||
parādhyāpanataḥ kleśaṃ puruṣastu yadaśnute ||
tapastatparamaṃ tasya brahmalokaṃ paraṃ smṛtam || 3 ||
dānānāmuttamaṃ loke tapasā ca tathottamam ||
vidyādānaṃ mahābhāgāḥ sarvakāmaphalapradam || 4 ||
vedadānādavāpnoti sarvayajñaphalaṃ naraḥ ||
upavedapradānena gandharvaiḥ saha modate || 5 ||
vedāṅgānāṃ ca dānena śakralokamavāpnuyāt ||
dharmaśāstrapradānena dharmeṇa saha modate || 6 ||
siddhāntānāṃ pradānena mokṣamāpnotyasaṃśayam ||
śāstrāṇi dattvā cānyāni naro nāke mahīyate || 7 ||
vidyādānādavāpnoti pradānātpustakasya ca ||
śilpāni śikṣayedyastu pauṇḍarīkaphalaṃ labhet || 8 ||
śikṣayitvā dhanurvedamaśvamedhaphalaṃ labhet ||
śilpabhāṇḍapradānena śilpadānaphalaṃ labhet ||9 ||
ahiteṣu pravṛttasya tathā kṛtvā nivāraṇam ||
vidyādānaphalaṃ proktaṃ nātra kāryā vicāraṇā||3.303.10||
pāpapravṛttasya tathā naraḥ kṛtvā nivāraṇam ||
vidyādānaphalaṃ prāpya svargaloke mahīyate || 11 ||
śikṣayetpuruṣaḥ karma karmaṇā yena jīvati ||
śikṣayitvā mahābhāgāḥ svargaloke mahīyate || 12 ||
yena jīvati bhāṇḍena tasya bhāṇḍasya dāyakaḥ ||
sarvakāmasamṛddhasya yajñasya phalamaśnute || 13||
antarvantīha dānāni sarvāṇyeva dvijottamāḥ ||
ekaṃ mahāphalaṃ jñeyaṃ vidyādānamanantakam || 14 ||
vidyāṃ yatheṣṭāṃ puruṣasya dattvā mantraṃ śubhaṃ vāpyatha vāpi śilpam ||
śilpasya bhāṇḍaṃ yadi vā dvijendrāḥ prāpnoti lokaṃ manasā tvabhīṣṭam||15||
iti śrīviṣṇudharmottare tṛ0 kha0 mā0 saṃ0 munīnprati haṃsagītāsu vidyādānaphalanirūpaṇo nāma tryadhikatriśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ||303||
<3.304>
haṃsa uvāca ||
brahmadeyāṃ tu yaḥ kanyāṃ puruṣasya prayacchati ||
saptātītānbhaviṣyām̐śca svakule sapta mānavān ||1||
tena kanyāpradānena tārayiṣyatyasaṃśayam ||
lokamāpnoti ca tathā dakṣasyeha prajāpateḥ || 2 ||
dattvā daivena vidhinā kulaṃ tripuruṣaṃ naraḥ ||
samuddharati durgebhyo dakṣalokaṃ ca gacchati ||3 ||
prājāpatyena vidhinā dattvātmānaṃ samuddharet ||
mahatpuṇyamavāpnoti svargalokaṃ ca gacchati || 4 ||
bhūkarīndrāśvadānāni godānañca tathaiva ca ||
antarbhavanti vai yasminkiṃ dānamadhikaṃ tataḥ || 5 ||
dattvā tāṃ varṇahīnāya ghore tamasi majjati ||
bahūnyabdasahasrāṇi tathaivāśucibhuṅnaraḥ||6||
samavarṇāya yo dadyātsa tūktaphalabhāgbhaveta ||
dattvā cādhikavarṇāya dviguṇāṃ triguṇāṃ tathā || 7 ||
dvijaputramanāthaṃ yaḥ saṃskuryādyaśca karmabhiḥ ||
cūḍopanayanādyaiśca so'śvamedhaphalaṃ labhet || 8 ||
anāthāṃ kanyakāṃ dattvā sadṛśe vādhike vare ||
dviguṇaṃ phalamāpnoti kanyādāne yadīritam || 9||
vivāhe samanuprāpte kṛtvaupakaraṇāni ca ||
nākalokamavāpnoti karmasvanyeṣvapi dvijāḥ || 3.304.10 ||
vastraṃ patramalaṅkāraṃ vivāhārthaṃ prayacchati ||
mahatphalamavāpnoti nākaloke mahīyate || 11 ||
kanyayā saha yaddattaṃ draviṇaṃ vahnimūlake ||
saphalāddviguṇaṃ tasya phalamuktaṃ purātanaiḥ || 12 ||
kanyādānādavāpnoti dakṣalokaṃ narottamaḥ ||
viṣṇupūjāsamaṃ puṇyaṃ tatpatyuḥ pūjayā bhavet || 13 ||
vimānamāruhya manobhirāmaṃ surāṅganāgītavilāsahṛdyam ||
prāpnoti lokāṃstridaśottamānāṃ kanyāprado nātra vicāraṇāsti ||14 ||
iti śrīviṣṇudharmottare tṛ0 kha0 mā0 saṃ0 munīnprati haṃsagītāsu kanyādānaphalanirūpaṇo nāma caturadhikatriśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ || 304 ||
<3.305>
haṃsa uvāca ||
bhūmirdhātrī vidhātrī ca bhūmau sarvaṃ pratiṣṭhitam ||
bhūmirhi mātā sarvasya sarvakāmapradā varā || 1 ||
tadudbhūtena haviṣā tṛptimāyānti devatāḥ ||
bhūmirhi dhāraṇī viprāstrailokyasya nigadyate || 2 ||
bhūmau hi sarvaṃ bhavati bhūmau sarvaṃ pratiṣṭhitam ||
sarvakāmapradaṃ jñeyaṃ bhūmidānaṃ narottama || 3 ||
hastamātrāṃ tu yo dadyādbhuvaṃ puruṣasattama ||
tenaiva dhruvamāpnoti bhūmidānaphalaṃ naraḥ || 4 ||
yadutpannamathāśnāti naraḥ saṃvatsaraṃ dvijāḥ ||
ekaṃ gocarmamātraṃ tu bhuvaḥ proktaṃ vicakṣaṇaiḥ || 5 ||
brāhmaṇāya tato dattvā vasūnāṃ lokamāpnuyāt ||
śākabhūmiṃ tato dattvā lokamāṅgirasaṃ vrajet || 6 ||
ārāmabhūmiṃ dattvā ca mārutaṃ lokamaśnute ||
jalāśayārthaṃ yo dadyādvāruṇaṃ lokamāpnuyāt || 7 ||
yasya dattvā tu veśmārthaṃ tasya devasya so'śnute ||
yastatra vidyate nityaṃ loko dvijavarā dhruvam || 8 ||
udyānabhūmiṃ dattvā ca gandharvaiḥ saha modate ||
ratnākaraṃ tu vai dattvā śakraloke mahīyate ||9||
dattvā kanakabhūmiṃ ca vahniloke mahīyate ||
anyeṣāmākarabhuvaṃ lohānāṃ yaḥ prayacchati || 3.305.10 ||
tasyāpi matiruddiṣṭā loke hautāśane dvijāḥ ||
dhānyākarabhuvaṃ dattvā nākaloke mahīyate || 11 ||
añjanākarabhūmiṃ tu yaḥ prayacchati vai dvijāḥ ||
pitṛbhiryamalokasthaḥ pūjyate'sau narottamaḥ || 12 ||
lavaṇākarabhūmiṃ ca somaloke mahīyate ||
śimbisasyaprarohāṃ tu bhuvaṃ dattvā narottamaḥ || 13 ||
sādhyānāṃ lokamāpnoti nātra kāryā vicāraṇā ||
indhanākarabhūmiṃ tu nāsatyaiḥ sahitastathā||14||
śūkadhānyabhuvaṃ dattvā rudraloke mahīyate ||
kedāra bhūmiṃ dharmajñā yaḥ prayacchati dharmataḥ || 15 ||
sa mahatpuṇyamāpnoti brahmalokaṃ ca gacchati ||
ikṣubhūmiṃ naro dattvā drākṣābhūmimathāpi ca || 16 ||
somalokamavāpnoti sarvakāmām̐śca vindati ||
soparā taskarākīrṇā tathā vyāḍavatī ca bhūḥ||17|
na dātavyā dvijaśreṣṭhā yā ca sandhiṣu vai dvijāḥ ||
gulmapuṣpapradāṃ yastu latāṃ vā saṃprayacchati||18||
vimāne nandane so'tha krīḍatyapsarasāṃ gaṇaiḥ||
puṣpadaṃ phaladaṃ vṛkṣaṃ dattvā viprāya bhaktitaḥ||19||
svargalokaparibhraṣṭo nagarādhipatirbhavet||
brāhmaṇāya gṛhaṃ dattvā vasūnāṃ lokamaśnute||3.305.20||
ārāmamatha sodyānaṃ nandane krīḍate vane||
dhanadhānyayutaṃ sphītaṃ gṛhopakaraṇairyutam||21||
śayyāsanayutaṃ ramyaṃ gojāśvāviyutaṃ tathā||
gṛhaṃ dattvā dvijendrāya tāvatsvargamupāśnute ||22||
yāvatkalpāvasāne tu kalpādau pārthivo bhavet||
grāmapradaḥ syānnṛpatistathā hyaśanado nṛpaḥ || 23 ||
samagravasudheśaḥ syāccakravartī ca rājyadaḥ ||
svadattāṃ paradattāṃ vā na hareta vasundharām || 24 ||
bhūmeraṅgulamātraṃ tu hṛtvā narakamṛcchati ||
bahūnyabdasahasrāṇi dharme'pi puruṣo rataḥ || 25 ||
viṣṭhākṛmitvamāpnoti pitṛbhiḥ sahitastathā ||
tasmādbhūmiṃ na hiṃseta dadyādeva vicakṣaṇaḥ || 26 ||
bhūmidānātparaṃ dānaṃ nānyallokeṣu vidyate ||
bhūmiṃ yuddhajitāṃ dadyādbrāhmaṇeṣu mahīpatiḥ || 27 ||
paraṃ rāṣṭraṃ samāsādya prāgdattaṃ naiva hiṃsayet ||
svarāṣṭre pararāṣṭre vā devabrāhmaṇasātkṛtam || 28 ||
ciraṃ narakamāpnoti hṛtvā bhūmipatirbhuvam ||
dattā na hiṃsanīyā syādripuṇāpi vasundharā || 29 ||
bhūmihartā paraṃ duḥkhaṃ pretya ceha tathāśnute ||
bhūmiṃ krītvā tu yaḥ kuryāddevabrāhmaṇasānnaraḥ || 3.305.30 ||
svargalokamavāpnoti puruṣo'pi sudāruṇaḥ ||
ṣaṣṭivarṣasahasrāṇi svarge vasati bhūmidaḥ || 31 ||
ācchettā cānumantā ca tāvanti narake vaset ||
pakvasasyāṃ vasumatīṃ yo'laṅkṛtya prayacchati || 32 ||
kāmagena vimānena brahmalokaṃ sa gacchati ||
yathā bhavati viprendrāḥ phalaṃ bhūmau punaḥpunaḥ || 33 ||
tathā bhūmikṛtaṃ dānaṃ sasyairiva vivardhate ||
yastu rājā dvijātibhyaḥ samyagbhūmiṃ prayacchati ||
punastu jananaṃ prāpya rājaiva bhuvi jāyate || 34 ||
śastraṃ bhadrāsanaṃ vastraṃ narāśvavaravāraṇāḥ ||
bhūmidānasya puṣpāṇi phalaṃ svargaḥ prakīrtitaḥ || 35||
saṃgrāme ye hatāḥ śūrāstathā ye yajñayājinaḥ ||
vaneṣu ye mṛtāste ca nātikrāmanti bhūmidam || 36 ||
nāsti viṣṇusamastrātā nāsti gaṅgāsamā nadī || 37||
homamantrastathā nāsti mahāvyāhṛtibhiḥ samaḥ ||
gāyatrīsadṛśo japyo yathā nāsti dvijottamāḥ||38||
antarjale tathā nāsti mantro'nyo'pyaghamarṣaṇāt ||
vidhirmānasamo nāsti tapo nānaśanātparam ||39||
gururmātṛsamo nāsti nāsti bhāryāsamaḥ sakhā ||
nīcāvamānanādduḥkhādduḥkhaṃ nāsti yathā param || 3.305.40 ||
yathā ca nāsti lokeṣu sukhaṃ śatrujayādhikam||
cakṣurjñānasamaṃ nāsti suhṛddharmasamastathā || 41 ||
bhūmidānātparaṃ dānaṃ tathā nāsti dvijottamāḥ ||
bhūmidānātparaṃ nāsti vimānaiḥ kāmagairiha || 42 ||
devastrīnṛtyabahule vīṇāmurajanādite ||
kiṃkiṇījālanirghoṣe manorathaphaladrume ||43||
mukhyābhirdevarāmābhirvījyamānāstu cāmaraiḥ||
saṃgīyamānā gandharvaiḥ stūyamānāstathaiva ca ||44||
bahūnyabdasahasrāṇi devabhogāṃstu te narāḥ ||
svarge bhuktvā yadā yānti mānuṣyaṃ kālaparyayāt ||
bhavanti sarvabhogāḍhyāḥ sarvaduḥkhavivarjitāḥ||45||
vikhyātaśabdāśca janābhirāmāḥ prataptacāmīkarabhūṣitāṅgāḥ ||
sugandhigandhaiśca tathānuliptā bhavanti loke ca sukhairupetāḥ ||46||
iti śrīviṣṇudhamottare tṛ0 khaṃ0 mā0 saṃ0 munīnprati haṃsagītāsu bhūmidānavarṇano nāma pañcādhikatriśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ||305||
<3.306>
haṃsa uvāca||
gāvaḥ pavitrā maṅgalyā goṣu lokāḥ pratiṣṭhitāḥ||
tāsvāyattāni yajñāni devatātithipūjanam||1||
balantuṣṭiśca puṣṭiśca tathā śrāddhakriyā dvijāḥ||
nāsau sampannamaśnāti yasya dhenurna vidyate||||
tasmātsarvapradānānāṃ gavāṃ dānamanuttamam ||
hīnāṅgīṃ kṣīrapāṃ vṛddhāṃ dugdhadohāṃ nirindriyām||3||
vyādhitāṃ viṣamāṃ vyālāṃ tathaiva prapalāyinīm ||
mūlyaistathāpyasaṃśuddhairhṛtāśca dvijapuṅgavāt||4||
na dātavyā smṛtā dhenurdattvā narakamāpnuyāt ||
brāhmaṇo yastu kurute gorasānāṃ tu vikrayam||5||
gavāṃ ca vikrayaṃ viprā viṣamo yastathā gavām||
apātradoṣayuktaśca na deyā tasya cāpyatha||6||
naikā bahūnāṃ dātavyā na dvayordvijapuṅgavāḥ||
sā tu vikrayamāpannā dāturnarakadāyinī||7||
ekaṃ pātramathāsādya deyāstu bahavaḥ smṛtāḥ ||
gavāṃ pradāne yatnena parīkṣyo brāhmaṇo bhavet || 8 ||
yathākathañcid dattvā gāṃ dhenuṃ vā'dhenumeva vā ||
arogāmaparikliṣṭāṃ dātā svarge mahīyate ||9||
dattvā vatsatarīṃ viprāḥ svargaloke mahīyate ||
gurviṇīṃ ca tathā dattvā vasūnāṃ lokamaśnute || 3.306.10 ||
prasūyamānāṃ tāṃ dattvā bhūmidānaphalaṃ labhet ||
sravaṃtyāṃ ca tathā dattvā nandane modate naraḥ ||11||
śvetāṃ dattvā somaloke raktāṃ dattvā vibhāvasoḥ ||
kṛṣṇāṃ dattvā tathā yāmye nīlāṃ dattvā ca vāruṇe || 12 ||
indraloke tu śabalāmindraloke tu rohiṇīm ||
vātareṇusavarṇāṃ tu vāyuloke mahīyate || 13 ||
dhūmravarṇāṃ tathā dattvā vasūnāṃ lokamaśnute ||
āṣāḍhena savarṇāntu lokaṃ vāruṇamāpnuyāt || 14 ||
suvarṇavarṇāṃ kauberaṃ tāmrāmāṅgirasaṃ tathā ||
palāladhūmravarṇābhāṃ pitṛlokaṃ tathaiva ca||15||
śitikaṇṭhāpradānena vaiśvadevamavāpnuyāt ||
gaurīṃ dattvā bhṛgūṇāñca vairāṭābhāṃ prajāpateḥ || 16 ||
pāṇḍukambalavarṇābhāṃ rudrāṇāṃ lokamāpnuyāt ||
śuklabinducitāṃ kṛṣṇāṃ gandharvaiḥ saha modate || 17 ||
(kapilāñca tathā dattvā yatheṣṭaṃ lokamāpnuyāt ||)
gāvaḥ prakṛtivarṇāstu kapilāḥ parikīrtitāḥ || 18 ||
kṛtantu varṇavaicitryaṃ pūrvaṃ tāsāntu śambhunā ||
yathā tu gaṅgā saritāṃ dhenūnāṃ kapilā tathā || 19 ||
sāyaṃ prātaśca satataṃ homakāle dvijottamāḥ ||
gāvo dadati vai haumyamṛṣibhyo nātra saṃśayaḥ || 3.306.20 ||
duṣkṛtāni duriṣṭāni durgāṇi viṣamāṇi ca ||
taranti caiva pāpebhyo dhenudānena mānavāḥ || 21 ||
ekāṃ gāṃ daśagurdadyāddaśa dadyācca gośatī ||
śataṃ sahasragurdadyātsarve tulyaphalā hi te || 22 ||
śatavarṣasahasrāṇi tapastaptaṃ suduścaram ||
gobhiḥ pūrvavisṛṣṭābhirgacchema śreṣṭhatāmiti || 23 ||
loke'smindakṣiṇānāñca sarvā gā vayasottarāḥ ||
bhave tāni ca liṃpyema doṣeṇātiparantapāḥ || 24 ||
śakṛttāsāṃ pavitrārthaṃ kurvīrandevamānuṣāḥ ||
tathā sarvāṇi bhūtāni sthāvarāṇi carāṇi ca || || 25 ||
pradātāraśca golokāngaccheyuriti mānadāḥ ||
etaddhanaṃ dadau tāsāṃ sarvameva pitāmahaḥ || 26 ||
surabhirdevadhenuryā tasyāḥ putratvamāgatāḥ ||
dharmo vṛṣabharūpeṇa mahādevadhvajaḥ smṛtaḥ || 27 ||
daśadhenupradānena yatheṣṭāṅgatimāpnuyāt ||
śatañca dattvā dharmajñaḥ śakralokamupāgataḥ || 28 ||
kalpāvaśeṣaṃ sa sukhī modatyapsarasāṃ gaṇaiḥ ||
dattvā sahasraṃ dhenūnāṃ sarvapāpairvimucyate || 29 ||
ukṣṇāṃ ca ye prayacchanti śatena saha yūthapam ||
sarvapāpavinirmuktāste prayāntyamarāvatīm || 3.306.30 ||
kalpāvaśeṣaṃ tridive sukhamupya narottamāḥ ||
mānuṣye dhanasaṃyuktā bhavanti sukhinastataḥ || 31 ||
sahasreṇa gavāṃ sārdhaṃ ye prayacchanti yūthapam ||
alaṃkṛtaṃ yathāśaktyā golokaṃ yānti te narāḥ || 32 ||
ukṣāṇamindriyopetaṃ yastu dadyāddvijātaye ||
daśadhenuphalaṃ tasya nātra kāryā vicāraṇā || 33 ||
goyugaṃ ye prayacchanti sīrayūpayutaṃ dvijāḥ ||
sarvakāmasamṛddhasya prāpnuvanti phalaṃ narāḥ || 34 ||
gobhiścaturbhiḥ saṃyuktaṃ rathaṃ dattvā dvijātaye ||
dhānyairvastrairyutaṃ śaktyā śakralokaṃ vrajanti te || 35 ||
mānuṣyamāsādya tathā bhavanti vasudhādhipāḥ ||
śakaṭasya pradānantu vinā gobhirmahāphalam || || 36 ||
viśeṣeṇa mahābhāgāstasya gobhiryutasya ca ||
sasasyaṃ śakaṭaṃ dattvā nākalokaṃ mahīyate || 37 ||
dattvā tu dhenuṃ homārthe brāhmaṇāyāhitāgnaye ||
godānaphalamāpnoti homadānaphalaṃ tathā || 38 ||
snapanārthaṃ tathā dattvā dhenuṃ rudrasurālaye ||
rudralokamavāpnoti kulañcaiva samuddharet || || 39 ||
dattvā sacailaṃ vṛṣabhaṃ mahādevālaye naraḥ ||
rudralokamavāpnoti kulamuddharate tathā || 3.306.40 ||
dhenūnāṃ kapilā śreṣṭhā vṛṣabhaḥ śveta ucyate ||
śvetantu vṛṣabhaṃ dattvā phalasyānantyamaśnute || 43 ||
dadhikṣīrahitārthāya vāsudevasurālaye ||
dattvā gāṃ madhuparkārthaṃ cānantaṃ phalamāpnuyāt || 42 |||
gopradānaratā yānti golokaṃ mānavottamāḥ ||
sahāpsarobhirmuditā vimānavaramāsthitāḥ || 43 ||
apāmadhastālloko yo tasyopari mahīdharāḥ ||
nāgānāmupariṣṭādbhūḥ pṛthivyupari mānavāḥ || 44 ||
manuṣyalokādūrdhvantu khagānāṃ gatirucyate ||
ākāśasyopari ravirdvāraṃ svargasya tatsmṛtam || || 45||
devalokaḥ parastasmādbrahmalokastataḥ param||
tatropari gavāṃ loko viṣṇulokastataḥ param ||46||
ekastvadho duṣkṛtināṃ karmiṇāṃ pṛthivī matā||
brāhme tapasi yuktānāṃ brahmalokaḥ sanātanaḥ ||47 ||
gavāmeva hi goloko durārohā hi sā gatiḥ ||
ekāntino viṣṇubhaktā viṣṇulokaṃ vrajanti te | 48 ||
gopradānaratā yāṃti golokaṃ sarvakāmadam ||
tatra kāmaphalā vṛkṣā nadyaḥ pāyasakardamāḥ || 49 ||
suvarṇasikatāścānyā vimalāmbuyutāḥ śubhāḥ ||
yathākāmepsitāstatra bhavanti śubhakarmiṇām || 3.306.50 ||
deśā manoharāḥ śubhrā devārāmavibhūṣaṇāḥ ||
divyena nṛtyagītena vāditreṇa manoharāḥ || 51 ||
tatrepsitānāṃ kāmānāmāptirbhavati nityadā ||
hemaśṛṅgīṃ rūpyakhurāṃ muktālāṃgūlabhūṣaṇām || 52 ||
ahatāmbarasaṃvītāṃ tathā kāṃsyopadohinīm ||
trirātraṃ sthaṃḍile suptvā trirātraṃ gorasāśanaḥ || 53 ||
tṛptāntu tarpite dattvā vāsasā saṃvṛtāṃ dvijāḥ ||
sadakṣiṇāṃ mahābhāgāstrirātraṃ gorasaistataḥ || 54 ||
prāṇasaṃdhāraṇaṃ kṛtvā gavāṃ loke mahīyate ||
yāvanti dhenuromāṇi tāvadvarṣāṇi mānavaḥ || 55 ||
dattaivaṃ kapilāṃ viprāḥ prāpnotyabhyadhikaṃ phalam ||
sāyaṃ prātarmanuṣyāṇāmaśanaṃ devanirmitam || 56 ||
tatraikakālamaśnanto dvitīyādaśanānnaraḥ ||
yathoktavidhinā dadyātkrītvā tu kapilāṃ naraḥ || 57 ||
sarvakāmasamṛddhasya yajñasya phalamaśnute ||
gavāṃ lokamavāpnoti kulamuddharati svakam || 58 ||
alaṃkṛtāṃ tato dattvā godānaphalamāpnuyāt ||
ajāmalaṃkṛtāṃ dattvā vahniloke mahīyate || 59 ||
tadeva lokamāpnoti dattvājaṃ vidhivannaraḥ ||
vāruṇaṃ lokamāpnoti dattvā ca mahiṣīṃ naraḥ||3.306.60||
mahiṣasya pradānena yāmye loke mahīyate ||
vāruṇaṃ lokamāpnoti dattvorabhraṃ narottamaḥ || 61|
avipradātā śatadhā tadeva phalamaśnute ||
āraṇyapaśudānena vāyuloke mahīyate || 62 ||
etadeva phalaṃ proktaṃ pradānena ca pakṣiṇām ||
śastrapiṇḍaki jālādirudakādānatastathā || 63 ||
gorasānāṃ pradānena tṛptimāpnotyanuttamām ||
tatra vāśvayuje māse yastu dadyāddvijātiṣu || 64 ||
ārogyaṃ mahadāpnoti yatrayatrābhijāyate ||
tathā dadhnaḥ pradānena maṅgalānyāpnute sadā || 65 ||
kṣīradastṛptimāpnoti sarvaduḥkhairvimucyate ||
kṣīreṇa snapanaṃ kṛtvā harasya prayato naraḥ || 66 ||
mano'bhitāpanirmukto rudraloke mahīyate ||
paramānnapradānena kāmānāpnoti śāśvatān|| 67 ||
māsi māsi tu revatyāṃ brāhmaṇānghṛtapāyasam ||
sadakṣiṇaṃ bhojayitvā rūpamāpnotyanuttamam || 68 ||
ājyantejaḥ samuddiṣṭaṃ cājyaṃ pāpaharaṃ param |
ājyaṃ surāṇāmāhāraścājye lokāḥ pratiṣṭhitāḥ || 69 ||
ājyena liṅgasnapanaṃ naraḥ kṛtvā tu śūlinaḥ ||
sarvaduḥkhavinirmukto rudraloke mahīyate || 3.306.70 ||
alakṣmīśamanaṃ proktaṃ ghṛtasnānaṃ tathā dvijāḥ ||
kalyamutthāya yo gavyaghṛte mukhamathātmanaḥ | 71 ||
paśyate sa jahātyeva kṛtvā pāpamaharniśam ||
alakṣmīrnaśyate cāsya kṣiprameṣā karīṣiṇī || 72 ||
ghṛtadānānnaro yāti svargalokamasaṃśayam ||
māse cāśvayuje nityaṃ ghṛtaṃ yastu prayacchati || 73 ||
kalyamutthāya viprebhyaḥ prīṇayecca tathāśvinau ||
sa rūpaṃ mahadāpnoti tathā rogaiśca mucyate ||
evaṃ mahāphalaṃ dānaṃ gorasānāṃ tathā gavām || 74 ||
prayacchate yaḥ kapilāṃ sacailāṃ kāṃsyopadohāṃ kanakāgraśṛṅgīm ||
taistairguṇaiḥ kāmadughā ca bhūtvā naraṃ pradātāramupaiti sā gauḥ || 75 ||
yāvanti romāṇi bhavanti dhenvāstāvatphalaṃ prāpnute gopradātā ||
putrāṃśca pautrāṃśca kulañca sarvamāsaptamaṃ tārayate paratra||76||
iti śrīviṣṇudharmottare tṛtīye khaṇḍe mārkaṇḍeyavajrasaṃvāde munīnprati haṃsagītāsu godānaphalanirūpaṇo nāma ṣaḍadhikatriśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ || 306 ||
<3.307>
haṃsa uvāca ||
ghṛtadhenupradānañca nibodhata dvijottamāḥ ||
gavyasya sarpiṣaḥ kumbhaṃ pūrṇantu parikalpayet ||1||
kalaśañca tathā pūrṇantasyā vatsaṃ prakalpayet || 
kāṃsyāpidhānasacchannasitavastrayutantathā || 2 ||
hiraṇyagarbhau kartavyāvanuliptau ca bāhyataḥ ||
ikṣuyaṣṭimayānpādānkhurānrūpyamayām̐stathā || 3 ||
sauvarṇe cākṣiṇī kārye śṛṅge cāgurukāṣṭhaje ||
saptadhānyaśca kartavye tasya pārśve pṛthakkṛte || 4 ||
tasya paṭṭaṃ ca kartavyaṃ kambalaṃ ca dvijottamāḥ ||
kuryātturuṣkaraṃ dūre prāṇe hemamayāṃstanān || 5 ||
tadvaccharkarayā jihvāṃ guḍakṣīramayaṃ mukham |||
kṣaumasūtreṇa lāṃgūlaṃ romāṇi sitasarṣapaiḥ || 6 ||
tāmrapatramayaṃ pṛṣṭhaṃ kuryācchraddhāsamanvitaḥ ||
etadviracanaṃ kṛtvā ghṛtakṣīrābhiṣecanaiḥ || 7 ||
harirghṛtārciṣetyevaṃ nāmnā sampūjayedvibhum ||
tatastu gandhapuṣpānnadhūpavastrādibhirnaraḥ || 8 ||
ghṛtena dīpaṃ dadyācca tathaiva ca ghṛtaṃ diśaḥ ||
homaṃ viprā yadā dhenuṃ dadyādviprāya śaktitaḥ || 9 ||
ghṛtācī prīyate vipraṃ kalpayitvā ghṛtārciṣam ||
ekarātrañca tāṃ dattvā ghṛtāhārī bhavennaraḥ || || 3.307.10 ||
vimuktaḥ sarvapāpebhyo lokamāpnoti śāśvatam ||
anena vidhinā snātvā sopavāso naraḥ śuciḥ || 11 ||
sarvakāmasamṛddhasya yajñasya phalamaśnute ||
vimānenārkavarṇena gavāṃ loke mahīyate || 12||
ghṛtakṣīravahā sadyo yatra pāyasakardamāḥ ||
yatra kāmaphalā vṛkṣā yatra siddhistu mānasī || 13 ||
gandharvāpsarasāṃ yatra satataṃ gītanartanam ||
tatrāste suciraṃ loke yāvadindrāścaturdaśa || 14 ||
mānuṣyamāsādya bhavatyarogaḥ śīlena rūpeṇa dhanena yuktaḥ ||
manobhirāmaḥ pramadāpriyaśca dhanānvitaḥ sarvasukhopapannaḥ || 15 ||
iti śrīviṣṇudharmottare tṛ0 kha0 mārkaṇḍeya vajrasaṃvāde munīnprati haṃsagītāsu ghṛtadhenukalpavarṇano nāma saptādhikatriśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ || 307 ||
<3.308>
|| haṃsa uvāca ||
tiladhenuṃ pravakṣyāmi sarvapāpabhayāpahām ||
dhenvākārāntu tāṃ kuryādāvike kambale budhaḥ || 1 ||
tilaṣoḍaśabhiḥ prasthaiścaturbhirvatsameva tu ||
tilaprasāraṇaṃ kuryāddhenvā dhenupramāṇataḥ || 2 ||
tatastu racayeddhenuṃ ratnairvakṣyāmyahaṃ dvijāḥ ||
hemaśṛṅgīṃ rūpyakhurāṃ gandhaghrāṇavatīṃ śubhām || 3 ||
miṣṭānnajihvāṃ kurvīta guḍāsyāṃ sūtrakambalām ||
ikṣupādāṃ tāmrapṛṣṭhāṃ kuryānmuktāphalekṣaṇām || 4 ||
praśastapatraśravaṇāṃ phaladantavatīṃ śubhām ||
sragdāmapuṣpāṃ kurvīta navanītastanānvitām || 5 || 
phalaimarnoharairbhakṣyairmaṇimuktāphalānvitām ||
śubhravastrayugacchannāṃ cārucchatrasamanvitām || 6 ||
kāṃsyopadohanāṃ kṛtvā vāsudevantu pūjayet ||
gandhamālyanamaskāradhūpadīpānnasampadā || 7 ||
juhuyācca tilāneva nāmnā tasyaiva mānavaḥ ||
tameva prīṇayeddadyāddhenuntāmāhitāgnaye || 8 ||
trirātraṃ yastilāhārastiladhenuṃ prayacchati ||
dattvaikarātrañca punastilānatti dvijottamāḥ || 9 ||
cāndrāyaṇādabhyadhikaṃ śastantattilabhakṣaṇam ||
sarvapāpavinirmuktaḥ pitaraṃ sapitāmaham || 3.308.10 ||
tasyāpi pitaraṃ caiva tārayed duṣkṛtānnaraḥ ||
ātmānaṃ putrapautrāṃśca prapautramapi tārayet || 11 ||
suhṛdaṃ vāpyathoddiśya tiladhenuprado naraḥ ||
tārayed duṣkṛtāttañca yamuddiśya prayacchati || 12 ||
yaśca gṛhṇāti vidhivaddīyamānāñca paśyati ||
so'pi pāpavinirmuktaḥ svargaloke mahīyate || 13 ||
mahāpātakasaṃyukto yukto yaścopapātakaiḥ ||
tiladhenupradānena so'pi pāvanamāpnuyāt || 14 ||
salokamāsādya naro vasūnāntatrāpi bhuktvā tridaśendrabhogān||
mānuṣyamāsādya bhavatyarogo dhanānvito rūpasukhopapannaḥ ||15 ||
iti śrīviṣṇudharmottare tṛ0 kha0 mārkaṇḍeyavajrasaṃvāde munīnprati haṃsagītāsu tiladhenudānavidhivarṇano nāmāṣṭottaratriśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ || 308 ||
<3.309>
haṃsa uvāca ||
jaladhenuṃ pravakṣyāmi prītaye jalaśāyinaḥ ||
jalakumbhaṃ dvijaśreṣṭhāḥ suvarṇarajatānvitam || 1 ||
kalpayedratnagarbhañca sthāpayettaṃ yavopari ||
sarvadhānyaistathā grāmyaiḥ samantātparivārayet || 2 ||
sitavastrayugacchannaṃ dūrvāpallava śobhitam ||
kuṣṭhamāṃsīmurośīravālukāmalakairyutam || 3 ||
priyaṅgupatrasahitaṃ sitayajñopavītinam ||
sacchatropānahañcaiva soṣṇīṣaṃ parikalpayet || 4 ||
tilapātrairyutaṃ kṛtvā sahiraṇyaiścaturdiśam ||
dadhyodanayutenātha pātreṇa sthagitaṃ mukhe|| 5 ||
kalpayedevamevantu vāridhānīṃ tu vatsakam ||
upoṣitaḥ samabhyarcya deveśaṃ jalaśāyinam || 6 ||
puṣpadhūpopahāraistu yathāvibhavamāśritaḥ ||
ahatāmbaravītāya dadyādviprāya bhaktimān|| 7 ||
jalaśāyī jagadyoniḥ prīyatāṃ mama keśavaḥ ||
iti coccārya taṃ dadyātprīṇayecca dvijo'pi tam||8||
ahorātroṣito dadyādyathāvidhi mayeritam ||
apakvānnāśinā stheyamahorātramataḥ param || 9 ||
anena vidhinā dattvā jaladhenuṃ dvijottamān ||
sarvāhlādamavāpnoti kāmānāpnoti śāśvatān ||3.309.10||
mokṣamāpnoti sarvebhyastapobhyaḥ sa tathā naraḥ ||
saubhāgyaṃ mahadāpnoti rūpañca paramaṃ tathā ||11||
puruṣaḥ sa tathā yāti yasya yasya ca darśanam ||
karoti paramāhlādaṃ tasya tasya dvijottamāḥ || 12 ||
nityābhitṛpto bhavati tathaiva ca nirāmayaḥ ||
sarvabādhāpraśamanaṃ prāpnoti gatakalmaṣaḥ || || 13 ||
vāruṇaṃ lokamāpnoti sakṛddātā dvijottamaḥ ||
dattvā tāmasakṛdviprā lokamāpnoti vaiṣṇavam || 14 ||
āhlādahetoḥ paramaṃ pavitraṃ dānaṃ mayedaṃ kathitaṃ dvijeṃdrāḥ ||
dhanyaṃ yaśasyaṃ duritāpahāri kāmapradaṃ lokahitaṃ praśastam||15||
iti śrīviṣṇudharmottare tṛ0 kha0 mārkaṇḍeyavajrasaṃvāde munīnprati haṃsagītāsu jaladhenuvarṇano nāma navottaratriśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ||309||
<3.310>
haṃsa uvāca ||
suvarṇamuttamaṃ ratnaṃ dhanānāmapi cottamam||
lohānāmuttamañcaiva sarveṣāmapi cottamam||1||
agnerapatyaṃ prathamaṃ pavitraṃ maṅgalaṃ param||
dhāraṇātsarvapāpaghnaṃ dātustridaśalokadam||2||
mukuṭaiḥ kuṇḍalairhāraiḥ keyūrairaṅgadaistathā||
dhāryate yatsadā devaiḥ pavitratvāddvijottamāḥ||3||
jāmbūnadaṃ taddevānāmindragopakasannibham ||
pitṝṇāñcandraraśmyābhaṃ daityānāṃ śabalopamam || 4||
nāgānāṃ tacchukābhaṃ syānnārīṇāṃ pītamucyate ||
dattvā kṛṣṇalamātrantu naraḥ pāpātpramucyate||5||
dattvā ca māṣakaṃ tasya puṇyaṃ phalamupāśnute ||
suvarṇamāṣakaṃ dattvā sūryasyodayanaṃ prati || 6 ||
hutvāgniṃ sarvapāpebhyo mokṣamāpnotyasaṃśayam ||
tretāgnivratametattu kīrtitaṃ tasya nityadā || 7 ||
dinamadhyagate sūrye hutvāgniṃ yaḥ prayacchati ||
tasyāgninityadāne ca mahatphalamudāhṛtam || 8 ||
sūryasyāstamaye vahniṃ hutvā tadvai prayacchati ||
puṇyaṃ mahatsamāpnoti nityadānena vai dvijāḥ || 9 ||
trisandhyaṃ kanakaṃ yastu hutvā vahniṃ prayacchati ||
na sa lipyeta pāpena padmapatramivāmbhasā || 3.310.10 ||
sakṛtkṛṣṇaladānena svarge varṣaṃ sukhī vaset ||
tathā ca pañcavarṣāṇi svarge dattvaiva māṣakam || 11 ||
yaḥ suvarṇaṃ suvarṇasya brāhmaṇāya prayacchati ||
nirdoṣaḥ syātsa nāke tu modatyabdaśataṃ dhruvam || 12 ||
ātmatulyaṃ suvarṇasya yaḥ prayacchati vai dvijāḥ ||
sarvapāpavinirmukto yatheṣṭāṅgatimāpnuyāt || 13 ||
brahmaghno vā surāpo vā steno vā gurutalpagaḥ ||
sarvapātakayukto'pi tena dānena mucyate || 14 ||
śraddhānāmuttamañcaitaddānānāmuttamottamam ||
rukmaṃ dattvā brāhmaṇāya sukhī sarvatra jāyate || 15 ||
cirānnākaparibhraṣṭo rūpasaubhāgyasaṃyutaḥ ||
tāsāṃ kule tu susphīte guṇaiḥ sarvairyuto naraḥ || 16 ||
rukmapātrapradānena putrī bhavati mānavaḥ ||
sarveṣāmeva kāmānāṃ yaśasastridivasya ca || 17 ||
rūpyado rūpamāpnoti bhogamāpnoti tāmradaḥ ||
lohārakūṭayordānātkupyamāpnotyasaṃśayam || 18 || 
trapusīsakayordānādvahnivṛddhimavāpnuyāt ||
yatheṣṭaṃ lohapātrañca yaḥ pradadyāddvijātaye || 19 ||
pātraṃ bhavati kāmānāṃ viśeṣaścāsya lohataḥ ||
śilpabhāṇḍāni yo dadyāllohāni vidhivaddvijāḥ || 3.310.20 ||
vidyādānaphalaṃ tasya kathitaṃ nātra saṃśayaḥ ||
carmadātā naro nityaṃ rakṣāṃ samadhigacchati || 21 ||
āyudhānāṃ pradānena ripunāśamavāpnuyāt ||
rājaliṅgapradānena rājā bhavati bhūtale || 22 ||
ratnānāṃ ca pradānena rājaiveha prajāyate ||
ratnadānasya yallokaṃ tadbhuktvā suciraṃ dvijāḥ || 23 ||
vajradaḥ śakralokantu padmarāgaprado raveḥ ||
sadyo marakataṃ dattvā viṣṇuloke mahīyate || 24 ||
muktāphalapradānena vāruṇaṃ lokamaśnute ||
vaidūryasya pradānena dvīpānāṃ sapta modate || 25 ||
ratnānyanyāni dattvā ca rājarājasya ca dvijāḥ ||
sarveṣāmeva ratnānāṃ kanakaṃ śreṣṭhamucyate || 26||
dhāraṇaṃ kīrtanaṃ dānaṃ kanakasya praśasyate ||
duḥsvapraśamanaṃ dānaṃ duriṣṭasya ca nāśanam || 27 ||
paraṃ pāpaharaṃ jñeyamalakṣmīśamanaṃ tathā ||
dattvā suvarṇaṃ lokeṣu yatheṣṭeṣu ca modate ||28||
vimānenārkavarṇena haṃsayuktena bhāsvatā ||
devarāmāgaṇāḍhyena kāmagena vihāyasā|||||
bhuktvā divi ciraṃ bhogānmānuṣye sukhamāpnuyāt ||
agnerapatyaṃ prathamaṃ suvarṇaṃ bhūrvaiṣṇavī brahmasutāśca gāvaḥ ||
sarvaṃ hi dattaṃ bhavatīha tena yaḥ kāñcanaṃ gāñca mahīñca dadyāt || 3.310.30 ||
iti śrīviṣṇudharmottare tṛtīyakhaṇḍe mārkaṇḍeyavajrasaṃvāde munīnprati haṃsagītāsu suvarṇadānavidhinirūpaṇo nāma daśādhikatriśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ || 310 ||
<3.311>
haṃsa uvāca ||
narastvāsanadānena sthānaṃ sarvatra vindati ||
śayyādānena cāpnoti bhāryāṃ brāhmaṇasattamāḥ || 1 ||
dattvā dvijāya śayanaṃ svāstīrṇaṃ sottaracchadam ||
kule mahati sambhūtāṃ rūpadraviṇasaṃyutām ||2||
tathā pakṣavatīṃ bhavyāṃ prāpnoti vaśagāṃ tathā ||
anenaiva pradānena bhāryā patimavāpnuyāt || 3 ||
vitānakapradānena sarvapāpaiḥ pramucyate ||
chatradaśca tathā viprā nātra kāryā vicāraṇā || 4||
chatraṃ śataśalākaṃ tu dattvaivātapavāraṇam ||
sarvatāpavini rmuktaḥ svargaloke mahīyate || 5 ||
yāmyaṃ mārgaṃ tathā yāti sukhena dvijasattamāḥ ||
upānahe tathā dattvā ślakṣṇe snehasamanvite || 6 ||
rathamaśvatarī yuktaṃ tridivaṃ pratipadyate||
sarvapāpavinirmukto yāmyamārgaṃ sukhaṃ vrajet || 7 ||
tālavṛntapradānena sarvapāpaiḥ pramucyate ||
pādukānāṃ pradānena gatimāpnoti śobhanām || 8 ||
pādapīṭhapradānena sthānaṃ sarvatra vindati ||
sthānameva tathāpnoti daṇḍaṃ dattvā dvijātaye || 9 ||
yajñopavītadānena vastradānaphalaṃ labhet ||
uṣṇīṣadānasya tathā phalametadudāhṛtam || 3.311.10 ||
dantakāṣṭhapradānena saubhāgyaṃ mahadāpnuyāt ||
mṛttikāyāḥ pradānena śuciḥ sarvatra jāyate || 11 ||
śaucasnānodakaṃ dattvā virogastvabhijāyate ||
roganāśamavāpnoti tathābhyaṅgapradaḥ sadā || 12 ||
tathaivauṣadhadānena roganāśamupāśnute ||
sthānīyāni sugandhīni dattvā saubhāgyamaśnute || 13 ||
anulepanadānena rūpavānabhijāyate ||
kāyāgnidīptaṃ prākāśyaṃ gatiñcaiva tathottamām || 14 ||
yastu saṃsādhanārthāya brāhmaṇāya prayacchati ||
sa yāti vahnisālokyaṃ śatrunāśaṃ ca vindati || 15 ||
taijasāni tu pātrāṇi dattvā salavaṇāni ca ||
pātratāmeti kāmānāṃ lāvaṇyaṃ cāpnuyānmahat || 16 ||
taijasāni tu pātrāṇi satailāni prayacchataḥ ||
ārogyamuttamaṃ proktaṃ lāvaṇyamapi cottamam || 17 ||
taijasāni tu pātrāṇi samadhūni prayacchataḥ ||
lāvaṇyamuttamaṃ proktaṃ saubhāgyamapi cottamam || || 18 ||
naraścandanadānena sarvapāpaiḥ pramucyate ||
tathaivāgurudānena yaśasā bhuvi rājate || 19 ||
saubhāgyakārakaṃ proktaṃ pradānaṃ kuṅkumasya tu ||
tathā karpūradānena sarvānkāmānavāpnuyāt || 3.311.20 ||
mṛgacarmapradānena yaśasā bhuvi rājate ||
dattvā jātīphalaṃ viprāḥ saphalāṃ vindate kriyām || || 21 ||
snānānāṃ mukhavāsānāṃ dhūpānāṃ ca pradāyakāḥ ||
loke prayānti priyatāṃ bhavanti ca sugandhinaḥ || 22 ||
dattvā pūgaphalānviprāḥ saphalāṃ vindate kriyām ||
tāmbūlasya pradānena saubhāgyamapi vindati || 23 ||
samudrajānāṃ bhāṇḍānāṃ śaṅkhādīnāṃ pradāyakaḥ ||
pātrī bhavati kāmānāṃ yaśasaśca na saṃśayaḥ || 24 ||
kaṅkatasya pradānena parāṃ vācaṃ pramuñcati ||
śayyāpradāno lokeṣu tathā sthānakaraṃ param || 25 ||
arthine yadabhīṣṭaṃ syātsvalpaṃ vā yadi vā bahu ||
taddattvā puṇyamāpnoti rājasūyāśvamedhayoḥ || 26 ||
kimcchikaiḥ pūjakairbrāhmaṇānāṃ lokaṃ gatvā brahmaṇo brāhmaṇebhyaḥ ||
devairvipraiḥ pūjyamānaḥ sukhī syāttasmāddeyaṃ prārthitaṃ brāhmaṇebhyaḥ || 27 ||
iti śrīviṣṇudharmottare tṛtīyakhaṇḍe mārkaṇḍevajrasaṃvāde munīnprati haṃsagītāsvanekavastudānapraśaṃsāvarṇano nāmaikādaśādhikatriśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ || 311 ||
<3.312>
|| haṃsa uvāca || ||
nṛvāhānpuruṣo yastu dvije samyakprayacchati ||
aśvamedhaphalaṃ tasya kathitaṃ dvijasattamāḥ || 1 ||
śibikāyāḥ pradānena vahniṣṭomaphalaṃ tathā ||
dāsaṃ karmakaraṃ dattvā gosavasya phalaṃ labhet || 2 ||
sa yāti śakrasālokyaṃ yastu yodhaṃ prayacchati ||
yastu karmakarīṃ dāsīṃ brāhmaṇāya prayacchati || 3 ||
lokāstu subhagāstasya vasūnāṃ samudāhṛtāḥ ||
taruṇīṃ rūpasampannāṃ dāsīṃ yastu prayacchati || 4 ||
sopsarobhirmudāyuktaḥ krīḍate nandane vane ||
uṣṭraṃ vā gardabhaṃ viprā yo yacchati dvijātaye ||5||
alakāṃ sa samāsādya yakṣendraiḥ saha modate ||
dānānāmuttamaṃ proktaṃ pradānaṃ turagasya tu ||6 ||
vaḍavāyāḥ pradānena tathā bahuphalaṃ smṛtam ||
turaṅgaṃ ye prayacchaṃti sūryalokaṃ vrajanti te || 7 ||
yāvanti tasya romāṇi tāvadvarṣaśatāni ca ||
śuklaṃ turaṅgamaṃ dattvā phalaṃ daśaguṇaṃ labhet || 8 ||
gauryathā kapilā śreṣṭhā tathaiva turagasya tu ||
turaṅgamaṃ saparyāṇaṃ sālaṅkāraṃ prayacchati || 9 ||
puṇḍarīkaphalaṃ proktaṃ nātra kāryā vicāraṇā ||
caturbhisturagairyuktaṃ sarvopakaraṇairyutam || 3.312.10 ||
rathaṃ dvijāye dattvā rājasūyaphalaṃ labhet ||
pradāya kariṇīṃ samyagetadeva phalaṃ labhet || 11 ||
kuñjarasya pradānena svargaloke mahīyate ||
rājā bhavati lokesminyaśovikramasaṃyutaḥ || 12 ||
suvarṇakakṣyaṃ mātaṅgaṃ dattvā viprāya vai dvijāḥ ||
rājasūyāśvamedhābhyāṃ phalaṃ vindatyasaṃśayam|| 13 ||
caturbhiḥ kuñjarairyuktaṃ rathaṃ dattvā dvijātaye ||
samagrabhūmi dānasya phalaṃ prāpnotyasaṃśayam || 14||
bhūmiḥ saśailā savanā satoyā dattā hi loke bhavatīha tena ||
iṣṭām̐śca lokānpuruṣaḥ prayāti sampūjyamānastridaśairmahātmā || 15 ||
iti śrīviṣṇudharmottare tṛtīyakhaṇḍe mārkaṇḍeyavajrasaṃvāde munīnprati haṃsagītāsu vāhanadānavidhivarṇano nāma dvādaśādhikatriśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ || 312 ||
<3.313>
haṃsa uvāca ||
dānānāmuttamaṃ loke vastradānaṃ prakīrtitam ||
vāso hi sarvadaivatyaṃ sarvaprāyojyamaśnute ||1 ||
vastradātā suveśaḥ syādrūpadraviṇasaṃyutaḥ ||
yukto lāvaṇyasaubhāgyairvirogaśca tathā dvijāḥ || 2 ||
nīlaṃ raktaṃ tathā jīrṇaṃ na deyaṃ brāhmaṇasya tu ||
deyaṃ varañcāṅgirasaṃ kṣaumaṃ dattvā bṛhaspateḥ ||3|
dattvā kārpāsikaṃ vastraṃ svargaloke mahīyate ||
dattvā saromaṃ tadapi phalaṃ daśaguṇaṃ bhavet || 4||
āvikaṃ vasanaṃ dattvā bhṛgūṇāṃ lokamāpnuyāt ||
chāgaṃ dattvā cāṅgirasaṃ kṣaumaṃ dattvā bṛhaspateḥ || 5 ||
vasūnāṃ lokamāpnoti kuśakauśeyavāsasī| ||
krimijañca tato dattvā somaloke mahīyate || 6 ||
agniṣṭomamavāpnoti dattvaiva mṛgalomikām ||
dattvā valkalajaṃ vāso vasūnāṃ lokamāpnuyāt || 7 ||
sumerakadalyādīnāṃ dattvā caiva tathājinam ||
vāravāṇaṃ śirastrāṇaṃ hastatrāṇaṃ tathaiva ca || 8 ||
pādatrāṇaṃ ca dadataḥ pṛthagyaphalaṃ smṛtam ||
vastradānaṃ pavitrantu pitṝṇāmapi tāraṇam ||9||
kausuṃbharaktaṃ dattvā tu saubhāgyaṃ mahadāpnuyāt ||
dānātkuṅkumaraktasya lāvaṇyaṃ mahadāpnuyāt ||3.313.10 ||
raṅgairanyairanīlaiśca raktaṃ dattvā sukhī bhavet ||
yathāyathā tu vastrasya bhavatīha maharghatā || 11 ||
yathāyathā śubhratā ca tathā puṇyaphalaṃ labhet||
anyeṣāmapi dānānāmetadeva prakīrtitam || 12 ||
sarvado vastradaḥ prokto yataḥ sarvatra vastravān ||
pūjāmāpnoti dharmajñāstaddhi tasmādviśiṣyate || 13 ||
vastradaḥ suciraṃ kālaṃ bhuktvā devasukhāndivi ||
vimānenārkavarṇena haṃsayuktena bhāsvatā || 14 ||
mānuṣyamāsādya bhavatyarogaḥ śaucānvitaḥ saukhyaparo vinītaḥ ||
janābhirāmaśca tathā suveśaḥ pūjyaśca loke viduṣāṃ sadaiva || 15 ||
iti śrīviṣṇudharmottare tṛtīyakhaṇḍe bhārkaṇḍeyavajra saṃvāde munīnprati haṃsagītāsu vastradānapraśaṃsāvarṇano nāma trayodaśādhikatriśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ || 313 ||
<3.314>
haṃsa uvāca ||
dhānyānāmuttamaṃ dānaṃ kathitaṃ dvijapuṅgavāḥ ||
dhānyebhyo'pi paraṃ dhānyaṃ raktaśāliḥ prakīrtitaḥ || 1 ||
raktaśāliṃ naro dattvā sūryaloke mahīyate ||
dattvā tathā sugandhām̐śca gandharvaiḥ saha modate || 2 ||
kalamānāṃ pradānena śakraloke mahīyate ||
mahāśāleḥ pradānena vasūnāṃ lokamāpnuyāt || 3 ||
kāṣṭhaśāliṃ tathā dattvā alakāyāṃ prasīdati ||
vrīhīnāṃ cānyadhānyānāṃ dānātsvargamavāpnuyāt || 4 ||
yava pradānena naraḥ śakraloke mahīyate ||
tathā godhūmadānena vasūnāṃ lokamāpnuyāt || 5 ||
priyo bhavati lokasya priyaṅguṃ yaḥ prayacchati ||
dadāti yastu śyāmākaṃ tasya prīyanti devatāḥ || 6 ||
anyeṣāṃ śūkadhānyānāṃ pradānābhirato naraḥ ||
svargalokamavāpnoti nātra kāryā vicāraṇā || 7 ||
mudgadaḥ śakralokantu yamalokantu māṣadaḥ ||
yatheṣṭaṃ lokamāpnoti tathā viprāstilapradaḥ || 8 ||
ṣaṣṭikānāṃ pradānena lokaṃ nairṛtakaṃ labhet ||
sacīṇacaṇakaṃ dattvā lokaṃ vāruṇamāpnuyāt || 9 ||
vāyavyañca masūrāṇi rājamāṣāṇi dhānadam ||
anyeṣāṃ śiṃbidhānyānāṃ dānātsvargamavāpnuyāt || 3.314.10 ||
ikṣumṛdvīkayordānātparaṃ saubhāgyamāpnuyāt ||
guḍapradastathārogyaṃ sitādaḥ kāmamīpsitam || 11 ||
phāṇitasya pradānena parāṃ tṛptimavāpnuyāt ||
khaṇḍapradaśca saubhāgyaṃ sarvānkāmānmadhupradaḥ || 12 ||
ghṛtado jīvitaṃ dīrghaṃ cārogyaṃ tailadastathā||
sarvānkāmānavāpnoti lavaṇaṃ yaḥ prayacchati || 13 ||
saktudastṛptimāpnoti sikatādastathā śriyam ||
akīrtitānāmanyeṣāṃ dānātsvargamavāpnuyāt || 14 ||
pakvānnadānātphaladaṃ nṛloke śuṣkānnamuktaṃ hi tadāpya yasmāt||
yajanti yajñaiḥ satataṃ dvijendrāstasmācca tatpuṇyatamaṃ pradiṣṭam || 15 ||
iti śrīviṣṇudharmottare tṛtīyakhaṇḍe mārkaṇḍeyavajrasaṃvāde munīnprati haṃsagītāsu dhānyadānapraśaṃsā nāma caturdaśādhikatriśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ || 314 ||
<3.315>
|| haṃsa uvāca ||
sarveṣāmeva dānānāmannadānaṃ viśiṣyate ||
annadānātparaṃ dānaṃ na bhūtaṃ na bhaviṣyati ||1||
nātra pātraparīkṣā syānna kālaniyamastathā ||
na ca deśaḥ parīkṣyo'tra deyamannaṃ sadaiva tat||2||
śvabhyaśca śvapacebhyaśca patitebhstathaiva ca ||
krimikīṭapataṅgānāṃ deyamannaṃ sadaiva tu ||3||
annaṃ hi jīvitaṃ loke prāṇāścānnanibandhanāḥ ||
annadaḥ prāṇado loke sarvadaśca tathānnadaḥ || 4||
bhuktvā tathā ca yasyānnaṃ santatiḥ syāddvijottamāḥ ||
jñātavyaṃ brāhmaṇaśreṣṭhā yasyānnaṃ tasya santatiḥ||5||
dātavyaṃ sarvavarṇebhyo bhoktavyaṃ brāhmaṇasya ca ||
yasyānnenodarasthena brāhmaṇo mriyate dvijāḥ ||6||
tāntu yonimavāpnoti nātra kāryā vicāraṇā ||
bhakṣyadaḥ svargamāpnoti śakralokantu bhojyadaḥ || 7 ||
lehyadopsarasāṃ lokaṃ vasūnāmapi coṣyadaḥ ||
vāruṇaṃ lokamāpnoti tathā pānaprado naraḥ || 8 ||
pānakāni sugandhīni śītalāni prayacchataḥ ||
sarvakāmasamṛddhaḥ syānnātra kāryā vicāraṇā || 9 ||
paramānnapradānena tṛptirbhavati śāśvatī ||
pipāsamāno mriyate gavāṃ loke mahīyate ||3.315.10||
yo dadyādaparikliṣṭamannamadhvani vartate ||
śrāntāyādṛṣṭapūrvāya tasya puṇya phalaṃ mahat || 11 ||
sahasrapariveṣyantu narakaṃ sa na gacchati ||
vimānenārkavarṇena svargaloke mahīyate || 12||
apahanyāttathā viprā ekasyārttasya yatkṣudhā ||
tenāsau karmaṇā pretya mahatphalamupāśnute||13||
yadyadiṣṭatamaṃ loke yaścāsya dayitaṃ gṛhe||
tattadguṇavate deyaṃ tadevākṣayyamicchatā|| 14 ||
dānānyanekāni phalañca teṣāṃ bahuprakāraṃ puruṣaḥ samīkṣya ||
śraddhānvito brāhmaṇasātprakuryādbhūyastadevāptamanā nṛloke || 15 ||
iti śrīviṣṇudharmottare tṛ0 khaṇḍe mārkaṇḍeyavajrasaṃvāde munīnprati haṃsagītāsvannadānapraśaṃsā nāma pañcadaśādhikatriśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ || 315 ||
<3.316>
|| ṛṣaya ūcuḥ ||
kasya dānasya kaḥ pātraṃ tadvadasva mahāmate ||
kasmindeśe tathā kāle kiñcid dattaṃ mahāphalam || 1 ||
|| haṃsa uvāca || ||
na dānaṃ yaśase dadyānna dadyānnopakāriṇe ||
na nṛtyagītaśīlebhyo nāstikebhyaśca dhārmikaḥ || 2 ||
pāpaśīle na dātavyaṃ kadācidapi kiñcana ||
pāpaśīle tu yaddattaṃ taddātudoṣamāvahet || 3 ||
dhanadastena pāpena śīghrameveha yujyate ||
tasmātprāpya dhanaṃ puṇyaṃ puṇyaśīlaṃ samācaret || 4 ||
dhanadastena puṇyena yogamāpnotyasaṃśayam ||
annadānena kartavyaṃ pātrāvekṣaṇamaṇvapi || 5 ||
annaṃ sarvatra dātavyaṃ dharmakāmena vai dvijāḥ ||
sadoṣepi tu nirdoṣaṃ saguṇetiguṇāvaham || 6 ||
tasmātsarvaprayatnena deyamannaṃ sadaiva tu ||
vidyādhyayanasaktānāmannadānaṃ mahāphalam || 7 ||
śubhānāmapyathānyeṣāṃ karmaṇāṃ dvijapuṅgavāḥ ||
yasyānnamupayuñjāno naraḥ karmāṇi sādhayet || 8 ||
karma sādhayataḥ puṇyamannadātā tathāśnute ||
dattvā nṛpatibhītānāṃ dhanināñca tathā dhanam || 9 ||
taskarebhyaśca bhītānāṃ phalamakṣayamaśnute ||
yiyakṣatā tathā dattaṃ vyasane haṃtumicchatā || 3.316.10 ||
duḥkhānvitānāṃ dīnānāñcākṣayaṃ tatprakīrtitam ||
vivāhamekhalābandhapratiṣṭhādiṣu karmasu ||11||
āpanneṣu ca yaddattaṃ tadakṣayyamudāhṛtam ||
yajñopakaraṇaṃ dravyaṃ kṣattriye dvijapuṅgavāḥ || 12 ||
puṇyopayogi tadvaiśye śūdre śilpopajīvinam ||
yasyopayogi yaddravyaṃ deyaṃ tasyaiva tadbhavet || 13 ||
yenayena ca bhāṇḍena yasya vṛttirudāhṛtā ||
tattu tasyaiva dātavyaṃ puṇyakāmena dhīmatā || 14 ||
daṇḍaṃ kṛṣṇājinañcaiva tathā viprāḥ kamaṇḍalum ||
cīraṃ puṇyamavāpnoti dattvaitāṃ brahmacāriṇe || 15 ||
vastraṃ śayyāsanaṃ dhānyaṃ dravyaṃ veśma paricchadam ||
gṛhasthāya tu taddattaṃ jñeyaṃ bahuphalaṃ sadā || 16 ||
nīvāravalkalaṃ śākaṃ phalamūlaṃ ca gorasam ||
vānaprasthāya taddattamanantaṃ parikīrtitam || 17 ||
bhikṣāpradānaṃ yataye pātradānaṃ tathā hitam ||
saubhāgyadravya tāmbūlaraktavastrādikaṃ striyaḥ || 18 ||
strīṇāṃ pradānaṃ dātavyaṃ bhartṛhaste tu nānyathā ||
proktaṃ saṃgrahaṇaṃ hyetadguptaṃ bhartuḥ prayacchataḥ || 19 ||
annaṃ pratiśrayaṃ cobhau pānthe dattaṃ mahāphalam ||
vivāhādikriyākāle tatkriyāsiddhikārakam || 3.316.20 ||
yaḥ prayacchati dharmajñāḥ sośvamedhamavāpnuyāt||
āturebhyo dhanaṃ dattvā dānaṃ bahuphalaṃ labhet || 21 ||
bāle krīḍanakaṃ dattvā miṣṭamannaṃ tathaiva ca ||
phalaṃ manoharaṃ vāpi agniṣṭomaphalaṃ labhet ||22 ||
miṣṭānnaṃ mānavo dattvā miṣṭānnāni tu kāṃkṣatām||
akṣayaṃ phalamāpnoti svargalokaṃ ca gacchati || 23 ||
śrīvihīne tathā dattvā bhojanaṃ dvijasattamāḥ ||
vastraṃ śubhaṃ vā dharmajñāḥ puṇyaṃ mahadupāśnute || 24 ||
kṛpāsthānaparo viprā vidyutaḥ puruṣaḥ śriyā ||
tasyānukampā katarvyā satāṃ vartmani tiṣṭhataḥ || 25 ||
kārye samudyamaṃ kṛtvā pareṣāṃ samupasthite ||
akṣayaṃ phalamāpnoti nātra kāryā vicāraṇā ||26 ||
yavasānāṃ pradānena dhenumatsu dvijātiṣu ||
lavaṇānāñca dharmajñā phalamakṣayamaśnute ||27||
prasaveṣu tadā dattaṃ vyasanānyubhaye tataḥ ||
taddānamakṣayaṃ proktaṃ puruṣasya vipaścitaḥ ||28||
dattvā brāhmaṇaśārdūlā jalamātramathārthine ||
phalamakṣayamāpnoti nātra kāryā vicāraṇā ||29||
kleśaṃ vinā dattamathārthine tu dānaṃ hi loke phaladaṃ niruktam ||
abhyarthitenātha tato na kāryā vimānanā tvarthijanasya śaktyā||3.316.30||
iti śrīviṣṇudharmottare tṛtīyakhaṇḍe mārkaṇḍeyavajrasaṃvāde munīnprati haṃsagītāsu pātraviśeṣeṇa dānaviśeṣavarṇano nāma ṣoḍaśādhikatriśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ ||316||
<3.317>
ṛṣaya ūcuḥ ||
dānaṃ bahuphalaṃ kāle yasminyatparikīrtitam ||
tattvamasmākamācakṣva tvaṃ hi sarvaviducyase || 1||
haṃsa uvāca ||
saṃvatsare tathā dānaṃ tilasya ca mahāphalam ||
paripūrve tathā dānaṃ yavānāṃ dvijasattamāḥ || 2 ||
iḍāpūrvennavastrāṇāṃ dhānyānāṃ cānupūrvake ||
udāsaṃvatsare dānaṃ rajatasya mahāphalam ||3||
uttare tvayane proktaṃ vastradānaṃ phalapradam ||
snānānulepanādīnāṃ vasante dvijapuṅgavāḥ||4||
pānakānāṃ tathā grīṣme chatrāṇāṃ tadanantare ||
śaradyannasya dharmajñā vastrāṇāmapi haimane||5||
vahnidānaṃ naraḥ kṛtvā sūryasyodayanaṃ prati ||
śiśire satataṃ vahniṃ tarpayitvā tathā tilaiḥ ||6 ||
kulmāṣaṃ saghṛtaṃ dattvā yathāśaktyā dvijātiṣu ||
kāyāgnidīptiṃ prākāśyaṃ śatrunāśañca vindati ||7|| 
saktūnsitākhaṇḍayutāṃstrapusorvārukānvitān ||
jalakumbhoparikṛtānakṣayaṃ phalamaśnute ||8||
madhyāhne prāvṛṣi snātaḥ satataṃ dvijapuṅgavāḥ ||
tilapradānānmāghe tu prāpyalokaṃ sa gacchati || 9||
priyaṅguṃ phālgune dattvā priyo bhavati bhūtale ||
caitre citrāṇi vastrāṇi saubhāgyaṃ mahadaśnute||3.317.10||
apūpānāṃ pradānena vaiśākhe svargamaśnute ||
chatradānaṃ tathā jyeṣṭhe sarvānkāmānupāśnute ||
dattvā copānahau svargañcāṣāḍhe dhruvamaśnute ||11||
śrāvaṇe vastradānasya kīrtitaṃ sumahatphalam ||
proṣṭhapāde tathā māsi pradānātphāṇitasya ca || 12 ||
āśvine ghṛtadānena rūpavānabhi jāyate ||
kārtike dīpadānena sarvatraujasyamaśnuyāt || 13 ||
lavaṇaṃ mārgaśīrṣe tu dattvā saubhāgyamaśnute ||
pauṣe kanakadānena parāṃ puṣṭiṃ tathaiva ca || 14 ||
puṣpāṇāṃ ca site pakṣe dānaṃ lakṣmīkaraṃ smṛtam ||
phalānāṃ ca tathā dānaṃ kṛṣṇapakṣe mahāphalam||15||
lavaṇājyaguḍopetamapūpaṃ sūryavāsare ||
sahiraṇyaṃ naro dattvā na rogaistvabhibhūyate ||16 ||
evaṃvidhaṃ cendudine dadatsaubhāgyamāpnuyāt ||
kāṣṭhadānaṃ bhaumadine śatrunāśamavāpnuyāt || 17 ||
bodhe krīḍanakānāñca dānaṃ bāleṣu śasyate ||
jīvāhni vastradānena parāṃ puṣṭiṃ samaśnute || 16 ||
sarvatra ratimāpnoti śukre dattvā ratistriyam ||
abhyaṅgaṃ sauradivase dattvā jīvitamāpnuyāt ||19||
kṛttikāsu suvarṇasya dānaṃ bahuphalaṃ smṛtam ||
raktavastrasya rohiṇyāṃ saumyabhe lavaṇasya ca|3.317.20||
kṛsarasya tathārdrāyāṃ cāditye rajatasya ca ||
ghṛtasya ca tathā puṣye gandhānāmatha sarpabhe || 21 ||
tilānāñca maghāyoge priyaṅgorbhagadaivate ||
āryamṇe cāpyapūpānāṃ sāvitre pāyasasya ca || 22 ||
citrāyāṃ citravastrāṇāṃ saktūnāṃ vāyudaivate ||
aindrāgne caiva lohānāṃ maitre mālyaphalasya ca ||23||
chatrasya ca tathā śāke mūle mūlaphalasya ca ||
himasya madhuyuktasya dānamāpye mahāphalam || 24 ||
viśveśvarennadānasya śravaṇe vasanasya ca ||
dhānyasya vāsave viprā vāruṇe cauṣadhasya ca ||25||
ajapāde ca bījānāṃ sasyānāṃ tadanantare ||
gorasānāṃ tathā pauṣṇe snānānāmatha cāśvine || || 26 ||
tilānāñca tathā dānaṃ bharaṇīṣu mahāphalam ||
pratipadyatha puṣpāṇāṃ dvitīyāyāṃ ghṛtasya ca || 27 ||
tṛtīyāyāṃ tu vastrāṇāṃ caturthyāṃ kanakasya tu ||
pañcamyāntu phalānāṃ vai ṣaṣṭhyāṃ snānasya mānadāḥ ||28||
saptamyāṃ cāpyapūpānāmaṣṭamyāñca guḍasya ca ||
kulmāṣasya navamyāntu daśamyāṃ rajatasya ca || 29 ||
ekādaśyāṃ suvarṇasya dvādaśyāṃ vasanasya ca ||
trayodaśyāṃ sugandhānāṃ sitāyāstadanantaram || 3.317.30 ||
dānañca paramānnasya pañcadaśyāṃ mahāphalam ||
vaiśākhaśuklapakṣe tu tṛtīyāyāṃ dvijottamāḥ || 31 ||
yaddadāti naraśreṣṭhāstattadakṣayyamaśnute ||
viśeṣeṇa tathā dānamakṣatānāṃ mahāphalam || 32 ||
māghe śravaṇasaṃyuktā yuktā syāddvādaśī yadā ||
tiladānaṃ mahatpuṇyaṃ vināpi śravaṇena tu || 33 ||
māsi bhādrapade śukle navamyāṃ dvijapuṅgavāḥ ||
godhūmabhaktāṃ saguḍāṃ dattvā puṇyamavāpnuyāt || 34 ||
etāni dānāni ca kālayoge proktāni puṇyāni mayā dvijendrāḥ ||
deyāni sarvāṇi vināpi yogādyogena puṇyaṃ tvadhikaṃ niruktam || 35 ||
iti śrīviṣṇudharmottare tṛ0 kha0 mārkaṇḍeyavajrasaṃvāde munīnprati haṃsagītāsu kālayoge dānavidhivarṇano nāma saptadaśādhikatriśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ| || 317 ||
<3.318>
ṛṣaya ūcuḥ ||
vrataṃ nakṣatrasatrākhyaṃ sadānaṃ kathayasva naḥ ||
sarvadharmabhṛtāṃ śreṣṭha pratyakṣāmaladarśana || 1 ||
haṃsa uvāca ||
kṛttikāsu mahābhāgāḥ pāyasena sa sarpiṣā ||
saṃtarpya brāhmaṇānsādhūm̐llokānprāpnotyanuttamān || 2 ||
rohiṇyāṃ prathitairmāsairmāṣairannena sarpiṣā ||
payonupānaṃ dātavyaṃ cānṛṇārthaṃ dvijātaye || 3 ||
dogdhrīṃ dattvā savatsāṃ tu nakṣatre somadaivate ||
gaccheta mānuṣām̐llokānsvargavāsamanuttamam || 4 ||
ārdrāyāṃ kṛsaraṃ dattvā tailamiśramupoṣitaḥ ||
narastarati durgāṇi kṣuradhārāṃstu parvatān || 5 ||
pūpānpunarvasau dattvā tathaivānnañca śobhanam ||
yaśasvī rūpasaṃpanno bahvanno jāyate kule || 6 ||
puṣye tu kanakaṃ dattvā kṛtaṃ vākṛtameva ca ||
anālokeṣu lokeṣu somavatsa virājate || 7 ||
aśleṣāsu ca yo rūpyaṃ vṛṣabhaṃ vā prayacchati ||
sa sarvabhayanirmuktaḥ śubhaṃ cāpyadhigacchati || 8 ||
maghāsu tilapūrṇāni varṇamānāni mānavaḥ ||
pradāya putrapaśumāniha pretya ca modate || 9 ||
phālgunīpūrvasamaye brāhmaṇānāmupoṣitaḥ ||
bhakṣyaṃ phāṇitasaṃyuktaṃ dattvā saubhāgyamṛcchati ||3.318.10||
ghṛtakṣīrasamāyuktaṃ vidhivatṣāṣṭikaudanam ||
uttarāviṣaye dattvā svagarloke mahīyate || 11 ||
yadyatpradīyate dānaṃ cottarāviṣaye naraiḥ ||
mahāphalamatītañca bhavatīti viniścayaḥ || 12 ||
haste hastirathaṃ dattvā caturyuktamupoṣitaḥ ||
narastarati durgāṇi kṣuradhārāṃśca parvatān || 13 ||
citrāyāṃ vṛṣabhaṃ dattvā puṇyagandhāṃśca mānavaḥ ||
caratyapsarasāṃ loke ramate nandane vane || 14 ||
svātīṣvatha dhanaṃ dattvā yadiṣṭatamamātmanaḥ ||
prāpnoti lokānsa śubhānihaiva ca mahadyaśaḥ || 15 ||
viśākhāyāmanaḍvāhaṃ dhenuṃ dattvā ca dugdhadām ||
saprāsaṅgaṃ ca śakaṭaṃ sadhānyaṃ vastrasaṃmitam || 16 ||
pitṝṃśca devānprīṇāti prāpya cānantyamaśnute ||
na ca durgāṇyavāpnoti svagarlokaṃ ca gacchati || 17 ||
dattvā yathoktaṃ viprebhyo vṛttimiṣṭāṃ sa vindati ||
narakādīṃśca sa kleśānna prāpnotīti niścayaḥ || 16 ||
anurādhāsu prāvāraṃ vastrāntaramupoṣitaḥ ||
dattvā yugaśataṃ cāpi naraḥ svarge mahīyate || 19 ||
kālaśākaṃ tu viprebhyo dattvā martyaḥ samūlakam ||
jyeṣṭhāyāmṛddhimiṣṭāñca gatimiṣṭāñca gacchati||3.318.20||
mūle mūlaphalaṃ dattvā brāhmaṇebhyaḥ samāhitaḥ||
sa pitṝnprīṇayatyeva gatimiṣṭāñca gacchati || 21 ||
atha pūrvāsvāṣāḍhāsu dadhipātrāṇyupoṣitaḥ ||
kule vṛttopasampanne brāhmaṇe vedapārage || 22 ||
pradāya jāyate pretya kule sa bahugokule ||
udakumbhaṃ sasarpiṣkaṃ prabhūtaṃ madhuphāṇitam || 23 ||
dattvottarāsvāṣāḍhāsu sarvām̐llokānsamaśnute ||
dugdhe tvabhijite yoge dattvā madhughṛtāplutam ||24 ||
varma nityaṃ manīṣibhyaḥ svargaloke mahīyate ||
śravaṇe kambalaṃ dattvā vastrāntaritameva ca || 25 ||
śvetena yāti yānena svargalokānasaṃvṛtān ||
goprayuktaṃ dhaniṣṭhāsu yānaṃ dattvā samāhitaḥ ||26 ||
vastraraśminavaṃ sadyaḥ pretya rājyaṃ prapadyate ||
gandhāñchatabhiṣagyoge dattvā sāgurucandanān || 27 ||
prāpnotyapsarasaḥ lokānpretya gandhāṃśca śāśvatān ||
pūrvābhādrapadāyoge rājamāṣānpradāya ca || || 28||
sarvabhakṣyaphalopetaṃ sa vai pretya sukhī bhavet ||
aurabhraṃ tūttarāyoge yastu māṃsaṃ prayacchati ||29||
sa pitṝnprīṇayatyeva pretya cānantyamaśnute ||
kāṃsyopadohanāṃ dhenuṃ revatyāṃ yaḥ prayacchati || 3.318.30 ||
sa pretya kāmānādāya dātāramupatiṣṭhate ||
rathamaśvasamāyuktaṃ dattvāśvinyāṃ narottamaḥ || 31 ||
hastyaśvarathasampannaṃ varcasvī jāyate kule ||
bharaṇīṣu dvijātibhyastiladhenuṃ pradāya vai ||
gāḥ suprasūtāścāpnoti naraḥ pretya yaśastathā || 32 ||
nakṣatrasattraṃ kathitaṃ mayaitadyatheṣṭalokābhigamāya puṃsām ||
kṛtvā sakṛnnākagatastu kalpamāste mukhaṃ devapatiryathāsau || 33 ||
iti śrīviṣṇudharmottare tṛtīyakhaṇḍe mārkaṇḍeyavajrasaṃvāde munīnprati haṃsagītāsu nakṣatrasatravidhānaṃ nāmāṣṭādaśādhikatriśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ || 318 ||
<3.319>
|| ṛṣaya ūcuḥ ||
bhūya eva tvamācakṣva dānānāṃ kālayogataḥ ||
phalaṃ dvijavarāsmākaṃ pratyakṣāmaladarśana || 1 ||
|| haṃsa uvāca || ||
saṃprāpya caitramāsasya śuklāṃ pañcadaśīṃ naraḥ ||
citraṃ vastrayugaṃ dattvā sopavāso dvijātaye ||2 ||
brāhmaṇāṃścāparaṃ viprāḥ saubhāgyaṃ mahadāpnuyāt ||
vaiśākhyāṃ paurṇamāsyāntu brāhmaṇānsapta pañca vā || 3 ||
kṣaudrayuktaistilaiḥ kṛṣṇairvācayedyadi vetaraiḥ ||
prīyatāṃ dharmarājeti yadvā manasi varttate || 4 ||
yāvajjīvakṛtātpāpāttatkṣaṇādeva mucyate ||
tasminneva tathā kāle sakhuraṃ kṛṣṇamārgaṇam || 5 ||
tilaiḥ pracchādya vāsobhiḥ sarvaratnairalaṃkṛtam ||
tapasyante ca dātavyo mahī savanakānanā || 6 ||
kṛṣṇājine tilānkṛtvā hiraṇyamadhusarpiṣā ||
dadāti yastu viprāya sarvaṃ tarati duṣkṛtam || 7 ||
sopavāsastathā jyeṣṭhe pūrṇe tu śaśalakṣmaṇe ||
upānahau tathā cchatraṃ dattvātyantasukhī bhavet || 8 ||
āṣāḍhyāmannadānasya prāpnotyannaṃ naro bahu ||
jaladhenupradānena śrāvaṇyāṃ svargamāpnuyāt || 9 ||
godānaṃ proṣṭhapāde tu paurṇamāsyāṃ mahāphalam ||
āśvayujyaṃ kāṃsyapātrāṃ ghṛtapūrṇaṃ dvijātaye || 3.319.10 ||
suvarṇañca tathā dattvā dīptimāṃstvabhijāyate ||
kārtikyāṃ candravarṇābhamanyavarṇamathāpi vā || 11 ||
ratnairgandhaistathā dhānyairbījairvastraistathaiva ca ||
kṛtvā yuktamathokṣāṇaṃ dattvā dīpānsamantrataḥ || 52 ||
candrodaye naro dattvā sarvapāpaiḥ pramucyate ||
kāntāre yamamārge tu tenāsau vrajate sukham || 13 ||
sarvāṇi cāsya bhogāni tatra cāsau prayacchati ||
mārgaśīrṣe tathā māse pūrṇe śiśiradīdhitau || 14 ||
mahārajatayuktena vāsasā dvija puṅgavān ||
ācchādya kāṃsyapātrasthaṃ prasthaṃ kṛtvā samāhitaḥ ||15 ||
lavaṇasya tu mukhyasya cūrṇitasya dvijottamāḥ ||
dattvā suvarṇanābhantu tasminneva dvijātaye || 16 ||
saubhāgyarūpalāvaṇyayukto bhavati mānavaḥ ||
gaurasarṣapakalkena pauṣyāmutsāhito naraḥ || 17 ||
gavyasyājyasya kumbhena sobhiṣiktastvanantaram ||
virūkṣitastathā snātaḥ sarvabījauṣadhījalaiḥ || 18 ||
gandharatnaphalopetairghṛtena tadanantaram ||
sa suvarṇaṃ mukhaṃ dṛṣṭvā tatpradadyāddvijātaye || 19 ||
ghṛtena snapitaṃ viṣṇuṃ śaktyā sampūjayettataḥ ||
ghṛtañca juhuyādvahnau ghṛtaṃ dadyāddvijātaye || 3.319.20 ||
kartre vastrayugaṃ dadyātsopavāsaḥ samāhitaḥ ||
karmaṇānena dharmajñāḥ puṣṭimāpnotyanuttamām || 21 ||
māghyāṃ kṛtvā tilaiḥ śrāddhaṃ sarvapāpaiḥ pramucyate ||
svāstīrṇaṃ śayanaṃ dattvā phālgunyāṃ brāhmaṇasya tu || 22 ||
rūpadraviṇasaṃyuktāṃ bhāryāṃ pakṣavatīṃ tathā ||
naraḥ prāpnoti dharmajñaḥ puṣṭimāpnotyanuttamām || 23 ||
tathā nāryapi bhartāraṃ nātra kāryā vicāraṇā ||
paurṇamāsīṣu caitāsu māsarkṣasahitāsu ca || 24 ||
eteṣāmeva dānānāṃ phalaṃ daśaguṇaṃ bhavet ||
mahatpūrvāsu caitāsu phalamakṣayyamaśnute || 25 ||
dvādaśyāṃ caitraśuklasya citravastraprado naraḥ ||
akṣayaṃ phalamāpnoti nākaloke ca gacchati || 26 ||
vaiśākhamāse dvādaśyāṃ rukmadānāttathaiva ca ||
chatropānahayordānājjyeṣṭhe māsi dvijottamāḥ || 27 ||
svāstīrṇaṃ śayanaṃ dattvā prīṇayedbhogiśāyinam ||
āṣāḍha śukladvādaśyāṃ śvetadvīpe mahīyate || 28 ||
śrāvaṇe vastradānena viṣṇuloke mahīyate ||
godaḥ prayāti golokaṃ māsi bhādrapade dvijāḥ || 29 ||
prīṇayedaśvaśirasaścāśvaṃ dattvā tathāśvine ||
viṣṇulokamavāpnoti kulamuddharati svakam || 3.319.30 ||
saromavastradānena kārtike divamāpnuyāta |||
pradānaṃ lavaṇānāntu mārgaśīrṣe mahatphalam || 31 ||
dhānyānāñca tathā pauṣe dāruṇaṃ cāpyanantare ||
phālgune sarvagandhānāṃ nātra kāryā vicāraṇā| ||32||
bhāgyarkṣasaṃyutā caitrī dvādaśī syānmahāphalā ||
hastayuktātha vaiśākhī jyeṣṭhe tu svātinā tathā || 33 ||
jyeṣṭhāyāñca tathāṣāḍhe mūlopetā ca vaiṣṇave ||
tathā bhādrapade māsi śravaṇena tu saṃyutā || 34 ||
āśvine dvādaśī puṇyā bhavatyājarkṣasaṃyutā ||
kārtike revatīyuktā saumye kṛttikayā tathā || 35 ||
pauṣe mṛgaśiropetā māghe cādityasaṃyutā ||
phālgune puṣyasahitā dvādaśī pāvanā parā || 36 ||
nakṣatrayuktāsvetāsu tathā dānamupoṣitaḥ ||
sarvakāmaphalaṃ jñeyamanantaṃ dvijasattamāḥ || 37 ||
meṣasaṃkramaṇe bhānormeṣadānaṃ mahāphalam ||
vṛṣasaṃkramaṇe dānaṃ gavāṃ proktaṃ tathaiva ca|| 38 ||
śayanāsanadānañca mithunopagate ravau ||
karkapraveśe saktūnāṃ sitayā ca tathaiva ca || 39 ||
guḍaphāṇitakhaṇḍānāṃ sitāyāśca tathā dvijāḥ ||
lavaṇājyaphalānāñca tathā haritakasya ca || 3.319.40 ||
tathā caivodakuṃbhānāṃ dānaṃ jñeyaṃ mahāphalam ||
siṃhapraveśe pātrāṇāṃ taijasānāṃ tathaiva ca || 41 ||
kanyāpraveśe vastrāṇāṃ surabhīṇāṃ tathaiva ca ||
tulāpraveśe dhānyānāṃ bījānāmapi cottame || 42 ||
kīṭapraveśe vastrāṇāṃ veśmanāṃ dānameva ca ||
dhanvipraveśe vastrāṇāṃ yānānāñca mahāphalam || 43 ||
mṛgapraveśe dārūṇāṃ dānamagnestathaiva ca ||
kumbhapraveśe dānaṃ tu gavāmarthe tṛṇasya tu ||
mīnapraveśe sthānānāṃ mālyānāmapi cottamam || 44 ||
dānānyathaitāni mayā dvijendrā proktāni kāle tu naraśca dattvā ||
prāpnoti kāmānmanasā tvabhīṣṭāṃstasmātpraśaṃsanti mahāpradānam||45||
iti śrīviṣṇudharmottare tṛtīyakhaṇḍe mārkaṇḍeyavajrasaṃvāde munīnprati haṃsagītāsu dvādaśapaurṇamāsīdānaphalanirūpaṇaṃ nāmaikonaviṃśādhikatriśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ || 319 ||
<3.320>
ṛṣaya ūcuḥ ||
puṇyayajñakriyāḥ proktā ye narā dhanasaṃyutāḥ ||
tathā madhyamavṛttānāṃ dānāni vividhāni ca || 1 ||
kathitānyalpavṛttānāṃ dānāni vividhāni ca ||
ye narā vittarahitāsteṣāṃ dharmakriyāṃ vada || 2 ||
haṃsa uvāca ||
upavāsakriyā teṣāṃ paramaṃ dharmakāraṇam ||
vittānvitānāñca tathā praśastā sā mahāphalā || 3 ||
sāyaṃ prātastu bhuñjāno nāntarātūdakampiban ||
ṣaḍbhirvarṣairnaro lokaṃ prāpnoti manasepsitam || 4||
śubhānāmuttamaṃ caitaddhanyaṃ nityakṛtaṃ bhavet ||
yatheṣṭalokadaṃ puṇyaṃ mānuṣyepi mahāphalam || 5||
upavāsaṃ naraḥ kurvanmāsimāsi dvijottamāḥ ||
uṣitvā suciraṃ loke mānuṣye jāyate yadā || 6 ||
tadā bhavati dīrghāyurdhanavānrūpasaṃyutaḥ ||
pakṣepakṣe tathā kurvandviguṇaṃ phalamaśnute || 7 ||
māsemāse trirātrantu yaḥ kuryānmānavottamaḥ ||
gāṇapatyamavāpnoti svargalokaparicyutaḥ || 8 ||
pakṣepakṣe trirātrantu kṛtvā bhavati pārthivaḥ ||
svargalokātparibhraṣṭaḥ sucireṇa mahābalaḥ || 9 ||
dvijastrirātraṃ kurvīta na tu śūdraḥ kathañcana ||
ṣaṣṭhakālāśanaṃ tasya tapaḥ paramihocyate || 3.320.10 ||
śūdrastrirātraṃ kurute viṣaye yasya bhūpateḥ ||
tasya sīdati tadrāṣṭraṃ vyādhidurbhikṣataskaraiḥ || 11 ||
tapaḥ kurvīta śūdrastu caikadvidivasāntaram ||
yathāśakti dvijaśreṣṭhāstena kāmānupāśnute || 12 ||
ataḥ parantu varṇānāṃ māsemāse dvijottamāḥ ||
eka kālāśanānāntu śṛṇudhvaṃ phalamuttamam || 13 ||
caitrantu niyato māsaṃ caikabhaktena yaḥ kṣipet ||
suvarṇamaṇimuktāḍhye kule mahati jāyate || 14 ||
nirasyedekabhaktena vaiśākhaṃ yo dvijottamaḥ ||
naro vā yadi vā nārī jñātīnāṃ śreṣṭhatāṃ vrajet || 15 ||
jyeṣṭhāmūlaṃ tu yo māsamekabhaktena vartayet ||
aiśvaryamatulaṃ śreṣṭhaṃ pumāṃstrī vābhijāyate || 16 ||
āṣāḍhamekabhaktena sthitvā māsamatandritaḥ ||
bahudhānyo bahudhano bahuputraśca jāyate || 17 ||
śrāvaṇaṃ niyato māsamekabhaktena yaḥ kṣipet ||
yatra tatrābhiṣekena yujyate jñātivardhanaḥ || 18 ||
proṣṭhapādantu yo māsaṃ yaḥ kuryādekabhojanam ||
dhanāḍhyaḥ sphītamatulaṃ caiśvaryaṃ pratipadyate || 19 ||
tathaivāśvayujaṃ māsamekabhaktena yaḥ kṣipet ||
medhāvī vāhanāḍhyaśca bahuputraśca jāyate || 3.320.20 ||
kārtikantu naro māsaṃ yaḥ kuryādekabhojanama ||
śūraśca bahubhāryaśca kīrtimāṃścaiva jāyate || 21 ||
māgarśīrṣantu vai māsamekabhaktena yaḥ kṣipet ||
kṛṣibhāgī bahudhano bahudhānyaśca jāyate || 22 ||
pauṣamāsantu viprendrā bhaktenaikena yaḥ kṣipet||
subhago darśanīyaśca yaśobhāgī ca jāyate || 23 ||
māghantu niyataṃ māsamekabhaktena yaḥ kṣipet ||
kṛṣibhāgī bahudhano dhānyavānabhijāyate || 24 ||
bhagadaivantu yo māsamekabhaktena yaḥ kṣipet ||
śrīsatkule jñātimadhye sa mahattvaṃ prapadyate ||25||
strīṣu vallabhatāṃ yāti vaśyāścāsya bhavanti tāḥ ||
yathoktaphaladāścaite varṣairdvādaśabhiḥ smṛtāḥ ||26||
yastu saṃvatsaraṃ pūrṇaṃ caikāhāro bhavetsadā ||
atirātrasya yajñasya phalaṃ prāpnoti mānavaḥ||27||
tathā saṃvatsaraṃ pūrṇamekāntaritabhojanaḥ ||
yajñaṃ bahusuvarṇākhyaṃ dhruvamāpnoti vai dvijāḥ ||28||
abdamekaṃ tṛtīyehni bhuṃjānaśca tathā naraḥ ||
vājapeyasya yajñasya kathitaṃ dvijasattamāḥ || 29 ||
ekakālaṃ tu bhuñjānastvabdamekaṃ tu pañcame ||
svarge dvādaśapadmāni varṣāṇāṃ sa ca modate||3.320.30||
ṣaṣṭheṣaṣṭhe tathaivābdaṃ tvaśnānaḥ prayato naraḥ ||
aṣṭādaśaiva padmāni varṣāṇāṃ divi modate || 31 ||
divase saptame cābdaṃ bhuñjānastvekabhojanam ||
ṣaṭtriṃśatsa tu padmāni varṣāṇāṃ divi modate || 32 ||
aṣṭame divase cābdaṃ bhuñjānaḥ prayataḥ sadā ||
padmāni nāke pañcāśadvarṣāṇāṃ divi modate || 33 ||
yastu saṃvatsaraṃ bhuṅkte daśame daśame'hani ||
kalpamekaṃ sa viprendra devavaddivi modate || 34 ||
ekādaśe tu divase yaḥ prāpte prāpnute haviḥ ||
śatakalpadvayaṃ nāke modate devavaddivi || 35 ||
divase dvādaśe yastu prāpte vai prāśnute haviḥ ||
trīṇi kalpānyasau nāke bhuṅkte devasukhānvitaḥ || 36 ||
trayodaśe tu divase yaḥ prāpte prāpnute haviḥ ||
tasya kalpāni catvāri gatirnāke prakīrtitā || 37 ||
caturdaśe tu divase yaḥ prāpte prāpnute haviḥ ||
sapta tasya tu kalpāni gatirnāke prakīrtitā || 38 ||
saṃvatsaraṃ tu bhuñjāno naraḥ pañcadaśe haviḥ ||
nāke vasati dharmātmā deśe kalpānyasaṃśayam || 39 ||
abdamekaṃ tathā'śnānaḥ pakṣe dinayutena yaḥ ||
sa tu kalpaśataṃ viprā devavaddivi modate || 3.320.40 ||
dine saptadaśe prāpte yaḥ prāśedekabhojanam ||
svarge sa modate viprāḥ pañca kalpaśatāni ca || 41 ||
aṣṭādaśe ca divase yaḥ prāśedekabhojanam ||
nāke kalpe sahasraṃ tu naraḥ kartābhimodate || 42 ||
ekonaviṃśe divase yo bhuṃkte caikabhojanam ||
sa tu kalpasahasrantu sārdhaṃ nāke ca modate ||43||
ekakālaṃ ca bhuñjānaḥ prāpte viṃśatame dine ||
kalpānāṃ tu sahasre dve nākasthaḥ pūjyate suraiḥ ||44||
ekaviṃśe tu divase yaḥ kuryādekabhojanam ||
trīṇi kalpasahasrāṇi tasya nāke gatirbhavet || 45 ||
dvāviṃśe divase prāpte yo bhuṃkte caikabhojanam ||
catvāri sa ca kalpānāṃ sahasraṃ prāpnuyāddivi || 46 ||
trayoviṃśe ca divase yaḥ prāśedekabhojanam ||
pañca kalpasahasrāṇi svargasthaḥ sa tu modate || || 47 ||
caturviṃśe tu divase yaḥ prāśedekabhojanam ||
sapta kalpasahasrāṇi nākasthaḥ sa tu modate || 48 ||
pañcaviṃśattame cāhni yaḥ prāśedekabhojanam ||
daśa kalpasahasrāṇi svargasthaḥ sa tu modate || 49 ||
ṣaḍviṃśe divase yastu prāśnīyādekabhojanam ||
sa tu kalpasahasrāṇi caturviṃśaḥ naro bhavet || 3.320.50 ||
saptaviṃśe tu divase yaḥ prāśedekabhojanam ||
sa tu kalpasahasrāṇi nākasthopi ca modate || 51 ||
aṣṭāviṃśe tu divase yaḥ prāśedekabhojanam ||
sa tu kalpasahasrāṇi catvāriṃśatsu modate || 52 ||
ekonaviṃśe divase yaḥ prāśedekabhojanam ||
sa tu kalpasrahasrāṇāṃ pañcāśannākago bhavet || 53 ||
yastu triṃśaddine prāpte cāśnīyādekabhojanam ||
sa tu kalpasahasrāṇāṃ nākasthaḥ pūjyate śatam || 54 ||
māsemāse gate prāyaḥ prāśnīyādekabhojanam ||
pauruṣaṃ sa dinaṃ nāke devavanmodate sadā || 55 ||
sarve vratāstu kavyāya varṣamekaṃ sukhāvahāḥ ||
varṣe tu phaladāḥ proktā nātra kāryā vicāraṇā || 56 ||
snānaṃ sarveṣu kartavyaṃ kāryo homastathaiva ca ||
na bhoktavyaṃ tathā māsaṃ vratasya phalamaśnute || 57 ||
āśritya tīrthaṃ yadi vāpi devaṃ vratānyathaitāni mahāphalāni ||
kāryāṇi naitānyadhikāstu māsāstataḥ paraṃ nānaśanaṃ pradiṣṭam || 58 ||
iti śrīviṣṇudharmottare tṛtīyakhaṇḍe mārkaṇḍeya vajrasaṃvāde munīnprati haṃsagītāsūpavāsavidhivarṇanaṃ nāma viṃśatyadhikatriśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ || 320 ||
<3.321>
|| ṛṣaya ūcuḥ || ||
kva kāle karmaṇā kena prāpnuvanti narottamāḥ||
tattvamasmākamācakṣva tvaṃ hi sarvavidarhasi || 1 ||
|| haṃsa uvāca || ||
jyeṣṭhasvasāraṃ pitaraṃ mātaraṃ gurumeva ca ||
nityaṃ sampūjayedbhaktyā yāmyaloke mahīyate || 2 ||
bhojanācchādanādyena tvatithiṃ caiva pūjayet ||
rājarājasya lokeṣu modate nātra saṃśayaḥ || 3 ||
meroḥ samīpaṃ gacchanvai nityādhyayanatatparaḥ ||
sudarśanā yatra ca sā jambūḥ khyātā dvijātayaḥ || 4 ||
nṛtte gīte ca kuśalaṃ nandanaṃ yānti te vanam ||
himāni vṛttā gacchanti tathā caivottarānkurūn || 5 ||
nityaṃ dānaparāḥ santo lokaṃ gacchanti śāśvatam ||
ādityalokaṃ gacchanti tathā ye tapasi sthitāḥ || 6 ||
tīrthayātrāparā yānti tathā lokaṃ pracetasaḥ ||
saṃgrāme nihatā yānti śakralokamasaṃśayam || 7 ||
prājāpatyaṃ tathā yāṃti samyagvṛtvā mahīkṣitaḥ ||
gavāṃ patyā tathā yāṃti golokaṃ mānavottamāḥ || 8 ||
yānti lokaṃ ca sūryasya nityaṃ ye satyabhāṣiṇaḥ||
pratigrahānnivṛttāśca vasūnāmapi mānavāḥ || 9 ||
vāyuloke mahīyante rogiṇāṃ paricārakāḥ ||
lokaṃ gacchantyathāgneyaṃ vahniśuśrūṣaṇe ratāḥ || 3.321.10 |||
yāṃti te nairṛtaṃ lokaṃ prapārakṣaṇatatparāḥ ||
bhṛgūṇāmapi lokeṣu modantyākāśaśāyinaḥ || 11 ||
yāṃti cāṅgirasaṃ lokaṃ vratenānena mānavāḥ ||
marutāṃ ca tathā lokaṃ yāṃti yānapradā narāḥ || 12 ||
nāsatyaloke modante tathaivauṣadhadāyinaḥ ||
rudralokaṃ prapadyante govipranaravatsalāḥ ||13 |||
svācāraniratā yāṃti vaiśvadevamasaṃśayam ||
ādityaiḥ saha modaṃte dayāvantastu ye narāḥ || 14 ||
brahmalokaṃ viṣṇulokaṃ rudralokaṃ tathaiva ca ||
tadbhaktaireva labhyante nānyathā dvijasattamāḥ||15||
yasya devasya yo bhaktiṃ sadā vahati mānavaḥ||
sampadastasya sālokyaṃ yāṃti nāstyatra saṃśayaḥ||16||
lokeṣu divyeṣu gatirmayoktā karmānurūpā puruṣasya viprāḥ ||
ataḥ paraṃ kiṃ kathayāmyahaṃ vai tanme gadadhvaṃ tapasi pradhānāḥ || 17 ||
iti śrīviṣṇudharmottare tṛtīyakhaṇḍe mārkaṇḍeyavajrasaṃvāde munīnprati haṃsagītāsu lokavibhāgavarṇano nāmaikaviṃśatyadhikatriśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ || 321 ||
<3.322>
|| ṛṣaya ūcuḥ ||
kathayasvāmitaprājña strīṇāṃ dharmamataḥ param ||
tadvayaṃ śrotumicchāmastvaṃ hi sarvaviducyase || 1 ||
|| haṃsa uvāca ||
strīdharmaḥ pūrva evāyaṃ vivāhe bandhubhiḥ kṛtaḥ ||
sadharmacāriṇī bharturbhavedagnisamīpataḥ||2||
