susvabhāvā suvimanā suvibhaktā sudarśanā ||
ananyacittā sumukhī bhartussā dharmacāriṇī || 3 ||
putravaktramivābhīkṣṇaṃ bharturvadanamīkṣatī ||
yā sādhvī niyatācārā sā bhaveddharmacāriṇī || 4 ||
śrutvedaṃ patidharmaṃ vai mahāśramakṛtaṃ śivam ||
yā bhaveddharmaparamā nārī bhartṛsamavratā||5||
devavatsatataṃ sādhvī bhartāramanupaśyati||
śuśrūṣāṃ paricaryāñca devatulyaṃ prakurvatī ||6||
vaśyābhāvena sumanāḥ suvratā susamāhitā ||
ananyacittā sumanā bhartuḥ sā dharmabhāginī||7||
pāruṣyamapi voktā yā dṛṣṭā vā krūracakṣuṣā ||
suprasannamukhī bharturyā nārī sā pativratā||8||
na candrasūryau tadrūpaṃ nenau yā ca nirīkṣate||
bhartṛvarjaṃ varārohā sā bhaveddharmabhāginī||9||
daridraṃ vyādhitaṃ dīnaṃ cādhinā parikīrtitam ||
patimputramivārakṣetsā nārī dharmabhāginī || 3.322.10 ||
yā nārī prayatā dakṣā yā nārī putriṇī bhavet ||
pativratā patiprāṇā sā nārī dharmabhāginī || 11 ||
śuśrūṣāṃ paricaryāñca karotyavimanāḥ sadā ||
supratītā sumanāḥ sā nārī dharmabhāginī || 12 ||
na kāmeṣu na bhogeṣu naiśvarye na sukhe tathā ||
spṛhā yasyā yathā bhartuḥ sā nārī dharmabhāginī || 13 ||
kāmārthā na vratā nityaṃ gṛhaśuśrūṣaṇe ratā ||
susammṛṣṭakṣayā caiva gośakṛtkṛtalepanā || 14 ||
agnikāryaparā nityaṃ sadā puṣpabalipradā ||
devatātithibhṛtyānāṃ nirūpya patinā saha || 15 ||
śeṣānnamupabhuñjānā yathānyāyaṃ yathāvidhi ||
tuṣṭabhṛtyajanā nityaṃ nārīdharmeṇa yujyate || 16 ||
śvaśrūśvaśurayoḥ pādām̐stoṣayantī guṇānvitā ||
mātāpitṛratā nityaṃ yā nārī sā tapodhanā || 17 || 
brāhmaṇāndurbalānāthadīnāndhakṛpaṇām̐stathā ||
bibhartyanena yā nārī sā pativratabhāginī || 18 ||
vratañcarati yā nityaṃ duścaraṃ spaṣṭamānasā ||
paticittā patiratā sā pativratabhāginī || 19 ||
puṇyametattapaścaiva svargaścaiva sanātanaḥ ||
yā nārī bhartṛparamā bhavedbhartṛvatā śivā ||3.322.20||
patirhi devo nārīṇāṃ patirbandhuḥ patirgatiḥ ||
patyā gatiḥ samā nāsti daivataṃ vā yathā patiḥ ||21||
patiḥ svargaprado nāryā yadā talpamanā bhavet ||
yadyatkāryamakāryaṃ vā yadi vā prāṇanāśanam || 22 ||
patiḥ pātakayukto vā vyādhito vā kathañcana ||
āpanno ripusaṃstho vā brahmaśāpārdito'pi vā || 23 ||
āpaddharmānanuprekṣya tatkāryamaviśaṅkayā ||
svātaṃtryamuktamparamaṃ hi pāpaṃ strīṇāṃ sadā tena sadaiva nārī ||
svātantryahīnā satatambhaveddhi yā kāmayennākamitaścirāya || 24 ||
iti śrīviṣṇudharmottare tṛtīyakhaṇḍe mārkaṇḍeyavajra saṃ0 munīnprati haṃsagītāsu strīdharmanirūpaṇonāma dvāviṃśatyadhikatriśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ || 322 ||
<3.323>
|| haṃsa uvāca || || ||
svesve dharme vyavasthānaṃ varṇānāṃ pṛthivīpateḥ ||
paro dharmaḥ sadā proktastatra yatra bhavennaraḥ || 1||
devabrāhmaṇagosādhvīsādhūnāmapi pūjanam ||
nigrahaścaiva duṣṭānānnṛpaterdharmakāraṇam || 2 ||
samatā vyavahāreṣu svayañcaivānvavekṣaṇam ||
rājñāṃ dharmaṃ paraṃ vidyāttathā vai cāradṛṣṭitā || 3 ||
svadeśapālanaṃ dharmaḥ pararāṣṭrābhimardanam ||
yajñadānakriyā caiva saṃgrāme cāpalāyanam || 4 ||
mṛgayākṣā tathā pānaṃ garhitāni mahībhujām ||
vāgdaṇḍayostu pāruṣyantathaivārthasya dūṣaṇam || 5 ||
sandhivigrahadānānāmāsanasya tathaiva ca ||
dvaidhībhāvasya cāpyuktā sevā vai saṃśrayasya ca || 6 ||
jñātvā kālañcalaṃ rājā ṣāṅguṇyasya hi niścaye ||
indriyāṇāñjaye yatnaḥ sadā syāddvijapuṅgavāḥ || 7 ||
tato bhṛtyajayaḥ kāryaḥ samastavijayastataḥ ||
kāmaḥ krodho mado lobho māno harṣastathaiva ca || 8 ||
ābhyantarā vijetavyā ripavaḥ ṣaṇmahībhujā ||
tatastu śatravo jeyāḥ kulyāścetarakṛtrimāḥ ||9||
bhūmimitrahiraṇyānāmarjjane yatnavānbhavet ||
dharmārthakāmasevā ca sadā kāryā mahīkṣitā || 3.323.10 ||
svāmyamātyañjanaṃ durgaṃ kośo daṇḍaḥ suhṛttathā ||
rājye prakṛtayaḥ sapta rakṣyā yatnena bhūbhujā || 11 || 
daivamantraprabhūtsāhaśaktiyuktastathā bhavet ||
maṅgalācārayuktānāṃ sidhyante devaśaktayaḥ || 12 ||
caturaṅgabalaṃ kuryātsubhṛtañca tathā nṛpaḥ ||
karmaṇā manasā vācā sadā lokaṃ prasādayet || 13||
mahaccāvaraṇaṃ kāryannityameva samṛddhaye ||
rājā sahāyasampannaḥ paśavyañjāṅgalaṃ śivam||14||
vaiśyaśūdrajanaprāyamarogaṃ deśamāvaseta||
tatra durgāṇi kurvīta svadurgañca viśeṣavat || 15 ||
dhanvadurgammahīdurgaṃ giridurgantathaiva ca ||
adurgaṃ vṛkṣadurgañca naradurgantathaiva ca || 16 ||
eṣāmanyatame durge dhanadhānyasamāyute ||
hastyaśvarathasampanne maṇibhīrūpaśobhite || 17 ||
pattyāḍhye yodhabahule yuddhopakaraṇairyute ||
susañcite dhanāḍhye ca bahupeye bahūdake || 18 ||
hastyaśvanaragovaidyasāṃvatsarayute śubhe ||
ghṛtatailauṣadhiyute sarvopakaraṇaistathā || 19 ||
prākāraparikhāvapragopurāṭṭālakairyute ||
pāpataskaradurvṛttakruddhalubdhādivarjite||3.323.20||
daivajñasthapatibhyāntu saṃmatyā śubhalakṣaṇam ||
tatrāvasetsvayaṃ rājā sarvopakaraṇairyutam || 21 ||
kulīnāṃ rūpasampannāṃ savarṇāñca narādhipaḥ ||
kuryādbhāryāṃ suśīlāñca vinītāṃ dharmavatsalām || 22 ||
tayā saha sadā rājā gṛhasthāśramacoditam ||
pañcayajñavidhānantu kārayeta purohitam || 23 ||
yajecca vividhairyajñaiḥ pūjayeddevatāstathā ||
devatāyatanānkūpāṃstaḍāgāṃścaiva kārayet || || 24 ||
udyānāni vicitrāṇi gobrāhmaṇagṛhāṇi ca ||
duṣṭadaṇḍaḥ satāmpūjā dharmeṇa ca dhanārjanam || 25 ||
rāṣṭrarakṣā samatvañca vyavahāreṣu pañcakam ||
bhūmipānāmmahāyajñāḥ sarvakalmaṣanāśanāḥ || 26 ||
gaṇaṃ lakṣaṇasaṃyuktaṃ turagañcābhiṣecayet ||
salakṣaṇaṃ tathā ratnaṃ khaḍgakambibhṛyānnṛpaḥ || 27 ||
durgasthaśca niyuñjīta grāmegrāme ca pālakān ||
daśagrāmādhipāṃścānyaṃ śatagrāmādhipāṃstathā || 28 ||
deśeśvarāṃstathā kuryānnagarasthānviśeṣataḥ ||
grāmadoṣānsamutpannān grāmapālaḥ śamaṃ nayet || 29 ||
aśaktaśca daśeśāya sa tu gatvā nivedayet ||
śateśāya daśeśastu śateśo deśapāya ca ||3.323.30||
grāmādipālakānāṃ tu śaucāśaucaṃ narādhipaḥ ||
cāraiḥ suviditaṃ kuryājjñātvā duṣṭāṃśca ghātayet || 31 ||
rājño hi rakṣādhikṛtāḥ parasvādāyinaḥ śaṭhāḥ ||
bhṛtyā bhavanti prāyeṇa tebhyo rakṣedvasundharām ||32 ||
tathā ca rakṣādhikṛtānanurūpeṇa yojayet ||
vibhavena dvija śreṣṭhāḥ parīkṣya ca punaḥpunaḥ ||33||
rājā karmaniyuktānāṃ strīṇāṃ preṣyajanasya ca ||
pratyahaṃ kalpayed vṛttiṃ sthānakarmānurūpataḥ ||34||
āyadvārāṃśca nocchindyādviśeṣadvārakānapi ||
samyagbaliśca gṛhṇīyānmālākāropamāṃ nṛpaḥ || 35 ||
krayavikrayamadhvānaṃ bhaktañcāsaparivyayam ||
yogakṣemañca samprekṣya vaṇijo dāpayetkaram || 36 ||
yathā phalena yujyeta rājā karttā ca karmaṇām ||
tathāvekṣya naro rāṣṭre kalpayetsatataṃ karam || 37 ||
pañcāśadbhāga ādāyo rājñāmpaśuhiraṇyayoḥ ||
dhānyānāñca tathā ṣaṣṭhaṃ sasyānāñca mahīkṣitā || 38 |||
ṣaṣṭhameva tathā bhāgaṃ māṣāṇāṃ madhusarpiṣām ||
gandhauṣadhirasānāñca puṣpamūlaphalasya ca || 39 ||
patraśākatṛṇānāñca carmaṇāṃ dvaidalasya ca ||
mṛnmayānāñca bhāṇḍānāṃ sarvasyāśmamayasya ca || 3.323.40 ||
yatkiñcidapi varṣasya dāpayetkarasaṃjñitam ||
vyavahāreṇa jīvantaṃ rāṣṭre rājyaṃ pṛthakpṛthak || 41 ||
rājadūtakarebhyaśca gṛhṇīyātpañcakaṃ śatam ||
pañcapañcaśatantatra samikaḥ prāptumaharti || 42 ||
kārukāñśilpinaścaiva śūdradharmopajīvinaḥ ||
ekaikaṃ kārayetkarma māsimāsi mahīpatiḥ || 43 ||
svadeśapaṇyācchulkāṃśaṃ gṛhṇīyāddaśamaṃ nṛpaḥ ||
videśapaṇyācca tathā deyampañcaśataṃ samam || 44 ||
śulkasthānambhavedekaṃ na dvitīyaṃ kathañcana ||
śulkaṃ bhūyastu gṛhṇītaścaurasyāpnoti kilbiṣam ||45||
na bhinnakārṣāpaṇamasti śulkaṃ na śilpavṛttau na śiśau na dūte ||
na bhaikṣyalabdhena hatāvaśeṣe na śrotriye pravrajite na yajñe || 46 ||
iti śrīviṣṇudharmottare tṛ0 kha0 mārkaṇḍeyavajrasaṃvāde munīnprati haṃsagītāsu rājadharmmanirūpaṇo nāma trayoviṃśatyadhikatriśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ || 323 ||
<3.324>
ṛṣaya ūcuḥ ||
rājño dharmastvayā prokto vyavahārānvavekṣaṇam ||
tadasmākaṃ tvamācakṣva tvaṃ hi sarvaviducyase ||1||
haṃsa uvāca ||
āsītkṛtayuge pūrve prajādharmanibandhanaḥ ||
tatastretāyuge teṣāṃ dharmo nāśamupāgamat || 2 ||
dharme naṣṭe manuṣyāṇāṃ vyavahāraḥ pravartitaḥ ||
draṣṭā ca vyavahārāṇāṃ rājā daṇḍadharaḥ kṛtaḥ || ||3 ||
kulāni śreṇayaścaiva gaṇāścādhikṛto nṛpaḥ ||
draṣṭāro vyavahārāṇāṃ guravo hyuttarottaram || 4 ||
vyavahārānnṛpaḥ paśyedvidvadbhirbrāhmaṇaiḥ saha ||
dharmaśāstrānusāreṇa rāgadveṣavivarjitaḥ || 5||
kulodgatā sthitā dharme mṛjāyuktāḥ śrutānvitāḥ ||
rājñā sabhāsadaḥ kāryā ripau mitre ca ye samāḥ || || 6 ||
apaśyatā kāryavaśādvyavahārānnṛpeṇa tu || ||
sabhyaiḥ saha niyoktavyo brāhmaṇaḥ sarvadharmavit || 7 ||
kṣattriyastvatha vā vaiśyo na tu śūdraḥ kadācana ||
yasya rājñastu viṣaye śūdro dharmavivecakaḥ || 8 ||
tasya sīdati tadrāṣṭraṃ durbhikṣavyādhitaskaraiḥ ||
vivādād dviguṇaṃ daṃḍaṃ kūṭakṛtsākṣiṇastathā || 9 ||
dviguṇaṃ ca tathā sabhyā rāgadveṣavyavasthitāḥ ||
durmṛṣṭavyavahāreṇa rājñā vittaṃ yadarjitam || 3.324.10 ||
triṃśadguṇaṃ tu dātavyaṃ brāhmaṇeṣvatha vāṃbhasi ||
smṛtyācāravyapetena mārgeṇādharṣitaḥ paraiḥ || 11 ||
āvedayati cedrājñe vyavahārapadaṃ hi tat ||
pratyarthinastathā kuryādāhvānaṃ pṛthivīpatiḥ || 12 ||
anāgacchañjito rājñā vineyaḥ puruṣo bhṛśam||
nadīsantārakāraṇyadurdeśopaplavādiṣu ||13||
nāgastho na sa daṃḍyaḥ syānnāsau rājñoparādhyati ||
niveṣṭukāmo rogārto yiyakṣurvyasane sthitaḥ||14||
abhiyuktastathānyena rājakāryaratastathā ||
gavāṃ pracāre gopālāḥ sampatkāle kṛṣīvalāḥ ||15 ||
śilpinaścaiva tatkālaṃ cāyudheyāśca vigrahe ||
apāstavyavahārāśca dūtadāno mukhī vrate || 16 ||
viṣamasthaśca nāsādhyā nacaināṃścāhvayennṛpaḥ ||
pratyarthino'grato lekhyaṃ yathāveditamarthinaḥ || 17 || 
samāmāsatadardhāharjñātināmādicihnitam ||
śrutārthasyottaraṃ lekhyaṃ pūrvāvedakasaṃnidhau ||18||
tatorthī lekhayetsadyaḥ pratijñātārthasādhanam ||
caturdhā tacca kathitaṃ munibhistattvadarśibhiḥ ||19 ||
likhitaṃ sākṣiṇo bhuktiścaturthī vaidikī kriyā ||
sārastu vyavahārāṇāṃ pratiṣṭhā samudāhṛtā ||3.324.20 ||
tatsiddhau siddhimāpnoti viparītamatonyathā ||
abhiyogamanistīrya nainaṃ pratyabhiyojayet | 21 ||
kuryātpratyabhiyogaṃ ca kalahe sāhaseṣu va ||
ubhayoḥ pratibhuvau grāhyau samarthau kāryanirṇaye || || 22 ||
sāhasasteyapāruṣye hyabhiśāpātyaye striyam ||
vivādayetsadya eva kālo'nyatrecchayā smṛtaḥ || 23 ||
deśāddeśāntaraṃ yāti sṛkkiṇī parileḍhi ca ||
lalāṭaṃ svidyate cāsya mukhaṃ vaivarṇyameti ca ||24||
pariśuṣyatskhaladvākyo viruddhaṃ bahu bhāṣate ||
abhiyogetha vā sākṣye duṣṭaḥ sa parikīrtyate || 25 ||
ākārairiṅgitairgatyā ceṣṭitairbhāṣitena ca ||
netravaktravikārābhyāṃ gṛhyateṃ'targataṃ manaḥ || 26 ||
tripakṣādvijito jñeyastūttarasyāprayacchataḥ ||
sarveṣveva vivādeṣu balavatyuttarā kriyā||27||
ādhau pratigrahe krīte pūrvāttu balavattarā ||
sarvopadravakartāramekadeśavibhāvitam ||28||
sarvasmāddhīyate cārthāditi dharmavido viduḥ ||
balopadhinivṛttām̐stu vyavahārānnivarttayet ||29||
strīnaktamantarāgārabahuśatrukṛtām̐stathā ||
rogārttabhītavyasanibālaśiṣyakṛtāni tu || 3.324.30 ||
akṛtāni vijānīyāttathā dāsakṛtāni tu||
gurorvākyena śiṣyasya pitṛvākyātsutasya ca || 31 ||
dāsasya svāmivākyena vyavahārastu siddhyati ||
strīkṛtānyapramāṇāni kāryāṇyāhuranāpadi ||32 ||
viśeṣato dhanakṣetre dāyādadhanavikramāḥ ||
etānyeva pramāṇāni bhartā yadyanumanyate || 33 ||
puraḥ patyurabhāvena rājā vā pitṛputrayoḥ ||
bāla āṣoḍaśāvarṣātpaugaṇḍaścābhiśasyate || 34 ||
parato vyavahārajñaḥ svatantraḥ pitaraṃ vinā ||
aprāptavyavahārasya svātaṃtryaṃ māturucyate || 35 ||
asvatantrakṛtānyāhurapramāṇāni paṃḍitāḥ ||
nāniyuktena vaktavyaṃ vyavahāre kathaṃcana || 36 ||
aniyuktavaco daṇḍyo rājā rukmasya kṛṣṇalam ||
niyukto na niyukto vā vidyā vākyamudāharan || 37 ||
sarvasvarahitaḥ kāryo lekhako'nṛtalekhakaḥ ||
lekhakasya ca sabhyānāṃ sākṣiṇāṃ yaśca dūṣakaḥ ||38||
anāhūtasya cāhvāne āhūtasya ca mokṣaṇe ||
ajito'smīti manvāno brūyāttaṃ tu parājitam || 39 ||
rājā tānvinayedviprāndaṃḍena dviguṇena ca ||
sabhā vā na praveṣṭavyā vaktavyaṃ vāsamaṃjasam || 3.324.40 ||
abruvanvibruvanvāpi naro bhavati kilbiṣī ||
pādo dharmasya kartāraṃ pādaḥ sākṣiṇameva ca || 41 ||
pādaḥ sabhāsadaḥ sarvānpādo rājānamṛcchati ||
rājā bhavatyanenāstu nindārho yatra nindyate || 42 ||
eno gacchati kartāraṃ mucyante ca sabhāsadaḥ ||
chalaṃ nirasya bhūtena vyavahārānnayennṛpaḥ||43||
bhūtamapyanupanyastaṃ vyavahāreṇa hīyate||
sapaṇaścedvivādaḥ syātaddhanaṃ dāpayennaraḥ||
daṇḍaṃ ca svapaṇaṃ caiva dhanike dhanameva ca||44||
na sā sabhā yatra na saṃti vṛddhā na te vṛddhā ye na vadaṃti dharmam||
na taddharmaṃ yatra na satyamasti na tatsatyaṃ yacchalenānuviddham||45||
iti śrīviṣṇudharmottare tṛ0kha0 mā0 saṃvāde munīnprati haṃsagītāsu vyavahāranirūpaṇo nāma caturviṃśatyuttaratriśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ ||324||
<3.325>
haṃsa uvāca ||
saṃbhogo dṛśyate yatra na dṛśyetāgamaḥ kvacit ||
āgamaḥ kāraṇaṃ tatra na bhogastatra kāraṇam||1||
āgamena viśuddhena bhogo yāti pramāṇatām ||
aviśuddhāgamo bhogaḥ prāmāṇyaṃ naiva gacchati || 2 ||
āhaṃtaivābhiyuktaḥ sannarthānāmuddharetpadam ||
bhuktireva viśuddhā syātprāptānāṃ pitṛtaḥ kramāt||3||
anvāhitaḥ ..... hele vispaṣṭayācitam ||
apratyakṣaṃ ca yadbhuktaṃ na cedbhogena jīryate||4||
paśyato'bruvato bhūmerhānirviṃśativārṣikī ||
pareṇa bhujyamānāyā dhanasya daśavārṣakī || 5 ||
tribhireva tu yadbhuktaṃ puruṣaistu yathāvidhi ||
lekhyābhāvepi taṃ tatra caturthaḥ samavāpnuyāt || 6 ||
āgamopyadhiko bhuktervināpūrvakramāgatāt ||
āgamopi balī naiva bhuktistatrāpi yatra na||7||
ādhyābhihastā sa bhṛśaṃ vineyā daṇḍena viprā dviguṇena rājñā ||
loke na duṣṭaḥ sa tu kaṃṭakaḥ syātpāpātmanāṃ mūrdhani tiṣṭhatīti||8||
iti śrīviṣṇudharmottare tṛtīyakhaṇḍe mārkaṇḍeyavajrasaṃvāde munīnprati haṃsagītāsu bhogalakṣaṇavarṇano nāma pañcaviṃśatyuttaratriśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ || 325 ||
<3.326>
haṃsa uvāca ||
pauruṣaścaiva divyaśca nirṇayo dvividhaḥ smṛtaḥ ||
dvividhaḥ pauruṣastatra likhitaṃ sākṣiṇastathā || 1 ||
trividhaṃ likhitaṃ tatra tasya vakṣyāmi lakṣaṇam ||
rājasākṣikamekaṃ tu dvitīyaṃ tu sa sākṣikam ||2 ||
svahastalikhitaṃ nāma tṛtīyaṃ parikīrtitam||
niyuktakāryasya kṛtaṃ rājādhikaraṇetaram || 3 ||
tadadhyakṣyeṇa martyaiśca svahasta paricihnitam ||
tallekhyaṃ kathitaṃ loke rājasākṣikamityuta||
yatra kvacana vipreṃdrā likhitaṃ yena kenacita ||
cihnitaṃ yatsvahastena sākṣibhiśca guṇānvitaiḥ ||5||
dvābhyāmūrdhvaṃ tu tajjñeyaṃ likhitañca sasākṣikam ||
sākṣī svahastalikhite bhavedvāpi na vā bhavet||6||
vināpi sākṣibhistasya prāmāṇyamupapadyate ||
svahastalikhitaṃ patraṃ balātkāreṇa kāritam||7||
hastapramāṇaṃ sarvairvā tathopadhikṛtaṃ dvijāḥ ||
mattonmattakṛtaṃ lekhyamasvatantrakṛtaṃ ca yat||8||
lekhyaṃ vinā nāsti siddhiḥ sthāvarasya tu karhicit ||
bhuktistatra pramāṇaṃ vā bhavettripuruṣāgatā||9||
hastena dvipadaṃ deyaṃ sāmnā dadyāccatuṣpadam ||
sthāvarāṇāṃ ca sarveṣāṃ siddhirlekhye pratiṣṭhitā ||3.326.10||
deśācārādviruddhaṃ yadvyaktāvavadhilakṣaṇam ||
tatpramāṇaṃ smṛtaṃ lekhyaṃ viluptadhanamakṣayam || 11 ||
prākprasādāḥ pradīyante paścādvāpi na pārthivāḥ ||
devadvijātivitteṣu na syātsvāmī nṛpaḥ kvacit || 12 ||
samūhārthe na bhogo'sti lekhyaṃ tatrāpahārakam ||
ubhayorapi tatra syādbhuktaṃ yena sa vai prabhuḥ || 13 ||
cirantanamavijñātaṃ bhuktaṃ bhogānna cālayet ||
ekārha likhite tatsyātsambhogo yasya tasya tu || 14 ||
arvākkālikalabdhaṃ tu lekhyenaiva viśodhayet ||
mudrāśuddhaṃ kriyāśuddhaṃ pāraṃparyānumoditam ||15 ||
adūṣitaṃ ca spaṣṭaṃ ca siddhimāpnoti nānyathā ||
svaveśātkīrtayedrājā ātmānaṃ tadanantaram||16||
dāyādeyapramāṇaṃ ca sahasīmāparicchadaiḥ ||
tatastu lekhayettatra dānacchedopavarṇanam || 17 ||
tallekhyaṃ siddhimāpnoti mudritaṃ rājamudrayā ||
matāntare tu lekhyasya śrāvaṇācchravaṇe tathā || 18 ||
naiva siddhirvinirdiṣṭā bhāvanānyā na cedbhavet ||
svahastalikhitaṃ patraṃ nihnuke tu vibhāvayet || 19 ||
varṇaiśca tatkṛtaiścihnaiḥ prāpyaireva ca yuktibhiḥ ||
yatrarṇalekhako vāpi sākṣī vāpi mṛto bhavet || 3.326.20 ||
likhitānte praviṣṭe tu dhanī lekhyaṃ tu pāṭayet ||
asamagre praviṣṭe tu lekhye dūragate tathā || || 21||
dhanikasya dhanī dadyāllikhitaṃ svamathāparam ||
daivadopaniviṣṭaṃ tu lekhyaṃ lekhyaṃ tu kārayet || 22 ||
anyamevādhamarṇāstu lekhyaṃ yatsmaryate yadi ||
samudre tu yadā lekhye mṛtāḥ sarve tu tatsthitāḥ || 23 ||
likhitaṃ tatpramāṇaṃ tu mṛteṣvapi hi sākṣiṣu ||
sopadhyaṃ yadbhavellekhyaṃ divyādyaistatprasādhayet || 24 ||
balātkārabalairlekhyaiḥ satyaścecchachrāvaṇādbhavet ||
jñāpitenāpyasiddhiḥ syātsiddhistasmādyathā smṛtā ||
paitāmahānāṃ rikthānāṃ prabhuḥ pautro'pi gāyate || 25 ||
ye kūṭakārā vasudhādhipena bhṛśaṃ vineyāstridaśepsunā vai ||
te kaṇṭakāḥ sarvajanasya viprāsteṣāṃ niṣeddhā nṛpatirbhavedvai || 26 ||
iti śrīviṣṇudharmottare tṛtīyakhaṇḍe mārkaṇḍeyavajrasaṃvāde munīnprati haṃsagītāsu lekhyalakṣaṇavarṇano nāma ṣaiviṃśatyuttaratriśatatamo 'dhyāyaḥ || 326 ||
<3.327>
|| haṃsa uvāca || ||
ekādaśavidhaḥ sākṣī śāstradṛṣṭo manīṣibhiḥ ||
kṛtaḥ pañcavidhastatra ṣaḍvidho'kṛta ucyate || 1 ||
likhitaḥ smāritaścaiva yadṛcchābhijña eva ca ||
gūḍhaścottarasākṣī ca kṛtaḥ pañcavidhaḥ smṛtaḥ || 2 ||
grāmaśca prāḍvivākaśca rājā ca vyavahāriṇām ||
kāryeṣvabhyantaro yaḥ syādarthinā prahitaḥ sadā || 3 ||
kulaṃ kulavivādeṣu ṣaḍvidhastvakṛtaḥ smṛtaḥ ||
dvayorvivadatorarthe dvayoḥ satsu ca sākṣiṣu ||4||
pūrvapakṣo bhavedyasya bhaveyustasya sākṣiṇaḥ ||
ādhāryaḥ pūrvapakṣasya yasminnarthavaśādbhavet || 5 ||
vivāde sākṣiṇastatra praṣṭavyāḥ prativādinaḥ ||
na pareṇa samuddiṣṭamupeyātsākṣiṇaṃ prati || 6 ||
bhedayettu na vānyonyaṃ hīyatyevaṃ samācaret ||
sākṣidviṣṭo yadi preyādgacchedvāpi digantaram || 7 ||
taccobhayoḥ pramāṇaṃ tu pramāṇe hyantarā kriyā ||
anirdiṣṭastu sākṣitve svayamevaitya yo vadet || 8 ||
sūcītyuktaḥ sa sākṣī tu na sa sākṣitvamarhati ||
na sākṣī nṛpatiḥ kāryo na kārukakuśīlavau || 9 ||
na śrotriyo na liṅgastho na sarvebhyo vinirgataḥ ||
na vyādhito na vaktavyo na dasyurna vikarmakṛt || 3.327.10 ||
na vṛddho na śiśurnaiko nāndho na vikalendriyaḥ ||
nārto na matto nonmatto na kṣuttṛṣṇopapīḍitaḥ || 11 ||
na śramārto na kāmārto na kruddho nāpi taskaraḥ ||
nārthasambandhinorāpto na hāyanavicāriṇaḥ || 12 ||
na dṛṣṭadoṣāḥ kartavyā na vyādhyārtā na dūṣitāḥ ||
strīṇāṃ sākṣyaṃ striyaḥ kuryurdvijānāṃ sadṛśā dvijāḥ || 13 ||
śūdrāśca sādhuśūdrāṇāṃ martyānāṃ martyayonayaḥ ||
anubhāvī tu yaḥ kaścitkuryātsākṣyaṃ vivādinām || 14 ||
antarveśmanyaraṇye vā śarīrasya tathātyaye ||
striyo'pi sambhave kāryā bālena sthavireṇa vā || 15 ||
śiṣyeṇa bandhunā vāpi dāsena bhṛtakena vā ||
bālavṛddhāturāṇāṃ tu sākṣye tu vadatāṃ mṛṣā ||16||
jānīyādadhivācamutsiktamanasāṃ tathā ||
gṛhiṇaḥ putriṇo maulāḥ śūdrā viṭkṣatrayonayaḥ ||17||
atyuktāḥ sākṣyamarhanti naye kūṭadhanāpadi ||
āptāḥ sarveṣu varṇeṣu kāryāḥ kāryeṣu sākṣiṇaḥ ||18||
bahutve parigṛhṇīyātsākṣitve vetanādhipam ||
sameṣu tvaguṇotkṛṣṭaṃ guṇitve vai dvijottamān ||19||
samakṣadarśanātsākṣyaṃ śravaṇāccaiva sidhyati ||
tatra satyaṃ bruvansākṣī dharmārthābhyāṃ na hīyate || 3.327.20 ||
sākṣī dṛṣṭaśrutādanyadvibruvannarakaṃ vrajet ||
pṛṣṭāni baddho'pi vadedyathā dṛṣṭaṃ yathāśrutam || 21 ||
eko lubdhastvasākṣī syādbahavo'pi na vā kvacit ||
svabhāvenaiva yadbrūyustadgrāhyaṃ vyāvahārikam || 22||
na jātu sākṣiṇaḥ prāptānarthipratyarthisannidhau ||
prāḍvivāko niyuṃjīta vidhinānena sāntvayan || 23 ||
yattayoranayorvettha kārye'smiṃśceṣṭite'pi vā ||
tad brūta sarvaṃ satyena yuṣmākaṃ hyatra sākṣitam || 24 ||
devabrāhmaṇasānnidhye sākṣyaṃ pṛcchettato dvijāḥ ||
udaṅmukhaḥ prāṅmukho vā pūrvāhṇe vai śuciḥ śucīn || 25 ||
brūhīti brāhmaṇaṃ pṛcchetsatyaṃ brūhīti pārthivam ||
gobījakāñcanairvaiśyaṃ śūdraṃ sarvaistu pātakaiḥ || 26 ||
brahmaṇe ca smṛtā lokā ye ca strībāla ghātinaḥ ||
mitradruhaḥ kṛtaghnasya tatte syurbruvatāṃ mṛṣā || 27 ||
janmaprabhṛti yatkiñcitkiñcitpuṇyaṃ tvayā kṛtam ||
tatsarvaṃ tasya jānīhi yaṃ parājayase mṛṣā || 28 ||
nagno muṇḍaḥ kapālī ca bhikṣārthī kṣutpipāsitaḥ ||
andhaḥ śatrugṛhaṃ gacchedyatsākṣyamanṛtaṃ vadet || 29 ||
etāṃstu doṣānvīkṣya tvaṃ sarvānanṛtabhāṣaṇāt ||
yathāśrutaṃ yathādṛṣṭaṃ sarvamevāñjasā vada || 3.327.30 ||
satyaṃ sākṣyaṃ bruvansākṣī lokānprāpnotyanuttamān ||
iha vānuttamāṃ kīrtiṃ vāgyeṣāṃ brahmapūjitā || 31 ||
manyante vai pāpakṛto na kaścitpaśyatīti naḥ ||
tāṃśca devāḥ prapaśyanti svasyaivāntarapūruṣaḥ || 32 .||
ādityacandrāvanalānilau ca dyaurbhūmirāpo hṛdayaṃ yamaśca ||
ahaśca rātriśca ubhe ca sandhye dharmaśca jānāti narasya vṛttam || 33 ||
iti śrīviṣṇudharmottare tṛtīyakhaṇḍe mā0 vajrasaṃvāde munīnprati haṃsagītāsu sākṣinirṇayo nāma saptaviṃśatyuttaratriśata tamo'dhyāyaḥ ||327||
<3.328>
haṃsa uvāca ||
pauruṣo nirṇayaḥ prokto divyaṃ vakṣyāmyataḥ param ||
asākṣikeṣu tvartheṣu mitho vivadamānayoḥ || 1 ||
rājadrohābhiśāpeṣu sāhaseṣu tathaiva ca ||
vivāde tattvataḥ satyaṃ śapathenāpi lambhayet || 2 ||
maharṣibhiśca devaiśca kāryārthaṃ śapathāḥ kṛtāḥ ||
vasiṣṭho'datta śapathaṃ samaye javane nṛpe || 3 ||
na mṛṣā śapathaṃ kuryātsvalpe'pyarthe naro budhaḥ ||
vṛthā hi śapathaṃ kurvanpretya ceha ca naśyati || 4 ||
sthāvareṣu tu divyāni varjanīyāni pārthivaiḥ ||
likhitena tathā bhuktyā teṣāṃ bhavati sādhanam || 5 ||
kośaṃ dhaṭaṃ viṣaṃ cāgnimudakaṃ taptamāṣakam ||
phālaṃ ca taṇḍulaṃ caiva divyānyaṣṭau vidurbudhāḥ || 6 ||
kośavarjantu divyāni saśiraskāni dāpayet ||
aśiraskaḥ smṛtaḥ kośaḥ kālāntaravibhāvanāta || 7 ||
aśiraskāni divyāni rājñā kaṇṭakaśodhanaiḥ ||
rājñā sarvāṇi deyāni svayaṃ ca parimārgiṇā || || 8 ||
lobhānmohādbhayādvāpi bhidyante sākṣiṇaḥ paraiḥ ||
ajñānādbālabhāvācca bhede dharmo vyavasthitaḥ || 9 ||
sarvadravyeṣu kanakaṃ mūlyaṃ tu parikalpayet ||
māṣakeṇa suvarṇasya ..........kālyamasukhe dvijam || 3.328.10 ||
satyaṃ vadāmīti śapennānyatkiñcana pārthiva ||
śastramādāya vaktavyaṃ kṣattriyeṇa tathaiva ca || 11 ||
dhenuṃ saṃspṛśatā satyaṃ vācyaṃ vaiśyena vai dvijāḥ ||
śūdraḥ kurvīta śapathaṃ tathā sarvaistu pātakaiḥ || 12 ||
kṛṣṇalena suvarṇasya tilavṛddhyā tu śāpayet ||
śūdrānna śāpayedrājā kadācidapi vai dvijāḥ ||13 ||
vipravarjaṃ tathā kośaṃ varṇināṃ dāpayennṛpaḥ ||
suvarṇamāṣakādūrdhvamarvāgaṣṭabhya eva ca || 14 ||
vipravarjaṃ tu vipro'pi varṇināṃ pūrasaṃyutam ||
kośasthāne tu śapathaṃ kārayedavidhārayan || 15 ||
ataḥ paraṃ tu divyānāṃ kuryādanyatamaṃ naraḥ ||
yathā tathā mahīpālaḥ śṛṇudhvaṃ gadato mama || 16 ||
na śīte tu dhaṭaṃ deyamuṣṇakāle hutāśanam ||
varṇināṃ na tathā phālaṃ taṇḍulaṃ mukharogiṇām || 17 ||
śvāsakāsārditānāṃ ca brāhmaṇānāṃ ca no viṣam ||
taptamāṣakamarhanti sarve varṇā niratyayam || 18 ||
na vyādhimarakākrānte deśe kośaṃ tu pātayet ||
mantrairasvaradivyāni sañcayantīha mānavāḥ ||19||
pratimantravidaḥ stambhanānāṃ vināśane ||
niyuñjīta narendropi nibhṛtāṃ dhanavatsalām || 3.328.20 ||
divyānāṃ stambhakaṃ jñātvā pāpānnityaṃ mahīpatiḥ ||
vivāsayetsvakādrāṣṭrātte hi lokasya kaṇṭakāḥ || 21 ||
teṣāmanveṣaṇe yatnaṃ rājā nityaṃ samācaret ||
te hi pāpasamācārāstaskarebhyopi taskarāḥ || 22 ||
dṛgdṛṣṭidoṣātsvalpepi divyeṣu viniyojayet ||
mahatsvapi na cārtheṣu dharmajñāndharmavatsalān || 23 ||
jñātvā dharmiṣṭhatāṃ rājā puruṣassa vicakṣaṇaḥ ||
svalpe vāpyatha vāsvalpe śapathe tānniyojayet || 24 ||
na mithyāvacanaṃ yeṣāṃ janmaprabhṛti vidyate ||
śraddadhyātpārthivasteṣāṃ vacane dharmadarśinām ||25 ||
atyantopahatāḥ pāpā nāstikā bhinnasetavaḥ ||
divyānyarhaṃti sarvāṇi svalpadoṣā api dvijāḥ || 26 ||
ādau bhūmiṃ parīkṣeta dhaṭasyārthe vicakṣaṇaḥ ||
indrasthāne sabhāyāṃ vā digbhāge pūrvadakṣiṇe || 27 ||
dhaṭaṃ niveśayettatra susamāyāṃ tathā bhuvi ||
yajñiyasya tu vṛkṣasya kartavyaṃ maṇḍapadvayam || 28 ||
maṇḍapasya pramāṇaṃ tu saptahastaṃ prakīrtitam ||
dvau hastau nikhanetkāṣṭhaṃ dṛśyaṃ syāddhastapañcakam || 29 ||
antarañca tayoḥ kāryaṃ tathā hastacatuṣṭayam ||
maṇḍapopari kāṣṭhaṃ ca dṛḍhaṃ kuryādvicakṣaṇaḥ || 3.328.30 ||
caturhastaṃ tulākāṣṭhaṃ tvavraṇaṃ kārayetsthiram ||
khadirārjunavṛkṣāṇāṃ śiṃśipāsālajaṃ tathā || 31 ||
tulākāṣṭhaṃ tu kartavyaṃ tathā vai śikyakadvayam ||
prāṅmukho niścalaḥ kāryaḥ śucau deśe dhaṭastathā || 32 ||
vaṇiksuvarṇakārau ca kulālaḥ kāṃsyakārakaḥ ||
tulādhare dhaṭaḥ kāryo ripau mitre tathā samaḥ || 33 ||
śrāvayetprāḍvivākopi tulādhāraṃ vicakṣaṇaḥ ||
brahmaghno ye smṛtā lokā ye ca strībālaghātinām || 34 ||
tulādharasya te lokāstulāṃ dhārayato mṛṣā ||
ekasmim̐stolayecchikye snātaṃ sūpoṣitaṃ naram || 35 ||
dvitīye mṛttikāṃ śubhrāṃ gaurīṃ tu tulayedbudhaḥ ||
iṣṭakābhasmapāṣāṇakapālāsthīni varjayet || 36 ||
tulayitvā tataḥ pūrvaṃ tasmāttamavatārayet ||
mūrdhni patraṃ tatastasya nyastapatraṃ niveśayet || 37 ||
mantraścāyaṃ purā proktaḥ svayameva svayambhuvā ||
brahmaṇastvaṃ sutā devi tulānāmnīti kathyase || 38 ||
tukāro gauravo nityaṃ lakāro laghuni smṛtaḥ ||
gurulāghavasaṃyogāttulā tena nigadyase || 39 ||
saṃśayānmocaya svainaṃ cābhiśastaṃ naraṃ śubhe ||
bhūya āropayettaṃ tu naraṃ tasyāṃ sapatrakam || 3.328.40 ||
tulito yadi vardheta śubho bhavati dharmataḥ ||
hīyamāne na śuddhaḥ syāditi dharmavido viduḥ || 41 ||
śikyacchede tulābhaṅge punarāropayennaram ||
tathā niḥsaṃśayaṃ jñānaṃ bhavatīti viniścayaḥ || 42 ||
agnervidhiṃ pravakṣyāmi śṛṇudhvaṃ gadato mama ||
saptamaṇḍalakānkuryāddevabrāhmaṇasannidhau || 43 ||
pratyakṣaṃ kārayeddivyaṃ rājño vādhikṛtasya vā ||
brāhmaṇānāṃ śrutavatāṃ prakṛtīnāṃ tathaiva ca || 44 ||
paścime maṇḍale tiṣṭhetprāṅmukhaḥ prāñjaliḥ śuciḥ ||
caraturasre yathā asminkṛtvā caiva samau karau || 45 ||
lakṣayeyuḥ kṣatādīni hastayostasya kāriṇaḥ ||
saptāśvatthasya patrāṇi badhnīyuḥ karayostataḥ || 46 ||
navena sitasūtreṇa kārpāsena dvijottamāḥ ||
tatastu susamaṃ kṛtvā aṅgulaṃ tvekamāyasam ||47||
piṇḍaṃ hutāśasantaptaṃ pañcāśatpalikaṃ dṛḍham ||
ādau pūjāṃ hutāśasya kārayedvasudhādhipaḥ ||48||
raktacandanadhūmābhyāṃ raktapuṣpaistathaiva ca ||
abhiśastasya mantraṃ ca badhnīyāttasya mūrdhani || 49 ||
mantreṇānena saṃyuktaṃ brāhmaṇābhihitena tu ||
tvamagne vedāścatvārastvaṃ ca yajñeṣu hūyase||3.328.50||
tvaṃ mukhaṃ sarvadevānāṃ tvaṃ mukhaṃ brahmavādinām||
jaṭharasthopi bhūtānāṃ tvaṃ ca vetsi śubhāśubham||
pāpeṣu darśayātmānamarciṣmānbhava pāvaka || 51 ||
atha vā śuddhabhāveṣu śīto bhava mahābala ||
tatobhiśastaḥ śanakairmaṇḍalāni parikramet || 52 ||
parikramya śanairjahyāllohapiṇḍaṃ tathā kṣitau ||
vimuktahastaṃ taṃ paścātkārayedvrīhimarddanam || 53 ||
nirvikārau karau dṛṣṭvā śuddho bhavati dharmataḥ ||
bhayādvā pātayedyastu dagdho vā na vibhāvyate ||54||
punastu cāharellohaṃ vidhireṣa prakīrtitaḥ ||
toyasyātha pravakṣyāmi vidhiṃ dharmyaṃ sanātanam || 55 ||
śaivālanakramatsyādyairvarjite sthāvare jale ||
dharmasthūṇā tatra kāryā puruṣaṃ dharmavatsalam || 56 ||
nābhidaghnodakasthasya gṛhītvottaṃ samāviśet ||
madhyamenātha dhanuṣā madhyasthaḥ puruṣo'paraḥ || 57||
iṣumokṣaṃ tadā kuryāddeśe tṛṇavivarjite ||
śaramokṣe same kāle nimajjetpuruṣopyasau || 58 ||
mūrdhni vinyastapatrastu petrai caitānniveśayet ||
ādidevo'si devānāṃ śaucasyāpyāyanaṃ param || 59 ||
pañcānāṃ lokapālānāṃ tvaṃ variṣṭhaṃ jagatpate ||
trāyasvainaṃ naraṃ pāpātpaśyasi tvaṃ śubhāśubhama || 3.328.60 ||
antaścarasi bhūtānāṃ sarveṣāṃ mokṣavatsthitaḥ ||
iṣuprakṣepaṇasthānādyuvā ca balavānnaraḥ ||| || 61 ||
dhāvansa parayā śaktyā śaraṃ tatsamupātayet ||
āgate tu śaragrāhe toyasthaścenna dṛśyate || 62 ||
śuddho bhavati dharmeṇa yaścāpyaṅgaṃ na darśayet ||
viṣasyātaḥ pravakṣyāmi vidhiṃ dharmyaṃ sanātanam ||63||
śṛṅgiṇo vatsanābhasya himaśailodbhavasya ca||
yavāssapta pradātavyā atha vā yadghṛtāplutāḥ || 64 ||
mūrdhni vinyastapatrasya patre caitanniveśayet ||
tvaṃ viṣa brahmaṇaḥ putraḥ satye dharme vyavasthitaḥ | 65 ||
trāyasvainaṃ naraṃ pāpātsatyenāsya bhavāmṛtam ||
yena vegairvinā jīrṇaṃ chardimūrcchāvivarjitam|| 66 ||
taṃ tu śuddhaṃ vijānīyāditi dharmavido viduḥ ||
taṇḍulānāṃ pravakṣyāmi vidhiṃ dharmyaṃ sanātanam || 67 ||
caurye tu taṇḍulā deyā na cānyatra kathaṃcana ||
taṇḍulānudake siktvā rātrau tatraiva sthāpayeta || 68 |||
taṇḍulānkārayecchuddhāñśālīnāṃ pṛthivīpatiḥ ||
mṛnmayaṃ bhājanaṃ kṛtvā savituḥ purataḥ sthitaḥ || 69 ||
taṇḍulānmaṃtrayecśuddhānmaṃtreṇānena dharmataḥ ||
dīyate sattvadharmajñairmānuṣāṇāṃ viśodhanam ||3.328.70||
tatastaṇḍula satyena dharmatastrātumarhasi ||
prabhāte kārayedeva bhakṣaṇāya na saṃśayaḥ ||71||
trividhaḥ prāṅmukhasyeha patre niṣṭhīvayettataḥ ||
pippalasyātha bhūrjasya na tvanyasya kathaṃcana || 72 ||
śoṇitaṃ dṛśyate yasya tamaśuddhaṃ vinirdiśet ||
athātaḥ saṃprakṣyāmi taptamāṣakanirṇayam || 73 ||
kārayedāyasaṃ pātraṃ tāmraṃ vā ṣoḍaśāṅgulam ||
caturaṅgulakhātaṃ tu mṛnmayaṃ vāpi kārayet || || 74 ||
pūrayedghṛtatailābhyāṃ palairviṃśatibhistataḥ ||
sutapte nikṣipettatra suvarṇasya tu māṣakam || 75 ||
vahnyaktaṃ vinyasenmantramabhiśastasya mūrdhani ||
aṅguṣṭhāṅguliyogena taptamāṣaṃ samuddharet || 76 ||
śuddhaṃ jñeyamadagdhaṃ tu visphoṭādivivarjitam ||
phālaśuddhiṃ pravakṣyāmi śṛṇudhvaṃ dvijapuṅgavāḥ || 77 ||
āyasaṃ dvādaśapalaṃ ghaṭitaṃ phālamucyate ||
aṣṭāṅgulaṃ samaṃ dīrghaṃ caturaṃgulavistṛtam || 78 ||
vahnyaktaṃ vinyasetpatramabhiśastasya mūrdhani ||
triḥ parāvartayejjihvāṃ lihatassārdhamaṅgulam || 79 ||
gavāṃ kṣīraṃ pradātavyaṃ jihvāśodhanamuttamam ||
jihvāparīkṣaṇaṃ kuryāddagdhāṃ cenna vibhāvyate||3.328.80||
taṃ viśuddhaṃ vijānīyādviśuddhaṃ caiva mokṣayet ||
koṣasyātha pravakṣyāmi vidhiṃ dharmyaṃ sanātanam || 81 ||
yoyo yāṃ devatābhaktaḥ pāyayettasya tūttaram ||
samabhaktaṃ tu devānāmādityasyaiva kārayet||82||
ugrāṇāmapi devānāṃ vadhasyaiva tu dāpayet ||
sarveṣāmeva devānāṃ snāpayedāyudhaṃ svakam ||83||
snānodakaṃ vā sakalamāhareta vicakṣaṇaḥ ||
unmukhaṃ karaṇaṃ kṛtvā pāyayetprasṛtitrayam ||84||
idaṃ mayā naiva kṛtaṃ kṛtaṃ vāpyatha śāyinā||85||
pīte tu kośe puruṣasya dṛṣṭā devopaghātaṃ nṛpasaṃbhavaṃ vā ||
triḥsaptarātrāṃtarato nṛpeṇa bhṛśaṃ vineyaḥ puruṣaḥ sa viprāḥ||86||
iti śrīviṣṇudharmottare tṛtīyakhaṇḍe mārkaṇḍeyavajrasaṃvāde munīnprati haṃsagītāsu divyapramāṇanirūpaṇaṃ nāmāṣṭāviṃśatyuttaratriśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ || || 328 ||

haṃsa uvāca ||
ekā dve ca tathā tisraścatasraśca yathākramam ||
śūdraviṭkṣatraviprāṇāṃ bhāryāḥ proktā manīṣibhiḥ || 1 ||
śūdraiva bhāryā śūdrasya sā ca svā ca viśaḥ smṛte ||
te ca svā ca tathā rājñastāśca svā cāgrajanmanaḥ || 2 ||
na vivarṇā bhavedbhāryā kadācidapi kasyacit ||
savarṇaiva bhavejjyeṣṭhā asavarṇāsu satsvapi || 3 ||
aṣṭau vivāhā nirdiṣṭāsteṣāṃ vakṣyāmi lakṣaṇam ||
āhūya dānādbrāhmastu ṛtvijairdaiva ucyate || || 4 ||
goyugagrahaṇādārṣaḥ prājāpatyorthite tathā ||
āsuro draviṇādānādgāndharvastu matāddvayoḥ || 9 ||
rākṣaso yuddhaharaṇātpaiśācaḥ kanyakācchalāt ||
prājāpatyastathā daivo brāhmaścāpi śubhāstrayaḥ || 6 ||
gāndharvo rākṣasaścaiva madhyamau parikīrtitau ||
āsuraścaiva paiśāca ārṣaścaiva tathādhamaḥ ||| || 7 ||
na te kāryāḥ prayatnena kadācidapi kenacit ||
āsurasya tathārṣasya viśeṣo naiva vidyate || 8 ||
śuklameva tato jñeyamalpaṃ vā yadi vā bahu||
prāṇivikrayiṇaḥ sarve narakaṃ yānti mānavāḥ||||
viśeṣeṇa ca yaḥ putraṃ vikrīṇāti svakaṃ naraḥ ||
narake vasatiṃ tasya bahūnabdagaṇānviduḥ || 3.329.10||
mṛte bhartari yā kanyā kevalaṃ hastadūṣitā ||
sā cedakṣatayoniḥ syātpunaḥ saṃskāramarhati || 11||
ityevaṃ kecidicchanti na tanmama mataṃ dvijāḥ ||||
saptame hi pade vṛtte nānyamarhati sā patim||12||
vācā dattā tu yā kanyā mantraiścaiva na saṃskṛtā||
anyasya sā bhaveddeyā sati bhartari doṣiṇī||3||
naṣṭe mṛte pravrajite klībe'tha patite patau||
pañcasvāpatsu nārīṇāṃ patiranyo vidhīyate||14||
pitāpitāmaho bhrātā sakulyo jananī tathā ||
kanyāpradaḥ pūrvanāśe prakṛtisthaḥ paraḥ paraḥ || 15 ||
yastu doṣamanākhyāya kanyakāṃ saṃprayacchati ||
sa rājñā dviśataṃ dattvā bibhṛyāttāṃ ca kanyakām ||16 ||
aduṣṭāmatha yo duṣṭāṃ brūyāddoṣeṇa mānavaḥ ||
prathamaṃ sāhasaṃ tasya rājā daṇḍaṃ prakalpayet || 17 ||
yastu doṣavatīṃ patnīṃ samyaktvenānuvarttate ||
daṇḍayitvā svaśaktyā taṃ same pathi niveśayet||18||
bhartāraṃ laṅghayedyā tu strī jñātibaladarpitā||
tāṃ śvabhiḥ khādayedrājā saṃsthāne vadhyaghātinām||19||
bhāryāyā vyabhicāriṇyā parityāgo vidhīyate || 3.329.20 ||
hīnavarṇagame cāsyā vadhamuktaṃ manīṣibhiḥ ||
bhartranujñāṃ vinā rājā strīṇāṃ ca prabhavet kvacit || 21 ||
aśaktabhartṛkāṃ nārīṃ śāsayettadanujñayā ||
yonyasmai kanyakāṃ dattvā bhūyonyasmai prayacchati || 22 ||
vittasyāṃśaṃ turīyaṃ tu taṃ rājā daṇḍayennaram ||
bhartṛdoṣe samutpanne na sā daṇḍamavāpnuyāt || 23 ||
yastu na vrajate bhāryāmṛtukāle narādhamaḥ ||
ṛtāvṛtau bhaveddaṃḍyaḥ sa suvarṇasya māṣakam || 24 ||
āhūyamānā yā bhāryā bhartāraṃ na vrajedṛtau ||
vivāsyā sā bhavedbhartrā doṣaṃ vyākhyāpya mātaram ||25 ||
yastyajecca tathā bhāryāmaduṣṭāṃ puruṣādhamaḥ ||
sa suvarṇaṃ bhaveddaṃḍyo bibhṛyāttāṃ tathā punaḥ || 26 ||
viṣamaṃ pariṇītāsu vartate yastu mānavaḥ ||
savarṇāsu bhaveddaṇḍyaḥ suvarṇaṃ sa mahīkṣitā || 27 ||
trīṇi varṣāṇyupāsīta kumāryṛtumatī satī ||
uktakālātparaṃ kanyā vindeta sadṛśaṃ patim || 28 ||
alaṅkāraṃ nādadīta pitryaṃ kanyā svayaṃ varāt ||
mātṛkaṃ bhrātṛdattaṃ vā steyaṃ vā syāddhi taṃ haret || 29 ||
kanyāntu cedṛtumatīṃ puruṣo na dadyācchaktopi na dvijavaraśca sa daṇḍanīyaḥ||
svāmyaṃ na tāṃ prati ca tasya bhavetkadācitputrīmṛtau na dadato na punardvijatvama||3.329.30||
iti śrīviṣṇudharmottare tṛtīya khaṇḍe mārkaṇḍeyavajrasaṃvāde munīnprati haṃsagītāsu vivāhadharmanirūpaṇo nāmaikonatriṃśaduttaratriśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ || 329 ||

|| haṃsa uvāca || ||
aurasaḥ putrikāputraḥ kṣetrajaśca tathā paraḥ ||
paunarbhavaśca kānīnaḥ sahoḍhaśca tathā sutaḥ || 1 ||
gūḍhotpanno dattakaśca krītaścaiva tathā paraḥ ||
apaviddhastūpagataḥ śūdrāputrastathaiva ca || 2 ||
ete dvādaśa vai putrāsteṣāṃ vakṣyāmi lakṣaṇam ||
saṃskṛtāyāṃ svayaṃ jātaḥ putra aurasa ucyate || 3 ||
asyāmutpadyate putro yaḥ sa me bhavitā sutaḥ ||
eṣā dattāpi yā pitrā putrikā sā nigadyate || 4 ||
vinā vacanadattāpi bhrātṛhīnā tu putrikā |||
tatputraḥ putrikāputro dvitīyaḥ parikīrtitaḥ || 5 ||
devarādvā sagotrādvā brāhmaṇādvā vicakṣaṇāt ||
niyogotpāditaḥ putraḥ kṣetrajaḥ parikīrtitaḥ ||6|||
svairiṇī yā patiṃ hitvā kāmatonyaṃ samāśrayet ||
paunarbhavastu tatputraḥ caturthaḥ parikīrtitaḥ || 7 ||
asaṃskṛtāyāmutpannaḥ kānīno bhartureva saḥ ||
garbhiṇī yā saṃskriyate sahoḍhastatsuto mataḥ || 8 ||
gṛhe ca gūḍhamutpanno gṛhotpannaḥ prakīrtitaḥ ||
tasyāṃ sakāśātkrītaśca krītaputro nigadyate ||
tābhyāṃ caivāpaviddhastu tvapaviddhastu sa smṛtaḥ ||9||
vidhṛto yena yasyāsau putratvena nigadyate ||
svayaṃ yaścābhyupagataḥ putratve dvijasattamāḥ||3.330.10||
ādau tu putrikāputraḥ paścājjātastvathaurasaḥ ||
aurasena samāṃśastu tatra syātputrikāsutaḥ||11||
jāte tu putrikāputre kṣetrajotpādanaṃ vṛthā ||
samāḥ samāṃśino jñeyā yādṛśāstādṛśāḥ sutāḥ ||12||
pratilomabhavāḥ putrā na tu rikthasya bhāginaḥ ||
dhanabhāgbharaṇaṃ teṣāṃ karmaṇā kartumarhati || 13 ||
tadapatyāḥ pitṛvadhe bhāginaḥ parikīrtitāḥ ||
paitāmahena te vitte svāmyamarhati kenacit || 14 ||
jaḍāndhabadhironmattā bhartavyāstu niraṃśakāḥ ||
teṣāmrimṛkthaharāḥ putrā dhane paitāmahe sutāḥ||15||
patitānāṃ na bhāgosti tatsutānāṃ ca dharmataḥ ||
karmaṇaḥ prāksamutpannāḥ patanīyasya ye sutāḥ||16||
teṣāmrikthaṃ pradātavyaṃ na tu te patitāḥ smṛtāḥ||
prāyaścitte tathā cīrṇe patito bhāgamarhati||17||
tatputro'pi tathā viprā nātra kāryā vicāraṇā ||
pitā cedvibhajetputrānsvecchā tasya svake dhane || 18 ||
paitāmahe tvasvatantraḥ pitā bhavati dharmataḥ ||
pitāmahārjite vitte pituḥ putrasya cobhayoḥ || 19 ||
svāmyantu sadṛśaṃ jñeyaṃ putraścedguṇavānbhavet ||
piturūrdhvaṃ vibhajatāṃ mātāpyaṃśaṃ susaṃharet || 3.330.20 ||
savarṇāstu sutāḥ sarve tathā jñeyāḥ samāṃśinaḥ ||
jyeṣṭhasya kiñcidadhikaṃ dātavyaṃ nātra saṃśayaḥ || 21 ||
brahmadevāsutasyāpi tau hi mānyatamau samau ||
paiśācā'surayorjātau yathā paunarbhavastathā || 22 ||
catasrastu yadā bhāryā bhavanti brāhmaṇasya tu ||
tāsāṃ putreṣu dāyaṃ yattadvittaṃ vibhajennṛpaḥ || 23 ||
caturoṃśānharedviprastrīnaṃśānkṣatriyāsutaḥ ||
vaiśyāputro hareddvyaṃśamaṃśaṃ śūdrāsuto haret || 24 ||
brāhmaṇau yadi putrau dvau caikaḥ śūdrāsuto bhavet ||
navadhā brāhmaṇadhanaṃ vibhajyamiti niścayaḥ || 25 ||
aṣṭau brāhmaṇayorbhāgāḥ śūdrasya navamo bhavet ||
dvau vijñeyau sutau syātāmekaśca brāhmaṇīsutaḥ || 26 ||
brāhmaṇasvaṃ tathā ṣoḍhā vibhajyamiti niścayaḥ ||
dvāvaṃśau śūdrayordeyau caturoṃśā hi vai dvijāḥ || 27 ||
anenaivānusāreṇa sarvatraivāṃśakalpanā ||
asavarṇāsu jātānāṃ kartavyā pṛthivīkṣitā || 28 ||
ṣoḍhā tatkṣattriyadhanaṃ kartavyamiti niścayaḥ ||
kṣattriyāyāṃ tataḥ putrā vittatoṃśatrayaṃ haret || 29 ||
vaiśyāputro hareddvyaṃśamaṃśaṃ śūdrāsuto haret ||
vaiśyāsvaṃ tu tridhā kāryaṃ śṛṇu tatrāpi kalpanā || 3.330.30 ||
vaiśyāputro haredvyaṃśamaṃśaṃ śūdrāsuto haret ||
aputrasya tu dauhitro rikthamarhati dharmataḥ||31||
dauhitrasyāpyabhāve tu duhitāpi dhanaṃ haret||
tadabhāve tadā bhāryā tadabhāve sahodaraḥ ||
tasyāpyabhāve jananī tadabhāve pitā haret ||
bhrātarastadabhāve tu bhrātṛputrastato haret || 32 ||
sahodarabhrātṛsuto vidyamāne kathaṃcana ||
nānyabhrātṛbhavo bhrātā prāpnuyāditi niścayaḥ || 33 ||
anyabhrātṛbhavabhrātuḥ putro yatra vyavasthitaḥ ||
pitṛvyabhrātṛputrastu naiva tatra dhanaṃ haret || 34 ||
anantaraṃ sapiṇḍādyastasyatasya dhanaṃ haret ||
tadabhāve sakulyasya tadabhāve gurorbhavet || 35 ||
śiṣyasya tadabhāve tu dvijānāṃ tadabhāvataḥ ||
traividyāḥ samayā dāntāstasya vittasya bhāginaḥ || 36 ||
brāhmaṇasvamanāhāryaṃ pārthivānāṃ sadā bhavet ||
itareṣāntu varṇānāṃ sarvābhāve harennṛpaḥ ||37||
putrau tu vivadeyātāṃ dvābhyāṃ jātau striyā dhane ||
tayoryadyasya pitryaṃ syāttatsaṃgṛhṇīta netaraḥ ||38||
mātṛkaṃ yadbhavedvittaṃ kumārīṇāṃ tu tadbhavet ||
tadabhāve tu soḍhānāṃ sutānāṃ tadabhāvataḥ || 39 ||
adhyagnyadhyāvanikaṃ dattaṃ ca prītikarmaṇā ||
bhrātṛmātṛpitṛprāpte ṣaḍvidhaṃ strīdhanaṃ smṛtam || 3.330.40 ||
brāhmadaivārṣagāndharvaprājāpatyeṣu yaddvayam ||
aprajāyāmatītāyāṃ bhartureva tadiṣyate ||41||
yathānyeṣu pitṛgāmi . . . dhanāni . . . bhavet ||
patyau jīvati yaḥ strībhiralaṅkāro dhṛto bhavet||42||
na taṃ bhajerandāyādā bhajamānāḥ patanti te ||
bhrātṛṇāmekajātānāṃ yadyekaḥ putravānbhavet || 43 ||
sarve te tena putreṇa putravanta iti śrutiḥ ||
sarvāsāmekapatnīnāmekā cetputriṇī bhavet || 44 ||
putravatyastu tāssarvāstena putreṇa kīrtitāḥ ||
anupaghnaṃ pitṛdravyaṃ śrameṇa yadupārjitam || 45 ||
svayamīhitalabdhaṃ taṃ nākāmo dātumarhati ||
yeṣāṃ jyeṣṭhaḥ kaniṣṭho vā hīyetāṃśapradānataḥ || 46 ||
mriyetānyataro vā'pi tasya bhāgo na nulupyate ||
sodaryā vibhajeraṃstaṃ sametya sahitāḥ samam || 47 ||
bhrātaro ye ca saṃsṛṣṭā bhaginyaśca sajātayaḥ ||
vibhakte tu dhane jātāḥ pitryameva dhanaṃ haret || 48 ||
vibhaktairvāsya dātavyamaṃśamaṃśānurūpataḥ ||
svabhāgasyānusāreṇa dattvā bhāgaṃ yathākramam || 49 ||
asaṃskṛtāstu saṃskāryā vibhaktaiḥ kanyakāstathā ||
pravibhāge kṛte yatra paścāchiṣyeta vai dhanam || 3.330.50 ||
pūrvoktenānusāreṇa vibhāgaṃ tatra kārayet ||
vastraṃ patramalaṅkāraṃ kṛtānnamudavakaṃ striyaḥ || 51 ||
yogakṣemapracāre ca na vibhajyaṃ ca pustakam ||
rikthagrāhī ṛṇaṃ dadyātpiṇḍaṃ dadyātsa eva tu || 52 ||
bhartavyānbibhṛyāttasya sarvāniti matirmama ||
rikthagrāhī na yaḥ kuryātpretakarma yathāvidhi ||53 ||
taskaraṃ taṃ svakādrāṣṭrātkṣipraṃ rājā vivāsayet ||
rājagāmī bhavedviprā yasya vittaṃ tu karhicit || 54 ||
rājāpi dāpayetpiṇḍaṃ tasya bhṛtyairyathāvidhi ||
yasyāṃ narāṇāmiha vittabhāgī ṛṇasya bhāgī sa tathā niruktaḥ ||
ṛṇāvaśiṣṭaṃ puruṣastu nityaṃ bhuktvā dvijendrāḥ sukṛtī sadā syāt || 55 ||
iti śrīviṣṇudharmottare tṛtīyakhaṇḍe mārkaṇḍeyavajrasaṃvāde munīnprati haṃsagītāsu dāyavibhāgavarṇano nāma triṃśaduttaratriśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ || 330 ||
<3.331>
|| haṃsa upāca ||
uttamarṇāya dātavyamadhamarṇena yācitam ||
brāhmaṇendrā dhanaṃ sarvaṃ nihnave tacca bhāvitam || 1 ||
sarvopāyairdhanī vittamṛṇikādāpnuyātsvakam ||
na sa rājñābhiyoktavyaḥ svakaṃ saṃsādhayandhanam || 2 ||
arthanihnavamānaṃ tu bhāvanābhirvibhāvitam ||
dāpayeddhanikasyārthaṃ rājño daṇḍaṃ ca tatsamam || 3||
mithyābhiyoge dviguṇamiti yogāddhanaṃ haret ||
bhāgaṃ viṃśatimandadyātprāptārtho dhaniko nṛpe || 4 ||
yadi rājabalenāsau taddhi tasmādavāpnuyāt ||
dvikaṃ trikaṃ catuṣkaṃ ca pañcakaṃ ca tathā śatam || 5 ||
varṇānukramato vṛddhiḥ pratimāsaṃ prakīrtitā ||
svayaṃ kṛtāṃ tathā vṛddhiṃ sarve sarvāsu yoniṣu || 6||
dātumarhati viprendrā iti me niścitā matiḥ ||
na kvacitprītidattānāṃ vṛddhiḥ saṃvatsaraṃ bhavet || 7 ||
teṣāmapi bhavedvṛddhirgate saṃvatsare tathā ||
sā ca vṛddhiryathoktā vai varṇānāmanupūrvaśaḥ || 8 ||
tathāvidhā grahītavyā nānyatheti hi niścayaḥ ||
ādhibhugdīyate vṛddhiṃ naṣṭāmādhiṃ ca dāpayet || 9 ||
daivarājakṛtaṃ doṣaṃ vinā brāhmaṇasattamāḥ ||
antyavṛddhau praviṣṭāyāṃ cārtau tyāgo vidhīyate || 3.331.10 ||
bhojyasyādheḥ parityāgaḥ praviṣṭe tu dhane bhavet ||
bhogasya gaṇanā kāryā na tatra vacanādṛte || 11 ||
dhaninorthaṃ na hīyeta ādhau prāṇayute mṛte ||
hetubhūtastu maraṇe na cetsyāduttamarṇikaḥ || 12 ||
ādhau vivadamānānāṃ bhuktā jīvanti cāparāḥ ||
anyasyādhīkṛtāṃ bhūmiṃ yaścānyasya prayacchati || 13 ||
svayameva narendrastaṃ ghātayedavicārayan ||
dravyeṣvevamathānyeṣu daṇḍaḥ syāddviguṇaṃ bhavet || 14 ||
ādhau samudre naṣṭe tu mudrā yatra na dṛśyate ||
na tatra hīyate vittaṃ dhanikasya kathañcana || 15 ||
anyathā tu tathā naṣṭe dhanikasyaiva hīyate ||
antyā vṛddhirhiraṇyasya dviguṇā parikīrtitā || 16 ||
vastrasya triguṇā proktā dhānyasya tu caturguṇā ||
rasasyāṣṭaguṇā vṛddhiḥ strīpaśūnāṃ ca santatiḥ || 17 ||
strīpaśūnāmṛte vṛddhiḥ paśūnāṃ caiva vidyate ||
urabhrāṇāṃ bhavedvṛddhiḥ sati puṃstve hi vai dvijāḥ || 18 ||
ūrṇāvṛddhirbhavetteṣāṃ varṣāṇi dvādaśaiva tu ||
dravyāṇāmapyanuktānāṃ vṛddhirjñeyā caturguṇā || 19 ||
dīyamānaprayuktaṃ tu puruṣasyāpragṛhṇataḥ ||
tataḥ paraṃ na vṛddhiḥ syātkathaṃciddvijasattamāḥ || 3.331.20 ||
svamarthaṃ yo na gṛhṇāti dīyamānaṃ punaḥpunaḥ ||
tasmātsa hīyate viprā dātā tatrānṛṇo bhavet || 21 ||
pitā pravasito yasya brāhmaṇā dvidaśāḥ samāḥ ||
ṛṇaṃ tasya sutairdeyaṃ mṛtasya yadi vā punaḥ ||22||
paitṛkaṃ tu ṛṇaṃ putraḥ pautro vā dātumarhati||
na kāmastaccaturthaṃ tu dātumarhati karhicit|| || 23 ||
rikthagrāhī tathā putraḥ strīgrāhī dhanadāsikaḥ ||
putre sthitepi dātavyamṛṇaṃ vai dhanahāriṇāma || 24 ||
rikthagrāhī sutābhāve strīgrāhī dātumarhati ||
svayaṃ dātumaśakto yaḥ kartumicchetpunaḥ kriyām || 25 ||
sa dattvā nirjitāṃ vṛddhiṃ kārayetparivarttayet ||
adarśayitvā tatraiva hiraṇyaṃ parivartayan || 26 ||
yāvatī saṃbhavedvṛddhistāvatīṃ dātumarhati||
prātibhāvye vṛthā dānaṃ dyūtamadhyayanaṃ ca yat || 27 ||
daṇḍaśulkāvaśeṣaṃ tu na putro dātumarhati ||
darśane pratyaye dāne prātibhāvyaṃ tridhā smṛtam ||28||
ādau tu vitathe dadyāditarasya sutāvapi ||
darśane pratibhūryatra mṛtaḥ prātyayiko'pi vā || 29 ||
na tatputrā ṛṇaṃ dadyurdadyurdānāya ye sthitāḥ ||
prakāśaṃ pratibhūryatra dāpito dhanināṃ dhanam ||
soyaṃ tasya tu taddravyamṛṇiko dātumarhati || 3.331.30||
varṇatrayaṃ vipravarā ṛṇārthaṃ kṣīṇe viśuddhāviha karma kāryam ||
kṣīṇograjaṃ sā tu yathodayaṃ taddāpyo na karmāharaṇe tadartham || || 31 ||
iti śrīviṣṇudharmottare tṛtīyakhaṇḍe mārkaṇḍeyavajrasaṃvāde munīnprati haṃsagītāsu ṛṇadānavarṇano nāmaikatriṃśadadhikatriśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ || 331 ||
<3.332>
|| haṃsa uvāca || ||
śīlāḍhye kulaje śaktaḥ nikṣepe nikṣipedbudhaḥ ||
yācyamānaṃ tathaivāsau tasya taddātumarhati || 1 ||
yācate yaśca nikṣipya nikṣiptaṃ na prayacchati ||
tāvubhau cauravacchāsyau dharmmajñena mahīkṣitā || 2 ||
nikṣepopanidhī caiva na deyau pratyanantare ||
naśyete vanipātetāvanipāte tvanāśinā || 3 ||
yasya haste vinikṣiptaṃ tasya haste tathā vinā ||
naṣṭaṃ tu yadi nikṣiptaṃ nāsau dadyātkathañcana ||4||
nikṣepabhogī daṇḍyaḥ syādbhogamūlyantu ṣaḍguṇam ||
eṣa eva vidhirdṛṣṭo yācitānvāhiteṣu ca ||
śilpiṣūpanidhau nyāse pratinyāse tathaiva ca || 5 ||
pratigṛhṇāti yo gaṇḍaṃ yaśca sa pradhane naraḥ ||
tasyāpyeṣa bhaveddharmmaḥ ṣaḍete nidhanāḥ smṛtāḥ || 6 ||
nikṣeparakṣā puruṣeṇa kāryā yatnena viprapravarāssadaiva ||
nikṣepaharttā narakaṃ prayāti kalpāvaśeṣaṃ nihitastu sarvaiḥ || || 7 ||
iti śrīviṣṇudharmottare tṛtīyakhaṇḍe mārkaṇḍeyavajrasaṃvāde munīnprati haṃsagītāsu nikṣeparakṣāvarṇano nāma dvātriṃśadadhikatriśata tamo'dhyāyaḥ || 332 ||
<3.333>
haṃsa uvāca ||
vaṇikprabhṛtayo yatra karma saṃbhūya kurvate ||
tatsaṃbhūya samutthānaṃ śṛṇudhvaṃ gadato mama || 1 ||
phalahetorupāyena karma saṃbhūya kurvatām ||
ādhārabhūtaḥ prakṣepaḥ tatastiṣṭheyuraṃśakāḥ || 2 ||
samo'tirikto hīno vā yatrāṃśo yasya yādṛśaḥ ||
kṣayavyayau tathā vṛddhistatra tasya tathāvidhā || 3 ||
sarveṣāṃ ca mataṃ kāryaṃ bhāṇḍapiṇḍavyayādikam ||
samaṃ sarvasya vibhajam̐lloke tasmānmahīyate || 4 ||
daivike rājake vāpi vyasane samupasthite ||
tatra tatra svaśaktyā tu tasyāṃśo daśamaḥ smṛtaḥ || 5 ||
ekasya cetsyādvyasanaṃ dāyādastadavāpnuyāt ||
tasyābhāve ca sarveṣāṃ teṣāmeva hi taddhanam ||6||
ṛtvijo vyasaneṣvevaṃ mānyastvaṃ karmanistaraḥ ||
labhate dakṣiṇābhāgaṃ sa tasmātsaṃprakalpitam || || 7 ||
ṛtvigyājyāṃ ca duṣṭau tau santyajetāṃ parasparam ||
prathamaṃ sāhasaṃ daṃḍyau na ca tau tyāgamarhataḥ || 8 ||
anvāhitaṃ yācitakaṃ sārdhiḥ sādhāraṇaṃ ca yat ||
nikṣepaḥ putradāraṃ ca sarvasvaṃ cānvaye sati || 9 ||
adeyāni dvijaśreṣṭhā yaścānyasmai pratiśrutam ||
adattaṃ tu bhavetkrodhabhayaśokānvitairnaraiḥ || 3.333.10 ||
bālavṛddhāsvatantraiśca mattonmattastathaiva ca ||
ajñānācca tathā pātraṃ spardhayā dvijasattamāḥ || 11 ||
gṛhṇātyayañca yo mohādyaścādeyaṃ prayacchati ||
tāvubhau cauravacchāsyau dharmajñena mahīkṣitā || 12 ||
dattaṃ svayaṃ nātha pareṇa dattaṃ dattaṃ naro yastu haretparasya ||
dhruvaṃ sa viprā narakaṃ prayāti dattaṃ svayaṃ vā na ca yaśca dadyāt || 13 ||
iti śrīviṣṇudharmottare tṛtīyakhaṇḍe mārkaṇḍeyavajrasaṃvāde munīnprati haṃsagītāsu saṃbhūyasamutthānadeyādeyavarṇano nāma trayastriṃśadadhikatriśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ || 333 ||
<3.334>
haṃsa uvāca ||
kṛtakastrividhaḥ proktastasya vakṣyāmi lakṣaṇam ||
ekatra kālāvadhiko deśāvadhirathāparaḥ || 1 ||
karmāvadhistṛtīyastu sarve karmakarāḥ smṛtāḥ ||
adeśakāle karmādau bhṛtakaścettyajedbhṛtim || 2 ||
bhṛtiścādīyate sarvā svāmidoṣo na cedbhavet ||
hārite svāmidoṣeṇa dāpyaśca bhṛtiko bhavet || 3 ||
rājā ca daṇḍanīyaḥ syāttathā paṇaśataṃ bhavet ||
gṛhītamūlyaṃ yaḥ śilpī kāle dravyaṃ na yacchati || 4 ||
dviguṇaṃ dīyate mūlaṃ dravyaṃ dāpyaṃ tathaiva ca ||
taddravyaṃ ca vinā hāniṃ sakalāṃ dātumarhati || 5 ||
bhṛtyadoṣaṃ vinā svāmī bhṛtyamardhapathe tyajet ||
tulyaṃ tu sakalaṃ dadyādrājñaḥ kanakamāṣakam || 6 ||
ārtasya bhaktaṃ dātavyaṃ svasthastatkarma pūrayet ||
svastho'pi cenna tatkuryāddviguṇaṃ bhaktamutsṛjet || 7 ||
pramādānnāśitaṃ dāpyaṃ samaṃ chidrāhatā śatam ||
prasahya yaddhṛtaṃ caurairna taddāpyo bhavetkvacit || 8 ||
toyāgnināśe śaktyā cetpravartenava dāpayet ||
rājñā hṛtaṃ tathā dravyaṃ na sa dāpyaḥ kathaṃcana || 9 ||
yatra guptyā bhavennityaṃ tatra cedvyāpako bhavet ||
tatsarvaṃ dāpayeddravyaṃ yatkiṃcittatra naśyati || 3.334.10 ||
śakaṭādyaṃ tu gṛhṇīyādbhāṭakena tu yo naraḥ ||
adadadbhāṭakaṃ dāpyo vyūḍhasyāpi ca bhāṭakam || 11 ||
bhṛtiṃ yaḥ pratipadyeta vinaiva samayakriyām ||
deśakālau ca śaktiṃ ca bhaktiṃ cāvekṣya śaktitaḥ || 12||
bhṛtistasya pradātavyā dāpyo rājñā balādatha ||
pravrajyāvasito rājño dāsa āmaraṇāntikaḥ || 13 ||
durbhikṣavidhṛto yastu mokṣataḥ prāṇasaṃśayāt ||
pratiśīrṣaṃ pradānena mucyate ca dhvajāhṛtaḥ || 14 ||
grāsamācchādanaṃ deyaṃ sarveṣāmeva sarvadā ||
tathaiva rājā bhṛtyānāṃ bhṛtiṃ varṣaśatādapi || 15 ||
putrapautrānugaścetsyādyadyasau tatra karmakṛt ||
adhyāpane yo bhṛtakastasyāpyeṣa viniścayaḥ || 16 ||
kāle bhṛtyairjayetkiṃcidyadi cittaṃ svakarmaṇā ||
bhaktadasyaiva tatsarvamiti dharmavido viduḥ || 17 ||
bhāryā putraśca dāsaśca traya evādhanā matāḥ ||
yatte samadhigacchanti yasya te tasya taddhanam || 18 ||
varṇānāmānulomyena na dāsyaṃ pratilomataḥ ||
prātilomyena ceddāsyaṃ kārayettaṃ vināśayet || 19 ||
śulkaṃ gṛhītvā paṇyastrī necchatī dviguṇaṃ vahet ||
aprayacchanpumāñśulkaṃ dāpyaṃ tvaṣṭaguṇaṃ bhavet || 3.334.20 ||
paśūnnayedaraṇyaṃ tu pālako rajanīkṣaye ||
tṛptānpītajalānsarvānsāyāhne tu samarpayet || 21 ||
divā vaktavyatā pāle rātrau svāmini tadgṛhe ||
paśūnāṃ vyasanaṃ gopo vyāyacchettatra śaktitaḥ || 22 ||
aśaktānapi pattau tu svāmine tannivedayeta ||
adhyāyacchannavikrośaṃ svāmine vinivedayet || 23 ||
voḍhumarhati gopastu vinayaṃ cāpi rājani ||
naṣṭaṃ vinaṣṭaṃ kṛmibhiḥ śvahataṃ vyasane mṛtam || || 24 ||
hīnaṃ puruṣakāreṇa pālako vinivedayet ||
ajāvike tu saṃruddhe vṛkaiḥ pāle tvanāyati || 25 ||
yāṃ prasahya vṛko hanyātpāle tatkilbiṣaṃ bhavet ||
tāsāṃ ceddaivaruddhānāṃ carantīnāṃ mitho vane||26||
yāmutpatya vṛko hanyānna pālastatra kilbiṣī||
vidviṣya tu hṛtaṃ caurairna pālo dātumarhati || 27 ||
yadi deśe ca kāle ca svāminastasya śaṃsati ||
etena sarvapālānāṃ vivādaḥ samudāhṛtaḥ || 28 ||
sākaṃ saśṛṅgaṃ sakhuraṃ ca carma pradarśayitvātha paśormṛtasya ||
pālasya śuddhiḥ kathitā dvijendrā atonyathā tasya na śuddhiruktā || 29 ||
iti śrīviṣṇudharmottare tṛtīya khaṇḍe mārkaṇḍeyavajrasaṃvāde munīnprati haṃsagītāsu svāmibhṛtyaniyamavarṇano nāma catustriṃśadadhikatriśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ || 334 ||
<3.335>
|| haṃsa uvāca ||
dravyamasvāmivikrītaṃ prāpya svāmī tadāpnuyāt ||
prakāśakrayataḥ śuddhiḥ krītaḥ steyo rahaḥ kramāt || 1 ||
hīnamūlyaṃ balātkrītaṃ tatastaddoṣabhāgbhavet ||
na gṛhetāgamaṃ krītācchuddhistasya tadāgamāt || 2 ||
svāmī tu prāpnuyādvittaṃ prakāśaṃ raha eva vā ||
vikrīya paṇyamūlyena yaḥ krītaṃ na niyacchati || 3 ||
sthāvarasya phalaṃ dāpyo jaṅgamasya kriyāphalam ||
ardhaṃ cenna prahīyeta sodayaṃ paṇyamāvahet || 4 ||
sthānināmeṣa niyamo diglābhaśca diśāgate ||
upahanyeta vā paṇyaṃ dahyetāpahriyeta vā || 5 ||
vikretureva so'nartho vikretā samprayacchati ||
dīyamānaṃ na gṛhṇāti krītaṃ paṇyaṃ tu yaḥ krayī || 6 ||
vikrīṇānaṃ tadanyatra vikretā nāparādhnuyāt ||
nirdoṣaṃ darśayitvā tu sadoṣaṃ yaḥ prayacchati || 7 ||
mūlyaṃ taddviguṇaṃ dāpyā vineyaśca tathā bhavet ||
dattamūlyasya paṇyasya vidhireṣa prakīrtitaḥ || 8 ||
adattenyatra samayānna vikreturatikramaḥ ||
krītvā mūlyena yatpaṇyaṃ duṣkrītaṃ manyate krayī || 9 ||
vikrītaṃ pratideyaṃ tu yathā viprāstathā śṛṇu ||
dvyahādeva bhaveddeyaṃ pañcāhādvāhyameva tu || 3.335.10 ||
saptāhena tathā ratnaṃ lohastasmiṃstathāhani ||
dvipadaṃ cārddhamāsena bījāni daśabhirdinaiḥ ||
tṛtīyehani vastraṃ ca śaraṃ caivāparehani || 11 ||
paribhuktaṃ tu yadvāsaḥ kliṣṭarūpaṃ mahīsamam ||
sadoṣamapi tatkrītaṃ vikreturna bhavetpunaḥ || 12 ||
dravyaṃ tu vikrītamapi svakaṃ hi vināyako vā tadavāpnuyādvai ||
kretā na kuryādyadi vañcanāṃ vā vikretureveha punastadiṣṭam || 13 ||
iti śrīviṣṇudharmottare tṛtīyakhaṇḍe mārkaṇḍeyavajrasaṃvāde munīnprati haṃsagītāsu krayavikrayanirṇayonāma pañcatriṃśadadhikatriśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ ||335|| |

|| haṃsa uvāca || ||

<3.337>
haṃsa uvāca ||

|| haṃsa uvāca ||

|| haṃsa uvāca ||
caturthamāyuṣo bhāgamantyaṃ yatyāśrame vaset ||

indriyebhyaḥ parāṇīha catvāri kathitāni tu ||

gandho rasastathā vāyuḥ sparśaḥ śabdastathā dvijāḥ || 3.340.20 ||
ete pañcaguṇā bhūmeḥ sthūlā bhūmeṣu lakṣyate ||
raso rūpastathā sparśaḥ śabdāścāmbuguṇā matāḥ || 21 ||
rūpaṃ sparśastathā śabdaḥ tejasastu guṇāḥ smṛtā ||
sparśaḥ śabdastathā vāyoḥ śabdaśca nabhasastathā ||22||
ākāśājjāyate śabdo vāyoḥ sparśaguṇastathā ||
rūpaṃ tu tejasaḥ prokto rasaścāmbuguṇo mataḥ|||23||
pārthivaṃ gandhamityāhurindriyārthāḥ prakīrtitāḥ ||
indriyebhyaḥ parā arthā arthebhyastu paraṃ manaḥ ||24||
manasastu parā buddhirbuddherātmā mahānparaḥ ||
mahataḥ paramavyaktaṃ tvavyaktātpuruṣaḥ paraḥ||25||
puruṣānna paraṃ kiñcitsā kāṣṭhā sā parā gatiḥ ||
etatparataraṃ guhyametadakṣaramuttamam||26||
oṃkāravācakaṃ hyetadguṇātītaṃ sanātanam ||
aśabdamarasasparśamarūpaṃ gandhavarjitam || 27||
nirduḥkhamasukhaṃ duḥkhaṃ tadviṣṇoḥ paramaṃ padam ||
vālāgraśataśo bhāgaḥ kalpitastu sahasradhā||28||
tasyāpi śataśo bhāgo jīva ityabhidhīyate ||
hṛdyarkacandramāḥ sūryaḥ somamadhye hutāśanaḥ||29||
tasya madhye sthitaṃ sattvaṃ sattvamadhye sthitocyutaḥ ||
svadehamaraṇiṃ kṛtvā praṇavaṃ cottarāraṇim || 3.340.30 ||
dhyānanirmathanābhyāsādviṣṇuṃ paśyeddhadi sthitam ||
eṣa devastu martyānāṃ vaṇaireva vidhīyate || 31 ||
ajñānapaṭasaṃvītairindriyairviṣayepsubhiḥ ||
eṣa vai puruṣo viṣṇurvyaktāvyaktaḥ sanātanaḥ || || 32 ||
eṣa dhātā vidhātā ca pramāṇo niṣkalaḥ śivaḥ ||
dve brahmaṇī veditavye śābdabrahma paraṃ ca yat ||33||
śābdabrahmaṇi niṣṇātaḥ paraṃ brahmādhi gacchati ||
sūkṣmaṃ nityamupāsīta śubhaṃ ca satataṃ dvijāḥ || 34 ||
sūkṣmasyopāsanācchreṣṭhāṃ yāti tejomayīṃ gatim ||
tattvaṃ vahati vai buddhiṃ buddhistu paramāṅgatim || 35 ||
gatirvahati caṃdrārkau tapasā parameṣṭhinaḥ ||
tayorvahati vai sattvaṃ sattvaṃ ca vahati prabhum || 36 ||
prabhuvarhati saukṣmyaṃ tu paramātmānamātmanaḥ ||
tayorvṛttirajaḥ sattvaṃ sattvasya paramā gatiḥ || 37 ||
sattvaṃ vahati sūkṣmatvātparaṃ nārāyaṇaṃ prabhum ||
paramātmānamāsādya hariṃ vaikuṇṭhamīśvaram || 38 ||
amṛtatvāya kalpeta tato naiva nivarttate ||
daśārdhayuktena rathena yukto manonugenāryapathānuvartinā ||
tañcedrathaṃ nāpavahaṃti vājinastathāgataṃ nāvajayeti śaktaḥ || 39 ||
aṇoraṇīyān mahato mahīyānātmā guhāyāṃ nihito'sya jantoḥ ||
tejomayaṃ paśyati vītaśoko dhātuḥ prasādānmahimānamātmanaḥ || 3.340.40 ||
vedāhametaṃ puruṣaṃ mahāntaṃ cādityavarṇaṃ tamasaḥ parastāt ||
yaṃ vai viditvā na bibheti mṛtyornānyaḥ panthā vidyate'yanāya || 41 ||
eko hyātmā mahimā brāhmaṇasya svakarmaṇā vai jāyate'sau kanīyān ||
yasmātparaṃ nāparamasti kiñcitsaukṣmyānmahattvācca tataśca nānyaḥ || 42 ||
vṛkṣa iva stabdho divi tiṣṭhatyekastenedaṃ pūrṇaṃ puruṣeṇa sarvam ||
susvāduparṇaḥ succhāyo dṛśyate sa vṛkṣaḥ pariṣasvajāte||
tayoranyatpippalaṃ nāma svādvanyadaśnanyo abhicākaśīti || 43 ||
akāmadhīramṛtaḥ svayaṃbhū rasena tṛpto na ca kaściddrumāṇām ||
pareṇa nākaṃ nāpatirna gṛhāya vibhrājate taṃ niyatā viśanti | 44 ||
iti śrīviṣṇudharmottare tṛtīyakhaṇḍe mārkaṇḍeyavajrasaṃvāde munīnprati haṃsagītāsu yatidharma varṇano nāma catvāriṃśadadhikatriśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ || 340 ||
<3.341>
|| ṛṣaya ūcuḥ || ||
varṇānāmāśramāṇāṃ ca proktā dharmāstvayānagha ||
rahasyabhūtā ye dharmā ye ca svalpā mahāphalāḥ || 1 ||
yebhyaścābhyadhikaṃ nāsti sarveṣāṃ tu samāśraye ||
tāndharmānkathayasvādya sarveṣāṃ vinatirdvija || 2 || ||
|| haṃsa uvāca || ||
āśritya devadeveśaṃ jagadyoniṃ jagadgurum ||
viṣṇuṃ jiṣṇuṃ hariṃ kṛṣṇaṃ kṛtā dharmā mahāphalāḥ || 3 ||
patanāttrāyate yasmāttasmātpātreti kīrtyate ||
patatāṃ trāṇadastvekaḥ sa devo madhusūdanaḥ || 4 ||
smaraṇātkīrtanād dhyānānnamaskārātpradakṣiṇāt ||
tathābhistuvanādyasya mucyate sarvakilbiṣaiḥ || 5 ||
kṛtvā devagṛhaṃ tasya devasya parameṣṭhinaḥ ||
rājasūyāśvamedhānāṃ phalena sa tu yujyate || 6 ||
suraveśmāni yāvanti dvijendrāḥ paramāṇavaḥ ||
tāvadvarṣasahasrāṇi svargaloke mahīyate ||7||
prāsāde mṛnmaye puṇyaṃ mayaitatkathitaṃ dvijāḥ ||
tasmāddaśaguṇaṃ puṇyaṃ kṛtaṃ daṇḍamaye bhavet || 8 ||
tato daśaguṇaṃ puṇyaṃ tathā śailakṛte bhavet ||
tato daśaguṇaṃ lohe tāmre śataguṇaṃ tataḥ || 9||
sahasraguṇitaṃ raupye tasmātphalamupāśnute ||
tataḥ śatasahasraṃ ca sauvarṇe dvijasattamāḥ || 3.341.10 ||
anantaphalamāpnoti ratnacitre manohare ||
pratimāṃ kārayitvā tu devadevasya cakriṇaḥ ||
na durgatimavāpnoti viṣṇulokaṃ ca gacchati || 11 ||
devasya pratimāyāṃ tu yāvaṃtaḥ paramāṇavaḥ ||
tāvadvarṣasahasrāṇi viṣṇuloke mahīyate || 12 ||
prāsādavattathā tasya phalasaṃpadudāhṛtā || 13 ||
uttarottarato divyairvinā pakveṣṭikī dvijāḥ ||
yathoktavidhinā devaṃ pratiṣṭhāpya janārdanam || 14 ||
akṣayaṃ lokamāpnoti viṣṇoramitatejasaḥ ||
ijyāvidhipuraskārakṛte tatra tathā bhuvam || 15 ||
tasyaivānucarellokastasyaivāpnoti cākṣayam ||
karoti devaprāsādaṃ yāvadvāraṃ sudhāsitam || 16 ||
janmāntarāṇi tāvaṃti yaśasvī bhuvi jāyate ||
citraṃ lakṣaṇasaṃyuktaṃ kārayitvā harergṛham || 17 ||
gandharvalokamāpnoti bahūnyabdagaṇāni tu |||
dattvā ca gītaṃ dharmajñā gandharvaiḥ saha modate || 18 ||
svayaṃ gītena saṃpūjya tasya cānucaro bhavet ||
vādyaṃ dattvā tathā viprā śakralokamavāpnuyāt ||| || 19 ||
svayaṃ vādyena saṃpūjya tasyaivānucaro bhaveta ||
vādyānāmapi devasya tantrīvādyaṃ sadā priyam || 3.341.20 ||
tena saṃpūjya varadaṃ gāṇapatyamavāpnuyāt ||
nṛtyaṃ dattvā tathāpnoti rudralokamasaṃśayam || 21 ||
svayaṃ nṛtyena saṃpūjya tasyaivānucaro bhavet ||
prekṣaṇīyapradānena śakralokamavāpnuyāt| || 22 ||
abhyarcanāṃ kārayitvā brahmalokaṃ prapadyate ||
toyenābhyukṣaṇaṃ kṛtvā devadevājire naraḥ || 23 ||
sarvapāpavinirmukto vāruṇaṃ lokamaśnute ||
yastu sammārjanaṃ kuryāddevadevājire naraḥ || 24 ||
sapāpajaṃ malaṃ dehādapāmārjati satvaraḥ ||
karoti paramāṇūnāṃ yāvatāmapamārjanam ||| || 25 ||
tāvadvarṣāṇi nākasthastridaśaiḥ saha modate ||
kṛtvopalepanaṃ viṣṇorgomayenālaye naraḥ || 26 ||
gavāṃ lokamavāpnoti yatrayatrābhijā yate ||
kṛtvā ca vividhairgandhairgandharvaiḥ saha modate || 27 ||
bahūnyabdasahasrāṇi bhūmiśobhāṃ harergṛhe ||
gandhodakena yaḥ kuryādviṣṇorabhyukṣaṇaṃ gṛhe || 28 ||
daśavarṣasahasrāṇi gandharvaiḥ saha modate ||
dattvopakāraṃ kusumairgandharvatvamavāpnuyāt || 29 ||
puṣpatulyāni varṣāṇi divyābharaṇarūpavān ||
kṛtvā śīrṇasya saṃskāraṃ tathā deveśaveśmani||3.341.30||
dviguṇaṃ phalamāpnoti nātra kāryā vicāraṇā ||
sāyaṃ prātastathā kṛtvā vāsudevasya kīrta nam||31||
sarvapāpavinirmuktaḥ svargaloke mahīyate ||
śayanādutthito yastu kīrttayenmadhusūdanam||32||
kīrtanāttasya pāpāni nāśamāyāntyaśeṣataḥ |||
ahorātrasya śāntiśca tathā tena kṛtā bhavet || 33 ||
prabhavaścāpyanarthānāṃ nāsya syāttadaharniśam ||
kīrtanāddevadevasya viṣṇoramitatejasaḥ || || 34 ||
yakṣarākṣasavetālabhūtapretavināyakāḥ ||
ḍākinyo vidravanti sma ye tathā ye ca hiṃsakāḥ || 35 ||
sarvānarthaharaṃ tasya nāmasaṅkīrtanaṃ smṛtam ||
devadevasya nāmāni bahūnīha vidurbudhāḥ || 36 ||
teṣāmanarthaśamanaṃ yadeveṣṭaṃ mahātmanaḥ ||
ekaṃ nāma japedyastu devadevasya cakriṇaḥ || 37 |||
sa ca pāpavinirmuktaḥ svargaloke mahīyate ||
hṛdi kṛtvā tathā kāmamabhīṣṭaṃ dvijapuṅgavāḥ || 38 ||
ekaṃ nāma japedyastu sa tānkāmānavāpnuyāt ||
nāmasaṃkīrtanaṃ kṛtvā kṣutapraskhalanādiṣu || 39 ||
viyogaṃ śīghramāpnoti sarvānarthairasaṃśayam ||
kṛtvā nāmasahasreṇa stutiṃ tasya mahātmanaḥ || 3.341.40 ||
viyogamāpnoti naraḥ sarvānarthairasaṃśayam ||
sāmnā ṛbhiryajurbhiśca tathaivātharvaṇena tu || 41 ||
kṛtvābhiṣṭavanaṃ tasya divyairārṣaistathā stavaiḥ ||
sarvānarthavinirmuktaḥ kāmānāpnoti śāśvatān || 42 ||
abhraṣṭalakṣaṇaiḥ kṛtvā svayaṃ svaracitākṣaraiḥ ||
stavaṃ brāhmaṇaśārdūlāstasmātkāmānavāpnuyāt| ||| 43 ||
na vittadānanicayairbahubhirmadhusūdanaḥ ||
tathā toṣamupāyāti yathā stotrairdvijottamāḥ || 44 ||
stutiḥ pavitrā maṅgalyā sarvapāpapraṇāśinī ||
sarvānarthapraśamanī devadevasya kīrtitā || 45 ||
tatkathāśravaṇaṃ kṛtvā sarvānarthairvimucyate ||
tatstotraśravaṇātpāpaṃ jahāti sakalaṃ tathā || 46 ||
yatra viṣṇukathā divyāḥ śrutayaśca susaṃhitāḥ ||
tacchrotavyaṃ manuṣyeṇa paramaṃ padamicchatā || 47 ||
viṣṇorāyatane dattvā tatkathāpustakaṃ naraḥ ||
brahmalokamavāpnoti bahavo vatsarāndvijāḥ || 48 ||
pustakām̐śca tathaivānyānyaḥ pradadyānnarastviha ||
sārasvatamavāpnoti lokaṃ kālaṃ tathā bahu || 49 ||
tatkathāṃ śrāvayedyastu tadbhaktānmānavottamaḥ ||
godānaphalamāpnoti sa narastena karmaṇā||3.341.50||
śrāvake tu kṛtaṃ puṇyaṃ sa phalāṃśena yujyate||
sukṛtaṃ vācakaṃ kṛtvā devāgāre tathā naraḥ || 51 ||
vidyādānaphalaṃ prāpya brahmalokaṃ prapadyate ||
viṣṇuṃ śaṅkhapradānena nirvāṇaṃ lokamaśnute || 52 ||
mānuṣyamāsādya tathā khyātaśabdaśca jāyate ||
ghaṇṭāpradānena tathā mahadyaśa upāśnute|| 53 ||
saubhāgyamathavāpnoti kiṅkiṇīḥ pradadanhareḥ ||
kūṭāgāraṃ tathā dattvā nagarādhipatirbhavet || 54||
kīrtyā yuktastathā loke dhārmikaścāpi jāyate ||
bahūnyabdasahasrāṇi nākaloke sa modate ||55 |||
patākāṃ ye prayacchanti kūṭāgāre manoharām ||
santyajya sakalaṃ pāpaṃ vāyuloke vrajanti te || 56 ||
patākāṃ ca śubhāṃ dattvā tathā keśavaveśmani |||
vāyulokamavāpnoti bahūnyabdagaṇāni tu || 57 ||
dodhūyate yathā sā ca vāyunā keśavālaye ||
tathātathā hi sakalaṃ dehātpāpaṃ vidhūyate || 58 ||
yathāyathā ca sā śobhāṃ karoti tridaśālaye ||
tathātathā sa yaśasā mahatā bhuvi rājate || 59 ||
vitānakapradānena sarvapāpaiḥ pramucyate |||
parāṃ nirvṛtimāpnoti yatrayatrābhijāyate || 3.341.60 ||
dattvā tu devakarmārthaṃ navāṃ vedīṃ dṛḍhāṃ śubhām ||
pārthivatvamavāpnoti vedirhi pṛthivī yataḥ||61||
yaḥ kumbhaṃ devakarmārthaṃ naro dadyānnavaṃ śubham ||
sa lokamāpnoti naro vāruṇaṃ gatakalmaṣaḥ || 62 ||
caturaḥ kalaśāndadyādyastu devagṛhe naraḥ ||
catussamudravalayāṃ sa ca bhuṅkte vasundharām || 63 ||
dattvaikamapi viprendrāḥ kalaśaṃ susamāhitaḥ ||
rājā bhavati dharmātmā bhūtale nātra saṃśayaḥ || 64 ||
vāridhānīṃ tathā dattvā vāruṇaṃ lokamaśnute ||
kamaṇḍalupradānena godānaphalamāpnuyāt ||65||
uśīrakūrcakaṃ dattvā sarvapāpaiḥ pramucyate ||
dattvā gopālakānviprāḥ sarvapāpānapohati || 66 ||
dattvā cāmarajaṃ kūrcaṃ śriyamāpnotyanuttamām ||
āsanānāṃ pradānena sthānaṃ sarvatra vindati || 67 ||
pādapīṭhapradānena gatimāpnotyanuttamām ||
godānaphalamāpnoti tathā pādyaprado naraḥ || 68 ||
lokeṣu dhvajabhūtaḥ syādviṣṇordattvā tathā dhvajam ||
śakralokānavāpnoti bahūnabdagaṇānnaraḥ || 69 ||
narastvācamanīyasya dānādbhavati nirmalaḥ ||
mukhalepapradānena paraṃ rūpamihāpnuyāt || 3.341.70 ||
darpaṇasya pradānena darpavānrūpavānbhavet ||
darśayitvā tathā taṃ ca subhagastvabhijāyate || 71 ||
mātrāṃ tu paricaryārthe nivedya vidhivattathā ||
sarvakāmasamṛddhasya yajñasya phalamaśnute || 72 ||
dhānyānāmatha sasyānāṃ bījānāmatha dhārmikaḥ ||
suvarṇaraupyalohānāṃ rūpakāṇāṃ tathaiva ca ||73 ||
sahasraguṇamāpnoti phalamanyatra kīrttanāt ||
rūpyado rūpamāpnoti viśeṣādbhuvi durlabham || 74 ||
naraḥ suvarṇadānena sarvānkāmānupāśnute ||
naḍa dānena lokeṣu grāmaṇyamupagacchati || 75 ||
gavāṃ lokamavāpnoti dhenuṃ dattvā payasvinīm ||
anaḍuhaḥ pradānena daśadhenuphalaṃ labhet ||76 ||
ajāvimahiṣoṣṭrāṇāṃ dānamaśvatarasya ca ||
sahasraguṇitaṃ dānaṃ pūrvaṃ proktaṃ prakīrtitam || 77 ||
āraṇyamṛgajātīnāṃ tathā dānācca pakṣiṇām ||
agniṣṭomamavāpnoti subhagaśca tathā bhavet || 78||
mṛdbhogadānāddevasya bhūmimāpnotyanuttamām ||
dantakāṣṭhapradānena tathā saubhāgyamarhati || 79 ||
jihvā nirlekhanaṃ dattvā virogastvabhijāyate ||
yuktaḥ pītapatākābhirnivedya garuḍadhvajam || 3.341.80 ||
keśavaṃ yo dvijaśreṣṭhāḥ śakraloke mahīyate ||
yuktaṃ nīlapatākābhistālaṃ dattvā dvijottamāḥ||81||
yasmāllokātparibhraṣṭo virāgastvabhijāyate ||
yuktaṃ śvetapatākābhiragāraṃ yaḥ prayacchati||82||
rājā bhavati lokesminbhuktvā lokānpracetasaḥ ||
yuktaṃ raktapatākābhirdatvā ṛṣyamanuttamam || 83 ||
alakāṃ samavāpnoti tathā pāpakṣayaṃ naraḥ ||
toraṇaṃ kārayedyastu devadevālaye naraḥ || 84 ||
lokeṣu tasya dvārāṇi bhavanti vivṛtāni tu ||
śayanāsanadānena sthitiṃ vindati śāśvatīm ||89||
uttaracchadadānena sarvānkāmānavāpnuyāt ||
devaveśmopabhogāni śilpabhāṇḍāni yo naraḥ || 86 ||
dadyādvā vādyabhāṇḍāni gaṇeśatvamavāpnuyāt ||
devaśriteṣu yatkiñciddadyādbhaktisamanvitaḥ || 87 ||
tadevākṣayamāpnoti devadevaprasādataḥ ||
dattvā dhānyāni bījāni sasyāni vividhāni ca ||88||
rūpakāṇi ca tānyeva prāpnotyayutaśaḥ punaḥ ||
dattvā śākāni rasyāṇi viśokastvabhijāyate || 89 ||
dattvā ca vyaṃjanārthāya tathopakaraṇāni ca ||
puṣpavṛkṣaṃ tato dattvā grāmasyādhipatirbhavet||3.341.90||
phalavṛkṣaṃ tathā dattvā nagarādhipatirbhavet||
ārāmaṃ kārayitvā tu rājā vai pārthivo bhaveta||91||
jalāśayaṃ tathā kṛtvā sarvapāpaiḥ pramucyate ||
sapuṣpaissaphalairvṛkṣairyuktaṃ kṛtvā jalāśayaiḥ || 92 ||
udyānaiḥ padminīkhaṇḍairaiśvaryaiśca manoharaiḥ ||
śvetadvīpamavāpnoti bahūnyabdagaṇāni tu || 93 ||
prīṇayeddevadeveśaṃ gavāṃ grāmeṇa yo naraḥ ||
tasya prītaḥ sa bhagavānabhīṣṭaphalado bhavet || 94 ||
atīva dayitāstasya brāhmaṇā dvijasattamāḥ ||
brahmaṇaiḥ pūjatairviṣṇuḥ svayaṃ bhavati pūjitaḥ || 95 ||
dīnāṃdhakṛpaṇānātha bahūnāmapi dānataḥ ||
yaḥ prīṇāti jagannāthaṃ tasya devaḥ prasīdati || 96||
upavāsavrataiḥ snānaistīrthānusaraṇena ca||
āyatasthānasaṃsthānāṃ paśūnāṃ ca vivarjanāt || 97||
guruśuśrūṣayā kṣāntyā tathā vai śrāddhakarmaṇā ||
āhāraniyamaistaistaiḥ parāpīḍairviśeṣataḥ || 98 ||
pūjayitvā jagannāthaṃ śvetadvīpamavāpnuyāt ||
paraṃ padamavāpnoti prasanne madhusūdane || 99 ||
abhivādya jagannāthaṃ kṛtārthaśca tathā bhavet ||
namaskārakriyā tasya sarvapāpapraṇāśinī || 3.341.100 ||
praṇāmaṃ pāpaśamanaṃ tathaiva parikīrtitam ||
jānubhyāṃ caiva pāṇibhyāṃ śirasā ca vicakṣaṇaḥ ||101||
kṛtvā praṇāmaṃ devebhyaḥ sarvānkāmānavāpnuyāt ||
paramāṇūni vartante lalāṭe puruṣasya ca || 102 ||
pārthivāni bhavaṃtīha namato madhusūdanam ||
tāvantyabdāni viprendrāḥ svargaloke mahīyate||103||
devārcādarśanādeva praṇamenmadhusūdanam ||
sthānāpekṣā na kartavyā dṛṣṭvārcāṃ dvijasattamāḥ || 104 ||
devārcādṛṣṭipūtaṃ hi śuciḥ sarvaṃ prakīrtitam ||
devārcādṛṣṭipūtena snānaṃ kṛtvā jalāśaye || 105||
sarvapāpavinirmuktaḥ kṣaṇādbhavati nirmalaḥ ||
tīrthānāṃ tatra sarveṣāṃ sannidhānaṃ prakīrtitam||106||
pādodakena vābhyukṣya tathā mūrddhānamātmanaḥ ||
snāto bhavati dharmātmā nātra kāryā vicāraṇā ||107||
pādodakaṃ śāntikaramamaṅgalyavināśanam ||
duṣṭaduḥsvapnaśamanaṃ sarvapāpavināśanam ||108||
dhanyaṃ pavitraṃ maṅgalyaṃ sarvapāpaharaṃ śivam ||
śirasā mānavaśreṣṭhāḥ kālyapuṇyāya dhārayet||109||
sarvā ṅgapraṇipātena sarvakāmayutāṃ naraḥ ||
sarvānarthavinirmukto godānaphalamāpnuyāt || 3.341.110 ||
rukmamāṣakadānasya phalaṃ viprāḥ padepade ||
puruṣastu samāpnoti yaḥ karoti pradakṣiṇām || 111 ||
dhyānaṃ tu devadevasya sarvabādhāvināśanam ||
yāvatkālaṃ karotīha dhyānaṃ vai niścalaṃ naraḥ || 112 ||
tāvadvarṣasahasrāṇi svargaloke mahīyate ||
nirāśīrnirmamo yastu viṣṇordhyānarato bhavet || 113 ||
tatpadaṃ samavāpnoti yatra gatvā na śocati ||
yajñānāṃ tapasāṃ caiva dhyānameva paraṃ smṛtam ||114||
devāgre kārayedyastu ramyāmāpaṇavīthikām||
rājā bhavati lokeṣu vijitārirmahāyaśāḥ||115||
nagaraṃ ca tathā kṛtvā sāmrājyamadhigacchati ||
yānavāhanadānena sarvakālaṃ sukhī bhavet ||116||
śibikāṃ ye prayacchaṃti te prayāntyamarāvatīm ||
aśvasthāḥ sūryalokasthā rājante divi sūryavat ||117||
karīndradānācchakrasya cirāllokāccyuto naraḥ ||
rājā bhavati dharmātmā pṛthivyāṃ pṛthivī patiḥ || 118 ||
dāsaṃ dattvā sukhī loke svargabhraṣṭobhijāyate ||
dāsīṃ dattvā tathā viprā nātra kāryā vicāraṇā || 119 ||
gaṇikāṃ ye prayacchaṃti nṛtyagītaviśāradām ||
sarvaduḥkhavinirmuktāste prayāntyamarāvatīm || 3.341.120 ||
dundubhiṃ ye prayacchanti kīrtimanto bhavanti te ||
rājā bhavati lokesmiñchatraṃ dattvā dvijottamāḥ || 121 ||
nāpnoti ripujaṃ duḥkhaṃ saṃgrāme ripujidbhavet ||
tālavṛntapradānena nirvṛtiṃ prāpnuyātparām || 122 ||
tathācamanadānena śrīmānbhavati bhūtale ||
mucyate ca tathā pāpaiḥ svargalokaṃ ca gacchati || 123 ||
upānatsaṃpradānena vimānamadhigacchati ||
yatheṣṭaṃ tena lokeṣu vicaratyamaraprabhaḥ || 124 ||
yathādeśaṃ yathākālaṃ rājaliṅgaṃ surālaye ||
dattvā bhavati rājaiva nātra kāryā vicāraṇā || || 125 ||
dhāraṇīyopayogyāni dattvātyantaṃ sukhī bhavet ||
mālyādhāraṃ tathā dattvā dhūpādhāraṃ tathaiva ca || 126 ||
gandhādhāraṃ tathā pātraṃ kāmānāṃ pātratāṃ vrajet ||
samudrajāni pātrāṇi dattvā vai taijasāni vā || 127 ||
pātraṃ bhavati kāmānāṃ vidyānāṃ ca dhanasya ca ||
yatkiñciddevadevāya dadyādbhaktisamanvitaḥ || 128 ||
tadevākṣayyamāpnoti svarlokaṃ ca gacchati ||
vismāpanīyaṃ yatkiñcitkṛtvātyantaṃ sukhī bhavet || 129 ||
snānaśāṭakadānena vahniṣṭomaphalaṃ labhet ||
tailena snapanaṃ kṛtvā saubhāgyamadhigacchati || 3.341.130 ||
gaṃdhatailāni divyāni sugandhīni śucīni ca ||
keśavāya naro dattvā gandharvaiḥ saha modate || 131 ||
ghṛtena snapanaṃ kṛtvā sarvapāpānvyapohati ||
ghṛtaprasthena deveśaṃ pauṣapuṣyamitetaraḥ || 132 ||
snāpayitvāśvamedhasya phalaṃ prāpnotyasaṃśayam ||
etadeva phalaṃ proktamuttarāyaṇavāsare || 133 ||
ghṛtaṃ pavitramatulaṃ ghṛtamannamanuttamam ||
tena gavyena saṃsnāpya pratimāṃ vaiṣṇavīṃ naraḥ || 134 ||
sarvakāmasamṛddhasya yajñasya phalamaśnute ||
gavāṃ lokamavāpnoti kṣīrodamatha vā punaḥ || 135 ||
rogārto mucyate rogādbaddho mucyeta bandhanāt ||
bhayānmucyeta bhītaśca ghṛtena snapayeddharim || 136 ||
sarvānkāmānavāpnoti sarvānarthavivarjitaḥ ||

godhūmayavacūrṇaistu tatotsārya janārdanam ||

nivedya devadevāya gandharvaiḥ saha modate || 142 ||
tadā kalaśadānena priyāmāpnotyanuttamām ||

tvakpradānāttathā loke bhavatīha balānvitaḥ || 149 ||
dattvā mṛgamadasnānaṃ sarvānkāmānavāpnuyāt ||
ratnodakapradānena śriyamāpnotyanuttamām || 3.341.150 ||

phalatoyapradānena saphalāṃ vindate kriyām ||

sopavāso harerdattvā putramāpnotyasaṃśayam ||
kṣīrapallavasaṃyuktānkalaśānsuvibhūṣitān || 153 ||
dattvaiva devadevāya vājapeyamavāpnuyāt ||

śārīramānasairduḥkhaistathā caiva vimucyate ||

śārīrairmānasairdukhaistathaiva ca vimucyate ||

yo'nulipyeta deveśaṃ kīrtitairanulepanaiḥ ||

tilaprasthapradānasya phalaṃ prāpnotyasaṃśayam ||

śuklakstrapradānena śriyamāpnotyanuttamām ||

kṛtvā puṣpagṛhaṃ viṣṇoḥ puṣpairvātha vitānakam ||
phalena yogamāpnoti rājasūyāśvamedhayoḥ || 246 ||
kiṅkiṇījālasaṃyuktaṃ devarāmāgaṇojvalam ||
vīṇāmurajagītāḍhyaṃ vimānamadhirohati || 247 ||
puṣpapradānaṃ maṅgalyamalakṣmīvipraṇāśanam ||
lakṣmīvṛddhikaraṃ jñeyaṃ saumanasyavivarddhanam || 248 ||
saubhāgyajananaṃ caiva tejobalavivṛddhidam ||
na ratnairna suvarṇena na ca vittena bhūriṇā || 249 ||
tathā prasādamāyāti yathā puṣpairjanārdanaḥ ||
vāsantīmallikāpuṣpaṃ tathā ca vārṣike ca vā || 3.341.250 ||
kupyakaṃ yūthikā caiva tathā caivātimuktakam ||
ketakīṃ campakaṃ caiva māṣavṛṇakameva ca || 251 ||
purandhramañjarīpuṣpaṃ cūtapuṣpaṃ tathaiva ca ||
bandhujīvakapuṣpaṃ ca kusumaṃ kuṅkumasya ca || 252 ||
jātīpuṣpāṇi sarvāṇi kundapuṣpaṃ tathaiva ca ||
pāṭalī ca tathā vṛttānnīlamindīvaraṃ tathā || 253 ||
kumudaṃ śvetaraktaṃ ca śvetaraktau tathā śubhau ||
evamādīni puṣpāṇi dātavyāni sadā hareḥ || 254 ||
svayamāhṛtya yo dadyādāraṇyakusumāni ca ||
sa rājyaṃ sphītamāpnoti loke nihatakaṇṭakam || 255||
patraṃ ramyaṃ phalaṃ ramyaṃ pallavaṃ vā dvijottamāḥ ||
bhaktyā nivedya devāya phalasyānantyamaśnute || 356 ||
karpūrabodhitānīva paṭṭavāsāṃsi yo naraḥ ||
dadāti devadevāya sośvamedhaphalaṃ labhet || 257 ||
paṭṭavāsāṃsi mukhyāni dattvā svargamupāśnute ||
yaśaścāpnoti vipulaṃ nākapṛṣṭhe mahīyate || 258 ||
tathā saubhāgyamāpnoti paṭṭavāsaprado naraḥ ||
yūpenābhyarcya deveśaṃ svargaloke mahīyate||259||
dhūpānāmuttamaṃ dhūpaṃ guggulaṃ kṣaudrasarpimat||
dattvā guggulujaṃ dhūpaṃ nākapṛṣṭhe mahīyate ||3.341.260||
madhusarpiḥsamāyuktaṃ dvādaśāhātphalaṃ labhet ||
dattvā candanasāreṇa saubhāgyaṃ paramāpnuyāt ||261||
tathaivāgurusāreṇa parāṃ siddhimavāpnuyāt ||
dhūpānāmuttamaṃ tadvatsarvakāmaphalapradam || ||262||
dhūpaṃ turuṣkakaṃ dattvā vahniṣṭomaphalaṃ labhet ||
dattvā ca kṛtrimaṃ mukhyaṃ sarvānkāmānavāpnuyāt ||263||
gandhayuktikṛtaṃ dattvā yajñaṃ gosavamāpnuyāt ||
dattvā karpūraniryāsaṃ vājimedhaphalaṃ labhet || 264 ||
vasante gugguluṃ datvā vahniṣṭomamavāpnuyāt ||
grīṣme candanasāreṇa dvādaśāhātphalaṃ labhet ||265||
turuṣkadānena tathā prāvṛṣyamaratāṃ vrajet ||
karpūradānāccharadi rājasūyamavāpnuyāt || 266 ||
hemante mṛgadarpeṇa vājimedhaphalaṃ labhet ||
śiśire'gurusāreṇa sarvamedhamavāpnuyāt || 267 ||
vinā mṛgamadāddhūpaṃ jīvajātaṃ vivarjayet ||
dhūpadaḥ svargamāpnoti dhūpadaḥ puṣṭimaśnute |||268||
dhūpalekhā yathaivordhvaṃ nityameva prasarpati ||
tathaivordhvagatiṃ nityaṃ dhūpadātā bhavennaraḥ ||269||
dhūparājiryadordhvaṃ syādekādīnāṃ dvijottamāḥ ||
sannidhānaṃ tathā vidyāddevadevasya cakriṇaḥ||3.341.270||
dīpaṃ cakṣuḥpradaṃ dadyāttathaivordhvagatipradam ||
urdhvaṃ yathā dhūpaśikhā dātā cordhvagatistathā ||271||
khyāto bhavati lokeṣu svargalokaṃ ca gacchati ||
yāvadakṣinimeṣāṇi dīpo devālaye jvalet || 272 ||
tāvadvarṣasahasrāṇi dharmātmā lokamaśnute ||
mahāvartipradānena kule deśe tathaiva ca || 273 ||
śraiṣṭhyamāpnoti sarvatra yathā sarvaṃ virājate ||
madhuparkapradānena paraṃ padamihāśnute ||274||
ghṛtaṃ dadhi tathā kṣaudraṃ madhuparkaṃ vijānatā ||
tatastvarhaṇadānena sarvapāpaiḥ pramucyate || 275 ||
tathaivācamanīyasya dānādbrāhmaṇasattamāḥ ||
anantatṛptimāpnoti svargalokaṃ ca gacchati || 276 ||
dattvā cāsaṃvibhāgāya tathaivānnamatandritaḥ ||
trailokyatarpite puṇyaṃ tatkṣaṇātsamavāpnuyāt || 277 |||
akṣayyamannadānaṃ ca pitṝṃstasyopatiṣṭhate ||
odanaṃ vyañjanopetaṃ dattvā svargamavāpnuyāt || 278 ||
paramānnapradānena tṛptiṃ vindati śāśvatīm ||
viṣṇulokamavāpnoti kulamuddharati svakam || 279 ||
ghṛtaudanapradānena dīrghamāyuravāpnuyāt ||
dadhyodanapradānena śriyamāpnotyanuttamām || 3.341.280||
kṣīraudanapradānena dīrghaṃ jīvitamāpnuyāta ||
ikṣūṇāṃ caiva dānena paraṃ saubhāgyamāpnuyāt ||281||
rasānāṃ caiva bhāgī syātsvargalokaṃ ca gacchati ||
sarvānkāmānavāpnoti kṣaudraṃ yastu prayacchati || 282 ||
tadeva tuhinopetaṃ rājasūyamavāpnuyāt ||
phāṇitasya pradānena vahnayādeyaṃ phalaṃ labhet || 283 ||
tathā guḍapradānena daśagodo bhavennaraḥ ||
mukhyaṃ daṇḍaṃ tathā dattvā maitrīṃ sarveṇa vindati || 284 ||
strīṣu vallabhatāmeti dattvā ca guḍikāṃ tathā ||
sitāyāśca pradānena sarvānkāmānavāpnuyāt || 285 ||
nivedyekṣurasaṃ bhaktyā paraṃ saubhāgyamāpnuyāta ||
vahniṣṭomamavāpnoti yāvakasya nivedakaḥ || 286 ||
atirātramavāpnoti tathā dhūpanivedakaḥ ||

dīrghajīvitamāpnoti ghṛtapūranivedakaḥ ||
modakānāṃ pradānena kāmānāpnotyabhīpsitān || 288||
nānāvidhānāṃ bhakṣyāṇāṃ dānātsvargamavāpnuyāt ||
rāgaṣāṃḍavadānena pauṇḍarīkamavāpnuyāt || 289 ||
bhojanīyapradānena tṛptimāpnotyanuttamām ||

balavarṇamavāpnoti coṣyāṇāṃ vinivedakaḥ ||
peyānāṃ ca pradānena tṛptimāpnotyanuttamām ||291||
pānakāni sugandhīni śītalāni viśeṣataḥ ||
nivedya devadevāya vājimedhamavāpnuyāt || 292 ||
tvagelānāgakusumakarpūrasitasaṃyutaiḥ ||
sitākṣaudraguḍopetairgandhavarṇasamanvitaiḥ || 293 ||
rājasūyamavāpnoti pānakairviniveditaiḥ ||
nivedya nālikerāmbu vahniṣṭomaphalaṃ labhet || 294 ||
dhānyānāṃ kṣaudrayuktānāṃ lājānāṃ ca nivedakaḥ ||
mukhyānāṃ caiva saktūnāṃ vahniṣṭomamavāpnuyāt || 295 ||
vānaprasthāśritaṃ puṇyaṃ labhecchākanivedakaḥ ||
dattvā haritakaṃ caiva tadeva phalamāpnuyāt ||| 296 ||
sukule prāpnuyājjanma kandamūlanivedakaḥ ||
nīlotpalavidārīṇāṃ tarūṭasya tathā dvijāḥ || 297 ||
kaṃdadānādavāpnoti vānaprasthaphalaṃ labhet ||
trapuñca cārukaṃ dattvā puṇḍarīkaphalaṃ labhet || 298 ||
karkandhubadarau dattvā tathā pālevataṃ dhavam ||
pārūṣakaṃ tathā proktaṃ panasaṃ nārikelakam || 299 ||
bhavyaṃ mocaṃ tathā caucaṃ kharjūramatha dāḍimam ||
āmrakādiṣu cākṣoṭamāranālapriyālujam || 3.341.300 ||
jāmbū bilvāmale caiva jātyaśrīnārukastathā ||
nāraṅgabījapūrau ca rājaphalguphalānyapi || 301 ||

evamādīni cānyāni yaḥ phalāni prayacchati ||302||
kriyāsāphalyamāpnoti svargalokaṃ tathaiva ca ||
udumbaraṃ kapitthaṃ ca tathā dantaśaṭhaṃ ca yat || 303 ||
evamādīni devāya na deyāni kadācana ||

rasānmukhyānavāpnoti saubhāgyamapi cottamam ||

tāmbūlīnāṃ kisalayaṃ svargalokamavāpnuyāt ||
saubhāgyamuttamaṃ loke tathā rūpamanuttamam || 307 ||
mukhavāsapradānena strīṇāṃ vallabhatāṃ vrajet ||
