mātrābhiḥ pūrayedbhojyāṃstathā bhakṣyānasambhave ||
yatkiñcidbhaktisaṃyukto devadeve prayacchati || 309 ||
tadevākṣayyamāpnoti svargalokaṃ ca gacchati ||
na devastoṣamāyāti prabhūtairdhanasañcayaiḥ || 3.341.310 ||
yathā toṣamavāpnoti bhaktyā bhaktajanapriyaḥ ||
bhaktyā kāmānavāpnoti bhaktyā svargaṃ ca gacchati || 311 ||
bhaktyā tatpadamāpnoti yatra gatvā na śocati ||
sāmānyabhaktyā yaddattaṃ taddhi padbhyāṃ prayacchati || 312 ||
ekāntabhāvopagamairmūrdhnā dvijavarottamāḥ ||
śvetena vastrayugmena tathā muktāphalaiḥ śubhaiḥ || || 313 ||
mukhyakarpūradhūpena payasā pāyasena ca ||
padmasūtrasya vartyā ca ghṛtadhūpena cāpyatha || 314 ||
pūjayansarvaśuklā syātsarvakāmapradā śivā ||
kṛtvemāṃ mucyate rogī rogācchīghramasaṃśayam ||315||
duḥkhārto mucyate duḥkhādbaddho mucyeta bandhanāt ||
rājagrastaśca mucyeta tathā rājabhayānnaraḥ || ||316||
kṣemeṇa gacchedadhvānaṃ tathānarthavivarjitaḥ ||
tailena snāpitaṃ devaṃ kuṅkumenānulepayet || 317 ||
raktairvastraiḥ samabhyarcya vidrumaiśca tathā naraḥ ||
bandhujīvakapuṣpaiśca dhūpenāgurujena ca || 316 ||
tailadīpena vartyā ca mahārajataraktayā ||
drākṣāpānakayuktena vaṭakaiḥ kṛsareṇa ca || 319 ||
pūjayetsarvaraktā syācchatrupakṣakṣayaṅkarī ||
saubhāgyajananī loke dhanadhānyavivarddhanī || 3.341.320 ||
svargadā phaladā caiva naranārīvaśaṅkarī ||
evaṃ dānaphalaṃ proktaṃ rājasānāṃ mayā dvijāḥ||321||
akāmaḥ sāttvike loke yatkiñcidvinivedayet ||
tenaiva sthānamāpnoti yatra gatvā na śocati ||322||
dharma vācanikā mūḍhāḥ phalakāmā narādhamāḥ ||
yercayanti jagannāthaṃ te kāmānprāpnuvantyatha || 323 ||
antavattu phalaṃ teṣāṃ tadbhavatyalpamedhasām ||
akāmānāṃ manuṣyāṇāṃ sāttvikānāṃ dvijottamāḥ || 324 ||
sarvaduḥkhavimokṣāya bhavantīyamapi dhruvam ||
ananto bhagavānviṣṇustasya kāmavivarjitaiḥ ||| ||325||
yadeva dīyate kiñcittadevākṣayyamucyate ||
padbhyāṃ pratīcchate devaḥ sakāmena niveditam || 326 ||
mūrdhnā prapadyate dattamakāmena dvijottamāḥ|||
naivedyenātha puṣpairvā vividhairdhanasaṃcayaiḥ || 327 ||
nāsti prayojanaṃ tasya sarvagasyāmitātmanaḥ ||
bhaktyā pratīcchate dattaṃ bhaktānugrahakāraṇāt || || 328 ||
tasmātsarvāsvavasthāsu bhaktirevātra kāraṇam ||
ekāntabhāvopagatāḥ sarvakāmavivarjitāḥ ||329||
vaiṣṇavaṃ padamāsādya na nivarteta karhicit||
na bhayaṃ nārakaṃ teṣāṃ na ca vyādhikṛtaṃ bhayam||3.341.330||
na vṛttiśatrujaṃ vāpi ye prapannā janārdanam||331||
nivedya bhaktyā madhusūdanāya duracchadaṃ vāpyatha tatprasūnam ||
dūrvāṅkuraṃ vā salilaṃ dvijendrāḥ prāpnoti lokānmanasā yatheṣṭān || 332 ||
iti śrīviṣṇudharmottare tṛtīyakhaṇḍe mārkaṇḍeya vajrasaṃvāde munīnprati haṃsagītāsu dānadharmanirūpaṇo nāmaikacatvāriṃśaduttaratriśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ || 341 ||
<3.342>
ṛṣaya ūcuḥ ||
namaste devadeveśa vāsudeva janārdana ||
bhaktānukaṃpa varada puṇḍarīkākṣa suvrata || 1 ||
brahmaṇya puruṣācintya sṛṣṭisaṃhārakāraka ||
trailokyanātha govinda śaraṇāgatavatsala ||| || 2 ||
sarveśa sarvageśāna sarvavyāpinnamostu te ||
namostu te jagannātha jagatāmīśvareśvara || 3 ||
surārtihara deveśa pitāmahaharastuta ||
viṣṇo jiṣṇo hare kṛṣṇa vibho sūryendulocana || 4 ||
sahasrasūryapratima jagatpālanatatpara ||
varāhanarasiṃhādya vāmanānaṃtavikrama || 5 ||
trivikrama kramākrāṃtabrahmāṇḍataṭamaṇḍala ||
cakradhārāhatāśeṣadevaśatrugaṇeśvara || 6 ||
smṛtimātrasamastādyavipranāśanakāraka ||
śāstropadeśādasmākaṃ hṛdi sthitatamontaka || 7 ||
vaikuṇṭhakuṇṭhitāśeṣa dānavāstrabalānagha ||
jaṭharāntarasaṃlīna samastabhuvanatraya ||8||
śaśāṅkalekhāsaṃkāśa daṃṣṭroddhṛtavasuṃdhara ||
mahāvarāha vidhvastahiraṇyākṣamahāsura || 9 ||
daityendravakṣorudhiraraktīkṛtanakhāṅkura ||
jvālāmālāparikṣiptanarasiṃhavapurdhara || 3.342.10 ||
lakṣmīkaṭākṣavikṣepaśabalīkṛtavigraha ||
kaustubhodbhāsitoraska tathā cāmīkarāṃbara || 11 ||
bhogibhogāsanāsīna tārkṣyapravaraketana ||
śaśāṅkaśatasaṅkāśa saptakandhara vedapa||12||
madhukaiṭabhavidhvaṃsamedovardhitabhūtala ||
nābhīsarojasaṃbhūtacaturmukha namostu te ||13||
sarvataḥ pāṇipādānta sarvatokṣiśiromukha ||
sarvendriyaguṇopeta sarvatra puruṣottama ||14||
yogināṃ tvaṃ paraṃ cakṣuryogināṃ tvaṃ gatiḥ parā ||
yoginastvāṃ ca saṃprāpya nivartante na karhicit ||15||
namaste purato deva pṛṣṭhataśca namostu te ||
tiryaṅnamostu te deva sarvatra ca namostu te||16||
mārkaṇḍeya uvāca ||
evaṃ stutaḥ sa bhagavānṛṣibhistaistadānaghaḥ||
divyaṃ cakṣurdadau teṣāmṛṣīṇāṃ bhāvitātmanām || 17 ||
tepaśyansakalaṃ dehe tasya tribhuvanaṃ tataḥ ||
haṃsarūpadharasyātha viṣṇoramitatejasaḥ| 18 ||
viśvarūpadharaṃ haṃsaṃ dṛṣṭvā rūpaṃ tapodhanāḥ ||
mūrdhabhiḥ praṇatā jagmustataḥ sarvavasuṃdharām || 19 ||
tatastānabravītsarvāndevo madhurayā girā ||
meghadundubhinirghoṣaḥ praṇatārtivināśanaḥ||3.342.20||
uttiṣṭhata dvijaśreṣṭhā varaṃ varayatānaghāḥ ||
amoghadarśanaṃ dṛṣṭvā sametā manasepsitam ||21||
ṛṣaya ūcuḥ ||
naṣṭo mohaḥ smṛtirlabdhā jñānaṃ prāptaṃ janārdana ||
viśvarūpo bhavāndṛṣṭaḥ kimanyena vareṇa naḥ || 22 ||
tathāpi yadi deveśa deyo'smākaṃ varastvayā ||
bhaktirbhavatu nirvighnā tvayi nityaṃ janārdana||23||
tvayā saha ca yosmākaṃ saṃvādamimamuttamam ||
paṭhetsamastapāpebhyo mokṣastasya tathā bhavet|| 24 ||
rogādrogī bhayādbhīto baddho muktastu bandhanāt ||
dharmārthī prāpnuyāddharmamarthārthī cārthamāpnuyāt ||25||
kāmānavāpnuyātkāmī mokṣārthī mokṣamāpnuyāt ||
yaścedaṃ dhārayedvidvānsa yāti paramāṃ gatim || 26 ||
śravaṇādasya śāstrasya svargalokaṃ ca gacchati ||
dhanyaṃ yaśasyamāyuṣyaṃ putrīyaṃ sarvakāmadam || ||27 ||
puṇyamastu tathaivaitacchāstraṃ madhuniṣūdana ||
viṣṇoḥ prasādātsaṃvṛttācchrutvaitacchāstramuttamam ||28||
śāstrātsamagrādapi sārabhūtaṃ saṃvādametatpuruṣottamasya ||
dvijottamānāṃ puruṣastvadhītya kāmānabhīṣṭānmanasā labheta || 29 ||
iti śrīviṣṇudharmottare tṛtīyakhaṇḍe mārkaṇḍeyavajrasaṃvāde munīnprati haṃsagītāsu munidṛṣṭahaṃsaviśvarūpadarśano nāma dvicatvāriṃśaduttaratriśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ || 342 || haṃsagītā samāptā ||
<3.343>
vajra uvāca |
atīva sumukhārādhyo devo viṣṇururukramaḥ ||
stutena darśayāmāsa viśvarūpaṃ dvijanmanām || 1 ||
prasādaṃ paramaṃ tasya brūhi bhaktajane mama ||
tṛptiṃ brahmanna gacchāmi śṛṇvannetatkathāmṛtam || 2 ||
mārkaṇḍeya uvāca ||
sa vidhijño mahārāja mātaliḥ śakrasārathiḥ ||
tasyāsti sadṛśī kanyā guṇakāśīti viśrutā || 3 ||
mātalirnāradaścaiva anveṣṭuṃ tadvanaṃ purā ||
caṃkramyamāṇau sakalamārghakasyātmajātmajam || 4 ||
sumukhākhyaṃ dadṛśatustau gṛhītvā gatāvubhau ||
nāge śakraṃ tataḥ śakrādayācetāṃ tu tāvubhau || 5 ||
sumukhasyābhayaṃ tārkṣyāttayoḥ śakro dadau varam ||||
vīryajñastārkṣyavīryasya sa yayau keśavāntikam || 6 ||
sumukhasyābhayaṃ prādāttārkṣyaṃ madhuniṣūdanaḥ ||
ājagāma tatastārkṣyaḥ saroṣaḥ keśavāntikam || 7 ||
|| garuḍa uvāca || ||
kimavajñāya māṃ kṛṣṇa sumukhe dīyate varaḥ ||
mama pṛṣṭhagataḥ śatrūṃstvaṃ nihaṃsi ca buddhyase ||
kuṭumbābharaṇaṃ śatruṃ pannagaṃ mama rakṣasi || 8 ||
|| mārkaṇḍeya uvāca || ||
evamuktastadā tena viṣṇurgaruḍamabravīt || || 9 ||
na me śakto jagadvoḍhuṃ sakalaṃ vihageśvara ||
ahamevātmanātmānaṃ dhārayāmi raṇeraṇe || 3.343.10 ||
śaktiścedasti voḍhuṃ te bhujamekaṃ vahasva me ||
evamuktvā dadau pṛṣṭhe tasya savyaṃ bhujaṃ hariḥ ||11 ||
tato viṣaṇṇavadanaḥ pakṣatyāgātkhagottamaḥ ||
klānto dīnaśca tuṣṭāva deveśaṃ mathusūdanam || 12 ||
|| || garuḍa uvāca || ||
namaste devadeveśa surāsuranamaskṛta ||
ajeya puṇḍarīkākṣa śaraṇāgatavatsala || 13 ||
na mayā tvadbalaṃ jñātamātmasambhāvitātmanā ||
tvameva deva trailokyaṃ sadā dhārayase'nagha ||14||
ātmānamātmanā deva tvameva vahase sadā ||
yasya te deva sakalaṃ dehe tribhuvanaṃ sthitam || 15 ||
tasya voḍhuṃ kathaṃ śaktā madvidhā hi sahasraśaḥ ||
tasmātprasīda bhagavanmama dhvajanivāsinaḥ || 16 ||
nāstyantastava deveśa karmaṇāṃ bhuvanatraye ||
kaste kīrtayituṃ śaktaḥ karmāṇyanavaśeṣataḥ || 17 ||
karmaṇāṃ kīrtanaṃ vā te kā stutiḥ parameśvara ||

|| śrībhagavānuvāca || ||

ādistvaṃ sarvadharmāṇāmṛṣīṇāṃ ca jagadguro || 5 ||
sūkṣmastvaṃ sarvabhūtebhyo mahadbhyaśca mahattaraḥ ||
dūragastvaṃ mahābhāga sarveṣāmapi cāgataḥ || 6 ||
sarvabhūtāntarastho'pi karmabhirna ca lipyase ||
aśarīraḥ śarīrasthaḥ sarvabhūteṣvavasthitaḥ || 7 ||
puruṣastvaṃ tvamavyaktastvamātmā buddhireva ca ||
mahābhūtāni bhagavastvaṃ tathaivendriyāṇi ca || 8 ||
yogibhirmṛṣyase yogajñānajñeyapurātana ||
ye tvāṃ bhajanti te yānti bhagavanparamāṃ gatim || 9 ||
jayamanaghamajeyaṃ viśvagaṃ devadevaṃ tridaśaripuvināśe nityamudyuktaśaktim || dvijasuravaravandyaṃ nāśanaṃ kalmaṣāṇāṃ śaraṇamupagato'haṃ vāsudevaṃ śaraṇyam || 3.347.10 || iti śrīviṣṇudharmottare tṛtīyakhaṇḍe mārkaḍeyavajrasaṃvāde vāsudevastotraṃ nāma saptacatvāriṃśadadhikatriśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ || 347 ||
<3.348>
|| mārkaṇḍeya uvāca ||
vasunā vasuveśena vāgminā viṣṇurīḍitaḥ ||
uvāca prāñjaliṃ prahvaṃ pārthivaṃ purataḥ sthitam||1||
vāsudeva uvāca ||
nānṛtaṃ nṛpa vaktavyaṃ prāṇaiḥ kaṇṭhagatairapi ||
dharmapraśno na vaktavyastathaikena viśeṣataḥ || 2 ||
bahujñenāpi dharmajña dharmakāmena karhicit ||
durvijñeyāstathā dharmāḥ sūkṣmā rājanduranvayāḥ || 3 ||
tasmānnaikena vaktavyāḥ kadācidapi jānatā ||
uktānṛtaṃ mahatprāptaṃ tvayā kṛcchraṃ narādhipa || 4 ||
gatibhraṃśastathaivāptā devayajñārthavādinā ||
mama bhakto'si satataṃ tena te niṣkṛtiḥ kṛtā||5||
kālenālpena kalpena yā na śakyā surāsuraiḥ ||
gaccha pālaya rājyaṃ tvaṃ tathaivāvicalo bhava || 6 ||
nāvamānaṃ tvayā kāryaṃ brāhmaṇānāṃ kathañcana ||
brāhmaṇo hi mahadbhūtaṃ vijñeyaṃ daivataṃ param || 7 ||
mārkaṇḍeya uvāca ||
evamuktvā vasuṃ viṣṇustatraivādarśanaṃ gataḥ ||
tārkṣyeṇa sahito rājanvismitaścābhavadvasuḥ||8||
rājyaṃ cāvāpa bhūyo'pi khecaratvaṃ tathaiva ca||
tataḥ sabhājitaścāpi devaiḥ sarvaiḥ samāgataiḥ || 9 ||
evaṃ sa devena hi bhaktiyukto rasātalastho vasudhādhipeśaḥ ||
bhayātsughorātpravimucya yukto rājyena gatvā nabhasastathaiva || 3.348.10 ||
iti śrīviṣṇudharmottare tṛtīyakhaṇḍe mārkaṇḍeyavajrasaṃvāde vasūpākhyānaṃ nāmāṣṭacatvāriṃśadadhikatriśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ ||348|| ||
<3.349>
|| vajra uvāca || ||
viśvarūpaṃ samācakṣva devadevasya cakriṇaḥ ||
kasya tūddarśitaṃ tena bhṛguvaṃśavivardhana || 1 ||
mārkaṇḍeya uvāca ||
ekāntabhāvanābhaktairdṛṣṭāni bahuśaḥ purā ||
viśvarūpāṇi devasya yānyaśakyāni bhāṣitum || 2 ||
vāsudevaprasādena na bhaveddivyadarśanaḥ ||
viśvarūpadharaṃ draṣṭuṃ tāvacchaktirna vidyate || 3||
tatra yaddarśitaṃ caiva nāradasya purānagha ||
tatte'haṃ saṃpravakṣyāmi viśvadevena viṣṇunā || 4 ||
nāradaḥ sumahadbhūtaṃ naranārāyaṇāśrayam ||
badaryāśramamityuktaṃ jagāmātimanoharam || 5 ||
caturātmā hariryatra dharmaputratvamāgataḥ||
hariḥ kṛṣṇo naraścaiva tathā nārāyaṇaḥ prabhuḥ || 6 ||
hariḥ kṛṣṇaḥ sthito vṛtte tadā lokāntare kila ||
naranārāyaṇau devau tapasyugre tathā ratau || 7 ||
aṣṭacakre sthitau yāne bhūtiyukte manohare ||
ekapādau nirālambau tathaivordhvabhujāvubhau || 8 ||
dadarśa nāradastatra tāvubhau dīptatejasau ||
abhivādya gatau devau tayostu purataḥ sthitaḥ || 9 ||
pādyārghācamanīyādyaiḥ pūjitaḥ saṃstadā dvijaḥ ||
naranārāyaṇābhyāntu tatrovāsa tadāśrame || 3.349.10 |
āśramasthaḥ sa viprendraḥ kadāciddevasattamau ||
dadarśa vighnaṃ kurvāṇau devakarma tathānaghau ||11||
tataḥ kṛtāhniko prāha nāradastau jagadgurū ||
bhavantau jagatāṃ nāthau bhavantau parameśvarau ||12||
ārādhanārthaṃ kasyeha tapasyabhiratāvubhau ||
bhavadbhyāmarcyate kaśca daive pitrye ca kalpyate ||13||
prakṛtiryā parāsmākaṃ yataḥ sarvasya saṃbhavaḥ ||
maryādādarśanārthāya navaṃ tamaparājitam || 14 ||
pūjayāmaḥ sadā brahmandaive pitrye ca kalpite ||
viprāndeva gurūngāśca pitṝṃścaiva jagattraye || 15||
ye'rcayantyarcayantyeva taṃ devaṃ parameśvaram ||
yo'yamapyarcayeddevaṃ tena syātpūjito hariḥ ||16||
vidhihīnaṃ mahābhāga sa hi sarvagato yataḥ ||
devasyānyasya yaccārcāṃ karttukāmo'rcayeddharim || 17 ||
tena tasya kṛtārcā syāddvijaśreṣṭha vidhānataḥ ||
deve yasmātsurāḥ sarvaṃ trailokyamapi yadgatam ||18||
kṛtā trailokyapūjā syādviṣṇupūjāvidhāyinā ||
nāsti lokeṣu tadbrahma yatra nāsti janārdanaḥ ||11|||
na tadapyasti lokeṣu yanna viṣṇau pratiṣṭhitam ||
yasminsarvaṃ yataḥ sarvaṃ yaḥ sarvaṃ sarvataśca yaḥ||3.349.20||
yaśca sarvamayo nityaṃ tasmai sarvātmane namaḥ ||
kṣīroda madhye dvīpo'sti vahuyojanavistṛtaḥ ||21||
tatra te puruṣāḥ śvetāḥ śaśāṅkasamatejasaḥ ||
upetāśca tathā sarve cakravatyupalakṣaṇaiḥ || 22||
pañcarātravidhānajñāḥ pūjayanti sadā harim ||
māyādehamathāsthāya devo'pi madhusūdanaḥ ||23||
tatpūjāṃ pratigṛhṇāti śirasā satataṃ hariḥ ||
taissārdhaṃ ramate nityaṃ taiśca saṃpūjyate sadā ||24||
ekāntabhāvopagatā ye bhavantīha mānavāḥ ||
akāmāśca jagannāthamarcayanti sadā ca ye || 25 ||
te yānti dehamutsṛjya tadadvīpaṃ śvetasaṃjñitam ||
aniṣyandā nirāhārāḥ sarvajñāḥ sarvadarśinaḥ||26||
sarveśvarā nityatṛptāḥ paraṃ sukhamupāgatāḥ ||
pūjayanti hariṃ tatra divyaṃ varṣaśataṃ punaḥ ||27||
tato'rkamaṇḍalaṃ bhittvā aniruddhaṃ viśanti te ||
brahmam̐stapobhiḥ pradyumnaṃ tataḥ saṃkarṣaṇaṃ prabhum||28||
vāsudevaṃ tataḥ prāpya tenaiva sadṛśāstataḥ ||
bhavanti sarve sarvatra sarvadā sarvaśaktayaḥ || 29 ||
muktāśca sarvaduḥkhebhyaḥ paraṃ sukhamupāgatāḥ ||
sa tvaṃ śīghramito gatvā śvetadvīpanivāsinaḥ ||3.349.30||
paśya tvaṃ puruṣānbrahmanprasannaḥ sa vibhustava ||
darśayiṣyati rūpaṃ tu trailokyādbhutamadbhutam ||31||
viśvaṃ brāhmaṇaśārdūla prītaṃ madhuniṣūdanam ||
ekataśca dvitaścaiva tritaścaiva mahātapāḥ || 32 ||
taṃ tu deśaṃ gatā brahmanna tairdṛṣṭo janārdanaḥ ||
ekāntabhāvopagatāstena yasmājjagadguroḥ || || 33 ||
kevalaṃ taiḥ śritaḥ ślokaḥ śvetadvīpanivāsibhiḥ ||
udīrito mahābhāgaiḥ sarvakalmaṣavarjitaiḥ || 34 ||
jitaṃ te puṇḍarīkākṣa namaste viśvabhāvana ||
namaste'stu hṛṣīkeśa mahāpuruṣa pūrvaja || 35 ||
ślokametaṃ tu te śrutvā śrutavantastathā giram ||
aśarīrāṃ mahābhāga sarvabhūtāvahāṃ hareḥ || 36 ||
śvetadvīpamathāsādya mṛtyumāpnoti karhicit ||
śvetadvīpe gatirnāsti vinā caikāntibhirdvijāḥ || 37 ||
sāmanyabhāvanā bhaktā bhavantaśca tapodhanāḥ ||
tapasogreṇa saṃyuktāstena me deśamāgatāḥ || 38 ||
sāmānyabhaktisattvāśca punarvrajata mā cirama ||
bhavadbhiḥ puruṣā dṛṣṭāḥ śvetadvīpanivāsinaḥ || 39 ||
ebhiḥ ślokaistathā dṛṣṭo bhavatīti janārdanaḥ ||
ekāntabhāvanāsaktaiḥ ślokaistu puruṣairimam || 3.349.40 ||
japtavyaṃ satataṃ viprāstataḥ punariheṣyatha ||
śloko'yaṃ pāvanaḥ puṇyaḥ sarvāghaviniṣūdanaḥ || 41 ||
ślokairbhāvena kartavyau japahomau vijānatā ||
dhūpadīpānnapuṣpāṇāṃ tathaiva ca nivedanam || 42 ||
ślokametaṃ paṭhedviprā namaskāre pradakṣiṇe ||
snānametena kartavyaṃ tathā cānte jalaṃ dvijāḥ || || 43 ||
sarvapāpaharaṃ puṇyaṃ mokṣadaṃ sarvakāmadam ||
śloka eṣa vinirdiṣṭaḥ sarvakāmapradaḥ śivaḥ || 44 ||
āpatprāptena japtavyaṃ tanmokṣārthaṃ tathaiva ca ||
etasya jāpaḥ kartavyo nityameva vijānatā || 45 ||
etasya jāpātpuruṣaḥ sarvānkāmānupāśnute ||
etāṃ śrutvā tu te vāṇīṃ cāgatā magadhāḥ punaḥ || 46 ||
dṛṣṭavanto vasuṃ tatra yajamānaṃ mahīpatim ||
yājako yasya dharmātmā devācāryo bṛhaspatiḥ || 47 ||
adṛśyena hṛto bhāgastatra devena viṣṇunā ||
jagṛhurdevatāḥ sarvā dṛśyabhāgātpurodhasaḥ || 48 ||
viṣṭaṃ prati gatakrodhaṃ devācāryaṃ bṛhaspatim ||
ekataśca dvitaścaiva tritaścaivamavocatam || 49 ||
adṛśyaḥ sarvadevānāṃ taṃ kathaṃ draṣṭumicchasi ||
ityevamanunītastu devācāryo bṛhaspatiḥ || 3.349.50 ||
samāpayāmāsa tadā taṃ yajñaṃ pārthivasya ca ||
ekāntabhāvopagatā ṛṣayo'pi janārdanam || 51 ||
āgatā devakāryārthaṃ śvetadvīpātpurā dvija ||
śvetadvīpaṃ punaḥ prāptaṃ punarāvṛttidurlabham || 52 ||
tasmāttaṃ devadeveśaṃ tatrasthaṃ paśya nārada ||
tvayāpi devakāryāṇi kartavyāni bahūnyatha || 53 ||
śvetadvīpagatasyāpi tena te dvija sāmpratam ||
na bhaviṣyati lokeṣu gatirnirvāṇakāriṇī ||
kalpāvasāne tu gatiṃ tāṃ tvaṃ vipra gamiṣyasi || 54 ||
mārkaṇḍeya uvāca ||
evamuktastataḥ śīghraṃ manomārutaraṃhasā ||
abhivādyātha tau devau taṃ dvīpaṃ nārado gataḥ || 55 ||
sa tatra gatvā dadṛśe śvetadvīpa nivāsinaḥ ||
pūjayāmāsa śirasā tāṃśca bhaktyā sa nāradaḥ || 56 ||
prāpya śvetaṃ mahādvīpaṃ nārado hṛṣṭamānasaḥ ||
dadarśa tāneva narāñśvetāñcandraprabhāñchubhān || 57 ||
pūjayāmāsa śirasā manasā taistu pūjitaḥ ||
didṛkṣurjāpyaparamaḥ sarvakṛcchradharaḥ smṛtaḥ || 58 ||
bhūtvaikāgramanā vipraścordhvabāhurmahābhujaḥ ||
stotraṃ jagau sa viśvāya nirguṇāya guṇātmane || 59 ||
vāsudevāya śāntāya tathā viśvasṛje dvija ||
anantāyāprameyāya sarvagāyātiraṃhase || 3.349.60 ||
amitabalaparākramāya tasmai tridaśamunipratipūjitāya nityam ||
bhavabhavabharanāśanāya viṣṇoḥ praṇatajana pratipāpanāśanāya || 61 ||
iti śrīviṣṇudharmottare tṛ0 kha0 mārkaṇḍeyavajrasaṃvāde śrīnāradasya śvetadvīpagamanavarṇano nāmaikonapañcāśadadhika triśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ || 349 ||

<3.351>
mārkaṇḍeya uvāca ||
sa dadarśa tadā viṣṇuṃ viśvarūpadharaṃ harim ||
yuktaṃ varṇaiḥ śukaprakhyaiḥ pītaraktasitāsitaiḥ || 1 ||
kvacinmecakasaṃyuktaiḥ kvacinmecakarañjitaiḥ ||
cicchaśāṃkasaṃkāśaṃ kvaciddhiṅgulasannibham || || 2 ||
kvacinmarakataprakhyaṃ padmarāganibhaṃ kvacit ||
kvacitkanakasaṃkāśaṃ kvacinnīlotpalaprabham || 3 ||
kvacitpāṭalapuṣpābhaṃ kvacidgaganasannibham ||
kuṃkumakṣodasaṃkāśaṃ haridrakṣodasannibham || 4 ||
kvacidrajopalaprakhyamindranīlanibhaṃ kvacit ||
anirdeśyāni varṇāni tathā bibhratkvacitkvacit || 5 ||
nānāvidhānāṃ sattvānāṃ vadanāni bahūni ca ||
brahmaśaṃkaraśakrāṇāṃ yamasya varuṇasya ca || 6 ||
dhanadasya hutāśasya vāyornirṛtinastathā ||
grahāṇāmatha ṛkṣāṇāṃ rudrāṇāṃ vasubhiḥ saha || 7 ||
ādityānāṃ samarutāṃ bhṛgūṇāṃ ca tathā dvija ||
tathaivāṅgiraso rājansādhyānāmaśvibhiḥ saha || 8 ||
daityānāmatha nānānāṃ gandharvoragarakṣasām ||
pitṛpannagayakṣāṇāṃ tathā ca piśitāśinām || 9 ||
manuṣya hayanāgānāṃ gajāśvamṛgapakṣiṇām ||
dvīpiśārdūlasiṃhānāṃ sarveṣāṃ prāṇināmatha || 3.351.10 ||
kānicittasya saumyāni kānicidbhīṣaṇāni ca ||
dadarśa tasya vaktrāṇi śataśo'tha sahasraśaḥ || 11 ||
vaktreṣu tasya raudrasya prāṇisaṃghānyanekaśaḥ ||
dadarśa sarvataścāgnīngrasyamānām̐śca viṣṇunā ||12||
saumyebhyastasya vaktrebhyo nirgacchaṃtyastathā prajāḥ ||
vāhāṃśca vividhām̐stasya sa dadarśātha nāradaḥ || 13||
hastām̐śca vividhākārānnānāmudrāsamanvitān ||
yuktānnānāvidhairbhāṇḍairāyudhaiśca sahasraśaḥ||14||
sruksruvāvedicamasakuśakṛṣṇājināgnibhiḥ ||
kecicca kalaśaṃ ghaṇṭāṃ śaṅkhapadmotpalādibhiḥ||15||
kecitkhaḍgagadācakracarmaśūlaparaśvadhaiḥ ||
kecitpāśamahādaṇḍakṛpāṇaśarakārmukaiḥ || 16 ||
kecidbhuśuṇḍīlaguḍaśaktivajrāśmapaṭṭiśaiḥ ||
bījapūrṇākṣamālāśca pūrṇapātraistathā paraiḥ || 17 ||
evaṃ nānāvidhairbhāṇḍaiḥ karāṃstasya samanvitān ||
asaṃkhyeyānmahātejā dadarśa munisattamaḥ || 18 ||
nṛttaśāstravinirdiṣṭo nṛttakarmaṇi cāpyatha ||
śirāṃsi dṛṣṭayo hastāṃstasya devasya dṛṣṭavān || 19 ||
trailokyaṃ ca tathā kāye sakalaṃ munisattamaḥ ||
devadānavagandharvasayakṣoragarakṣasām || 3.351.20 ||
dvīpāmbhonidhilokaiśca pātālaiścaiva saṃyutam ||
jagatsamagraṃ devasya dehasthaṃ dṛṣṭavānmuniḥ || 21 ||
sa dṛṣṭvā paramaṃ rūpaṃ bhīto hṛṣṭaśca vismitaḥ ||
papāta pādayostasya śirasā sāśrulocanaḥ || 22 ||
stotreṇa caiva tuṣṭāva pūrvoktena janārdanam ||
tamuvāca tato devaḥ prahasannṛṣisattamam || 23 ||
|| śrībhagavānuvāca || ||
uttiṣṭha mābhairdharmajña varaṃ vṛṇu yathepsitam ||
|| śrīnārada uvāca || ||
nityamekāntike deva bhaktirbhavatu sā tvayi ||
nānyaṃ varaṃ kāmayāmi narāṇāmidamuttamam || 24 ||
|| mārkaṇḍeya uvāca || ||
tamuvāca tato devaḥ praṇatārtiharo hariḥ ||
etannisargasiddhaṃ te yena tvaṃ dṛṣṭavānmama || 25 ||
viśvarūpamidaṃ brahmannavajñeyaṃ tathā tvayā ||
tapastaptaṃ yato vipra mamāpi ca tatastava || 26 ||
rūpametatsamāsthāya rūpaṃ svaṃ dvija darśitam ||
icchārūpāṇyahaṃ kuryāṃ śataśo'tha sahasraśaḥ || 27 ||
trailokye yāni rūpāṇi devādīnāṃ dvijottama ||
mamaiva tāni jānīhi matto'nyannāsti kiñcana || 28 ||
ahaṃ bhūtaṃ ca bhavyaṃ ca vartamānamahaṃ tathā ||
sthāvaraṃ jaṅgamaṃ caiva sarvamevāsmi nārada || 29 ||
saccā'saccāhamevātra tattvametadbravīmi te ||
aśabdamarasaṃ sparśagandharūpavivarjitam || 3.351.30 ||
sarvagaṃ māṃ vijānīhi paramārthena nārada ||
yaḥ kariṣyati me stotraṃ stotreṇa tvatkṛtena tu || 33 ||
tasya kāmānvidhāsyāmi ye divyā ye ca mānuṣāḥ ||
śvetadvīpe gatistasya mṛtasya ca bhaviṣyati || 32 ||
śvetadvīpamavāpyāpi naraḥ svastho bhaviṣyasi ||
varāhasyāsya kalpasya yāvatkālo'vaśiṣyate || 33
vārāhe tu gate kalpe tato mātsyo bhaviṣyati ||
devakāryāṇi kāryāṇi subahūni yatastvayā || 34 ||
prādurbhāvagataścāhamanuśāsyastathā tvayā ||
prādurbhāvāṇi me brahmanvyatītāni sahasraśaḥ || 35 ||
bhaviṣyanti tathānyāni tatra me śṛṇu kānicit ||
ahamekārṇave loke śeṣaparyaṃkaśāyikaḥ || 36 ||
lakṣmīsahāyastiṣṭhāmi yadā suptaḥ pitāmahaḥ ||
ahaṃ matsyastathā kūrmo haṃsohmapi nārada || 37 ||
strīrūpeṇa mayā vipra vañcitā daityadānavāḥ ||
mayāśvaśirasā vedā dānavebhyastathā hṛtāḥ || 38 ||
vārāheṇa mayā bhūtvā vasudheyaṃ samuddhṛtā ||
vārāheṇa mayā daityāḥ pātālatalagā hatāḥ || 39 ||
nṛvarāheṇa nihato hiraṇyākṣo balotkaṭaḥ ||
dānavāśca hatā yuddhe naranārāyaṇātmanā || 3.351.40 ||
lokottare tathā mārge hariḥ kṛṣṇastathāpyaham ||
nārasiṃhena rūpeṇa hiraṇyakaśipurhataḥ || 41 ||
vāmanena mayā bhūtvā balestribhuvanaṃ hṛtam ||
tribhiḥ kramaiḥ purā brahmandevānāṃ hitakāmyayā || 42 ||
vāḍavo'haṃ samudrasthaḥ pibāmi salilaṃ sadā ||
pṛthvī vasumatī brahmanpṛthunā ca mayā kṛtā || 43 ||
kāpilena mayā duṣṭā dagdhavyāḥ sagarātmajāḥ ||
dattātreyeṇa vasudhā devā dvijagaṇāḥ punaḥ || 44 ||
jāmadagnyena rāmeṇa laghvī vasumatī kṛtā ||
trisapta kṛtvaḥ kartavyā kṣatrabhāraprapīḍitā || 45 ||
dattvā caturdhā putrām̐śca mayā daśarathasya ca ||
rāmeṇa rāvaṇavadhaḥ kartavyo janatāsukham || 46 ||
kāryaṃ gandharvanidhanaṃ tathaiva bharatātmanā ||
meghanādavadhaḥ kāryo lakṣmaṇena tathā mayā ||47||
śatrughnena ca karttavyo lavaṇasya vadhastathā||
mayā vālmīkinā kāryaṃ kāvyaṃ rāmāyaṇaṃ tathā ||48||
vyāsen vedā vaktavyā ākhyānaṃ bhārataṃ mahat ||
mayā brāhmaṇaśārdūla tathā dvaipāyanātmanā||49||
pāṇḍoḥ putratvamāsādya pañcadhā ca tathā mayā ||
dvidhā ca vasudevasya laghvī kāryā vasundharā ||3.351.50||
naraloke nihantavyā daityā mānuṣarūpiṇaḥ ||
balabhadreṇa ca mayā hantavyā mauṣṭikādayaḥ || 51 ||
mayā kṛṣṇena hantavyāḥ kaṃsapūrvā mahābalāḥ ||
pradyumnena nihantavyaḥ śambaraśca mahābalaḥ || 52 |||
hantavyāścāniruddhena daityāḥ śatasahasraśaḥ ||
sāṃbena yuyudhānena mayā bhūtvā tathaiva ca ||53||
mayā buddhena vaktavyā dharmāḥ kaliyuge punaḥ ||
hantavyā mleccharājānastathā viṣṇuvaśātmanā ||54||
mamāṃśena vijānīhi pṛthivyāṃ cakravartinaḥ ||
ṛṣayaśca tathā jñeyā mamaivāṃśasamudbhavāḥ ||55||
yadyadvibhūtimatsattvaṃ śrīmadūrjitameva ca ||
tattadevāvagacchestvaṃ mama tejoṃśasambhavam || 56 ||
atha vā bahunaitena kiṃ jñānena tavānagha ||
viṣṭabhyāhamidaṃ kṛtsnamekāṃśenāsthito jagat || 57||
tadgaccha mānasī siddhiḥ sadā te'stu dvijottama ||
kalpāvasānamāsādya maccharīraṃ pravekṣyasi || 58 ||
etāvaduktvā vacanaṃ tatraivāntaradhīyata ||
bhagavānnāradaścakre pūjāṃ taddeśavāsinām ||59||
taiśca saṃpūjito viprastānpraṇamya yatavrataḥ ||
ājagāma tataḥ śīghraṃ naranārāyaṇāśramam || 3.351.60 ||
pādayornyapatattatra sa tayormunipūjyayoḥ ||
tābhyāṃ saṃpūjito viprastatrovāsa tadāśrame || 61 ||
tataḥ kadācittaṃ vipramūcatustau tapodhanau ||
āvābhyāṃ tvaṃ tathā dṛṣṭaḥ śvetadvīpagate'cyutaḥ || 62 ||
samīpaṃ devadevasya taddehasthairyathāsukham ||
loke'sminmuniśārdūla nāsti dhanyatarastvayā || 63 ||
dṛṣṭavānasi yadbrahmanviśvarūpadharaṃ harim ||
trailokyasāraṃ viśveśaṃ praṇatārtivināśanam || 64 ||
yataste'nugrahastena kṛto devena viṣṇunā ||
tato guhyaṃ nibodhemaṃ tatpūjāvidhimuttamam || 65 ||
sarvakarmakaraṃ divyaṃ sakalārthapradaṃ śivam ||
nābhaktāya ca taddeyaṃ tvayā brāhmaṇasattama ||66||
ekāntabhāvopagataṃ janārdanaṃ narastu sampūjya tathā vidhānataḥ ||
prayāti taddvīpamanuttamaṃ śivaṃ na yatra gatvā vinivartate punaḥ || 67||
iti śrīviṣṇudharmottare tṛtīyakhaṇḍe mārkaṇḍeya vajrasaṃvāde viśvarūpākhyānaṃ nāmaikapañcāśaduttaratriśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ||351||
<3.352>
naranārāyaṇāvūcatuḥ ||
ādāveva saraḥsaritprasravaṇasamīpagṛhopavanaparvatamastakānāmanyatame hṛdye deśe susamaṃ manoharaṃ hastamātraṃ maṇḍalakamupakalpayet ||
tatra madhye'ṣṭapatraṃ padmamālikhet tatastūdāhṛtacaturasrayā caturdvāralekhayā vibhajet |
tataḥ padmakarṇikāmadhye śvetaṃ śatapatraṃ padmaṃ dhyāyet |
tatkarṇikoparyarkamaṇḍalam tadupari candramaṇḍalam tadupari agnimaṇḍalamtanmadhye paramaṃ puruṣam aśarīram agandham arasam arūpam asparśam aśabdaṃ sarvagaṃ plutāntam oṃkāraṃ vinyaset |
kamalapūrvadale akāraṃ vāsudevaṃ nyaset śvetavarṇaṃ dhyāyet dakṣiṇe ākāraṃ saṃkarṣaṇaṃ vinyaset padmapatrābhaṃ taṃ ca dhyāyet|
paścime dale oṅkāre pradyumnaṃ vinyaset pītavarṇaṃ ca dhyāyet ||
uttare dale akāramaniruddhaṃ vinyasetkṛṣṇavarṇañca dhyāyet ||
aiśāne dale taditi brahmāṇaṃ vinyaset padmapatrābhaṃ dhyāyet ||
sūryakoṭisamaṃ dhyāyet ||
vāyavyadale varāhaṃ vinyaset|
bhinnāñjanasamaṃ dhyāyet||
prapūrvadrāre oṃ keṃ ṭeṃ yeṃ etānyakṣarāṇi vainateyaṃ vinyaset||
hemavarṇaṃ ca dhyāyet||
dakṣiṇe dvāre tejoratharatṛcakraṃ sudarśanaṃ vinyaset |
vajranābhaṃ sahasrāraṃ sūryakoṭisamaprabhaṃ dhyāyet ||
paścimadvāre śārṅgaṃ vinyaset |
indracāpanibhaṃ dhyāyet ||
sveṃ vai ṭheṃ heṃ theṃ uttare dvāre aniruddhaṃ nyasyet |
sarvavarṇaṃ dhyāyet |
veṃ ṇeṃ kṣeṃ kṣeṃ vidikṣu pāñcajanyaṃ śaṃkhaṃ nyaset |
śaśāṅkavarṇaṃ dhyāyet ||
gheṃ heṃ bheṃ heṃ śriyaṃ padmaṃ dakṣiṇato nyaset |
śuklāṃ dhyāyet ||
geṃ ḍeṃ veṃ śeṃ padmāntarataḥ puṣṭiṃ nyaset |
śvetāṃ dhyāyet |
heṃ seṃ purato vanamālāṃ nyaset |
heṃ seṃ bahuvarṇāṃ dhyāyet ||
oṃ cityaiśānyāṃ nandakaṃ nyaset |
ākāśavarṇaṃ dhyāyet ||
oṃmityāgneyyāṃ varma nyaset |
bahuvarṇaṃ dhyāyet ||
oṃ kṣeṃ leṃ śrīvatsaṃ nyaset |
śuklāṃ dhyāyet||
oṃ cheṃ ṭheṃ vāyavye kaustubhaṃ nyaset ādityaṃ dīptaṃ dhyāyet||
tato maṃḍale bāhyasthāṃ yathāsvadiśamokāraukārau dikpatī śakrāgnī yamanairṛtavāruṇapavanakuberānaiśānyāṃ nyaset |
yathoktarūpaṃ dhyāyet ||
oṃ namo bhagavate vāsudevāyetyantena dvādaśākṣareṇa karṇikāmadhye puruṣāyārghyapādyācamanīyānulepanapuṣpadīpadhūpamadhuparkanaivedyaphalabhakṣyāṇi nivedayet | oṃnamo nārāyaṇāyetyatha vāṣṭākṣareṇa mantreṇa |
oṃ jitante puṇḍarīkākṣa namaste viśvabhāvana ||
namastestu hṛṣīkeśa mahāpuruṣa pūrvaja ||
ityanena maṃtreṇa puruṣasūktena vā |
athānyāndalaniviṣṭāndevānyathābhihitairmantraiḥ pṛthakpṛthagarcayed |
anena vidhinā yastu viṣṇumabhyarcayennaraḥ |
sarvānsaṃsādhayedarthānkṛtakṛtyo hi jāyate || 18 ||
kṛtvā vyādhiṃ vyādhitaṃ bhakṣayati na karoti na tadāpnoti idaṃ tvanyat |
dhanyaṃ yaśasyaṃ puṇyaṃ ca pavitraṃ pāpanāśanam ||
ārogyaṃ dhanadhānyavarddhanaṃ śatrughnaṃ vaśīkaraṇaṃ yaḥ puruṣo'nena vidhinā satataṃ devamabhyarcayati sampattiṃ janayati atha vā tasya na rocate tadā śvetadvīpamavāpya arkamaṇḍalaṃ bhittvā niruṇaddhi pradyumnasaṃkarṣaṇānprāpnoti sudevatvamavāpya niṣkalatvamāpnoti ||
iti śrīviṣṇudharmottare tṛ0 khaṃ0 mā0 va0 saṃ0 nārāyaṇapūjanaprakāravarṇano nāma dvipa ñcāśaduttaratriśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ || 352 ||
<3.353>
|| mārkaṇḍeya uvāca || ||
etaddhi nāradaḥ śrutvā naranārāyaṇeritam ||
anenaiva vidhāsatataṃ madhusūdanam || 1 ||
pūjayāmāsa dharmātmā tadgatenāntarātmanā ||
tataḥ puṃsavano bhagavān nāradasya mahātmanaḥ || 2 ||
pratyakṣataḥ punarbhūtaṃ nāradaṃ vākyamabravīt ||
gaccha nārada bhadra tvaṃ lokāṃścara yathepsitān||3||
darśanaṃ tava dāsyāmi kālekāle yathepsite||
anugrahamidaṃ prāpya deveśātpuruṣottamāt ||4||
cacāra nārado lokānnityaṃ saṃpūjayanharim ||
viśvarūpamidaṃ dṛṣṭaṃ nāradena mahātmanā || 5 ||
prahlādena punastena narakesarirūpiṇaḥ ||
tathā cāmṛtakādehe tenaiva ca mahātmanā || 6 ||
brahmaṇā ca purā dṛṣṭaṃ trailokyākramaṇe punaḥ ||
paścime sāgare dṛṣṭaṃ daśagrīveṇa rakṣasā || 7 ||
kṛṣṇāvatāre dūtyena gatasya madhuvidviṣaḥ ||
dhārtarāṣṭrasabhāmadhye ṛṣibhiśca parīkṣitaḥ || 8 ||
mahābhāratasaṃgrāme dṛṣṭavānarjunastathā ||
bandhunāśabhayodvigno yatra tena prabodhitaḥ ||9||
nivṛtte bhārate yuddhe gacchanto dvārakāṃ punaḥ ||
mārgāgatena taddṛṣṭamuttaṃkena mahātmanā ||3.353.10||
trailokyanātho govindaḥ satataṃ bhaktavatsalaḥ||
tasya karmāṇi yo nityaṃ puruṣaḥ parikīrtayet || 11 ||
śṛṇuyādvā mahīpāla śucistadgatamānasaḥ ||
sarvapāpavinirmuktaḥ sa yāti paramāṃ gatim|| 12 ||
evaṃ sa rājandvijavaryamukhyaḥ saṃprāptavāndevavarātprasādam || bhaktikramakraiyanṛpaprasādāttasmāddhikāmānpuruṣā labhaṃte || 13 ||
iti śrīviṣṇudharmottare tṛ0 kha0 mārkaṇḍeyavajrasaṃvāde nāradaprasādo nāma tripañcāśaduttaratriśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ || 353 ||
<3.354>
|| vajra uvāca || ||
māhātmyaṃ devadevasya viṣṇoramitatejasaḥ ||
tṛptirna vidyate tasmānmāhātmyamaparaṃ vada || 1 ||
tasyaiva devadevasya viṣṇoramitatejasaḥ ||
rakṣāyuktaṃ pavitraṃ ca lokeṣvāścaryamuttamam || 2 ||
|| mārkaṇḍeya uvāca || ||
ekāntabhāvopagataḥ kaścidāsīddvijottamaḥ || 3 ||
sāttvato gautamaśreṣṭho viṣvaksena iti śrutaḥ ||
pañcakālavidhānajñaḥ pāñcarātravidhānavit || 4 ||
satataṃ pūjayāmāsa devadevaṃ janārdanam||
kṛṣṇāyatanapūjārthaṃ sa babhrāma vasundharām ||5||
āsasāda kadācittu parvataṃ gahvaraṃ mahat||
yatra māheśvaraḥ sarvo jano yadbalajo bahu||6||
sa tatra nirjhare snātvā pūjayāmāsa keśavam||
vedyāṃ prakalpitāyāṃ tu bhogaiḥ sannihitaiḥ prabhum||7||
tasmāttu gahvārātkaścidviniṣkramya yadṛcchayā|
nibaddhakhaḍgairbahubhiḥ puruṣaiścāpapāṇibhiḥ||8||
sahitastaṃ dadarśātha pūjayanvai janārdanam||
tasya gatvā samīpe tu grāmasvāmikumārakaḥ||9||
tamuvāca tvamabhyehi devakarma kuruṣva me ||
śirorugarditastāta snātuṃ śaknomi nādya vai || 3.354.10 ||
devakarmaṇyaśaktirme tāta pūjaya śaṅkaram ||
devatāyatanaṃ gatvā yatra tāta pratiṣṭhitam || 11 ||
liṅgamasti sureśasya mahādevasya nirmitam ||
etaduktaṃ pratyuvāca vayamekārcinaḥ śrutāḥ || 12 ||
caturātmā hariḥ pūjyaḥ prādurbhāvagato'tha vā ||
pūjayāmyadya naivānyaṃ tasmāttvaṃ gaccha mā ciram || 13 ||
evamuktaḥ kumārastu khaḍgamākṛṣya sattvaram ||
tamuvāca tadā vipraṃ tvāṃ vadhiṣye na saṃśayaḥ || 14 ||
yadi liṅgārcanaṃ gatvā na karoṣi hi durmate ||
śastrādadhiko devo na hyekosti narādhama ||15||
khaḍgena pātayiṣyāmi bhavacchīrṣamasaṃśayam ||
evamukteḥ sa tenātha prāṇarakṣā parāyaṇaḥ || 16 ||
bahirgacchāmi tatreti tena sārdhaṃ tadā yayau ||
dṛṣṭvā ca liṅgaṃ devasya cintayāmāsa dharmavit || 17 ||
viṣṇuḥ sarvagato devaḥ sarvātmā madhusūdanaḥ ||
tasya raudreṇa bhāvena mahādevaḥ pratiṣṭhitaḥ || 18 ||
tasya vai raudrabhāvena narasiṃhastathā prabhuḥ ||
liṅgesminpūjayiṣyāmi narasiṃhavapurdharam ||
devadevaṃ mahātmānaṃ tena nityaṃ surārcanāt || 19 ||
pratyapāyo na bhavitā prāṇatrāṇamabhīpsitaḥ ||
tataḥ sa devadeveśaṃ narasiṃhavapurdharam || 3.354.20 ||
manasā tu tadā dhyātvā pūjayāmāsa liṅgagam ||
namostu te nṛsiṃhāya vadanneva kṛtāñjaliḥ || 21 ||
namostu narasiṃhāya śrutvā tasmātsubhāṣitam ||
grāmeśanandanaḥ kruddhaḥ khaḍgamākṛṣya sattvaram || 22 ||
prahāramudyato dātuṃ tasya viprasya dhīmataḥ ||
liṅgaṃ bhittvā tato devo narasiṃhavapurdharaḥ || 23 ||
samutthāya tataḥ krodhād grāmasvāmikumārakam ||
dadāha bhagavāñchīghraṃ sānugaṃ netravahninā || 24 ||
tasya sāttvatamukhyasya divyaṃ cakṣurdadau tataḥ ||
dadarśa yena veśena narasiṃhavapurdharam || 25 ||
dṛṣṭvā stotreṇa tuṣṭāva tadā tasya janārdanaḥ ||
stotrasya tu varaṃ prādāttanme nigadataḥ śṛṇu || 26 ||
rakṣoghnaṃ pāpaśamanaṃ bhūtavetālanāśanam ||
yo japettatsadaivāstu maṅgalyaṃ śrīvivardhanam || 27 ||
nārasiṃheṣu lokeṣu modaṃte sahito mayā ||
yāvatkalpāvaśeṣaṃ tu tato māntvamupeṣyasi || 28 ||
etāvaduktvā sahasā tu tena tatraiva devapravaro mahātmā ||
apaśyatāṃ yādavavaṃśamukhya jagāma śīghraṃ narasiṃhamūrtiḥ || 29 ||
iti śrīviṣṇudharmottare tṛtīyakhaṇḍe mārkaṇḍeyavajrasaṃvāde liṅgasphoṭananarasiṃhadarśanavarṇano nāma catuṣpañcāśadadhikatriśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ || 354 ||

vajra uvāca ||
sāttvatena stuto yena stotreṇa madhusūdanaḥ ||

tvamāgacchāgaccha bhagavannātmabalavīryatejoguṇātmavikramasāṃkhyayogādhyātmapavitraśaucadharmamāhātmyānvitāvirmāvitotti ṣṭha paramadeva sarvagātrāṇyabhidhunvansarvapāpānyapamṛjya vipranāśāviṣṭānāṃ vipranāśāya vidyānāṃ paramapuruṣa paramamāṅgalya bhaktavatsala bhaktānugrahāyābhayaṃpradāya tadbrahmasatyamanādyamanuttamaṃ niḥśreyasaṃ tadbhagavānācaṣṭāṃ tadbhagavānvidatāṃ nirasya pāpmānaṃ nirasya kalmaṣaṃ śaśvacchāntimupanaya śreya upanaya svastimupanaya āśiṣamupanaya brahma prapadye tvāṃ viṣṇo bhagavanpuṇḍarīkākṣa jñānaiśvaryaśaktibalatejoguṇātmaka paramapuruṣa paramamaṅgalya padmanābha hayaśiraḥ ādivarāha narasiṃha vāmana trivikrama rāma rāma vāsudeva saṅkarṣaṇa pradyumnāniruddha puruṣa satyāśruta vāsudeva ādimadhyanidhanakaceṣṭita ceṣṭātmamāyāhorātrayatimāñjiṣṭhaśuklavāsaḥ suparṇa tālamakaradhvajaparamavidyākarmakālanābha svastyasmabhyaṃ svasti prajābhyaḥ ||
tvameva maṃtraṃ tvaṃ rakṣā tvamauṣadhamanuttamam ||
trividhādapi duḥkhānmā pracodaya jagatpate || 1 ||
brahmanprapadye tvāṃ viṣṇo śaraṇāgatavatsala ||
tadā śuddho mahāmatirbhavaduḥkhavinirgataḥ || 2 ||
ityuktaḥ sa ca rājendra ṛṣiṇā divyacakṣuṣā ||
namaskṛtya ṛṣeḥ pādau sagaṇaḥ saha rājabhiḥ ||
pradakṣiṇamupākṛtya praviveśa puraṃ svakam || 3 ||
ṛṣayaste'pyanujñātā mārkaṇḍeyena dhīmatā ||
namaskṛtya hariṃ devaṃ svāśramaṃ jagmire tadā || 4 ||
rājāpi vajro dharmātmā mārkaṇḍeyena bhāṣitam ||
purāṇaṃ cintayannityaṃ nārāyaṇaparāyaṇaḥ ||
rājyaṃ ca prāśiṣannityaṃ prajā dharmeṇa pālayan || 5 ||
iti śrīviṣṇumahāpurāṇe dvitīyabhāge śrīviṣṇudharmottare tṛtīyakhaṇḍe mārkaṇḍeyavajrasaṃvāde śrīnarasiṃhastotravarṇanaṃ nāma pañcapañcāśaduttaratriśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ || 355 ||
iti śrīviṣṇudharmottaraṃ samāptam || ||
samāptaṃ cedaṃ viṣṇumahāpurāṇama ||
